(1a)
oṃ namaḥ śivādigurubhyaḥ ।

pāyādvaḥ kālakālakṣayaraṇisamahārāvaraudrāṭṭahāsā,
haḍḍāhaḍḍāyudhāśca pravimathitamahābhairavāghoradīptāḥ ।
ekāśīti prasarpadravipathavigaladrasmicakrapravāhā,
mudrāsphoṭāsphuṭaughasphuṭitadaśadiśā kālarātrīpracaṇḍā ।।

yā bhīmā bhairavā(ddhi) bhramadamalalasallolabhogeṃdragarbhā,
paṃcāsyoddāmajihvākṣapitakulavilakṣobhacaṃcatpracārā ।
pāyādvorāviṇīyā'pahṛtabhavabhaṭā bhairavībhīmarāvā,
sarvvādhvādhīṣamuṃḍā truṭadamalamahāhāra(hā)riprahārā ।।

yasyāḥ kālāgnilakṣāṇyaviralavigaladromakūpaiḥ patanti,
vyādhunvaṃtyāḥ śiroruk prabhṛtivaraśikhākṣepasaṃghaṭṭaghoṣaiḥ ।
pāyādvaḥ sā karaṃkāka(ra)varaṇaśiroddāmadhāmapragalbhā,
bhārākrānteva caṃcacaṭacaṭitacaṭaccoṭayantīnsvapiṇḍaṃ ।।

yasyāścaṇḍaprapātābhihatividalitaṃ kośamuccaiḥ ṣaḍrasrāṃ,
tasmādutthāya kopādgrasati jagadidaṃ paṃcabhiḥ paṃcarāvā ।
sā māyā nityakālaṃ kṛṣatanuramalā kālayaṃtīṃ svakālaṃ,
krodhādudbhrāntadṛṣṭiḥ pracalitasumahadbhīmahuṃkāragarbhā ।।

prāṇāpānakṣayābdhikṣubhadamalamahāvāḍavāgnau vilāpaḥ,
kṛtvā narttanti madhyoditaśikhiśaśibhṛtsūryasadvāmasaṃdhau ।
sānaṃdoddāmasatvodrumaraḍamarutoddāmaraṃ vādayantī,
(1b)
pāyādvaḥ sā karaṃkākavalitamṛtabhibhairavākāraveśā ।।

ṣaṭcakrādhāralakṣakṣapaṇagurubharābhairavī citkṛpāṇau,
retobhā bhānumadhye śaśi carati tathā vātacakrepi kākve ।
pāyādvaḥ sā sama(stu)ramukuṭaśiracaṃdrikācitparākhyā,
kālā kaṃkālakaṃdodbhaṭakuṭakuṭilākoṭisaṃcidvilāsā ।।

yasyāḥ pādaprahārāhatasakalasuroddāmacakrogracaṃcat,
pātātyevodabhūvaṃ dhararacitamahārāviṇī rāvakāṇi ।
ekāśītiprakārāṇyapi navakalitānyeva kālīvatādvaḥ,
sā ghorā haḍḍarūpā tridaśagatiratā saptasaptādaśārṇṇā ।।

kādyaṃ kaṃkālayuktā kulakamalakalā kālikā kaṃdakoṇā,
kaṃkālī kālayantī kulanakulakalā kovidaṃ kārgalasthā ।
pāyādvaḥ kālakāmāraṇikalitakalā kelikekā kalāpaiḥ
kādye koṣṇaṃ kaleśī(kra)valayati sadā sā karaṃkā krameṇa ।।

a(vvo)pakarṇya sā bhūyo devadevānmaheśvarān ।
aṣṭādaśasahasrāṇi tantrarājaṃ jayadrathaṃ ।।
punaḥ pṛṣṭavatī devī rahasyaṃ rāviṇīkramaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrutaṃ mayā devadeva ṣaṭkatrayamanuttamaṃ ।
mantravīryasamopetaṃ rahasyānekasaṃkulaṃ ।।

nānā(śca)ryamahākīrṇṇaṃ sara*syaṃ mahādbhutaṃ ।
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi caturthaṃ ṣaṭkamuttamaṃ ।।

kathaṃ sā rāviṇī devī ekāśītipadairgatā ।
(2a)
kāni karmāṇi deveśa saṃsādhayati sādhitā ।।

mudrāvīryaṃ na vijñātaṃ tantresminsarahasyakaṃ ।
kati mudrāḥ kathaṃ mudrā tāsāṃ vīryaṃ kathaṃ sthitaṃ ।।

kasyāṃ bhūtiḥ kathaṃ deva kathaṃ siddhyanti maṃtriṇāṃ ।
kā kasyādeveṇyāḥ * * sānidhyaṃ prakariṣyati ।।

evamākarṇya devendro bhairavī bhīṣaṇākṛtiḥ ।
āviśya mudrāvīryo(ghā)dvikāśyakramabaṃdhanaṃ ।।

pramathyasurasaṃdohaṃ prāśya paṃcāmṛtaṃ rasaṃ ।
rāviṇīkulamāśritya provāca parameśvaraḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge pṛṣṭohaṃ yattvayādhunā ।
jaya * thahitārthāya vīrāṇāṃ ca maheśvari ।।

mahārahasyamatulaṃ haravaktrāṃbujodbhataṃ ।
akathyamatulaṃ śāntaṃ śivātprāptaṃ mayā purā ।
mudrāmnāyaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarahasyaṃ yathāsthitaṃ ।।

tacchṛṇuṣva mahābhāge tavādya kathayāmyahaṃ ।
yosau paramanirvvāṇaḥ śivaḥ paramakāraṇaḥ ।।

sadasatyakṣanirmuktaḥ ṣaḍadhvātāta vigrahaḥ ।
paripūrṇṇapraśāntātmā vyāpakaḥ kalanojjhitaḥ ।।

nityatṛptaśca sarvvajñaḥ sarvvakarttā ca citparaḥ ।
tasya vīryaṃ mahāguhyaṃ sadrūpaṃ samavāyajaṃ ।।

mudritaṃ tena tatsarvvamakhilaṃ jagadadbhutaṃ ।
tatsvarūpā tu bedhena yayākāraparigrahaḥ ।।

ākṣepaghoraśāntādivimiśrakaraṇānugaḥ ।
mudrāvīryaṃ sa evaṃ syādabhinno vīryamaṃtharaḥ ।।

paṃcadhā tatparijñeyaṃ kāryabhedānnagātmaje ।
(2b)
cittavākkāyabhedena lekhyagolakabhedataḥ ।।

evaṃ vīryaṃ vibhinnaṃ hi yathāvadupadiśyate ।
ādyasya paripūrṇṇasya saṃvidullāsa eva ca ।।

tadbhāvanānubaṃdhena śāntāraudrādibhedataḥ ।
śṛṃgāravīrabībhatsa hāsyakāruṇya ādarān ।।

tadbhūtāṃtarasaṃsthityā tadā veśavaśena hi ।
prathamaṃ tatsamākhyātaṃ punarbhedādanaṃtatāṃ ।।

sa eṣa gacchate devi dvitīyamadhunocyate ।
parā paśyanti madhyamā vaikharī yā caturthikā ।।

tāsāṃ vyakti yathā proktā pratibhāgena mātṛkāṃ ।
dvitīyaḥ saduhaproktastṛtīyamadhunocyate ।।

rasāveśavaśasthityā devatākaralaṃbanaṃ ।
karadṛṣṭyāṃgacidvadvaghanatvāṃgatṛtīyakaḥ ।।

caturthamadhunā devi śṛṇu samyagyathāsthitaṃ ।
devatākṛtiṃ padmādi triśūlāsipavirgaṇaiḥ ।।

vyaktimeva prakārādipaṃcaraṃgavibhedataḥ ।
piṃḍaratnacarudravyamṛnmayākṛtisaṃbhavaḥ ।।

paṃcamaḥ sa samākhyāto vīryabhedo gaṇāṃbike ।
evaṃ sarvvatra mudrākhyaṃ pīṭhe vyaktiṃ vyavasthitaṃ ।।

yacchāmbhavaṃ mahāvīryamanumarttavyameva tat ।
sarvvamudrāstvanenaiva sūryaṃ vīryeṇa bhairavī ।।

ato bhedaṃ na caivātra karttavyaṃ kuliśodare ।
avvocyate prapānākhyā mudrātantrasya bhedataḥ ।।

vijṛṃbhate mahābhāge bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।
mahāmudrā kapālākhyā gītimudrā tathāparā ।।

makārākhyā mahāghorā padmamudrā tathāparā ।
(3a)
tathā paṃcaśikhā nāmnī tathā cānyā karoṭikā ।।

muśalaṃ ca mahāraudraṃ mudgaraṃ ghorameva ca ।
loṣṭhakākhyā tathaivānyā vajramudrā mahābalā ।।

khaḍgamudrā sureśānī bhusuṃḍī tomarāstathā ।
prāsādamudrā cakramudrā matsyā duṣṭātakī mahān ।।

kacchapākhyā potamudrā vimānākhyā tathāparā ।
tena mudrā patāko(tha) jvālinī ca phalābhidhā ।।

mukulā kalaśākhyā ca chatramudrā tathaiva ca ।
mahāyogeśvarī cānyā mahāvyomā tathāparā ।।

triśūlamudrā duḥprekṣā tathānyāsurabhīti - ।
mṛgamudrā tathā cānyā yonimudrā tathāparā ।।

sumanā nāma mudreśī ekaliṃgā prakīrttitā ।
pannagā ca ulūkākhyā śavamudrā mahābalā ।।

pātramudrā tathā cāśvāmudrā vārāhikā tathā ।
siṃhavaktrābhidhā mudrā tathaivānyā viśālikā ।।

siddhirohā mahāmudrā yogavajrā tathaiva ca ।
śaṃvarākhyā mahāmudrā vajrasūcā tathā smṛtā ।।

mahāmāyābhidhā caiva śāntākhyā karttarī tathā ।
svamaṃgalā tathā mudrā praṇatā ca tathā priye ।।

jyeṣṭhā ca kāmarohānyā tathā cānyātha śaṃkarā ।
samayākhyā mahāmu(drā) namaskārī tathāparā ।।

mudrā prabhakṣaṇī nāma mahātārkṣī tathāparā ।
vikaṭhā bhīṣaṇā caiva bhairavī ca tathā priye ।।

kāmarūpā tathaivānyā sūcīmudrā maholbaṇā ।
(3b)
vajrapaṃjaraevānyā sarvvarakṣākarī mahān ।।

sūrpamudrā karoṭā ca kaṃbumudrā tathā smṛtā ।
vicitrā ca tathā proktā tathaiva ca surohiṇī ।।

karaṃkākhyā mahāmudrā kṣurikā ca maheśvari ।
pāśapātrābhidhā mudrā tathā saṃgrāmaneśvarī ।।

mudreśvarī vicitrākhyā caṃcalākhyā tathāparā ।
capalā nāma mudreśī kāntā cānyā prakīrttitā ।।

sumudā cāparā jñeyā bhṛkuṭī paramāmṛtā ।
nāyikā kālakarṇṇī ca mudrā viśvamukhīparā ।।

rohiṇī nāma mudreśi citramālā parāmatā ।
kabaṃdhī nāma cākhyātā mokṣaṇī ca tathāparā ।।

nādākhyā mūrcchanī cānyā kaṃpanī cāparā smṛtā ।
dhruvākhyā ca tathaivānyā mudrā cānyā visarjanī ।।

parikīrṇṇākhyā mahāmudrā tathānyānaṃdanīparā ।
vyomacārī mahāmudrā viśvasūcī tathā priye ।।

mudrā saptaśikhānāmnī cāmarā ca tathāparā ।
vitānākhyā tathā jñeyā tathā cānyā manoruhā ।।

svaśāntā pa(ramapro)ktā bhogahastā tathāparā ।
kroṣṭrakī ca śivā caiva viśālākṣāparā priye ।।

vitānākhyā tathā jñeyā tathā cānyā manoharā ।
tathaivāṃśumatīnāma mudrā(vākarṣaṇī) smṛtā ।।

daṃṣṭriṇī lelihānā ca tathā phetkārikāparā ।
karaṃkiṇī khecarī ca bhairavī paramā smṛtā ।।

ṣaṇmukhā divyamudrā ca bhairavā vikṛtānanā ।
ma(tta) - - vikā nāmnā āviddhā cāparā smṛtā ।।
(4a)
mahāsaṃrāviṇī nāma sarvvāsāṃ nāyakī smṛtā ।
ekaviṃśādhikaśataṃ mudrāṇāṃ parikīrttitaṃ ।।

pratyekādaśadhā jñeyā punaśca sa tathā smṛtā ।
punarbhedairanantāstā mudrāpīṭhe prakīrttitāḥ ।।

āsāṃ kramānugalamadbhutameva vīrya-
taṃtre śiracchidavare pravicāryatatve ।
vakṣyāmi yena nikhilāgamasāravācī,
vīro bhavedakhilayogikulekapūjyaḥ ।।।।

।। iti bhairavasrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiracchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
mudrācadre prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrāṇāṃ parameśvari ।
vidhānaṃ sarahasyaṃ te baṃdhamantrakramaṃ tathā ।।

yannākhyātaṃ mahādevi kasyacittantrasaṃgrahe ।
yaṃ jñātvā sidhyate devi sarvvāvasthopi yo bhavet ।।

priyo mānyaśca sarvvāsāṃ devīnāṃ yo na jāyate ।
yadi dīkṣā tapo yogayukto japaparāyaṇaḥ ।।

ābhirvviyukto śubhage na sa siddhimavāpnuyāt ।
ata āsāṃ vibhāgoyaṃ śṛṇu śailendranaṃdini ।।

parasparaṃ samāyukto hastāmūrddhamukhau kṛtau ।
viralāṃguliyogena dakṣiṇāṃguṣṭhamūrddhagaṃ ।।

anyonyālalitā sarvvā mahāmudreyamuttamā ।
mahāsiddhipradā jñeyā mahāmantraprasādhanā ।।

trailokyaṃ prīṇayetsarvvaṃ baṃdhamānānna saṃśayaḥ ।
(4b)
sarvvādau sarvvamadhye ca sarvvāṃte sarvvamantare ।।

mahāmudrā baṃdhitavyā sarvvakāmārthasādhane ।
prāgvīryasaṃyutā devi mahāmantraprayojitā ।।

ṣaṇmāsādutpatatyeva sādhakaḥ khecarīpadaṃ ।
mahatvaṃ siddhyate tasya mudreśyābaṃdhayogataḥ ।।

mahatvaṃ cāsti vai yasmāttasmānmudrā mahāntikīṃ ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale mahāmudrā ।। ।।

athāmṛtasamākṛṣṇau kapālākhyādhunocyate ।
kṛtvottānaṃ vāmakaraṃ prasṛtāṃgulikurvvatāṃ ।।

vaktrāgragaṃ samāyojya pūrvvavīryāvalokanāt ।
drāvayeccāmṛtaṃ cakraṃ kalāṣoḍaśamaṇḍitaṃ ।।

galantaṃ tatra saṃcintya tarpayedakhilaṃ jagat ।
anayā karṣayetsamyagvā tavā āśusaṃtanau ।।

paṃcāmṛtaṃ mahābhāge mudritā (māṃ) prakarṣayet ।
pūryapātraṃ tu tenaiva devyai datvā svayaṃ pibet ।।

tatkṣaṇātplavate vyomni khecarī cakranāyakaḥ ।।
bhavatyeva mahādevi sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

anayā baddhayā gauri prīyate mātṛmaṃḍalaṃ ।
vidhirūpātiriktaṃ yattatsarvvaṃ pūryate kṣaṇāt ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathakapālamudrā ।।।।

atha kṛtvā sulalite locane lulināntare ।
ullāsayedbhrūlatobhe(tsā)naṃdaḥ kiṃcideva hi ।।

īṣat prahasanaḥ kāryā pūrvvavīryāvalokanāt ।
adhoṅguliniyogena madhyenātra kareṇa hi ।।

tisraḥ saṃśleṣayettatra gītimudrā hayaṃ smṛtāḥ ।
(5a)
etāṃ badhvā pragāyeta caryāso samayopi vā ।।

tacca samyakpravakṣyāmi śṛṇu suṃdari tatvataḥ ।
ho ho ghoreśvarītyādau hāca hāca padaṃ punaḥ ।।

haṃ haṃ haṃ padamuddhṛtya vīrasuṃdari yojayet ।
hāḍapadaṃ tridhā yojya praṇavādyaṃ sudīrghakaṃ ।।

caturvviṃśākṣaraṃ sāma mahāmelā ca siddhidaṃ ।

sa alamahātaaduriavighorakarāla,
kālaṃ kaṃkālini bhairavikālapamaṃtī ajammi ।।

eru hi amāṃsavasabhakṣiṇitahaādhā,
magaṃjasuvi akhiṇi bhairavikālipramantī ।।

evaṃgā - ttadāviro paṭhetsāmamathāpi vā ।
tadā melāpamevāsya praharādbhavati priye ।।

kevalā yadi mudreyaṃ ṣaṇmāsagṛhagobhyaset ।
tadā khecaratāṃ yāti devinā - tra saṃśayaḥ ।।
anayā baddhayā devi prīṇayenmātṛmaṃḍalaṃ ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe gītimudrā ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi vighnavinnāśanīparā ।
makarākhyamahāmudrā sarvvasiddhipradā śubhā ।।

karadvaye sureśāni jyeṣṭhā samyaṅnipīḍayet ।
anāmike tu tatpṛṣṭhe tarjanyastiryapīḍayet ।।

saṃśliṣṭādapi cāṃguṣṭhau ananābhau prasārya tu ।
makarākhyā iyaṃ mudrā * * sarvvavināśanī ।।

paramudrā sphoṭakarī trāsanī sarvvahiṃsakāṃ ।
enāṃ badhvā mahādevi maṃtrāḥ sidhyaṃti vai nṛṇāṃ ।।

nirvvighnasya mahā(bhā)ge rakṣantyeva ca devatāḥ ।
(5b)
evaṃ tava samākhyātā mudreyaṃ mantrarakṣaṇe ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe makāramudrā ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi padmamudrā sulocane ।
yāgasāṃnidhyatāṃ yāti vidyā maṃtreśvareśvaraṃ ।।

maṇibaṃdhau samāśliṣṭau karayorubhayorapi ।
aṃguṣṭhau prasṛtau kuṃcya viralāṃguliyojitaḥ ।।

padmamudrā iyaṃ devi tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।
anayā baddhayā devi trailokye yat pratiṣṭhitaṃ ।।

bhogyasaubhāgyamatulaṃ kathayennātra saṃśayaḥ ।
pūrvvavīryabalenaiva yadā mudrā samabhyaset ।।

māsatrayātkarṣayeta yogajñānaṃ tadā naraḥ ।
evaṃ baddhā sadā sarvve sāṃnidhyaṃ yānti bhairavāḥ ।।

vidyāmantragaṇāḥ sarvve yatheṣṭaṃ sādhakeśvaraḥ ।। ।।

।। jayadrathe padmamudrāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paṃcaśikhā nāmnī mudrā śṛṇu nagātmaje ।
sarvvākarṣakarī yā sā yogabhogaḥ susiddhidā ।।

uttānau viparītau tu veṇībaṃdhau karau kuru ।
anāmne corddhasaṃlagne madhye tiryaṅnipiḍite ।।

karītyaṃguṣṭhasaṃspṛṣṭe tiryaksaṃsthānamaṇḍite ।
bhavatyevaṃ kṛte devi mudrāpaṃcaśikhā priye ।।

carācarasya jagato yadratnaṃ cottamaṃ smṛtaṃ ।
saubhāgyaṃ bhogasantānaṃ jñānaṃ cittaṃ ca suṃdari ।।

karṣayennātra saṃdehaḥ pūrvvavīryabalena ca ।
īdṛśī naiva vidyeta mudrātrailokyamohanī ।।
mahābhāgyapradā jñeyā mokṣadā tanumadhyame ।
(6a)
।। iti jayadrathe paṃcaśikhā ।। ।।

athānyatsaṃpravakṣyāmi karoṭīnāma bhairavī ।
sarvvasampatkarīmudrā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।

viparītatalau hastau saṃgraṃthyānyonyamaṃguli ।
saṃcchādyata pāṇitale pīḍayete vicakṣaṇaḥ ।।

karoṭīnāma mudreyaṃ bāhyeṅguṣṭhanipīḍanāt ।
vināśayati mudraiṣā pāpaughaṃ yadupārjitaṃ ।।

mahāpātakasaṃbhāraṃ (sa)mayollaṃghanaṃ ca yat ।
tatsarvvaṃ nāśayatyeṣā tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā ।।

pūrvvoktavīryasaṃpannā yadā bhairavamaṃdire ।
baṃdhayeddevadeveśi tadā melāpabhāgbhavet ।।।।

।। iti jayadrathe karoṭībaṃdhaḥ ।। ।।

mudrālākṣā śṛṇu śreṣṭhā mudrāduṣṭabhayaṃkarī ।
yaṃ yā saṃcūrṇṇayatyeṣa vighnavrātamanantakaṃ ।।

maṇibaṃdho susaṃśliṣṭe karāvarttaritāṃgulī ।
ūrddhamunnāsya deveśi mudrāmuśalasaṃjñitā ।।

mardanīgiriśṛṃgānāṃ saptapātāladāriṇī ।
sarvvaduṣṭapramathanī ityupadravaghātinī ।।

kṛtyā khākhodalanī bhūtavetālamarddanī ।
duṣṭanāgendramathanaduṣṭāriṣṭakṣayaṃkarī ।।

mārayecchatrusaṃghātaṃ śatrutulyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe muśalamudrā ।। ।।

śṛṇu mudgalamudreyaṃ trailokyatrāsanodyatā ।
yā ca nigrahate sarvvaṃ cūrṇṇayetparvvatopamaṃ ।।

mukulau dvau karau śliṣṭau āku(ryya)ni niyojitau ।
punaḥ punaścālayeta mudrāmudgarasaṃjñitā ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā bhūtaṃ daṃṣṭreṇa darśayet ।
(6b)
darśanādūsmaśāghāti grahosau nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

grahanāgaviṣādīni darśanādeva nāśayet ।
cūrṇṇayedvilayaṃtrāṇi pīḍaye - nnagānapi ।।
bhayaṃ nāśayate sarvvaṃ sādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe mudgaramudrā ।। ।।

atha loṣṭhābhidhāneyaṃ mudrā duṣṭabhayaṃkarī ।
śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi tava samyaksulocane ।।

dakṣiṇaṃ procchṛlaṃ hastaṃ kṛtvā saṃśleṣatastataḥ ।
aṃguṣṭhāṃguliyogena śleṣayeṅguṣṭhakopari ।।

sarvvāṅgulyaḥ smṛtā devi loṣṭhamudrāmahābalā ।
vaśīkaroti bhuvanaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣān ।।

vīryayuktā * sadevi sādhako vigatajvaraḥ ।
baṃdhayeddeva deveśi tadā tasya jagadvaśaṃ ।।

bhavatyeva na saṃdeho vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
nānayā sadṛśī mudrā duṣṭavidhvaṃsinīparā ।।
tava devi samākhyātā mahāmelāpasiddhidā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe loṣṭhamudrā ।। ।।

atha vajrābhidhāneyaṃ mudrāṃ vakṣyāmi yāṃ parāṃ ।
yayā saṃsiddhayā maṃtrī sarvvasiddhiyuto bhavet ।।

viparītakṛtau hastau prasṛtāṃguliyogataḥ ।
kaniṣṭhāṃguṣṭhayogena graṃthayecca parasparaṃ ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā vajramudrā bhavediyaṃ ।
nidhibaṃdhaṃ prayoktavyo tathā kaṃkālasādhane ।।

paraprayuktā maṃtrāṇi dahatyeṣā na saṃśayaḥ ।
pāpaughamakhilaṃ sarvvaṃ samayādivilaṃghanaṃ ।।
(7a)
nāśayatyeva mudreyaṃ yathārkaḥ śārvvarītamaḥ ।
saṃgrāme jayadā jñeyā sarvvapātāladāriṇī ।।

mahāmelāpakaraṇī pūrvvavīryabalena ca ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe vajramudrā ।। ।।

atha khaḍgābhidhāneyaṃ mudrāṃ vakṣyāmi pārvvati ।
ūrddhamunnāmitaṃ hastaṃ cālayeta punaḥ punaḥ ।।

tisroṅgulyorddhagāḥ kāryā kaniṣṭhāṃguṣṭhapīḍitā ।
khaḍgamudreyamākhyātā duritaughanikṛṃtanī ।।

trāsanī sarvvaduṣṭānāṃ ma * saṃvaraṇīparā ।
vighnaughadarpadalanī trailokyabhayadāyaṇī ।।

śmaśānasthaṃ tu melāpaṃ divasaistribhireva ca ।
jāyate sādhakasyaiva devi nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।।

yāvanti bhayajātāni tāvantyeṣā pranāśayet ।
anayā baddhayā devi mantrachidraṃ na jāyate ।।
naśyanti śatravastasya haṃtuṃ ye samupasthitāḥ ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe khaḍgamudrā ।। ।।

athātaḥ paraśuṃ vakṣye sarvvavighnavināśanaṃ ।
chedakaṃ sarvvaduṣṭānāṃ trailokyahitavarddhanaṃ ।।

ūrddhamunnāsya dordaṇḍaṃ tiryaghastaṃ prasārayet ।
cālayeti muhurdevi mudrā paraśusaṃjñitā ।।

kṛtyā khārkhodavighnaughabaṃdhanā dhvaṃsayiṣyati ।
mahāmohapraśamanaṃ sarvvālakṣmīnivarhaṇaṃ ।।

sarvvasaṃkaradoṣaghnaṃ sarvvotsāhaguṇapradaṃ ।
anena siddhimantraḥ syādyatheṣṭā sādhakātmanāṃ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe paraśumudrā ।। ।।
(7b)
bhuśuṃḍī nāmamudreyaṃ pravakṣyāmyadhunā priye ।
yayā vighnāḥ pranaśyanti japaśca saphalo bhavet ।।

yathā paraśurevaṃ ca tanmuṣṭiṃ ca prasādhayet ।
ūrddhagā parameśāni bhusuṃḍī parikīrttitā ।।

pūrvvavīryasusaṃyogā vighnavidrāvaṇīparā ।
etāṃ badhvā pra(ka)rṣeṇa prīṇāti parameśvari ।।

vetālasiddhaye yojyā mahākarṣaṇakarmaṇi ।
yadyat śmaśānikaṃ karma tatra tatra niyojayet ।।
tadā siddhyatyasaṃdehāditi śāstrasya niścayaḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe bhuśuṃḍīmudrā ।। ।।

adhunā tomarākhyeyaṃ śṛṇu mudrāṃ maheśvari ।
sarvvasiddhyarhatā jāye sādhakasya yathā sadā ।।

vāmaṃ mukulitaṃ kṛtvā sabāhustiryaksaṃsthitaḥ ।
saṃgṛhya dakṣiṇaṃ bāhuṃ mudrā tomarasaṃjñitā ।।

vetālasādhane śreṣṭhā nāstyasyāḥ sadṛśī kvacit ।
pūrvvabījasamopetā sarvvakāmaprasādanī ।।

na tadastīha jagati mudrā yanna prasādayet ।
mahāmudrā bhogavatī bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।

melāpaṃ sādha(ya)tyāśu yadi vācayutā sadā ।
smaśānasthā sa badhnāti devi nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe tomaramudrāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi prāsamudrāṃ mahābalāṃ ।
yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa sādhakaḥ sarvvasiddhibhāk ।।

bhavatyeva na saṃdeho vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
tarjanyāvāmamaṃguṣṭhe kanīyā * sakuṃcite ।।

madhyame prasṛte sliṣṭhe prāsamudrā bhavediyaṃ ।
(8a)
etāṃ badhvā mahāvīro pūrvvavīryāvalaṃbakaḥ ।।

kālānalāyutaprakhyaiḥ sarvvabhūtaiḥ pradṛśyate ।
mahāmohaṃ prayāṃtyete bhūtavetālajātayaḥ ।।

khanyavāde kṣetravāde mantravāde vilātmake ।
oṣadhīrasavā * dau yojyaṃ siddhiphalapradā ।।

sarvvakarmasusaṃsiddhaṃ mudreyaṃ yojayet priye ।
tatkṣaṇātsiddhimāyānti devi nāstyanṛtaṃ vacaḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe prāsamudrā ।। ।।

cakramudrāṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvvamātṛgaṇānvitāṃ ।
yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa sarvvavighnakṣayo bhavet ।।

maṇibaṃdhasusaṃlagnau karau tu viparītagau ।
baddhamuṣṭivimokṣāntaṃ prabhrāmyaṃ prasṛtaṃ punaḥ ।।

punaḥ punaḥ prakṛtyaivaṃ cakramudrā vidhīyate ।
sarvvamudrāprabhedārthaṃ vātāśaninivāraṇe ।।

meghānāṃ nigrahe yojyā mahāpālanasādhane ।
grahānāṃ damanī raudrā sarvvaduḥkṛtanāśanī ।।
mahāmohapraśamanī bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe cakramudrā ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi matsyamudrāṃ mahābalāṃ ।
yayā prīṇāti deveśaḥ sādhakasya mahātmanaḥ ।।

tāṃ śṛṇuṣva mahābhāge mātṛṇāṃ vallabhāparā ।
dakṣiṇaṃ pṛṣṭhato vāme tiryagaṃgulisaṃhate ।।

uttānaviparīte ca ubhayāṃguṣṭhacālanāt ।
matsyamudrā bhavatyeṣā pavitrīkaraṇehitā ।।

pāvanāṃ sarvvabhakṣāṇāṃ pānabhojanakeṣvapi ।
vidhena nātiriktasya dravyalābhaprapūraṇī ।।
(8b)
prīṇanti devatāḥ sarvvā yathāmaṇḍalatarpitāḥ ।।

sarvvasaubhāgyajananī bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā sarvvakarmakarī smṛtā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe matsyamudrā ।। ।।

adhunā kacchapākhyeyaṃ mudrāṃ vakṣyāmi suvrate ।
yāgādi pūraṇīyā sā mahāsiddhikarī nṛṇāṃ ।।
vāmaṃ cādhomukhaṃ kṛtvā tasyopari ca dakṣiṇe ।।

tarjanīnyāsamākṛṣya madhyamādhomukhasya tu ।
jyeṣṭhāgre cāṃguliṃ kuryā śeṣaṃ pādākṛtiryathā ।।

praveśanirgamau kuryā mudreyaṃ kacchapābhidhā ।
ceṭakānāṃ pranāśeyaṃ kiṃkarāṇāṃ tathā priye ।।

priyā ca sarvvadevīnāṃ devadevyāḥ priyā sadā ।
yāgādike ca yaddravyaṃ naraṃ bhavati pārvvati ।।

sarvvaṃ prapūratyeṣā devī nāsyāḥ samā kvacit ।
vidyate varamudreśī haṭhamelāpasiddhidā ।।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā sarvvasaṃpadguṇodayā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kacchapamudrā ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi saṃsārārṇṇavatāraṇī ।
potamudrāṃ mahābhāge sarvvādhārā mahābalā ।।

prasṛtāḥ pāṇitalagāḥ aṃgulyastiryagāḥ śubhāḥ ।
tarjanye cotsṛjecchreṣṭe tadādhoṅguṣṭhake sthite ।।

potamudrāsamākhyātā yayā dṛśyanti devatāḥ ।
nṛtyanti ca gaṇāḥ sarvve pūjitāśca bhavanti ca ।।

sarvvasaubhāgyadā jñeyā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।
(9a)
tāraṇī sarvvadurgebhyaḥ sarvvāpadbhyo vimocanī ।।

mahāpātakadoṣaghnā tava samyak prakīrttitā ।
japasiddhikarī proktā vidhisaṃpūraṇī smṛtā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe pātamudrā ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrā sarvvārthasiddhidā ।
vimānākhyā mahāguhyā ākāśagamane pradā ।।

akāryākāryasaṃsthāne aṃguṣṭhe kuṃcite bhṛśaṃ ।
madhyame corddhaparvveṇa vimānaṃ susamaṃ sadā ।।

ākhyāteyaṃ mahāmudrā ḍākinīnāṃ mahāpriyā ।
mayā sakṛnniyuktāyāṃ dūtyo nṛtyanti harṣitāḥ ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।
harṣā nṛtyanti sā devī sā prītā sādhakeśvarī ।।

bhavatyeva hi deveśi pūjitā tarpitā sadā ।
ākāśasādhane yojyā mahāmelāpake vidhau ।।
iyaṃ sā mokṣaṇī śreṇī tavādyatha kaṭīkṛtā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe vimānamudrā ।। ।।

adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi yayā dṛśyanti devatāḥ ।
netrābhidhānāmudreśī sarvvajñatvaprasādhanī ।।

ubhayorhastayordevī tarjanī prasṛtā yadā ।
śeṣāṃguṣṭhodaragatā tadvatsaṃyojitā kare ।।

netrābhidhānā mudreyaṃ sarvvajñātāvalokadā ।
anayā baddhayā gauri dṛśyate parameśvare ।।

pūrvvavīryaprayogena yadā saptāhamabhyaset ।
tadbhāvanārasāviddhi sarvvajñapadavīṃ labhet ।।
jayadrathe tantravare taveyaṃ saṃprakāśitā ।।
(9b)
।। iti jayadrathe netramudrāvidhi ।।

ākāśagamane gauri mudrā śṛṇu mahābalā ।
patākā nāma yā mudrā sarvvajñatvaprasādhanī ।।

procchṛtāṃ tarjanīṃ kṛtvā muṣṭinā vāmahastakaṃ ।
dakṣiṇaṃ tiryagaṃ hastaṃ viralāṃguli cālitaṃ ।।

iyaṃ sā śubhagā mudrā patākīnāma kīrttitā ।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā smaśāne baṃdhayetsadā ।।

saptāhāddevadeveśi melakaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ ।
khecaro bhavate martyo mahābhogabharāvahaḥ ।।

nānayā sadṛśī mudrā triṣu lokeṣu kīrttitā ।
pūrvvavīryasamopetā rasabhāvānuvedhitā ।।
sarvvakarmakarī jñeyā sādhakasya mahātmanaḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe patākāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrānyā jvālinīti yā ।
yayā saṃjvalayetsarvvaṃ vighnajālamanantakaṃ ।।

pādalepādikāḥ siddhī yā sādhayati līlayā ।
sarvvavighnapraśamanī duṣṭadāridryamarddanī ।।

aṃguṣṭhakārayeddevi ubhayoḥ pṛṣṭhayoḥ priye ।
saṃśleṣite prakarttavye caṃcale kuṃcitāṃritāḥ ।।

kiṃcit prakaṃpitā seyaṃ mudrā sarvvottamā smṛtā ।
jvālinī nāma vikhyātā sarvvasatvavaśaṃkarī ।।

pūrvvabījasamākṣiptā rasabhāvānuveditā ।
sādhayetsarvvamakhilaṃ smaraṇādeva sādhakaḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe jvālinīmudrā ।। ।।

halamudrāṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu śailendranaṃdini ।
mahābhāgyagirau yena āruhetsādhakeśvaraḥ ।।
(10a)
tāṃ pravīryakarī samyacchṛṇu pāpāntakāriṇi ।
tiryakkuṃḍaline haste tasyāṃguṣṭhe ca dakṣiṇe ।।

saṃgṛhītaṃ halaṃ hyeṣa tava devi prakāśitaṃ ।
mahābhāgyodayaṃ caiva sādhakasya nibaṃdhanāt ।।

halamadhyagatāhālī halavāhāhaleśvarī ।
paśyate sādhakendrosau divi saṃbhūtayena hi ।।

tāṃ dṛṣṭvā khecaro jāyetsarvvacakreśvarānvitaḥ ।
sarvvamudrottamā hyeṣā tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe halamudrā ।। ।।

mukulākhyā mahāmudrā śṛṇu vīrendravatsale ।
yayā prabuddhayā devi maṃtrakoṭiśatānyapi ।।

sādhakābhimukhāḥ sarvve bhavantīha surārcite ।
mukulaṃ hastamevādau hṛtpradeśe niyojayet ।।

tiryaksame kurparake kṛtvā sutanu sarvvadā ।
mukulākhyā bhavatyeṣā maṃtrāvāhanasiddhidā ।।

yadā haṃtuṃ samāyātaṃ ṛpuṃ sarvvakaraṃ * * ।
tatkṣaṇādeva deveyaṃ mudrā mohayate ṛpuṃ ।।

pūrvvavīryabalenaiva haṭhamelāpasiddhidā ।
yāṃ yāṃ sādhayate siddhiṃ tāṃ tāṃ sādhayate balāt ।।

eṣā mudrā pradhāneśī bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।
na mayā kasyacit khyātā muktvā tvāṃ tridaśārcite ।।
devī priyā bhāgakarī sarvvamantrama{he}śvarī ।।

।। iti jayadrathe mukulamudrā ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi sarvvā(pya)kakārikā ।
kalaśākhyā mahāmudrā mahāsaubhāgyadāparā ।।

ubhayorhastayoḥ śākhāḥ paryante viniyojitā ।
(10b)
anyatra madhyame hyeṣā sudhāsārarasāplutā ।।

kalaśākhyā smṛtā seyaṃ mudrā sarvvārthasiddhidā ।
chinne traste tathā dagdhe bhinne ruddhe vināsine ।।

mantre āpyāyakī khyātā vaṃdhā devi na saṃśayaḥ ।
anayā devadeveśi ātmānamabhiṣecayet ।।

āpyāyanaṃ bhavettasya samantrasya na saṃśayaḥ ।
anayā baddhamātrāyāḥ prīṇante sarvvadevatāḥ ।।
bhogamokṣapradā jñeyā tava samyakprakāśitā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kalaśamudrā ।। ।।

athānyatsaṃpravakṣyāmi mudrāṃ sarvvārthasādhanīṃ ।
sarvvasānnidhyakaraṇī chatrākhyā bhogamokṣadā ।।

vāmena kārayenmuṣṭiṃ procchritā tarjanī tataḥ ।
tasyāpṛṣṭhe praviralaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ vāpyadhomukhaṃ ।।

kṛtvā chatryabhidhāneyaṃ mudrā sarvvārthasiddhidā ।
pūrvvavīryasamopetā trailokyāntakakāriṇī ।।
mahābhāgyakarī jñeyā sarvvasampadguṇojvalā ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale chatramudrā ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahāyogeśvarīti yā ।
mudrāgaurimahābhāgā sarvvamudrottamottamā ।।

anayā rahitā siddhistantresminnaiva jāyate ।
ākṣipyāpūrvvavīryaṃ ca mahākālikulārṇṇavā ।।

nibodhya paṃcaphalinīṃ mudrābaṃdhaṃ samācaret ।
aṣṭāṃgulī karau kṛtvā niṣkrāntā madhyaparvvagā ।।

aṃguṣṭhau ca sasaṃlagnau kuṃbhodarakṛtau karau ।
(11a)
devyākāraṃ tadā cakraṃ jagadgrāsaikaghasmaraṃ ।।

ārāvamāravadghoraṃ mudrā  * ghoramā priye ।
yogeśvarī tavākhyātā sarvvamudreśvareśvarī ।।

yayā hīnā na siddhyaṃti prayatnenāpi sādhakaḥ ।
sarvvakarmakarī hyeṣā mahāpātakanāśanī ।।

baddhayā tatkṣaṇāddevi priyamelāpakaṃ bhavet ।
yāvanti mantrataṃtrāṇi vidyābījotthitāni ca ।।

tāvanti sarvve vaśyāni bhavantīha na saṃśayaḥ ।
anayā baddhayā samyak jvalate kaṃpate bhṛśaṃ ।।

pūjyate devatāvṛṃdai yathā paramabhairavī ।
paśūnāṃ madhyato naiṣā baṃdhitavyā kadācana ।।

patanti sarvve muhyanti mriyante ca na saṃśayaḥ ।
saṃpūjya parameśānīṃ madirānaṃdanaṃditaḥ ।।

rātrau pitṛvane devi baṃdhitavyā sadaiva hi ।
tadā cotpatate vegāt sādhakaḥ khecaraiḥ saha ।।

bahunātra kimuktena nānayā sadṛśī kvacit ।
vidyāpīṭhe maṃtrapīṭhe mudrāpīṭhesti vā kvacit ।।

।। iti jayadrathe mahāyogeśvarīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

mahāvyomābhidhānā yā purā proktā mayā tava ।
tāṃ pravakṣyāmi suśroṇi śṛṇuṣva gaṃdato mama ।।

yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa khecarī patitāṃ vrajet ।
uttānaviparītāṃ ca kṛtvā madhye hyanāmike ।।

pṛṣṭhasaṃyojite śliṣṭe śeṣe pīṭhākṛtiṃ sthite ।
evamāhṛtyamarutaṃ yāvatsaṃjīvanī kulaṃ ।।
(11b)
punastatpreṣayeddevi yāvaddvā daśamaṃ padaṃ ।
evamutpatate vyomni vyomeśvaryā prasādataḥ ।।

mahāvyomābhidhāneyaṃ tava samyagudāhṛtā ।
ākāśagamane yojyā sarvvākhecarivaṃditā ।।

bhogamokṣapradā jñeyā bahunā kathitena kiṃ ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale mahāvyomamudrā ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrā ghoraparākramā ।
triśūlākhyā mahādevi sarahasyā mahābalā ।।

mattagaṃdhaṃ samāhṛtya yāvacchūlaṃ vinisṛtaṃ ।
trināḍibaddhayogena triśūlaṃ dvādaśāntagaṃ ।।

jvalajjvalanasaṃkāśaṃ sphuliṃgānekasaṃkulaṃ ।
cintayitvā karau samyaganyonyaḥ śleṣayojitaiḥ ।।

viśā * dūrddhagau kāryau kaniṣṭhāṃguṣṭhayojanāt ।
viśṛtaṃ sumahacchūlaṃ sarvvavighnapramarddanaṃ ।
māsādabhyāsayogena khecaro jāyate naraḥ ।।

sarvvasiddhiguṇopetaḥ guṇāṣṭakasamanvitaḥ ।
baṃdhenāsya mahābhāge sākṣāddevo vṛṣadhvajaḥ ।।

dṛśyate sarvvalokeṣu yathāsau parameśvaraḥ ।
anayā baddhayā devi ḍāvyo ḍāmarikāstathā ।।

khecaryo nakracūṣyāśca lāmā bhūcarya eva ca ।
te sthitā gatadarpāśca mohitāstā bhavanti hi ।।

mahocchūṣmādayaḥ sarvvā vaśe tiṣṭhanti tasya tāḥ ।
eṣā sarvvottamā mudrā triśūlākhyā prakīrttitā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe śūlamudrā ।।।।
(12a)
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi sarvvāpyāyakarī śubhā ।
surabhī nāma yā mudrā kāmadhenuriti smṛtā ।।

parasparau samāśliṣṭau karāvaṃtaritāṃgulī ।
dakṣiṇasya karasyaiva madhyā vāmasya tarjanī ।।

vāmamadhyā tathā dakṣā tarjanī cāgragāṃ kuru ।
kanīyasā anāmākhye anāmā ca kanīyase ।।

saṃyojyā grāsamāśliṣṭā aṃguṣṭhau mūlamadhyagau ।
evaṃ baddhvā dvādaśāntā chivacaṃdrasudhārasaṃ ।।

śravantaṃ cintayedvidyāṃ yāvadvṛdgaganāntaraṃ ।
evaṃ mudrāmṛtā nāmnī surabhīti tavoditā ।।

etāṃ baddhvā mahābhāge sarvvadravyāṇi vīkṣayet ।
tāni sarvvasudhāpūraiḥ pūritāni bhavanti hi ।।

akṣayāni anantāni devatānāṃ bhavanti ca ।
akālamṛtyumathanīṃ kaliduḥsvapnanāśinīṃ ।।

ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena sadāśivasamo bhavet ।
bhogamokṣaphalāvāptiḥ sādhakasyopajāyate ।।

।। iti jayadrathe surabhimudrā ।। ।।

adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrā suravarārcite ।
mṛgīnāma mahābhāge sarvvasaṃpatkarī śubhā ।।

aṃguṣṭhāgrasamāyukto madhyanāme maheśvari ।
tarjanī ca kaniṣṭhā ca procchṛtā viralāṃgulī ।।

mṛgīnāma mahāmudrā sarvvakarmasusiddhidā ।
pūrvvavīryasamopetā sarvvasatvavaśaṃkarī ।।

puṣṭisaubhāgyajananī trailokyatrāṇatatparā ।
bhogamokṣaphalāvāpti sādhakānāṃ bhavetpunaḥ ।।
(12b)
।। iti jayadrathe mṛgīmudrā ।।।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mahāpāpapranāśanī ।
viśvayonīti yā mudrā śṛṇu bālamṛgekṣaṇe ।।

agrāṃgulinibaddhena vīṇī viralabaṃdhane ।
vāmasya nirgatā pṛṣṭhe dakṣiṇasyāntare gatā ।।

viśvayoniriyaṃ mudrā duḥprāpyā akṛtātmanāṃ ।
pūrvvavīryasamopetā sarvvayāgapravarttikāḥ ।।

vidhisaṃpūraṇī jñeyā sarvvaduḥkṛtahāriṇī ।
anayā baddhayā devi kāmadevasamo bhavet ।।

kṣubhyanti yoṣitaḥ sarvvā madanānalapīḍitāḥ ।
subhago jāyate siddho yathā kāmeśvaraḥ svayaṃ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe viśvayonimudrā ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi liṃgamudrā mahābalā ।
yasyāstulyā na cānyāsti sarvvasiddhikarī nṛṇāṃ ।।

trailokyaṃ tarpayedyā tu sṛjate saṃharatyapi ।
mahāmudrā pradhāneyaṃ aṇimādyaṣṭasādhanī ।।

sāṃnidhyakaraṇī jñeyā mantracakrasya pārvvati ।
muṣṭyaṃguṣṭhena vāmena dakṣiṇasyāntare gataḥ ।।

grāhayeṅguṣṭhakāṃ vāme dakṣiṇena kareṇa tu ।
śeṣādho pīṭhikā kāryā kārayeta sumadhyame ।।

ekaliṃgābhidhāneyaṃ sarvvamudrottamottamā ।
na cāsya sadṛśīmudrā vidyate bhuvanodare ।।

pūrvvavīryasamopetā sarvvākarṣakarī smṛtā ।
na tadastīha tadvastu anayā yanna siddhyati ।।

।। iti jayadrathe ekaliṃgamudrā ।। ।।
(13a)
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvvamudreśvareśvarī ।
tāṃ pravakṣyāmi suśroṇi śṛṇuṣvekamanādhunā ।।

vāmena * * saṃgṛhya dakṣiṇasya pradeśinī ।
ubhayoṅguṣṭhabaṃdheyaṃ paṃnagīti prakīrttitā ।।

grahāntare niyojyeyaṃ mokṣaṇārthaṃ maheśvari ।
sarvvarakṣākarī śreṣṭhā bhūtāpasmāranāśanī ।।

mahāpātakadarpaghnī sarvvākarṣakarī smṛtā ।
trividhaṃ samayatyeṣā viṣaṃ vai jaṅgamādikaṃ ।।

meghavātāśanīnāṃ ca samanī sā prakīrttitā ।
mattanāgendraśamanī trailokyatrāṇatatparā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe pannagīmudrā ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrāghoraparākramā ।
ulūkākhyā mahāsiddhā siddhayogavivarttitā ।।

tāṃ śṛṇuṣva mahābhāge sarvvamudrāprabodhikā ।
mukulā dakṣiṇe haste kuṃcitāścaturekataḥ ।।

adhoguṣṭhaṃ vinamitaṃ vicacārapradarśanāt ।
koṭare picchate netramukhamatyantabhīṣaṇaṃ ।।

mudrā ulūkinī nāmnā tadākārā pramardanā ।।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā ṣaṇmāsābhyantareṇa ca ।।

utpate gaganābhāgaṃ sarvvajñaścaiva jāyate ।
satataṃ smaraṇāddevi sarvvānkāmānavāpnuyāt ।।

tannāsti yanna siddhyeta asyā mudrāprabhāvataḥ ।
mahāmohapraśamanī aṇimādiprasādhanī ।।

vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣamāraṇoccāṭanādikaṃ ।
(13b)
staṃbhe stobhe tathā dehe viśanirnāśanepi ca ।।

nāvāśakaṭayaṃtrādistambhane śatrunāśane ।
sakṛdvaddhā siddhidā syānmudreyaṃ śatrumardanī ।।

ulūkākhyā tavākhyātā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe ulūkamudrāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi śavamudrā mahābalā ।
yasyāḥ saṃsiddhyate maṃtrī kālaṃ(jinati)līlayā ।।

kṛtvārddhamilite netre vikṛte cānane tathā ।
bāhuyugmaṃ ślathaṃ kāryaṃ grīvā cāpi tathā kṛtā ।।

akāle mṛtyuśamanī śavamudrā iyaṃ parā ।
pūrvvabījasamopetā ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogataḥ ।।

mṛtyujidbhavate mantrī yathā hariharau tathā ।
siddhyaṃti sarvvakarmāṇi asyā baṃdhā na saṃśayaḥ ।।

mahāmelāpakādyāśca siddhya(ntai) nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātā śavamudrā mahottamā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe śavamudrā ।। ।।

athāśvamudrāṃ vakṣyāmi yathāvatkuliśodari ।
yayā saṃbaddhayā maṃtrī bhavenmelāpakādhipaḥ ।।

kṛtvāśvākāramākāraṃ heṣāravaravāriṇāṃ ।
svaraikātkhaṃḍayedbhūmiṃ kṣaṇaṃvatto sadaśvavat ।।

aśvamudrā tviyaṃ devi tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।
(anayā sarvva)sadvṛtti jāyate sādhakātmanaḥ ।।

mahābhāgyasamāpattī vyomavyāpī bhavatyapi ।
śmaśānastho yathābaddho tadā melāpamāpnuyāt ।।

khecarī cakrapū(jyāsau) bhavatyeva hi sādhakaḥ ।
(14a)
bhogamokṣaphalāvāptirjāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।
aśvamudrā samākhyātā tava devi yathārthataḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe aśvamudrāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi trailokyavaśakārikā ।
vārā hyeṣā mahāmudrā śṛṇuṣvekamanādhunā ।।

bāhuṃ saṃkucitaṃ vāmaṃ tasya madhye mukhaṃ kṛtaṃ ।
viśāluṃ dakṣi(ṇa)vādho adhākāraprakāśite ।।

āliḍhavāmanamitau kurparo dhriyate bahu ।
iyaṃ mahāvarāhī syānmaithunādau prayojayet ।।

pūrvvavīryasamopetā * * * * * * * * ।
ṣaṇmāsādyāti gamanaṃ khecaraiḥ paripūjitāḥ ।।
parāparaphalāvāptirjāyate sādhakātmanāṃ ।

।। iti jayadrathe vārāhīmudrā ।। ।।

siṃhamudrāṃ mahāghorā śṛṇu vakṣyāmi bhairavi ।
yayā saṃsiddhayā maṃtrī lokapālapatirbhavet ।।

saṃpūrya vāyunā vaktraṃ koṭare locane kṛte ।
kuṃcite jānunī kṛtvā karaṃ dvaṃdvaṃmadhomukhaṃ ।।

vaktradeśe niyojye ca cālayeta karau dṛśaṃ ।
nṛsiṃhākhyā mahāmudrā jagadvidveṣaṇakṣamā ।।

ataḥ paraṃ ghoratarā mudrenaiveha vidyate ।
pātāla yatra (mathanī) bhūtavetālamarddanī ।।

kṛtyā khārkhodadamanī nāgavidrāvaṇī parā ।
sarvvasaṃpatkarī śreṣṭhā pūrvavīryasamanvitā ।।
bhogamokṣapradā sārā mantrasiddhiphalapradā ।
(14b)
।। iti jayadrathe siṃhamudrā ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ viśālākṣyāṃ mudrāṃ śṛṇu maheśvari ।
yayā vijñātamātrāyā trailokye nāsti sādhitaṃ ।।

lokapālapriyā nityaṃ sarvvamaṃtramaheśvarī ।
divāsamāpravṛttena samyagvaktreṇa līlayā ।।

muktakeśā viśāleti (ci)dvipālā prakīrttitā ।
brahmahatyādikānpāpān praruhāmāvadhānapi ।।

(vyayāyeddhaṃ) samātrā * nmahādevapraharṣaṇī ।
sarvvakriyāvihīnasya grāmadharmaratasya ca ।।

sarvvasiddhipradākhyātā mantrayogapravarttikā ।
triṣu lokeṣu tannāsti yanna(mitya)ti maṃtriṇaḥ ।।

prīṇāti sarvvadevebhyaḥ sarvvayāgaprapūraṇī ।
sarvvamudrāsusāreyaṃ tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe viśālāmudrāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrā sarvvottamottamā ।
siddhimohābhidhāneyaṃ mahāsiddhikarī nṛṇāṃ ।।

viśāle vihidigvāsā devīcakraṃ vicintayet ।
homakarma prakurvvīta mahākṣmājapalādikaiḥ ।।

tadvīryavalitaḥ samyaksiddhirohā prakīrttitā ।
uttamādhamamadhyasthā siddhiyogaprakīrttitā ।।

aṇimādyaṣṭakāṃtāśca siddhyante nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
enāṃ mantrī avijñāya sādhanaṃ yaḥ samīhate ।।

ha(nate) muṣṭinākāśaṃ kṣudhārttaḥ khaṇḍaye tuṣāṃ ।
tṛṣārttā mṛgatṛṣṇāṃbu sevatesau na saṃśayaḥ ।।
trailokyasārametaddhi tava devi prakāśitaṃ ।।
(15a)
।। iti jayadrathe siddhirohā ।। ।।

yogamudrābhidhā mudrā śṛṇu bhairavi tatvataḥ ।
yayā saṃsiddhayā maṃtrī brahmāṇḍādhipatirbhavet ।।

yayā devī viśedehaṃ sādhakendrasya bhairavi ।
tāṃ śṛṇuṣva mahābhāge sarvvakhecarivaṃditā ।।

madhyamā veṣṭitāṃ kṛtvā anāmāṃguṣṭhakena hi ।
prasṛtā madhyamā kṛtvā dvābhyāṃ tiryordhvayojitaṃ ।।

vasti deśevavadhnīyā digvastreṇaiva maṃtriṇā ।
aṣṭāṅgaṃ praṇavaṃ kāryaṃ yogavajraṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।।

nāstyasyāḥ sadṛśīmudrā sarvvakāryārthasiddhaye ।।

sarvvāvarṇyaprasarpyeṇa devyā dehe viśaṃti hi ।
vāksiddhirjāyate devi sarvvatrailokyasiddhidā ।
bhogamokṣapradākhyātā mahāmelāpasiddhidā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe yogamudrā ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi tava devi yathārthataḥ ।
saṃvarākhyā mahāmudrā sarvvasiddhiphalapradā ।।

sarvvagaṃdhaviliptena digvāsenaiva maṃtriṇā ।
muktakeśaśikhenaiva muktesītkarayojanāt ।।

kukuṭāsanasaṃsthena vidyāṣṭaśatamaṃtriṇā ।।
dhyātaṃ padmakaṃ kṛtvā mudrājñeyā tu śaṃvarā ।।

sarvvottamā samākhyātā mahāmelāpasiddhidā ।
anayā māsaṣaṭkena siddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

sarvvasāratamā hyeṣā tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।
pratyahaṃ baṃdhanāddevi devīcakraṃ na saṃśayaḥ ।।

pratyakṣatāṃ prayātyeṣā sādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ ।
(15b)
sarvvākāmānavāpnoti aṇimādyaṣṭakaṃ tathā ।।

।। iti (dra)yadrathe śaṃvaramudrāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣāmi vajrasūcī bhayānakā ।
mahāmāraṇakāryeṣu satataṃ devi yojayet ।।

grathitottānakau kṛtvā viśālau parivarttitau ।
svastikāsanasaṃrūḍhastasyorddhe viniveśayet ।।

eṣā eva samākhyātā vajrasūcī visarjane ।
yogasiddhipradā jñeyā mahāpātakanāśinī ।।

deśātsthāsya mahātejā jāyate * mekaṃ mahat ।
prīṇāti devadeveśī parivārasamanvitā ।।

sarvvakāmapradākhyātā tava devi na saṃśayaḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe vajrasūcīrvidhiḥ ।। ।।

mahāmāyābhidhāneyaṃ śṛṇu mudrā maheśvari ।
yayā saṃsiddhayaṃ samyaktrividhaṃ melakaṃ bhavet ।।

grathitau viparītau ca pṛṣṭhāṃguliviniḥsṛtau ।
parikramaṇayogena digvāsāsu samāhitaḥ ।।

pūrvve dakṣiṇaniḥkrāmaṃ jaṃghābhyāṃ parivarttitaṃ ।
mahāmāyābhidhāneyaṃ mudrā sarvvottamottamā ।।

mahāsaṃvarasaṃsiddhirjāyate sādhakātmanāṃ ।
saubhāgyakaraṇī śreṣṭhā sarvvā padanivāraṇī ।।

bhāgyārohaṇasaṃsiddhau duṣṭasatvavimarddane ।
uccāṭanādikāryeṣu nānayā sadṛśā kvacit ।।

haraveśmagatasyāsya bhavenmelāpamuttamaṃ ।
trividhā jāyate siddhirnityameva susevanāt ।।

na tadastīha talloka anayā yaṃna siddhyati ।।
(16a)
।। iti jayadrathe māyāmudrā ।। ।।

atha śāntābhidhāneyaṃ trailokyavaśakārikā ।
tava vakṣyāmi suśroṇi yayā siddhyati sādhakaḥ ।।

svastikāsanamārūḍho baṃdhamudrāṃ mahābalāṃ ।
prasṛtaṃ nāti deśe ca vāmaṃ tasyopariryadā ।।

prasṛtāṃguṣṭhake kṛtvā mudrā sarvvottamottamā ।
rājyaprasādhanī nityaṃ putradā bhāgyavarddhanī ।।

sumaṅgalā smṛtā tantre citrākārapravarttikā ।
mahāśāntipradā nityaṃ sarvvasiddhiphalapradā ।।

manorame sucau deśe sudhūpāmodamaṃthare ।
abhyaseta imāṃ mudrāṃ sarvvopadravanāśanīṃ ।।

ṣaṇmāsāddevadeveśi khecaro jāyate naraḥ ।
aṇimādiguṇairyukto harttā karttā yatheśvaraḥ ।।

pūrvvabījasamāyukto bhavatyeva narottamaḥ ।।
sarvvamudrā pradhāneyaṃ tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe śāntividhiḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi karttarī nāma nāmataḥ ।
yayā saṃbaddhayā maṃtrī sarvvakāmakṛto bhavet ।।

viralāṃgulike haste cakravatparitasthite ।
eṣā vimardane devi karttarīti prakīrttitā ।।

bhūtavetāladamanī kṛtyā khārkhodamardanī ।
nigrahe sadṛśā nāsyā vidyate parameśvarī ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā kṛtyā vetālasādhane ।
cakṣuvyomāhane caiva mahāmāṃsādivikraye ।।

indrajālavidhau caiva trailokyadalane tathā ।
(16b)
sarvvakarmasuyojyā vai nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kartarīmudrā ।। ।।

pramāṇamudrāṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu vīrendravaṃdite ।
yayā devāvaśaṃ yānti varadāśca bhavanti ca ।।

uttānagrathitau hastau veṇibaṃdhākṛtī yathā ।
tarjanyāṃguṣṭhamadhye tu nisṛtā aṃguṇīmukhā ।।

praṇaveti iyaṃ mudrā devākarṣaṇakārikā ।
mantrayaṃtrādihastānāṃ dravyādiṣu ca kā kathā ।।

sarvvasaubhāgyadā nṛṇāṃ pūrvvavīryabalena tu ।
bhogamokṣaphalāvāptyai taveyaṃ saṃprakāśitā ।।

।। iya jayadrathe pramāṇamudrā ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ prabhā nāma mudrāṃ śṛṇu nagātmaje ।
sarvvakarmaṇi deveśi yathā pūrṇṇāni saṃhati ।।

viparitocchṛte madhye tarjanīti kanī * ke ।
pramarthite tiryagate madhyepyucchritayojite ।।

āmūlākrāntamaṃgulyo jyeṣṭhā saṃjñeti viśrutā ।
eṣā mahāprabhā nāma sarvvadevaprapūjitā ।।

homakāletha naivedye badhnīyātkāryasiddhaye ।
nāsyāḥ paratarā caiva mudrā tantreṣu vidyate ।।

sarvvasaubhāgyadā jñeyā nāmasaṃkīrttanādapi ।।

।। iti jayadrathe mahāprabhā ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi trailokyakṣobhakārikā ।
nāmnā kāmaruhāmu(drā) sarvvasaubhāgyadāparā ।।

harṣitānaṃ tataḥ kuryāddhastisīrya ca vādayet ।
lalite locane kārye īṣatprahasanaṃ kuru ।।

eṣā kāmaruhānāmnī tava mudrā prakāśitā ।
(17a)
sarvvapāpapraśamanī bahuputratvakārikā ।।

mahāsaubhāgyajananī pūrvvavīryasamanvitā ।
gatvā harālayaṃ devi madirānaṃdanaṃditaḥ ।।

sudhūpitaḥ prasannāsyo mudrābaṃdhaṃ samācaret ।
narttanaṃ kārayedvarṣādvastiśīrṣañca vādayet ।।

kroṃkitaṃ recitaṃ kāryaṃ mudrā kāmaruhā smṛtā ।
mahāsaubhāgyadā seyaṃ trailokyavaśakārikā ।।

sarvvapāpapraśamanī bahuputrapradā sadā ।
mahāmelāpasaṃsiddhiṃ janayatyeva nāmataḥ ।।
eṣā sarvvottamā khyātā tava devi yathārthataḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kāmaruhāmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mudrārājñī vaśaṃkarī ।
yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa trailokyaṃ dāsatāmiyāt ।।

ācare narttanaṃ hṛdyaṃ digdhā sā muktacūlikaḥ ।
sasītkāraṃ mahācaṃcu jātvādiparicuṃbinaṃ ।।

bhuvanaṃ pāṇipādādau salīlasamasarpaṇaṃ ।
vaśaṃkarīti nāmeśā mudrā sarvvārthasādhanī ।।

siddhasaṃrohaṇī (muktā) mahālokavaśaṃkarī ।
sādhanī kiṃkarāṇāṃ tu siddhadravyaprasādhanī ।।

śāntikī pauṣṭikī caiva nānyasyā sadṛśī kvacit ।
mahāmelāpasāmagrī sādhayatyanivāritaṃ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe vaśaṃkarīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahāsama * saṃjñitā ।
sādhanī samayānāṃ ca duḥkṛtaughanikṛṃtanī ।।
(17b)
mahāsamayadoṣaghnī śṛṇu pārvvati tatvataḥ ।
piṃḍināṃgulivāmena kanīnīprasṛtātha ca ।।

saṃdhāraye nābhigatāṃ mudrā sarvvottamottamā ।
aviditvā yadā hyeṣā yāgaṃ vā japameva vā ।।

ārabheta yadā devi tadaiṣā sādhanī smṛtā ।
anyathā khecaragaṇairbhakṣatesau na saṃśayaḥ ।।

praviṣṭaṃ putrakaṃ caiva samayena parīkṣayet ।
sarvvasiddhipradā seyaṃ japādiparipūraṇī ।।

।। iti jayadrathe samayamudrā ।। ।।

athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrā nāmnā prabhakṣaṇī ।
yayā saṃsiddhayā devi sādhakāṃścarubhugbhavet ।।

viparītāṃguli(badhvā) parivartteta pṛṣṭhataḥ ।
tasmiṃcchiraḥ samādhāya praṇāmeti vimarṣaṇaṃ ।।

patākākārasaṃsthāne vivṛtāsyā yadā bhavet ।
prabhakṣaṇī tadā jñeyā anayā prāśanaṃ bhavet ।।

siddhyaṃti sarvvakāryāṇi yathā manasi rocate ।
sarvvākarṣādisaṃsiddhau siddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

nāstyasyā sadṛśī devī cāmukhī siddhidāyikā ।
mahāmevāpakaraṇe proktā sarvvatra bhairavī ।।

trailokyasādhanī śreṣṭhā pūrvvavīryabalānvitā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe prabhakṣaṇīmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

mahātārkṣīṃ pravakṣyāmi mudrā vaivasvataḥ paraṃ ।
yayā prabaddhayā vīraḥ sādhako garuḍāyate ।।

vāmaṃ cādhomukhaṃ kṛtvā viralāṃgulikuṃcitaṃ ।
dakṣiṇoparisaṃdhyāt madhyamāyā ca madhyamā ।।
(18a)
ābaddhatarjanī pṛṣṭhe tiryaksaṃkucitākhilā ।
tasyopari yadā * ṣṭhaṃ mahātārkṣīti viśrutā ।।

saṃgrāme śatrumadhyasthā rājadvāre bhayeṣu ca ।
gareṣvādhva ca sarvveṣu niṣedhaṃ ca vidhe tathā ।।

nivārayatirikṣī * suvele makarālaye ।
ākāśagamane cāsya na samā vidyate bhuvi ।।

sarvvasaṃpatkarā śreṣṭhā trailokyoddharaṇakṣamā ।
pūrvvavīryasamopetā mahāmelāpasiddhidā ।।

sarvvottamā samākhyātā tantrarāje jayadrathe ।
pātālasiddhidā jñeyā duṣṭasatvajighāṃsinī ।

।। iti jayadrathe mahātārkṣīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi vikaṭā ghoravikramā ।
yayā prabuddhayā devī brahmāṇḍamapi kaṃpate ।।

yasyā nāmagrahāddevi khecaryotāyameti ca ।
tāṃ śṛṇuṣva samāsena sarvvasaṃpadguṇāvahā ।।

vitataṃ cānanaṃ kṛtvā jihvāṃ niṣkrāmayedvudhaḥ ।
ati śuṣkodaraṃ kṛtvā stanacūcupralaṃbite ।।

(ume) mukulite haste nagnadehena maṃtriṇā ।
vikaṭā ghoramudreyaṃ sarvvākarṣavidhau matā ।।

saṃhārikā prayogena nāstyasyā sadṛśī kvacit ।
sarvvān kāmānavāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyukto gatvāpi vanaṃta * ḥ ।
madirānaṃdacaitanyastanūbaṃdhaṃ samācaret ।।

yāvat praharamātraṃ tu tāvadāyāṃ ca devatāḥ ।
tān praṇamya naraśreṣṭha praṇamaivāntarātmanā ।।
(18b)
hṛṣṭatuṣṭāḥ pranṛtyaṃti tāsāmarghaṃ pradāpayet ।
datterghe bhairavaḥ sākṣādbhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ ।
eṣā mudrottamā khyātā trailokye durlabhā nṛṇāṃ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe vikaṭavidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi punareva tanūdari ।
vikaṭābaṃdhamatyugraṃ ghoraṃ sarvvabhayānakaṃ ।।

tarjanīṃ guhyavāmasya ubhau piṃḍīkṛtau sthitau ।
cibuke yojayeddhīmān jihvāniṣkrāmaṇaṃ tathā ।।

viśāle tu kṛte * tre uttaroṣṭhaṃ lihedvudhaḥ ।
vikaṭā nāma mudreṣā sarvvavighnakṣayaṃkarī ।।

balivalyopahārādi na hiṃsati kadācana ।
sakṛnmelāpasiddhiḥ syātsādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ ।।

pūrvvavīryaguṇopetā sarvvasaṃpadguṇāvahā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe vikaṭāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi parivarttanikādhunā ।
sarvvamudrottamā hyeṣā mahāśāntisukhapradā ।।

madhyame ubhayoreva mahṛhīnau parasparaṃ ।
koṭimārgaṃ ca piṇḍastha niveśya hṛdaye tathā ।।

parivarttaviśālena tripradakṣiṇayogataḥ ।
tatoṣṭāṃgapraṇayanaṃ parivartyati kenya (ya)t ।।

mahāmudrāsamākhyātā pūrvvavīryasamanvitā ।
anayā baddhayā devi balipūjyopahārakāḥ ।।

na hiṃsati kadācidvai yātudhānavināyakaiḥ ।
anyathaiṣāmavijñāya (yaḥ pūjāṃ prakariṣyati) ।।
(suhṛ)nenmuṣṭinākāśaṃ tuṣāṃ kaṃḍayate jaḍaḥ ।।
(19a)
japahomādikāṃ sarvvāṃ hīyate na videdyadi ।
anādirūpā vijñeyā śivaśaktimayā sadā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe parivarttanīmudrā ।।

athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrābhairavasaṃjñitā ।
yathā ṣaṇmāsayogena bhairaveva bhaviṣyati ।।

sarvvasaṃpatsukhādhāraḥ pūrvvavīryāvalaṃbakaḥ ।
madirānaṃdacaitanyo bha(tvā)ddhūritavigrahaḥ ।।

digvāso muktakeśastu pitṛveśmasthitopi vā ।
baṃdhayetparamāṃ mudrāṃ mahābhairavasaṃjñitāṃ ।।

hastābhyāṃ kārayenmuṣṭhī prasṛte ca kaniṣṭhike ।
tābhirvidārayeddevi sṛgvinyastu mahākulaḥ ।।

aṃguṣṭhe prasṛte tadvadbhūkṣepaṃ kārayetsakṛt ।
avaguṃṭhanake vaktraṃ bhairavaṃ vikṛtaṃ kṛtaṃ ।।

jihvāniṣkrāmayetparvvamīṣatsaṃkucitekṣaṇaḥ ।
kāṭare tu punaḥ kārye nāśā sā cipiṭotkaṭā ।।

nṛttākārasthito maṃtrī dakṣe * vyaktajānunī ।
ekāṃguṣṭhasthitaṃ kṛtvā parivartya punastathā ।।

viparyaya tathā vāme mudrābhairavasaṃjñitā ।
anayā baddhayā samyagbrahmāṇḍaṃ kampate bhṛśaṃ ।।

bhayārttā khecaśaraṇaṃ vepate tu diśodaśa ।
kālāgniścalitasthānādbhūyāṅki * māsaḥ ।।

sarvvākarṣaṃ bhavetūrṇṇaṃ yoginīmelakaṃ mahat ।
vināśamayasaṃghena naitāṃ devi pradarśayet ।।

ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena sākṣādbhavati bhairavaḥ ।
asyāstu sadṛśi mudrā trailokye naiva tiṣṭhati ।।
(19b)
।। iti jayadrathe bhairavamudrā ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi nāmnā sā kāmarūpiṇī ।
siddhyate māsaṣaṭkena sarvvaṛddhisamanvitaḥ ।।

sevyate suragaṃdharvvaiḥ siddhavāruṇapuṃgavaiḥ ।
apsarobhirdevakanyaiḥ patitve prārthyate sadā ।।

tāṃ śṛṇuṣva mahābhāge pūrvvavīryāvalaṃbanāt ।
ubhaye(rttādhyā)nāmeva aṃguṣṭhodarasaṃsṛte ।।

sarvvārddhanamite kuryānnityaṃ kuryādvyatikramaḥ ।
ubhayorhastayoreva kaniṣṭhe prasṛtorddhataḥ ।।

viparīte śrite haste tarjanyorddha na saṃyute ।
adharoṣṭhamaviśliṣṭau saumyavaktro tvitena tu ।।

apāvṛtāsyaṃ saṃvarttapunarjihvāṃ vinirgamaṃ ।
punaḥ saumyaṃ kṛtaṃ tasmānmukule ca kare kṛte ।।

maṇibaṃdhapraguṇitadakṣiṇasphijasaṃsthite ।
vāmanābhimatiṃ kuryātparivarttavilomakaṃ ।।

tadvatsāmyādisaṃvarttā mudrāsyā kāmarūpiṇī ।
palālitṛptamanaso sugaṃdhāmodavāsitaḥ ।।

baṃdhena niyataṃ mantrī ṣaṇmāsāplavate nataḥ ।
aṇimādiguṇairyukto bhavatyeva narottamaḥ ।।

satataṃ baṃdhanāddevi sarvvasaubhāgyasaṃyutaḥ ।
bhavatyeva mahābhāge divasaireva pārvvati ।।

।। jayadrathe kāmarūpiṇīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

sūcimudrā pravakṣyāmi sarvvakarmakarīti yā ।
pūrvvavīryasamopetā bhairavatvapradāyikā ।

mukulau tu karau kṛtvā digvāsā kuṭilena tu ।
mahāyāgena medhāvī dakṣiṇe jānukurpare ।।
(20a)
talenaiva viniḥkrānte haste vāmocchrite tathā ।।

jaṃghāsaṃvṛttake tadvatpunarvāmena bodhayet ।
hastena dakṣiṇe tadvattulyakālaṃ prasādhayet ।।

jaṃghātriparivarttena ūcumudrā prakīrttitā ।
anayā baṃdhamānāyā yasyā bhāgyāḥ sasaṃhatāḥ ।।

tasyākāraṃ samācintya bhāgyānākarṣayiṣyati ।
jayākhyā sā trimāsena yadi syādvrahmahā naraḥ ।।

viditosau bhavenmantrī sarvvayogini saṃsadi ।
mantrasiddhidhruvaṃ jāye nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

sarvvasiddhisamāyuktaḥ pūrvvavīryāvalambanāt ।।

।। iti jayadrathe sūcīmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vajrapaṃjarasaṃjñitā ।
mahāpratyaṃgirākāraṃ sarvvāsvāghātacodanā ।।

sarvvarakṣākarī raudrī pūrvvavīryabalena tu ।
mahāmudrākṛtiṃ kṛtvā niḥśeṣeṇa vinirgatā ।।

kanīnyaṃguṣṭhake corddhe maṇibaṃdhe pṛthagvidhe ।
vajrapaṃjarakā nāmnī mudrā hyeṣā mahābalā ।।

japāgnipūjane kāle nityaṃ yuṃjīta sādhakāḥ ।
tadā pūrṇṇaṃ bhavetsarvvaṃ vidhihīnaṃ na jāyate ।।

na chidrayanti vighnāni pūrvvavīryabalena tu ।
eṣā sarvvottamā tubhyaṃ mudrā proktā mayā priye ।।

।। iti jayadrathe vajrapaṃjaravidhiḥ ।। ।।

athānyā saṃpravakṣyāmi śūrpamudrā prakīrttitā ।
yā samastasya jagato vidhipūraṇatatparā ।।

aṃjalyākārasaṃsthāne vilīnāṃguṣṭhake kare ।
(20b)
viśāle suritejve ca sūrpamudrā prakīrttitā ।।

anayā baddhayā devi vidhihīnaṃ na jāyate ।
balivalyupahārādyaṃ sarvvaṃ pūrṇṇaṃ bhaviṣyati ।।

caruṃ nivedayetseyaṃ mudrārājñī praśasyate ।
na parā vidyate cānyā asyā devi mahābalā ।।

।। jayadrathe sūrpamudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

karoṭikīṃ vidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇu pārvvati tatvataḥ ।
sarvvatra pūraṇī yāge pūrvvavīryabalena tu ।।

grathitau viparītau ca hastau viparivarttaye ।
samāvṛttiprayogena tadvadeva prakīrttitau ।।

karoṭīti sucārvvaṅgi sarvvadoṣanivarhaṇī ।
vidhihīnādhikā caiṣā vedhitavyā sadaiva hi ।।

prāṇāpānavicāreṇa kālakūṭasamanvitā ।
samagravīryasaṃrūḍhā prabodhajananīparā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe karoṭīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ śaṃkhamudrā vighnavitrāsanīparā ।
saṃgrāme jayadā devi śṛṇu pārvvati tatvataḥ ।।

antaragrathitāṃgulyo maṇibaṃdhe susaṃsthite ।
aṃguṣṭhe saṃhate kuryātkanīne prasṛte kṛte ।।

śaṃkhamudrā samākhyātā vighnavitrāsanīparā ।
saṃgrāme yojayeddhīmānsarvvatra jayadā smṛtā ।।

dyūtādau jayamāpnoti vicāre nātra kaścana ।
mahāśaṃkhamahāśāntimahābhāgyāvarohaṇaṃ ।।

dadātyeṣā baṃdhayogātṣaṇmāsānmṛtyunāśanī ।
sarvvamudrottamā śreṣṭhā tava devi prakāśitā ।।

।। śaṃkhamudrāvidhiḥ ।।
(21a)
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi sarvvakarmakarī śubhā ।
vicitrā nāma yā mudrā pūrvvavīryabalānvitā ।।

madhyame cocchrite kārye tarjanye cāpyanāmikau ।
parasparaṃ mūlagate kaniṣṭhāṃguṣṭhaucchrite ।।

vicitrā nāma mudreyaṃ sarvvakāmaprasādhanī ।
paradehapraveśārthaṃ baṃdhaye cānayogataḥ ।।

helādolāvihāreṇa priyasaṅgamamelakau ।
niyojayati niyataṃ pūrvvavīryasamanvitā ।।
sarvvasiddhipradā proktā viśeṣo na vidhīyate ।।

।। vicitrāvidhiḥ ।।

surāhā saṃpravakṣyāmi tava pārvvati tatvataḥ ।
cūrṇṇayogāṃjanādīni yayā siddhyanti bhāmini ।।

tadvaccādhomukhe haste veṇibaṃdhāṅgulisthite ।
niḥśeṣaṃ grathitāṃgulyo surāheti prakīrttitā ।।

siddhadravyaṃ prasiddhyarthaṃ cūrṇṇayogeṣu sarvvasu ।
niyojayeta vidhivatsarvvakarmakarī smṛtā ।।

mahāvīryasamopetā tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।
sarahasyā sarvvakarī ataḥ paratarā na hi ।।

।। iti jayadrathe surohā vidhiḥ ।।

śṛṇuṣva kanakākhyā yā mudrā pūrvvaṃ tavoditā ।
sarvvā bhāgyaharīkhyātā sarvvā vaśakarī mahat ।।

karau muktacitau kṛtvā maṇibaṃdhe vilomagau ।
viśeṣavidhiyogena pūrvvavīryasamanvitā ।।

kanaketi samākhyātā sarvvāveśeṣu yojayet ।
svasthāveśanakāryeṣu vetālotthāpanādike ।।
melake sarvvamātṛṇāṃ mudrā seyaṃ prakīrttitā ।।
(21b)
।। iti jayadrathe kanakamudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sadyaḥ prāṇanikṛṃtanī ।
kṣurikātimahāmudrā sarahasyā śṛṇu priye ।।

kaniṣṭhāṃguṣṭhatarjanyo piṃḍīkuryāt prasārite ।
madhyānāme same ṛjvī karasyodho niyojite ।।

dakṣiṇasyeṣa kṣurikā sadyaḥ prāṇanikṛntanī ।
paradehapraveśe ca sadyotkrāṃtividhau tathā ।।
niyojayati mudraiṣā vighnoccāṭanakarmaṇi ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kṣurikāmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi pāśamudrā mahābalā ।
ākarṣayejjagatsarvvaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣaṃ ।।

yasyā baṃdhānmaheśāni sādhakā nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
tarjanyānāma mudrā ca aṃguṣṭhopari saṃsthitā ।।

dakṣiṇena gṛhītavyā gāḍhamasya kanīyasī ।
pāśamudrā samākhyātā sarvvakarmaṇi yojayet ।।

asyāḥ prabhāvato devi saubhāgyamatulaṃ labhet ।
devyāgrato nibadhnīyāt sadaiva puruṣottamaiḥ ।।

prāṇāpānaprayogena sarvvamākarṣayet kṣaṇāt ।।

।। iti jayadrathe pāśamudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ato vicitrāṃ vakṣyāmi mudrāṃ sarvvārthasiddhidāṃ ।
sarvvasaṃpatpradākhyātā bahusiddhiphalapradā ।।

citrānāḍyābhidhānena parikramaṇayogataḥ ।
pallavākārahastena jñeyaṃ saṃgrāmaṇe budhaiḥ ।।

anena mantrasiddhena yadi syādgrahakoṭihā ।
saubhāgyaṃ jāyatetyarthaṃ kṣipraṃ cārthena yujyate ।।
(22a)
di(gvā)sā dhārate yastu sucigaṃdhavilepanaḥ ।
saptāhātsiddhyate maṃtrī sarvvāvastho vyavasthitaḥ ।।

paśyate devatāḥ sarvvā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
pradakṣiṇena hastena nṛttaṃ yaḥ kurute sakṛt ।।

di(gvā)sa sā vicitraṃ ca nṛttaṃ yaḥ kurute grahaḥ ।
sasītkāramukhaṃ rāvaṃ tālusphoṭananṛttanaṃ ।।

vicitreti samākhyātā yadyanṛttaṃ pranṛtyate ।
saptāhābhyantare maṃtrī sarvvāvasthopi siddhyati ।।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā sarvvākarṣakarī smṛtā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe vicitrāmudrāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

potamudrāṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvvākarṣakarī nṛṇāṃ ।
yayā saṃsiddhayā maṃtrī ṣaṇmāsādgagatāṃtaraṃ ।।

plavatesau mahābhāge devi nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।
viralāṃgulimānena īṣannapitamastakā ।।

potamudrā samākhyātā devyā saṃyācane ratā ।
ṣaṇmāsābhyantare mantrī svecchā saṃyācitaṃ labhet ।।

anayā mantrayā maṃtrī hiraṇyaṃ viniveśayet ।
svecchākāmāllabheccāsau nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।
pūrvvavīryasamopetā pātālākāśasiddhidā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe potamudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mudrākāntābhidhānikā ।
yayāpi siddhayā maṃtrī sarvvakarmāruho bhavet ।।

pradakṣiṇena hastena nṛttaṃ yaḥ kurute sakṛt ।
daṇḍakāsanamārūḍho ṛjugrīvaśirasthitaḥ ।।
(22b)
vāyukurparake haste jānukurparayoḥ samaṃ ।
tarjanī prasṛtākāryā muṣṭibaṃdhā * se kṛt ।।

dvitīyaṃ cibukonyastaṃ kuṃcayitvā vidhānataḥ ।
pradeśinī tatoṅguṣṭhe yojayitvā nikuṃcayet ।।

śeṣāḥ prasārayeṅgulya sopānavidhivat kramāt ।
prasannaṃ śāntarūpaṃ ca mukhaṃ kuryādvidhānataḥ ।।

kāṃtā nāma mahāmudrā baddhājñānaṃ pravarttate ।
svecchāsaṃvaraṇaṃ tasya abhyāsāt krīḍanīyake ।।

saumyā devyo bhavaṃtyasya sarvvakāmaiḥ pradṛśyate ।
pūrvvavīryasamopetā sarvvakarmakarī smṛtā ।।

mahāmelāpakaraṇī pitṛveśmani sādhake ।
asyāstu sadṛśī mudrā vidyate bhuvanodare ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kāntāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi nṛttanī nāma yā smṛtā ।
mudrā sarvvārthasampannā sādhakānāṃ mahātmanāṃ ।।

unmattā ca pralāpī syādvālavat krīḍate punaḥ ।
śiṣṭālāpāprabuddhāni mudre(ṣā) samudāhṛtā ।।

tuṣyante devatāstasya deśalābhaśca jāyate ।
vālava(nvartta)naṃ kuryāt phatkāraravayojanaṃ ।।

nṛttanī nāma mudraiṣā sarvvalokavaśaṃkarī ।
mahāmelāpasaṃsiddhau nāstyasyāḥ sadṛśī priye ।।

kimanyadvā samākhyātā vistareṇa sumadhyame ।
na sā siddhirihāstīti yānavā naiva siddhyati ।।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā sarvvākarṣakarīparā ।।
(23a)
।। iti jayadrathe nṛttamudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ kālakarṇyā śṛṇu mudrā mahottamā ।
siddharṣīṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ mahābhāgyāvarohaṇī ।।

ubhayoḥ prasṛtiḥ kuryāttarjanyā tiryage śubhe ।
vilomamaṇibaṃdhena yojyā cānyānusaṃharet ।।

kālakarṇṇīti mudreyaṃ ghorā sarvvottamottamā ।
mahākṛtyāsādhane ca mahāvetālasādhane ।।

yojyā sarvvātmanā devi sādhakena vijānatā ।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā sarvvasampatkarī hi sā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kālakarṇṇīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ viśvamukhī śṛṇu mudrottamottamā ।
yasyā vīryaṃ samāsṛtya lokapālāstu kiṃkarāḥ ।।

bhavatyeva na saṃdeha vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
uttāne madhyame pṛṣṭhe phālite nāmite kṛte ।।

valite tarjanī tiṣṭhe svakarasthe kanīnike ।
pṛṣṭheṅguṣṭhaviniryāte * * * * mukhī smṛtā ।।

anayā baddhamātrāyā lokapālāḥ sakiṃkarāḥ ।
nṛtyaṃti hṛṣṭasaṃtuṣṭā sarvvakāmavarapradā ।।

nāḍīsacāriṇī śreṣṭhā sarvvakāmavarapradā ।।

māsanmātrāmahābhāge rātrau pitṛvane sthitaḥ ।
plavate sāvataṃrikṣaṃ lokapālaiḥ prapūjitaḥ ।
siddhavidyādharairyakṣaiḥ pūjyate sādhakeśvaraḥ ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyuktāṃ sarvvopadravanāśinīṃ ।
mahāmelāpakaraṇīṃ pātālākāśasādhanīṃ ।।
(23b)
।। iti jayadrathe viśvamukhīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

rohiṇī supravakṣyāmi aṣṭapratyayakārikā ।
mahākṣobhapraśamanī bhayadāridraghātanī ।।

mudrā vīryasamopetā śaktiyuktā maheśvarī ।
uttāne grathite kṛtvā anāmevakaniṣṭhike ।।

prasṛte śleṣite kṛtvā tarjanī madhyame śubhe ।
vilīnāṃguṣṭhasaṃśliṣṭe rohiṇīti prakīrttitā ।।

eṣā mudrā mahāmudrā vṛkṣālabhanakarmaṇi ।
anagnijvalane śreṣṭhā stobhāveśāni yojayet ।।

yakṣarākṣasabhūtānāṃ darśanānnāśayiṣyati ।
mahāmudrottamāhyeṣā vijñeyā ṛtumālinī ।।

siddhidā sarvvakāryeṣu pānabhojanapoṣaṇī ।
sarvvakarmakarī hyeṣā devatānāṃ priyaṃkarī ।।

jvalatprāṇaśikhāyogāt pratyayāṣṭau kariṣyati ।

।। iti jayadrathe rohiṇīmudrā ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi kabaṃdhī nāma nāmataḥ ।
sarvvasiddhipra(dāṃ nā)rī pūrvvavīrye samanvitā ।।

prasṛtāṃgulike devi * lomāṃguliveṣṭite ।।
vāmena hastaṃ saṃcā(lpa) kabaṃdhīti prakīrttitā ।।

anayā baṃdhayā maṃtrī devīnāṃ bhavati priyaḥ ।
caurāṭavikaśūleṣu vadhnīyātkāryasiddhaye ।।

ākāśagamane yojyā sarvvasiddhipradāyikā ।।
mahānādakabaṃdhastho helādolavihāravat ।।

karṣayeddevatāḥ sarvvā divasaiḥ saptabhirnnaraḥ ।
sarahasyā tavākhyātā mudreyaṃ vīravaṃdite ।।
(24a)
sarvvāttarmamahā guhyā sakṛnmelāpasiddhidā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kabaṃdhīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

adhunā mokṣaṇī mudrā śṛṇu parvvatanaṃdini ।
yayā pāpaughamatulaṃ neśyate baṃdhamātrataḥ ।।

ubhayorhastayoreva muṣṭiṃ badhvā vimokṣayet ।
saṃyojyate kare kṛtvā mokṣaṇīti prakīrttitā ।।

anayā pāpasaṃghātaṃ brahmahatyāyutānyapi ।
badhvā kṣipraṃ harantīha biṃdunādasamanvitā ।।

calatsastambhayennetre sarvvasiddhiphalapradā ।
mahāvīreśvareśānāṃ vallabhā satataṃ smṛtaṃ ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe mokṣaṇīmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

athānyā saṃpravakṣyāmi durgasantāraṇīti yā ।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā trailokye bhayanāśanī ।।

navābhidhānā deveśi śṛṇu saṃkṣepatodhunā ।
grathitā pṛthusaṃlīnā tiryagyukte kaniṣṭhike ।।

tathaivāṃguṣṭhake nāvāmudrā sarvvārthasādhanī ।
durgasaṃtāraṇī hyeṣā mahāpāpakṣayaṃkarī ।।

apamṛtyuharā hyeṣā sarvvabhāgyāvaro harī ।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā mahāmantrasamanvitā ।।
rakṣayetsaṃkaṭāchīghraṃ sāgarādāpadārṇṇavāt ।।

।। iti jayadrathe nāvāmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ mūrchanākhyā śṛṇu mudrā mṛgekṣaṇe ।
yāṃ badhvā devatāḥ sarvvāḥ kṣipramāyānti saṃnidhiṃ ।।

ubhayorhastayorevaṃ yāgaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi ।
varṣaṃ prapatate tatra surāmadyavimiśritaṃ ।।
(24b)
yena santarpitā devyo bhavantīha mahātape ।
yāvadeṣā prabaṃdhena nāvatṛpyati maṃḍaluṃ ।।
pūrvvavīryabalopetā sādhayenmanasepsitaṃ ।।

।। mūrcchanā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

atha mudrāṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvvaduḥkṛtahāriṇī ।
mahāvīryasamopetā duḥkṛtaughanikṛntanī ।।

prasṛte tu kare kṛtvā pallavākārakalpite ।
tanuṃ tathaiva saṃkalya kaṃpanīti prakīrttitā ।।

anayā duṣkṛtaṃ sarvvaṃ mahāpralayasambhavaṃ ।
prasāmyate na saṃdehaṃ udakenaiva citrarāṭ ।।

prabaddhā yasya vīkṣeta taṃ kuryāttu vipātakaṃ ।
yatra vai maṇḍale badhvā pūrvvavīryabalānvitā ।।
vipāpmānaṃtamevāsu sādhakaḥ prakariṣyati ।

।। iti kaṃpanīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrārājñī hyanuttamā ।
dhruva nāmeti yā proktā susiddhā yoginī kule ।।

hastaiḥ pāṇitalaṃ gṛhya dṛḍhamaṃgulitiryagā ।
piṃḍākāra(ṇavidhinā) dhruvā nāmeti kīrttitā ।।

uccāṭanādisaptānāṃ pratyānayanakārikā ।
śāntikādividhāneṣu staṃbhanādiṣu vinyaset ।।

sarvvārambhādikāryeṣu sarvvaduṣkṛtaśāntaye ।
sarvvasiddhinimitteṣu (mudrā) hyeṣā prakīrttitā ।।

।। dhruvamudrābaṃdhavidhiḥ ।। ।।

visarjanī pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu mudrā mahottamā ।
yayā viṣaṃ vinaśyeta baṃdhamātrā na saṃśayaḥ ।।
(25a)
prasṛtāṃgulyadho hastau aṃgulyānyonyaveṣṭitau ।
parakṣepapracalitau mudrājñeyā visarjanī ।।

visarjane niyoktavyā nirviśīkaraṇe tathā ।
asyāstu sadṛśī(nā)nyā mudrā devi prakīrttitā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe visarjanīmudrā vidhiḥ ।।।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mudrā sarvvottamottamā ।
prakīrṇṇānāṃ mayā pūrvvaṃ vā * le saṃprakāśitā ।।

ubhayorhastayoreva aṃgulībhiḥ samaṃ tataḥ ।
vādayaṃ bhūmibhāgaṃ tu prakīrṇṇeti prakīrttitā ।।

mudrā hyeṣā mahābhāgā pūrvvasaṃpattikārikā ।
aveśe yojayeddhīmān sarvvavyādhinivāraṇe ।।
susobhanā sarvvakarī pūrvvavīryasamanvitā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe prakīrṇṇāmudrāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi śamanī nāma yā priye ।
pūrvvavīryasamopetā duritaughanikṛntanī ।।

parivarttitajaṃghena uttamāṃgapraṇāmite ।
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo maṃtrī śamanīti prakīrttitā ।।

anayā baddhayā maṃtrī mahāyogena siddhyati ।
anugrahaṃ prakurvvanti devi nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe śamanīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

atha vyomavarīmudrā śṛṇutvaṃ vīravaṃdite ।
yayā khegamanaṃjāye ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogataḥ ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyogātsurūpastho hi suvrate ।
maṇibaṃdhayute haste ūrdhvāṅgulisamucchrite ।।

valitāṃgulinikṣipta mudrā(syānnāma)cāriṇī ।
(25b)
ākāśagamane yojyā āvāhanavidhau tathā ।।

nirvviśīkaraṇe śreṣṭhā mahāpāpavināśane ।
mahāmohapraśamane ityupadravanāśanī ।।

mahāmelāpasaṃsiddhau sadṛśānāsya vidyate ।
tannāsti devadeveśi yanna sādhayate balāt ।।
khecarīṇāṃ priyā seyaṃ tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe (vyomavarī)vidhiḥ ।। ।।

athānyā saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrā ghoraparākramā ।
viśvaśūcī tu yā nāmnī sarvvasiddhikarī nṛṇāṃ ।।

vilomāṃguṣṭhavalite kaniṣṭhe kuṃcitakṛtā ।
(saṃṣitā prasṛtā) śleṣā viśvasūcī prakīrttitā ।।

etāṃ baddhā yadā rātrau vicalāpaṃ * svadṛtaḥ ।
bhūtaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ * * ca paśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

bālānāṃ rakṣaṇī hyeṣā baṃdhyā (mūrddhapragṛhyate) ।
dūtīnāṃ ca priyojāye nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

mahāvīryasamopetā mahāmelāpasiddhidā ।
evaṃvidhā nacānyāsti mudreśī kālavedinī ।।

।। iti jayadrathe viśvasūcīmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

atha saptaśikhādevī sa rahasyāṃ śṛṇuṣvame ।
yasyāstulyā na paśyāmi mudrāpīṭhe samastake ।।

yāṃ baddhā kaṃpate sarvvaṃ bra(hmāṇḍaṃ) sacarācaraṃ ।
yasya darśanamātreṇa * madīkṣāphalaṃ bhavet ।।

yāvadvā vīrabhuvane mahāmelāpabhāgbhavet ।
tāṃ śṛṇuṣva samāsena gopayet prāṇavat priye ।।
(26a)
* kuṃcite kanīnye ca pṛṣṭhasaṃśleṣitāṃgulī ।।

ūrddhakeśī suviralā mudrā saptaśikhāsmṛtā ।
praviśya vahnicakraṃ svaṃ saṃkocyodvyātayogataḥ ।।

somāntamārgavāhena saptasaptakayojanāt ।
saptasaptasamārūḍho saptamīta vivarttavat ।।

jñeyā saptaśikhā bhadre pūrvvavīryasamanvitā ।
anayā baṃdhamātrāyā rātrau devyastu paśyate ।।

(prītāstāsaṃ)vibhāgena yuṃjata nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
pātālākāśagamane * praneyuṃ prakīrttitā ।।

ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena sākṣāddevi karaṅkiṇī ।
āgatya nayate devī saṃsthānaṃ sādhakeśvarāt ।।

duḥprāpaṃ sarvvadevīnāṃ yāti nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।
sarvvamudrottamāhyeṣā nāmnā sa(pta)śikhā priye ।।

tavākhyātā yathā siddhā kimanyatkathayāmi te ।
jñātvā guru mukhātsamyaksarvvasiddharhatā bhavet ।।

।। iti jayadrathe saptaśikhā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ cāmurākhyā mudrā śṛṇu mahādhipe ।
yayā śāntisukhaṃ bhūtiḥ prāpnuyādvīrapuṃgavaḥ ।
dakṣiṇabāhuvāmena gāḍhaṃ saṃgṛhya pīḍayet ।।

prasāritāṃgulikaraṃ stathāṃgulivikalpitaṃ ।
cāritaṃ ca sakṛt proktaṃ mudrā cāmarasaṃjñitā ।।

ārādhanārthaṃ devīnāṃ mukhaniṣkaṃṭakārthināṃ ।
badhnīyātkāryasiddhyarthaṃ nirvviṣīkaraṇāya ca ।।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā sarvvakarmakarī nṛṇāṃ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe cāmarāmudrā vidhiḥ ।।
(26b)
vitānamudrāṃ vakṣyāmi mantrasaṃgopanīparā ।
yayā saṃsiddhayā maṃtrī sarvvakarmaprasiddhyati ।।

agrāṃgulya bhaye cāpi vitāmya ca vidhānataḥ ।
bhadravaccaturasreṇa badhnīyātkāryasiddhaye ।।

vitāneyaṃ samākhyātā maṃtrāṇāṃ gopanīparā ।
doṣo na jāyate tatra yatra mudrā prabadhyate ।।

samyagvīryasamāyuktā sarvvakarmakarī nṛṇāṃ ।
śāntikarmaṇya tulyāṃ tu asyā naivāsti suvrate ।।

।। iti jayadrathe vitānamudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ parataraṃ bhadre śṛṇu mudrā manoharā ।
yasyā devyastu muṣyanti pakvanti paramaṃ paraṃ ।।

makaryākārasaṃjñena madhyamāṃgulipīḍite ।
kanīyā nirgate biṃbe mūlāṃguṣṭhapradeśakaḥ ।।

manoharā mahāmudrā yayā cedaṃ pragṛhyate ।
devatādiṣu sarvvāsu kā kathā manujeṣvapi ।।

taṃ nāsti yasya taṃ cittaṃ na harotsādhakottamaḥ ।
vaśīkaroti sakalaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaraṃ ।।

yatra yatra prabaṃdhena deśe grāme ca pattane ।
taṃ taṃ vai kṣobhayet sarvvaṃ sakṛdvīryāvalokanāt ।।
eṣā sarvvottamā mudrā tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe manoharā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi suśāntā vidhimuttamaṃ ।
yena siddhiṃ samāyāti sarvvasiddhisamanvitāṃ ।।

sādhakā devadeveśi baṃdhamātrā na saṃśayaḥ ।
ṛjugrīvaśirovakṣasvastikāsanasaṃsthitaḥ ।
(27a)
muṣṭiṃ kṛtvobhayoreva aṃguṣṭhe pronnate tathā ।।

vāme nābhipradeśe tu aṃguṣṭhe yugadakṣiṇe ।
su(śā)ntā nāma mudreṣā aṣṭasiddhyanuyogataḥ ।।

baddhyate ca dhruvaṃ maṃtrī manasepsitakarmaṇi ।
na sā ṛddhi ṛhāstīha yā na siddhyati sādhake ।।

pūrvvavīryasamopetā ṣaṇmāsādyogabhogadā ।
nānayā sadṛśī mudrā triṣulokeṣu vidyate ।।

।। iti jayadrathe suśāntāmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

bhogahastābhidhānākhyā śṛṇu mudrā maheśvari ।
yayā paṃcakuleśāno bhavatyeva hi sādhakaḥ ।।

svastikādyāsanasthena ṛjugrīvaśirodare ।
karau bhūmau vidhātavyo nayane na ca mīlayet ।।

bhogahastā samākhyātā sābhimukhyā parāparā ।
śīghrameneneyagena maṃtrī siddhyati nānyathā ।।

kuṃḍalī - dasaṃsthānādbhogyabhogeśvarīṃ kalāṃ ।
vedhapātādi kurute mudrābaṃdhe na saṃśayaḥ ।।

ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena sākṣādbhairavavadbhavet ।
eṣā sarvvottamā mudrā pūrvvavīryyasamanvitā ।।
tavākhyātā yathārthena siddhimokṣaphalapradā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe bhogahastā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrā kroṣṭrakasaṃjñitā ।।
yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa mahāmelāpabhāgbhavet ।।

viśālanayanaṃ kṛtvā vāmahaste mukhaṃ kṛtaṃ ।
tasyārdho dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ kiṃcidvāmaṃ prakuṃcitaṃ ।।

kroṣṭrakīti samākhyātā mudrāmelāpasādhanī ।
(27b)
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā baṃdhetpitṛvane sadā ।।

ekaviṃśatimātreṇa mahāmelāpakaṃ bhavet ।
carukaṃ janmasaptotthaṃ yaṃ prāpya khecaro bhavet ।।
tava eṣā samākhyātā gopitā sarvvakāmadā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe koṣṭrakīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

śukābhidhānā vakṣyāmi śṛṇu mudrā sulocane ।
yayā corddharitā jāye sādhakasya mahātmanaḥ ।।

aṃgulyā saṃniyojyeta madhyamā yā svayaṃ budhaḥ ।
aṃguṣṭhamukhasaṃlagnā mukheṣu ca varānane ।।

namrākārā tarjanī syādekadehanavaiśukī ।
sarvvasampatkarākhyātā mahotsāha balapradā ।।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktā sarvvakarmasu yojayet ।

।। iti jayadrathe śukīmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

adhunā karma vakṣyāmi mudrā trailokyakarṣaṇī ।
yayā saṃkarṣayenmantrī devatā cakramuttamaṃ ।।

mahāmudrā kṛtau badhvā tarjanyobhau nikuṃcayet ।
prāṇāpānasamāyuktā iḍāyogagatā satī ।।

ākarṣaṇī samākhyātā devī sānnidhyakārikā ।
sarvvamākarṣati hyeṣā mudrāyāgavidhau budhaḥ ।।

tuṣyanti devatāḥ sarvvā baddhayā varavarṇṇini ।
māsatrayeṇa śubhage ni(tyābhyā)sarato yadā ।।

tadā karṣayate sarvvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaraṃ ।
tena sākarṣaṇī nāma mudrā hyeṣā prakīrttitā ।।
sa rahasyā mahābhāge pūrvvavīryasamanvitā ।।

।। iti (jaya)drathe karṣaṇīmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।
(28a)
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrā sarvvottamottamā ।
yāvadvā yāgasambhāraṃ paripūrṇṇaṃ bhaviṣyati ।।

uttānaṃ kāraye(ddha)staṃ parāvartyāparaṃ budhaḥ ।
viśatyeti samākhyātā yāgakarmaṇi siddhidā ।।

bhāvināṃ tu purāpuṣṭi mudrā yā baṃdhamāśrayā ।
sarvvaṃ viśalyaṃ bhavati viśalyā tena kīrttitā ।।

yadā pūrṇṇaṃ na vai yāgaṃ tadā baṃdhetsadaiva hi ।
tadā pūrṇṇaṃ bhavetsarvvaṃ vilopo naiva jāyate ।।
pūrvvavīryasusaṃyuktā sarvvakarmakarī smṛtā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe viśalyāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

atha bhāgyasamāpannā śṛṇu mudrā mahottamā ।
nāmnā bhāṃśumatī guhyā sarvvasaubhāgyavarddhanī ।।

karau mukulitau kṛtvā mukhasthāne nidhāpayet ।
raśmirūpāstathāṃgulyāḥ kārayeta vicakṣaṇaḥ ।।

aṃsumanyetha vikhyātā bhāgye rohaṇakarmaṇe ।
anayā hyalpabhāgyānāṃ bhāgyānyeveha vṛddhidā ।।

pūrvvavīryasamopetā sarvvakarmaphalapradā ।
nānayā sadṛśī mudrā triṣu lokeṣu vidyate ।।

।। aṃśumatīvidhiḥ ।। ।।

adhunā viśvayonīti tava vakṣyāmi bhairavi ।
yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa trailokyaṃ varttategrataḥ ।।

vaśīkaroti bhuvanaṃ pūrvvavīryāvalokanāt ।
vāmahastaṃ nikuṃcitvā anāmā madhyameśu ca ।।

madhyame nikṣipeṅguṣṭhe viśvayonīparā smṛtā ।
(28b)
baddhayā hyanayā devi devatāsaṃnidhiḥ parā ।।

śakticakrasya sāmānyā sarvvasya bhavati priye ।
prīyanti devatāḥ sarvvā baddhamātrānna saṃśayaḥ ।।

vāme ca dakṣiṇe taṃtre hyatimārgakulākule ।
sāmānyā kīrttitā hyeṣā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।

pūrvvavīryasamopetā mahāmelāpasiddhidā ।
vaśyārthe viniyoktavyā pūjārthe ca viśeṣataḥ ।।

asya mudrā gaṇasyeha mantracakraṃ niśāmayet ।
ye yuktā siddhimāyānti sādhakānāṃ surottame ।।

prastārya mātṛkāvarṇṇaṃ paṃcaviṃśati lopayet ।
trayastriṃśati bījāni tristhāne vinipātayet ।।

adhastādbhedayeddevi dhāmnā sarvve vane punaḥ ।
pāśena yojayitvā tu vi * nā ekato bhavet ।।

rudraśaktyā parākuryā ttrayastriṃśadvarānane ।
ādiśaktyā trayastriṃśadyojayitvā tu pārvvati ।।

mantrā hyāsāṃ samākhyātā navānāmadhunā śṛṇu ।
śaktostrayoviṃśamaṃ * vajradehoparisthitaṃ ।।

guhyaśaktirvvinirbhinnaṃ biṃdunā śirasānvitaṃ ।
viśvayonyādyayaṃ mantraṃ mantrarājaikaliṃgayā ।।

māyā varṇṇaṃ bhaccheṣaṃ * śatādupari pārvvati ।
baṃdhate yāgakāle tu mantra(śa)ktāti yojayet ।।

siddhidā ca bhavatyevaṃ badhvā vai sādhakātmanāṃ ।
deśikānāṃ ca deveśi mahāmelāpasiddhidā ।।

śāntaraudrādinavadhā rasabhāvānurūpataḥ ।
pratyekā navadhā jñeyā ekaikaṃ paṃcadhā punaḥ ।।
(29a)
mudrāvīryasvarūpeṇa bhedairbhinnaṃ tu śā * kaṃ ।
śataṃ yaduktaṃ tu mayā parisaṃkhyā nigadyate ।।

sahasrāṇāṃ catuṣkaṃ syācchatānyaṣṭau ca sa(ṣṭhi)kā ।
saṃkṣepādidamākhyātāṃ anantaṃ vistarāt priye ।।

mudrāpīṭhe purā proktā meta(dvi)starato mayā ।
ataḥ paraṃ pravaktavyā yāstāsāṃ bhedanā na hi ।।

।। iti jayadrathe viśvayonīmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

।। sarvvāsāṃ mantroddhāraḥ ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sarahasyaṃ mahādbhutaṃ ।
yanna kasyacidākhyātaṃ tadadya kathayāmyahaṃ ।।

yena samyagadhīte nu bhairaveva bhavennaraḥ ।
vedhayetsarvvamakhilaṃ jagadetaccarācaraṃ ।।

tavādau daṃṣṭriṇī śreṣṭhā tāṃ śṛṇuṣva nagātmaje ।
madirānaṃdacaitanyaḥ sadhūpāmodamohitaḥ ।।

supuṣpitasutāṃbūlaścaṃdanāgurucarcitaḥ ।
devīrūpaṃ samālaṃbya pūrvvavīryasamanvitaḥ ।।

sahasotthāya deveśi śvāsanāsphoṭayogataḥ ।
vāmajānu prasāryeta madhyameva vikuṃcayet ।।

koṭare locane kṛtvā bhruvoryugmaṃ pracārayet ।
sarpavadvalitāḥ sarvvā hyaṃgulyo karayobhayoḥ ।।

pradeśinye sṛkkiṇībhyāṃ dāritāsyo vinikṣipet ।
jihvā saṃcālayedvegā svāhākāraṃ samu * dan ।।

aṭṭahāsaṃ nadedghoraṃ aṣṭadhā bhairavātmakaṃ ।
eṣā sā daṃṣṭiṇī mudrā raśmicakrodayātmikā ।।

sarahasyā tavākhyātā nākhyeyā yasya kasyacit ।
(29b)
anayā baddhayā gauri kālāgnyādi śivāntakaṃ ।।

kaṃpate viśvamakhilaṃ darśanātsādhakasya hi ।
yathā śrīdaṃṣṭriṇī devī yathā devā karaṃkiṇī ।।

tathā sādṛśyate sarvvaiḥ sadevāsuramānuṣaiḥ ।
saptāhā dṛṣṭiratulo sādhakāya pravarttate ।।

devyākāro dvisaptāhā ttrisaptāhātsamutpatet ।
mahāmelāpasiddhiḥ syādduḥprāpyamakṛtātmanāṃ ।।

sarvvataṃtreṣu deveśi nedṛśī kvaciducyate ।
iyaṃ prāpya na śocanti sādhakendraḥ kadācanaḥ ।।

tadā hyapaścimaṃ janma jñātavyaṃ vīrapuṃgavaiḥ ।
mantrayantraparijñeyaṃ sahasrākṣaramālitāḥ ।।

athavā devadeveśyā hṛdayānyeva yāni ca ।
uktāni vakṣyamāṇāni tairyuktā siddhyate dhruvaṃ ।।
sarvvakarmakarī jñeyā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।
।। iti jayadrathe daṃṣṭriṇībaṃdhaḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi ghorā mudrā bhayānakī ।
lelihānābhidhāneti sarvvadā vedyakārikā ।।

yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa bhavedromodgamaṃ sphuṭaṃ ।
kaṃpate piṃḍamatulaṃ śivā rāvāḥ kariṣyati ।।

mahāhāsyaṃ ca geyaṃ ca narttanaṃ valganaṃ tathā ।
sarvvacakravikāśaṃ ca jāyate baṃdhamātrataḥ ।।

tacchṛṇuṣva samāsena sādhakānāṃ hitāya vai ।
utthitaścāsanāttūrṇṇaṃ haṭhādgātraṃ viśoṣayet ।।

kanīnike locane ca kruddhadṛṣṭividhūnayet ।
cālayellehayejjihvā oṣṭhau saṃcūṣayedbhṛśaṃ ।।
(30a)
muṣṭibaṃdhaiḥ karairdevi karasthe kucite kṛte ।
nābhisṛkkiṇisaṃkṛṣṭiṃ kuryātsādhakasattamaḥ ।।

vivecayenmahājvālā pūrvvavīryavalolvaṇām ।
pratyālīḍhasthānakaṃ syāllirovyādhunvataṃ sakṛt ।।

aṭṭahāsaṃ nadedvyāraṃ mudreyaṃ lelihānikā ।
etāṃ badhvā mahāvīro kṣobhayedbhuvanābaliṃ ।।

mārayedbhūtayakṣaughāṃ vilayantrāṃ prabhaṃjayet ।
pātayedyoginīvṛṃdaṃ vimānaṃ siddhasantatiṃ ।।

kālāgnirbhayabhītastu trāhi trāhiti jalpataḥ ।
sadāśivādayo devāḥ śāntiṅkurvvanti bhītitaḥ ।।

arddharātre smaśānastho mahāvīrakulopari ।
yadā prabaṃdhayenmudrāṃ madirānaṃdanaṃditaḥ ।।

tadā āyānti yoginyastricakrasthā mahābalāḥ ।
tābhiḥ sārddhaṃ plāvayedvyoma mahābhairavarūpadhṛk ।।

yadyatkarmasannicchettatsādhayate * balāt ।
īdṛśā nāsti mudreśī jagatyasmiṃścarācare ।।

mudrāpīṭhe devadevī pradhānā parikīrttitā ।।
yāvanti devadeveśyā vidhānāni bhavanti hi ।।

anayā rahitāḥ sarvvā na siddhiphaladāstu te ।
tasmātsādhāraṇā proktā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।

।। iti jayadrathe lelihānāmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mudrā sarvvottamottamā ।
phetkāriṇīti yā proktā sarvvasiddhiphalodayā ।।

palālitṛptapūrṇṇātmā śmaśānastho dṛḍhavrataḥ ।
(30b)
vedimadhyaṃ samāśritya mudrā rājñī prabaṃdhayet ।।

atha varddhitasaṃdhāne śūnyaveśmethavā naraḥ ।
anyathā naiva baṃdheta baṃdhāddoṣaṃ prajāyate ।।

upaviśya gude kṣipya vāmapādamataṃdritaḥ ।
prasāraye dakṣiṇaṃ ca īṣatsaṃkucitaṃ priye ।।

dviguṇīkṛtya bāhū dvau karṇṇamūle sumuṣṭike ।
ūrddhavaktrā nadedraudraṃ kroṣṭrārāvaṃ (su)dāruṇaṃ ।।

kruddhanetraḥ kruddhamukhaḥ krūrāṃ rāvāssamunnadet ।
dṛkchire madirāmasyā madhyaṃ cāmṛtamuttamaṃ ।।

evaṃ vipuladhunvasva phetkāriṇi samuccaret ।
evaṃ melāpamāpnoti ghaṭikā ca dvayāntare ।।

kaṃpate yoginīcakraṃ bhītabhītaṃ suvihvalaṃ ।
vaśagāstasya tiṣṭhanti yathā bhairavanāyakaḥ ।।

eṣā tava samākhyātā sarvvakarmakarī nṛṇāṃ ।
phetkārī nāma mudreśī jagatyasmin sudurlabhā ।।

nānāmudrāsu deveśi baṃdhanātsaṃprakītitā ।
yadā proktaḥ sādhakendrairgurorvvaktrānmahottamā ।।

apaścimaṃ tadā janme jñātavyaṃ vīrapuṃgavaiḥ ।
yadā karṇṇapathaṃ yātā tadā yukto na saṃśayaḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe phetkārīmudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mudrā nāmnā karaṃkiṇī ।
durlabhā bhuvaneśānāṃ baṃdhamātrātsusiddhidā ।।

sarvvamudrā pradhāneyaṃ trailokyaṃ bhayakārikā ।
asyāstulyā na paśyāmi mudrānyā bhuvanodare ।।
(31a)
tāṃ śṛṇuṣva mahābhāge siddhimokṣaphalapradā ।
gatvā harāspadaṃ ghoraṃ madirānaṃdanaṃditaḥ ।।

mahāvīravratadharo mahākaṃkālaśobhitaḥ ।
citibhasmoddhulitātmā śavayajñopavītikaḥ ।।

baṃdhenmudrāṃ ghoratarāṃ karaṇagrāsatatparāṃ ।
ālīḍhagatisaṃrūḍhaḥ pratyālīḍhā punaḥ sthitaḥ ।।

śoṣayeta balāṅgātraṃ bāhū arddhanikuṃcitau ।
sa vismayagatau hastau nayane koṭare punaḥ ।।

bhrāmayeta sakṛdvaktraṃ nadernādaṃ subhairavaṃ ।
vikāśayedgraṃthikulaṃ kramavyutkramayogataḥ ।।

mahādhāmakulo rūḍho jagadetadgrasanniva ।
vametyeve llihe bhavedgrasetsarvvamalaṃ priye ।।

pūrvvavīryasamārūḍho baṃdhenmudrāṃ mahāprabhāṃ ।
anayā baddhayā devi trailokyaṃ * bhasmādvrajet ।।

grasetkālāgnilakṣāṇi pātayetsūryasantatiṃ ।
māyāṃ utkṣobhayetsarvvaṃ baṃdhamātrā na saṃśayaḥ ।।

praharārddhā plavedvyogni yoginīcakravaṃditaḥ ।
sa ca bhairavanāthosau bhavatyeva na cādbhutaṃ ।।

nānayā sadṛśī mudrā triṣu lokeṣu vidyate ।
sarvvakarmakarī jñeyā sarvvasaṃhārakārikā ।।

mahākṣobhakarī jñeyā na baṃdhetsamayaṃ vinā ।
sarahasyā tavākhyātā gopanīyā prayatnataḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathe karaṃkiṇīmudrābaṃdhaḥ ।। ।।

adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi sarvvakarmakarī śivā ।
khecarīnāma yā mudrā sarvvataṃtreṣu gopitā ।।
(31b)
yayā khecaratāṃ yāti sādhakaṃ vīravaṃdite ।
guruvaktrasthitā nityaṃ yoginī hṛdaye sthitā ।।

yamāpyeva na śocanti sādhakāḥ siddhikāṃkṣiṇaḥ ।
yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa khecaraiḥ sahamodate ।।

yasyāstulyā na paśyāmi mudrāpīṭhe suvistare ।
tāṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ mahābhāge sarvvajyeṣṭhā jagaddhitā ।।

kṛtvā manorame deśe puṣpaprakarasaṃkule ।
paṃcāmṛtaviliptaṃ tu sudhūpāmodavāsitaṃ ।।

madirānaṃdacaitanyaṃ kṛtapūjāvidhikramaḥ ।
svadehamarccayeddevī yathā devī karaṃkiṇī ।।

paścātsvarūparūpastho cittabhāsāvalokakaḥ ।।

triśūlacakrasaṃrūḍho maṃḍalaṃ jānu kalpayet ।
satyaṃ tadūrddhagaṃ kāryaṃ hastenāṃguṣṭhamākramāt ।।

vāmapādāṃ tadvadeve gudayantraniveśitāṃ ।
ūrddhavaktro viśālākṣo hāsyavaktreṇa dedbhṛśaṃ ।।

aṣṭadhātumahānādaṃ śūle preṃkhatprayogataḥ ।
prerayetkārgalāntāntaṃ yāvadvāmāsu saṃsthitaḥ ।।

tatrārūḍhaṃ plavaddevi praharāt khecarī padaṃ ।
eṣā devi tavākhyātā mudrā nāmnā tu khecarī ।।

sarvvāmudrā tu rājeśī sāmānyā sarvvatasthitāḥ ।
guruvaktrakramāyātā nākhyātā kasyacinmayā ।।

baddhaināmāviṣetūrṇṇaṃ ghūrṇṇamānā vilocanaḥ ।
śivārāvā(mi) kurute valganāt sphoṭanāni ca ।।

mahāhāsyaṃ mahāgeyaṃ mahāmelāpamuttamaṃ ।
(32a)
mudrābaṃdhādikaṃ sarvvaṃ kuryācca sādhakeśvaraḥ ।।

plavate gaganābhogaṃ khecaraiḥ paripūjitaḥ ।
bhavatyeva hi saptāhādyathā devī karaṃkiṇī ।।

haḍḍāhaḍḍamukhāraudrā tathānyā nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
mahārahasyamudreśī nākhyeyā jātucittvayā ।।
sārātsāratarā hyeṣā tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।

।। iti khecarīmudrā ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahātantrottamottamā ।
mahābhairavanāmeti yā mudrā sarvvasādhanī ।।

yasyāstulyā na paśyāmi mudrāpīṭheṣu vistare ।
yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa bhavedbhairavanāyakaḥ ।।

tāṃ śṛṇuṣva mahābhāge sarvvasrotesu nāyakā ।
iyaṃ devi purā guptā sarvvataṃtreṣu bhāmini ।।

anayā saptarātreṇa vīrabhūmau sthito naraḥ ।
plavate khecaraiḥ sārddhaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

gatvā vīravarodyānaṃ madirānaṃdanaṃditaḥ ।
udvāsanopaviṣṭastu samakāyaśirodharaḥ ।।

spaṣṭavakṣā koṭarākṣo bhrūvilāsakṛtāśramaḥ ।
arddhamākuṃcayedvāhū gajacarmadharo yathā ।।

punaḥ punarvvi * * * vismaṣe ca punaḥ punaḥ ।
nāsāvaṃśesu cipiṭe vitate krodhamūrchite ।।

īṣadvidāritāsyosau jihvāmantaśca lālayet ।
hāhākāraṃ nadedraudraṃ kramādaṣṭakramodgataṃ ।।

sṛṣṭhadeho bhāsurātmā pūrvvavīryāvalaṃbakaḥ ।
punaḥ punaḥ plavadvalganmudreyaṃ bhairavābhidhā ।।
(32b)
anayā baddhayā gauri kaṃpate bhuvanāvaliṃ ।
kṣobhayecchaktisadanaṃ pātayedgraṃthisaṃcayaṃ ।।

palālipūrṇṇaḥ sānaṃdā vināyogaṃ madarṇṇayet ।
(i)śānabaṃdhayedvīro vedimadhye vyavasthitaḥ ।।

praharārddhāt khecarosau sādhakendro bhaviṣyati ।
sarvvaiśvaryasamāyukto yathā bhairavanāyakaḥ ।।

sakṛtsvarūpalābhaṃ syādvaddhā paśyati yo naraḥ ।
śilā śarīropi sadā samayācāralopakaḥ ।।

mudreśyā darśanāddevi svarūpastho bhaviṣyati ।
anayā rahitaḥ sarvvā bhairavāśeṣa(vistaraṃ) ।।

nirjīvantaṃ samākhyātaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
sarvvatantreṣu sāmānyā vidyāpīṭhe tathaiva hi ।।

na prakāśyā maheśāni śiṣye maṃdaparīkṣite ।
mudrā(rājñāmahāyogā) bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe bhairavamudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrā yā ṣaṇmukhottamā ।
yayā sārddhaṃ (tamaṃ)ghoraṃ naśyati - - - tmanāṃ ।।

yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa devadevī pradṛśyate ।
sā śṛṇuṣva mahābhāge mudrā sarvvottamottamā ।।

sudhūpāmodabahule puṣpaprakarasaṃkule ।
sthānabaṃdheta mudreśī madirānaṃdanaṃditaḥ ।।

nātitṛpto nātirikto tāṃbūlamukhapaṃkajaḥ ।
raśmisaṃdhānamāsādya raśmināthaṃ vilokayet ।।

karāṃgulibhireveha (mukharaṃdhrādvidhaṭṭa)yet ।
(33a)
alakātaniveśmeta locane staṃbhitāni ca ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyukto raśmināthaṃ catuṣkalaṃ ।
tenaiva mantranāthena tatrasthaṃ paśyate śivā ।।

ṣaṇmukhākhyā iyaṃ mudrā tava devi prakāśitā ।
ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena piṃḍasiddhipradāyikā ।।

brāhmaṇasyāpi śubhage kimuvīravarasya ca ।
raśmināthaṃ tatkṣaṇena baṃdhamātrāt prapaśyati ।।

māsamātrāddevadevī tatrārūḍho vilokayet ।
atītānāgatānarthān varttamānaṃ vadeta sā ।।

mahāmantrasvarūpāṇi trimāsān - - - - ti ।
eṣā rahasyamudreśī tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।
tvayā guptatarākāryā nākhyeyā yasya kasyacit ।।

।। iti jayadrathe ṣaṇmukhabaṃdhaḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrāyogeśvareśvarī ।
yasya baṃdhanamātreṇa kulasāmānyatāṃ vrajet ।।

divyamudrā mahāguhyā baddhayogamavāpsyasi ।
tāṃ śṛṇu tvaṃ mahābhāge divyamudrāṃ mahā(balā) ।।

sthānamāsādyanirdvandvaṃ sudhūpāmodamaṃtharaṃ ।
madirānandacaitanyaḥ puṣpasraggaṃdhacarcitaḥ ।।

mṛdumāsanamāsthāya netrapadvādiguṃṭhanaṃ ।
pūrvvāmukhastatrarūḍhaḥ samakāya śirodharaḥ ।।

spaṣṭavakṣo ṛjugrīvo vāmamuttānamācaret ।
tatpṛṣṭhe dakṣiṇaṃ kāryaṃ aṃguṣṭhaṃ ca pradeśinī ।।
saṃlagnaṃ kārayetsamyak punaḥ punarvvi(mā)vayet ।
(33b)
sānaṃdavaktrakamalaṃ īṣadāsyaṃ vikāśayet ।
kiṃcidvikāśayennetre dante dantā na saṃspṛśet ।।

ālokayetpūrvvavīryaṃ madirānaṃdananditaḥ ।
divyamudreyamākhyātā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।

ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena plavate gaganāṃtaraṃ ।
sādhakendro na saṃdehaḥ khecarīvaṃdito bhavet ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe divyamudrābaṃdhaḥ ।। ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mudrā bhairavavallabhā ।
sarvvasiddhipradā devi duḥprāpyā hyakṛtātmanāṃ ।।

anayā baddhayā devi trailokyaiśvaryadā bhavet ।
gatvā harālayaṃ rātrau madirānaṃdananditaḥ ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyukto vedimadhyaṃ samāśritaḥ ।
vāmajānu prasāryeta dakṣiṇaṃ gudasaṃsthitaṃ ।।

dviguṇīkṛtya dve bāhū muṣṭirucchritatarjanī ।
karṇṇapārśvenidhāyaivaṃ locane kiṃcideva tu ।।

melayedūrddhage devi ūrddhavaktrā samunnadet ।
śivārāvaṃ mahāghoraṃ mulādhārabalāśritaḥ ।।

iyaṃ sā pheravī nāmnī mudrā yoginivallabhā ।
anayā baddhayā sadyo vedate sacarācaraṃ ।।

yoginyo deśavarttinyo āgacchanti samantataḥ ।
tāsāmarghaṃ pradātavyaṃ bhitvā vāmāṃgamātmanaḥ ।।

datterghabhairavaḥ sākṣātsādhakendro bhavet priye ।
mahadaiśvaryasaṃyuktaḥ praharārddhā na saṃśayaḥ ।।

yāvanti devi mantrāṇi teṣu sādhāraṇā smṛtā ।।
prāṇānte devadeveśi deyeyaṃ nānyathā kvacit ।।
(34a)

।। iti jayadrathe pheravābaṃdhamudrā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

adhunā taṃtrarājyasya madhye śṛṇu kṛśodari ।
mahārahamahātulaṃ sarvvavīrendradurlabhaṃ ।।

guptaṃ vīryaṃ mahābhāge sarvvataṃtreṣu gopitaṃ ।
mukhapāraṃparāyātaṃ tavādya kathayāmyahaṃ ।।

gatvā harālayaṃ devi madirānaṃdanaṃditaḥ ।
vīranāthā mahāsatvo vedimadhyaṃ samāśrayet ।।

tatra pretasamārūḍho baṃdhe mudrāṃ maheśvari ।
mahāsaṃrāviṇī nāma bhairavāyutaghaṭṭanī ।।

madhyastho madhyago bhūtvā madhyago madhyamaṃdire ।
madhyārūḍho mahāmadhya trimadhya parimadhyataḥ ।।

mahāmadhyalaye śūle ṣaḍaśre ṣaṭpadojvale ।
śaktyaṃhadhāmacakrānte sphuradā(dyā)yogataḥ ।।

vikāśākuṃcanenaiva prakāśānaṃdayogataḥ ।
aṣṭadhā rāviṇīrādhya tathā ṣoḍaśadhā punaḥ ।।

koṭare locane kārye balādgātraṃ viśodhayet ।
ālīḍha svā * saṃdhāne plaveddardurayogataḥ ।।

ekāśītipadodvānta devī tejodvatārciṣaṃ ।
tāvatsahāyasaṃyukto mudrābaṃdhena rāviṇī ।।

anayā baddhamātrāyā trailokyaṃ kṣobhayennaraḥ ।
devatā cakramakhilaṃ kiṃkareva bhaviṣyati ।।

mahābhairavavīrendrā rāvaṃ muṃcati dāruṇaṃ ।
raktavarṣaṃ prajāyeta hāhārāvaṃ sudāruṇaṃ ।।

kṣobhayedakhilaṃ viśvaṃ hūhukādiśivāntakaṃ ।
ravanti sarvva tatrasthāḥ sadevāsuramānuṣāḥ ।।
(34b)
tryahātpiṃḍasya saṃsiddhiḥ sādhakaiḥ sahitasya ca ।।

bhavatyeva na saṃdeha ityāha pārameśvaraḥ ।
yāvatyau tatra madhyasthā mudrā rājñaḥ prakīrttitāḥ ।।

tāvatyeva maheśāni nāyakī parikīrttitā ।
haḍḍācakroditaṃ sarvvaṃ anumantavyamambike ।।

mudreśyā mantrakośaṃ tat kimanyatkathayāmi te ।
atomurmāni jānāti sphuṭitā parameśvarī ।।

tāraṇī bhīmagahanā saṃsārātparamā kalā ।
sarvvagaganaṃ * * * mudreśvaryā prabhāvataḥ ।।
etadrahasyamākhyātaṃ tantrasāraṃ mahāpriye ।।

।। iti jayadrathe caturthaṣaṭke mahāsaṃrāviṇīmudrābaṃdhaḥ ।।।।

athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrā sarvvottamottamā ।
ṣaṭtriṃśatisahāyaiśca sahito baṃdhayetsadā ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyukto madirānaṃdanaṃditaḥ ।।
(pra)tyaharāspadaṃ rātrau baṃdhayenmantratāṇḍavī ।।

yathākāmaṃ pravartteta devīnāmanukāravat ।
brahmāṇi lakṣaṇaṃ nartte kṣaṇaṃmāhe śivatpriye ।।

kaumārimudrāṃ baddhena (kṣaṇa)meva samunnadet ।
kṣaṇaṃ vaiṣṇavibiṃbena punarvvārāhivat priye ।।

śākīmudrā prabaṃdhena kṣaṇaṃ nartta maheśvari ।
cāmuṃḍā tu yathādevī rurusainyavidāraṇī ।।

(tadvannaye) tavonantadvosamyagrasethavā ।
punareva yathāśuṣkā(tpā)tatāsyā bhayānakā ।।

sunaṃdane vadetkruddhā tadvannarhedavihvalaḥ ।
vyāvyeśī caiva yā yā - - tadvannarhe kṣaṇaṃ priye ।
(35a)
punaḥ śaurī yathākruddhā tadvannartte svalīlayā ।।

vāyavye ca kṣaṇaṃ narttedvanadīvakṣaṇaṃ tathā ।
yathā hyekatarā devī tadvannartte kṣaṇaṃ muhuḥ ।।

vaināyakī gāruḍī ca cāṃdrī kāpālinī tathā ।
tathā narttetkṣaṇaṃ veti yadicchetkāmacāravat ।।

khalvīkṛtaṃ vā narttavyaṃ mātṛṇāṃ cāruveṣṭitaiḥ ।
valganīyaṃ gahānaṃttaḥ sapadaiḥ sādhakeśvaraiḥ ।।

mattatāṃḍavikā nāmnī mudrā sarvvottamottamā ।
etāṃ badhvā sādhakendraḥ plavate gaganāntaraṃ ।।

vaṃdyamānaḥ khecarībhistārābhireva caṃdramāḥ ।
sahitaḥ parivāreṇa yathābhimataśobhanā ।।

sakṛnmelāpamāpnoti baddhamātra na saṃśayaḥ ।
bhogamokṣapradā seyamākhyātā te yathārthataḥ ।।
pūrvvavīryasamāyuktaḥ sādhayenmanasepsitaṃ ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe mattatāṇḍavikā vidhiḥ ।। ।।

athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sarahasyā mahārthadā ।
mudrā sarvvottamā śreṣṭhā yā nākhyātā kadācana ।।

āviddhā nāma yo ghoro bhairavasyātivallabhā ।
sahāyaiḥ sahito vīro diṅmantrakṣatasannidhiḥ ।।

madirānaṃdacaitanyaḥ (pa)lālipravipūritaḥ ।
daṇḍavatpādavinyāsa kāryameva punaḥ punaḥ ।।

tālāśabdaṃ hanedbhūyo yathākāmaṃ punardadet ।
kakṣakaṭṭanamevātra punareva samuplavan ।।
punarāviddhamadhāvanmattotsahavaconnadet ।।
(35b)
cidgarbhastho mahāśaktirānaṃdagṛhasavyagaḥ ।।

lalate locane kārye punaḥ kruddhetha koṭare ।
kheṭātsaraśme * * saṃmāṃgahāsuṃmucetpunaḥ ।।

evaṃ pitṛvanāsīno mudrābaṃdhaṃ samārabhet ।
sarvvadhāvanti valganti gāyanti ca madoddhatāḥ ।।

krīḍanti majñamadhuraṃ punaḥ kekāravākulā ।
sītkārarāvāṃśca muhuraślīlānpunareva hi ।।
