evaṃ vīryasamāyuktā sādhako baṃdhayetsadā ।
saptāhāddevadeveśi plavate gaganaṃ naraṃ ।।

sahāyaiḥ sahito vīro bhogamokṣabharālasaḥ ।
asyāstu sadṛśī nānyā mudrā trailokyabhūṣaṇī ।।

vidyate devadeveśi sarvvasampadguṇāvahā ।
āviddheti samākhyātā sarvvakhecarapūjitā ।।

baṃdhamātrānmahādevi plavate gaganaṃ priye ।
na sā siddhirihāstīti anayā yā na siddhyati ।।

mahāmelāpamatulaṃ devatāsādhanaṃ mahat ।
mahāyogeśvaratvaṃ ca aṇimādyaṣṭakena tu ।।

pātālākāśagāmitvaṃ siddhyate līlayā nṛṇāṃ ।
mokṣameva maheśāni sarvvātītaṃtramuttamaṃ ।।

sakṛdāpnoti vīrendro mudreśvaryā prabhāvataḥ ।
evaṃ devi tavākhyātaṃ mudrā kāṃḍamaruttamaṃ ।।

yanna kasya cidākhyātaṃ sarahasyaṃ mahārthadaṃ ।
tadadya tava deveśi proktaṃ sarvvamatīndriyaṃ ।।

mantratantraṃ samākhyātaṃ yā sā naiva mayā priye ।
tāsāṃ sāmānyato devi devī hṛdayamuttamaṃ ।।

prakīrttitaṃ mahābhāge sarvvasādhāraṇaṃ mahat ।
(36a)
etanmudrāgaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sarvvadevīkulonnatāṃ ।।

tatra madhye samākhyātā hṛdayāye maheśvari ।
teṣāṃ sādhāramākhyātaṃ atānyatra nava priye ।।

pūrvvavīryasamāyuktaṃ bahubhogabharāvahaṃ ।
kimanyatkathayiṣyāmi nātaḥ parataraṃ priye ।।

vidyate pīṭhasaṃdohe mudrākhye sarahasyake ।
iti tava mudrākośaṃ sarahasyamaśeṣataḥ ।
proktaṃ vaktavyamataḥ paramaṃ yatsaṃrāviṇi śabdakāṇi sarvvasvaṃ ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
mudrākośaḥ dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

evaṃ niśamya girirājasutā maheśyā,
romāṃcakaṃcusamutsalakā babhūva ।
bhūyaḥ punaḥ parameśvarameva devī,
vācyaṃ jagāda paramārtharahasya yuktaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

kṛtakṛtyāsmi bhagavan prasādāttava sāṃprataṃ ।
śrutaṃ mayā devadeva mudrākośamanuttamaṃ ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi kathaṃ sā śabdakālikā ।
sūcitā yā mahābhāga tvayā mahyaṃ surādhipa ।।

kiṃ vīryā sā mahādevī kathaṃ sā kīdṛśī vada ।
dūtīnāmagraṇī yā sā vīrānāmagrasiddhidā ।।

caryābhedaḥ kathaṃ tasyāḥ siddhidā sā kathaṃ vada ।
triṃśatkoṭistu maṃtrāṇāṃ koṭitrayamathāpi vā ।।

tvayoddiṣṭānekamukhā hyanekacārasatphalā ।
asaṃkhyayāgabhuvanā hyasaṃkhyācāralakṣaṇā ।।
(36b)
saṃkhyā ca viniyogādya asaṃkhyāścaiva vigrahā ।
asaṃkhyācitiyogādyā hyasaṃkhyodaya so yadā ।।

nānādūtipratīhāraiḥ sadasyasakhikiṃkaraiḥ ।
citato jñānavi(ṭa)pastvayā proktā purā kila ।।

śabdakālī kathaṃ khyātā śabdabrahmasusiddhidā ।
agraṇī jñānavṛkṣasya dūticakramanuttamaṃ ।।

siddhyate yaṃ niyuktā sā śabdabrahmādyasaṃkhyadṛk ।
tatprasīdā matendrendra yathāvacchaṃdamuttaraṃ ।।

jñānekṣaṇena cālokya śabdamuṣṭirvvadasva me ।
evamākarṇya devendro vākyadaṃ girijāmukhāt ।।
sāṭṭahāsamuvācedaṃ parameśaśivovyayaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva śubhage vacmi praśnametadanuttamaṃ ।
durvvāsasya yathā proktaṃ purālaśata eva hi ।।

mahāhimācalapṛṣṭhe āsamastaṃ maheśvari ।
tadadya (kaṃ)thayiṣyāmi vistareṇa yathātathaṃ ।।

akīlitamasaṃdigddhaṃ sarvvajñānoghasaṃkulaṃ ।
mābhūte saṃśayaṃ devi kathayiṣyāmi tatvataḥ ।।

yathā sā kālikā bhīmā sarahasyā sadūtikā ।
śabdabrahmāparā puṣṭyā agrayughatisaṃkaṭe ।।

pracchāditaṃ vīrabhī(magarṇṇe)rvvāpyatha kiṃkaraiḥ ।
* * nardūti sma hityā kṣipraṃ vaśamupaiti hi ।।

kiṃkarairagrato ruddhaṃ pratīhārī vilocitaṃ ।
tatvaṃ sādhakavīrāṇāṃ dūticakreṇa siddhyati ।।
yanmātrārddhapadairhīnaṃ mitra * * viparyayaṃ ।।
(37a)
śabdamuṣṭiprasādena maṃtrī prāpnotyabhīṣṭitaṃ ।
yastyakto kṣetrapālo hi cāryodvyo vīranāyakaḥ ।।

siddhyate dūti muṣṭyātmā acirāllabhate naraḥ ।
puṣṭaikadeśayogātmā cakroddharaviparyaye ।।

naktaṃ caraṃto caryāsu sucirāmṛtamaśnute ।
vīrabhairavanāthena vidhisaṃskāravarjitaiḥ ।।

muṣṭiśaṃkākayogātmā ṣakādvastollabhepsitaṃ ।
ubhau vā śaktiyogena tadarghabalasāhasāṃ ।।

maṃtrī prāpnotyabhiṣṭaṃ vai rāvaṃrāveṇa pūjayet ।
rāvahare yugenātha samakṣayegnikoṣṭabhuk ।।

caryācarantaścarataḥ prāpnotyabhimataṃ phalaṃ ।
uta bāhyaśidhārādvai saṃgrahā sadarūhayā ।।

vīrabhairavanāthaṃ ca dhyāyaṃ muktyāgrago labhet ।
tata vibhyāsa valyeyaḥ paramātmani darśayet ।।

sapūrvvabuddhibhāvātmā agrāhyā plavate naraḥ ।
tathā hi sūtayā hyāsī svaveśmenātha pūrvvake ।।

yaṣṭvā madhye śabdamuṣṭidṛṣṭādṛṣṭaguṇāṃ labhet ।
athābhyāsāgraṇī hyekā yuktādyaṃte kriyā vidhau ।।

jayaṃ prāpnotyabhīṣṭārthaṃ caryādvau puṣkaraṃ vrajet ।
abhyasya tatvapathagā yajedvai yojayetsadā ।।

tataḥ samartya dharmātmā īśvarāṣṭaguṇaṃ labhet ।
antepyevaṃ yathā coktaṃ adya matyayugānanā ।।

agniṇyepi jape yogī śivamūrttiḥ paraṃ bhavet ।
rāvādyenāgraṇī hyekā yuktvā japtvā yutāṣṭakaṃ ।।
(37b)
hutvā trimadhuranāraṃ ca lokayātrā yutāṃ vadet ।
athaiva maṃtrayogena brīhibhidyagrahā vanāt ।।

darśayedindrajālāni viniyogena nigrahāt ।
ādyantamadhyayogaikā pūrvvabaddho grahomayet ।।

śūnyaveśme japedrātrau hayatkaulāni bhuṃjate ।
agraṇībhyāṃ yugapadā cādyante rāvarāviṇī ।।

asurāsurasaṃgrāme tatkṣaṇādeva darśayet ।
tathaivaṃ vā yathāyogā kṣetre digdakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ।।

chaṃdāpayati yoginyaḥ pūrvvabhaktā paśūttamā ।
utavrataṃ ca kaṃkālī hutvā tu kṣetrasaṃkaṭe ।।

brahmarakṣasagoṇāsyā cakraṃ vaivaśvamānayet ।
tusakeśākṣetrabhasmadūtare tu rmahāghṛtaṃ ।।

piṇḍamālāsavaparaṃ romāryyeni catuṣpadaṃ ।
yatedaṃ muśalārūḍhaścakrapadamalīmukhaṃ ।।

saṃvitoṣṭakule pakṣe yathādbhyo vaśayekṣitaḥ ।
sarāvamuṣṭyā gaṇikāṃ jayantāṃ pūrvvavāhanāṃ ।।

svasthānasthe darśayātaṃ śaktijālamanekadhā ।
pitaro rākṣasā yakṣāḥ kiṃkarā vīraguhyakāḥ ।।

brahmarakṣāpiśācāśca nṛpasenā mahāhavāḥ ।
daityāvaikādraveyāśca asurāḥ kanyakā saha ।।

devāsurāvahaṃ ghoraṃ vīrakhecaryamelakā ।
icchārūpamanekātsyaṃ parāṣāmātmanasya tu ।।

lokālokā carcayaṃto viniyogena sādhakaḥ ।
(38a)
sarāvenāṭṭahāsena saṃdhyayo'sasamūhayoḥ ।।

niśā svagrahaṇī juṣṭvā niśvanudatinuttamā ।
śataṃ paśupalānāṃ vai śāstre śāstroktakalpanā ।।

nṛnṛtī devi cakramo ninṛtaṃ vaśamānayet ।
hathaśaṣasahayatne ca kakhāvanānyuharake ।।

yaṣṭvā (vā) śrayaṇī pūrvvaṃ abhīṣṭaṃ phalamaśnute ।
raudravīropacakraṃ vai jñeyapuṇyātha laṃpaṭau ।।

aghoraṃ bhāskaraṃ caiva siddheśvarapulomajaṃ ।
brahmāvairhāya vārāhā samaste nātha bhūrbhuvaṃ ।।

saṃkhyaṃ vrajati mantrajñaḥ kulānāṃ sakulaiḥ saha ।
evamanyānyanekāni siddhayastu sahasraśaḥ ।।

agraṇīnāṃ prabhāvena helayā sādhako bhavet ।
asaṃkhyāni yathā kasya raśmayo viśvamaṇḍale ।।

vrajatyevaṃ mantriṇaśca āmnāya siddhayoditāḥ ।
prāvṛtya nātha jalada dhārāpātamanekadhā ।।

sādhakasya tathācchidrā pataṃte vighnapuṣkalā ।
śālipṛṣṭe yathāmeghā pataṃto maṃtranāyakaiḥ ।।

nirvvāsayati yuktātmāgraṇīvaṃ vighnacchidrasu ।
tasmātsarvvaprayatnena vighnacchidravyapohanaṃ ।।

kṛtvādau tatra muṣṭyā vai yukto maṃtriśnute phalaṃ ।
māhātmyaṃ tatvamuṣṭyā vai devadevī maheśvarī ।।

śrutvaivaṃ dūticakrasya punaḥ provāca śaṃkaraḥ ।।।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

trāhi trāhi maheśāna sarvvabhūtahitecchayā ।
brūhi me paramaṃ jñānaṃ yacchrutaṃ na surāsuraiḥ ।।
(38b)
sā samastā talaṃ muṣṭī dūtī ṣaṭkāgrasiddhidā ।
saṃkṣepena ca vistīrṇṇā sarvvāmnāyaphalodayā ।।

atibhāro hi me deva agniśālyopasaṃhṛdi ।
duḥsaho rudratulyānāṃ sādhakeṣviha kā kathā ।।
tat prasīda maheśāna jñānavijñānavigrahaḥ ।।

saṃjñārūpavibhedena prāṇā(nyaṃ)dhagajādiṣu ।
saṃskāracakraṃ ca tathā adhikāraphalodayaṃ ।।

caryācāravidhānena saṃkṣepātsiddhisaṃgrahān ।
vikāraphalasāphalyamekā dve namṛte kathā ।।

tathā śaktyārghagrahaṇaṃ * * śaktilakṣitaṃ ।
asiddhārabravīdeṇaṃ kṣipraṃ yena sukhaṃ vrajet ।।

praśredaṃ vidhivistīrṇṇaṃ kṛtvā samyagyathoditaṃ ।
yathā ca devadeveśyā rudranprājñā na dṛkṣaṇaḥ ।।।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi jñānametatsudurlabhaṃ ।
maṃtroddhāramataścādau saṃskārātsādhakaḥ punaḥ ।।

karmabhogamataḥ paścādvīravīryādyanantaraṃ ।
ekopadeśayogena tathā vratacareśvaraṃ ।।

śaktiyogaprabhāvena vīrendra tvaṃ yathā labhet ।
ekānte nirjane ghore parvvatasyāntare tathā ।।

nadīnāṃ saṅgame vāpi mātṛkṣetretha vā śubhe ।
dvātriṃśadvārukādyaindre uddharetkoṇagraṃthitaḥ ।।

sarvvakoṇentare hyanto āsanaṃ pauruṣodaye ।
koṣṭeśvaccānurūpeṇa uttamādhamamadhyamāḥ ।।
(39a)
vinyasedātmatatvaṃ tu visargeṇa tu bhūṣitaḥ ।
antyakoṇeṣu vargāntā śeṣāgryāgryaṃ vikoṇake ।।

athānyā saha anyobhyo adhorddhakoṣṭapārśvayoḥ ।
evaṃ nyastvā yathāmnāyaṃ sarvvāmnāyavidho punaḥ ।।

mātṛkāṃ pūjayitvā tu tatvamuṣṭiṃ samuddharet ।
maṃtrārddhaṃ rasamuddhṛtya bhūyaḥ piṃḍavidhau smaret ।।

akṛtyantognivāyuśca sāhaṭṭitulamāsanaṃ ।
rājāśvaryāgravidyāhnā rāvognikṣaṇamekataḥ ।।

ṣoḍaśena tadantābhyāṃ bhārūḍho ca * jaddharaṃ ।
bhūyotha śivavargo vai satejognyo vivarjitaḥ ।।

anyā * * agniveśmena pauruṣaścaṃcukaiḥ saha ।
kevalo jñānapūrvvo vai datryātrya * tribiṃdunā ।।

paṃcamena stavaṃ vakṣye muttaratveva kevalaṃ ।
rāvagni veśma piṃḍekṣe śāstre saṃrājusaṃpadaṃ ।।

tarjatoṃkuśamantraṃ vai bhūyostro pāvakānukaṃ ।
jaṃbhaṃnyantekṣayā rāvī mantrānana(su)khaṃ smaret ।।

rāvapiṃḍā hi rāvānte astrāgraṃ tu nivedayet ।
maṃtrā tayāṃtognipiṃḍadīrghīsvarasahaṃ hara ।।

akṣarajvaraniśāyāge smare duṣṭabharūṭikā ।
agravidyodayotyo vai ananāśca tataḥ punaḥ ।।

bhramārddhe ca dvijā tejo sapiṃḍāśanirākṣasī ।
sramo nilokṣaiśapiṃḍamuddhare grāmadaiśikaṃ ।।

ūhanaśca tathā hyastro hemabījāntakāriṇi ।
rodhāgninā samāveśyā astrāgne viniveśayet ।।
(39b)
rākṣasaṃ ca padaṃ bhūyo maṃtrāṇāṃ nāma saṃsmaret ।
bharūṭīnāma dūtyeṣā hṛdayaiḥ ṣaḍbhirvviśrutā ।।

vibhāgaṃ cāsya vakṣyāmi hṛdayānāṃ pṛthak pṛthak ।
bharūṭīnāma yā vidyā caturvviṃśākṣarairyajet ।।

saptākṣarā yajennityaṃ krūrākhyā tu mahābalā ।
yajeta tryakṣarā nityaṃ mahāghorā mahābalā ।।

vakraṃ bharūṭikā hyevaṃ yajate yo niśāmukhe ।
acireṇā hi tatvātmā khecaratvamavāpnuyāt ।।

śāṃtyāgre madanaṃ muktvā saṃjñā rāvāgrasaṃsmaret ।
saptādaśākṣaraiḥ pūrṇṇāṃ jaṃbhanīnāma yā yajet ।।

dvādaśārṇṇaiśca saṃpūrṇṇā yajedbhaṭṭabharūṭikā ।
rākṣaseśvaristathaivaṃ * yugahīkṣarairyajet ।।

namontaṃ hi mahāsaṃjñā bhavatyagreva sanmukhā ।
varṇṇavāhāragrapadyā nadante ūrddhakeśinī ।।

vikṛtānane ca punaḥ kālarātrinirodhitā ।
mānuṣāṇāṃ ca sāyojya bhojane rudhirāgragā ।।

aprāptakālasya (ca)bhate mṛtyuprade padadvayaṃ ।
dvidhā hānayugasyāgre tarjanyaḥ kevaloddharet ।।

tathā syādahapadaṃ dvidhātvevaṃ samuddharet ।
bhagavatyante saṃjñā vai namaskārāgravaktragā ।।

kālamukhyairdhukeśyagre virūpākṣapadadvayaṃ ।
tajī * bhīṣaṇī caiva dravinī pratitarjanī ।।

pūrvve vā prāptakāleva rākṣasāstvathavāriṇaṃ ।
amā tṛtīyā(ma)graiva śāstragrāsamataḥparaṃ ।।
(40a)
vadarogravidā mahā śāntāgranipadaṃ dvidhā ।।
dhāriṇyantaṃ tarjanaṃ ca jvarasāmastu yojayet ।
rāvaṇastu sapiṇḍo vai rāvāstreṇa hi saṃpuṭaṃ ।।

pūrvvavidyo paṃcapadī uddhareccāntimaṃ punaḥ ।
grāsāgrestvānaroddhṛtya akṣaraṃ ca dvidhā pṛthak ।।

kalmaśavimūḍhanityagre kāliśabdaṃ niyojayet ।
dvidhā padauntyā śāstraṃ vai pūrvvasaṃjñāgrasaṃsmaret ।।

dvidhā hyekanaroddhṛtya saṃjñāntemagramānanaṃ ।
kālamukhaṃ kālarātryā padaṃ pādāgrasaṃsmaret ।।

cakraṃ bharūṭisumukhaistarjanaṃ ca sadāṃkuśaṃ ।
jvaraṃ bharūṭivadyojya grāsamūrttimatoddharet ।।

jvaraṃ dvidhāgnimadhye vai piṃḍaveśmā sahaikataḥ ।
yojayetkālabhedena apunarbhava siddhidaṃ ।।

agrānte punareveha tarjanyaṃta gataṃ smaret ।
tryakṣakarṇyābhidhānena dūtyeṣā pūrvvavigrahā ।।

asya bhedaṃ pravakṣyāmi hṛdayānāṃ nibodhita ।
vidyā huṃkāriṇī pūrvvā saptaṣaṣṭhyakṣaraiḥ samā ।।

dvitīyā tryakṣakarṇṇīti śatādvikṣaravi * hā ।।
huṃkāriṇī tṛtīyā tu trayastriṃśākṣaraiḥ samāḥ ।।

niśūdanī kalmaśasya dvātriṃśārṇṇā caturthikā ।
kālamukhī paṃcamātrā ekatriṃśākṣaraiḥ samāḥ ।।

kuleśvarī saptavarṇṇā tryarṇṇā tu * vamekhalā ।
tryakṣakarṇyā ca cakreśāṃ yajate yo niśāmukhaṃ ।।

acirātsograṇīryukto labhedvīrendratāṃ muniḥ ।
(40b)
ānanānte varṇṇakṣayo varṇṇapiṃḍena lakṣayet ।।

dūtīrājeśvarīyuktā anyādāvāhanatrayaṃ ।
dvidhākulaṃ kaulikāgne yojayet praviśākṣaraiḥ ।
karaṃkamukhikhyevaṃ * yojayedavibhākṣarāḥ ।।

pṛthagakṣarāvatāryosau savisarge jvaregragau ।
agnikṣaye rāvaṇākṣau astrāgreva supiṃḍitaṃ ।।

sanamaskārapraṇavo bhavatyagre niyojayet ।
punastu keśimuddhṛtya dvidhākṣoravako balaṃ ।।

tanyagre hi sarvvāgryo anyasaṃjñāhatīśvarī ।
* * * * * * * * agnibījapadadvayaṃ ।।

vrājamanapi nihyagre astrena tu nirodhayet ।
saṃjñādūtīśvarī yojyā agrabhuṃjita padadvayaṃ ।।

vājamudvayamīhyagre yojayeta karaṃkiṇī ।
nitye naimittike yojya diśi niścitataḥ kramāt ।।

urdhakeśiṃ samuddhṛtya dvidhā kṣaurāvakevalā ।
tarjanyagre hi sarvvāgryā astreṇa tu nidāpayet ।।

kaṭṭanikaṭṭani dviyuktā śāntarāvastrapūrvvake ।
jvarapiṃḍograṇe vaktra bhūyaḥ piṃḍognidīrghitā ।।

rā * kṣi veśmā piṃḍāgra kārakāvegryarāvaṇaṃ ।
nāthasaṃjñā dṛnyagraṇi jvaro bhūyo visargiṇaṃ ।।

akṣarāvī pṛthakcintya aśvavaktrāṃ tatoddharet ।
karaṃkiṇeśvaśuklekṣe āditya dayite kramāt ।।

samonyagni sadīrghaṃ vai vāyu piṃḍākṣaraṃ smaret ।
rāvākṣapiṃḍapūrvvapiṃḍapūrvve ca astraruddaviveśayet ।।
(41a)
pūrvvapiṃḍo hyaṃkuśaṃ ca ṛkṣapiṃḍo mahābalaḥ ।
bhaṭṭānāsyeti dūtyeṣā hṛdaye pūrvvasaṃkhyayā ।।

hṛdayānāṃ punaścāsya bhedaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣvataḥ ।
bhaṭṭānu prathamā proktā saptatriṃśākṣaraiḥ smṛtā ।।

catvāriṃśaiśca varṇṇādyairdūtīśvaryaparāviduḥ ।
striṃśākṣarāṃstrimāyāvai saṃjñā paśunibaṃdhanī ।।

caturthī phetkāraravā ekaviṃśākṣarottamā ।
aṣṭādaśākṣaraiścādyaṃ paṃcamā tu karaṃkiṇī ।।

viśvabaṃdheti vai ṣaṣṭhā akṣaraistribhiviśrutā ।
dūtibhaṭṭī ca cakraṃ vai vidhinā yojayenniśi ।।

kulakaulīśasāhasye ekacakre jayaṃ bhavet ।
rāvognipiṃḍamāyī ca bhūyo tasmādvisargiṇī ।।

cakrāṃkuśaṃ madhyavedo ādyante tarjane punaḥ ।
māyāmayeva khaṃcārī pathā ājye padena ca ।।

pṛthagastreṇa hi padī padaṃ bhūyogramagrabhuk ।
padāntaṃ paṃcadhā yojya agre saṃjñā sa rāvikā ।।

padānte te rāvapiṃḍo astreṇa tu nirodhayet ।
dūtyāgraṇī punaḥ saṃjñā raktarāvāgragā smaret ।।

kuṃbhodaripadayugaṃ kārarāvāvaṇaṃ * * ।
rāvākṣaḥ savisarge vai rāvaṇāstrapadaṃ kramāt ।।

sarāvaṇastu vaktrotai saṃjñā dūtīśvarī grahā ।
huṃḍamukhitarjanānte tathā bhaṭṭakhalūṣikā ।।

rāvāstramaṃkuśaṃ gṛhya tarjanyastraṃ punarmukhaṃ ।
(41b)
nirharaṇi haraniśca jīvaṃ hāriṇi rāvagoḥ ।।

haṃkāriṇyantarāvaṃ vai rāvarāstraṃ punarmukhaṃ ।
harahuṃkāriṇi agrahe ehi śabdaṃ niyojayet ।।

haṭṭabharūṭisaṃcintyakirānāgratrayaṃ kramāt ।
caṃḍālicaṃḍisaṃyojya astrānte haṭṭamūlikaṃ ।।

jagadgrāsaṃ samuddhṛtya vaktraṃ vai tarjanaṃ pṛthak ।
jvarapiṃḍāṃkuśaṃ cakrā sarāvo jagataḥ kṣayaṃ ।।

eṣā phetkāriṇīnāma dūtī pūrvveva bhedayet ।
tathā śṛṇu vadiṣyāmi hṛdbhedo devatā yathā ।।

phetkāriṇīti prathamā paṃcatriṃśākṣaraiḥ samāḥ ।
trayoviṃśārṇṇā dvitī * kuṃbhodareti saṃjñi  * ।।

tṛtīyā haṭṭakhaluśī saptaviṃśākṣarairyutā ।
jīvahartṛcaturthī ca ekaviṃśākṣarairyutā ।।

paṃcamā harahuṃkāśca aṣṭāviṃśākṣaraiḥ samā ।
saptārṇṇaṃ tu jagadgrāsāṃ antimā dūtināyikā ।।

cakreṣa phetkāriṇyākhyā yajate yo niśāmukhe ।
siddhyate tasya cakrāṃkā vīrarudrapadodayī ।।

satvaraśmi ca piṃḍoyaṃ praṇavāntaṃ niyojayet ।
dūti rājeśvari hyagre bhagavati namohyataḥ ।।

caṃḍeśvareti saṃjñā vai mahāśabdāgamā yajet ।
padaṃ caṇḍeti śāktaṃ vai āvāhanatrayaṃ kramāt ।।

(sarvvavīraṃ) mahāśabdo bhairavīti tatoddharet ।
padadvayaṃ phetkāriṇī nirācāre tathā punaḥ ।।

māyā ghoreti ve māyā aṃkuśo tarjanaṃ pṛthak ।
(virātavaṃḍā - - - praviśati) pṛthak kṣayoḥ ।।
(42a)
dvidhā punaḥ khaṣolketi * * * * * * * * ।
vīrocchūṣmarudreśvarī rāvaphetkāriṇī punaḥ ।।

tridhā rāvo rāvaṇāśca graso verākapiṃḍayuk ।
punareva caturddhāstro yathā * vadvanaṃ punaḥ ।।

śākto vilāśane yuktyā smaredāvāhanatrayaṃ ।
trailokyadīpake ṣaṭke vaśārudhirapaṃcakaṃ ।।

laṃbodari catuṣkaṃ tu paṃcakā bhojanaṃ priye ।
laṃbike ca tathā saṃjñā bhaṭṭaśabdaṃ ca tarjanaṃ ।।

aṃkuśaṃ ca tathā rāvaṃ rāvaṇāstraṃ tatoddharet ।
dvidhā gacchetyānanāṃte tadbhūtakapadadvayaṃ ।।

dayitena trayoddhṛtya bhagavati niyojayet ।
laṃbastanistathā rāvo dvidhākṣastu tataḥ pṛthak ।।

jvaro visargamāveśya rāvapiṃḍastu āsanaṃ ।
grāmamūrttidvidhākṣastu laṃbogre rāvalaṃbinīṃ ।।

aṃkuśaṃ tarjanoddhṛtya rāvaṇostraṃ punarmukhaṃ ।
pūrvvaśabdaṃ samuddhṛtya siddheśī māyayā saha ।।

laṃbogne praviśatrayaṃ dahanaśca trayaṃ punaḥ ।
sūcayante huṃkāreṇa huṃḍaśabdāṃtatarjanī ।।

mukhiśabdo visargeṇa tadante bhajvaraṃ smaret ।
akṣarāvaṃ rāvaṇaśca tejamadhyestranāyakaṃ ।।

brahmāṇḍāyā samevaṃ hi uddharetkevalaṃ punaḥ ।
laṃbānāmeti dūtī sā hṛdaye parisaṃkhyayā ।।

asya bhedaṃ punarbhūyo hṛdayānāṃ bravīmi te ।
laṃbānāmeti prathamā navatyekākṣarādhipa ।।

laṃbaketi dvitīyā vai saptatriṃśākṣarāṃ yajet ।
laṃbastanīti tṛtīyā aṣṭāviṃśākṣaraiḥ sadā ।।
(42b)
laṃbinīti caturthī vai caturvviśārṇṇavigrahā ।
triṃśākṣarā huṃḍumukhī paṃcamātveva nirdiśet ।।

jagadāha karīviddhi aṃtimā vāḍavaprabhā ।
laṃbāṅkañca kramevaṃ vai yajate yo niśānane ।।

jñātvā patitvaṃ labhate caryāyuktena taṃ vrajet ।
cakramūrttijvarāpiṃḍo jvaramāyā samāptikā ।।

āvāhano brahmadhāma śivahare padadvayaṃ ।
biṃdvaṃ satyāgniśivi * tathā śiva padaṃ punaḥ ।।

rāvaṇāgre ca dayite kārarāviṇi paṃcake ।
saṃjñādanīśvarītyakṣe rāve ṛkṣapade vrajet ।।

brahmāṇḍaṃ samanityante aṃkuśokaṭṭatarjanaṃ ।
rāvāsraniraṃcaketi grāsāgraṃ viniveśayet ।।

jvaregryaṃte savāyustho piṇḍavatpiṃḍitaṃ smaret ।
māryātavādho māyānte agryastrau cāṃtṛrāvaṇe ।।

huṃkāriṇyā navānte vai rāviṇīti trayaṃ punaḥ ।
siddhihyavatarānte vai dayite paṃcake purā ।।

siddheśvari samuddhṛtya śivamukhipadadvayaṃ ।
dvidhākṣastu pṛthakciṃtya muṃḍadhāriṇi paṃcakaṃ ।।

rāvojvarimatītenyo rāvapiṃḍostra āsanaṃ ।
rāvānte tāramāyī ca praviśeta dvidhā smaret ।।

ghorarāviṇi saṃcintya aghoradayite tathā ।
śivāmukhegro rāvaṃ vai māstreṇa tu virodhayet ।।

vaktraṃ siddheśvarī yojya jvaramāyā punaryathā ।
bhagavanti catuṣkānte pūrvvavadayitā smaret ।।

madhyasaṃjñāntare rāvaṃ hana daha satarjanaṃ ।
(43a)
huṃkāricatuṣkonyo akṣajvarasarāva vai ।।

rāvaṇostre pṛthakcintya rāvapiṇḍamataḥ paraṃ ।
vaktro grāmastathāstraṃ vai vīramāyāstrarodhitā ।।

astro rāvaṃ saṃjñāte vai tarjanaṃ ca tathāṃkuśaṃ ।
rāvapiṃḍaṃ samuddhṛtya astrosaṃsrādva astrasu ।।

brahmamūrttijagādo hyomagnitejyaṃ(tra)tirvvalī ।
evaṃ dūtīśvarīviddhi pūrvvavacca śiveśvarī ।।

asya bhedaṃ pravakṣyāmi hṛdayānāṃ punaryathā ।
śivādūtīti prathamā catuḥṣaṭparṇṇavigrahā ।।

dvitīyā śivamukheti aṣṭātriṃśākṣaraiḥ śubhā ।
ṣaṭviṃśākṣaraiḥ saṃpūrṇṇā tṛtīyā ghorarāviṇī ।।

huṃkāriṇīti caturthā dvātriṃśadakṣaraiḥ samāḥ ।
paṃcādaśākṣaraiḥ pūrṇṇā vīraśaktistu paṃcamī ।।

ekākṣarā mahāghorā viśvagrāsā tu cāntimā ।
śivā cakramathorddhaṃ vai yajate yo niśāmukhe ।।

sākṣātpaśupatirmantrī vīrabhadreśvaro bhavet ।
maṃtroddhāramathaivaṃ vai agraṇīnāmanukramāt ।।

śabdamuṣṭiśca te caiva kathitā tedhunā mayā ।
adhunānantaraṃ vakṣye vīrendrāṇāṃ tu nirṇṇayaṃ ।।

aṃgānāṃ cakramudrāṇāṃ saṃkṣepādvāhanāyudhaṃ ।
jvarapiṃḍajīvapiṃḍe uddhareta pṛthagyathā ।।

anyanteha nilāsastho śramapiṃḍā niyojayet ।
astraivarāvapiṃ * * * * * takalā śikhaṃ ।।

tṛtīyaḥ toṣasasamo agryanillośca muktyamaṃ ।
(43b)
ityevaṃ vīranāthendrā vibhāgena virodhitaḥ ।।

prathamastu aghorākhyo mudrāmuṃḍo dvitīyakaḥ ।
bhṛṃgiriṭi tṛtīyaṃ vai kuṃkurākṣaścaturthakaḥ ।।

udbhūtapaṃcamo viddhi mahochuṣmastathāntimaḥ ।
evaṃ te vīranāthendrā saṃjñā bhāvena bhedayet ।।

grāmamūrttisuhṛdayo bhaṭṭagryastu śiraṃ smṛtaṃ ।
sajvarāstraṃ śikhā dvi * aparaṃ vaikṣikāmadaṃ ।।

vaktraśākto vivaro * netrametattrirakṣaraṃ ।
rāvapiṃḍastu kavaco samrāḍastreśvareśvaraṃ ।।

dīrghamātrā samāveśya biṃdudraśme nilānilau ।
mūlacakrāstu dūtīnāṃ vīrendrāṇāṃ krameṇa tu ।।

śaktivajradaṃturā ca vikaṭā muśaleśvaraṃ ।
atyohu svastu mudrā vai dūtī vīreśvarīṣu ca ।।

rāvogninā samāveśya te dayeta kramāmvaraiḥ ।
visargabiṃdusaṃyuktā dūtīcāreṣu vāhanā ।।

bhujeddhaste evamevaṃ samastā vīradevatā ।
kalācarābharaṇeṣu ubhayārthe niveśayet ।।

gajadvarāvāyupīṭhe tejānte ca svaraiḥ samaṃ ।
dūtī vīreṣvabhayato yojaye tadbhujā yathā ।।

jvaravaṃ rāvapiṃḍaśca yojayedvāḍavopamaṃ ।
nādamūrttibhīmanādau kaulikānāṃ kulodaye ।।

evaṃ maṃtrāṇi vaṃdevyamādau saṃcintya niścitaṃ ।
śiṣyabuddhimato bhūtvā dvaṃdvayogastu varttayet ।।

vaktrāṃgaistu narābhistu cakrāntaṃ daṃpatīyutaṃ ।
samastā bāhyarekhāstu nyastvā śiṣyaṃ praveśayet ।।
(44a)
uta vā yuktaśiṣyaistu ṣaṭsureva samantataḥ ।
śabdamuṣṭiryajedevaṃ samantā caraṇodare ।।

na kasya hi ca ṣaṇmūrtyau ṣaṭsaikanopadiśyate ।
ṣaṭsātadūti cakrosmi nicchaṃsidyubhayojayet ।।

tathā siddhyastrabhāvena * * * * * vā punaḥ ।
pravakṣyāmyanupūrvveṇa saṃkṣepādyajñamuttamaṃ ।।

adhivāsavidhānena dantakāṣṭhaṃ niśāmukhe ।
bhuktvā labdhā hyanujñāṃ vai pārameśvaracodanāt ।।

agraṇīti padasyānte yathāvacchaktikalpanā ।
pūjayitvā tu tasyādha yaṣṭvādūtīśitapradā ।।

siddhimāsādyubhayato śaktyāgre vīranāyakaḥ ।
yaṣṭvā caiva tu taṃ kṛtvā idaṃ vīreśvaraṃ padaṃ ।।

ekānte nirjane ghore nadānākhyātasaṃgame ।
mahodadhestaṭe ramye ūrddha vā kuṭaparvvate ।।

tricatuṣpadyarthorddhe tu śmaśāne vā suviśrute ।
mātṛveśma svayaṃbhe vā ekaliṃge gṛhethavā ।।

purastādbhūtalaṃ śodhya yathāmnāyaviśoṣaṇāt ।
tadvatsāṃḍūkamāpūrya nyastvā vai pūjayetpaṭe ।।

kṛtvāhutistadājyena mudrāyā madaiḥ samaiḥ ।
saṃkhyāsamairyathālābhaṃ tato maṇḍalamālikhet ।।

caturasraṃ caturdvāraṃ kṣmādibhūtaiḥ samāpitaṃ ।
yathākāmapramāṇena svābhāvāmnāyadarśanāt ।।

dvādaśārantadante vai dvādaśādityavarcasaṃ ।
varttulaṃ musalaṃ dīptaṃ lāvaṇāṃ kāmamadhyagaṃ ।।

saḍasrastu tadante vai pāvakaḥ pavanopamaṃ ।
(44b)
kṣīrasāraparyaṃke * jvālāmālāvalaṃvitaṃ ।।

hetirājaṃ tadanteśa śaradākāśavarcasāṃ ।
jvālājvalisahasrāḍhyo bhṛśaṃgāyavabhīpraṇaṃ ।।

likhetvevaṃ dvārāsrāsu toraṇānidhvajāni ca ।
patākādarśasaṃkhyāni vitānāni niveśayet ।।

dvāreṣu ratnakuṃbhāni dvārāgreṣu pṛthakpṛthak ।
tricakravasayādīpa kāmāṃge vaiva dīpayet ।।

tathāstra saṃvarāścānyā anekākārabhūṣaṇā ।
yojayitvā dūtigehe yojayenmanadevatā ।।

cakrasaṃjñā namaskāro didvideśvaradehināṃ ।
vinyāsakalpayenmūle locanāṃ vāsunāyakā ।
dvādaśāgrā vaktramudrā kalādyaṃgarabhodare ।।

pṛthagāścā(śvā)nalāntanādaikaikastu vinyaset ।
asumadhye dūtyo vai * * ekāntavidhā prati ।।

vinyasya pūjayedvīro tatvamūrttivibhedajāṃ ।
bhramadīrghāṃtyakālānte vāyastho śikhayā samaṃ ।।

sadāśive vanāsyante mahāvīrorkkamādiśaḥ ।
ṛṣanakrakūrmaratnā nānāprāṇiśatānanā ।।

likhellāvaṇyakṣīrādau ṛkṣaiḥ saṃbhūṣayedgṛhaṃ ।
evaṃ mantrārṇṇavaṃ nyastvā trimūle śākhaśākhayā ।।

pṛthagnyānena hi punardūtivārāstravinyaset ।
meghahema hi varṇṇābhā sadhūmāgnisamāparā ।।

jvalatpāvakasaṃkāśa sirīśakusumaprabhā ।
nānācaṃdrāmbaradharā nānāvastraśatairvṛtā ।।
(45a)
nānāhārasahasrāḍhyā nānāmaṇiśatojvalā ।
nānāgonāśabharaṇā nānāpuṣpaśatairvṛtā ।।

nānāṃgaracitā * * nānāśvaryaśatojvalā ।
ūrddhvakeśā virūpākṣā caturdaṃṣṭrātibhāsurā ।।

saṃkruddhā kalpate jīva jvalatpāvakaromagā ।
divyapātraṃ khaḍgabhujā ghaṃṭātriśūlapānayaḥ ।।

ulkāpāśabhujāghorā dvijasāvakarodyatā ।
śaktinīrajahastāstu gadākhaṭvāṃgapāṇayaḥ ।।

cintayeccakramādevyo * * yuttarabhujottarā ।
dvijottarā dvijaṣyaṣṭabhujā daśadvādaśa āyudhaḥ ।।

uttarottara evaṃ vai vaktrā bhedavikāriṇā ।
raktamālā vṛkālyādyā kṛṣṇaścākṛtivṛkṣurā ।।

tṛtīyāsthimukhāghorā aśvavajrācaturthikā ।
laṃbāgnigomāyumukhā śivā svākṛtisādṛśā ।।

vibhinnaivaṃ vaktraguṇā ṣaḍaśrinyā pṛthagyajet ।
ajāśvāścātha khaṭvāṃgāṃ kaulikorddhedvamiśritaṃ ।।

vāhanāstvathadūtīnāṃ nyāyedvai kāmarūpiṇā ।
ādyante madhyayugme ca śūlāgrāsuravanti vai ।।

vidhiśādhahyadhamayā antamadhyā hyadhorddhagaḥ ।
vāhanākṛtirūpaṃ ca vīrendrāṇāmanukramāt ।।

ye jaye vairikārāstrātsmaredāmnāyapādapān ।
mahāvīro rāvaṇoṣṭau vyāghrarūpamanekadhā ।।

bhūṣayetsvarṇṇaratnaistu vidhivadūtivāhanaṃ ।
evaṃ vai dūti vīreṣu sakalāṃ dhyānakāṃkṣaṇāṃ ।।
(45b)
ādau kṛtvāstravinyāsaṃ balipūjā punaḥ smaret ।
yathā vai devatārūpaṃ vīranātheṣu vai yathā ।।

tathā carā pānaphalā vastrāṇi kusumāṇi ca ।
nivedayati dūtīnāṃ bhūṣaṇāni anukramāt ।।

paśakāni anekāni citāni chatrasudhvaja ।
dhūpāni vividhānyevaṃ yadānyoraṇyamaṃtriṇaḥ ।।

grāmyānāṃ ca paśūnāṃ vai āraṇyānāmanekaśaḥ ।
kṛtvaivādau baliṃ dānaṃ ācāryeṇa suvistaraṃ ।।

baddhanetraṃ punaḥ śiṣyo śanaistatra niveśayet ।
samālabdhārghatoyena dhyeyavastreṇa saṃvṛtaḥ ।।

maṇiratnaphalaiḥ puṣpaiḥ (sa)phalaṃ dāpayeñjaliṃ ।
punaru - yanayanaṃ vistareṇa pṛthak pṛthak ।।

dātavyo dūtivīreṣu balipuṣpāṃ varāṇi ca ।
tatogni śaraṇaṃ gatvā yathāmnāyaṃ vidhānataḥ ।।

tarpaye - ti vīrāstrāya svājyeṣu tilaiḥ saha ।
mūlavidyāpratiśataṃ nādracakroṣṭhanodhikāṃ ।।

atorddhaḥ sarvvasaṃgānāṃ śoṣāṇāṃ ca tatorddhataḥ ।
punarvvidhiviśeṣeṇa āmnāyedriḥ pradarśanaṃ ।।

avisiṃce dūtivīrāṃ yuktimūrttihalūkhale ।
divyā * garbhāpatraiśca tadvalūkhalapṛṣṭhataḥ ।।

abhiṣiktvāgratādeyaṃ tato sānaṃdamārabhet ।
punaḥ(kaṃkālā - nāya) yathāmnāyanidarśanaṃ ।।

viraṃ japena dūtīnāṃ vīreśo vātha sādhayet ।
ekānte niśi satvastho pitṛveśmaṣaḍadhvake ।।

japataḥ śūnyaveśme vā mātṛveśme yathā śubhe ।
(46a)
śaktiśuktyagramālātmā tatsūtrakṛte vāsasī ।।

tathāgne vāgnihotraṃ vai juhvaṃ lakṣāṣṭakaṃ yathā ।
tataḥ karmeṣu nipunaṃ divyabhaumānyanekaśaḥ ।।

sādhayenmaṃtravīrastu kaulikānitya saṃkhyayaḥ ।
sparśāddahati bhūtāni vācayā viśayaḥ * * ।।

darśanānmeghasaṃghāni vyādhayo lakṣilakṣaṇaṃ ।
mṛgajāstrīṇi paṃcāśat tavājyaistilapallavaiḥ ।।

hutvāyutaṃ japellakṣaṃ daśāṅgaṃ paśyate bhṛśaṃ ।
trikālāmaladṛgyogī dūrāśabdaṃ śṛṇoti sā ।।

hṛdayādivibhedena paśyatestu tiraskṛtaṃ ।
sakalāṃtakalāmadhye saturyātītaparvvaṇe ।।

bāhyaṃ na sāmarasyena ubhayobje sakuṃbhake ।
gaṃtānanyasamādhānamubhaye dayasādhayet ।।

sarvvabhūtāśca pātena nānādhātu vikāriṇaṃ ।
jvarāṇāṃ karabhāṇāṃ ca vi * ṇāṃ cāpi nihnate ।।

bāhye ca puṣkare vaṃdyā tvānaye narapuṣkaraṃ ।
tatonteha satau veśya sakalo veśyavaccalaḥ ।।

dadṛśuḥ sakalodvāgau pūrṇṇeṃndu lalitānane ।
caṃḍevyācāravadghoraṃ kālakūṭaṃ mahābalaṃ ।।

kapitvamuṣkojaraye tyayavadviprajātayaḥ ।
pūrvvavadbrīhihomena vyāmiśreṇa anekadhā ।।

lakṣāyutāgrahomena krameṇākarṣayejjagat ।
tilaistu narakanyā vai nṛpakanyasuguggulaiḥ ।।

godhūmairyakṣakanyā vai piśācaiḥ sakulatthakaiḥ ।
tathaiva surakaṃnyā vai * divyakaśriyā saha ।।
(46b)
sādhako mānayeddevī mānayebjadalaiḥ saha ।
tathaiva agrahomena kalpajvāśi(ma)saṃkulaiḥ ।।

avejāte yakṣakanyā (labhate kālaya)kṣasā ।
juṣamā(dhorddha)caṃdreṇa saspṛhaṃ vīravaiklavaṃ ।।

bāhyevaṃ māmayānastu śukrapiṃḍāstradhātuṣu ।
śīcī kuśī ca bhṛśaṃgā bhāradvāja sakauśikaṃ ।।

astramudrā svamudrābhyāṃ mūrttimārjāracillaye(t) ।
kākakokilaśā(khā)ṇāṃ dṛṣṭagonāsaṣaṭpadaṃ ।।

lakṣaṇaṃ lakṣayitvā tu tatsvabhāvo labhesthitaṃ ।
athaivaṃ (tuṣamiśreṇa tiṣu vā svasthiro)matiḥ ।।

agnihotravimiśreṇa digrakṣaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaṃ ।
vaśyākarṣapramāde ca dvayabhedena kīlane ।।

jambhastambhaśca yuddhañca (vyādhibījenava) tathā ।
(kāmohemudrāśca vaiva sārendriya - - tathā) ।।

bhūtānāṃ kurute yogī lakṣādgrahavanodbhṛśaṃ ।
śrīphalaistu śriyo lebhe utpalaistu sarasvatī ।।

vāgeśvarī sapharyākṣi varastrī tṛmahāvanāt ।
(pathatraye catuḥ - - bhāvasāreṇa sādhayet) ।।

paṃcādvasthidvipaṃcātmā sāgramūlaparaṃ juṣaṃ ।
trayālukhalabhedena mausalī musalānugaṃ ।।

bhrāmyorddhacakramaparaṃ viṣṭatavya visādayet ।
sādayaṃ vāṃ punarbāhye vyāmiśreṇa bhramadbhramāt ।।

nirodhaya samāveśya catuḥsāmagryamāsuke ।
saskaṃdagaṇaśukrāyā bhūtakuṃbhāṇḍasaṃkulaṃ ।।

ku(lāṣṭa)bhaṇibhirgupto bhedayebdaṃ daśānanaṃ ।
(47a)
caturthave(ṣaca)krevaṃ niśidaṃti trivalgalaṃ ।।

samāvartya yathākāma mātṛbhirgardabhonnamaṃ ।
vajete dūtivārāstu juṣitvevamanaṃtaraṃ ।।

tadā saṃpaśyate yogi brahmarakṣakulāṣṭakaṃ ।
cidagre arddhasaṃdrendrā yaṣṭvā mūlodare (samaṃ) ।।

samaśrāntirasārānte jaṭharāgni samaḥ samaṃ ।
prerayeccūlavajrātmā yoginyo(rasti)vedayet ।।

sā(mrevaṃ) bhagavaṃ * * sopadaśapadottamaiḥ ।
niśi hutvā palaśataṃ uta cā rāvarāvaṇaṃ ।।

uccairnadati vidyānte rāvapiṃḍeśa maṃtramayet ।
paryaṭe(śa)varā u(ccai ca)rārāvaye * * * ।।

rarāve ye rāvanastu rāvapiṃḍāgrasaṃjuṣaṃ ।
tadā tasyāgramāyā tad dhūtacakrāsapannakaṃ ।।

utmanaḥ sādhake(ndro) vai icchayā vicarennaraṃ ।
śaktyātmaviniyogena arghayitvā paṭottamaṃ ।।

abhidhārāvrato vīrā saparyā dhūpadīpakaṃ ।
śmaśāne niśicaryā ghorā varāvaṇarāva(ṇaṃ) ।।

sarāva(ṇapra)bhāveṇa dūticakraṃ vaśaṃ nayet ।
svaprabhā rāvaṇoddhṛtya abjacakre parāvayaṃ ।।

rāvaṇobhayarāveṇa bhedayenmūlarāvaṇaḥ ।
samūlaṃ pāvaṇoddhṛtya turyamūlapradohayaṃ ।।

jape dūtithavīro vā icchayā khecaro bhavet ।
mahāvīro viraśeko yugapadrāvadrāvaṇau ।।

mahārāvaṇamāviśya rāvarāvaṇarāvayaṃ ।
sarāvakhaṃbhavā hyeko śubhrādveka(mahodayaṃ) ।।
(47b)
sodayodaya me vādya padaṃ yāti nirodhayet ।
vrataśaktyārghabhedena caṭasyāgre vṛhāvayaṃ ।।

agraṇī juhvate vīro vīre vīramadhūnvayaṃ ।
sādhūnvayaṃ hyekabhāvaṃ magṛṇyā sādhyasādhayet ।।

sarvvāṅgāgi samāviśya sarvvodhvānunamaṃ vrajet ।
nabhodarodare nābhau nābhau nābhātra vikramaṃ ।।

saṃbhrāmyāsu senāgre ca nistaraṃgasuvibhramaṃ ।
athoktvevaṃ mahāṣṇena * medaśotamajjayā ।।

vasāśukrādiyogena samastakulamaśnute ।
śrīphalaṃ brahmaśaktyādi dūtirājamahodare ।।

navapiṃḍodare yuktvā saṃkaleva kalottare ।
sasūre samacchriyāsu bījavaktrānyanekadhā ।।

samākarṣantayuktātmā icchayā ca visetsvakaṃ ।
rāvarāvaṇagrāsānte jarokṣapaṭamaṃḍale ।।

nyastvāgraṃ nyāsanyāsena kāṣṭhoṣmaṃśacchakhādivaṃ ।
piṃḍe piṇḍaṃ samāhṛtya agrasu * * mudraṇaṃ ।।

pibati jāmṛtaṃ yogī icchayā tu sukhaṃ vrajet ।
uta cāgramadhyaparyante śaktigrāsaṃ sabrahmamuk ।।

vrajeta vīrakuharaṃ tridhaikena subījayā ।
saṃpraviśyādvaramukhe rasaśukrādiśoṇitaṃ ।।

kṣaṇādākarṣayedyogī icchayā svasukhaṃ vrajet ।
vidhivatpūrvvahotreṇa śaktyarghā kusumaiḥ saha ।।

arghayitvā paṭavaro nisyagre caryayā vrajet ।
sarāvayaṃ rāvapiṃḍo rāvaṇaṃ vidhirāvaṇaṃ ।।

phalatṛṇasamagryanto māsaṣaṭkopacaryayā ।
karaṃkākhye kulendrendro cakraṃ khorvvārisaṃjñitaṃ ।।
(48a)
sādhakaḥ paśyate kṣipraṃ tulyo vyoma nibho bhavet ।
athaivaṃ kṛtavārātmā asidhārāgravistaraṃ ।।

śaktiyoganiyogena prāśayaṃ carusaptakaṃ ।
dīpadhūpaniyogena japāgnividhikalpanā ।।

saṃkalpaṇāgramagryaṇa sodayena nirodayaṃ ।
juṣaṃ vivarddharārddhena tridhākṣara nirakṣaraṃ ।।

agṛṇī vīrabhedena sāmarasyena sodayaṃ ।
dhyāyayogavarendrendraṃ agṛṇyekagravīrayuk ।।

dṛṣṭvā vīro nabhaṃ yāti tattulyasya mukhaṃ vrajet ।
akaṃṭakaṃ dumasyā vidinaṃ rātriparyaye ।।

supraśaktā sukele ca parṇṇaskaṃdasuvistare ।
adholīnā divārātrau sukhāmanonarājine ।।

jape taṃtraharā vīro mādhīro satsvatāṃ juṣaṃ ।
tatsvāṃgaiḥ pāraṇaṃ yuktvā adhabhramavidhūnane ।।

savīrāgṛṇyadvihitā svābhāvāntanirantaraṃ ।
sadbrāhmā kuharamukhe anulomavilomataḥ ।।

dhyāyaṃ māsāśṛyogena sādhako * mataṃ labhet ।
śoṣakṛtsaptakṣatraikā kāṣṭhameva samārabhet ।।

kāmameva catuddhastho vīracaryā paṭogrataḥ ।
ādau kṛcchapatākādye taptagomūtra(yo)thavā ।।

tasminnagavanamukhe pūrvvendhanatṛṇānalaṃ ।
jvālyabhūmiṃ pratāpyeta samaste nāgninā dṛvāṃ ।।

tato nṛmojya ājyaistu saptataile yathodbhavaiḥ ।
pūrayitvāsya kuharaṃ homayet kṣitipāvakaṃ ।।

tato maṇḍalamāpnoti jvālā * gnau hi yogināṃ ।
(48b)
bhasmamevaṃ tato gṛhya ājyamāsyena bhāvitaṃ ।।

lakṣaṃ hutvā vaśaṃ yānti pṛthvīśo pṛthivī saha ।
dyaṃgāsapta tathāṣvevaṃ svapalā dvādaśaiva tu ।।

lavaṇā paṃcabhiryojya kulācāsyai punarhunet ।
tatoṅgagaṃ triyogena samadvārasamanmukhaṃ ।।

prāyānte kuśayogena mūrddhnā pāśena kaṃṭhataḥ ।
ubhayo nāharedgrāhyaṃ chūnyamevaṃ nirambaraṃ ।।

śrutvā śriyāla dṛṣṭistu muktvā bharaṇamūrddhajaḥ ।
niḥsaṃjñapatito bhūmau saṃjñāmātraḥ kalevaraṃ ।।

vāmapādena cākramya ahorātre śatāniśaṃ ।
homayetpūrvvayogena sarvvasaṃvyāmukhaṃ jape ।।

pūjayetpaṭamukhyāgre tadgatenāntarātmanā ।
tadā tasya bhavedvaśyo nṛpatirmanagarvvitaḥ ।।

śarīradhanadāraistu dinendrastu vaśaṃ nayet ।
juhvate gaṃdhamāṃsāni kṣetrastho niśi parvvate ।।

kṣetrādijvālayitvā ca śuṣkakāṣṭhairvvanodbhavaiḥ ।
tato śavaṃ samānīya āmnāyo * dinakṣaṇaiḥ ।।

snāpayitvā paṭhāgre tu hutvāgni vakṣamāruhet ।
pūrvvoditaistu guṭikā satailaistu mukhaṃ hunet ।।

tadvadvākpaśuvegena muhlānādaṃśacāvravīt ।
ahaṃte kiṃkaro vīra nama skaṃde samāruha ।।

kāmaṃ vraja svalokāni pṛṣṭhe vāpi samākramāt ।
vākyaivaṃ kiṃkaramukhā samākarṇyātha niścitaṃ ।।

kṛtvā cātmahitaṃ bhūyo dṛṣṭe kāsyaṃ tyajecchavaṃ ।
atheyaṃ sādhayetkhaḍgaṃ niryayonyāsya vāhanaṃ ।।
(49a)
khaḍgavidyādharo yogī bhavate yāva medinī ।।

trilocanā muṃḍapuṭe tathaivāgrau susādhayet ।
vidyādharastilakano adṛśyo bhavate ciraṃ ।।

ghrātena kaṃṭako yojya tathaiva naracakṣuṣī ।
vipakte jananāthastu adṛśya karaṇaṃ smṛtaṃ ।।

evaṃ trigaṃḍiśca te netre kṛtvā kajjalanāyakaṃ ।
netrastho darśayetsarvvaṃ paśavo nidhanastathā ।।

pacaṃnātra paṭasyāgre kṛtvāgnivīratarpaṇaṃ ।
kulanāgahṛdāntastho jape lakṣaṃ yathāvidhau ।।

tadā tasya bhavedvaśyaḥ kulabhogī mahābalaḥ ।
ātmanā svadhanenaiva kanyābhistvamunaiḥ saha ।।

samakhaḍgaṃ padavaraṃ paṃcapārthivamaṃdire ।
evaṃ paregnihotre vai hutvāgnau vidhivat kramāt ।।

lakṣamekastathāgre vai sarṣapāguḍaguggulaiḥ ।
hutvā labheddhi vīrendraḥ khecarendrapadaṃ labhet ।।

divāya arddhacakrendro garbhabhājyeta śāśvataḥ ।
tadyogādakṣiṇādigrā yadicchetaddiśāmukhaṃ ।।

rāvarāvaṇarāveṇa rāvaye hyubhayodayaṃ ।
grāmaśaktau tu lābhaṃ ca vidyante japavovaraṃ ।।

hantu svareti svarodhaṃ aṭṭahāse sarāvaṇaṃ ।
bhūyo bhaya parendrendro rāvayaṃ pāyakaṃ juhet ।।

bhūyaiva prātarutthāya dinarātrau hi saṃsmaret ।
paśunaivedya yogena samapyeyaṃ vidhau * * ।।

pratyakṣamagrahīccakramicchayaikaṃ yathāgamaṃ ।
paśyate sādhakendro vai suvīre nābhilaṃkṛtaṃ ।।
(49b)
dṛṣṭvā vāmāṃgamutpādya grāhayecchā nitāṃjaliṃ ।
cakrevaṃ dūtivīrākhyo yajñavyomācaro bhavet ।।

paśyate nṛpalājyena bharūṭī sarvvakāmadā ।
ṛkṣajāśca ṛkṣakarṇṇī paśyate huṃḍacakradhṛk ।।

bhaṭṭājanṛgabhojena paśyate dhanadaṃ mahat ।
phetkārājadṛśāśvityāṃ paśyate kiṃkarorddhagaḥ ।।

laṃbhate vāsimāṃsena vṛśago yena paśyati ।
śivā paśyati kūrmeṇa nakrācakrāntare nṛjaiḥ ।।

viparītaṃ vīranāthā agrebhyamavilaṃbino ।
padaṃ padaṃ vilomyāśu paśyedvīrāṃśubhairavaṃ ।।

athaivamarpayecchaktiṃ paṭasyāgre yathāvidhau ।
puṣpendrantu samuddhṛtya dūtī vīrārddharaṃ japet ।।

yaṣṭvā vrajeta śavarāṃ khānakṣetrasusaṃmukhaṃ ।
sarvvāsveva diśāntāsu varjayetpūrvvapaścimāt ।।

nṛgojamalalāḍena juṣṭvā cakramanuttamaṃ ।
bhrajetihe diśo bhāgī vīreśastu vibhāgaśaḥ ।।

bharūḍhyāgneyamukhare gardabhāstu tathā ca yaḥ ।
brahmarakṣakulaṃ divyaṃ paśyate lampaṭeśvaraṃ ।।

gojena hita - - myāṃ codayo rakṣikarṇṇikāṃ ।
brahmāpaiśācajaṃ vaktraṃ paśyate ca kulāṣṭakaṃ ।।

nirirityagre hi bhaṭṭā prokṣayitvā nṛrājikā ।
paśyate yoginīsāraṃ brahmayakṣakulāṣṭakaṃ ।।

vṛṣaveśyājamāṃsaistu hakārā vāyudiṅmukhaṃ ।
pratīhārakulāḥ sapta yaṣṭvā paśyatyayonijā ।।

evaṃ laṃbāgovṛṣeṇa digudīcyā juṣaṃ naraḥ ।
bhramadvīrakulaṃ divyaṃ paśyate saṃkulaiḥ saha ।।
(50a)
samastevaṃ brīhiphalairaiśānyāṃ ca śiveśvarī ।
yaśca paśyati vidvāṃso bhairavā vīrasaṃkulāḥ ।।

evamevapyanekāni siddhya yastu hyasaṃkhyayā ।
dūtyaśca yogavīryeṇa nūnaṃ prāpnoti sādhakaḥ ।।

goṣṭharājo hyaśvakṛtyo yaṣṭo bhaume hyanekadhā ।
anulomavilomena samastamapi cāśnute ।।
tathāpi vidhipūrvveṇa saṃkṣepeṇa suvistaraṃ ।।

siddhayo dūtikhaḍgeṇa vīrāṇāṃ vīrakalpanā ।
krameṇāmnāyavidhinā mlānodakaviśeṇa hi ।।

astāvatyajya amṛtau kṣetrareṇa supāṃśubhiḥ ।
upalipyaśitaṃ puṣpaṃ liṃgaṃ pūjya nilānanaḥ ।।

hate hāristu dehādvā praharā bāhyasaṃśayaṃ ।
ekāntareva puṣpaivaṃ brīhibhiḥ saptabhirbhavet ।।

tuṣaireva vibhedaṃ vai cālane bahubhiḥ saha ।
pūrṇṇena (mla)cchidyamaistu jaṃbhamohanakīlanaṃ ।।

carma vai lehya tailena dūtī vīrairanukramāt ।
evaṃ krameṇa dūtyaṣṭa(śa)trutyāgādyanukramāt ।।

kurvvaṃ vīre tvatāṃ yāti asidhārā vrajena tu ।
baddhā tu saiṃhinīmudrā vrataśaktisamodayāṃ ।।

modayī śaktiśivena samastaṃ kulamāpnute ।
śrutvā tu śabdo gṛṇyā(vaṃ)jayanto vīramagraṇī ।।

anuvrajeta tāṃ bhūyaḥ pūjayetsvāṃjaliryathā ।
tataḥ samarghayitvā tu mudrāyogena cāvṛṇī ।।

tanmanastu nabhaṃ yāti vīro vīrendratāṃ labhet ।
(50b)
paṃcamudreśvarī mudrā dhāvayaṃ rāvarāvaṇe ।।

sarūpyobhayarāveṇa śaktigrāsāntaparyayī ।
paryaṭe vaṃśaśaktyātmā svaśaktividhipuṣkaraṃ ।।

puṣkaraṃ vāmaveśyaṃ tu daṭṭahāsaṃ hasaṃ japet ।
evaṃ vidhisamāpanno hāyanāntaṃ vratodayī ।।

śaktiveśena śaktyāḍhyo juṣaṃjidbhāvamaṃjuṣaṃ ।
vidyevaṃśaṃ kulākhyā vai dvāsaptati mukhānanāṃ ।।

paśyate sādhakaḥ kṣipraṃ kalaireva samodayaṃ ।
sodayodayamāviśya yogodayanirodayaṃ ।।

prāpyadignādi yā dīkṣā madhyākṣātītapuraḥ * ।
virūḍhāplavatavīro mahāvīrendraviṣṭaraṃ ।।

atastattulyayogena yogī yuktosmi taṃ bhavet ।
evameva anekāni sarvvāmnāyavidhau tathā ।।

samyakproktāṃ hri endreṇa siddhayastu latoccayet ।
tathāpi vicayaṃ yogī kurvvasiddhiṣṭamuttamaṃ ।।

saṃprāptonyāstayoddhṛtya pṛthaktvena yathoditā ।
tathāpyevā viśeṣeṇa dūtīvī * mudvahan ।।

prāpnoti vratanārthena śaktipuṣpāgrayojanāṃ ।
vināśaktiniyogena śaktiyoganiyojanāṃ ।।

vinā labhedvīravaro vīradūtī vratottamaṃ ।
bhājatyakro yathāraśmo vedyā yukto yathānalaḥ ।।

bhājateṣu niśāmadhye vīraśaktyā samaṃ tathā ।
tasmātsarvvaprayatnena samyagāmnāya codanāt ।।
prākśaktaiḥ punayanaṃ vai parato vīrasādhanaṃ ।

śaktestu lakṣaṇaṃ yādṛgya - puṣpodayaṃ tataḥ ।
(51a)
vratacaryādibhedena vacmi saṃkṣepato hite ।।

nīlotpaladalaśyāmā saṃpūrṇṇe dvāranottamā ।
protphulendīvaranayanā nīlaṃ dīrghālakaiḥ śubhā ।।

vaddhaveṇī vetrilīnā muktāphalarajojjvalā ।
navaśīmantaracitā karpūrāgurudhūpitāḥ ।।

dvikhaṃḍacikulāṃ saumyāṃ kuṃkumādhararaṃjitāṃ ।
sunāsā sulalāṭā ca ratnakuṃḍalamaṃḍitā ।।

nānāratnadharā devī nānāratnaviśobhitā ।
divyāmṛtakuṃbhakucā divyaskaṃdhī mahābhujāḥ ।।

kaṃbugrīvāsamaśirā prasannā śaśinānanā ।
mahākeyūrabharaṇā vajramadhyā mṛdutvacā ।।

mahānitaṃbe pravarā bṛhatkuṭimadāṃburā ।
suruṣaṃ ca cakraghanā kaṭisūtravibhūṣitāḥ ।।

sugaṃbhīravaraṇābhivanarhaṃsā vacottamāḥ ।
gūḍhasaṃdhigūḍharomā gūḍhāsthimāṃsapesalā ।।

pṛthuvakṣā yonicakrā gūḍhagulphā mahābalā ।
gūḍhapādāṃgulitalā puṣparatnā surottamā ।।

dharmamukhāstikyamūlā * * śabdaparāyaṇāḥ ।
guruvīrapatirbhaktā devakarmaṇi tatparā ।।

evaṃ sā tu vīrayogā * * * khya mahāguṇā ।
prasastā sarvvakarmeṣu vratacaryā phalātmikā ।।

śuddhābhaktikriyā yuktā anubhāvyena bhāvinā ।
athaiva vīrayogena yuktavyā vapuṣā tayā ।।

yāvanmataguṇā nāsti suvīrasūtraśaktiṣu ।
vīrā sarvvamanastārakṣapyate vīrayoṣitā ।।
(51b)
tathāpyaṣvaguṇā * * cakrayogaviparyaye ।।

raudravīrendraśaktīnāṃ gatā cakrā(dvasa)tphalaṃ ।
nāsti vīrasamaṃ dānaṃ nāsti vīrasamaṃ dhanaṃ ।।

nāsti vīrasamo baṃdha nāsti vīrasamā gatiḥ ।
vīro hi bhairavaḥ sākṣādvīrabhāryā subhairavī ।।

bhairavye bhairavī yuktā samagraṃ viṣatemṛtaṃ ।
bhairavī kāraṇaṃ yatte bhairavena sadocyate ।।

bhairavyā bhairavāvasthā tatte sarvvaṃ vadāmyahaṃ ।
bhairavyānāma mattānāṃ śaktiṇā śaśirottamā ।।

pādādhuttārataḥ pūjyā samadigddhasavāsasā ।
arvvotvitasamācāryamuttarābhimukha - taḥ ।।

samāhninā tricaraṇau śiṣyābhimukhasaṃmukhaḥ ।
* grāhyāṃguṣṭhamaṃguṣṭhau kṣālyakṣīrodakena ca ।।

dhūpaye(nnapa)lājyena tadutvena samālabhet ।
śirasthānādadhasthena biṃdubhrāmyārddhasaṃbhavaṃ ।।

pūjayedrāvaṇendreṇa rāvarāvaṇasaṃcchive ।
śiṣyotpanne ca śiṣyeṇa tadutthena tu vā punaḥ ।।

nivartyo devadevena saśiṣyaṃ śiramāviśat ।
pratyavaśyaśi grāmeṇa bhūyaḥ śaktena pūjayet ।।

avītārṇṇe tadaṣva kāmo brahmastrāmalaśāyakaiḥ ।
śubhanayane caṃdrasūryo mukhegnirjavavakṣakā ।।

dūtyagre caiva (daṅgeṇḍe) akṣarāvaumūnodayet ।
nātyagni vāmavāyu * * adhastānyāṃ ca bhautikaṃ ।।

hastādūrddhamukhādyojyaṃ śravaṇāvaravāriṇau ।
ācchādate svayaṃ viṣṇu saṃśunyaṃ sve svarāhukā ।
(52a)
aṃtyajāti mukhe rūḍhaṃ martyo jāti vijātitā ।।

nābhau vikalpa vidvāṃsau bhairavī bhairavaḥ punaḥ ।
atastrārkṣavirodhena samaste piṃḍavijuṣaṃ ।।

caraṇau caraṇe yuktvā sarvvasarvveṇa baṃdhayet ।
yuktyabījaṃ samuddhṛtya ātmayo śiṣyajothavā ।।

saṃyojya gharghapuṣpeṇa puṣparāśisamarcayet ।
evaṃ purā samabhyarcya dṛḍhīkaraṇabhāvanaiḥ ।।

prāpya puṣpetha bhairavyā ācāryaṃ śirasā viśet ।
utthāyya bhairavī paścādarghapuṣpeṇa cārcayet ।।

puṣpabaṃdhasamabhyarcya balidānena pūjayet ।
śaktilakṣovaddhūtena avadhūte * tojayet ।।

vibhedaye vidīśastu śivohamiti biṃdutaḥ ।
śṛṇutā kṣetramadhivācitvato sādha(kā)vidhau ।।

prabhṛvāsānuvāsesu vibhatvāha śivaḥ svayaṃ ।
tyaktvābhimāsaṃ tvevaṃ cetasāstāvabhāvanā ।।

kṛtvā manasi tuṣṭirvvai śiṣya tu * * kārayet ।
bhairave bhairavaṃ yuktā akṣudhvenāṃtarātmanā ।।

śiṣyasvākṣobhacittena śaktyā puṣpeṇa sātvatā ।
kāmaro(gī) yadā śiṣyai vālisya pratidarśanī ।।

(yā)ni tadā kālamukhaṃ bhavenna preṣakaḥ punaḥ ।
vetābhimānā kṣobhena vīro varttati vai yadā ।।

tadā sasiddhimāpnoti dūtī vīrendratāṃ labhet ।
caryāgre yadi mātā syāt pūjyā yāvānna cāśiṣu ।।
bhaginī gurubhāryā vai tathāntevāsi yositā ।।

samyagyuktā śaktimune śālāvāciguṇaśubhā ।
(52b)
tasyādhiluptadhiyayā aviluptapadaṃ labhet ।।

mahābhairavamabhyarcya bhairavī bhairavapriyā ।
utsaṃgaṅkaṃ samāropya dampatīkastu bhāvanā ।।

bhāvaye tatsvabhāvena vibhāvye nā tu bhāvanā ।
ekatā bhavatā yena sādhyameva vidāṣasāṃ ।।

samādhuryastradhocāraṃ yāvacchoṇitamāvahaṃ ।
parāmṛtaṃ tataḥ pītvā dampatī khecaro bhavet ।।

kurvvanneva striyā yoga maṃtriṇo vidya śāśinī ।
savīraṃ ca dhvaje vyoma * chriyā bhairavodayaṃ ।।

ubhayatyevamamṛtī caratī vīracānniśi ।
ubhayāgamayogena vīro vīratvatāṃ labhet ।।

tatvatasvasidhārākhyo vīro * vīdhvakalpanā ।
asevayaṃ vyaktiyogā kalpakalpakakalpanāṃ ।।

bhavate hyamṛtaṃ vīro varaśaktiparāyaṇaḥ ।
modayantaṃ paraprāptaṃ punarvvīrotihodayī ।।

paṭorgho śaktipuṣpevaṃ samadigvāsasā śaśī ।
asidhārā vratāsīnaḥ piṃḍabhedānṛtaṃ bhavet ।।

śaktilagno kṛtaṃ nyāso bāhyaśālā samudbhavān ।
paścādūtyāṃ kuṇḍapuṭe yuktā sallakṣaṇo paśoḥ ।।

svaśālā kaṃdharā madhye kālamnāyīsu gūhitaḥ ।
tadromaṃ cakṣuṣaṃ skaṃdha grathitvā muṃḍasaṃpuṭaḥ ।।

tathārghadhūpakusumaiḥ saṃpuṭaṃ muṇḍamarghayet ।
japatossaṃgagāśaktaṃ trisaṃdhyaṃ saptavāsaraṃ ।।

dhūpadīpe avyucchinne saṃdhyāyāti tṛtīyayā ।
udvyādya labhate padmaṃ devāsurabhayapradaṃ ।
(53a)
susaṃskṛtaṃ punaḥ śaktyā vīrohati parottare ।।

datvā bhāgaṃ paraṃ bhuktvā vyomaṃ yānti padaṃ yatī ।
kena cidvābhyupāyena neyātsapaśupuṣkarān ।।

bhūyosidhārāyogena āharedbhuvanatrayaṃ ।
svayaṃ snātvā kalā yena di(gddhā)sau puṃḍasaṃpuṭe ।।

paśureṇa na saṃsthāyya rajvā pūrvveva baṃdhayet ।
vidyasā śikhapūrvveṇa liṃgoliṃgamadūṣayaṃ ।।

pratisaptāha mānena saptāsaptāha saptake ।
pūrvvavadvidhadhīyeta vidhisītkāramuccakaiḥ ।।

utsaṃ na janayogena prakṛtyāṃ kāravaikṛtiḥ ।
agnikarmasavidvaktrā grāsaṃ grāsānna sādṛśī ।।

bhūyaḥ sa vīranātheśo saptasaptāha paryayet ।
labhate kamalaṃ divyaṃ yadi syātsarvvakilviṣī ।।

saṃbhuktvā saptakarmāṇi ūrddhatvā cāṣṭamajūjāḥ ।
vīreṣvarapathe vīro bhuktvā yātyapunardbhavaṃ ।।

uta vā śaktiyogena kevalena vratena hi ।
pāraṃparyedamṛtīśe tārayaṃti taranti ca ।।

prākṛto vaikṛto vāpi vaikṛtyā prākṛto janaḥ ।
sadojasī cidvyomātmā na vrajedvikramakramāt ।।

uta vā trimathāpannaḥ tridhā madhyo mayaṃ payaṃ ।
caturatve catuḥ prāpya niśreṇyātītapuṣkaraṃ ।।

juṣaṃ samastaparyāyamaviluptadhiyodayaṃ ।
sodayaṃ paramaṃ yāti sallīnaśca nirodhayet ।।

nirvvāpaye catuḥ prāpyaṃ lokālokodayaṃ payaṃ ।
kuṣuṇmātrā sahasreṇa mārute sūlaparyayaṃ ।।
(53b)
adhvavyome vītarālā kramavikramasaṃbhrame ।
śabdādipaṃcakaṃ tyaktvā saṃyātonyamasodayaṃ ।।

catuṣkalodhvavācāhva rāvayogena bhāvaśaḥ ।
caturddhā kṛtyarāmaistu nibaddhorddhakaja(lpi)taiḥ ।।

tarjanyastu grahaṃ grāhī saṃnyāsī grāhaśaktiyuk ।
grāsaśaktisamācāra icchārūpāṇi vibhrate ।।

evaṃ pūrvvājane mukho jagaccandratimaṇḍalaṃ ।
siddhapatramanekādya māharati mataṃ yataḥ ।।

vidhāne vidhino baṃdhamulliyadvakṣakottare ।
sthālīpākaistu romaistu niravadyā naśā viśaṃ ।।

yadabhīṣṭa yathā yo vai yogā ātmīyadarśanaṃ ।
svaveśmamānayatyevaṃ hastāddhastane presyayaṃ ।।

kṛtvā vā sugṛhayāgaṃ yāgojakaraṇaṃ laghuṃ ।
śaktipuṣpaiḥ paṭhaṃ pūjya prāśayitye narottamaṃ ।।

sacakremyenkṣititalatyageṃdrodvālake gṛhaṃ ।
sā evaṃ grasate devi suramuṃḍābhravistaraṃ ।।

rasadravyamanekātmā mānaye svagṛhe bahūn ।
punarnnayati yatrecchaṃ icchayā krīḍayanmuniḥ ।।

trisānaḥ kākapa(kṣākti) veṣṭayitvāritā mukhaḥ ।
sādhyagaṃ kalpayitvā tu devatā ca samuddiśa ।।

jaṣaṃ niśānte tadvaktrā tatpaśya krodhavigrahaḥ ।
catuḥṣaṣṭhamaparyāye yamicchasi tadā havet ।।

pakṣairhi khaṃjirīṭhasya sā evaṃ yā ca sammukhaḥ ।
grasechaktipadāṃgeva samastaṃ gahanairhanet ।।

kauśikena vidhūnāṃ ca kṣīrādi pravikāriṇāṃ ।
samānayati aṃgāṅgā grāme grāmethavā samaṃ ।।
(54a)
paśuśca tejo viguṇā madhyeṣāṃ ca catuṣpathaṃ ।
icchāṃśapāniharata cātha bhākaṃḍakākiṇi ।।

ugraśaktivikāśena rāvarāvaṇakalpanā ।
dīpayaṃ śaktirājena śaktonā hi virāvayaṃ ।।

japaṃ sāgnistu vīrendraḥ ṣaṇmāsādvadanastrayaṃ ।
sarvvārthaistu bhavedveṣyo saśriyonidhibhiḥ saha ।।

uttarottaratuṣyo me ebhiḥ pūrvvakalodaye ।
arddhamanye tathāturye cakraṃ prāpya vidhūnvite ।।

grāhyagrāhakayogena * * * * ca sacchive ।
* dālayaṃ ca vinmadhye bhramayaratripuṣkaraṃ ।।

athottarāṇyāpāhṛtya sādhyāsādhyātsasaṃkhyayaḥ ।
gamāgamāniyogena svaveśmānteṣu puṣpati ।।

tata evaṃ nāvadehe samastaṃ ca kalodaye ।
maharyā padye homena śrīniketaphalaiḥ saha ।।

japellakṣatrayaṃ vīro vīracaryāvidhau sthitaḥ ।
bhavedvācaspatiḥ sākṣācchaṇmāsenātha yaṃtritaḥ ।।

khe saṃpūjya sacitpūrṇṇaḥ sātītaturyaviṣṭare ।
madhye pārthivavaśmeṣu svarūpeṇa svarūpataḥ ।।

pāśāṃkuśavidheyātmā agraṇī hyagranādhikā ।
antareṇa paśuryāto sauṣṭamūle suṣumnayā ।।

ātmāpramāṇayogena yujanā hyagrajāṣu muk ।
samastajīvamākarṣe sāṃgike bhayathe kvayā ।।

mātari svānilaṃ madhye kātyedaṃṣāvavopamaṃ ।
sādhyanābhau samedrāṃhre hṛnmūrddhiṇi kharāntare ।।
(54b)
smarantu bhayamaṃtreṇa rathakuṃbhabhramākṛtī ।
samānayatyatha krodhā ṣaṭketha paśubhiḥ saha ।।

pranyagāgrasaṃdhyagreṣu samasteṣu vikāriṣu ।।
athaivaṃ pāvakākāraṃ mātari svāṃbusādṛśaṃ ।।

vyāyamāharate vīraḥ sarvve bāhyaṃgajātayaḥ ।
vikārakṣayakṣayāti surasubhiḥ sa viyujyate ।।

samastavarge saptādyā vidhivadrāvaṇākṛtau ।
yuktāni viṣṭarorddhe ca kanyayetpiṃḍakalpanā ।।

samastārthi ca * kṣetre saturyātītasaptake ।
sabiṃdūlaṃ devatātmā bhrāmaya sa tripujvarāt ।।

rathabhramasamāddhekai visargānto hi yā tataḥ ।
tacca śūnye visargānte astre  drodrognisādṛśaḥ ।।

nivartya saptapiṃḍeṣu madhogre vīranāyakaṃ ।
japetaṃ kumbhakāpanno yathāśaktyājñasaṃjuṣaṃ ।।

nyasetatkāla (kāṣṭhavaṃ) sapuṣparasasasphale ।
dyaṃgāsthibhiḥ samāveṣṭya tarjanyalūkhalottare ।।

punarevāpānagaṃ te adhaśaktyāṃkuśena tu ।
badhvā tu kṛṣṇapāśena dhyaṃgavastrī (sva)kalpanā ।।

sa paśursantrapisthenaṃ ghaṭṭaye pṛṣṭhapārthivaiḥ ।
(tatodya)vasyai * lena savajrena ca ghaṭṭayet ।।

yāgabhramodare hyevaṃ mudrayitvā * * ttama ।
ekataḥ samarasthe - - - - hyekataḥ śivaṃ ।।

dhyātvaivaṃ ca punaḥ śaktyā nāsāgrāsastu codanāt ।
koṣṭhehe preśayitvevaṃ lakṣaṇātpiṃḍamāhavet ।।

tridhā turyamatītārddhe dhvanividdhā śikhāṃ prati ।
(55a)
purayitvā kāṣṭapaśurvvikāraiḥ paśusādṛśaiḥ ।।

upabhuṃjacchavṛśanaṃ nānārasavikāribhiḥ ।
vīrendrādi mahāvīrai rathatarkyāgraṇījuṣaṃ ।।

athaiva kāṣṭhapiṇḍābhyāṃ paśubhyāmṛtamāharet ।
saṃvyāmṛtatvamāpnoti kalā (brahmā)śivaṃ vrajet ।।

evaṃ siddhisahasrāṇi sarvvāmnāyāhripodayaṃ ।
dṛṣṭvā vīrāgraṇīśāsa saṃkhyādhvahalamaśnute ।।

- - - dūtī vīrākhye samastaśaktikaṃdale ।
pravikīrṇṇā caraṇo dve vidhivatkalpakalpanā ।।

jyeṣṭhānaṃdagṛhe nityaṃ samyagevaṃ pratiṣṭhayet ।
jñānādivahano vīro gṛhasthopi samaśnute ।।

lokasiddhi sahasrāṇi divyasiddhi śatāni ca ।
caturddhā tu mahākṛṣṭistripūrvveṇa vidhānataḥ ।।

labhate satataṃ vīro dehaveśamataḥ paraṃ ।
icchayāmṛtamaśnanto kaleyātmatvasatphalaṃ ।।

jyeṣṭhā kṣetre(ṣvṛ)ṇī vaktraṃ praviṣṭapyatha yoditaṃ ।
samādhau tatparasthā tu avatārā vadhūtakī ।।

sajuṣṭvātītasagare viṣamodaramaṃḍale ।
yathā kāmasamādheyā tathā bāhye pavitrake ।।

dhyaṃgajaiḥ puṣpasahitaiḥ tacchaṃkūdiśigraṃthayet ।
tattai ladhūpayogena piṃḍakaulikasaptakaṃ ।।

japamānastvānayane lokālokāntare hi yat ।
rakṣamāṇo paraḥ saṃghaiḥ svagṛhe sthāpito yathā ।।

dhātumūlaṃ jīvayoni * * * * * yoṣitaṃ ।
samāharati āḍhopā pakṣeśo yadvapannagaṃ ।
(55b)
naṃdikṣetre yathaivaṃ vai pūrvvaprākārakalpanā ।।

codayantaḥ kaulikendraḥ samatīte nirocayaṃ ।
grahagrāhakabhedena vidhivadbāhyabhāvanā ।।

maṃtrikālaśilāśnaṃto icchayā svamukhaṃ vrajet ।
vānaprastho gṛhasthaśca śaktiyogena khecarān ।।

bhavatyeva śaktiśaktyāmaiṣṭikā brahmacāriṇaḥ ।
yathoktalakṣaṇaiḥ pūrṇṇā śaktitve śaktikalpayet ।।

mukhyā bhaktyubhacāreṇa savīrā vrajate nabhaṃ ।
evamāmnāyayogena na kvaciccaiva kalpanā ।।

naro vīratvamāpnoti śaktirvvā nirguṇīkṛtā ।
atorddhā samavaikalpā nāmnāyāṃ hṛpadaiśikā ।।

prāpyanujñāṃ gurumukhā naiṣṭhikaṃ naiṣṭhikaṃ vrajet ।
mahākaṃkālavarṣeṇa asidhārā vrajena ca ।।

parṇṇaśālāṃ parvvatorthe kṛtvā vai nadisaṃgame ।
yayārūḍhaphalaṃ vīro gṛhasthopi sama(jute) ।।

ataḥ śālāntare pure rājiteta ghṛtena ca ।
ārādhya pratimāṃ divyāṃ kārayellakṣalakṣaṇāṃ ।।

āharedvai pūrṇṇareṇuṃ niśānte muṇḍasaṃpuṭe ।
tasminpūrvve ca padmāni pavitrāmṛtavāriṇā ।।

atha chinna mahādīpe dhūpe vai mūrttipādayoḥ ।
evameva hi naivedyaṃ pṛtha(kyā)trā mṛtoddhatā ।।

tathā vaktrāmvarā puṣpairbhūtaye pretakiṃkarāḥ ।
pratiṣṭhayeṣṭhadiśāsu pāśāṃkuśamahāyudhīḥ ।।

niśāgresminnahorātre kṛtvā samyaktripuṣkaraiḥ ।
(56a)
rāvarāvaṇarāveṇa rāvayaṃto diśāṃ vrajet ।।

kulasaptakamuddiśya agnittṛgnāgnakalpayet ।
japaṃtoccairhasantopi aṭṭahāsaḥ sarāvaṇaṃ ।।

tathā mahāphalośnato tatpuṣpakṛtaśekharāḥ ।
tathaivanādisragdāmī - -  (dbhaṃ)gavalāvayaṃ ।।

hasaṃto hyevahāsena bhūtagrāmaṃ vihāyasaṃ ।
śaktyuddhṛta punargrāsā hasegrāsena rāvaṇaḥ ।।

punaruccairyajapo * kaulamiśrapunaḥ pṛthak ।
śaktirāvaṇasaṃgrāsaṃ grāsahāsanahāsayaṃ ।।

praveśataḥ punaḥ śālā vaiśroṇadakṣiṇāmukha ।
sukhāsīnonyakṛtyorddhe prāguttaradiśānanaṃ ।।

samatītānatītātu uktamuttaratottaraṃ ।
sakālaḥ pūrṇṇimādhāro grāmārddhe biṃduviṣṭaraṃ ।।

recayeta (vinadina) cintitaḥ sabidurbhavaṃ ।
bhavatyevaṃ śūnyaśūnyaṃ niśāsācanasīdane ।।

aṭata ca vyomacakre cakre puṣkaravādinaṃ ।
sarvvevaṃ vikṛtyabjena prākṛtena samuddharet ।।

divyabaṃdhyā na japyātmā varttate hyagnicaryayā ।
tathā vibhitra naivedyaḥ kriyākaraṇakalpanā ।।

rātricaryāṃ tathā yādṛk bhaktāmnāyakakalpanā ।
tathā rāvāvahā sārddho vidagddho dhyānasaṃmukhī ।
ya bhujaḥ prathamaṃ padmaṃ śudhyastaṃ śubhajīvitaṃ ।।

anākāṃkṣalabheddivyaṃ svargādvaśaguṇaṃ bilaṃ ।
atha bhuktvā mahāpadmaṃ dvitīyaḥ sā yakṣoditaṃ ।।

vasaṃtasya layaṃ yānti kṣetrapālamahābalāḥ ।।
(56b)
bhuktādvaitatṛtīyaṃ vai padmaḥ padmavarottamaṃ ।।

yakṣarākṣasabhūtāni layaṃ yāṃtyagraṇīśvarī ।
yaccaturthastu padmaṃ vai bhuktā sa(syagya)thoditaṃ ।।

preśakātsādhakendrasthā savai yānti ca saṃkramāt ।
paṃcamo na hi padmena vidhṛte vidhikalpanā ।।

siddhayogagataṃ divyaṃ sādhakasya vaśaṃ vrajet ।
padme ṣaṣṭhe vibhuktyā vai rāvaśabdena preṣake ।।

vīrendrasya layaṃ yānti * * * sarvvadevatāḥ ।
arthottarebjendredre vai uddhṛte saptakaulike ।।

sādhakasyāgramāyānti samastaṃ saptakaulike ।
sārameva tathā gṛhye bhairavaśca pravaṃdite ।।

astrabhrāmasahastena mahāvīravalocchṛtā ।
teṣāmalpaṃ yathāpūrvvaṃ svakotthena anukramāt ।।

dātavyaṃ kulapūrvvāṇāṃ samastānāṃ pṛthakpṛthak ।
anantaraṃ parīcāre pratīhārāḥ sakiṃkarāḥ ।।

kularakṣā dūticakre nirgamena kulākule ।
dattarghe gaṇanāprāya cakrāṇāṃ vīranāyakaḥ ।।

cakrādhipatyamāpnoti bhuktvā pālaṃ śivaṃ vrajet ।
ityevaṃ dūtiṣaṭkasya saṃkṣepeṇa sureśvari ।।

yathā teṣāntataḥ kalpaṃ samastaphalasatphalaṃ ।
anena jñātamātreṇa adhītena yathā punaḥ ।।

apradṛśyo bhavenmaṃtro vīrāṇāṃ jāyate * le ।
ekāntaśīlayogasya nityaṃ śīlakriyāpare ।।

tasyevamupadeṣṭavyā saphalastasya siddhayaḥ ।
evaṃ śabdāraṇī devī tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।
(57a)
kālikā rāviṇī samyak adhunā kathayāmi kiṃ ।।।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiracchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
dūtīcakraṃ śabdakālividhiḥ ।। ।।

evaṃ śrutvā bhagavatī haravaktrāṃbujādbhutaṃ ।
prāha prasaṃnagaṃbhīraṃ punareva śivaṃ vacaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

tṛptāsmi bhagavansamyagjñātaṃ tatparamaṃ padaṃ ।
mayā tava prasādena sarvveśvaramaheśvara ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi kaṃkālākhyaṃ kulārṇṇavaṃ ।
yatra sā kālikā devī vidakkocchā vijṛṃbhate ।।

sekharādhasthitā yā sā raśmipuṃjavirājitā ।
etasmin yā * * kramaṃ bāhyābhyantarabhedataḥ ।।

etadicchāmi bhagavan jñātuṃ suravarārcitāṃ ।
evamākarṇya devendraḥ prāṇya paṃcāmṛtaṃ kulaṃ ।।
cidakāṃ śekharīkṛtya uvāca paramaṃ mahat ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi rahasyamidamuttamaṃ ।
muṣṭibhūtaṃ mahākaulaṃ khecareva sthitaṃ hata ।।

avikalpannasaṃdigddhaṃ prapūrṇṇamubhayojjhitaṃ ।
cidarkkaṃ śekharī bhūtaṃ tasyāpi pratidīpikā ।।

kālikā sā pradīpā ca sadākāli pradīpikā ।
tatrāntare tatra gatā tantresmintatravatsadā ।।
saravirbhāsurādhārastadādhārā hi kālikā ।।

ṣaḍāre vipulādhāraḥ ṣoḍaśodyotasannibhā ।
sphuradvamanasaṃ grāsarāvikī sṛṣṭikārikā ।
(57b)
saravirdevatākāraravireva tadā kṛtiṃ ।।

ravi pradīpako loke sūryamadhyātsamutthitaḥ ।
ravirantargatobhānurbhāsayaṃtyakhilaṃ jagat ।।

bhānavī kaulinī yā sā tatpuṃjabharitaṃ jagat ।
tatrotpannā mahāmaṃtrā bhairavāṣṭāṣṭayonayaḥ ।।

na prakāśena cākāśe (nobha)yo no bhayojjhite ।
sarvvāvaraṇanirmukte sarvvabhogātibhāskaraḥ ।।

āvṛtaṃ prāvṛtaṃ yena racitaṃ ca kulākulaṃ ।
saraviḥ sūryaturyānte bhrājate raudraḍāmare ।।

svasaṃcitya ramātitya nityoditamarīcibhiḥ ।
bhacakraṃ bhāsitaṃ yena sa vai kālaṃ jarā bhavet ।।

bāhyamābhyantaraṃ kṛtvā mantraṃ cābhyantaraṃ bahiḥ ।
advaitasantāmantrajño maṃtrī maṃtrodayaṃ labhet ।।

viviktārthaparispaṃdanaṃdanodyānakaṃdarān ।
prāpyate paramānandaṃ nispaṃdamubhayojjhitaṃ ।।

jñānaṃ jñeyaṃ tathā jñātā jñaptigarbhā nibhālayaṃ ।
layaṃ yāti paradhāmni nirahaṃkāradharmiṇi ।।

cidānaṃdaparispaṃdagarbhasthaspaṃdarūpayā ।
vyāptā paramayā bhāti ṣaḍadhvāvaraṇāṃtige ।।

satvasatvāntarā(metyaṣṭāṅgulaṃ) babhave nave ।
saṃyogo - - saṃsāramūle nirmathanakṣamaḥ ।।

ātmamadhye khamādāya ātmā caiva khamadhyataḥ ।
khātmanorekadhā yogā na kiṃcidavaśiṣyate ।।

antastejo vahistejaḥ sarvvābhāso nirāmayaḥ ।
(58a)
sāmayaṃ kāyagahanaṃ madhyamaṃ karparaṃ tyajet ।।

khacaṃdracaṃdrikā yena ciccakre carcitāciraṃ ।
mokṣalakṣmyāspandanante kṛtamātmā namotmanā ।।

caṃdreśaścandradhāmnisthastasthastatkālikojjhitaḥ ।
kālikāntaṃ kulāśakto * niḥkalo vijṛmbhate ।।

śubhau suvigatā bhāti tadbhātyā pūraye jagat ।
śuciragni(pretā sā)tmā tadā kālikuleśvaraḥ ।।

yadā paṃcātmake pañca raśmayo bhāsurāntarā ।
bhavanti paṃcadhāmātma tadā paṃcārthapāragaḥ ।।

yadā loke mūlakule lakṣamāneṣu sadmani ।
lakṣmicakraṃ vikāśitvaṃ tadā rāvasthitaḥ * * ।।

yadā dvādaśarāśistho raśmipuṃjaparodvamet ।
tāliko layate samyak tadā kulapatirbhavet ।।

bhavatyeva vinirmukta bhavabhāvāntaraśmimuk ।
mayā vipāṭalavyagrastadā kulapatirbhavet ।।

citsvecaratastvasthaḥ khātmākhakhamakhaṃḍitaḥ ।
tatra raśmikulākrāmī tadā (vene) bhāgbhavet ।।

dṛgadaśīṃ dṛganarttānto dṛśyadṛṣṭo virājate ।
na dṛdgādṛdgavā draṣṭā tadā raśmi kuleśvaraḥ ।।

abhomebhāgasaṃbhogī bhogabhogāgragddhahyaśaḥ ।
bhogapiṃḍāntako piṇḍī tathā kālikramāgraṇī ।।

śubhā sālā sa te bhāsyaṃ bhāsyaṃ bhāsati tadgataṃ ।
yasya bhāsyaṃ navābhāsā raśminātho hyasau smṛtaḥ ।।

maṃdiraṃ maṃdire līno maṃdiro maṃdirāntagaḥ ।
(58b)
vṛttidhāmavikāsātmā ravireva vijṛmbhate ।।

dvādaśātmā dvādaśastho dvādaśāntāntabhāsakaḥ ।
mantraraśmikulākāmī kālikā cakranāyakaḥ ।।

raśmibhedaprabhedena te sphuranti sphuraprabhāḥ ।
teṣāmantaḥ parādīptiḥ kālayantī virājate ।।

tatkramaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi mukhapāraṃparāgataṃ ।
pronmiṣadraśmikhacitau jṛmbhatyeva daśatrikaṃ ।।

sṛṣṭikālīya saṃhārī sṛṣṭāyā parameśvarī ।
sthitikālī tato ghorā tataḥ saṃhārakālikā ।।

raktakālī carvvayantī raktāgamavibhedataḥ ।
sukālī yamakālī ca mṛtyukālī bhayāvahā ।।

bhadrakālī tathā cānyā paramādityakālikā ।
mārttaṇḍakālī kālāgni rudrakālī maholvvaṇā ।।

mahākālakule kālī mahābhairavakālikā ।
trayodaśavidhā kālī vijñeyā raśmibhedataḥ ।।

paramārkaprabhāśāntasphuratsmaraṇadīpitāḥ ।
bhārūpā paramākālī kālayantī jagat sthitā ।।

ekadhā triprakārā sā tridhā sā turyagā smṛtā ।
paṃcadhā caiva ṣoḍhā ca saptāṣṭanavadhā smṛtā ।।

daśaikādaśadhā devi tathā dvādaśadhā priye ।
trayodaśa vibhedena grasate sacarācaraṃ ।।

te naiṣā kālikā jñeyā tasyā mantraṃ śṛṇuṣva me ।
yanna kūṭeśvarī cakre piṇḍamuktaṃ mayā tava ।।

tamuddhṛtya prayatnena punarnāma hyathoddharet ।
padāntaṃ parameśāni punaranyaṃ śṛṇu priye ।।
(59a)
mahātmānaṃ nādayutaṃ mahābiṃdusamanvitaṃ ।
kuṃḍalaṃ dāruṇākrāntaṃ biṃdunādasamanvitaṃ ।।

punarbindutrikoṇaṃ ca kuṇḍalaṃ dhāmasaṃsthitaṃ ।
nāmapādasamāyuktaṃ mantraṃ sarvvāsu kīrttitaṃ ।।

pūjyasiddhāṃstathā pīṭhāṃ rāśidvādaśasaṃgataṃ ।
saṃlikhyamaṇḍalaṃ devi mahādvādaśasammitaṃ ।।

madhye trayodaśaṃ kāryaṃ rāśivarṇṇasamanvitaṃ ।
raktena rajasā devi caturlekhyaṃ prapūrayet ।।

caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ tatra pūjyaṃ kramottamaṃ ।
mahākālī madhyamāsyā * dhyāghoramaholvaṇā ।।

sphuratkālāgnilakṣāṇi romakūpe pataṃti hi ।
yasyā garbhaśikhākoṭi * * koṭi vijṛmbhate ।।

jvaladvakadhakāra * rāviṇī bhāsurānanāṃ ।
vakrāvaviniṣṭhyūta * sphuratkalpāntapāvakaṃ ।।

gaṃbhīradvānahuṃkāratrāsito ccaṃḍamaṇḍalaṃ ।
anantādiphaṇīndrendragrāsaghasmarabhairavāṃ ।।

aparṇṇalakṣaparṇṇādya muṃḍau pīḍavirājitā ।
sarvvādhvapatikaṃkālamekhalā dhāmamaṃḍitāṃ ।।

kālarātrī śataṃ skaṃdha vasitāṃ bhairavekṣaṇāṃ ।
vidyudulkāsahasraughakṣepasaṃkāśalocanāṃ ।।

asakṛtpīḍabrahmāṇḍamaṇḍapāṃ bhṛkuṭīmukhāṃ ।
kākālikulakṛṣṇābhrā nirasatkekarekṣaṇāṃ ।।

calaśirohastaghaṭṭi * * tādhvapaṭīsvarāṃ ।
mahātaraṇimudraughakeśurīkṛtakurparā ।।

sphuradūrddhamahānetravahnijvalitavigrahāṃ ।
(59b)
dordaṇḍamuṇḍasaṃghaṭṭa sphuranmāyāṃḍagahvarāṃ ।।

uttasīkṛtasādākhyāṃ śatakaṃkālamālinīṃ ।
aśeṣadevatā * * cakramākramaghasmarāṃ ।।

nipatītatva(trā)vārakṛtārabhaṭibhairavāṃ ।
viśvakṣayakaroccaṇḍabhūkṣepodbhāsitānanāṃ ।।

sahasakālamuṃḍaughakṛtatripurabhairavāṃ ।
kṛtāntaśatakaṃkālakiṃkiṇījālamaṃḍitāṃ ।।

mārttaṇḍakaṃdalāpīṭhakṛtakaṃṭhasragulvaṇāṃ ।
kālāgnirudramuṃḍaughamekhalādāmamaṃḍitāṃ ।।

rudrarājeśvarākrāntakṛtanaṃdvanitaṃvakaṃ ।
nārāyaṇasphuraddeha * * pretamālāriṇīṃ ।।

brahmakaṃkālasadvīṇā vādanaikavinoditāṃ ।
aśeṣadevatācakraṃ kramākramaṇaghasmarāṃ ।।

nipītatvagamādhāna bhairaveśānabhāsakāṃ ।
aśeṣabhairaveśānadehakarṇṇāvataṃsakāṃ ।।

kṣapakṣaṇo sphuṭādvāmaṃ nṛttacitravinodinīṃ ।
dhyātvā svadhāmni vitato pūjanīyātha maṃḍale ।।

tadvadevya prapūjyevaṃ dhyeyāḥ sarvvā yathārthavat ।
pratibhedagatāsaikā devadevī karaṃkiṇī ।।

mahāpaśūpahārādyairjalajaiḥ sthalajaistathā ।
khecaraiścāpi deveśi devadevī prapūjayet ।।

mahāśaṃkhārghapātrādau kṛtvāghamalinā mahat ।
tena saṃprokṣayedādau paśughoreṇa cetasā ।।

paścādghorāṃ samālaṃvya vṛttiṃ paramabhairavāṃ ।
dvādaśārkodite mārge rūḍho maṃtreśvarātmakaḥ ।।

vakrodvamanakātpātapātātpātapate paśūn ।
(60a)
anyaghāpaśughātīsyādanena rahitaṃ yadā ।।

nivedya sahasenaiva dhunedvāyaṃtate punaḥ ।
tadā vidārya khaḍgena tīkṣṇaśastreṇa vā priye ।।

chitvāśi * * pādaṃ vau saraktāṃ vinivedayet ।
padmaṃ dakṣiṇamārāṃ ca dikṣuraktāṃ chaṭā kuru ।।

kardamaṃ kārayet tatra rudhireṇaiva bhairavi ।
nivedya vividhān bhakṣān balimantraprakalpayet ।।

ākaṃṭhataḥ pivedraktaṃ madyaṃ vāpi maheśvari ।
tatastu vaktrakusumai sphurjadarceta kālikāṃ ।।

mudrā karaṃkiṇīṃ ghorāṃ bhairavīṃ vāṃ pradarśayet ।
lelihānāṃ mahāghorāṃ atha devi prabaṃdhayet ।।

evamabhyarcayeccakraṃ yathā vibhavavistaraiḥ ।
tataḥ praveśayecchiṣyaṃ prokṣitaṃ cāvalokitaṃ ।।

bahirṣya ca nikāyānaṃ sthāpyāsmai tāḍanaṃ kuru ।
carukaṃ daṇḍakāṣṭhaṃ ca vīrapānaṃ samarpayet ।।

karayoḥ kṣobhayogena śaktiṃ preṃkhāṃ samāśritaḥ ।
sabhāvāntaprayogena sphuradunnāsya yogataḥ ।
karāveśaṃ prajāyeta tadāsau samayī bhavet ।।

paścāt praveśayeddevyā paśumeva kṛtārthavat ।
daṇḍavadvaṃdanaṃ kāryaṃ tena hṛṣṭena cetasā ।।

paścādullāpya pūjājya kramamenaṃ bhunaktavat ।
saśodhaye tatacchiṣyaṃ pūrvvaśaktiprayogavat ।।

kṛśāṃ vṛttiṃ samāsṛtya kṛśamevaṃ vilokayet ।
punaḥ pūrvvatarāṃ vṛtti avalaṃvyeva sādhakaḥ ।।
(60b)
pūrṇṇarūpaṃ samālokya kṛśamevaṃ vilokayet ।
punaḥ pūrṇṇataraṃ vṛttiṃ avalaṃbyeva sādhakaḥ ।।

pūrṇṇarūpaṃ samālokya mahākāliṃ kulākulaṃ ।
pañcabhitvādha paṃcaiva kāyavākcittabhedataḥ ।।

tadā kācaistathākārastadrūḍhyāstadvilāpakāḥ ।
evaṃ saṃbodhyataṃ śiṣyaṃ siddhaṃ bhavati bhāmini ।।

cidaikyāpūrṇṇadevatvamānandaṃ tatsvarūpataḥ ।
tadā vidvastu jānīyānnānyathonmādavat priye ।।

evaṃ tadīkṣiṇaṃ kuryādācāryo kramapāragaḥ ।
yasyevaṃ dīkṣayetsamyaksamukto bhavabaṃdhanāt ।।

anyathā devadeveśi tilājyānalayogataḥ ।
purāktāṃ vṛttimālaṃvya yasya yasyāvalokayet ।।

taṃ taṃ nayati sāyojya vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
anukrame yadā śaktaḥ kuryādvācyavahārakaṃ ।।

yasya yasya sa daivaṃ hi taṃ nayadbrahmaśāśvataṃ ।
atrārūḍhasparśayeta yaṃ yaṃ vā prītacetasā ।।

taṃ muktaṃ nātra saṃdehaḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ gaṇāmbike ।
evaṃ vidhiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā paścāddaivakramaṃ punaḥ ।।

yajeta parameśāni paścāttasyārpyayetmahat ।
kalaśaṃ cārghapātraṃ vā pūrvvoktakramayogataḥ ।।

tenābhiṣecyataṃ devi kramamasmai prakāśayet ।
etadabhyāsayogena ṣaṇmāsātsiddhimeṣyati ।।

devadevīśvarī yatra tatra yāti na saṃśayaḥ ।
bahunātra kimuktena vākprapaṃcena suṃdari ।।

ataḥ parataraṃ nāsti tathyametadbravīmi te ।
evaṃ prāpya mahābhāge kālikramavinirṇṇayaṃ ।।
(61a)
kena cicchubhapuṇyena janmanā paścimena tu ।
sadaivābhyasanīyaṃ hi abhyāsātsvagatirbhavet ।।

vācayā pūrvvasiddhiśca sādhayatyeva nānyathā ।
evaṃ rahasyamākhyātaṃ suguptaṃ kālikākramaṃ ।।

kathanīyaṃ na caiveha śiṣye maṃdaparīkṣite ।
anājñayā kathedyo hi samahāpātakī bhavet ।।

paśyate narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvadāhūtasaṃplavaṃ ।
na kadācidasau mucye narakādrauravādiha ।।

eva sthiti tamāsthāya varttitavyaṃ tadā naraiḥ ।।

iti nigaditametattantrasāraṃ yathāva-
ttava sakalamudāraṃ sarvvasaṃsiddhimuktaṃ ।
kimaparamadhunā te vacmi sadvīravaṃdye
vada mama hṛdayāntarjyotibhūte samagraṃ ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
kālikramavidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

evamākarṇya girijā romāṃcitaśarīriṇī ।
praṇamya paranātheśaṃ punaḥ punaruvāca ha ।।

stotrametadanudvignā svānaṃdabharitā satī ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

namaste parameśāya parātparatarāya ca ।
amoghā yāvyayā yāstu parahaṃsātmane namaḥ ।।

sadasatpakṣamuktāya guṇātītāya saṃbhave ।
śaśāṅkāṅkitaśīrṣāya kāntādehārddhadhāriṇe ।।

dvādaśārkapadasthāya mahākālāgnitejase ।
mahākālikulasthāya kālātītāya te namaḥ ।।
(61b)
sūryagrāsaikarūpāya citkalāntaracāriṇe ।
kālāgnidhāmagarbhāya jagadgrāsaikagṛhnave ।।

akālapadasaṃsthāya sphuratpadavikāsine ।
puryadhāmāntarasthāya mīnāvarttāya te namaḥ ।।

naraśaktiśivāntāya mahābhairavamūrttaye ।
sarvvā hārāya mūrttāya sarvvaraśmyantagāya ca ।।

namaḥ kamalarūpāya kapālāntaraśāyine ।
sudhādhāmasvarūpāya sudhāhārāya te namaḥ ।।

citrabhānuśaśāṃkāya dvādaśārkāni tejase ।
trisaptāhaṃ pareśānaśāstrāmṛtarasena ca ।।

sarahasyena bhagavan paramānaṃdanaṃditā ।
subhagāsmi mahādeva jagadvaṃdyāsmi sāṃprataṃ ।।

etena tantrarājena prāstenādya bhavanmukhāt ।
rājā jayadrathaḥ soyaṃ prasādāttava sāṃprataṃ ।।

paramānaṃdasaṃtṛptaḥ sthita eva tavāgrataḥ ।
etajjñānaṃ maheśāna rahasyaṃ kālikā kulaṃ ।।

maṃtrāmnāyasamopetaṃ prasādāttava śaṃkara ।
idānīṃ brūhi bhagavan yatpurā sūcitaṃ tvayā ।।

yā sā saṃrāviṇī devī ekāśītipadeśvarī ।
kathaṃ sā paramā proktā kathaṃ haḍḍā yudheti sā ।।

kasmātkaraṃkiṇī ghorā saptārṇṇā vā kathaṃ bhavet ।
mūlākhyā sa kathaṃ nātha kathaṃ kaṃkālabhairavī ।।

paṃcāśaddhṛdayā kasmātkasmātkauleśvarī hi sā ।
katidhā sādhanaṃ tasyāḥ kathaṃ vā maṇḍalādikaṃ ।।

kiṃ vā sādhayate kasmātkaṃvā yogamanuttamaṃ ।
(62a)
samayānyathavā ketra rahasyānyathavā na kiṃ ।।

vratādikamathānyathāmardhaṃ prabrūhi bhairava ।
prasādātparameśāna sarvvabhūtānukaṃpayā ।।

evaṃ śrutvā mahādeva stotraṃ praśnavaraṃ tathā ।
sāṭṭahāsamuvācedaṃ vistarātparameśvara ।।
rahasyānekasaṃkīrṇṇaṃ tantrasāramidaṃ mahat ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

tuṣṭāṃsmi tava deveśi stotreṇānena sāṃprataṃ ।
kiṃtu dūyati me cittaṃ kathaṃ pṛcchasi bhairavi ।।

mā bheṣṭa kathayiṣyāmi tathāpi tanumadhyame ।
atighorataraṃ praśnaṃ kṛtavānasi bhairavi ।।

svastyastu sarvvajagatāṃ chidraṃ mā bhūśca kiṃcana ।
sā eva rakṣatu jagaddevadevī karaṃkiṇī ।।

viśāgnyonmaṃthanamidamārarddhaṃ parameśvari ।
tasmātsusāvadhātvaṃ paratatvāvalaṃbinī ।।

bhavasva parameśāni bhūtvā taddhi śṛṇuṣvataḥ ।
rātrau meghākulāyāṃ tu kṛṣṇapakṣenyavāsare ।।

harāspade bhīmatare madirānaṃdanaṃditaḥ ।
śavakeśopavītī * * śivāchirabhūṣaṇaḥ ।।

svapacī puṣkasīkoṃkā mātaṃgī ca rajasvalā ।
dūtayastāḥ prakarttavyā madirānaṃdanaṃditāḥ ।।

prāśya paṃcāmṛtaṃ vīro bahuvīrasamanvitaḥ ।
vedimadhyaṃ samāsādya śodhayedvasudhātalaṃ ।।

kaṃkālaghṛṣṭaṃ kurvvīta mahāśṛgupalepitaṃ ।
prastare tatra deveśīṃ mātṛkāṃ mantramātaraṃ ।।

vīropacāraiḥ saṃpūjya paścāddevīṃ samuddharet ।
(62b)
nāmnā haḍḍāyudhāṃ raudrāṃ rāviṇīṃ ghoravikramāṃ ।।

sarvvapūrvvaṃ ca taddaṇḍaṃ vaktraṃ tatpṛṣṭagaṃ punaḥ ।
adhomukhaṃ punaḥ kāryaṃ bījaṃ prathamamuddhṛtaṃ ।।

sarvvāntarasthaṃ ca puna ekadaṃṣṭraṃ punardadet ।
ādyārūḍhaṃ ca taṃ kṛtvā tribiṃduparicuṃbitaṃ ।।

aśvaṃ tenaiva khacitaṃ dātavyaṃ surasuṃdari ।
śāntaṃ śūnye kalāyuktaṃ muśalaṃ svātmagaṃ punaḥ ।।

tametayonimuddhnāntaṃ kṛtvā cānyaṃ samuddharet ।
ṣātaṃ mahārāvakalā yojya rudrorddhagaṃ kuru ।।

sṛṣṭikṣayayutaṃ kṛtvā vidyāsaptākṣarānaghe ।
muṇḍapādavihīnaiṣā mahāghorāsurāviṇī ।।

ekavīrā sarvvagatā sarvvabhakṣamaholvaṇā ।
asyākhyā grahaṇā devī vidyātatveśvarāvadhiṃ ।।

jvalate viśvamakhilaṃ mahāsaṃhāravatsadā ।
śāntiṃ kurvvanti te devī bhītabhītā na saṃśayaḥ ।।

anayā devadeveśī sadāśivasahasrakaiḥ ।
kaṭisūtraṃ kṛtaṃ ghoraṃ tadante ratnamālikāṃ ।।

tanmaṃtrairmukhamālāṃ ca kṛtvā narttanti bhairavāṃ ।
asthiśeṣā yadā devī tadā haḍḍeti viśrutā ।।

suśāntareṇa rūpeṇa rāviṇīcakramadhyagā ।
sollāsadhāmasadvṛtvā śaśisūryahutāśanāḥ ।।

grāsīkṛtya punarvvātaṃ cakraṃ grasta tathā priye ।
evaṃ ṣaḍaratkuṇḍāntaṃ sphurajvālāvalīdharāṃ ।।

jvaladbhogiguhāgeha sahanoḍḍāmareśvari ।
ṣaṇṇāmanti kulaṃ ghoraṃ sphuraṃtavatsurāviṇaṃ ।
(63a)
agrameṇaiva saṃrāva rāvamulvāpya bhairavaṃ ।।

jagadgrāsātyujvalitāṃ yadā saṃrāviṇīkulaṃ ।
kṣubdaṃ maṃdasamāpatyā raśipuṃjavilāpanāt ।।

vamatyeva tadā ghorāmekāśīti maheśvarī ।
yadā marmaṇi saṃrūḍhā marmamarmāntaraṃgagā ।।

marmālokanadhāmnisthā tadā marmeśvarī hi sā ।
yadā rautisvarāvasthā rāvacakrāntaroditā ।।

mahārāvaravā raudrā tadā sā rāviṇī smṛtā ।
sā rāvayati kālāgni sā rāvayati hāṭakaṃ ।।

sā rāvayati brahmāṇḍaṃ sā rāvayati keśavaṃ ।
sā ca rudraṃ rāvayate punarīśvararāviṇī ।।

punaḥ sadāśivaṃ cakraṃ sā rāvati bhairavi ।
tato bhairava cakraṃ sā rāvamadhye ca rāviṇī ।।

svarāvārāvaravaṇī rāve rāveti rāviṇī ।
virauti rāviṇī cakre tena sā rāviṇī smṛtā ।।

ravate devatā vṛṃdaṃ raśmisaṃdhānayogataḥ ।
pibate sā punardevī rāvayantī kulakramaṃ ।।

tenābhimārgagā devī rāviṇī parikīrttitā ।
smaraṇena smṛti jñeyā smṛtyā smaraṇameva hi ।।

tayā smaraṇasaṃdhāna saṃdhānaṃ smaraṇena tu ।
smaraṇaṃ smārayedyasyāttena sā smaraṇeśvarī ।।

tanmatena mataṃ jñeyaṃ mataṃ matakulāntagaṃ ।
mataṃ mataṃ mataṃ bhūyo yadā rāvayate hi sā ।।

matasaṃhāriṇī nāmnā tena devī mateśvarī ।
kulaṃ kulaṃ kule līnaṃ akulaṃ rāviṇī kulaṃ ।।
(63b)
tatkaulakuladhāmasthaṃ virāvayati garvviṇī ।
tadā kuleśvarī jñeyā dhāmakuṃḍalimadhyagā ।।

paṃcārddhaṃ paṃcadhā jñeyā paṃcadhā caiva paṃcake ।
paṃcapaṃcakacakraughai yadā rauti subhairavaṃ ।।

tadā paṃcātmaravaṇī rāviṇī sā vidhīyate ।
atimārgeti mārge sve sve sve vā mārgamārgake ।।

sāti mārgāti mārgasthā mārge mārge virotpalaṃ ।
tadā saivāti mārgasthā rāviṇī paṃcabhakṣaṇī ।।

kādyakaṃkālinī khasthā karaṃkā kalanapriye ।
kakaṃdakamalāntasthā keśikārgalalolupā ।।

tadā karaṃkiṇī ghorā devī saṃrāviṇī parā ।
khacarā khamalā khasthā khādyākhaṃḍendukhādinī ।।

khacarā khagasaddhāmnī khakalā khagakuṃḍalī ।
cittāmalanirābhāsā yadā devī virauti sā ।।

tatra tatra tadā rūḍhā caturddaśasthapaṃcasu ।
tadā khecarasaṃrāva rāviṇī paramāraṇī ।।

ciccitparācitkuleśī tatra madhye viroti yā ।
sā ca vidrāraṇī proktā viddhāmni pravijṛṃbhate ।।

haḍḍāyudhā haḍḍadharā haḍḍeśī haḍḍavigrahā ।।
haḍḍasthūṇā haḍḍamukhī pratihaḍḍaṃ virauti sā ।।

haḍḍasaṃrāviṇī tena jñeyā haḍḍakaraṃkiṇī ।
carccaye vismayaṃ carcyaṃ carcikāṃ kulamaṃḍape ।।

carcayedakhilāṃ lokāṃ yopi sopīha carcyate ।।
tanmadhyagā virautyugraṃ tena carcikarāviṇī ।।

bharaṃ rauti bharaṃ rauti bhīmaṃ rauti bharannapi ।
(64a)
bharaṇādvamanādvā ca tena bhairavarāviṇī ।।

kāmāntake kāmakalā kāmagā kāmakāminī ।
kāme kāme kāmayantī kāmaṃ kāmaṃ virauti ca ।।

yadā kāmaravā devī jñeyā kāmaraṇī tadā ।
cakracakre cakracarā cakrasaṃrāviṇī * * ।।

tadā sā cakrarāvākhyā drāviṇī viśvarāviṇī ।
trikoṇadhāmni triskaṃdhe trikālatha supuṣpite ।।

virauti puṣpakālī sā nityaklinnā surāviṇī ।
prāṇāntarentarādhāre pratyakṣākṣavisarpiṇī ।।
kṣepavikṣepasaṃkṣobhaistanmadhyāsthā virauti yā ।।

tadā kṣobha kulāntasthā rāviṇī sā karaṃkinī ।
śaktirūpā śaktidharī śaktikṣobhāntarāviṇī ।।

śivoditā śivagamā tena śaktiḥ kuleśvarī ।।
bhakṣayantī sadā bhaktyā na bhaktyā nāpi sarvvadā ।।

bhakṣayanti virautyugraṃ sarvvabhakṣā mahāravā ।
sarvvabhakṣakarī kālī tena jñeyā karaṃkiṇī ।।

kāraṇeśvaracakraugharaśmibhāsuravigrahā ।
śoṣayantī grasaṃtī ca haṭhādekā karaṃkiṇī ।।

maṃtreśa parameśāna pataṃṣokhilacakramāṃ ।
tena śuktā na pūrṇṇā sā jvalitā kṣubhitā punaḥ ।।

tadā mahārāviṇī sā jñeyā śuṣkā karaṃkiṇī ।
brahmāṃvucaṃdanā jvāli bhakṣaṇodvarttanodyatāṃ ।।

ajāmarātmārayaṃtī akāla ravaṇī hi sā ।
yadā cillā mahārāvi svānakroṣṭakayostathā ।।
(64b)
gajāśvahāntamahiṣakharavārāhakukkuṭaṃ ।
haṃsādīnāṃ virautyuccairvviśeṣādrāvamuttamaṃ ।।

mahāśmaśānamadhyasthā tena sā rāviṇī smṛtā ।
ādhāradhāmni duryādho vīrā kṣobhanatatparā ।।

virauti grāsaniratā tadā devī sarāviṇī ।
evameṣā mahāghorā tava devi prakāśitā ।।

tantresmin ghorasarvvasve śiraśchede mahārthade ।
yasyā yatsakalaṃ rūpaṃ kastvāṃ varṇṇayituṃ kṣamaḥ ।।

yasya sphuranti dantā(nma)lagnāstatveśvareśvarāḥ ।
yasyāpyādhunanādeva brahmāṇḍā nāgnisakṣaṇāḥ ।।

romakūpairvviniryānti hyanyonya pravidāriṇaḥ ।
caṭaścaṭacaṭārāvarāviṇo gho * darśanāt ।।

kastvāmutsahate dhyātuṃ devadevī karaṃkiṇī ।
mahāpralayalakṣaughakṣayakṣīṇaiḥ sadāśivaiḥ ।।

kṛtāṅgaśobhāṃ tāṃ devīṃ ko vā dhyātuṃ samutsahe ।
yasyāḥ kālāgnilakṣāṇāṃ kādyairnūpuramālikāṃ ।।

bhrājate bhīmasatpādā calakraṭaraḍhāravaiḥ ।
kastāṃ dhyātuṃ devadevi protsāhetparameśvarī ।।

brahmāṇḍalakṣairduḥpūraṃ netrāgādhāṃ bhayānakāṃ ।।
kṣayakṣapātmanoddāmāṃ kastāṃ dhyātuṃ samīhate ।।

ākṣoṇita(bhasmī)rabhya * * yāvatsādāśivaṃ ।
tatveśvarāśca ye tatra svadūtiparivāritā ।।

karāgramadhye naiḥkatvā devadevyā prapīḍitāḥ ।
kṣiptā tu kuhare bhīme prajvalante mahāvaṭe ।।

yānatṛptā maheśāni kastvāṃ dhyātuṃ samutsahe ।
(65a)
catuḥṣaṣṭisamākhyātā ye ghorāḥ bhairaveśvarāḥ ।।

svamātṛsahitāḥ sarvve ravaṃto rāvamudvahaṃ ।
dantāntareṣu te lagnā devadevyā maheśvarī ।।

mahāghorapracaṇḍākṣī kastvāṃ dhyātuṃ samīhate ।
viśvarūpā viśvamukhī viśveśī viśvabhakṣaṇī ।।

viśvodarā viśvapādā viśvajānuru viśvadṛk ।
viśvakārā viśvarūpā kastāṃ dhyātuṃ samutsahe ।।

na dhyānaṃ vidyate cāsyāḥ sarvvākārāpadā priye ।
enāṃ karaṃkiṇīṃ devīṃ sarvvavīryopavṛṃhitā ।।

yāvanti tatvamadhyoktā hṛdayā ghoravikramāḥ ।
tāvatsā devi yadvīryaṃ tadasyāḥ parikītaṃ ।।

sadāśivākṣakalpānāṃ kāṃkṣīkoṭiśataiḥ priye ।
vaktrairasaṃkhyairdeveśi jihvānāma yutāyutaiḥ ।।

navavarṇṇayituṃ śaktā sohamasyāḥ parākramaṃ ।
vīryaṃ vā devadeveśi śatāṃśena maheśvarī ।।

evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ dhyānaṃ mantrasamanvitaṃ ।
sa rahasyaṃ mahābhāge bhairaveśvaradurlabhaṃ ।।

leśato devadeveśi adhunā śṛṇu sāṃprataṃ ।
pūjanaṃ devadeveśyā yathāvat pravadāmyahaṃ ।।

pūrvvoktā vedimāsādya bahusaṃbhārasaṃbhṛtaḥ ।
tatra yāgaṃ samāsthāya yathāvadvidhivistaraṃ ।।

savasūtreṇa saṃsūtrya asthicūrṇṇādibhirlikhet ।
cakrāravṛṃdaṃ deveśi susamaṃ lakṣaṇānvitaṃ ।।

padmamaṣṭadalaṃ madhye hetire dvādaśārakaṃ ।
catuḥ ṣaṣṭhyārakaṃ bāhye tathānyaṃ hetirājakaṃ ।
(65b)
raktakṛṣṇena rajasā kṛtvā devīṃ yajet tataḥ ।।

madhye niveśayeddevī śeṣānyāḥ paravāravat ।
vakṣyamāṇāstathā coktāstā pūjyāḥ parameśvarī ।।

evaṃ cakraṃ ca saṃpūjyamāhūya parameśvarīṃ ।
mantrastotreṇa deveśi sa rahasyena bhairavi ।।

nainaṃ samayanirmuktaṃ paṭhyate parameśvari ।
ghorāṃ vṛttiṃ samālaṃvya rāviṇī cakramadhyataḥ ।।
āhvānayeta deveśīṃ sarvvamātṛgaṇānvitāṃ ।

oṃ āgaccha paramārgādhirūḍhaprāṇasaṃdhāriṇe dhātri ।
tribhuvaṃ netravanāntaralīlā saṃbhāsaṃbhṛte devī ।।

brahmāṇḍavītamāye * tārārūpe pararaudri ।
rudraikādaśamāte * māte vighnaṃ samāgaccha ।।

ādityaraśmikalitacaṃdrakalaiḥ sarvvabhūtahṛdayasthe ।
kkālāmāle mālinī līlā vaḍavānalakṣobhe ।।

bhairavi varakhaṭvāṃgini śaṃkhi cakriṇi mahāghaṃṭe ।
śuśilini padme padmārūḍhe samatyehi ।।

ghori mahāghoreśvari jharjara jhaṃkāra hahahahahaṃsi ।
kālāgnirudramātestilakā kalitānala kṣobhe ।
vikṣullabhuvanaparikarudraṃḍasamastamaṃḍalakṣapite ।
kṣoṃkārākālanāyakikirāti kāpālikā liṃgi ।।

hrīṃ matamātaṃgini vilulihiṃkṛtavadane ।
khaṭvāṃga bhṛhannaraśirakṣobha mahābhairavākārena ।।

(66a)
yanāntarāntarasthe netratretāgnisapraṇave ābaddha ।
bhujagaśīrṣa caṃdrārddhakakarākaratalā pīḍe ।।

saṃhārarātrivigrahaharamāye māsamāgaccha ।
pretamukhighārāve vijñāya sākṣiptavihitabhujadaṃḍe ।।

ḍamalosphālanamācaladaṃṣṭrālikalakalāṭope ।
baddhavitālavadane surarudhiroṅgāramūrcchite māri ।।

saṃhārakārakaraṇatrailokyākrāntahuṃḍamukhe ।
khiṃkhiṃṇi valacālitanṛttamahālayavālāli ।।

diggajajatitamahāmadadarpitakuṃbhāṇḍanirbhede ।
kalpānta vā tadārūṇi laṃvirudrāṇi ।।

māhendri vaiṣṇavi maitri vināyaki gāruḍi saumye mahādevi ।
vighneśvari nārāyaṇi māruti yama daivate śānte pāvaki ।।

nairṛti pūtani bhairavi mahāvaruṇadevate brāhmi vārāhināgnadehe gajapati carmāmbare raudri cāmuṃḍa caṃḍa caṃḍa caṃḍini
caṃḍakāpālini bhairavarūpe samāgaccha nārāyaṇi baladhāriṇi nidrārūpe mahāmohe dākṣāyaṇi pāramāyanivātāyani
yoni niryāti sambibhirbhṛti nimitti bhagarūpe jīvaśukraddhare kaumāravratadhāriṇi śāntapade bhāsure śobhe naranārī
śiśuviśitāmnānādya maholkākerālamukhi । ātāmrarudhiramadamudarūkṣabhrūbhaṃganetrānte mahiśāsurasaṃcālini
(66b)
netrānta samabhūtapāvaka kṣuṇṇo hā hā gīte gītairgīta gāyati gīte valallatollāsā śivakālakāmamāyayātyamanantāṃ
samāgaccha vāme vāmācāre jyeṣṭhe bhūteṣu raudri raudrācāraraudrī ।। kalanātkālī parāsṛṣṭiḥ kalavikāraṇi
viniyogādvalakāriṇi ।।

māṃsamāgaccha balaviparītavināśani ।
balakṣobha virute māṃ ramaṇi padābhuvi-
mukto manasaṃproktā manonmanī samāyāti ।।
paramapavitramārge gītā devena śaṃbhuno tena ।।

daityāntrakīṃ dhāriṇi tathā parāmaṃtra jālasya ।
nānārūpe rūpaṃ bhajamāte sāṃ samāgaccha ।।

bhajavāya(vyaṃ) vegaṃ śaivaṃ yāgaṃ paraṃ sauraṃ ।।
vaiṣṇavamatulaṃ tejo svabhāvena bhajaparaśaktiṃ ।।

āgaccha bhūtagaṇapatimavisahitā tālivetāli ।
kāmāyani kāmamahe he he māhendrilokeśi ।।
śiva viśvāsasamutthitanādyākāśāntare śānte ।
aṃkuraṃ saṃbhṛta sāmudayo vedavaṣaṭkāra hārāṃge ।।

svāhā svadhā vaṣaṭgatavauṣaṭ ।।

vidhūya lelihyeṣāntāṃgāre revatīdīpte mahāpralayavahnisaṃbhūtaṃ ।।
surasamāmnāyakikṛtāntapāśakaratalāpīḍe ।
he devi sureśvari lolelākatrayākrānte ।

bhajasāntevyamaśeṣaṃ sarvvajanaṃ pāhi tu (vamamihaṃ) ।
tūrṇṇavijṛmbhanipuṇaṃ prāṇāpānādidiṣṭaṃ saṭṭaṃ ।
agne hiraṇyagarbhaṃ bhāsuragaganāntavibhurasau nāthāṃ sthitamacalacāliṃ tāṃ tāṃ bruvamiti tatvāgatastejaḥ ।।
(67a)
nivahaṃtu tava hṛdayāntarā kamalamadhye tridhāmārge prāṇapradā pramāvibhāvitahṛdayaṃ samudvahatu ।।
varakuharamālitānanadīptaśikhākārakāntamukhi bhīṣaṇavividhabṛhanmukhi maṃtreśvari rāsabhārūḍhe ābaddhavīni bhujaga
vimiśrita tailavasārudhirācita carcitāṃge ।।

khagapati pakṣāpiḍe sūryakalāpe namo mahāri ।
saṃhārakālasamaye janani mahābrahmāṇistvanyaḥ ।।

karakṛtakavāṭayugalapronmathitarudhiramadamatte ।
pātālādiśivāntaṃ gatadehā viśvarūpāsi ।।
ābaddhabhujagavare raudrākṣikṣu me giriguhāmukhare ।।

niśvāsabāhunirgamaparamārciyutatrivanavare ।
kalpāntāgnidagdhaloke bhasmoddhulitāṃgamukhi ।।

nistamitasakalabhuvane śūnyabhūte vibhūtagate ।
viṣṭhāṃvare variṣṭhe saṃtyevadāṃbike lakṣmi ।
kalpāntānalajalacarajalanidhisaṃkṣudhva bhīmarāve ।।

hāreti huṃkṛtahutabhugdāntamukhiparamadharmatanmātre ।
cikre prahve māheśvari havye kavye pratāvani prahve ।।

raviśaśimadhyaniviṣṭe raviśaśihutabhukkrame śānte ।
māye mohavināśani māyeśvaridevate sarvve ।।

saṃbodhani saṃmohani haribhagini citparārūpe ।

bhayabhavapāśavināśani guhyeśvari mātrike siddhe vāgeśvari ।
sadvidye gaganāntaracariṇi prajñe vicarahṛyaṃtrabhaṃjani ।
unmani mātrāgnikogopredhyānāhare durge kīrttismṛtinaṃdani kṣamāṃ rūpe ।।
(67b)
gāyatri vedamāte sāvitri tato yogajapatiṣṭe ।
jñānāṃbike sarasvati durge sarvveśvarakṣoṇi ।। ।।

oṃ jhĩ jhãpini jaṃbhamohe kṣā̃ hrī̃ mātṛke huṃ haḥ ।। ।।

jhī̃ bhairavi ghoraghoreśvari jhāṃkāri mahāhāse kālāgni rudramātastilakākalitānalakṣobhavikṣuṇṇabhuvanaparikarakamalāṃ
ca sarvveśvari māṃ sasamāyāhi ।।
kṣaṃkālarātri parame sarvvajñe huṃ namo divyai jṛṃbhani varavarade varavāre muru muru svāhā ।।
staṃbhani kini kini sara sara ghuruli mahāghurili gauri gāṃdhāri mālini bhāvāni moṭaya huluhu vilaṃghayaṃ kṣu kṣaḥ ।।
siri siri ciri ciri saśikhiṃ ciciṃni mahāsiṃciri caṃṭiki ehyehi ।।
mārge vahan nimittāsugamamayā tvapi ca cāvyaktā ।।
punarutpattinimittaṃ hũ hũ rāve mahāśakti krīḍartheṃ vidhi niṃditāsū nikṛṣṇaṃ mantraye sakalasurāsuranamite jagatrayaṃ
pāhirājamānaṃ ।।
tvameva laṃbikāṃbike devi rudragaṇagaṇanamite bhagavati namite site ha jvālāmaṇḍalaparikarakamalāṃbarālaṃbite devi ।।
bhajā bhuvanaśānti hetuṃ pratimāmarghaṃ tathā pādyaṃ dhūpamidaṃ madhuparkaṃ madyamāṃsādikāṃ dravyaṃ upahāra nṛttagītaṃ
ṣaṣṭāśaktiṃ samudvahatu ।।
sarveśvari tava bhaktyā yadvā tava cāvane dattaṃ kṣama paramadeva namite jagattrayaṃ pāhi(ṣvāṃ)bike
(68a)
devi yajamānaṃ pāhi mahābhayamocani - - - - - takasamayinaḥ sarvve ।।

evametanmayā proktamāhvānaṃ sarvvapūraṇaṃ ।
bhraṣṭasiddhādurāvāro mahāsamayalaṃghake ।।

pāṭhātsiddhi pradaṃ (jñeyaṃ) mantrāhvānaṃ gaṇāmbike ।
ekatra sarvvamaṃtrāśca vidyāsarvvāsu locane ।।

na samāstotrarājasya kimanyatkathitena hi ।
evamāvāhya deveśīṃ trailokya bhayadāyikāṃ ।।

mahāsṛgarghapādyādyairmadyairmāṃsairanekadhā ।
tarpayetparameśānīṃ paśubhirvvividhaistathā ।।

huḍukāraiḥ śivārāvairnānāmadyaravaistathā ।
yathāvibhavavistārairnānāpuṣpaiḥ samarcayet ।।

puroktadūtisaṃyuktastatkulotthaiḥ sahaiva tu ।
yajetkaraṃkiṇīṃ ghorāṃ parivārasamanvitāṃ ।।

yāvanti hṛdayānīha ṣaṭke proktāni vai mayā ।
tāvanti ca samānyeva viśeṣo na vidhīyate ।।

sarvvābharaṇayogena te pūjyāḥ surasuṃdari ।
ekaivaṃ devadeveśī vibhinnā svasvarūpataḥ ।।

ekāśītimarmavaraistasmādbhedena vā hi sā ।
yathā rucitapūjyāsyā svāmyāvaraṇayogataḥ ।।

vahūtpatrāvabhedāni bhavatyatra maheśvari ।
yeṣu niṣṭhā na caivāsti tantrarāje jayadrathe ।।

ekātha devadeveśī pūjyā devī karaṃkiṇī ।
saṃpūjya darśayetpaścānmudrā ghorā karaṃkiṇī ।।

saṃrāviṇī tato deyā ābi(ddhā) tadanantaraṃ ।
(68b)
paścāddhomaṃ prakarttavyaṃ citākāṣṭhe vitānale ।।

mahāpalaṃ samāhṛtya āhūtīnāṃ sahasrakaṃ ।
punarmudrāṃ prabaṃdhena āvidvān ghoradarśanāt ।।

sahāyaiḥ sahito vīraḥ plavate gaganāntaraṃ ।
pratyakṣameti sā devī saumyarūpeṇa bhāsvatā ।।

tayaiva sarvvadevesyā piṇḍasiddhi bhavenṛṇāṃ ।
ityevamiha cākhyātaṃ devyā yajanamuttamaṃ ।।

sarvvasiddhipradaṃ sāramajñānatimirāpahaṃ ।
nātaḥ parataraṃ kiñcidvidyate bhuvanodare ।।

saṃkṣepādevamākhyātaṃ vistaro nādito mayā ।
ataḥ paraṃ brūhi samyakkathayāmīha kiṃ priye ।। ।।

iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṅśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
rāviṇīyāgapaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

evamākarṇya deveśī harātparamakāraṇāt ।
punaḥ provāca caṃdrārddhacūḍāmaṇidharaṃ haraṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrutaṃ sarvvamaśeṣeṇa devyā mantrakramaṃ mahat ।
adhunā sādhanaṃ deva prabrūhi tripurāntaka ।।

kathaṃ cāsādhyate devī kati vā sādhanāni hi ।
vibhāgena samākhyāhi yogamārgaḥ suvistaraḥ ।।

śrutvaivaṃ prāha bhagavānumākāntasukāntavat ।
sphuratkhaṃḍendumaulāliprabhābhāsitadiṅmukhaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi yathāhaṃ coditastvayā ।
(69a)
sādhanaṃ devadevesyāḥ samāsānna tu vistarāt ।।

sarvvayogasamuddhāro bahuśāstrakṛtaśramaḥ ।
sarvvanāḍikalālīno vīrācāraparāyaṇaḥ ।।

sātvikodambhanirmuktastādṛksādhaka ucyate ।
gatvā harālayaṃ ghoraṃ mahāvīravarasvayaṃ ।।

eko vā susahāyo vā sarvvāvayavabhūṣaṇaḥ ।
ārabhetsādhanaṃ devyāḥ karaṃkiṇyā maheśvari ।।

mahāśmaśānamadhyastho japeddevīṃ subhairavāṃ ।
lakṣāyutaṃ mahāghorā juhuyāttaddaśāṃśataḥ ।।

nāramājaṃ * gocchaṃ ca tryaktaṃ suravarārcite ।
homāntādagnimadhyā tu svayaṃ devī karaṃkiṇī ।।

saumyarūpe rasābhyeti sādhakasya varapradā ।
tayaiva sārddhaṃ vrajati yatra sā parameśvarī ।।

tatra bhairavacakrāṇāṃ prabhuratyuttamottamaḥ ।
evamutpannamāsiddhirnākhyātā tava sāṃprataṃ ।।

lakṣajāpena deveśi mahāpātālasiddhidā ।
ajaptā devadeveśī śṛṇuyatsādhayiṣyati ।।

mahāvetālasiddhiṃ ca mahākiṃkarasādhanaṃ ।
antarddhānaṃ rasākṛṣṭirathasādhanamuttamaṃ ।।

bhūtasādhanamatyugraṃ kṛtyāsādhanamadbhutaṃ ।
nāgānāṃ darśanaṃ samyaṅmeghāśani nivāraṇaṃ ।।

pādacāraṃ tathā khaḍgaṃ pādukāścopavītakaṃ ।
manacchilāṃjanādīni daṇḍaṃ kulakamaṇḍaluṃ ।।

kuṇḍalānyajinaṃ sūtraṃ siddhidravyāni yāni ca ।
yakṣiṇyāḥ pūrvvamuktāyāḥ prasenasvadmasādhanaṃ ।।

preśanaṃ duṣṭadamanaṃ viśabhūtavināśanaṃ ।
(69b)
vyādhinigrahamevānyadvaśyākarṣaṇameva ca ।।

māraṇoccāṭanādīni stobhastambhanajādiṣu ।
nāvāśakaṭayantrāṇāṃ bhedanaṃ jambhanaṃ tathā ।।

śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ caiva saubhāgyakaraṇaṃ tathā ।
evamādīni yānīha pūrvvaṃ tantre pracoditāḥ ।।

vakṣyamāṇāśca ye sarvve sādhaye nānya yatnataḥ ।
līlayā parameśānīṃ ghorahaḍḍakaraṃkiṇīṃ ।।

sarvvasādhāraṇā jñeyā taṃtresmin parameśvare ।
pratikalpavidhānena sādhayanmanasepsitaṃ ।।

yasmātsarvvottamā devī tasmādetatprakīrttitaṃ ।
yogayogeśvarī ghorā saṃsmaret kṣobhaye dalaṃ ।।

parāpareṇa rūpeṇa dvaidhau veṣā prakīrttitā ।
kulabhedagatāmaulī mūlabhūtā kulāraṇī ।।

kāpālinī kulāntasthā sā ca devī manonmanī ।
tena bhedena śubhage jñātā siddhyupapādikā ।।

evametatsamākhyātaṃ kimanyatkathayāmi te ।। ।।

iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe jayadrathayāmale caturthaṣaṭke rāviṇīsādhanapaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrutvevaṃ parameśāna vaktrapadmodbhavaṃ vacaḥ ।
bhūyaḥ provāca sā devī caṃdracūḍāmaṇiṃ vacaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrutaṃ deva mayā tantraṃ śiraśchedamanuttamaṃ ।
mantravidyāsamopetaṃ mudrāmaṇḍalasaṃkulaṃ ।।

vāmadakṣiṇasiddhāntaṃ nānābhedopabhedataḥ ।
kravyādaṃ nakra cūṣākhyaṃmaprecchuṣmaṃ mahābalaṃ ।।
(70a)
yatra lehyaṃ samāhāraṃ majjā hārāhva ghasmaraṃ ।
jairṇṇarūpiṇya jaṭilaṃ raudraḍāmarajaṃbhanaṃ ।।

laṃpaṭaṃ suvihāriṇyaṃ keśacunvana hi hasan ।
śoṇitāhāramityevamaṣṭādaśavidhaṃ purā ।।

bhedopabhedaiścānantaiḥ śrutaṃ kaulaṃ kulādhipa ।
kulāvatāratantreya yatproktaṃ conmanodayaṃ ।।

tadapi vyāptasaṃyogāt sūcitaṃ na prakāśitaṃ ।
