kathamevāparaṃ rūpaṃ devadevyā vadasva me ।।

mantravīryā kriyāyogayantrataṃtrakriyādibhiḥ ।
akṣiṇī kramavetālasādhanā bhrāntacintayā ।।

na vijñātaṃ samāsānta kathayasva prasādataḥ ।
kulaṃ kimucyate nātha kaulaṃ vā kīdṛgucyate ।।

tadevaṃ kulakaulaṃ ca kasmādīśvara ucyate ।
evaṃ pṛṣṭho mahādevyā mahādevā mahāmahat ।।

mahārahasya vinyāsā mūrddhā cānaṃdanirbharaḥ ।।
kimeva matimugddhāsi pratārayasi suṃdari ।।

kiṃ tadvastu nayadvetsi trailokyodarasaṃsthitaṃ ।
trailokyaṃ cāpi nikhilamapi līnaṃ sadānaghe ।।

vihāya madvipusvāṃ ca na kadācit pṛthaksthitāṃ ।
sthūlasūkṣmavibhāgena parāparaparāparaiḥ ।।

vyāpyavyāpakarūpeṇa vācyavācakayogataḥ ।
ādhārādheya vinyāsādyojyādyojakayuktitaḥ ।।

adhiṣṭhātṛtathaivāpi svayarūpaguṇādibhiḥ ।
jñātijñānasamāveśāḥ kāryakāraṇayojanāt ।।

tvayā mayā ca sarvvedaṃ otaprotaṃ carācaraṃ ।
(70b)
kasya praṇīyate ko vā praṇetā kiṃ pralīyate ।।
janānāpyevamevatvaṃ kathaṃ praṣṭumupasthitā ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

yathaiva tvā * * * * mabhivatsi bhavāntaka ।
tathaiva sarvvaṃ sarvvajña kiṃ tu viśvānukaṃpayā ।।

pṛcchāmi tvāṃ samāsena bhagavanvaktumarhasi ।
japahomakriyā caryā dhāraṇā dhyāna sādhanaiḥ ।।

kliṣṭānāṃ sarvvajaṃtūnāṃ sukhopāyaṃ vadasva me ।
bhuktimuktiprajāyete jñānamātreṇa yena tu ।।

asminsaṃrāviṇī cakre rahasyatamamadbhutaṃ ।
tato muhūrttaṃ saṃcintya vyājahāra maheśvaraḥ ।। ।।

bhairava uvāca ।।

atisnehānubaṃdhena tāvākhyeyamidaṃ priye ।
śrutaṃ bhedopabhedena vistāraṃ yatkule tvayā ।।

vaināyakīṃ hitaṃ devi kriyāpūrvvamaśeṣataḥ ।
vināvidhānakleśena na siddhiḥ saṃprayacchati ।।

maulaṃ kaulaṃ punaḥ proktaṃ siddhakaulamataḥ paraṃ ।
vaināyakiprayatnānāṃ anākhyeyaṃ śivājñayā ।।

mohātprakāśati yovya * tkarmā tasya nigrahaḥ ।
śrotavyaṃ cettvayā vaśyaṃ tataḥ samayinī bhava ।।

yena te saṃpravakṣyāmi mayāpyāsāditaṃ yathā ।
āsītpurā purendrārirasurendro mahābalaḥ ।।

darpāndātoṃdhako nāmnā nipīḍita jagattrayaṃ ।
so vijitya surāṃ sarvvāṃ indropendrapurogamāḥ ।।

vibhāvasu vasuścāyaṃ vaivasvata vivasvataḥ ।
evavidracituṃ prāptastavameva raṇājite ।
(71a)
mayā samaṃ samādevi vidyānāśaṃ na mūrcchitaḥ ।।

akārisamaraṃ raudraṃ rudrāṇi dhutaviṣṭapaḥ ।
pinākimutsṛjya mayā tataḥ khaḍgena khaṃḍitaḥ ।।

dvidhā bhūtvā caturddhā tu dviguṇaṃ guṇavistarāṃ ।
khaḍyamāno na virato krameṇānantatāṃ gataḥ ।।

samucchalabhiradhikāṃ kṣatakṣatajavipluṣaiḥ ।
āprapūratribhuvanaṃ tena sarvvaṃ balīyasā ।।

tato jeyamiti jñātvā mayā śāntena suvrate ।
saṃsmṛtā paramā śaktiḥ kālikākhyā maheśvari ।।

adho ematisaṃkruddhamāvṛtya bahubāhubhiḥ ।
mahābhairavarūpeṇa matpuraḥ saṃvyavasthitaḥ ।।

uvāca ca vacaḥ kṣipraṃ pravidheyaṃ vidhīyatāṃ ।
mayoktānigrahāṇemadai * stṛṃdaityarādyaśuḥ ।।

tāvanmama samīpasthairbrahmarū * śikhidhvajaiḥ ।
viṣṇuvaivasvatamerutsahasrākṣo yathā mayā ।।

tathā smṛtavatī saivaṃ saṃjātaḥ punaraṣṭadhā ।
nābhiḥ kruddhābhiraṣṭābhirvviśeṣo kṣiptavigrahaḥ ।।

viparīta bahutvāsau tābhirvyāpādito śirāt ।
tatastu śoṇitāpāṇapalaparyāptatṛptayaḥ ।।

pravṛttā narttituṃ sarvvā rūpamāsthāya bhairavaṃ ।
bhīṣayatyasurāṃ sarvvāṃ sa daityāragarākṣasāṃ ।।

mayāpi bhayabhītena praṇamya śirasā stutā ।
ūcuḥ kimanyatkarttavyaṃ yatkarmasurasattamaṃ ।।

praśāntarūpamāsthāya rakṣadhvaṃ trijagatsadā ।
brahmādīnāṃ tatorūpaṃ varṇṇāyuṣakaricchadaḥ ।।
(71b)
vidhāya vidhivaddevyāścikrīḍāścāntacetasaḥ ।
punarabhihitaṃ vaṃtyo vinayoyasṛtaṃ punaḥ ।।

sāmānyatvaṃ kimasmābhiḥ sahecchati maheśvari ।
tadvadasva priyaṃ putra mātṛbhyaste yathā divaṃ ।।

nānyathā yoginīcakraṃ sāmānyo bhavate haraṃ ।
tataḥ prasannena mayā priyāṃ (tvā)miti śaṃkayā ।।

saṃśliṣṭhagāḍhamurasya chādayitvā ca varmanā ।
tā uktā nāsti me kaścidbhavatītyo dadāmi yaṃ ।।

vihasyo vadate devyā na doṣaḥ satyamuktavān ।
kiṃ tiṣṭhati na vā kālī nirujesi na paśyasi ।।

yāvadālokayāmitvaṃ tāvannāsi maheśvari ।
pramastā eva saṃpṛṣṭā ūcurdṛṣṭamidaṃ tvayā ।।

satvajijñāsayāsmābhirnandano darśitaṃ tava ।
nātropasanna saṃjñaṃ te bhavavādi vidhīyate ।।

eṣā sa tiṣṭhate devī samānyo yoginī kule ।
tvayāpi datta evāyamasmṛtya daityarāṭpaśuḥ ।।

anenaiva hi sāmānye jātastvaṃ kulanāyakaḥ ।
tanmayā tena vidhinā prāptametadvarānane ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

saṃsmarāmi samastaṃ hi purāvṛttaṃ mama prabho ।
evameva kulācāryo yathā proktastvayākhilaṃ ।।

tadupasannāsmi bhagavansamayo me vidhīyatāṃ ।
yena prāpnoti sakalaṃ kulasadbhāvamāditaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ācārākhyā parārthaṃ ca saṃsmaratyucitaṃ mayā ।
tadeva jī * bhavatī sāmānyā yoginīkule ।।

athāto madvacaḥ kṣipraṃ pratipadya yathākramaṃ ।
(72a)
caturyugavibhāgena saṃsthitākulamātaraḥ ।।

pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyubhirvyomabhiḥ sadā ।
sūryācaṃdramasoścaiva vyapodeśo na suṃdari ।।

vyāptametābhirakhilaṃ jagatasthāvarajaṃgamaṃ ।
nābhirvvinā bhavecchabdo na sparśo na rasastathā ।।

na rūpaṃ na bhavedgaṃdho na mano buddhireva ca ।
nāhaṃkāro bhaveddevi viśve cāturdaśātmake ।।

sarvvataśca samākārasvarūpaparamāṇubhiḥ ।
sarvvatra sarvvabhāvena vicaraṃti carācaraṃ ।।

brahmādyā iti vikhyātāstapataḥ kulamātaraḥ ।
kulametadbhaveddevi yanmaulaṃ parikīrttitaṃ ।।

etacchūlena rūpeṇa bahubhedopabhedataḥ ।
sa tiṣṭhate jagatyasmin sa devāsuramānuṣaṃ ।।

yaścāsya tejaḥ paraṃ śaktyāktamapi nisvinaṃ ।
sarvvaprapaṃcarahitamātmānubhavalakṣaṇaṃ ।।

maulātkulaviryātaṃ * bījāṃkura vadādibhiḥ ।
rahasyaṃ yantraṇā varjaṃ kaulaṃ devi taducyate ।।

nyagrodha bījanyāyena sarvvaprāṇiṣvavasthitaṃ ।
haṃti pāti jagatsarvvaṃ vidadhāti yathecchayā ।।

etaddevi sadārādhyamupāsyāstidameva hi ।
upāsya metada(vi)śaṃ guṇābhyudayamicchatāṃ ।।

brahmā brahmatvamadhuno viṣṇu viṣṇutvameva hi ।
indropīndratvamagamat prajāpatyaṃ prajāpatiḥ ।।

yena yenaiva bhāvena samupāsyamidaṃ priye ।
tasya tenaiva bhāvena tanmayatvaṃ sadā sadā ।।

abhāva bhāvanobhāvamabhāvabhavanodbhavaṃ ।
(72b)
bhavāti bhavabhāvena bhavyaṃ bhāvena bhāvayet ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
rāviṇīcakre unmanodayakulāvatāraḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

atha sarvveśvarākhyātāmetacchrutvā paraṃ vacaḥ ।
parameśamasaṃdigdhaṃ uvāca parameśvarī ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

maulaṃ kulaṃ tathā kaulaṃ vijñātaṃ tatprasādataḥ ।
sthūlakrameṇa sakalaṃ sūkṣmena tu kathaṃ bhavet ।।

evaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ praśāntātmā prāha māhātmyamāditaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu devi samāsena vakṣyamāṇamatīndriyaṃ ।
kuṇḍalyākhyā mahāśaktiḥ paramāmṛtarūpiṇī ।।

yasyā etadviniṣkrāntaṃ carācaramanekadhā ।
yasyāścaiva layaṃ yāti kramādvaitaṃ varānane ।।

sohaṃ sūkṣmeṇa rūpeṇa saṃsthitā siddhameva vā ।
saṃpṛktā cāsthitāṃ vācāṃ tejo vā pārameśvaraṃ ।।

sā sarvvajagadudbhūti sthitisaṃhārarūpiṇī ।
vāmadakṣiṇasiddhāntakaulānāṃ kulamātṛkāṃ ।।

jananī ṛgyajuḥ sāmnāṃ vācāṃ vāgeśvarīṃ parāṃ ।
samastasyārddhano heturmantramudrā gaṇasya tu ।।

yoni tatvasamūhasya sṛṣṭiṣaṣṭurapi priye ।
mālinyā lokamālinyā rāśinyā śabdarāśinā ।।

vidyāgaṇena gaṇaśo maṃḍalīkṛtamaṃḍalaiḥ ।
brahmādyāśca tathāṣṭābhirmantribhirbhairavairgrahaiḥ ।।

dikpīpairupapīṭhaiśca lokapālaistathaiva ca ।
(73a)
prāṇāpānasamānodānavyānādi prabhaṃjanaiḥ ।।

vedaiḥ sahopaniṣadairnānāprastāravastubhiḥ ।
dṛ(ṅma)sasparśaviṣayaiḥ tālvoṣṭhapuṭagocaraiḥ ।।

anyairapi mahādevi trailokyodaravarttibhiḥ ।
utpadyamānairanimaṃ līyamānairaharniśaṃ ।।

padinyāya padanyāsaṃ dehadairghya niyojanāt ।
saṃveṣṭyāṣṭapadāvartyā kuṇḍalyākārakuṃcikā ।।

vyāpyatrailokyajāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ sarvvabhūteṣu bhāmini ।
saṃsthitā sūkṣmarūpeṇa śaktirādyā manonmaṇī ।।

bhramaṇī bhrāmaṇī caiva jambhanī stambhanī tathā ।
gaṃtrī tathā ca gāyatrī hasanī hāsanī tathā ।।

paṭhanī pāṭhanī caiva nanīkī narttayatyapi ।
āsphoṭanī valganī ca samastākarṣaṇī tathā ।।

mahāmāyā mahāsthāyā mahāmohapraṇāśanī ।
mahāvidyā mahāprajñāmajñānasaṃpravarttinī ।।

damanī sarvvaduṣṭānāṃ māraṇī mārakarṣaṇī ।
yamanī yātanādīnāṃ duṣkṛtānāṃ nikṛntanī ।।

sā labhyate naraiḥ puṇyā * vitarkyānubhāvanā ।
nā(myū)hayāttaḍayā vā na tu dhyānna * dhāraṇāt ।।

tiryagantarvvahiradho madhyorddharahitā sadā ।
na ṛjvīnāpi kuṭilā na dīrghā nāpi varttulā ।।

caturasrā trikoṇā * * napi caṃdrākṛtistathā ।
na ca lāṃchena sthirā kvāpi naiva yāti na tiṣṭhati ।।

na nīlapītaharitā nāpi saṃgāsitāruṇā ।
surendracāpalekheva sarvvavarṇṇadharā sadā ।।
(73b)
taḍillateva ca (dula)kanakācalavasthirā ।
sūryāyutasavarṇṇābhā caṃdrakoṭisamaprabhā ।।

sācchidya tena kenāpi sarvvapāpācchinatti sā ।
dṛśyate na ca kenāpi spṛ(śyate) vyomamaṃḍalaṃ ।।

bhidyate naiva kenāpi bhitatti bhuvanatrayaṃ ।
nirudhyate na kenāpi dahyate nāpi vahninā ।।

ruṇaddhi sarvvā sa viśvaṃ dahatyeka kṣaṇena tu ।
etatte sūkṣmamuddiṣṭaṃ yatpṛṣṭaṃ kulayākulaṃ ।।

asyopavalayo yastu khecarānte vyavasthitaḥ ।
samastavyāpakānandaṃ paraṃ kaulaṃ taducyate ।।

etatpuryaṣṭakātītaṃ dhāradhyānavivarjitaṃ ।
prāṇāyāmavinirmuktamatītā niyatavrataṃ ।।

kaivalyajñānaviṣayamāhitānaṃdalakṣaṇaṃ ।
sthūlasūkṣmavaraṃ caiva parāparaparāparaṃ ।।

nānyadasyātparaṃ kiṃcittrisatvamavinasvaraṃ ।
anuśvāryākṣarapadaṃ ātmasaṃvitti rūpakaṃ ।।

samākhyātaṃ samāsena kimanyatparipṛcchasi ।
svānāmṛtamayaṃ divya(muktyā)svāditamekadhā ।।

yena kenāpi no tasya rocate yanmahītale ।
athaivaṃ varṇṇitāṃ śaktimākarṇya parameṣṭinā ।।

bhūyaḥ papraccha paramā parameśvaramīśvarī ।
ye yaṃ mayā samākhyātā kaulākhyā śaktirīśvarī ।।

kathaṃ sā(myakti)madhyeti kasyaveti maheśvarī ।
kīdṛśonubhavastasyāḥ kathayasva prasādataḥ ।।

bhagavānabravītsarvvaṃ kathayāmi tava priye ।
yasyābhivyaktimatyeti yathā yāti manonmanī ।।
(74a)
puṃsistriyo vā viśveśī tathā varṇṇādharāvarā ।
viśuddhabhaktiyuktasya śivasāyojyameṣyati ।।

vyāvṛttabhuvanāvṛtte śritasaṃsārasaṃśrite ।
mahātaṃtreti yuktasya sakalakṣīṇakarmaṇaḥ ।।

prāgjanmavihitātyantaṃ puṇyopaca * saṃcayāṃ ।
tīvraśaktinipātādvā divyapratyakṣadarśayet ।।

kāmākāmī samāyogādvyānādvā parameśvarāt ।
sadaiva pavanasyāsādaculakramayogataḥ ।।

atisnehopacārādvā siddhāsiddhavidhātataḥ ।
vāṅmanaḥkāyadṛṣṭya - saṃśleṣā layanā sadā ।।

saṃsargālokanāsthānadhyānāsananiveśanāt ।
guropadeśā deveśi guruvaktrādavāpyete ।।

kuladevakulācārya kulāmnāya kulakramāt ।
kulārddhaṃ vedavidhinā japate nānyathāṃ hi sā ।।

snānopavāsahomādi kliśyamānairapi priye ।
na sadā dhyāna nirataiḥ na prāṇāyāmatatparaiḥ ।।

neṣṭā pūjairanaśanairvvikalpena * vilpakaiḥ ।
gurūpadeśavidhinā na kathaṃcidavā(pya)te ।।

kadācitkākatālīyanyāyenāsāditāpi hi ।
mohamānīya niyatamunmādaṃ saṃprayacchati ।।

kulabhraṃśamano kuryātsamyagīśvarabhāṣitaṃ ।
tasmātsarvvaprayatnena vidhivatsamupācaret ।।

akṣayo yogayuktātmā gurudevāgnipūjakaḥ ।
ahiṃsā sarvvabhūtānāṃ śivapādābjabhāvitaḥ ।।

bhaktyā paramayā yukto yogināṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ।
(74b)
saṃdhyoyāsanasīlasya sadā dhyānākṛtaśramaḥ ।।
prāpnoti kulasadbhāvamavikalpastadicchayā ।। ।।

jayadrathe kālasaṃkarṣaṇyāṃ śaktini(dvargaḥ) ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

kulāveśakulācaryaṃ kulāmnāyaṃ kulakramaṃ ।
kulyarthavadhahatyete procyaṃte ke maheśvaraḥ ।।

svacchadatvaṃ svakartṛtvaṃ guṇāṣṭakavidhāyinī ।
mahāmṛte mahādhārā yaiḥ parijñāyate vibhoḥ ।।

sado yāgataśiṣyasya kasmādvopagatasya vā ।
darśanādarśanādvāpi saṃbhāṣādabhivādanāt ।।

bhavedādau samutkṣepo romāṃco vā tadāśrayaḥ ।
kṣaṇātsamastanāḍīnāṃ udayo dṛṣṭalakṣaṇaḥ ।।

aśarīriṇi vāṇī vā siddhābhigaditāthavā ।
śruyate niśi suptasya taṃ prati svapnadarśanaṃ ।।

niyojanaṃ tathā liṃgapratināṃ * cacālaṃ ।
ajātanāvicinvāsa samāgatyopasajyate ।।

devyābhijñānato vāpi suptotrā śṛṇuyādvacaḥ ।
paśyate vātamevādau sadā yuktaparisthitaḥ ।।

kuladiśoyamādeśa siddha vai pārameśvaraḥ ।
kulācāramato vakṣye brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyopi vā ।।

vaiśyaśūdrontyajo vāpi yo vāsyo vā sureśvari ।
divyajñānasamāyuktasthiradhīrdivyadeśakaḥ ।।

dambhapreṣaṃ ca rahitā divyacorakṛtādaraḥ ।
(a)mantrataṃtrakuhikāścākṣudrākṣudrakarmakṛt ।।

ananyamaṃtrajāpī vā na (cānyo) pāśanaḥ kvacit ।
(75a)
sa cāsmabhaktidadyukto na bahiliṃgapūjakaḥ ।।

ajijñā surasaṃsargī vītadveṣo vimatsaraḥ ।
anīrtho krodhalobhābhyāṃ na kadācidudrutaḥ * ।।

ābhūmilagnamānena namau nānātivādakaḥ ।
adharmapāpmāsaṃtuṣṭo yūtheṣva snānabhojanaḥ ।।

anupoṣī tathā snāyī vītatīrtha kriyāparaḥ ।
priyavākpriyaśiṣyaśca liṃgagrahaṇavarjitaḥ ।।

sadāvicitraliṃgī vā sarvvānugrahakṛtkṣamī ।
avikalpī na saṃkalpī maitrādi ca catuṣkabhṛk ।।

kuśabdena mahādevīparaṃ padamanāmayaṃ ।
layastatra bhavechakteryasmāttasmātkulaṃ smṛtaṃ ।।

tadā vāreṇa varttedyaḥ samaloṣṭhāsma kāṃcana ।
advitīyopapannātmā kulācāryaḥ sa ucyate ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

anyaśāstreṣu deveśa tvayā nigaditaṃ purā ।
caturṇṇāmeva varṇṇānāmācāryatvaṃ kramādbhavet ।।

atrānyajānāma * hi yaduktaṃ parameśvara ।
viruddhametalliṃgesminmamāścaryamidaṃ mahat ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

vaineyakeṣu sarvveṣu yathaivoktaṃ mayā purā ।
asminpunaḥ siddhakaule sadyaḥ pratyayalakṣaṇe ।।

yasya dhyeyamabhivyaktā śaktirādyāmanonmanī ।
nicchvāsocchvāsarahitā sa evaṃ parameśvaraḥ ।।

tasyāvayo vimuktatvānna bhavetkiṃcidantaraṃ ।
yathaivāhaṃ tathaivatvaṃ yathaivatvaṃ tathā hyasau ।।
(75b)
saṃpūjya satataṃ devi sarvvavyāpi sureśvari ।
brahmahatyāśvamedhādyairnāsau lipyeta bhāmini ।।

name priyaścaturvvedī na dveṣyaḥ śvapacopi me ।
yasyai tadvidyate jñānaṃ sa me devi sadā priyaḥ ।।

ācārya savimo * ca sa guruḥ sa sadāśivaḥ ।
taṃ prāpya ko na mucyeta baṃdhanādbhavasaṃbhavāt ।।

sacchettāsarvvapāpānāṃ garutmāniva bhogināṃ ।
sā pragṛhnāti bhuvanaṃ līlayāpyavalokanāt ।।

kulasnānaṃ tu śiṣyāṇāṃ abhiṣekaḥ prakīrttitaḥ ।
kathayāmi samāsena yathāvadavadhāryatāṃ ।।

ekaliṃge śmaśāne vā saritāṃ saṃgamepi vā ।
mahodadhervvā puline mahātte catvarepi ca ।।

mahāsamarabhūmau vā mahadbhūmi gṛhepi vā ।
mahāśrīmaṃḍape vāpi harmyapṛṣṭhepi vā priye ।।

yathābhiruci te vāpi pradeśe vā dvi śobhite ।
pīṭhaṃ samālikhe taṃtu yathā vibhavamānataḥ ।।

caturasraṃ caturbhiśca pādairvṛttamathāpi vā ।
paṃcabhirnamirekhābhiḥ paṃcavarṇṇojjvalaṃ śubhaṃ ।।

madhyeṣṭapatre nalinaṃ sitamālālakesaraṃ ।
tatkarṇṇikāyāmamṛtaṃ śaktyādhāraṃ nidhāpayet ।।

tataḥ suvarṇṇarajataṃ tāṃmramṛnmayameva vā ।
akālamūlakalaśaṃ śitacaṃdanacarcitaṃ ।।

tasyopari nyasedyogī kaṃṭhasūtropavītinaṃ ।
mukhāhitaśubhāśvattha cūtapallavalāṃcchitaṃ ।।
(76a)
saśaṃkhadarpaṇabhṛtaṃ mahāmṛtasamaplutaṃ ।
navavastraparītāṅgaṃ sahiraṇyaṃ samāhitaḥ ।।

mahāśaktisamārūḍhaṃ mahārūpāṃ mahodadhiṃ ।
hamālakṣmiyajetatra mahāmarmavidhāriṇī ।।

prayāgā varuṇā kollā aṭṭahāsā jayantikā ।
caritrā ca tathaikāmraṃ koṭivarṣaṃ tathāṣṭamaṃ ।।

prāgdakṣiṇena patreṣu prāgīrṇṇādyāṃtagāṃ nyaset ।
patrāgreṣu ca brāhmādyā pūjayitvā yathākramaṃ ।।

devyo mahāmṛtāpānapramodāhitadṛṣṭayaḥ ।
pūrvve pavitramātā ca keśamātātha dakṣiṇe ।।

paścime meṣamātātha dvijamātā tathottare ।
malamātā tathāgneyyāṃ kṛṣṇamātātha nairṛte ।।

tvaṅmātā cāpi vāyavyāṃ puṣpamātā tathaiśvare ।
pradīpadhūpanaivedyamajñāsumā(śi)ṣye * * ।।

vināśāḍhyaṃ vinātarpya tatonutvākṣamāpayet ।
tatastatpadmavinyasya saṃmohaṃ pīṭhapūrvvakaṃ ।।

payodadhi sarittīrtha samāveśaṃ ghaṭaṃ nyaset ।
nyastvābhiśecayecchiṣyaṃ lakṣajāpātkulādhipa ।।

tenābhiṣiktaśiṣyosau jāyate siddhibhājanaḥ ।
kaulamāpnoti sadbhāvaṃ yaduktaṃ paṃcamonmanaṃ ।।

ko gacchati layaṃ śaktikhyātamāsītkulaṃ tava ।
kramastu saṃkramastasya pṛthaktvāyātmagocaraḥ ।।

sa bāhyābhyantarānaṃdapramāṇopāttalakṣaṇā ।
kulasyākramaṇāddevi kulakrama iti smṛtaḥ ।।

kalātatvapadairvvarṇṇairmantraiḥ sa bhavanairayaṃ ।
(76b)
aśvaprapaṃco vyākhyātaḥ ṣadvidhopi mayā tava ।।

vijñānagopanādanyacchastreṣu prathitokhilaḥ ।
asminmunaḥ siddhakaule yatkulārddheti kīrttitaḥ ।।

sāntarātmā vibhāgastha pra(vo)vya śivayā śivaḥ ।
tasyāntarātmanaḥ proktā prabodho yo maheśvari ।।

rājanāḍī vibhāgena śivaśaktyā hyamoghayā ।
kulādvavedha ityuktastadvedārdha śivo bhavet ।।

sadāvedhaṃ vidadhate yataḥ saṃkrāmayogataḥ ।
nopasarpanti karmāṇi tṛṇāniva samīkṣyate ।।

sparśamātrādabhivyakti mātrasaṃskārayojanāt ।
brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ prākṛtaṃ dahate kṣaṇāt ।।

vyādhibhirnābhibhūyeta bhavetsarvvajanaḥ priyaḥ ।
na viśaṃ kramate dehe na ca hiṃsanti hiṃsakāḥ ।।

vinaṣṭarddhāntasaṃghātaparaṃ padamavāpnuyāt ।
saṃdhābhyāsā niyuktastu tallayastanmanaḥ śaśī ।।
aṇimādiguṇaiśvaryaṃ tuṣṭo nyasya prayacchati ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre kālasaṃkarṣaṇyāmabhiṣekādvavedhaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

prāk tvayā yadupanyastaṃ pīṭhakṣetrādivarjitaṃ ।
digvidi * se rahitā mama vidyārcanādikaṃ ।।

idānīṃ pīṭhasaṃdoha saṃdohoyaṃ mahānmṛma ।
adhyātmani yathā hyetaneṣātvaṃ vaktumarhasi ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

devi bāhya krameṇaiva sādhya ādhyātmiko vidhiḥ ।
vinā bāhyena vidhinā na ve * * dhyākāṃ vidhiṃ ।।
(77a)
dṛṣṭenādṛṣṭasaṃsiddhiḥ pratītiḥ saṃprayachati ।
tena bāhya krame proktaḥ ādhyātmikaḥ * * śṛṇu ।।

varāṃgamekaliṃgasyātyāyuḥ pitṛvanaṃ smṛtaṃ ।
saritāṃ saṅgamo nāsā nābhiḥ prokto mahodadhiḥ ।।

hṛnmaho catvaro devi mukhayuktaṃ mahāhavaḥ ।
pādāṃguṣṭho ca bhūveśma bhruvormadhye tu maṃḍalaḥ ।।

ghaṃṭikāgraṃthi harmyaṃ syādūrddhaśroto yathāruciḥ ।
cicchaktyālokanaṃ devi pīṭhe likhanamucyate ।।

madhyaṃ tu madhyame coktaṃ padmamukto mahāmatiḥ ।
karṇṇikāmṛtasaṃbhūtiḥ kuṇḍakuṃbhāṇḍa ucyate ।।

naṃdanaṃ pallavāstasya bhūtāśvaccho bhavāt priye ।
bahirmukhaṃ bhavecchaṃkhaṃ tadattavyomadarpaṇaṃ ।।

pararūpaṃ mahāśāntaṃ mahārūpamihocyate ।
mahālakṣmī mahāmāyāmātmādhānamanusmaret ।।

prayāgādīni pīṭhāni dalānyaṣṭau bhavanti tu ।
tiṣṭhanti sa niviṣṭhāstu brāhmyādyā devadevatāḥ ।।

gurudūtistathā pārśve bhujau kaṃṭhastathāṣṭamaḥ ।
pradakṣiṇāmavitrādyā jñātavyā kulamānataḥ ।।

manonmanīkulā vidyā tayā pṛṣṭobhiṣecitaḥ ।
unmanaskaṃdhamāyāti yatra sā śaktirīśvarī ।।

tāmabhyasya praśāṃtātmā tvachaṃdatvamavāpnuyāt ।
vivikto dehamutsṛjya prayāti paramaṃ padaṃ ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

siddhakaulaṃ tvayākhyātamadhune vetadakṣayaṃ ।
kathamabhyāsamasyāpi proktavānparameśvara ।। ।।
(77b)
śrībhairava uvāca ।।

siddhakaulamidaṃ devi yaduktaṃ na tadanyathā ।
yatastatvādhvaśuddhyādi kṛpākāraṇavarjitaṃ ।।

ālokanādupanyastā muktiḥ sā tu bhayātmanaḥ ।
siddhīnāmuttamāsiddhirmuktireva varānane ।।

prāsaṃginyo pravarttante tanmūlāḥ sarvvasiddhayaḥ ।
jñātena jñeyamāsādya jñeyānmu(kti)raneśvarī ।।

jñeyopapannasya punaḥ siddhayo vighnavat priye ।
balānpravalatāṃ yānti satyoyaṃ kaulaniścayaḥ ।। ।।

।। jayadrathe kālasaṃkarṣaṇyāṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

rā(jra)nāḍītikā proktā tasyā vedhaṃ kimucyate ।
bhuktimuktisamānyeti yena jñānena śaṃkara ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

dvāsaptatisahasrāṇi yānyuktāni mayā purā ।
nāḍi - tiryamūrddhādhaḥ pratilomānulomataḥ ।।

tāsāṃ madhye pradhānaikā suṣumnākhyeti kīrttitā ।
vicitranāḍīti ca sā tathā cāmṛtavāhinī ।।

tayāntarātmanaścāpi vedha ityucyate ddhanaḥ ।
viṣuvaḥ sa tu vijñeyaḥ śaktimuktaḥ karoti taṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

savidheyaḥ kathaṃ vedha * viddhasyānuvacchaṣaṃ ।
kīdṛśānyasya cihnāni kathaya(svapra)sādataḥ ।।।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

dīrghādvapreṣaṇāyāsa dravyastrīprārthanādibhiḥ ।
ādau parīkṣitaṃ śiṣyaṃ upasannaṃ jitendriyaṃ ।।

mayūrakaṃdagrīvaṃ ca mṛdukāyopaveśitaṃ ।
brahmāsanena sukhinaṃ nāsāgrāhitanārakaṃ ।।
(78a)
pradīpaśikhisaṃsthāne nocce naiva ca nimnake ।
adaṃśamaśakonoṣṇe nāti vā yo na śītale ।।

norddhe * mena saṃkīrṇṇe cittāhlādavidāyinī ।
brāhmādīnāṃ tu devīnāmātmano gurave tathā ।।

kṣaṇapramāṇavicchuddhaścidvyāpāravivarjitaḥ ।
aṣṭāṣṭapadavinyāsādaṣṭadhāmyekatatvataḥ ।।

somasūryānilaprotamantarātmā namātmanā ।
nūdbhinnabhrāmayettāvadyāvadānaṃdalakṣaṇaṃ ।।

adhorddhavedhena tato mahālakṣmyā samālabhet ।
etatpālanāddevi * chinnamūla ivadrumaḥ ।।

atha śīghratarāmicchettataḥ preryādi vāhinī ।
taṃ gṛhītvātmanipuṇaṃ kṣetrakṣiptvā pravarttayet ।।

parvvatānapi vedhena sthiralakṣaḥ kṣaṇena tu ।
tataḥ prodbhidyakālādvā ātyaṃtikapadāvadhiḥ ।।

uttiṣṭhate sadānaṃdā vidvādvānaṃ manonmanī ।
anyājye vātisaṃsarge tyaktvā mṛtakasūtake ।।

dvāsaptatyapi nāḍīnāṃ sahasrāṇi vibhidyasā ।
navātmā vipatigraṃthī buddhyeṣṭavaravarṇṇini ।।

sarvvaṃ vyāpya pravartteta sabāhyābhyantaraṃ punaḥ ।
mahodadhimahāvartta mahāmahamivātave ।।

mahālakṣmimamā(pro)ti mahāmaṃtharamaṃthara ।
svapnavatpaśyate sarvvaṃ padārthā samatīṃdriyā ।।

pratyakṣapratyayo hyeṣa satyoyaṃ kaulaniścayaḥ ।
śiṣyapratyaya hetostu kuryātkautūhalādyati ।।
(78b)
tadaikamevāvayavaṃ kṣobhayellakṣalakṣaṇāṃ ।
sa eva stobhamāyāti tāvannātmā na mānayet ।।

bhogahastaṃ bhujastobhaṃ kuṃbhālaṃbhanameva ca ।
mahāvanaspatikṣobhaṃ pratimā liṃgacālanaṃ ।।

yatra tatra bhāvadicchā tattadā veśayedbhutaṃ ।
na tithirnātra nakṣatraṃ nopavāso nayaṃ tathā ।।

najarakuṃḍasrugbhāṇḍaṃ na caryāvratadhāriṇāṃ ।
sarvvamicchati (pu)jyastu kurute nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

tena deyamabhaktāya na prapaṃcavidhāyine ।
na sadā dravya lubdhāya na caitadupajīvine ।।

vadāṃbike devaloke nālpasatvena tārkike ।
nā(sarvva)padravapare naiva yogajñagupsake ।।

parasparakṛtasparddhe jñānavijñāsanena tu ।
kuryāccāryena vidhivajjñānatā kulapaddhatiṃ ।।

kulābhiṣekaṃ kṛtvā tu prakāśya jñānamuttamaṃ ।
anyathā kurute yastu parīkṣā rahite trayaṃ ।।

bhaktyaṃte sa mahogrābhiḥ śākinībhirna saṃśayaḥ ।
vyādhibhirnābhibhūyeta nṛpadasphunipīḍitaḥ ।।

vighnavyāghātavihito narakaṃ yāti rauravaṃ ।
śiṣyopyaviditārthatvānnābhiṣṭaphalabhāgbhavet ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kālasaṃkarṣaṇyāṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

athopajātavedhasya śivaśaktyā hyamoghayā ।
śṛṇu cihnāni deveśi daiśikānugrahecchayā ।।

kaṃpasvedotha romāṃco naispadyaṃ spandanaṃ tathā ।
nicchvāsocchvāsayostulyaṃ rūpasya ca viparyayaḥ ।।

calanaṃ ca kṣitityāgo navadhāvedha ucyate ।
(79a)
pṛthaktvārūpameteṣāṃ saṃkṣepātsaṃpracakṣyate ।।

pāṇipādorujaṃghena sa śiro vāgbhaja * jaḥ ।
ekanāsāpuṭaśrautrabhrunetrāṇāṃ viśeṣataḥ ।।

kaṃpājñāyetada * dhā divyānubhavagocaraḥ ।
kapola bālakaṃ poka sveda eṣa caturvvidhaḥ ।।

bhuje vividhagātreṣu sarvveṣu parito bhavet ।
sarvvāvayavanaiṣyadyaṃ kāṣṭhapāṣānaloṣṭhavat ।।

athaikāvayavāstambhaḥ svabhāvasthasya bhāvayet ।
ciṃciṇīcīravākādi mānobhivyaṃjako dhvaniḥ ।।

mṛdaṃgakāhalāśaṃkhamahodadhipayomucāṃ ।
bhramaraughaghoṣaghaṃṭādvipādīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ ।।

jvalajvalanavātādi maṃdaśabdāvabhāsanāt ।
naiṣpadyaṃ trividhaṃ proktaṃ caturddhā spaṃdanaṃ bhavet ।।

ādi hṛdgraṃthisaṃsthānakramocāraṇayogataḥ ।
svābhāvikaṃ parityajya pravartte tatra bhau gatiḥ ।।

visragddhāvādbhuto vāpi sa śabdo dūragāminau ।
sthaulyametatsamākhyātaṃ niśvāsośvāsayogataḥ ।।

ātmānaṃ tu paraṃ paśye paramātmānameva ca ।
adho bhūmeśi saṃtaṃ vā vidhaurantaśiropi vā ।।

dvipasiṃhavarāhaśca vanitā divyayoginī ।
brahmendropendracaṃdrārka munirudrānalānilaṃ ।।

matsakacchapagosarpaśalabholūkabhūruhāṃ ।
rūpānāmātmanirbhāvo rūpasyāyaṃ viparyayaḥ ।।

ṛjukramabhramaccharddigātrākṣepo vighūrṇṇanaṃ ।
(79b)
ujṛṃbhāśītasaṃlāpasaṃkṣobhā hlādamūrchanā ।।

adhaḥ śīrṣorddhapādābhyāṃ parivarttāvaloṭhanaṃ ।
rathacārī yugāvartto gajendralalinaṃ tribhiḥ ।।

dṛgoktaṇaramogrīva troṭasthāṭāṃgamoṭanaṃ ।
savyāpasavyabhramaṇamantarākuṃdaraṃ tathā ।।

calanasya caturvi(gddhā)nyete bhedā udāhṛtāḥ ।
nirādhāratvamākāśe coṣṇāvasthānamicchayā ।।

maṃḍūkaplutivinyāsasvatorvvā vṛddhirekataḥ ।
sannibaṃdhvāsanasyaiva jānubhyāṃ sa vyavasthitaḥ ।।

bhūmau helāsamākṣiptakukuṃdaśca vimarṣaṇaṃ ।
paṃcaprakārasthā gobhū(ṅbhū)merbhūtabhayāvaha ।।

ityeṣa kathitonekacitracihnopalakṣitaḥ ।
yathārthanāmāvinyāsādvividho vedhavistaraḥ ।।

aśvakāśyo pradeśaśca sadāvinayacāriṇā ।
prāpya bodhānupūrvveṇa śaktimenāṃ manonmanīṃ ।।

kasminupāstakā siddhiryoginaḥ saṃprayacchati ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

kalāsaṃbhāvamataścā(gneccha)vijñātasaṃkulaṃ ।
kathayāmi samāsena rahasyaṃ snehatastathā ।।

sṛṣṭinyāyobhiyogena calavarṇṇayisādhanī ।
upāsyamānā kurute balipalitanāśanaṃ ।।

padbhyāṃ pādapracāraṃ tu cyāyāmānyāmamiṃdriyaṃ ।
nāḍīcakrasthitaṃ nābhau sarvvabhūtagataṃ hṛdi ।।

kṣutvipāśāṃ japaṃ kaṇṭhe devīyacchravaṇaṃ śrutau ।
nāsāyāṃ gaṃdhavijñānaṃ durādgrahaṇamocanaṃ ।।

gaṃdhanairgaṃdhakaraṇaṃ pratyānayanalakṣaṇaṃ ।
(80a)
akṣordūro vyavahitaṃ tathā tahritalakṣaṇaṃ ।।

jihvāyāṃ ṣaḍrasā vyāpti samastavyāpi vastunaḥ ।
bhrūmadhye jagadaiśvaryaṃ amaratvaṃ hi laṃbake ।।

saṃsthāne devatācāraṃ khecarānte parāparaṃ ।
parātparataraṃ nāsti satyoyaṃ kaulaniścayaḥ ।।

khageśvaraṃ jayaṃ cakraṃ pramāṇādabhivarttate ।
tadārūḍhasadā devi khecaratvamavāpnuyāt ।।

athavā kautukābhyāsādatikramya khageśvaraṃ ।
kuryādyābhilakhite viṣaye grāmapaṭṭane ।।

tatpaśyati na saṃdeha karāmalakavat priye ।
tadā rūḍhaḥ sadā devi khecaratvamavāpnuyāt ।।

tadā rūḍho mahādevi yadevecchitakathituṃ ।
tadyatraivāti saṃdadhyā tatraivā kathayedrutaṃ ।।

vaihāya samatikramya yojayata tripiṣṭape ।
yadya caivābhisaṃdhatte lokapālālayaṃ prati ।।

tattatpaśyatyasaṃdehādveti vṛttāttamīśitaṃ ।
vāre grahāṇāṃ sarvveṣāṃ nakṣatrāṇāṃ ca veṣṭitaṃ ।।

sumerau suvavistāraṃ pravāhaṃ sarito divāḥ ।
ṛjvī kṛtā tu yā śakti lakṣakoṭyāvabhāsanā ।।

sāvai nirghṛsya mānā sā sivārotthāgnikaṇopamāḥ ।
vinirgacchati yāvantyā visphurantaḥ sphuliṃgakāḥ ।।

tāvatyapupurastāni puramutthāpayatyapi ।
virakto cātmanāyogī kāyamucchraṣṭamicchati ।।

tato bhūmimahādhāmnastaḍicaṃdrānukāriṇī ।
samākṛṣyorddhagāṃ śaktiṃ śmaśāne sanniveśayet ।।
(80b)
tasmāccatuṣpacāntasthāmākarṣadbhujamavyaye ।
piṃḍībhūtāṃ samākṛṣya harmyapṛṣṭhaṃ samānayet ।।

yogodvāreṇa niḥkrāntā vaihāya sapadorddhagāṃ ।
utkrāmayeta saṃdeho dehaṃ tyaktvāti yogavān ।।

adhunātmaśarīraṃ hi vyādhivārdhikya pīḍitaṃ ।
tyaktumicchati nāgā sā jighṛṣya ca punaḥ punaḥ ।।

tato vaihāyase cakre saṃdhayecchavamīhitaṃ ।
ūrddhapādamadhaḥ śīrṣaṃ tasmin tyaktuṃ na kaṃdare ।।

prāguvanyastavinyastadutkrāme saṃkrameti ca ।
krameṇa yogadvāreṇa sṛṣṭinyāye niyojanāt ।।

pṛthvīparyantamāpnoti yāvadyāgī jigīṣayā ।
saṃkramyā tiṣṭhate tāvannātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

tathāpi tasya ākhyātaṃ etatsaṃkramaṇakramaṃ ।
athātaḥ kramavinyāsaṃ vakṣyavijñānakautukaṃ ।।

dūrasthamīpsitaṃ sādhyaṃ sacityāsannamātmanaḥ ।
tatpaśyendradhanurvvarṇṇāvarṇṇarāsī visarjayet ।।

tatsahasramathodbhinnaṃ sahasradalapuṣkare ।
jīvātmanaḥ pratipatti sthiralakṣontarātmanāṃ ।।

tatastamāhitātaṃka sphuratsvadyotasannibhaṃ ।
bhujaṃgavadanākramya vidhvādvaḥ pauṣkarodaraṃ ।।

adhiṣṭhānaṃ samādāya śmaśāne saṃniveśayet ।
[tatasta]ātmanāpi samāgamya saṃtiṣṭheta catuṣpathe ।।

yathātmani tathā tasminyathā tasmintathāni * ।
yuṃjāno yogamevaṃ ca saptarātrādataṃdritaḥ ।।

śākramasyākrameddevi kiṃ punaḥ kṣudramānuṣān ।
(81a)
kārayeta yathābhīṣṭasarvvāśceṣṭāstadātmikā ।।

dānaṃ pūjādisatkāraprabhāvā bhaktivatsale ।
jīvanidvāra * mataḥ prakuryādyadi rocate ।।

samutkrānti krameṇaiva tadvicitrā vibhāvanā ।
paryasya raṃdhramārgastho vaihāya sa pade raṇāt ।।

nivārayenmuhūrttena dhātāramapi saṃyamī ।
atha grahaṇamanvicchetpaśoḥ pāśa bhayāvahe ।।

tatastatkrāmya vinyāsādbhūmirūrddhasamīraṇaṃ ।
saṃgamotkṛṣṭiyogena jīvātmānaṃ vicintayet ।।
tato vaihāya sagataṃ vibhṛyānnikhilasthitaṃ ।।

ūrddhaśaktinirodhasthe muhūrtte nayaneśvaraṃ ।
paśuṃ gṛhnāti niyataṃ yāvannādho niyojayet ।।

athānyaṃ yogasadbhāvaṃ tava vakṣyāmi suvrate ।
śarīrasthairyajananaṃ yoginī kālavaṃcanaṃ ।।

saṃyogaśca viyogaśca prāvijñeyo yathākramaṃ ।
saṃyogasya viyogena viyuktaṃ yojanena ca ।।

samutpanna samādhistu samavāyena suvrate ।
yogino yogayuktasya vacanaṃ nirate bhavet ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

soyogaḥ kīdṛśaḥ prokto viyogaścāpi kīdṛśaḥ ।
kīdṛśo bhavate yosyā taṃ saṃyojanayojate ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ativāhikasaṃśleṣā saṃyogā hyātmano bhavet ।
tadviśleṣādviyogastu procyate paramātmanaḥ ।।

pūryaṣṭakasamaṃ śliṣṭo hyātmā kālena kalpyate ।
tyaktātivāhikaguṇo nā(sau) kālena kalpate ।। ।।
(81b)
śrīdevyuvāca ।।

purā puryaṣṭakaṃ proktaṃ tanusūkṣmaṃ tvatā punaḥ ।
tathā tyājyaṃ kathaṃ tyaktuṃ śaktuyātpudgalaṃ svayaṃ ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śvāsāśvāsa(jñā)nādṛtya tyaktvādhaḥ piṃḍamātmanaḥ ।
vaihāya sapadāvasthā kṣaṇātpuryaṣṭakaṃ tyajet ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śvāsocchvāsau parityajya gacchedvaihāyasaṃ yadi ।
kālopayāva evāsau kimanyadvaṃ(vuṣi)ṣyati ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

yogoddhāraṃ bhavedūrddhamavavidvā daśāṃgulaṃ ।
vaihāyaseti vikhyātaṃ asaṃkhyadalakeśaraṃ ।।

cakramākrāntasakalaṃ (ghasmarāstya)nyadūrddhataḥ ।
tadārūḍhapraśāntātmā na mṛtyuvaśamāpnuyāt ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

citrametatsamākhyātaṃ sadasamtyaktavigrahaḥ ।
na vaśaṃ yāti kālasya (āptakāle)na saṃyamī ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

dvidhā kṛtepi hi yathā padmanāle narātsmṛtaṃ ।
śūdraṃ saṃtiṣṭhate sūkṣmaṃ tathā śaktirapi (priye) ।।

muktepyātma * (lo)kāya vyā(pti)yāgādadhorddhataḥ ।
sū(kṣme)nāpi susūkṣmātmā naiva kālena kalpate ।।

yathāsyopāsanopāyaṃ śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane ।
āditya maṇḍalāntasthā bhujaṅgena visa(rpiṇī) ।।

dhārāsahasrasaṃpāte vihitekāmṛtārṇṇave ।।
plāvayanti savitaraṃ raudasīkaṃ carāca(raṃ) ।।

kaumudīṃ vukulākāntaṃ jyotiṣmantīṃ vicintayet ।
tasmin sadhumamudrāṃtasphuratkiraṇakaṃdalaṃ ।।
(82a)
locanacchavinirmuktamagnīṃdrapratimopamaṃ ।।

svacchaṃdatva svakarttṛtva maṇimāṇiguṇairyutaṃ ।
sarvvānugrahakartṛtvaṃ jāyate kālavaṃcanā ।।

(jñā)payodheḥ paraṃpāraṃ upagamya sudhāruṇaṃ ।
avimṛyaṃtītetyonaṃ mahārajjuṃ vibhāvayet ।।

pṛthvīrūpadharo yogī tado * sthānuśaṃginaḥ ।
stambhaye dhyānapātrāṇi jalarājo na saṃśayaḥ ।।

cakradvitayanābhisthāṃ pītavarṇṇāṃ garīyasīṃ ।
mahāmā * nijotthānā saṃsthānena sthitisthitāṃ ।।

dhyāyellakṣasamārūḍhā kṣaṇādapi vivasvataḥ ।
stambhayedrathamānyaistu kā kathā kāṣṭhasaṃcayaiḥ ।।

atrānyānyapi ke(pā)ti vidyante yāni kānicit ।
antarbhāvāṃ nivedyāni vijñānānupasevanāt ।।

yogajātīndriyaḥ sarvvā vijñānaviṣayo yataḥ ।
kathaṃ kathayituṃ śaktaḥ svayaṃ vedyaḥ sadānaghe ।।

hiraṇyagarbhoya mahā kila saṃkṣepataḥ purā ।
praṇīta ślokalakṣeṇa sapāṭena tato priye ।।

pradīpādi tu naivāyaṃ pa(kṣi)meva vasokhilaḥ ।
etadevāsya māhātmyaṃ sa śikṣayati yogināṃ ।।

yuṃjanaṃ natra bhāvīni vijñānāni na saṃśayaḥ ।
tasmādabhyāsa evātra sarvvajñatva pravarttakaḥ ।। ।।

jayadrathe kālasaṃkaṇyāṃ ।। ।।

vimuktiḥ prabhavāḥ proktāḥ siddhayaḥ siddhisādhitāḥ ।
saubhāgya bhāgyakāmānāṃ upāptirabhidhīyatāṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।
(82b)
ekaliṃgāśrito yogī jalajāpā na saṃśayaḥ ।
madabhonmādadāyinyo mahāsaubhāgyamāpnuyāt ।।

tasyopāstiṃ tu prathamā śṛṇuṣvekāgramānasā ।
kaśmīrajapayodhānāṃ sa(ni)bhāṃ sarvvatomukhīṃ ।।

aṃvyavacchedadhārābhirbhṛtaḥ kuṃkumacāradhī ।
saṃcityodyatsahasrāṃśu maṃḍalāruṇamaṃbujaṃ ।।

dalāṣṭakaparikṣepaprollasaccārukesaraṃ ।
tatropaviṣṭamātmānaṃ anekābharaṇojvalaṃ ।।

vibudhāśokabaṃdhūkasugaṃdhasragvibhūṣitaṃ ।
jagadāhlādavadanaṃ paramānaṃdanirbharaṃ ।।

dhyāyet pratidinaṃ yogī yāvatṣaṇmāsamātrakaṃ ।
tāvadāyānti satataṃ madanonmattacetasaḥ ।।

ahamahamikāyā sarvvā yoginaḥ prākṛtaḥ striyaḥ ।
na kṣubhyetāḥ samālokye yadiccheddivyasaṃgamaṃ ।।

tataḥ savatsarādivyā gaṃdharvvāpsarasaḥ sadā ।
samāgatyāvatiṣṭhanti prayacchanti yathepsitaṃ ।।

galaṃbakatvasaubhāgye dravagaṃdhānulepanaḥ ।
calacārusamāsaktarasaraktaṃ(ṅgukā)mbaraḥ ।।

bidumālohitodyānasupuṣpamadhumānavaḥ ।
nāda * ghadaśaṃgīta sadā ratisamanvitaḥ ।।

uddaṃḍaśaktikodaṇḍavaihāya samitidhvajaḥ ।
sphuratpaṃcamahābhūtaśaradvyāptajagattrayaḥ ।।

manobhavavratamidaṃ trailokyakṣobhakārakaḥ ।
vibhrāṇaḥ kāmataḥ kāmī kāmādvicarenmahīṃ ।।

ramyodyāneṣu tīrtheṣu devatā yataneṣu ca ।
(83a)
giriśṛṃgeṣu tuṃgeṣu ramyeṣvanyeṣvapi priye ।।

krīḍate kṛtasaṃsarga ābhiḥ sārddhaṃ varānane ।
athavāpi nameveta yogī yogaparāyaṇaḥ ।।

dadatte siddhayoginyastattatkaulikamuttamaṃ ।
saṃpradāyaṃ hi paramaṃ caruṃ vā saptajanmikaṃ ।।

yasminnādito yadyat khecaratvaṃ sadā labhet ।
brahmendropendracāṃdrāṇāṃ * loke ramate sadā ।।

vidyādharopsaroyakṣarakṣobhirabhivaṃditaḥ ।
caturyugopasaṃhāre saṃtyātmānamātmanā ।।

līyate parame vyomni yatra nāmālayeśvarī ।।
bhāgyārohaṇamadhunā saṃpravakṣye samāhitaḥ ।।

yena bhāgyopabhogatvamavāpnotyapasevanāt ।
pramaṃḍapasamāviṣṭo dhautacāmīkaraprabhāṃ ।।

endrībiṃdumatāmiśrāṃ vāṅgacchantīṃ aharniśaṃ ।
udyatpayovudbhudavadatyujvalamilaṃtviṣaṃ ।।

sphuradvicitramāṇikya śreṇiśobhā vibhāvayet ।
udyotavyāptaniḥśeṣa viśvaṃśaśca samabhyaset ।।

bhāgyavān jāyate yogī lakṣmīvān priyadarśanaḥ ।
bhuvanopāhitaḥ śrīkaḥ sadā rājyopabhogavān ।।

ṣaṇmāsādyogayuktastu jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
saṃvatsareṇa yuktasya yuvatatpūjitāśriyaḥ ।।

samāgamyopatiṣṭhaṃte rājānaṃ kiṃkarā iva ।
anayā kurvvato devi sarvvato melinītalāṃ ।।

nidhānātyupatiṣṭhanti vicitrāṇi viśeṣataḥ ।
(83b)
padmaśaṃkhasamopeta kuberopi varānane ।।

āvirbhūyapyathānyāyyaṃ prayacchati yathepsitaṃ ।
dhanairdhānyai hiraṇyaiśca gobhiraśvai(śca) vā gajaiḥ ।।

putraiḥ kalatraiḥ pālatraiḥ parītaḥ paritaḥ punaḥ ।
upayogocitāṃ bhogāṃ upabhuktvālayaṃ vrajet ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

vyādhi vā dhūtakāyasya yenollāsaḥ prajāyate ।
yogino yogayuktasya kathayasva prasādataḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

svalpopanyasya mānasya graṃthasyāsyātivistaraḥ ।
saṃkṣepodvīraṇenāpi āyurvvedaḥ prajāyate ।
tathāpi vividhavyādhi vedanopaśamakramaṃ ।।

pravakṣyāmi samāsena yathāyogabalāyuṣāṃ ।
dvividhā vyādhayaḥ proktā doṣajā karmajāstathā ।।

doṣakṣayena doṣācchāṃ karmajāḥ karmasaṃjñayān ।
nāśaṃ prayānti niyataṃ vyādhe yo vyāptavigrahā ।।

tāpadoṣodayotthānaṃ prāksamākhyāta eva hi ।
pitāmahāya niḥśeṣaṃ āyurvvede purā yayā ।।

karmodbhavānāṃ vyādhīnāmeṣā karmāpanovanī ।
sphaṭikopamā * vaddevyā prīyapādānaraṃjanāt ।।

vātapittaṃ tathā śleṣmaṃ sarvveṣāṃ prāgnibaṃdhanaṃ ।
utpatti kṣetrametaddhi vyādīnāṃ tritayaṃ mahat ।।

vātastu vāta evoktaṃ pṛthvī tasya vilāsinī ।
pittaṃ vahniṃ upanyastaśleṣmā śalila ucyate ।।

teṣāṃ puryaṣṭadhādyānaceṣṭābhirabhivarddhnitaṃ ।
pratipakṣavidhānasvaśaktiśca viniyojanāt ।।
(84a)
nihanti vyādhisaṃdoha mama dehān kṣaṇena tu ।
trayāṇāṃ caiva santānāṃ sannipātaṃ jayetsadā ।।

vyādhīnāṃ prathamāvyādhi jvaratsarvvāṅgasādhakaḥ ।
tridoṣavihitaḥ sadyaḥ tathānyatsaṃnipātikaḥ ।।

taṃ nidhānaṃ parijñāpya yathāvat kramayojanāt ।
yathāyogaṃ vijayate sarvvavyāpī viparyayāt ।।

vyomnasvargapathā dyotīṃ kṣīradhārā tu sāriṇīṃ ।
kṣīrodavelo kṣīrodaṃśī karocchitavigrahaṃ ।।

dhātu madhyagamadhyasthāṃ sarvvāṅgāntasthirasthitāṃ ।
sadā kramādupāsiddhi pakṣā kṣapitayā mayā ।।

nihaṃti rājayakṣmāṇaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
mārttaṇḍamaṇḍalāṃtasthāṃ śītāṃśu svacchadīdhitiṃ ।।

kulālacakravinyāsāddhamantī patiyogataḥ ।
nābhihṛnmūrddhnideśeṣu cintayedvahurūpiṇī ।।

māsopāsanayogena cānusādvyakramācyutiḥ ।
apā karoti niyataṃ kuṣṭāmayamasaṃśayaḥ ।।

pāyupadmodarāṃtasthāṃ bhinnāṃjana ca yacchaviḥ ।
sṛṣṭinyāyaviniṣkrāntīṃ sadā cintayato yateḥ ।।

bhagaṃdaragudhāvatīmāpsānamiti vānikāḥ ।
prathamaṃ yānti niyataṃ ye cānye kāṣṭhajātayaḥ ।।

pāṇipādorujaṃghāṃ sa pārśvahṛtkaṃṭhamūrcasu ।
sarvveṣāmeva yogānāṃ pratipakṣakramājjayaḥ ।।

mahāmṛtamahādhārā dhāraṇodyuktacetasāṃ ।
yā kṣepaprabhavāpyā yo jāyante vyādhayaḥ sadā ।।

tasmātsarvvaprayatnena vyākṣobhaṃ parivarjayet ।
(84b)
vyākṣepena hitaṃ śīghraṃ avāpnoti samīhitaṃ ।। ।।

jayadrathe kālasaṃkarṣaṇyāṃ bhāgyāvarohaṇaṃ ।। ।।
athopadeśadānārha syādavāsāditaḥ śriyaḥ ।।

dehe no deha śeṣasya saṃsthitasya yathāvidhi ।
śṛṇuṣvāyatanaṃ bhūyo na grahārthaṃ yathātmanaḥ ।
vaihāyasādviniḥkramya jvalatkālānalaprabhāṃ ।।

mahā(jā)lavadurmuktāṃ vyāpāro vyāpinī kramaṃ ।
trijagadvyaktibhūtānāṃ caturvvarga vihāriṇīṃ ।।

dhyāyedekamanāḥ sarvvamāvṛtāṃtī carācaraṃ ।
tadīndragopakākāramucchvasaṃti muhurmuhuḥ ।।

śaktyākṛṣṭaṃ praviśataṃ taccharīraṃ vicintayet ।
saṃviṣṭaṃ nābhi hṛdvyoma vaktrasrotrākṣiṣu nyaset ।।

muhūrttaṃ yojayedyāvattavadākṛṣṭa ucchvasan ।
svātmyaṃ kāyamadhiṣṭhāyamamuttiṣṭhanyasaṃśayaḥ ।।

tatastaṃ mṛtakarttavyaṃ svasthāvasthitamicchayā ।
yojayecchaktiyogena vaihāyasapadoddhṛtaṃ ।।

kṣaṇadyojitamātrastu prayatnākramya pudgalaḥ ।
prayāti ṣṭhānemucitaṃ vimukto nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

eṣate yogasadbhāvaḥ tavābhiśyāpito mayā ।
tvayāpi na prakāśyoyaṃ parīkṣarahite sadā ।।

purakṣoma * to va(kṣya) sadyaḥ pratyayalakṣaṇaṃ ।
siṃdūrarucirābhāsamudgacchantī yathecchayā ।।

bhramadvaihāya sā yāgaṃ visphurja nijatejasā ।
vyāptaniḥśeṣavapuṣaṃ kṣobhanī samanusmaret ।।

sthirīkṛtya svayaṃ kāyaṃ sāvye kāye niyojayet ।
(85a)
niyojanonmuhūrttena kṣubhetsāvyo na saṃśayaḥ ।।

tatastadātmako bhūtvā tatpriyastadviceṣṭitaḥ ।
upasṛtya yathāyogaṃ prayacchati yathepsitaṃ ।।

evameke dvayordevi triṣu caturṣu paṃcasu ।
ṣaṭsu saptatadāṣṭāsu yāvatsauramate naraḥ ।।

pureṣu varastrīṇāṃ karmaṇātyabhisaṃdhitā ।
kṣobhayet kṣaṇamātreṇa kṣobhiṇyākhyā na saṃśayaḥ ।।

strīpuṃ napuṃsaka purā samavāyaḥ punaḥ smṛtaḥ ।
purā draṣṭumatenūrvva viṣapaṃcātha maṃḍalaṃ ।।

yasmānmanasiviśrāmameti vastu mahītale ।
tatvadākrāntaśaktāṃke mataḥ praṇavamānasaṃ ।।

yathāvidhiṃ samālaṃvya śaktirādyā niyojitā ।
tathāvidhi samāhāraraṃjitaṃ svārkatejasā ।।

saptarātropayogena saṃkṣobhyābhimukhaṃ nayet ।।
na tu kā(ryā)vicārātte satyamīśvarabhāṣinaṃ ।।

yathādhvanyāsagamanaṃ śṛṇu śuśroṇi tatvataḥ ।
yena bhūgocaro yogī kṣaṇādyāti yathepsitaṃ ।।

vātacakraviniḥkrāntā varddhantī vyomamaṇḍalaṃ ।
kapola(pāṃ)vībāhyānta prādyatālīṃ vicintayet ।।

sadāsaṃkramaṇaṃ kurvvan saṃcittetsamanukramāt ।
saśvatparijñāya javaṃ sthānī bhīṣṭe layaṃ nayet ।।

evamabhyāsasaṃyogādahorātreṇa trīṇyapi ।
paryaṭitvā samāyāti jayanti jagataḥ priye ।।

tathāti bhojanaṃ pānamajñānamativāṃcchati ।
lokānāṃ kautukakṛte tata eva samabhyaset ।।
(85b)
pūrvve saurvvanalālokaṃ saṃbhukṣaṇamilacyute ।
jvalanmahāśmaśānāgne chinnamūlāṃ vicintayet ।।

tatsarpakṛṣṇasarpābhā sumāyāṃtī mahā havet ।
yāvatpramāṇaṃ purataḥ pānamannaṃ niveditaṃ ।।

tāvatpibatyenādhyātmo bhakṣayatyavicārataḥ ।
eta utkrāmamuddiṣṭo dvitīyaḥ saṃkramo bhavet ।।

tṛtīya ākramāddevi caturthagraha ucyate ।
vicāraḥ paṃcamo hyevaṃ antaḥkaraṇapaṃcamaṃ ।।

ṛnmanasyodaye khyātaṃ nānyasminsamudāhṛtaṃ ।
yathātmanastathānyeṣāṃ bahūnāmapi yojitaṃ ।।

karoti nātra saṃdeho yathākāmārthanirṇṇayaṃ ।
kiṃtu sarvvaistadārambhavighnopasamanapradāṃ ।।

sadāśivo parigatāṃ mahāmūrttivatīṃ parāṃ ।
mahākapālabharaṇāṃ kapālasthāṃ kapālinīṃ ।।

divyāmṛta na kāpā(līṃ) kapālastha na tarpayet ।
asyāstarpaṇa * * devi bhuvanatraya saṃsthitā ।।

tarpitāḥ prīti māyānti yoginyo yojitātmanaḥ ।
vidhūmavighnavyomoha vaimanasya viceṣṭitaṃ ।।

saṃprayacchati sadbhāvaṃ yatatkaulamatīṃdriyaṃ ।
siddhayaśca yathānyāyaṃ saumanaṣkodayoditāḥ ।।

rahasyametatparamaṃ tava premnā prakāśitaṃ ।
trisaṃdhyamadhunāpyūrddhaṃ kimanyatparipṛcchasi ।।

iti viracitacakraśrīmahāmīnacihnaḥ,
samuditakṛtakṛtyo vācitantraṃ svatantraṃ ।
sucitamathaprayogorddātmanasphodayākhyaḥ,
sakalajananutaśrī kāmarūpo vidhāya ।। ।।
(86a)
।। iti jayadrathe jīvapratyānayapurakṣobha unmādayaḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi hṛdayaṃ sarvvakāmadaṃ ।
yena sarvvaguṇāvāsaḥ sādhakendro bhaviṣyati ।।

tenedaṃ hṛdayādīśaṃ śṛṇu parvvatanaṃdini ।
sarvvakarmakaraṃ sāraṃ vidveṣaṇakaraṃ mahat ।।

maṃtroddhāraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvekamanādhunā ।
harāspadaṃ samāsādya vidhiṃ sodhya prayatnataḥ ।।

mahāśṛgupasaṃliptāṃ kṛ(ttha)maṃtreśvarīṃ yajet ।
saṃpūjya proddharenmaṃtrī vidyāṃ ghoraparākramāṃ ।।

mahādhāmaṃ mahānādaṃ dhāmarṇṇaṃ punareva tat ।
mukhacāraṇayākrāntaṃ biṃdunādavibhūṣitaṃ ।।

piṃḍametanmahāraudraṃ sarvvatrailokya bhakṣakaṃ ।
tribiṃduyuktaṃ varuṇāṃ karttanaṃ kevalaṃ tataḥ ।।

bāhuṃ vāruṇayā yuktaṃ śāntaṃ sūryatrayāṃkitaṃ ।
ajīvakaṭhasaṃyuktaṃ ṣaḍarṇṇaṃ mantramuddhṛtaṃ ।।

devadevyā mahābhāge vidveṣotpādanaṃ mahat ।
ekavīrasamākhyātaṃ muṃḍapāda vivarjitaṃ ।।

ṣaṭkoṇabhuvanāyāntaryajeda tena bhairavī ।
kāpotavarṇṇācchurite caturdvāropaśobhite ।।

vitānā hyupasaṃyukte digvāso yāgamārabhet ।
mahāśaṃkhārghapātraṃ tu kṣatajena prapūritaṃ ।।

tenārcayeta deveśīṃ sphutkālāntakopamāṃ ।
mahāpralayavātāliśatavegāṃ bhayānakāṃ ।।

mahāmāyā paṭākṣepakṣapitārigalolvaṇāṃ ।
nirmāṃśāsnāyucarmāsthi śeṣāṃ kākolisannibhāṃ ।।
(86b)
bhujāṣṭakamahottuṃgabhrāntabrahmāṇḍamaṇḍapāṃ ।
tridaśāripuraṃdrīṇāṃ mukhapaṃkajakālikāṃ ।।

dveṣayantī jagatsarvvamābrahma bhuvanāntagaṃ ।
sūrpavātaṃ pramuṃcantī jvalanodbhavavigrahāṃ ।।

ulūkāsanamārūḍhāṃ bhrukuṭī kuṭile kṣaṇāṃ ।
evaṃ dhyātvā devadevīṃ tarpayedvibhavaṃ yathā ।।

alivalyopahārādyaiḥ sāravairvvividhaistathā ।
kusumairvvividhairdevi nānāvādyopahārakaiḥ ।।

evamabhyarcya deveśīṃ tataḥ sādhanamārabhet ।
japedakṣarasaṃkhyākaṃ lakṣameva gatavyathaḥ ।।

japānte plavate vyomni bhairavyā sadṛśo bhavet ।
lakṣajāpācca deveśi madhyamā siddhisādhanaṃ ।।

vidadhāti na saṃdeho vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
daśasāhasrajāpena sāmānyaṃ devi sādhayet ।।

tadidānīṃ vidhāsyāmi śṛṇu vīrendravallabhe ।
nāyasaṃpu(ṣva)sādhyābhyāṃ vidveṣeṇa padairyutaṃ ।।

mahāvātaprayogena jagadvidveṣayiṣyati ।
dvikakośeyavadanau mahiśāśvamukhau tu vā ।।

saṃcintyasaudhyau subhage bahupraharaṇāṃkitau ।
pītalocanasaṃyuktau praharantau parasparaṃ ।।

vyomnirūḍho mahābhāge devadevīṃ japettataḥ ।
evaṃ vidveṣayet sarvvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaraṃ ।।

śuklamāsāṭakairyukte kārayet pratimā tataḥ ।
raktena vīrikālikhya sādhyānāmā samanvitāḥ ।।

vidveṣaya padairyuktā sthāpaye tu parāṅmukhe ।
mūrpavātaṃ tayormadhye prāṇaṃ vidyāṃ subhairavaṃ ।।
(87a)
deyaṃ syātsādhakendreṇa dveṣayetsacarācaraṃ ।
athavā karapatrottha cūrṇṇaṃ vidyābhimaṃtritaṃ ।।

puraṇakāṣṭhātsubhage kṣeptavyaṃ ṛpu madhyataḥ ।
vidviṣṭau tau bhavatāṃ vai sarvvalokasya bhāmini ।।

parasparaṃ ca suśroṇi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
piśācata * koṣṭhottha paṭṭake pravilekhitaṃ ।।

nāma vai sādhya yugmasya viṣaraktālipūritaṃ ।
vidyā saṃpuṭitaṃ devi nadīkūlena veśayet ।।

parāṅmukhau tunau paṭṭau dveṣayetsacarācaraṃ ।
somagrahaṇasāsādya vidyāmāvarttabhairavī ।।

kṣapāntāccharkarā yugmaṃ grāhayedgrahaṇāditaḥ ।
tyāgakāle punaryugmaṃ gṛhītavyaṃ sumadhyame ।।

evaṃ pragṛhya medhāvī sthāpayeta surakṣitaṃ ।
ādimaṃ cātra yadyugmaṃ yābhyāṃ nāma graheṇa tu ।।

vadayetparameśānī vidviṣṭau māraṇāṃtike ।
tau bhavetāṃ mahābhāge tatkṣaṇādeva bhairavī ।।

paścime naiva yugmena vādanā cānulomataḥ ।
pratyānayanamevaṃ syāddevi nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।।

etaddhi dveṣaṇaṃ siddhiṃ tavākhyātaṃ yathārthataḥ ।
atha vaibhītakīṃ gṛhya latāṃ pāṇau vinikṣipet ।।

jvalamānāṃ citiṃ yatra jvalavakadvakāravaiḥ ।
avadagvā pragṛhyaiva gatvā kramaśataṃ punaḥ ।।

vidyā samuccareddevi paścāttāmabhimantrayet ।
sahasreṇa śatenaiva yayāyaṃ yaṃ pratāḍayet ।।

taṃ taṃ mugddha ca vidviṣṭaṃ sarvvalokasya jāyate ।
dvikakauśikapakṣābhyāṃ dhūpaṃ yasya pradīyate ।।
(87b)
sa vidviṣṭho bhavettūrṇṇaṃ yāvadāyurvvarānane ।
tābhyāṃ pakṣau samāhṛtya rātrau pitṛvane sthitaḥ ।।

yābhyāṃ nāmnāṃ maheśāni dveṣaye tāṃ na saṃśayaḥ ।
eva stambhaṃ mayākhyātaṃ śakramaṃḍalamadhyagaṃ ।।

tatra vajrāṃkitaṃ sādhyaṃ cintayitvā tu tadbhavet ।
anyānyapi hi karmāṇi sādhayetparameśvarī ।।

praśenāsvapnakathanaṃ pātrāveśanamuttamaṃ ।
svasthāveśaṃ namavānyatparataṃtropacārataḥ ।।

sādhayatyeva deveśī bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।
eṣā devī samutpannā pādacakrātkaraṃkiṇī ।।
evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ kimanyatkathayāmi ca ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe yāmale caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke rāviṇīcakre vidveṣakālipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrutaṃ sarvvamaśeṣeṇa tvayā khyātaṃ yathārthataḥ ।
adhunā śrotumicchāmi sūtakaṃ yattvayā mama ।।

kathaṃ marmeṣu deveśyaṃ sphuranti parameśvari ।
ekāśī * * sva me brūhi rāviṇyā surasattama ।।

ekā sā vā hyanekā vā bhedai bhinnā vada prabho ।
evamākarṇya bhagavānsurāsuranamaskṛtaḥ ।।
uvāca vacanaṃ śambhurbhavānyā sarahasyakaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi yatpurā sūcitaṃ mayā ।
mahārahasyamatulaṃ sarvvasampadguṇāvahaṃ ।।

sphuradullasadehoccairvyaktā yā vyaktiraṃbikā ।
jagadgarbhā mahāraudrā kālagrāsaikaghasmarā ।।
(88a)
sagabhā ca sabhārā ca sphuṭitāsphuṭi(taṃ) vaṇāṃ ।
pūrvvadhyānasamākhyātā tādṛśī viśvarūpiṇī ।।

yonikaṃdātkaṃdayogī grahādgrahaṇa * svarāt ।
nārākrāntā mahāghorā sughorā bhava mālinī ।।

sphurat prabhāgadorddhāmakiraṇānekasaṃkulā ।
śabdinī raśminikarā dhāmni dhāmni vijṛṃbhate ।।

sphoṭakūṭakamodāntabharaṇādbhairaveśvarī ।
alātakulakalpāntakālāgnikulamaṃḍapāt ।।

vegādudbhrāntamīmādhvi vāḍavāgnikulāntaraṃ ।
ucitāṃ rāviṇīṃ baddhvā mudrāṃ ghoratarolvaṇā ।।

sābaddhā sphuṭitā devī sarvvādhāraiḥ karaṃkiṇī ।
kālāgnicakrātprathamaṃ kūpacakraprayogataḥ ।।

sphuṭitā paramā kālī tatpradā tatprayogataḥ ।
tetra kūpakrameṇaiva samāhāravihāravat ।।

tadeti sā karaṃkeśī sādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ ।
atha saṃjñānukūpāntaścālanāveśanoditāṃ ।।

karaṃkiṇī dvitī * ti pātālākāśasiddhidāṃ ।
sphuraccālanayogena kūrmavatkūrmanāśanāt ।।

pūjitā sa bhavatyugrā karereṇa tu bhairavī ।
pātālākāśasiddhī sā saṃprayacchati sādhake ।।

ataḥ paraṃ sthijādvānta * cakralanayogataḥ ।
vyaktā karaṃkiṇī raudrā svadhāmni pravijṛṃbhitā ।।

evametena yogena yadā kūṭayate tu tāṃ ।
tadeti pūjitā raśmī tṛtīyā vīrasuṃdari ।।

sarvvasiddhipradā jñeyā sadā khecarasiddhidā ।
(88b)
kṛtonyo parameśānī saṃkoce cakratastathā ।।

sphuritā caṃḍakālāgnikoṭikoṭyā yutaprabhā ।
vedhapātādi karaṇī mahāmelāpasiddhidā ।।

te naiva kāraṇenaiva tasya taccarubhojanāt ।
tatsiddhidā samākhyātā devadevī karaṃkiṇī ।।

ataḥ paraṃ trikoṇākhyā cakracakrolvaṇā bhṛśaṃ ।
mahānaṃdarasonmādanityānityaiva kledinī ।।

vyaktā sphuraṇasaṃkoca cālanāveśayogataḥ ।
tatpuṣpāhāracaruṇā mahāmaṃthānayogataḥ ।।

maktimeti mahānaṃdakṣobhanī paramā kalā ।
tayāsaṃko bhavetsarvvamābrahma bhuvanaṃ jagat ।।

paṃcamaṃ dhāmate vyaktaṃ sarvvasiddhipradaṃ priye ।
tavākhyātaṃ mahābhāge durlabhaṃ tridaśeṣvapi ।।

ataḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ mahācakraṃ yadā saṃkṣobhitaṃ tayā ।
brahmagraṃthyabhidhānaṃ tadūrddhādho ghaṭṭanena tu ।।

prāṇamūlasya viśrāntya tadā vyaktā karaṃkiṇī ।
tenaiva kramabhedena mahāṃbuprāsanena tu ।।

vyaktimeti ca sā tatra kavitvapratipādinī ।
ataḥ paraṃ kaṃdacakre saptame paramā kalā ।।

vijṛṃbhāraṃbhayogena sphuritā sā padeśvarī ।
tadā vyaktisamāpannā ghoraghorā karaṃkiṇī ।।

kālāgnilakṣakoṭīnāṃ vapuśmāṃ sā virājate ।
sarvvasiddhipradā jñeyā mahākaraṇayogataḥ ।।

paṃcaratnadravadravya kharukātparameśvarī ।
ataḥ kuṃḍalinīcakre sadā saṃrāviṇī kalā ।।

udveṣṭanaprayogena vivṛttā vṛttiyogataḥ ।
(89a)
tadā sā sphuṭitā tatra sughorā sā karaṃkiṇī ।।

mahākhecarasiddhiṃ sā sādhatyanivāritā ।
anena karaṇeṃdreṇa devi nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।।

aṣṭame ye samākhyātā ghorarūpā karaṃkiṇī ।
atha hṛtpadmagamane yattadvāma hyakhaṃḍitaṃ ।।

tatrāpānaprayogena yadā sā sphuritā śivā ।
tadā vyaktā sarvvasiddhipradā paramabhairavī ।।

anena kramayogena śītasparśānubhāvataḥ ।
siddhidā navame cakre mahā alikulāraṇī ।।

ataḥ saṃrāviṇī cakre vivṛttā parameśvarī ।
tadā sā sphuritā devī hyamareśī karaṃkiṇī ।।

kālāhāraprayogena pūjyate sādhakeṣu sā ।
vyaktā karoti deveśi hyajarāmarapadeśvaraṃ ।।

evaṃ daśama cakreśī sarvvasiddhipradā nṛṇāṃ ।
ato ghaṭākhyaṃ yaccakraṃ tatra saṃsphuritā yadā ।।

tadā marmākhya saṃkoca saṃghaṭṭasphuṭitā hi saḥ ।
evamekādaśe cakre prayogaṃ yogamabhyaset ।।

tasya vyajyati sā devī kulācāryatva siddhidā ।
kaṃṭhakūpādike cakre punaḥ sphurati rāviṇī ।।

tadā vyaktā tatra devī śataraśmivirājitā ।
vivṛtena prayogena nādasaṃdhiryadābhyaset ।।

tadā vyajya * sau ghorā kṣutpipāśā nivarhiṇī ।
dvādaśī sā kalā ghorā nālājya carusaṃpayaḥ ।।

ataḥ paraṃ yadā pāti śūnyacakrasvakaṃṭhagaṃ ।
laṃbādho lolikāpṛṣṭe tadā sā sphuṭitā śivā ।।

aṭṭahāsā karakeśī mahānādākhya siddhidā ।
(89b)
evaṃ sā bhānubaṃdhena tatra siddhipradāyikā ।।

trayodaśe mahācakre kṣīrāhāraikalaṃpaṭā ।
ataḥ paraṃ mahāraṃbhā puṣpavallaṃbikā kriyā ।।

ghaṃṭā nāmeti sā jñeyā tatra sā sphuritā yadā ।
dvātriṃśallolacakrāgraghaṭṭanīṃ vyaktimeti sā ।।

evaṃ vidhena yogena sarvvasiddhipradāyikā ।
karaṃkiṇī vyaktimeti nityaṃ sā parameśvarī ।।

caturddaśamahācakre sarvvasaṃpatsukhāvahā ।
athānyamaiśvaraṃ cakraṃ yadā prāptā karaṃkiṇī ।।

tadā tadbhāsurādhāre vyaktā devī karaṃkiṇī ।
tatrāvyātaprayogeṇa vidyā kamalamaṃthanāt ।।

vidyāpatitvamebhyeti yogināstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।
etatpaṃcadaśaṃ cakraṃ tavādya prakaṭīkṛtaṃ ।।

atha biṃdukale devī yadā paramabhairavī ।
sphuritā paṃcacakrāṇāṃ tatrākalanaśālinī ।।

tadā sarvvajñatā samyaktasyāḥ sphurati sarvvadā ।
sāvyaktā ghorakaṃkālī bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।

śāntātītaṃ tathā śāntaṃ vidyākhyaṃ ca tṛtīyakaṃ ।
pratiṣṭhākhyaṃ caturthaṃ syānnivṛtyākhyaṃ ca paṃcamaṃ ।।

ete paṃca mahācakrāḥ ṣaṣṭhamenaṃ svarūpataḥ ।
eṣāṃ tadbhedayogena kāhikorddhakrameṇa tu ।।

netronmīlanasaṃsthityā tat prāpti bhavati priye ।
evaṃ viṃśatimārgāṇi tava proktāni bhairavi ।।

ataḥ paraṃ brahmacakre sphuritā sā karaṃkiṇī ।
tatrārddhacaṃdrarūpeṇa sphuṭitā parameśvarī ।।
(90a)
svabhāvāghātayogena netrotkṣepakrameṇa ca ।
tadvadeva tadenyeṣā svasibāhyā karaṃkiṇī ।।

prayacchati mahāśāntiṃ bhāgaṃ mokṣaṃ ca sāsvataṃ ।
ekaviṃśatimāseyaṃ marmarājeśvareśvarī ।।

ato nirodhacakreśī nādasthe ca tathāpare ।
nādāntage ca suśroṇi śakticakreṇa bhāmini ।।

vyāpinyākhye mahācakre samanonmanake tathā ।
sphuritā maṃtrapīṭheśī tadā sā sphuṭitā bhṛśaṃ ।।

evaṃ saptasu cakreṣu ghorarūpā karaṃkiṇī ।
mahāprameyayogena proccārāntena bhairavī ।।

vīrasiddhipradā jñeyā vedhāveśaprapāvanī ।
parasaṃvittijananī sarvvasiddhyāgraṇī tathā ।।

parokṣavedhakaraṇī sarvvāmaṃtrāraṇī kramāt ।
aṣṭāviṃśatimarmāṇi tathaitāni samāsataḥ ।।

akhyātāni mayā tubhyaṃ rāviṇīmūrttinirṇṇayaṃ ।
adhunā paracakrāṇi śṛṇu vīrendravatsale ।।

vidyāpīṭhamatastasmātspaṃdaṃ sphurati bhairavī ।
śakticāreṇa sā vyaktaṃ praśāntaṃ cakranāyakaṃ ।।

guruvaktraṃ tataścakraṃ sparśākhyaṃ viṣuvaṃ tataḥ ।
ajaṃ visargadhāmākhyaṃ tatārūḍhā karaṃkiṇī ।।

evaṃ ṣaṭcakrasaṃdohairyadā sphurati bhairavī ।
tadā vamati sā bhūyaḥ ṣaṭkaraṃkiṇijaṃ kulaṃ ।।

tatra siddhirudāsā * ṣoḍhā vyaktā sureśvarī ।
śakticāreṇa subhage bhavatyeva hi sādhake ।।
(90b)
mahāvyāptikuleśatvaṃ paramaṃ viśvarūpatā ।
śaktisaṃvedanaṃ cātra śivabhairavarūpatā ।।

paratvaṃ cātra deveśī saṃsādhayati sādhake ।
catustriṃśatimarmāṇi tava proktāni bhairavi ।। ।।

adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi yathā sā sphuritā kalā ।
śabdacakraṃ sparśacakraṃ rūpacakraṃ rasātmakaṃ ।।

gaṃgātmakaṃ mahācakraṃ tathā buddhyakṣacakrakaṃ ।
manaścakra tathāhaṃ kṛdvauddhamaṣṭakulāraṇiṃ ।।

tamaścakraṃ cājasākhyaṃ sātvikaṃ cātra ṣoḍaśa ।
eṣu cakreṣu sā ghorā yadārūḍhā karaṃkiṇī ।।

tadā sphuṭaṃti deveśyo raśmirūpāṃ mahābalāḥ ।
karaṃkiṇyo ghoratarāḥ kalākulavikārikā ।।

kālayaṃtyo jagatsarvvaṃ supravāhaiḥ samutthitāḥ ।
santarpayati svākāraṃ svākārāśca karaṃkiṇī ।।

tataḥ svadhāmaṃ ghorākhyaṃ mama vaktroddhatā svataḥ ।
svaṃ svaṃ siddhikulaṃ divyaṃ prayacchanti nareśvarī ।।

nareśvareṇa yogena raśmisaṃdhānabhāvanā ।
tanmantroccāravīryeṇa pūjācakrakulena ca ।।

dhukamaṃdhu tathā brahmā caṃdanaṃ vālireva ca ।
madhyamaṃ cottamaṃ chāgaṃ miśrakaṃ puṣpameva ca ।।

rudrajakuṃḍasaṃbhūtaṃ sṛṣṭitrayamathāparaṃ ।
nālajyaṃ madirāma(jyaṃ) ṣoḍaśātra ca ūṇi ca ।।

krameṇa devadeveśī dātavyānyeva sādhakaiḥ ।
evaṃ paṃcadaśai caite hṛdayāstre prakīrttitāḥ ।।

adbhutāni mahābhāge vakṣyamānāni śobhane ।
(91a)
uktāni ca krameṇātra maṃtradhyānaprayogataḥ ।।

pañcaiva devadeveśī yayā sphurati rāviṇī ।
tadā paṃcāśadevoktā hṛdayā bhedavarjitāḥ ।।

adhunā rāgacakrasthā tato niyati saṃjñake ।
kālacakre ṣoḍaśākhye kulacakre samujjvale ।।

māyācakretha puṃścakre tridhāmapari khalvite ।
evaṃ ṣaṭcakradhāmānte sphurate yā sadā kalā ।।

tadā sphuṭati tā devyaḥ ṣoḍaśakuladevatāḥ ।
atra bhāvānubaṃdhena paracāraprayogataḥ ।।

tat prayacchati vīrendro durlabhaṃ yatsureśvari ।
pratitatvāntare samyagvivekaṃ yatprakīrttitaṃ ।।

sphuraṇā sā samākhyātā karaṃkiṇyā maheśvari ।
nānātvaṃ nātra vidyeta bhede vā kāyabhedataḥ ।।

evaṃ ṣadmādyagaṃ rūpaṃ samāsātparikīrttitaṃ ।
asphurjanmamahāvātacakracakrormivigraha ।।

citrabhānuvahe bhūyo somasūryātmake punaḥ ।
evaṃ caturddhā prāṇāntaḥ sphuritā sā karaṃkiṇī ।।

tadā vyaktimupāyāti catuścakreśvarī kulaṃ ।
aṣṭāviṃśakulājālamekasmin vyaktimāgataṃ ।।

anyasmin ṣoḍaśakalā śatamabhyudgataṃ mahat ।
dvādaśaiva tatonyasmin daśadhārā pare smṛtaṃ ।।

evaṃ kālakulavyaktistasmādeva prakīrttitā ।
catasro ghorarūpāstāstattatsiddhiphalapradā ।।

mahāsaṃghaṭṭayogena sarvvāḥ sādhakasattame ।
evaṃ ṣaṣṭhiḥ samākhyātā marmastava mṛge kṣaṇe ।।

vāmadakṣiṇacārābhyāṃ kalitau cakranāyike ।
(91b)
yau paṃcadhā sureśāni tatra sā sphuritā yadā ।।

tadā vyaktāvyaktatarā sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakā ।
karaṃkiṇī mahāraudrā dakṣiṇeśvarasāṃprataṃ ।।

śuṣkā siddhāspalā raktā raśmināthaṃ paraṃ mahat ।
tatrodgataṃ kālanāya jagatyasmin carācare ।।

vāme vīreśvareśānaṃ jayā ca vijayā tathā ।
aparājitajayaṃtī sā vetyevaṃ paṃcakaṃ smṛtaṃ ।।

atra siddhivatī devī proditā parameśvarī ।
saṃhāradhāstriyā kālī sā vyaktā pūrvvacakrataḥ ।।

evaṃ cāradvaye kālī sṛṣṭisaṃhārakāriṇī ।
tatprabhāhābharasthityā tallābhasphuṭa eva hi ।।

sā sā siddhiprajāyeta sādhakasya mahātmanaḥ ।
evaṃ dvāṣaṣṭicakrāṇi tavākhyātāni bhairavi ।। ।।

adhunā netracakreṣu triṣu madhyavivarttanāt ।
sphuratā sā karaṃkeśī tadā vyaktatrayaṃ mahat ।।

karaṃkiṇīnāṃ ghorāṇāṃ kālayanaṃ jagatyalaṃ ।
rūpasaṃghātamakhilaṃ stobhastaṃbhaprayogataḥ ।।

koṭarākārasaṃcintyā sṛṣṭisaṃhārabhāsuraṃ ।
tadvatsādhakamukhyānāṃ madhye madhyotthavarmanā ।।

madhyamadhyagatā bhūtvā madhyastho jṛṃbhate bhṛśaṃ ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātā paṃcaṣaṣṭikulāparā ।।

adhunā skaṃdasaṃrūḍhā karadvayarutau ca yau ।
tadā pādāntagau cobhau evaṃ ṣaṭsu yadā gatā ।।

tadā vyaktāstutā devyaḥ śabdaghorabharolvaṇā ।
- - llāsaprayogena varttanā cārayogataḥ ।।
(92a)
ugrānanaprayogābhyāṃ sā vyaktiḥ sādhake bhavet ।
sudūrākṛṣṭirakhilā pādacāraṃ ca siddhyati ।।
ekasaptatimarmāṇi ā - - śca yathārthataḥ ।। ।।

atha paṃcamahābhūtaṃ cakrakramavivarttanaṃ ।
sphuritā sā yadā devī rāviṇī lokarāviṇī ।।

tadādhāreṣu ghoreṣu tadā vyaktāstu paṃca ca ।
karaṃkiṇyo ghoratamā tattadādhāramāsthitā ।।

turyārddhacaṃdraśṛṃgāṭaṣaḍasrasthā śaśūnyagāḥ ।
tatrādhārādisaṃkocadhāraṇāveśayogataḥ ।।

sādhakasya bhavedvyaktistattatsādhanasiddhidā ।
ṣaṭsaptatirime marmāstava devi prakāśitāḥ ।। ।।

sādāśivedhunā cakre tataḥ * * trayātmake ।
icchājñānakriyārūpe sphuritā parameśvarī ।।
śivabhairavacakrepi punareva mahābalā ।।

yadā saṃrāviṇī devī ghorarūpā karaṃkiṇī ।
tadā sphuraṃti paṃcaiva raśmayaḥ bhāsurāḥ priye ।।

yābhirvyāptamidaṃ viśvaṃ svabhāsābhāsanena tu ।
evaṃ paṃca mahāmarmā tava samyakprakīrttitā ।।

ekāśītirahaikatra ghorarūpāḥ prakāśitāḥ ।
śivabhairavadhāmniryā ullasatsa(rca)bhairavī ।।

ekaiva sā hyeneketi kāryabhede prakīrttitā ।
jvaladrasmisahasraughabhīmabhāsuravigrahā ।।

sā śivābhairavī devī kuladhāmni virājate ।
tayākrāntaṃ raśmipiṇḍaṃ dhāmnipiṇḍaṃ krameṇa tu ।।

pratipiṇḍaṃ samādhāya tripiṃḍakramayogataḥ ।
(92b)
ṛgityeva sphuratsāraparamānaṃdasuṃdaraṃ ।।

spaṃdoddāmalasallolaprakāśānaṃdamaṃdaraṃ ।
sarvvajñatādivisphārasphuradvadvotkarokara ।।

(nimeṣonyeśramaṃthāma) - - rānaṃdanirbhara ।
bhāśvadarkakuloddāmadhāmasaṃhāraṇodbhaṭaṃ ।।

bhogabhogibharāpīḍasamudveṣṭanapāṭanaṃ ।
vyaṃjayantaṃ bidurdāmavikasadbhāvabhairavaṃ ।।

samāsṛtatayākrā(nta)kāntatatva kulākulaṃ ।
tamāsṛtya paraṃdhāmasthitā yāvatkaraṃkiṇī ।।

rāvaṇīcakrasaddāma unmudrollāsasoṣṭavān ।
vyaktiraśmikulaṃ sāṃdramohavidyā vipāṭanaṃ ।।

(e)kāśīti karankāṃkā vaśmiṇyo vikṛtānanāṃ ।
pratimaṃdakulākrānti kharvvīṃkṛtakadaṃbakāṃ ।।

sphuranti viśvasadgrāsa(va)manāraṃbhajṛṃbhitāṃ ।
amāratamahākālaṃ kulakaulena kovidā ।।

marmayo vṛtta suprodyadbhāvagahvarabhairavāṃ ।
cakrānyatītavarttante iti cidgaganāṃtarā ।।

kulametanmavyaktaṃ kālikākhyaṃ maholvaṇaṃ ।
(ta)tra kāntara(tānaṃda) vikasadvṛttibhedataḥ ।।

sphuranti nānākāratva vyaktānekaprabhedataḥ ।
kapiṃḍakamalāntasthāṃ stripiṃḍabhrutitāntarāḥ ।।

sarvvārgalavinirmuktā (smanirargalabhavataḥ) ।
vyāptuvaṃti grasatyuccairjagatsthāvarajaṃgamaṃ ।।

atilīlāvilāsoccha kalābhāga kulolvaṇāṃ ।
yadā tadā nāntamasti śaktikoṇe kadācana ।।

sarvvatra cakrasaṃdohe sauvaikā pravirājate ।
(93a)
nānyakiṃcitprapaśyāmi tayā hīnaṃ hi yat priye ।।

atra mantra kulavyakti raśmiṣaṭketha kāśitā ।
sphuṭaṃ rahasya valitā sarvvatrānyatra gopitā ।।

siddhibhedaprabhedoyaṃ sarahasyamanantakaṃ ।
saṃkṣepāttatra sarvvasvaṃ vakṣyamāṇāmataḥ śṛṇu ।।

ukto rahasyamatametadihārasthamīśānavaktrakuharādalamāptametat ।।
vakṣyāmi te hṛdayakalpavarānaśeṣā,
sarvvānaśeṣaguṇasiddhividhānadhānaṃ ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
rāviṇīcakre tatsvarūpanirddeśapaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

evaṃ śrutvā devadevī haravaktrakuśeśayī ।
tadvākyaṃ prāhasphura * rutejo romāṃcitavigrahā samyak ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

deva tṛptāsmi tṛptāsmi parajñānāmṛtena ca ।
avāplavya na cāstīha mama bhairava bhairava ।।

tathāpi śrotumicchāmi yaduktaṃ me tvayā hara ।
hṛdayānāṃ samāsena kalpāni * * bravīhi me ।।

sarvvāṇi sa rahasyāni sphuṭitāni yathārthataḥ ।
evamākarṇya bhagavān prabuddha kusume kṣaṇaḥ ।।
marmāṇi sa rahasyāni samagrāṇi yathārthataḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇutvaṃ subhage samyagvakṣyāmyavasarāgataṃ ।
marmeśvarīṇāṃ kalpāni kiṃciduktāni pūrvvaśaḥ ।।

vādastambhakarīyāsya chṛṇu vakṣyāmi kālikā ।
(93b)
karaṃkiṇī ghorarūpā ghoreśī ghoravigrahā ।।

gatvā harālayaṃ prāgvadevadevīṃ samuddharet ।
yathā vidveṣaṇī saṃde pūjā proktā mayā tava ।।

evaṃ krameṇa maṃtreśamuddharedvīravaṃdite ।
muśalācchaṣṭamuddhṛtya vilomena maheśvari ।।

mahākrodhena puṭitaṃ māyābiṃdusamanvitaṃ ।
etatpiṇḍavaraṃ ghoraṃ trailokya kṣayakārakaṃ ।।

sarvvapūrṇṇaṃ punarbhūyaḥ kramameva punardadet ।
adho daṃṣṭraṃ sarvvapūrvvaṃ tribiṃdupiṃḍitaṃ kuru ।।

kālapaṃcamasaṃrūḍhaṃ kharparaṃ punaruddharet ।
adho daṃṣṭrasya yā mātrā tasya hṛtyāta cārghayet ।।

kūpavaktraṃ krodharūḍhaṃ sṛṣṭikṣayayutaṃ kuru ।
saptākṣarā smṛtā devi sarvvavidyā karaṃkiṇī ।।

vādastaṃbhe sadā yojyā śatrūṇāṃ nigrahepi ca ।
dhyāyetkālārka * ṇāṃ koṭikoṭyāyutaprabhāṃ ।।

raktakṛṣṇāṃ mahāraudrāṃ bhujāṣṭakasamanvitāṃ ।
kapālakhaṭvāṃgadharaṃ saṃdaṅgakarabhūṣitāṃ ।।

kṛṣṇamārjārahastāṃ ca khaḍgacakradharāṃ śubhāṃ ।
ghaṃṭāmuṃḍakaravyagrāṃ ūrddhakeśāṃ bhayānakāṃ ।।

muṇḍamālāvanaddhāṅgīṃ sarpamekhalamaṃḍitāṃ ।
kṛtāntapṛṣṭhasaṃrūḍhāṃ sarvvagrāsaikaghasmarāṃ ।।

mahārāvau gharaveṇī pibantī śoṇitaṃ mahat ।
kalpakṣayakṣayābhāsāṃ vidyunetrāṃ caturbhujāṃ ।।

evaṃ dhyāyedekavīrāṃ paścānmaṃḍalagāṃ yajet ।
ṣaṭkoṇapuramālikhya raktakṛṣṇarajonvitaṃ ।।
(94a)
tanmadhyagāṃ yajeddevīṃ sarvvavīropacārataḥ ।
surāmāṃsāsavairdivyai nānāvalyupahārakaiḥ ।।

nānāvāditrageyaiśca supuṣpairdhūpasaṃyutaiḥ ।
evamabhyarcya deveśīṃ paścāddhomaṃ samārabhet ।।

mahāpalaṃ ca mīnotthaṃ ātavaṃ vā samāhitaṃ ।
tryaktamevaṃ samāhūya paścātsādhanamārabhet ।।

parvvatāgraṃ samāsādya japedakṣarasaṃkhyayā ।
lakṣamekaṃ mahābhāge pūrvvoktaṃ juhuyāttataḥ ।।

homānnādeti vai tasya rathamatyantaśobhanaṃ ।
vahnimadhyātsamutthāya cārāvāmīkarānvitāṃ ।।

apsaro gaṇasaṃkīrṇṇaṃ nānāścarya * śārcitaṃ ।
nānāgeyaravoddāmaṃ sarvvalakṣaṇalakṣitaṃ ।।

duḥprāpantaṃ samāruhya yāti lokaṃ yathepsitaṃ ।
sarvvakāmasamṛddhārtha paryante mokṣamāpnuyāt ।।

atha lakṣatrayeṇaiva japtena tanu madhyame ।
madhyamā jāyate siddhiḥ sādhakasya mahātmanaḥ ।।

pātālākāśayo mitthaṃ sādhayellīlayā tataḥ ।
ayutaikena deveśi sāmanyaṃ sādhayiṣyati ।।

tāḥ sarvvāḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇu vīrendravaṃdite ।
prativādini jihvāgraṃ śadeśe noharaṃ smaret ।।

devadevī mahāghorā pūrvvadhyānāpavarṇṇitā ।
vākpaterapi deveśi vākstambhaṃ jāyate bhṛśaṃ ।।

athavā kṛṣṇamārjāraṃ jvalantaṃ lelihānakaṃ ।
prakṣipedvadane tasya sādhyasya ca maheśvari ।।

jihvāmutkarṣayaṃ cityaṃ kaṃṭhalagnaprapīḍitaṃ ।
(94b)
akṣiṇī pāṭanaṃ bhāvyaṃ mārjārāntasya ghorakṛt ।।

vidyāmudāharedraudrāṃ saptārṇṇāṃ bhrukuṭīmukhāṃ ।
staṃbhayedvākyanirvvāṇīṃ dhyānamaṃtrā na saṃśayaḥ ।।

apakvaṃ mallakaṃ gṛhya lekhayetsādhya nāmakaṃ ।
pītadravyeṇa subhage kaṃṭakaistu ṣamiśritaiḥ ।।

asthikhaṃḍasamāyuktaiḥ pūraye taṃ sulocane ।
vidyāyā varttitaṃ kṛtvā ṭhakāreṇaiva veṣṭitaṃ ।।

viṣṭāpūritamevaṃ syānmadhye vācchādya gopayet ।
tataśca harate vāṇīṃ yaṃtrarājaprabhāvataḥ ।।

evaṃ sarvvāśca yā siddhī yaṃtra(rā)japrabhāvataḥ ।
evaṃ sarvvāśca yā siddhīḥ sāmānyā api sādhayet ।।

vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣa māraṇoccāṭanādikaṃ ।
līlayā sādhayenmantrī vacastvavitapaṃ mama ।। ।।

।। (iti) bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
rāviṇīcakre vidveśakālipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

athaikārṇṇāṃ mahāghorāṃ śṛṇu kālīṃ karaṃkiṇīṃ ।
sarvvakarmakarī raudrāṃ harahuṃkārasiddhidāṃ ।।

āsādya vijanaṃ sthānaṃ su(maṃ)dhisu manoramaṃ ।
kṛtvā samuddhareccaṇḍāmekārṇṇaṃ parameśvarī ।।

śūnyaṃ śūnyaṃ tathā raudraṃ biṃdunādavibhūṣitaṃ ।
etanmantravaraṃ ghoraṃ krodharūpaṃ mahābalaṃ ।।

ekavīraṃ mahākhyātaṃ devyā vācakamuttamaṃ ।
ṣaṭkoṇacakrasphuritaṃ marmametanmahābalaṃ ।।
(95a)
dhyāyedatra mahākālaṃ jagadgrāsaikaghasmarāṃ ।
mahāpralayasaṃkṣobhakṣubhitārṇṇavanisvanāṃ ।।

taḍillakṣasphuranetraprabhābhāsitadidmukhāṃ ।
cala(dbhrū)bhaṃgavikṣepanipītajagadāsavān ।।

ulkāsahasraprasphurjadantaniṣpeṣavahnikāṃ ।
kākakokilakālāgnikālikākelilāṃ śubhāṃ ।।

samagramaṃtracakrā(ga) grāsodbhaṭabhayānakāṃ ।
samastacakrābharaṇāṃ bhujanetropaśobhitāṃ ।।

viśvarūpāṃ ca vimukhīṃ kālāgnyāstuta bhāsurāṃ ।
evaṃ dhyāvā ca ṣaṭkoṇaṃ maṃḍalānte prapūjayet ।।

nānāvalyupahāraiśca nānāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।
nānāpuṣpairmahādhūpaiḥ sarvveśāṃ paripūjayet ।।

juhuyānnaramāṃsādi kṣmājamāvikameva vā ।
tryaktamevātra taṃ hutvā paścātsādhana(mārabhe)t) ।।

gatvā girivaraṃ devi koṭijāpaṃ prakārayet ।
muṃḍapādavihīnāyā devyāḥ sarvveśvareśvari ।।

japaṃ kurvvan prakaṃpeta brahmāṇḍaṃ sacarācaraṃ ।
arddhakoṭiryāvadevi japetsuravarārcite ।।

vidyātatvāṃtamakhilaṃ kaṃpate tāvadeva hi ।
bhīta bhītā samagrāstā sādhakendraḥ sadaiva hi ।।

praṇamanti mahābhāge stuvanti ca muhurmuhuḥ ।
koṭijāpena subhage śivāntaṃ yāvadeva hi ।।

ābhūmito kaṃpate vai sphurate ghūrṇṇate tathā ।
tadā yāvanti tatvāni nāvatsupajitāmiyāt ।।

bhuṃkte sarvvagatā yogī yathā paramabhairavaḥ ।
(95b)
eṣā vai cottamāsiddhistavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।

pātālākāśagamanaṃ ṣaḍrasā haraṇe tathā ।
yakṣabhūtavaśīkāramantarddhānarasāyanaṃ ।।

mahāmelāpasaṃsiddhistavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।
pātālākāśagamanaṃ ṣaḍrasā haraṇe tathā ।।

* * * * * * * * pādacāraṃ maheśvari ।
tata kṣatraya prajāyācca sādhaye tena saṃśayaḥ ।।

evamādīni sarvvāṇi sādhayetsarvvameva ca ।
sādhayeddevi sāmānyā siddhayā lakṣajāpataḥ ।।

tasyākarṣaṇavidveṣamāraṇoccāṭanādikāṃ ।
prasenāsarṣapāvartti vetālotthāpanaṃ tathā ।।

yakṣiṇī sādhanaṃ cānyanmahākiṃkarasādhanaṃ ।
indrajālāni sarvvāṇi puroktānyapi sādhayet ।।

staṃbhastobhanasaṃkṣepaṃ meghanigrahamadbhutaṃ ।
līlayā sādhayeddevi yadyanmanasi rocate ।।
sarvvakarmakarī hyeṣā devadevī prakāśitāḥ ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
rāviṇīcakre sarvvakarmakarīvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi ghoraṃ śatru vināśanaṃ ।
mahāghoratarākālī galagaṃtā maheśvarī ।।

yā mārayati trailokyaṃ nāmamātreṇa bhairavi ।
tasyāḥ kalpaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvekamanādhunā ।।

gatvā harāspadaṃ rātrau mahāvidyāṃ samuddharet ।
(96a)
kaṃkālaghṛṣṭāṃ vasudhāṃ kṛtvā tarpasuvīravat ।।

madirānaṃdacaitanyaḥ sarvvāvayavabhūṣaṇaḥ ।
proddharedvīracakreśīṃ vahnisthaṃ cāṃkuśāhataṃ ।।

biṃdunādasamākrāntaṃ piṇḍaṃ trailokya bhakṣakaṃ ।
mataṅgamuddharetpaścācchakramaṃkuśaśobhitaṃ ।।

asyādyaṃ punareveha samuddhāryaṃ kuleśvaraṃ ।
paṃcamādyaṃ dhāmacihnaṃ śṛṃgāṭaśiraśolvaṇaṃ ।।

kharparaṃ vahnisaṃrūḍhaṃ viṣṇusṛṣṭisamujvalaṃ ।
catuṣkalaṃ tato deyaṃ bhīmaśaṃkunirodhitaṃ ।।

aṣṭārṇṇā paramā vidyā uddhṛtā parameśvarī ।
mārayedakhilāṃ lokāṃ sadevāsuramānuṣāṃ ।।

nāmamātreṇa subhage malāgarttā maholvaṇā ।
dhyāye nīhārakālālikākakokilasannibhāṃ ।।

jvalatkalpāntakālāgni mārttaṇḍāyutatejasāṃ ।
ahīndraphalaphutkāravirājitaśarīriṇāṃ ।।

dravaktānavapheṇormibudbudāvarttalocanāṃ ।
asaktādbhīmahuṃkāratrāsitatridaśādhipāṃ ।।

ghorapādatalāghātabhagnabrahmāṇḍamaṇḍapāṃ ।
bahubhīmabhujāvarttavivarttitanabhastalāṃ ।।

vamatkalpāntaghorāgninetraśrotravirājitāṃ ।।
rāsabhāsanaskaṃdhavasitāṃ ṛkṣavatkriyāṃ ।।

yathāsimatarūpāṃ ca dhyāyetsaṃhārabhairavīṃ ।
niḥśeṣakarmakaraṇī dhyānacakrakrameṇa tu ।।

tṛkoṇamaṃḍalasyāntaḥ pūjayetparameśvarīṃ ।
(96b)
santaptavasudhāṃ kṛtvā raktakṛṣṇopacārataḥ ।।

prapūjya parameśānīṃ juhuyānnara * sphusaṃ ।
paścātkarmāṇi kurvvīta japedayutasaṃkhyayā ।।

paśuṃ nivedayeddevyā durācāraṃ durātmakaṃ ।
niveditaṃ śūlabhinnaṃ bhakṣitaṃ cintayetsadā ।।

saptāhāt mriyate devi yadi * * tpuraṃdaraṃ ।
athavā cintayet sādhyaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ śavaśarīriṇāṃ ।।

vamantī rudhiraṃ ghoraṃ dvārairnavani vidrutaṃ ।
devyā jvālā kalā yena vidyutsaṃkāśa varcasā ।।

vicintya bhasmasādyāntamārayettriśānapi ।
saptāhānnātra saṃdehaḥ kiṃ punaḥ kṣudramānuṣaṃ ।।

atha saṃpuṭayogena yadā sādhyaṃ ca saṃpuṭe ।
japetkuṃbhakavannityaṃ mārayettridaśeśvarān ।।

athavā pratimā karma kārayeta vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
māṣāṭakaṃ samāhṛtya śuklaloḍitameva ca ।।

sakaṃṭakaṃ ca taṃ kṛtvā pratimāṃ kārayeddṛḍhāṃ ।
raktena cīrikālikhya viṣabhramaramiśritāṃ ।।

sādhya vāmātra puṭitaṃ vidyayā parameśvari ।
etāṃ hṛdi vinikṣipya sthāpayettāmadhomukhāṃ ।।

citimadhye nikhanyeva mānuṣāsthi mayena hi ।
kīlakena tu tāṃ kīlya paścāddāhaṃ pradāpayet ।।

saṃhāramudrāmāsphoṭya japedaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ ।
evaṃ kṛte mahādāhaṃ trāsaṃ cātrakṛtaṃ bhavet ।।

saptāhānmārayatyāśu pratimā dahanena tu ।
anivarttakayetatte māraṇaṃ saṃprakīrttitaṃ ।।
(97a)

yasya yasyai tu vai vyādhināma sādhyasya leyet ।
taṃ taṃ vyādhiṃ prakurute sādhya sātra na saṃśayaḥ ।।

tenaiva mārayetsādhyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
athavā vīrabhuvane saṃpūjya parameśvarīṃ ।।

raktakṛṣṇopacāreṇa santāpya kṣitimeva ca ।
paścādvijñāpayeddevīṃ dattaste paśukaṃ mayā ।।

dehi nirmālyakaṃ devi yāvattaṃ ghātayāmyahaṃ ।
evamuktā tataḥ sarvvaṃ āharetsarvvagaṃ caruṃ ।।

khāhi khāhīti vaktavyaṃ gacchatvaṃ sāmya maṃdiraṃ ।
āharecchuddhamanasā tacca vai ṛpumaṃdiraṃ ।।

kaṭutraya prayogena saṃbhṛtya balavāhanaṃ ।
mānayennātra saṃdeha mahānirmālya saṃgrahān ।।

ekārāntā tato devi yadā sādhyaṃ puraṃdaraṃ ।
evaṃ māraṇakarma * tavākhyātaṃ yathārthataḥ ।।

anyānya(pi) hi karmāṇi sādhayejjapayogataḥ ।
vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣamohanaṃ stambhanaṃ tathā ।।

līlayā sādhayenmaṃtrī mahāvidyā prabhāvataḥ ।
lakṣākṣaraprajāpena pūrvvokte tu sa siddhayaḥ ।।

sādhayennātra saṃdeho līlayā parameśvarī ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātā karaṃkāghoravikramā ।।
galāgarttā pradhāneyaṃ * * sampadguṇāvahā ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
rāviṇīcakre galāgarbhavidhiḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।
(97b)
adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi kālī ghoraparākramā ।
yathoccāṭayate brahmā keśavaṃ harameva vā ।।

tasyā vidhānamatulaṃ śṛṇu saṃkṣepataḥ priye ।
gatvā harāspadaṃ tatra samuccārya karaṃkiṇīṃ ।।

pūjya vīropacāreṇa mātṛkāṃ mantramātṛkāṃ ।
jagadbhramaṃ samuddhṛtya tripūrvvaṃ punaruddharet ।।

aśvaṃ marma tathaikatra vahnidhāmasamuddharet ।
tripūrvvapunaruddhṛtya asyājyaṃ lāṃcchanacyutaṃ ।।

vahnipraṇavasaṃyuktamamṛtādyaṃ ca kevalaṃ ।
aśvaṃ trisvaramūrddhānaṃ vāyuṃ vahni tathaikataḥ ।।

vidvaṃkuśasamāyuktaṃ bhīmaśaṃkuni(bo)dhitaṃ ।
maṃtrametatsamākhyātaṃ karaṃkiṇyā bhayāvahaṃ ।।

proccāṭanaṃ prakurute brahmāṇḍasyākhilasya ca ।
yajedetanmahāvāta cakrārūḍhā kuleśvarīṃ ।।

dhvajahastāṃ dhūmravarṇṇāṃ mārttaṇḍāyutaghasmarāṃ ।
samagradevatāvṛṃdamālinī bhuvaneśvarī ।।

ghorabhāṃkārarāvaugharāviṇī kulabhairavīṃ ।
bhujāṣṭakasamāyuktāṃ muṃḍakhaṭvāṅgabhūṣitāṃ ।।

mahāśaṭakaravyagrāṃ pāśāṃkuśadharolvaṇāṃ ।
kṛśāṃgīṃ bhīmavadanāṃ trinetrāṃ bhrukuṭīmukhāṃ ।।

ulkā netrāṃ mahācaṇḍāṃ māru * skaṃdhagāminīṃ ।
ja(ga)vāsavasaṃtṛptāṃ dhyātvā samyakprapūjayet ।।

raktakṛṣṇopacāreṇa surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।
evaṃ pūjya prakarttavyaṃ homaṃ devyāḥ pratarpaṇaṃ ।।

narolvamaṃthagodbhūtaṃ ajāśvaṃvāsumidaṃ ।
tryaktamatrahutaṃ paścātsādhayetkālabhakṣaṇī ।।
(98a)
gatvā girivaraṃ śubhraṃ samiddārudakānvitaṃ ।
tatra saṃsādhayetsamyaganantā bhairavāraṇīṃ ।।

japellakṣatrayaṃ devīṃ juhuyātpūrvvacoditāṃ ।
tadā sā madhyamāsiddhimāvahetsādhakātmanaḥ ।।

triguṇena tu jāpena sādhayeduttamāṃ sadā ।
siddhisādhakamukhyosau nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

sāmānyaṃ sādhayetkarma sādhakoyutajāpataḥ ।
tacca samyakpravakṣyāmi kiṃciduddeśataḥ priye ।।

vātacakragataṃ sādhyaṃ dhyātvā vidyāmudāharet ।
nāmasaṃpuṭyasādhyasya tryahāduccaṭayiṣyati ।।

pravisākṣātparvvatendraṃ mānuṣeṣvatra kā kathā ।
athavā bhūrjamādāya raṃdhravarjaṃ suśobhanaṃ ।।

viṣāṅgārayutaṃ nāma likhetkṣatajamiśritaṃ ।
nāma sādhyasya suśroṇi vidyayā ca vidarbhitaṃ ।।

tuṣāṃgārasamāpūrṇṇaṃ keśairvvā veṣṭayettataḥ ।
laṃbāyadbhīṣaṇe vṛkṣe vāyavyā kha * yā priye ।।

evamuccāṭayetsthānādamarānapi sādhakaḥ ।
kevalaṃ japayogena ṛpūrṇṇāṃ kulasaṃtatiṃ ।।

uccāṭayatyasaṃdeho devi nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।
vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣamāraṇastobhamohanāt ।।

stambhanadveṣaṇaṃ caiva smadhayellīlayā bhṛśaṃ ।
evameṣā samākhyātā vāyu kālī mahābalā ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
rāviṇīcakre proccāṭanaṃ kālipaṭalaḥ ।।।।
(98b)
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi saṃgrāmavijayaṃ mahat ।
yadā devyā prajāyeta līlayā sādhakātmanāṃ ।।

śubhaṃ deśaṃ samāsādya puṣpaprakaralālasaṃ ।
tatra prapū * maṃtreśīṃ sarvvavīropacārataḥ ।।

tataḥ samuddharedvidyāṃ anekādbhutadarśanāṃ ।
gajendraṃ ṣaṣṭhamuddhṛtya kṛtāntāsanasaṃsthitaṃ ।।

punarvvahnigataṃ kuryādviṣṇubiṃduvibhūṣitaṃ ।
piṇḍametatsamākhyātaṃ jaganmohanatatparaṃ ।।

(puna)rddhijihvamuddhṛtya samyagdeyaṃ samīraṇaṃ ।
triśūlāntaṃ karttanasthaṃ tribiṃdvaṃkaṃ sudhāmakaṃ ।।

punardvijihvaṃ biṃdvādyaṃ bhīmaśaṃkunirodhitaṃ ।
saptārṇṇeyaṃ samākhyātā mahāsaṃgrāmatāriṇī ।।

yajedenāṃ khaḍgagatāṃ sarvvavīropacārataḥ ।
surāmāṃsāsavairdivyairupahārairanekadhā ।।

dhyāyetkalpāntakālāgni lakṣakoṭyāyutaprabhāṃ ।
mahāsaṃhārarasitāṃ śastrārṇṇavapi śoṣaṇaṃ ।।

kṣubhitāṃbhodhisannādāṃ sukṛṣāṃ bhīmavikramāṃ ।
daṃṣṭrālāmūrddhakeśāṃ ca muṇḍasragdāmadhāriṇīṃ ।।

trailokyatrāsaniratāṃ śatrupakṣakṣayaṃkarīṃ ।
chāṃtrāsanagatāṃ dhyāyedarddhacaṃdraṃ tadullasaṃ ।।

viśvarūpāṃ viśvagatāṃ yathā * vārutāṃ smaret ।
evaṃ yāgaṃ purā kṛtvā paścātsaṃsādhayecchivā ।।

girīndrapṛṣṭamāruhya japellakṣaṃ samāhitaḥ ।
akṣarāṇāṃ varārohe yasmāddhomaṃ samācaret ।।

madhyato vahnikuhare tryaktaṃ ca naraphuphusaṃ ।
ayutaikaṃ samāhṛtya dṛṣyate parameśvarīṃ ।।
(99a)
vahnimadhyātsamuditāṃ saumyarūpāṃ karaṃkiṇīṃ ।।

tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sādhakendreṇa deyamarghyaṃ svalohitaṃ ।
bhitvā vāmāṃgamevātra tadā tuṣyati sā bhṛśaṃ ।।

yatheṣṭaṃ ca varaṃ tasmai sādhakāya dadatyasau ।
sadavānayane sthānaṃ yatra devī svayaṃ sthitā ।।

athavā yujatāpena saṃgrāme vijayaṃ labhet ।
pūjya pūrvvoktavidhinā yuddhāya pracalettataḥ ।।

maunamāsthāya vīrendro vidyāmeva tadā haret ।
upahārārthamāryeta śatrusainyaṃ samastakaṃ ।।

tadā vinaśyate sainyaṃ yadi sākṣātkṛtakratuḥ ।
śaṃkhabheryadhvajavarāṃ gajānyasvānyadātayaḥ ।।

kavacānāyudhāṃ sarvvāṃ saptena vāriṇā * * ।
prokṣayeddevadeveśīṃ avadhyāste bhavanti hi ।।

parābhavanavāsyaṃti jinanti ṛpuvāhinīṃ ।
athavā kāṃsajātīyaṃ pātraṃ kṛtvā samullikhet ।।

pūrvvarūpasamāyuktāṃ devadevīṃ karaṃkiṇīṃ ।
saṃpūjya pūrvvavidhinā sahasrāvarttitaṃ kuru ।।

tamāharetsādhakendraḥ tāḍayenmantramuccaret ।
śabdaṃ śrutvā tu taṃ ghoraṃ gajavājirathākulaṃ ।।

vidravetsahasā sainyaṃ vāyonmeghaṃ yathāghanaṃ ।
vinaśyate ca sakalaṃ mohaṃyāti na saṃśayaḥ ।।

jinatyanādareṇaiva rakṣitaṃ yadi bhairavi ।
śakreṇa sākṣādabhyetya tathāpi pravinaśyati ।।

evaṃ vinirjitaṃ sarvvaṃ saṃgrāmaṃ sādhakeśvaraḥ ।
athavā dhūpayetsamya * * gātmānamaṃtritaṃ ।।

anya dhūpaṃ samāvartya śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ purā ।
tamucca gatamevātra dhāryamagre surārcite ।।
(99b)
tasya gaṃdhaṃ samāghrāya naśyante śatrusainikāṃ ।
jayatyevāti vīrendro devadevyā prabhāvataḥ ।।

evaṃ saṃgrāmataraṇī vidyeyaṃ ghoravikramā ।
tavākhyātā mayā samyak sarvvasiddhi guṇāvahā ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
saṃgrāme kālyāvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi ghoraṃ hṛdayamuttamaṃ ।
sarvvakarmakaraṃ sāraṃ duḥprāpamakṛtātmanāṃ ।।

yena vijñātamātreṇa sarvvakarmaṇi sādhayet ।
tamahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvekamanādhunā ।
saptādaśārṇṇā caṃḍeśī jagadgrāsaikaghasmarā ।।

vīragṛhaṃ samāsādya proddharetparameśvarī ।
saṃpūrṇṇamantracakreśīṃ bahuvīropacārataḥ ।।

prathamaṃ proddharenmohaṃ puruṣaṃ tadanantaraṃ ।
haṃsaṃ dāraṇayā krāntaṃ tripūrvvaṃ punaruddharet ।।

aśvaṃ marma vahniveśmaṃ piṃḍībhūtaṃ samuddharet ।
kuṃḍalaṃ vahnipuṭitaṃ caṃdraṃ patitamuddharet ।।

amṛtaṃ kevalaṃ bhūyaḥ śūlāntaṃ karttanaṃ sthitaṃ ।
bāhumaṃkuśasaṃyuktaṃ bāhumaśvaṃ punarddadet ।।

tribiṃduśirasopetaṃ kūpavaktraṃ punardadet ।
vahnirvvā puṭitaṃ kṛtvā mahāsṛṣṭisamanvitaṃ ।।

vāyuṃ praṇavamūrdhnānaṃ gaṇendraṃ ca trikoṇa yuk ।
varuṇasthaṃ tadantaṃ ca bāhuṃ binduṃ trayāṃkitaṃ ।।
(100a)
saśaraṃ cāpamuddhṛtya mahādhāmapuṭīkṛtaṃ ।
biṃdubhramaṃ samāyuktaṃ piṇḍaṃ sūryāyutopamaṃ ।।

uddhṛtā paramā vidyā kālāntakaraṇī parā ।
asyoccāraṇamātreṇa janmakoṭyāyutārjitaṃ ।।

bhasmasātpātakaṃ yāti tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā ।
brahmāṇḍamakhilaṃ hatvā brahmavidbhiḥ prapūritaṃ ।।

na sa pāpena lipyeta padmapatramivāṃbhasā ।
asyāvīryaṃ vibhūtiṃ vā sādākhye vatsarāyutaiḥ ।।

na ca śaktosmyahaṃ vaktuṃ vaktrāṇāmayutāyutaiḥ ।
tathāpyuddeśataḥ kiṃcicchṛṇu vakṣyāmi bhairavi ।।

vīragṛhe yajedenāṃ ghorarūpāṃ karaṃkiṇīṃ ।
ṣaḍāracakramadhye tu raktakṛṣṇarajānvite ।।

caturddhāre same śubhre mṛganābhisamanvite ।
madhye niveśayeddevīṃ yathāvibhavarūpataḥ ।।

kṛśāṃgīṃ ghoravadanāṃ mahāpretakṛtānanāṃ ।
garjatkalpāntajīmūtanirbharāvāṃsubhairavāṃ ।।

vaktrapaṃcakasaṃyuktāṃ bhujāṣṭādaśabhīṣaṇāṃ ।
pāśāṃkuśamahāmuṃḍahṛtpa(tyā)śivirājitāṃ ।।

khaṭvāṃgapātraḍamarudhvajahastāṃ bhayānakāṃ ।
narāntrapūritamukhāṃ sukṛśāṃ bhāsurānanāṃ ।।

kākakokilavarṇābhāṃ narāntrasraksamujvalāṃ ।
surāsurendratatsiddhaṃ vaṃditāṃ parameśvarīṃ ।।

evaṃ dhyātvā mahākālīṃ paścādyajanamārabhet ।
surāmāṃsāsavairdivyairarcayedvibhavaṃ yathā ।।

evaṃ saṃpūjya juhuyānnaramāṃsaṃ trisaṃyutaṃ ।
evaṃ santarpya deveśīṃ paścātsādhanamārabhet ।।
(100b)
gatvā girivaraṃ ramyaṃ samidārukakānvitaṃ ।
japellakṣāyutaṃ tatra juhuyāttaddaśāntataḥ ।।

naramāṃsasama * tvaṃ tataḥ siddhyati bhairavī ।
darśanaṃ dadate tasya sādhakasya mahātmanaḥ ।।

uttamāṃ siddhimatulāṃ sādhakāya prayacchati ।
trilakṣajapayogena yovantyo madhyamadhyayoḥ ।।

siddhayastasya siddhyanti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
sāmānyāḥ siddhayaḥ sarvvāḥ siddhyantyayutajāpinaḥ ।।

yāṃvatāstatra madhyesminsiddhayastatprakīrttitāḥ ।
uttamottamamadhyāśca līlayā sādhayiṣyati ।।

anayaiva vidyeṣā sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
yadyadroceta vai karma tattatsādhayiṣyati ।।

sarvvakarmakarī devī trailokyatrāṇatatparā ।
sarvvatraiṣāpratihatā mahāvidyā karaṃkiṇī ।।

nāsyāstulyā prapaśyāmi maṃtrakośe samastake ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātā kālī sarvvaprasiddhidā ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
rāviṇīcakrasaptadaśārṇā paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvvakarmmakarī parā ।
mohanākhyā mahākālī jaganmohanatatparā ।।

vīragṛhaṃ samāsādya tatra vidyāṃ samuddharet ।
kālādyāmagnisaṃrūḍhaṃ biṃdvagnikṣayalāṃcchitaṃ ।।
(101a)
punaḥ kālaṃ sapravaṃhaṃsaṃ kevalamuddharet ।
graṃthīśaṃ ca tribiṃdvaṅkaṃ rasendraṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ ।।

vahniṃ varuṇasaṃrūḍhaṃ gaṇeśaṃ punaruddharet ।
trikoṇāṅkimūrddhānaṃ kuṃḍalaṃ vahnisaṃsthitaṃ ।।

ajīvaṃ caṃdramuddhṛtya vidyeyaṃ mohanātmikā ।
arddhāṣṭakamavijñeyā sarvvatrailokyadurlabhāṃ ।।

mohayantīṃ samastasya viśvasya surasuṃdari ।
mahāraktāruṇāṃ devīṃ athavā śukapiṃcchavat ।।

dvibhujāmekavadanāṃ mohāmvarakare sthitāṃ ।
mahāpretasamārūḍhāṃ bhoginī bhogalaṃpaṭāṃ ।।

kṛśāṅgīṃ ghoravadanāṃ mohakālīṃ vicintayet ।
yajedenāṃ padmagatāṃ sarvvātrāpratighāmalāṃ ।।

mahāmohakarīṃ ghorāṃ surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।
sarvvavīropacāreṇa juhuyānnaraphuphusaṃ ।।

evaṃ tarpya vidhānena paścātsādhanamārabhet ।
giryagramuttamaṃ gatvā prasiddhaṃ lokaviśrutaṃ ।।

tatra śṛṃgā * māruhya japedakṣaralakṣakaṃ ।
japānte juhuyāddevi pūrṇṇavāpi daśāṅgataḥ ।।

tadagnimadhyā sā devī viśvarūpā mahaujasī ।
sarvvākārā sarvvagatā varadā sā hyadeti hi ।।

tayaiva sākaṃ sāyojyaṃ yāti nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।
athavā mānasī siddhiḥ sādhakaḥ sādhayedyadi ।।

tadā trilakṣajāpena sādhayedakhilāḥ priye ।
atha sāmānyakarmāṇi tryayutā sādhayiṣyati ।।

vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣamāraṇoccāṭanādikaṃ ।
mohayedakhilaṃ viśvaṃ dhyānādeva na saṃśayaḥ ।।
(101b)
śatrumadhye smṛtā samyak mantravīryasamanvitā ।
tadā mohayate sarvvaṃ śatrusainyaṃ mahābalaṃ ।।

yadā yadā smṛtā devī kā(li)kā vīravikramā ।
tadā tadā mohayate jagadetatsamagrakaṃ ।।

evaṃ mohakarī mohā mohanā mohabhūṣitā ।
mohāmṛtā mohatejā mahāmoharasāvṛtā ।।

mohendrā mohaveśmāṃkā mohakuṇḍaladhāmakī ।
mohāṃnā tava deveśi vyākhyātādya yathārthataḥ ।।
bhogamokṣapradā seyaṃ kimanyatkathayāmi te ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasividyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
mohakālividhi paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

adhunā kathayiṣyāmi tava devi yathārthataḥ ।
kālavaṃcenamevāgryaṃ mahāsiddhiprasādhanaṃ ।।

tatra yā saṃsthitā kālī sā śṛṇuṣva maheśvari ।
śruvau deśetha subhage caṃdanāgurucarcite ।।

puṣpaprakarasaṃkīrṇṇe tatra vidyāṃ samuddharet ।।
biṃdumāyā mahākālamahādhāmoparisthitaṃ ।।

sarvvāḍhyaṃ ca punaḥ kālaṃ bāhumaṃkuśasaṃyutaṃ ।
niṣedhaṃ pañcavādyasthaṃ yonipūrvvaṃ trikoṇayuk ।।

varuṇasthaṃ tasya cāntaṃ bāhuṃ cihnatrayāṃkitaṃ ।
yonipūrvvaṃ śāṅkuśaṃ syādvedraṃ kevalamuddharet ।।

vāruṇaṃ kevalaṃ dadyādbhairavāntaṃ tatoddharet ।
tṛtīyādya sthitaṃ devi tribidvāṅkaṃ niṣedhakaṃ ।।

tejandubhāryayā yuktaṃ vidyā kālanisūdanī ।
(102a)
daśapaṃcākṣarā ghorā jagadārttivināśinī ।।

mahāmṛtyuharā jñeyā sitahārendunirmalā ।
sitapadmāsanārūḍhā caṃdrārvudanibhopamā ।।

sitacaṃdrakṛtāpīḍā sudhākallolavigrahā ।
lolalaṃvāghanādevī niyataṃ kālabhakṣaṇī ।।

jinatyeva mahāmṛtyuṃ saha kasyeha līlayā ।
atha kālipadāvasthā dhyātā sā parameśvarī ।।

tadā mṛtyubhayānmuktaḥ sādhakendro bhaviṣyati ।
tadā japeta niyataṃ vidyā kālanisūdanī ।।

tadā ṣaṇmāsamātreṇa kālaṃ vaṃcayate naraḥ ।
athavā kṣuralakṣeṇa japtena tanumadhyame ।।

bhaveddevīvapuḥ sākṣātsādhakotra na saṃśayaḥ ।
yasyaiṣā śrotragā devī sajīveccharadāṃ śataṃ ।।

śāntipuṣṭikarī saumyā śubhasaubhāgyasiddhidā ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।
sarvvakarmakarī devī bahusiddhisamākulā ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasividyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
amareśvarī vidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ataḥ paraṃ vidhāsyāmi cakraṃ melāpasiddhidaṃ ।
devadevīṃ mahāraudrāṃ anekādbhutasiddhidāṃ ।।

yayā vai jñātamātrāyā sarvvaiśvaryamanuttamaṃ ।
pravarttate cireṇaiva saptadhāsau prakīrttitā ।।

tasyā vidhānamatulaṃ tava vakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ ।
(102b)
araṃbhikarṇṇinīlakṣmī viśvalakṣmī śivāraṇī ।।

tridhāmadhātmī sarvvātmadhuridhvaryā gaṇāmbikā ।
tripiṃḍapiṇḍā karmāntavividhollāsamardinī ।।

nirādhārā dharākāśacaṃdrikā kāmaleśvarī ।
śivodaradarī bhīmā tāṃ kālī jñānabhaṃjanī ।।

sphuraccidvyomasallīlāhaṃsinī haṃsalālasā ।।
pañcavyomāntapathagā taduttīrṇṇākalālayā ।।

sarvvagrāsā kuṃḍaleśī sphuratpaṃcaphaṇolvaṇā ।
mahāratnavicitrāṃgī bhāsurā kālabhakṣaṇī ।।

calatsaudāmaṇīkaikāravaṇāhitavitkulā ।
krūranistriṃśadhārograkhaṇḍinīkhaḍgavigrahā ।।

khaṃḍacakreśvarī dhārā * krāntapathabhakṣiṇī ।
saṃkocamaktā sāṃtasthā śoṇitāntravasātmikā ।।

ṣoḍaśādhāraniratā tribrahmapravikāśinī ।
vāgeśī vā kulārūḍhā bhairavī bharitānanā ।।

sphuritā varṇṇarūpeṇa praticakrolvitāsatī ।
ṛkṣākṣikṣapaṇoccaṃḍā ṛkṣamaṇḍa{la}madhyagā ।।

tadgrāsaniratā yasmātkālacakrāntasarpiṇī ।
tadā sā ṛkṣakarṇeśīṃ (kālīṃ) kālabhayapradāṃ ।।

prasuptanāgendranibhā mahogrā,
saṃtrāsanī viddhiditā sadaiva ।
tasyāḥ pravakṣyāmi nitāntasiddhaṃ,
mantraṃ samastāsurasiddhapūjyaṃ ।।

ādau vilipya vasudhāṃ vividhaiḥ sugaṃdhaiḥ,
kāśmīraśītaśaśibhirmṛgajāṃbumiśraiḥ ।
varṇṇābaliṃ tadupari pravibhaktadehaṃ,
saṃpūjya dhūpakusumairbbalibhiśca pānaiḥ ।।

sā proddharettadanupūrvvamukhāṃbujotthāṃ,
dvirnāśanīṃ sasuradaityajagajjighāṃśuṃ ।
(103a)
sādhaikapaṃcakayugārṇṇamayīṃ vicitrāṃ,
svargāpavargaphaladāṃ bhuvi harṣakarṇṇīṃ ।।

sāntaṃ likhyavaśūnyamadhyapuṭitaṃ nyasyānyatāraṃ purā ।
krūge vahnigṛhāgniśūnyasahito jyeṣṭho dvijihvastataḥ ।।
īkārāgnivisargabhūṣitamayo nāmānudevyā smaret ।
svāhākāramathāstrarājasahitaṃ nyasyāvasānakramāt ।।

tāmevāṃgavidhāṃśuśaktikavayo vyastaṃ sajātaṃ kilaṃ ।
astraṃ sātvikameva vighnaśamane yojyāṃ viśuddhātmanā ।।
guroranujñāṃ samavāpya kuryādyāgaṃ gṛheṣvāṭṭatape vaṭeṣu ।
pīṭhe kaliṃgāśrayamātṛveśma śāśūnyālaye catvareṣu ।।

bhūmiṃ parīkṣyoktavidhānabhedai,
rjyotirvidhādāvapanītaśalyāṃ ।
kuryānnatastatra sahasramāṇāṃ,
kuśeyaṃ śomanavikramatvaṃ ।।

harālayotthaiḥ smitaraktakṛṣṇaiḥ,
sucūrcitervīrarajobhivaṃdyaiḥ ।।
kāryaḥ śarīre gurubhiryathokto,
nyāsaḥ prayatnādvidhivatkrameṇa ।।

āvāhayettadanuhṛtkamalobhayāntaḥ ।
svacetasā vimalacitpatinādayogāt ।।
hṛcchūlamadhyamatayā * * * rathaināṃ ।
vinyasya tārakaśikhāmiva padmakośaiḥ ।।

hṛtkaṃśikhāvarmacaturṣu patreṣu,
āgneyapūrveṣu pareṣu cāstraṃ ।।
netraṃ tu madhye vidhinā prapūjya,
citropahārairucitairviśeṣaiḥ ।।

tathābhiṣīthe juhuyaddhutāṃśaṃ,
tilājyamāvāvṛtaphuṣphusādyai-
ryāva tṛtīyehni tathā supūjā,
(103b)
kāryā vudhairvighnanikṛntanāya ।।

dhyāye devīṃ vidya na gaganāṃ bhogagaṃbhīradehāṃ ।
nīlāṃbhojabhramarabhujagotuṃgamedyopameyān ।।
kalpāṃtāgnipravitaśikhāśekharograśīrṣāṃ ।
niryadvyoṇābhavanapavanodbhūtahomāṃcalendrāṃ ।।

netrajvālāvalayavalanālokitānekalokān ।
vidyujjihvāṃ śaśidharakalāṃ koṭitīkṣṇāgradaṃṣṭrāṃ ।।
mīnaprothāṃ bhuvanakevalaprodyanetrāṃ suvaktrāṃ ।
keyūrādipravarabhūjagairbhūṣitāśeṣadehāṃ ।।

saṃpūrṇṇāsāmamṛtasubhṛtāmabhraṭaṃkāṃkuśāṅkāṃ ।
caṃdrādityāvaparakaragau pāśamanyatra raśmiṃ ।।
sādhyāmaināṃ uragamaniśaṃ pāṇinī bhrāmayantī ।
prottuṃgādripravaraśikharottuṃgahamūrddhasaṃsthāṃ ।।

saurvīvadhva pravitataśilāstaṃbhasuklakṣṇajaṃghāṃ ।
sphujakṣetrajvalanajaṭilo dvaṃdvagonāsapādāṃ ।।

baitālairdantatālāṃ cadulakaratalairasramālākarālāṃ ।
kaṃkālaprāṃtavalladhvajapaṭapavanodbhūtanakṣatramālāṃ ।।
huṃkārairghorarūpaiḥ suraśikharidarī dhārayaṃtīṃ nitāntaṃ ।
atyugrodbhūtarūpāṃ śivarajanimukhakhecarān trāśayaṃtīṃ ।।

śīrajvare bhāskarakoṭitulyāṃ,
dāhe mṛtāpūritasarvvadehāṃ ।
vaśyeti raktā susitā ca śāntā,
vuccāṭanādāvapi tāvaciṃtyā ।।

dhanyārthibhiḥ pītatarā maheśī,
purapraveśe viśasūtramātrā ।
(104a)
lasiddhiṣaṭkāṃ bhujacakramārgā-
dūrddhapravṛttā hyamṛtaughasiktāṃ ।।

sarvvātmanātmānamataḥ sasau * ,
yuktā sthiraṃ dīdhamivānta * sthāṃ ।
dhyānāt kṣayaṃ yānti tataḥ samastā,
cidvṛttayosyānilacaṃcalāsyā ।।

jyannimnagāvāha ivātiśaktaṃ,
