prapadyate cāsu vacaḥ suśuddhaṃ ।
sarvvajñatāropyathavāsyasamya-
grupaprabhāvādakhilābhyupaiti ।।

sarvveśvarī śivamayī paramā * mātrāṃ,
vyomāgato gaganagāṃ kharathāṃ viyasthāṃ ।
brahmaṇyanantavimale gagano bhayasthe,
yāṃtī ca cetanamayīṃ paramāmṛtasthāṃ ।।

saṃpuṣṇatī jaganana * * * * * *,
rūpojjhitāṃ dhvanimayīṃ navaśabdadehāṃ ।
brahmādayomṛtabhujoghamarārisaṃghāḥ,
samyagniśuddhamatayopi vadaṃti netāṃ ।।

ekāmanekavibhavāṃ śaśanekabhinnāṃ,
toyāśayeṣu vimaleṣvivacaṃdralekhāṃ ।।
mārgatrayojvalaphalaiḥ suravṛkṣalakṣmīṃ,
mokṣārthinī kila nirargalamārgaveṣīṃ ।।

nānārathasthitamānasānāṃ,
iṣṭāṃ viśiṣṭamacirādahatī na bhāvāṃ ।
pārādimadhyarahirasya vilakṣaṇasya,
śūnyasya yā subhamalaṃkaraṇaṃ hi svasya ।।

dṛṣṭaṣpadṛṣṭaviṣaye kila līlayaiṣā,
yā sādhanaṃ bhavati dehabhṛtāṃ sukhasya ।।

evaṃ vidhāṃ bhakti parai * * drai-
rvicitrapuṣpāsavagaṃdhadhūpaiḥ ।
māṃsaiḥ khatoyaprabhavairanekai-
rasaṃskṛtaiścāpi susaṃskṛtaiśca ।।
(104b)
yajeta devīṃ manasācalena,
saṃkalpajālaṃ hṛdayaṃ niyamya ।
badhvāsanaṃ svastikapadmakaṃ vā,
śṛgāṭakaṃ vārddhaniśākaraṃ vā ।।

devyāḥ puraḥ pīṭhavareṣu vīro,
nṛsaṃkhamālāvalayāṃkahastaḥ ।
japeta devīṃ bhuvi muktanātho,
mṛgāṃkabhāgo pulineṣa ramye ।।

kāliṃjaremarvudasaṃgame vā,
tathā vanecyamaradārunāmna ।
śrīparvate viṃdhyamahādramūrddhni,
māṃmrātake vāpyathavā prayāge ।।

caṃḍeśvarasyāṃ tu gṛhe gayāyāṃ,
prabhāsatīrtheṣvatha puṣkareṣu ।
mahāśmaśānestrapariṣkṛte vā,
gṛhetha yatrābhimataḥ prasādaṃ ।।

pūjāvidhāyātra japāvasāne,
kuṃḍe ca saṃskārasusaṃskṛtegnau ।
māṃsairnarakṣyāsubhavaistilapūri-
rvāmāmṛtāktaiḥ śapharībhireva ।।

pūrṇāhutirvai rjahuyātprayatnādvāmāmṛtenaiva hi pūraṇārthaṃ ।
gṛtena vāṃ tadṛśa bhāgatoṅneṣṭaktaṃ budhaiḥ sāṃprataṃ homakarma ।।

ṣaṭpatrapaṃkaruhasadmani pūjayedvā,
devīgaṇaṃ vividhagaṃdhavaropahāraiḥ ।
madhye niveśya ca dhiyābhimatā maheśaṃ,
pūjyaḥ sureśvarabalādyaparastu devyaḥ ।।

samyaṅmayātra kathitā khalu yā hi devya-
stāsāṃ pṛthakkaraṇapūjane japyahomaṃ ।
vittānurūpamatulaṃ labhate phalaṃ sa-
stasyādanena vidhinā sakalena pūjyāḥ ।।

nāmālikheduparatāṃ varakarmaṇānta,
phaṭkāraveṣṭitaparairviṣalohitāṃgaiḥ ।
sūyāthavā pitṛvanāvatikoṭarāntaṃ,
nyastaṃ nayatyahitasantatimattakāntaṃ ।।
(105a)
bhūrjāvarīruhajaganmaṇi ṛkṣakarṇyāṃ,
saṃdarbhitaṃ raya * rocanayobhidhānaṃ ।
rakṣāsagarvitamariṃ pṛthivīśvaraṃ vā,
siddhāṃganāni ca yadā suvaśaṃ karoti ।।

tadevāsitasiddhārtha rasena vilikheddhruvaṃ ।
bhūrje hi barhitvaṃ nādyaṃ nikhanyā svacatuḥpathe ।।

kānte vāthakārānte vā vidveṣaṇakaraṃ paraṃ ।
proktaṃ tridaśanāthesyā karmaṇā siddhimūrttinā ।

tacca pretapaṭe kṛtvā rudhirā ca surāṃgajaiḥ ।
laghudikpāvakāṣṭottha vinyasyāṃ tu tadādivat ।।

tatra bhramati tadyāvadaniśaṃ vā yune gataṃ ।
tāvadutsādayet sthānādahitaughaṃ suduḥsahaṃ ।।

kāśmīrarocanābhyāṃ tu bhūrje vinyasya vidyayā ।
śobhane paṭapare nāmamalaṃkusumapūjitaṃ ।

vidhinā bhaktyāvṛnaṃ vidhānaṃ,
tvabhilaṣitāśeṣakāryasiddhikaraṃ ।।

vāyuvege ca śikhāyāṃ,
galetha vastre tathopavīte vā ।।

jvaralūlākhilaviṣahṛddivyavidhvāvudyatārigaṇamadhye ।
rakṣati sutavadakāṇḍaṃ na bhavati bhūtādividravastasya ।।

anavaratasamaramukhe śaranikaraṃ viprakīrṇamarivṛṃdaiḥ ।
nipatanyevayavanicaye kusumavadaparāgale yasya ।।

likhiteyaṃ yantragṛhe tiṣṭhati vidyā na tatra bhogibhayaṃ ।
caurāgnivighnarakṣe nṛpanṛpatiṃ vā kācidapi tatra iti ।।

kathitaṃ bhuvaneśyā vidhānamamalaṃ samāsoktyā ।
(105b)
vistṛtiranena vidhinā noktaṃ yattatparā proktaṃ ।। ।।

iti prathamaḥ ।। ।।

sānaṃdaṃ sasmayaṃ ca tridaśamamṛtayā santataṃ prokṣyamāṇaṃ,
pādāṃbojaṃ jagati tribhuvanavijayatrāyanāmāśvarāyāḥ ।
yasminnyaste samastaṃ ripuśirasi rasāmaṃdralaṃ kampamānāḥ,
śeṣānāsādyamāne ciravidhuraphaṇāmaṇḍale nīlalaṃbhe ।।

samagrāsurataṃtrāsajananīṃ satīṃ raudrīṃ ।
praṇamya śirasā dvitīyāgamasiddhaye ।।

prastāraṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā sasaddeva sudhātale ।
sārddhadvipaṃcavarṇākā mālinīskaṃdasoddharet ।।

gagane malayayānaṃ vyomapaṃcāṃvakeṣu ।
kubhṛvarato bhogni dvādaśāṃtāṃtacihnaḥ ।।
tadanu bhavati dharmontesya varṇasvarāntaḥ ।
vahiśirasi hutāśastrisvarāṃkastatostraṃ ।।

svāhāntesyā praṇavamadhunāsaṃsmareccātra ।
hastau jaṃghā sakṛdanu tathā yojayedaṃjalisthaṃ ।।
vidyāṃ paścātsakalatanugāṃ vinyatsvottamāṅgāṃ ।
kṛtvā nyāsaṃ tadanu bhuvane pūrvaliptesugupte ।।

vīrāṃgārairālikhedyasya śīrṣa,
raudrarkāṃke kusumasahite viṣṭapercatirasyai,
bhairavyākhyāmapi ca nikhilā nābhihṛtkaṃṭhadeśe ।
kāryārṇānāṃ vikṛtanayanastīvramanyurniśāsu ।

saṃtāḍayetkāmarakātmajena,
tridhā triśūlena yathākrameṇa ।
kāpālakṛnmantrapavitritena,
yathārdhvaruṃ caṃdrakalādharohaṃ ।।

kāyokṣarairnāmayutaṃ dvidhānte,
mantrasya yājyajapakarmakāle ।
(106a)
kṛtau kilāriṃvigatābhimānaṃ,
vicintaye jñānamivābhidhāṃ ।।

ādāyamānāṃ chagalo gajotthā,
sthūlāṃ sphuṭāgraṃthiśatāṣṭagūḍhāṃ ।
saṃkhyārthamatyugratamāṃ trinetrāṃ,
dhyātvā surāsvātmani ṛkṣakarṇī ।।

ghorāṃdhakāre vighatāṃ varakālamegha,
kākālikālakulakokilameva kāntāṃ ।
śrīkaṃṭhakaṃṭhaśitikaṃṭhabhujaṅgasaṃga,
bhinnāṃjanāṃcalatamālanṛpopalābhān ।।

saṃhāraghoradahanodvataraśmicakra,
piṃgoccacaṃcupuṭitārkanibhorddhakeśī ।
ghoṇāsamutthapavanabhramitācalendraṃ,
trailokyasārakakaloghavājivaktrāṃ ।।

daṃṣṭrāviṣaṃkaṭavamasanānalogra,
grāsīkṛtākhilajagattritayāṃ * riṣṭāṃ ।
prāvṛ * śābhravisṛto jvalavidyudugra,
bhrūyugmabhāgabhayadā bhavanaṃgadakṣān ।।

caṃdravatkanakaphenataraṃgasaṅga,
prādyadviśālataravudvudaraudranetrāṃ ।
āsīviṣairviṣamabhūṣitapīnagātrīṃ,
tīvrāṭṭahāsavidhurīkṛtasaptalokāṃ ।।

jvālāsya bhīmabhujagākṛtipāṇimālāṃ,
daityādhipojvalaśiromaṇicaṃdragarbhāṃ ।
gaurārkakoṭikiraṇaprakaroparūḍhāṃ,
lākṣārasāruṇakalāhṛlāsyakarṇāṃ ।।

saudāmaṇīvalayanirmalalolavaṃśa,
nistriṃśabhāsurakarāṃ vikarālanetrāṃ ।
śūraprabhāprasarapūritadigvibhāgāṃ,
bhogīndramaṇḍalavimaṃḍitabāhudaṇḍāṃ ।।

ghaṃṭāṃ trilohaghaṭitāṃ parighaṭṭayantī,
(106b)
trailokyanāśasamayāni ca sūdayantīṃ ।
dhūmāvalīvalayanirmalapātrahastāṃ
hemā ca pravaratuṃgaguraṃgasaṃsthāṃ ।।

pretendrapannagacalāmalabhogabhīma,
jaṃghāyugākramaṇapūritasarvvalokāṃ ।
jvālāsahasraparilīḍhadigambarābhāṃ,
siddhārcitāṃ hri kamalāmanusaṃsmarettāṃ ।।

ekāmanekāṃgavibhāgabhinnāṃ,
yathai * lekhāṃ salilā * yeṣu ।
karmasvatekeṣu sadā narendrai-
rvicintanīyāḥ svamanohiteṣu ।।

pūjājapomaṇḍalahomakarma,
vidhistvathoktena samāsakāmaiḥ ।
yataḥ prasiddhaḥ sahalakṣaṇena
tantreṣvathoḍḍāmarasaṃjñiteṣu ।।

evaṃ hi karma kurvvāṇāḥ sādhako ṛpusādhane ।
dadyāttatsamaye nūnaṃ dīpaṃ vīryaprasādhanaṃ ।।

prathamehani sāṃdhyasya vyādhirutpadyate bhṛśaṃ ।
yamupāśayituṃ śakto na bhavedbhiṣagapyalaṃ ।।

yasyauṣadhairbhramantrāstraiḥ pṛpto vāmaṇisaṃcayaiḥ ।
nūnaṃ paṃcatvamāyāti śakraścakrāntakopamaḥ ।।

dinaiḥ saptibhirāpūrṇaiḥ pratīkāravivarjitaṃ ।
rakṣācchedādikaṃ kuryāt purākarma tataḥ paraṃ ।।

āgamoyaṃ mahāvīrairanubhūyo mayoddhṛtaḥ ।
sarvvasatvahitārthāya tatresmin śivāya ca ।। ।।

iti dvitīyaḥ ।। ।।

astraṃ nistriṃśamukhyaṃ na hi sirasā tyātitaṃ jātavegaṃ,
nāpi prāsaṃ salāsaṃ tata bhujalatayāṃ prerito durnirādhaḥ ।
nākāṃḍe kāṃḍamastaṃ karakamalalasadvyākulatve * tīta,
(107a)
pādāgreṇātyubuddhyā ṛpusahavadanaṃ yā śivā sārvvatārddhaḥ ।।

kāpālamālābharaṇāṃ śaśāṅkakṛtaśekharāṃ ।
vaṃde kāpālinīṃ kālīṃ tṛtīyā sarvvasiddhaye ।।

praṇavaṃ dharmasaṃmetaṃ bhamavatyante padaṃte padaṃte ṛkṣakarṇī ca,
tadanu caturbhuja iti padamaparamūrddhakeśī ca ।
vikṛtānane ca paścāttadātā kālarātrī ca ।
asyāṃte mānuṣāṇāṃ vasārudhirabhojane * ti ।।

ṛpugātraṃ ṣaṣṭhyāntaṃ paścādaprāptakālasya mṛtyu-
pradeśatāraṃ phaṭkārasamanvitaṃ ca deveśyāḥ ।

dvidhākṣapādyoparatotrakārṇa,
manādaśūnyaṃ tadanukṣapeśaṃ ।
brahmāsanāgnitvatha kārmukārṇaṃ,
salaṃvikaṃ khāṅkamathāhaśārṇaṃ ।।

nyasetya varṇasya ca mūrddhne pūrvaṃ,
māyāsada * stvatha yostu madhye ।
niyojayantī ma * te dharākhyā,
vargā dvitīyaḥ śaśalāṃchanāṅkaṃ ।।

nyasedikārāntamahābhidhānāṃ,
devyāstato dīpakasaṃprayuktāṃ ।
ekādhikāṃ saptatirakṣarāṇāṃ,
vidyayamuktā hi tadā hitānāṃ ।।

na cātra velā na tapo na ṛkṣaṃ,
siddhyatyasau nā yadi bhāvitātmā ।
liṃgaṃ karābhyāṃ pratigṛhya rātrau,
vidhānataḥ svāśravilepitābhyāṃ ।।

japettatra kruddhamānāḥ svadeha,
dhyātvā svavaktrāṃ pralayānalogrāṃ ।
evaṃ kṛte rātririkāya ugrāṃ,
kṛtāntadhārāṃ vividhā surādyāṃ ।।
(107b)
vrajanti rakṣāstra ivāsvataṃtro,
yadendravajrapratimopi hiṃsyāt ।
ucchādaye cāsya kulaṃ samagraṃ,
yadā japedrāma * taḥ samagrāṃ ।।

śiraḥ kapāle cānyonyaṃ nihanyātkruddhamānasaḥ ।
āyojanaśatāḥ śaktu nihanyeṣāṃ na saṃśayaḥ ।।

mantraṃ japennarakṛtyuparikṣapāyāṃ,
sāṣṭāṃ sahasramathavā dviguṇaṃ narendraḥ ।
nūnaṃ nipātayati śatrusamūhamugraṃ,
varṣeṇa vā niyatamaṣṭasahasrajāpān ।। ।।

iti tṛtīyaḥ ।। ।।

cittaṃ vṛttālimānā vicalikhitabhūtkārmukaṃ karmahīnaṃ ।
caṇḍaścaṇḍāśamunoḥ kṛtarudhirabhavakhaṃḍane maṃḍitopi ।।
pāśānasya prakāśaṃ paravalamathane dāruṇe vāruṇosau ।
yasmin dṛṣṭe nisṛṣṭe ṛpuśirasi manaḥ pātu pādau bhavānyā ।।

phaṇīndrabhogasaṃprota prahasatpremamekhalāṃ ।
vaṃde kātyāyaṇīṃ caṇḍāṃ caturyāgasasiddhaye ।।

vedādyamādyāvinijādyamante,
nadījamagne salilaṃ tadante ।
hayānilāṃtau gagaṇatrayāṃkā,
vaṃtyākṣataṃ cākṛtatasvarātaṃ ।।

sulaṃvikaṃṭhahrimathoragacchaṃ,
tripūrvvamaścāntanukārayānāṃ ।
rāvāntagaṃ vaktravidāraṇāṅkaṃ,
rākrāntamasyāgragato nabhorṇṇaṃ ।।

no yāstasaṃyuktamathorddhakeśi,
padaṃ tadante vikṛtānane ca ।
kāleti varṇāvanalaṃ sanādaṃ,
tārṇaṃ tato viṣṇuyutaṃ śikhisthaṃ ।।

triśūnya pūrvalāṃcchitaṃ sahasraraśmisaṃbhavaṃ ।
(108a)
śarā satāntasāntagā yathā gajāṃkuśāṅkitau ।।

turagamantaramaṃvarayuktaṃ,
sāmyavidāraṇamamaravanāntaṃ ।
tadaṃtu niśākaramambaramuktaṃ,
rudhirapadaṃ parato viniyujyān ।।

rāvān tṛtīyaṃ daśanāṃvarākaṃ,
dvijihvamīkārayutaṃ ca vāntaṃ ।
vinyasya ṣaṣṭhyāntamathābhidhāna,
* prāptakālasya tathottataḥ syāt ।।

tataḥ kṣurayojayenmṛtyusaṃjñe,
krūrādyamagradahanorddharucādisaṃsthaṃ ।
gāpīṭhapūrvvantu kalāṣṭamāṅkaṃ,
māyāsanādaṃ parato nidadyāt ।।

hanihiragne dahavāpi tadva-
ddhomaṃ tato māṃsanadantamanta ।
padaṃ tu paścādrudhiraṃ paceti,
krodhastathārghatyavukarṇisūryau ।।

vidyā tviyaṃ hyahitanāmapadārṇahīnā,
varṇatrisaptatiyutā hi tadā natānāṃ ।
samyaṅnipātayati yuktiniyojitānāṃ,
prāguktakarmavaya * * kute samagraṃ ।।

kiṃcidviśeṣamasyāḥ prathamaṃ tāvadvadāmi saṃkṣepād ।
vipinaṃ vraje śmaśānaṃ digvāsā yuktamūrddhajo bhūtvā ।।
tadbhasma samagraṃ trisaptastena bhūṣayetsāṅgaṃ ।।

vāmādbhavarevidhinā nāmālikhyātmaśośite care ।
anavaratajapābhyādyojanalakṣānnipātayenmantrī ।।
asyāḥ paraṃ vidhānaṃ devyadhunā guptamatigūḍhaṃ ।

nṛkaṃdalaṃ cintyalinā surekhaṃ,
kṛtirvilikhyāpyanupīḍayettāṃ ।
karaṇaṃ samyaṅmaṇimaṃtragūḍhaṃ,
hanyāṃ nṛpaṃ sāṣṭaṃ sahasrajāpā,
purohitaṃ bhraṣṭaṃ bhirasya śeṣaṃ ।।
(108b)
śainyaṃ tathā ṣaḍbhirataḥ sahasraiḥ,
śāntistathā saptabhireva nūnaṃ ।
narendrapatnī tava saṃprayāti,
* * * * * * * * * * * ।।

māṃsāśīlakṣasaṃyacca jape deva sādhako vacasā ।

utsādayati trailokyaṃ nātra saṃdeha kṛtvā ।
kṛtvā pūrvaṃ guruvacanakṛnmaṃḍale yāgabhūmau ।।

kravyāhāro vrataniyamāṃ ukta jāpādyathāvat ।
lakṣyābhyāsādatudāṃ vīkṣyate grāhayāsyāṃ ।।

siddhadravyādyasitasukhadāṃ siddhimabhyarcyanīyā ।
aśvatthapṛṣṭhasaṃstho yadi japati vaśāmṛkṣakarṇṇā sugupte ।।
saṃhehe sādhakendraḥ surabhikusumasṛkcandanādyairvicitraiḥ ।
sādhyā samyak sahasraṃ suramunivalāstūrṇamāyāṃtyupāntaṃ ।।
svargodyānāni hutvā kanakatarulatāmuṃḍalāmaṃḍitāni ।

maṃdārareṇu haricaṃdanagaṃdhahṛdya,
hṛdyāstuṃ naṃdanavanātvaravedikāsu ।
yatrocyate manasi yauvana vihvalānāṃ,
hāsudarasarāḥ surasuṃdarīṇāṃ ।।

yatsthīyatepyasuralokamavāpyarasya,
mākalyamujvalaphalāprasavaikahetuṃ ।
pūrṇendu tulya la * nājanatārthyamāna-
stannitya japya phalaleśajalavipluśāstā ।। ।।

iti caturthaḥ ।। ।।

vinyasto vairimūrdhni sphuṭakanakatulākoṭibhirme cakāte ।
sā devī mācavighnaṃ viṣadamavicalaṃ maṅgalaṃ vastunotu ।।
avo bhārābhirāmā viralajaladharaprāntaviśrāntamūrteḥ ।
(109a)
saudāmanyāvilāsaṃ kṣaṇamanukṛtavān pādapadmā yadīyaḥ ।।

paṃcamāgamasiddhyarthaṃ vaṃde ghoreśvarīṃ parāṃ ।
dṛḍhapāṣṇighaṃnāṃghāta bhava brahmāṇḍakarparāṃ ।।

kathitātra yā tṛtīye vidhau maheśī bhavecca ।
saiveha kiṃtu viśeṣo yosyāstamahaṃmato gūḍhatāṃ vacmi ।।

paṃcadaśārṇṇādūrddhaṃ paramasyadvarṇa vā khaciti ।
tāmaṃ vihāya madhyāttasthāne nyasyadeva dantapadaṃ ।।

sakalākṣarasaṃyuktaṃ vigrahapadamantare tathā yojyaṃ ।
hana yugmā daha śabdā dvidhāntato yojayenmatimān ।।

adharasakalopasaṃsthaṃ hitvemadīpaśāntagaṃ pūjyāt ।
karṇṇīti keśarā trikrameṇa yujyāṃ tu bhojanetyasmin ।।

evaṃ vidhaṃ tu viduṣā vidyāntaragaṃ padaṃ gaṃ catuṣkaṃ ।
prādhikāśīti varṇātidyeyaṃ saṃkhyayā vinirdiṣṭāṃ ।।

kaṭvamlaśnehātyaktā, kṛṣṇāṃvarasaṃvṛtositohrāsubhe ।
upaviśyo turagakṛtā varttakadisthaṃ sthito malaṃ juhuyāt ।।

nijarudhirākto vidhinā nṛpasiddhārthasapatnanāśayaliṃga ।
śirasyūrddhe vātairevārghyaṃ vidhānato deyaṃ ।।

upanataśirasijanirgitadṛḍhatarayāraśatayāvasaṃdhyāyāṃ ।
taṃ manyaye kruddhānta prāk sevārthaṃ japecchatasahasraṃ ।।

evaṃ kṛtena mahatā sasiddhidhinānena sādhakāḥ ।
kṣipraṃ pātayati vivudhavṛṃdanākasthaṃ kimuparodyamalyāstu ।।
(109b)
ityuktvā payaniṣilaḥ paramo vidhiruttamaḥ ।
plavadbhūto manuṣyāṇāṃ nimagnānāṃ paro vidhau ।। ।।

iti paṃcamaḥ ।। ।।

nāstrairastraṃ nirastaṃ navasubhaṭadyutā khaṃḍitā kāṇḍavakṣaiḥ ।
pādāgreṇāpahāraṃ raṇaśirasiruṣā sā vahuntopakāraṃ ।।
āsthāneyaṃ prahāsorvihita iti mayā lajjayā krāntamūrtyā ।
saṃkrāntyā khaḍgavalyāmivarugiti yayā sā vatālaṃvikāvāḥ ।।

bhogīndrabhogaprathitanarapotakanūpurāṃ ।
ṣaṣṭhyāgamaprasiddhyarthyaṃ vaṃde vivudhamātaraṃ ।।

devyā nāma samuddhṛtya tadantārṇā caladyuti ।
nirāgnī vāhanā ceti namaskārastataḥ paraṃ ।।

vāmādhogramadopetā dvisaptārṇṇeyamuddhṛtā ।
niyojitā nihatyugrāṃ durjñeyā ṛpusantati ।।

kṣetrāṣṭagryāṃ bhūrjapatre likhitvā,
vinyasyaivaṃ kārṇakotkīrṇṇasubhyāṃ ।
saṃsthānāntaścāhastau tathābhyāṃ,
liptānyasya nimathenmatramugraṃ ।।

kruddhastadvalliṃgaṃ masmādahaṃ,
kuryāttasmiṃścājiremekaṛkṣe ।।
rakṣā kāryāpyakṣavāyā dinaiṣāṃ,
liṃgādīnāṃ śreyase sādhakānāṃ ।

dhyāyedātmāmātrīśān kriyānalasamākulā ।।
mahābhairavasaṃkāśāṃ bhairavasyātibhītidāṃ ।
samagrāsurasaṃtrāsajananīṃ vītamātarāṃ ।।
śivāya sarvvasatvānāṃ kṣayāyāhitasantatau ।। ।।
(110a)
iti ṣaṣṭhaḥ ।। ।।

kālākṣiptāvakāśāya sadalaghaṭitā śṛṃkhalā prollasantī ।
pūrvadṛṣṭinnisṛṣṭā vibudhapatiramoḥ karṣaṇāyā sapīṭhe ।।
paścāt krīḍārthamānādavanivanaguruṃ pādacakraṃ yayāsau ।
durgā vargatrayaṃ vo diśatu bhagavatī sādaraṃ sarvvakālaṃ ।।

paretarājayāyinīṃ śaśāṅkakhaṇḍaśekharāṃ ।
namāmi saptamāgamaprasiddhayedhvasaṃbhavāṃ ।।

sarvvāṇacāpādṛtusaṃkhyamarṇṇa,
khatarjanī yojitayāvyatāraṃ ।
nyasyettadāgra svaramaśvasaṃkhyaṃ,
hāntaḥ svaraktāvaparau tatorṇau ।।

yonyaṃkamagnitvanuyojayāhe,
ruparyuṣomaṃ turagākṣareyaṃ ।
hitvā dvitīyākṣaramaṅgaṣaṭkaṃ,
śeṣaiḥ prakalpaṃ kramaśo hṛdādi ।।

dhyātvātmānaṃ devatārūpamugraṃ,
kālāṃ saṃsthaṃ saptarātraṃ śikhāyāṃ ।
sāṣṭaṃ smṛtvā śatamapyatra saṃsthaṃ,
bhītaṃ dīnaṃ cintayetsādhyadehaṃ ।।

tasyai vāgānyadhunotkṛtyakāyāṃ,
kālasyāsye citya saghorarūpe ।
evaṃ samyakkarma kurvansamagraṃ,
trailokyeśaṃ nāśadagū * deśa ।।

dhyāyedvā ghorarūpāṃ naracaraṇaśirasragdharāṃ bhīmarūpāṃ ।
vaktrākāśādbhṛśasthāṃ taḍitimiva vṛtāṃ sādhyadehaṃ viśantīṃ ।।
tatrasthāmabjamabjāṃ kṛtabhavapalalakṣetramedāvasośca ।
(110b)
bhuṃjānaṃ prāṇamabjātpibati matirivāhīnavṛṃdaṃ kṣudhārttaṃ ।।

dhyānenānena cetsyādabhimatavihito brahmaruṃdra * tulyaḥ ।
sopyugrāṃ kāladhārīṃ vrajati suviṣamāsasvataṃtrye hyarakṣaḥ ।।
dhyāyedevaṃ kṣapāyāṃ śaśi nidhanagatebhyudyatorervvadhāya ।
yogastadvyānahīnā janayati vividhā nāśayaṃ khalvasādhyāṃ ।।
yogānāmūrddhanīndoḥ sakalamiva sado mādhavasya sthitosau ।।

ityuktapaśoṣṭātsoktaḥ parātsa viṭapānalaḥ ।
vidhisuvihitodriṣṭipāṭanākliṣṭacetasā ।। ।।

iti saptamaḥ ।। ।।

ādau sahāyamabhayaṃ viśatākṣacittaṃ,
tacchāsanābhiratamugramalubdhamugryaṃ ।
nirmatsaraṃ sakalacittavikalpahīnaṃ,
rakṣā mamātmasadṛśaṃ niyataṃ prakuryāt ।।

ekasya maṃtriṇo yasmācchidyamutpādyate bhṛśaṃ ।
atyāgamo mahāghoraḥ prāṇahṛtsvalpacetasāṃ ।।

tasmādvīraguṇopetaḥ sarvvalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ ।
ātmano nikaṭasthāpya sadaivottarasādhakaḥ ।।

yadā akasmādbhīmodyātsādhako mahatī niśi ।
tadā khadhāraṇāṃ dhyāyedātmanyaśubhaśāntaye ।।

cidvajrapaṃjaraguhāmākramyaivāvatiṣṭhata ।
yanmanaḥ kesarī tasya na bhīrāḥ saṃhṛtātmanaḥ ।।

śucīnāmavikalpasthā samādhyasunimajjatāṃ ।
yoginīgaṇavṛṃdāṃ gabharīruḥ kamalaṃ kuru ।।

sarvvavṛrtinidha * ścabrahmāstrātsiktacetasāṃ ।
(111a)
kaulinīkulakālotthā ghoracchidrabhayaṃ kutaḥ ।।

viśuddhe bodharūpasya nirālaṃbāmṛtātmanaḥ ।
kevalasyādvitīyasya sādhakasya bhayaṃ kutaḥ ।।

savidbhāsvadravidvastu sāyāṃdhamasasya tu ।
praviṣṭasyādvaye śūnye kuto naiṣā vibhītayā ।।

jāgratsvapnasuṣuptācca bhūmī nityasādhakaḥ ।
cicchreṇyoparivartante ātmanestṛvibhītayaḥ ।।

śūnyāgnidagdhadurbhītaḥ kāraṇādvaitadhāraṇā ।
vidvāmannicarabhyuccairnnarakeśari * ściraṃ ।।

bhinnavijñānasaṃdhānarūpākhyāvaraṇātmanaḥ ।
maṃtriṇo brahmabhūtasya kuto bhavati bhūtayaḥ ।।

ātmarūpatayā yasya pararūpaṃ prakāśate ।
svarūpapracyutistasya naivāvasthāsu jāyate ।।

savisargasmarenmantrī tarjanyāṃ tarjanī krudhā ।
vācayālikamūrddhena muhyate māsakaiḥ tribhiḥ ।।

sādhyanāmodayaṃ prāpya divasatkṣakriyocate ।
pāpagrahaṃ tathāviṣṭametatkarmasamācaret ।।

pūrvamantre suvistīrṇaṃ sarvvametanmayeritaṃ ।
tato nirūpya kartavyaṃ yathāvatkarmakīrttitaṃ ।।

mṛtohamathavā siddhimityadhyāya hṛdi sthiraṃ ।
yathoktamanukartavyaṃ nānyathā siddhimāvahet ।।

ekāhādatha saptāhāttrisaptāhādathāpi vā ।
prasiddhyetā na saṃdeho yadi syādbhāvabhāvitaḥ ।।

avasādena gantavyā vidhihīnaṃ vivarjayet ।
nāstikatvamanutsāhagathālasyabhayaṃ tathā ।।
(111b)
varjayenityamevātra somacaṃḍālake yathā ।
nāstikeṣvatha pāpeṣu gurvagnyoniṃdakeṣu ca ।।

rakṣitavyaṃ prayatnena taskarebhyo yathā dhanaṃ ।
na dūratasta * * siddhiṃ evaṃ yo vetti tatvataḥ ।।

prāṇādisaṃ * * viloma yuktyā,
ciccetanopādhivaśānuyogāt ।
ghrāṇodare khātilaśabdadehe,
śaktyuṃtarehaṃkṛti buddhyagarbhe ।।

vittormimaṃtropari veśitātmā,
saṃvitaprabhābhāsvaralābhamārgāṃ ।
bhettavyabhāvaṃ vibudho vibhāvya,
buddhyādvṛttiṃ prātagṛhīta yātā ।।

yatheṣunmadā vāsavacittalābha,
mṛṇāladaṇḍāniva saptatālāṃ ।
sa evamālāvya śirāsya jidmaiḥ,
śarairyathā jāmnijakarttatīkṣṇaiḥ ।।

padmāni cakrāṇi ca vittayogāṃ,
cchiṃdyāttathā tatkaruṇapracāraṃ ।
akṛṣya puryaṣṭakaveṣṭitābja,
garbhāgryāraṃdhrādapi tasya jīvaṃ ।।

cidāṃkuśaprotpalatoyabaddhā,
ātmāmayaṃ cātmani sannidhāya ।
sauṣumnanālyantarapyatakodare,
prāpyeta tatraiva vitastare tanaḥ ।।

bhūyastataḥ suma * tāparaśaktipāśe,
noddhṛtya rajjughaṭakūpavadātmadehaṃ ।
bhūvyomni recakavaśādvyanavadvilāpya,
yābhyeva māsusadanaṃ ṛpunantakasya ।।

bhayā(dvi)dheyo vidhuyānavigra,
stathārthalobhā tu kadācaneva hi ।
jijñāsayāsu sthitacetasā satāṃ,
kilaiṣa mārgastvasatāmatonyathā ।।

kuryātkaṃṭhāśritaiḥ prāṇairnavā kuryādasāṃprataṃ ।
(112a)
āyuṣaḥ kṣaṇabhaṃgatvādattareṇātatāyinaṃ ।।

garāgnidārthastrīkṣetrataskarāyasapāṇayaḥ ।
prāhu paurāṇikāstvete munayo hyātatāyinaḥ ।।

anyathā kriyamāṇesmin mahāpātakabhāgbhavet ।
prāyaścittavidhiṃ paścāttantrādivihitaṃ caret ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kālasaṃkarṣaṇyāṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

bhagavan devadeveśa bālacaṃdrārddhaśekhara ।
saṃśayaṃ paramaṃ deva vaktumarhasyaśeṣataḥ ।।

śrutāni sarvvaśāstrāṇi tvayā nigatāni vai ।
yāmalā nimalā caiva tathā saṃmohanādayaḥ ।।

bhūtataṃtrā hyanekāsu tathā svāyaṃbhuvā pare ।
mantravādā hyanekāśca vāmaśrotā śrutāni vai ।।

kulaśāstrāṇyanekāśca devaśāstrāstathā pare ।
evamādiranekāśca ye cānye nānukīrttitāḥ ।।

kālasaṃkarṣaṇī devī kalpaṃ vada surādhipa ।
kā sā parāparā devī kālasaṃkarṣaṇī parā ।।

yayā vai jñātamātrāyā mahāpāpaṃ vyapohati ।
kālīti nāmakā tasyāḥ kena cotpāditāparā ।।

etanme vada deveśa paraṃ kautūhalaṃ mama ।
iti śrutvā tadā vākyaṃ devadevīvravīdvacaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi rahasyaṃ kathayāmi te ।
na kasyacinmayākhyātaṃ tavādya kathayāmi te ।।

kailāsaśikhare ramye nānādhātuvicitrite ।
nānādrumalatākīrṇe apsarogaṇasevite ।।
(112b)
skaṃdanaṃdigaṇāstatra sthitadeśe samantataḥ ।
dhyānena sahatāviṣṭo parabhāvasthito hyahaṃ ।।

tadā me paramāśaktiḥ kaṃdacakrāt prabodhitā ।
mahāsaṃvitsvarūpā sā saccidānaṃdanaṃdinī ।।

saṃghaṭṭavivarādhāre madhyatatve samāsataḥ ।
tatra madhye parājyotiḥ sadasadvayamadhyagā ।।

tasminmadhye yathābhāvaṃ sthitaṃ me parvatātmaje ।
kālī kālasya karaṇī kalpanāheturūpiṇī ।।

tadā sā paramā devī nirgatā kālabhakṣaṇī ।
svānaṃdānaṃdasaṃlīnā svabhāvābhāvanotkaṭā ।।

cidacitpadasaṃrūḍhā cidacitpadamadhyagā ।
mahāvyomātmikā devī unmanā kālabhakṣaṇī ।।

kālī kālasya karaṇī turyādipravikāśinī ।
samyagākarṣayedyasmāttena saṃkarṣaṇī smṛtā ।।

ākarṣayetparaṃ sthānaṃ khe yantrātpīḍayedyadā ।
prabodhaṃ kurute yasmāttasmātsaṃkarṣaṇī smṛtā ।।

vyomnā ca varttate yasmāt prāṇāpānapravāhakī ।
dvādaśāntasthitā yasmāttasmātkālī parā smṛtā ।।

kālaṃ kalayate yasmāttasmātkālī tu devatā ।
anacchāntarasaṃrūḍhā sūkṣmā sarvvagatā tu sā ।।

vyāpakī sarvvabhūtānāṃ mahākālī tadā smṛtā ।
jñānaśaktiḥ parānaṃdā svabhāvānaṃdanaṃdinī ।।

ekoccārasamādhārātsvarūpaparivarttinī ।
paṃcāntaragatā śū(ṣkā) prasarat prāṇarūpiṇī ।।

nirābhāsā nirākārā tanu kālīti sā smṛtā ।
(113a)
nityānaṃdapadārūḍhā prāṇāpānaprakakṣayī ।।

yadā samādhisallīnā tadā kālīti sā smṛtā ।
vyāpakā paramā śaktiḥ sūkṣmā taṃtusvarūpiṇī ।।

nirīhā nirguṇā śāntā prabodhakaraṇī parā ।
praviśetsā sṛte bhoge śaktirūpeṇa suvrate ।।

tanmadhye kṛkalārūpā gaṃdharūpā parāparā ।
vāsanāṃjanarūpā sā nirīha śaktirūpiṇī ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathe kālike prathamaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

bhagavan devadeveśa śūlapāṇin trilocana ।
kathayasva prasādena śarīre kā parā smṛtā ।।

etanme devadeveśa sakalā niṣkalā kathaṃ ।।
adhārabhedaṃ deveśa kathayādya prasādataḥ ।। ।।

śṛṇu samyakpravakṣyāmi rahasya kulagocare ।
ūhakādikrameṇaiva śṛṇu samyagvadāmi te ।।

ūha ūrddhagataḥ kāryā kālāgniyamagāstathā ।
sa eva gulphajānūrū sarvvādhāre layaṃ yayau ।।

sarvvādhāraṃ tu saṃkoce saṃkocaṃ turyamadhyage ।
turyakoṇaṃ tu janmākhya janmakandasya bhāmini ।।

kaṃdaṃ kuṇḍalimadhye tu asau cāpi ghaṭodare ।
taṃ vai saṃjīvanīsthānaṃ bhūtānāṃ bhūtasaṃpuṭe ।।

taṃtu lolālayaṃ devi asau laṃbākhya bhāmini ।
laṃbākhyaṃ gurucakrasthaṃ guruṃ ajamukhe sthitaṃ ।।

ajaṃ viṣuvamadhyasthaṃ viṣuvaṃ tu visargakaṃ ।
iti ṣoḍaśamādhārā piṇḍasthā tu prakīrttitā ।।

eṣāṃ svarūpabhāvaṃ ca yathā devi vyavasthitaṃ ।
yathā samyakpraveśoyaṃ yathārūpaṃ tathā śṛṇu ।।
(113b)
na kasyacittvayā bhadre rahasyaṃ śaktigocaraṃ ।
devīnāṃ paramaṃ tatvaṃ vaktrādvaktreṣu saṃsthitaṃ ।।

pāraṃparyakramāyātaṃ devīnāṃ hṛdaye sthitaṃ ।
tathā te katha(yiṣyā)mi yathā me śṛṇu gocare ।।

śrutaṃ suvistaraṃ devi uhakādivibhāgaśaḥ ।
pādādhāre sthitaṃ cakraṃ ūhakaṃ nāma viśrutaṃ ।।

udbhavaṃ sarvvacakrāṇāṃ sahasrārkaṃ samaprabhaṃ ।
saṃvidānaṃdabhāvena praviśe tu nagātmaje ।।

taṃ cakraṃ cakracakrāṇāṃ udayaṃ sarvvakāmadaṃ ।
ṣoḍaśāraṃ suvistīrṇaṃ karṇikākeśarairyutaṃ ।।

tatra sā paramāśaktiḥ sthitā bhāsātmikā parā ।
ata ūrddhaṃ mahācakraṃ kālāgnibhuvanottamaṃ ।।

dvātriṃśāraṃ mahādīptaṃ sarvvavarṇojvalaṃ śubhaṃ ।
tatra viśveśvarī nāma sthitā vai cakranāyakī ।।

tatra madhye paraṃ tejo (bhū)ribhāskarasaṃnibhaṃ ।
tasya madhye bhavetsā tu śāntā nāma parāparā ।।

tatorddha paramaṃ cakraṃ sarvvādhāraṃ mahāprabhaṃ ।
pradīptapāvakākāraṃ catuḥṣa(ṣṭhyā) - kairyutaṃ ।।

cakranāyaki sā devī mahogrā nāma viśrutā ।
tatorddhaparamaṃ cakraṃ saṃkoca nāma viśrutaṃ ।।

apāno nāma tatraiva marutsannihitaḥ sadā ।
cakreśī tatra bhavate aṃbe nāmeti viśrutā ।।

sadasatpadasaṃlīne bhavet samarasīgate ।
pakṣadvayaparityakte kāle viṣuvasaṃsthite ।।

uccaretsahajaṃ nādaṃ dvikubjaṃ vyomakhābjagaṃ ।
tena saṃbodhayettatra yāvadbrahmavilāntaraṃ ।।

saṃkocaṃ ca vikāśaṃ ca kārayedyogavitsadā ।
(114a)
yāvaccotpatate samyagabhyāsānmāsataḥ priye ।।

atītaṃ caiva jānāti varttamānaṃ tathaiva ca ।
sarvvavidbhavate so vai sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

evaṃ ta sarahasyaṃ tu devatāgamamuttamaṃ ।
kathitaṃ tantrarājesmiṃstathā mādhavake kule ।।

nānyatra ca mayākhyātaṃ prayogamidamuttamaṃ ।
yosya vettā bhavetkaścitsa siddhiṃ phalabhāgbhavet ।।

ataḥ parataraṃ cakraṃ śṛṇu samyagvadāmi te ।
turyannāma mahācakraṃ yogeśīnāṃ samālayaṃ ।।

tatra sā paramā śaktiḥ śivasya vaśavartinī ।
sahasrasūryasaṃkāśā viśveśajananī śivo ।।

tatra madhye kṣipe(cci)ttaṃ śabdarūpaṃ mahāparaṃ ।
etaddevi samākhyātaṃ atonyaṃ kathayāmi te ।।

kaṃdaṃ nāma mahācakraṃ ṣaḍāraṃ bhūtitejasaṃ ।
tatra sā paramā devī varṇamātā sarasvatī ।।

anāhatāni śabdāni karoti vikaroti ca ।
vācaḥ pravartanaṃ tasmāccaturdhā devi niścitaṃ ।।

tatrārūḍhāśca yoginyo yānti viśvaṃ carācaraṃ ।
rūpasya parivartantu kurvvantīha na saṃśayaḥ ।।

ataḥ paraṃ mahācakraṃ kuṃḍalī iti viśrutaṃ ।
prasuptorasaṃkāśaṃ yoginīnāṃ sadodayaṃ ।।

tatra sā vasate śakti ikārārddhārddharūpiṇī ।
asi(rohaṃ) sarūpā sā sparśamātraprabodhanī ।।

ataḥ paraṃ mahādevi śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane ।
ghaṭodaraṃ mahācakraṃ sarvvacakrottamottamaṃ ।।

tatra madhye sthitā devī bhāsvarī nāma nāmataḥ ।
yogināṃ paramaṃ sthānaṃ eṣa devaṃ sadoditaṃ ।।
(114b)
taṃ vai saṃjīvanī nāma bhūtānāṃ paramaṃ padaṃ ।
tatrāsau vasate madhye ūṣmatejomayaḥ śuciḥ ।।

tatra praviṣṭāḥ sākinyo bhavati bhayavarjitāḥ ।
tatorddhakaṃṭhacakraṃ tu yatra lolā vyavasthitā ।।

tatra devi śivā nāma rūpikā vararūpiṇī ।
tatorddhaparamaṃ cakraṃ laṃbākhyaṃ nāma viśrutaṃ ।।

tatra madhye vaseddevi vedhakī nāma sā smṛtā ।
tatrārūḍhaṃ japaṃ kurvvan ṣaḍrasaṃ ramānayet ।।

tatordhaṃ guru ktrākhyaṃ cakraṃ sarvvaprakāśakaṃ ।
tatra madhye mahātejo bhāskarākārarūpakaṃ ।।

tatra sā vasate biṃduḥ paramā(ṇu)svarūpakaḥ ।।
tadabhyāsānmahādevi sarvvavidbhavate guruḥ ।।

tatorddhaṃ hṛjavaktraṃ tu cakraṃ sarvvottamottamaṃ ।
tatra sā paramā śaktirvyāpakīrvaśavarttitā ।।

parāsaṃvitsvarūpā ca maṃtrasaṃvittikāriṇī ।
tatra madhye parāśaktirdīpakī nāma viśrutā ।।

tatorddhe viṣuvaṃ cakraṃ sarvvasatvasukhāvahaṃ ।
tatorddhe tu visargākhyaṃ cakraṃ paramakāraṇaṃ ।।

sarvveṣāmeva cakrāṇāṃ tatra madhye vyavasthitā ।
yathāmaṇigaṇānāṃ tu madhye taṃtupratiṣṭhitā ।। ।।

।। iti kālikule kālikramabhedaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

ādhārāṇāṃ tu deveśa svarūpaṃ jñānamadya vai ।
kathayasva paraṃ deva dhyānaṃ devyā yathāsthitaṃ ।।

sākārātha nirākārā yathā deva vyavasthitā ।
tathā me vada sadbhāvaṃ sādhakānāṃ yathā tu vai ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāva ।।

śṛṇu devi mahābhāge rahasyaṃ kathayāmi te ।
(115a)
na kasyacinmayākhyātaṃ tavādya kathayāmyahaṃ ।।

śuklā mahāparā devī kālasaṃkarṣaṇī parā ।
trinetrāmekavaktrāṃ ca śvetapadmopari sthitāṃ ।।

śvetapretasamārūḍhāṃ śvetāṃbaravibhūṣitāṃ ।
caturbhujāṃ suvapuṣāṃ kṛśāṅgīṃ sarpamaṃḍitāṃ ।।

vyālayajñopavītīṃ ca vyālāṃgadavibhūṣitāṃ ।
īdṛ(gvyā)nasamopetāṃ sarvvalakṣaṇasaṃyutāṃ ।।

niṣka * tu samāsena śṛṇu vakṣyāmi tatvataḥ ।
vyomarūpāṃ mahākālīṃ kīṭanaṃ tu nibhāṃ parāṃ ।।

cidrūpāṃ citkalāṃ saumyāṃ ana(cka)laśobhitāṃ ।
bhāvarūpāṃ mahādevīṃ dhyāyeddevīṃ sadoditāṃ ।।

paścāddevīṃ mahābhāgāṃ yathāvarṇā sutejasāṃ ।
etatte sarvvamākhyātaṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ paripṛcchasi ।।

etacchrutvā tadā vākyaṃ devadevamukhodgataṃ ।
punaḥ provāca deveśī praśnaṃ paramaśobhanaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

saṃśayaṃ paramaṃ deva vaktumarhasyaśeṣataḥ ।
maṃtroddhāraṃ mahādeva kathayasva prasādataḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

bhūmibhāge same śuddhekṣatajāsavalepitaṃ ।
prasūrenmātṛkāṃ tatra vargāṣṭakasamanvitāṃ ।।

tatastu proddharenmantraṃ ekavīraṃ mahābalaṃ ।
ācāryaḥ prāṅmukhaḥ sthitvā madirāsavasaṃsthitaḥ ।।

śaktyutsaṃgagato dhīmān brahmaprāśanatatparaḥ ।
uddharenmaṃtramevaṃ tu sarvvamantrottamottamaṃ ।।

ṣaḍviṃśamuddhareccādau pātujasyoparisthitaṃ ।
(115b)
adhastādvahnisaṃyuktaṃ ṣaṣṭhasyopari saṃsthitaṃ ।।

arddhendubiṃdusaṃyuktaṃ etadvarṇaṃ suśobhanaṃ ।
na cāsya dīpako deyaḥ svabhāvenaiva dīpitaḥ ।।

eṣa varṇo mahāvīraḥ saptapātālabhedakaḥ ।
anena smṛtamātreṇa brahmādyā devatāstathā ।।

tuṣyanti sarvvaśaktīnāṃ bhūcarīkhecarīstathā ।
etaddevi mayākhyātaṃ (mantroddhā)raṃ sulakṣaṇaṃ ।।

ataḥ paraṃ śṛṇuṣveva yāgaṃ devyā yathāsthitaṃ ।
parvvatāgre nadītīre gavāṃgoṣṭhe ca * tvare ।।

śmaśāne ekaliṃge vā mātṛṇāṃ ca gṛhethavā ।
bhūmigṛhe (suṣupte) vā yatra vā rocate manaḥ ।।

lepayedbhūmibhāgaṃ tu kṣatajairvā śivāmbunā ।
harṣadevena vā devi kuṃḍagodbha * vena ca ।।

śaktistatraiva saṃpūjyā tribhiḥṣaṇṇavameva vā ।
manastuṣṭikaraṃ pūrṇṇaṃ mantraṃ maṃtreṣu pūjayet ।।

biṃdumārgeṇa subhage yāvatsūryaṃ mahābalaṃ ।
somamaṇḍalasaṃprāptaṃ vahnimaṇḍalayāvataḥ ।।

trisaṃghaṭṭavirāmena pūjayedyāgamuttamaṃ ।
siṃdūrāṃjanayuktāstā śaktistatraiva pūjayet ।।

gāthātu gāyate tatra ācārye prayatātmanāṃ ।
phuṣphusaṃ tu daheddhūpaṃ devyā yātu * yaṃ sadā ।।

etaddevi mayākhyātaṃ yāgaṃ devi yathāsthitaṃ ।
ataḥ paraṃ mahābhāge śṛṇu vakṣyāmi cāparaṃ ।।

mudrābaṃdhasamāsena śṛṇu vakṣyāmi cāpare ।
ubhau tau sanniruddhau tu prāṇāpānavyavasthitau ।।

tatra madhye paraṃ tatvaṃ bhāvābhāvaparodbhavaṃ ।
(116a)
tatra madhye cidaṃ kuryānmudraiṣā khecarī parā ।।

na cāsyāḥ sadṛśī mudrā triṣu lokeṣu vidyate ।
etaddevi samākhyātaṃ mudrābaṃdhaṃ varānane ।।

ataḥ paraṃ mahābhāge adbhutaṃ kathayāmi te ।
cakrā ye pūrvamākhyātāsteṣāṃ madhye cidaṃ kuru ।।

dvau dvau cakrau tu deveśi yojayedbhāvarūpataḥ ।
dvayānāṃ vasate devi vargā cāṣṭau sumadhyame ।।

grahāṣṭau ca tathā devi varttate devi nityaśaḥ ।
cakre cakre muhurttantu vasate maṃtrarāṭsadā ।।

ādyamante vasadbhūyaḥ sarvve maṃtrā varānane ।
arddharātrāt prabhṛtibhiryāvacca praharatrayaṃ ।।

punaḥ saṃhārakenaiva yāvadanye trayaṃ paraṃ ।
punardvayaṃ mahābhāge ye sthitā dvau sumadhyame ।।

pādādyā dyāvakaṃ dāntaṃ ātmatatvaṃ vyavasthitaṃ ।
kaṃdādyā dyāvakaṃṭhāntaṃ vidyātatvaṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।।

kaṃṭhādyā dyāvaraṃ prāntaṃ śivatatvaṃ prakīrtitaṃ ।
arddharātre gate kāle prathame varavarṇṇini ।।

kaulikendro samādhiṃ ca abhyasetu sadā priye ।
tatkāle yāvadaśabhirmuhūrttāni varānane ।।

ātmanastasya udayaṃ mantrāṇāṃ vīravaṃdite ।
asyānte punareveha yāvaddaśamuhūrttakaḥ ।।

vidyātatvasya bhavate udayaṃ tena saṃśayaḥ ।
ato tṛtīyacakrasya bhavate udayaṃ paraṃ ।।

etaddevi mayākhyātaṃ rahasyaṃ kaulikaṃ priye ।
na cāsya sadṛśo nāsti jñānaṃ devi na kutracit ।।

iti sarvvaṃ mayā tedya tava sarvvaṃ prakāśitaṃ ।
(116b)
yanna kasyacidātavyaṃ mahādoṣamavāpnuyāt ।। ।।

।। kalikramabhedapaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

devadeva mahādeva śaraṇāgatavatsala ।
saṃśayaṃ paramaṃ deva vadasva tripurāntaka ।।

devatānāṃ mahādeva chummakā vada bhairava ।। ।।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi rahasyaṃ kathayāmi te ।
aṃguṣṭhaṃ darśayedyā tu tasya vaktraṃ pradarśayet ।।

kaniṣṭhā darśayedyā tu śikhā tasya pradarśayet ।
madhyamāyāḥ pradarśeta tasya svāsyaṃ pradarśayet ।।

vāmānāṃ caiva sarvvāsāṃ rahasyaṃ kathayāmi te ।
sarvvāsāṃ caiva śaktīnāṃ darśaye * dayaṃ padaṃ ।।

prāṇāpānāntayormadhye vasate gṛhya yattataḥ ।
devīnāṃ bhāvamevaṃ tu rahasyāṃ gopitaṃ mayā ।।

iti devi mayākhyātaṃ rahasyaṃ devatāgamaṃ ।
bhaktānāmapi dātavyaṃ rahasyaṃ sa ca devatā ।।

iti tedya mayākhyātaṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ paripṛcchasi ।
iti śrutvā tadā vākyaṃ devadevī bravīdvacaḥ ।।

punaḥ provāca deveśi (idaṃ vākya manutta)maṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

vadasva devadeveśa agnikāryaṃ mama prabho ।
yena vai jñātamātreṇa mahāpāpaṃ vyapohati ।।
saṃkṣepādbrūhi bhagavan vahnilakṣaṇamuttamaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu devi yathātathyaṃ kathayāmi tavākhilaṃ ।
yanna kasyacidākhyātaṃ mayā vai vīravaṃdite ।।

tattehaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi atisnehādvarānane ।
tatvakādītsamādāya prāṇāpāṇau prapīḍayet ।।
(117a)

kaṃdacakraṃtu dhamaye yāvatsaṃjvalate tu taṃ ।
prāṇāpānapravāhaṃ tu bodhayetparameśvarī ।।

tanmadhyā paramā śaktiḥ kṣobhayedvahnirūpiṇīṃ ।
dahate (tatva)saṃghātaṃ śabdādiguṇasaṃcayaṃ ।।

juhvate tatra deveśi śivacaṃdrājyasaṃyutaṃ ।
nādaṃ śruvaṃ gṛhītvā tu caitanyaṃ tava hūyate ।।

avyuṣṭamātrā tatraiva bhāvaye tu vyavasthitaṃ ।
yojayetparame dhāmni ravicaṃdrāgnimadhyage ।।

evamagnividhānaṃ tu paramadya prakāśitaṃ ।
etadvai yo na jānāti na sa ācārya ucyate ।।

etatkulakramaṃ sarvvaṃ mahāmādhava(ntu) kule ।
vadiṣyāmi punarbhadre tava maṃdaramūrddhani ।।

vistareṇātirabhasā kimanyatkathayāmi te ।
svaśiṣyāya pradātavyaṃ rahasyamidamuttamaṃ ।।

parīkṣya svaciraṃ kālaṃ anyathā pātakī bhavet ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe jayadrathayāmale caturthaṣaṭke rāviṇīcakre
kālikākulavidhiḥ ।। ।।

evamākarṇya sā devī praharṣānaṃdanirbharā ।
punaḥ provāca deveśaṃ praṇipatya jagadguruṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

kathayasva mahādeva sūcitaṃ yattvayādhunā ।
tatvaṃta mādhavakulaṃ vaktavyaṃ tatra bhairavi ।।

saṃkṣepeṇa samākhyāhi taṃtresmin sahasyakaṃ ।
yena saṃpūrṇatā cāsya taṃtresya bhagavati prabho ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi taṃtrarājasya madhyataḥ ।
yathā tanmādhavakulaṃ vaktavyaṃ maṃdaropari ।।
(117b)
yathā ca tatra sā kālā tathā sarvvaṃ bravīmi te ।
yosau śāntaḥ parākāśaḥ prapūrṇasarvvatomukhaḥ ।।

nityaḥ sadasanirmuktaḥ kāraṇātītavigrahaḥ ।
ajo hyanantaḥ sarvvajño vibhurdhātā śivovyayaḥ ।।

akulaḥ sarvvatodiktaḥ paritaḥ saṃvyavasthitaḥ ।
tasyodaramidaṃ vyaktaṃ avibhedi prakāśabhāk ।।

nityānaṃdarasoddāmasvābhāsā bharibhāsura ।
samādhaveti vijñeyaḥ kuleśaḥ kulabhāskaraḥ ।।

ajovyayaḥ praśāntātmā sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakaḥ ।
mahāghoravaraścaṇḍaḥ kulabhairava ityasau ।।

aṣṭātriṃśatkalāyukto raśmiyuktaḥ prakīrttitaḥ ।
ekadhā ca dvidhā tasya tridhā vai turyadhā tathā ।।

paṃcadhā caiva ṣoḍhā ca saptadhā kulanāyakaḥ ।
aṣṭadhā navadhā jñeyā daśaikādaśavigrahaḥ ।।

dvādaśo vai ṣoḍaśātmā biṃdurnārāyaṇo hyasau ।
nṛsiṃhavapuṣaṃ ghoraṃ trailokyagrāsaghasmaraṃ ।।

sarvvacakraprabhurnāthaṃ mahārāvavirāviṇaṃ ।
cakreśaṃ cakranirataṃ pracaṃḍakiraṇākulaṃ ।।

pratināḍisukhāsaktaṃ pratigraṃthivibhedakaṃ ।
ūhakādiśivāntaṃ ye mahādhārā prakīrttitaṃ ।।

teṣu tacchaktinirataṃ vibhāti kulamādhavaṃ ।
tadraśmicakracalitaṃ rahasyānaṃdanaṃditaṃ ।।

paṃcāmṛtarasāsaktaṃ sarvvayoginivaṃditaṃ ।
vātacakrakramāsaktaṃ viśvagrāsaikalaṃpaṭaṃ ।।

ghorāṭṭahāsanirataṃ ṣoḍhādhe ṣaṭprakārakaṃ ।
pureśamamarāvāsaṃ trisaṃghaṭṭaghaṭāntaraṃ ।।
(118a)
asya mantramanantasyātmahādhāmapuṭīkṛtaṃ ।
catuḥśaktimahābiṃdu yuktaṃ sarvvārthasiddhidaṃ ।।

praticakrakrame pūjyaṃ tadādhāraṃ tadīśvaraṃ ।
tatrāntaragataṃ bhāti tadrasmivalitā sadā ।।

navayāgaratā devyaḥ pūjayeta mṛheśvaraṃ ।
tadvatpūjā prakarttavyā navadhā na ca cakragā ।।

navānavoditānantā naṃdinī navagahvarā ।
yāva(cchā)trakaṃ cakraṃ tāvaddeva prakalpayet ।।

etadādhāragā ghorā kālī kālanisūdanī ।
saptādaśapadāṃtasthā sā jñeyā mādhaveśvarī ।।

mahāmādhavikāsaiva jñeyākulakaraṃkiṇī ।
yasmāttasmāttantravare prāptājñāmāvasaṃjñake ।।

tatsthā taccakragā kālī kulakālīti kīrttitā ।
tasmintatre vare devi sugūḍhāgūḍhavikramā ।।

uktā ca vakṣyamāṇā ca suguptā gūḍhagocarā ।
mahākālī mādhaveśi sugūḍhā gahanoditā ।।

mādhaveśakulākrānti vidadhāti punaḥ punaḥ ।
tena sā kālikā śaktiḥ kālayantī kulākulaṃ ।।

troṭayantī padārthāni sphoṭayantī guṇālayaṃ ।
vidrāvayantī nikhilaṃ bhedagrāmamaśeṣataḥ ।।

vāce caragatā kālī tena mādhavacakragā ।
vyākhyātā tava deveśi kimanyatparipṛcchasi ।।

evamukte sureśāne śaṃkare lokaśaṃkare ।
punaḥ provāca deveśīṃ praṇipatya surādhipaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrutāni bahutantrāṇi mahāguptatarāṇi ca ।
nistriṃśāni matānyeva vikalpakṣīṇavṛttayaḥ ।।
(118b)
asmintantravare cāpi vidhānāścāpyanekaśaḥ ।
sakriyā nikriyā cānyā mokṣadharmārthasiddhidā ।।

anyā ca vīramatulā yogamelāpake ratā ।
anye vai śaṃvarājālā rūpabhedānyanekaśaḥ ।।

haṭhamelāpakāścānye haṭhavedhayasmṛtā ।
anyā ca sṛṣṭiyogena vedhayantī balātpaśūna ।।

mantravīryeṇa caivānyā yoge śākte ca śāmbhave ।
melāpakaprayogena chadmācāreṇa cānya vai ।।

melāpakāśca divyāśca vīrācārāśca me śrutā ।
kulācāravidhānaṃ tu kaulācāramanekadhā ।।

viśvāsaṃ yoginīnāṃ tu vidhikalpena me śrutaṃ ।
nirantaraṃ tu yāneha prakaṭīkṛtya kaulikaṃ ।।

yena chadmamaviśvāsaṃ yena garbhīkṛtaṃ na hi ।
prakaṭīkṛtya yenedaṃ nirācāraṃ nirantaraṃ ।।

(viśvā)saṃ yena gacchanti tasupāpaṃ vadasva me ।
mukhaprayogayogena vaktrādvaktrakrameṇa tu ।।

guptācāraprabhāvena atiguptatareṇa ca ।
ācārātkathayiṣyanti baṃdhanopāyavarjitaṃ ।।

siddhikaulikavijñānaṃ cārācāreṣu saṃcaret ।
cārabhedāṅgamaṃ guhyaṃ aguhyaṃ yoginīkulaṃ ।।

cārasaṃcāramatulaṃ nānārūpaśatākṛtiḥ ।
pīṭhesu yogapīṭheṣu naṣṭachāyāsu vigrahaṃ ।।

smṛtamātreṇa yeneha bhavanti sarvvasiddhayaḥ ।
pūjanaṃ tu kathaṃ teṣāṃ viśvāsaṃ yena gacchati ।।

tadupāyaṃ vada vibho yena tṛptirbhavenmayi ।
madhye yā kālarāvīṇi akālamṛtyupātanī ।।
(119a)
madhye tu sarvvapīṭheṣu catustriṃśādhikeṣu ca ।
yajanaṃ vai kathaṃ teṣāṃ kathaṃ vai siddhidā na hi ।।

pūrvvataṃtre tvayā proktaṃ niśisaṃcārasaṃcare ।
gopitā tu tvayā nātha madhyahīnā na siddhidā ।।

caturviṅśati pāṭhā ye madhye yā pīṭhanāyikā ।
tayā hīnena siddhiḥ syādyajanaṃ niḥphalaṃ bhavet ।।

lalāvilokitaṃ cakraṃ jīvantasphuritaṃ bhavet ।
tayā siddhirmayā jāyettayā yā pūrvabhāṣitā ।।

mahāsamayalopārthe pramādādeva dhārite ।
mahāpātakinaḥ kecinmantratantreṣu dūṣikā ।।

dravyādvaitasamāyuktā samayāmalaśuddhayā ।
āśurvijñāna yeṣāṃ vai paryāyānteṣu kīrtyate ।।

samayācārasaṃkrānte prāyaścittaviśodhane ।
kathaṃ śuddhyanti vīre(ndra) narake gantumicchatā ।।

pramādā dūṣakā ye ca samayācāralaṃghakāḥ ।
praviṣṭā pīṭhamārgeṣu pīṭhānteṣu pravarttaye ।।

niśāmelāpake saktā devatā lopanisphalā ।
sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya siddhiḥ syātsarvvasiddhiṣu ।।

prāyaścittaviśuddhyarthaṃ sarvvasiddhyāruho bhavet ।
sakṛtsaṃpūjitenāśu khegatiḥ prāpnuyānnaraḥ ।।

taṃ vadasva sukhopāyaṃ mantravīryaṃ mahābalaṃ ।
vidhānaṃ paramārthaṃ ca ṣaṭkaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ niyojitaṃ ।।

sā mohaya svabhāvajña avighnena prasādataḥ ।
kuru kāryaṃ mahārthaṃ ca bhavabhaṅganisūdani ।। ।।
(119b)
śrībhairava uvāca ।।

atiguhyātiguhyaṃ ca atiguptataraṃ priye ।
mahāpraśnārthamatulaṃ yatsurairapi durlabhaṃ ।।

anākhyeyamidaṃ vākyaṃ brahmavi(ṣṇve)śvarādiṣu ।
tvadṛtena grahaṃ kasya buddhikauśalyayogyatā ।।

śāktaśāmbhavaniḥśeṣa aṇavaṃ kulameva ca ।
viśeṣānugrahaṃ devi pravadāmi tavānaghe ।।

sṛṣṭikramaṃ tu prathamaṃ avatāraṃ dvitīyakaṃ ।
saṃhāraṃ tu tṛtīyaṃ syādanākhyeyaṃ caturthakaṃ ।।

vaktrādvaktre tu yoginyaḥ kadācitpuruṣasya tu ।
āmnāyaṃ tasya caivāsti guptācāre tu saṃcaret ।।

yasya tuṣyāmyahaṃ bhadre tvaṃ ca devi viśeṣataḥ ।
sa kālikulasaṃbhūto bhāvanā bhāvaye sphuṭaḥ ।।

tasyaddhirmahājāye nānyamithyāpavādināṃ ।
yāvantavāṃśake maṃtrī kālī kulapade sthitaḥ ।।

bhavetmuktiṃ kutastasya dvitīyo nāthabhairavī ।
kramaṃ prastāra rahitaṃ vaktrādvaktre pratiṣṭhitaṃ ।।

evaṃ vai kramapūrvantu dvitīyaṃ siddhipūrvvakaṃ ।
saṃhāraṃ tu tṛtīyaṃ syādvaktrādvaktraṃ caturthakaṃ ।।

paṃcamaṃ tu idaṃ bhadre ṣaṣṭhaṃ naiveha vidyate ।
eṣāṃ vai bhāvanā hīnā yadā prastāravarjitā ।।

śāstrakramaṃ gurorvākyaṃ ātmābhāvena bhāvitaṃ ।
na ca siddhyati maṃtrajño mukhāmnāyaṃ na vindati ।।

martyā vai maṃdabuddhiśca nirvikalpena te sudhīḥ ।
tadā śāstrakramaṃ devi kathayāmi samāsataḥ ।।

tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi alakṣmīnāśanaṃ paraṃ ।।
prāyaścittavināśārthaṃ mahāsamayaśodhanaṃ ।।
(120a)
ṣaṭkaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ prakartavyaṃ caturdikṣu gaṇāmbike ।
madyahīnā na siddhyanti yaṣṭāyāgaśatairapi ।।

bhraṣṭāmnāyāśca te sarvvamantratantravibhramvakāḥ ।
āgamopāya vai hīnā guptācāraṃ na vindati ।।

gurvāmnāyena rahitā bhrāntirvai jāyate sadā ।
prāpnuvanti na te siddhiṃ bhramantajñānavānmahīṃ ।।

tadā te paribhūyante vighnairnānāvidhaiḥ priye ।
vijñānabhāvanā hanti hantirmantrāśca siddhaye ।।

ubhayabhraṣṭakāste vai aihikyāmuṣmikī priye ।
tasmāddevakramaṃ jñātvā śāstradṛṣṭena cetasā ।।

śāstrapramāṇataḥ sarvvaṃ tatpramāṇaphalaṃ labhet ।
tatphalātsiddhibhāgmokṣo mokṣātsaṃsārakaṃ na hi ।।

etasmātkāraṇādbhadre pramāṇaṃ pratyayatrayaṃ ।
kathitaṃ tava suśroṇi mayā sādhya tamopahaṃ ।।

duratyā māyayā ghorā durvijñeyā surāsuraiḥ ।।
pataṃtaḥ jñānataḥ sarvve na coddhāraḥ kadācana ।।

yāvanna śaktipātoyaṃ svayaṃ vai kālakṛṃtanī ।
tat prasādādvimucyanti yadā jñāyanti tat priye ।।

evaṃ krameṣu cāmnāyaṃ saṃkarṇyākhyā tvadhiṣṭhitaṃ ।
tairvinā na bhavetsiddhiḥ tatpadaṃ kathayāmi te ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale kālikulakramanirṇayo nāma ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ādau tāvat pravakṣyāmi duṣṭāyātmaiśca dūṣaṇaṃ ।
parīkṣayecca yatnādvai rahasyaṃ bhāvamānayet ।।
(120b)
atyantasaralābhāvā svamāyeyārthasiddhidā ।
kulācāraprapaṃnā ye sarvvate hyātmanaṃ prati ।।

bhāvatyakhilaṃ sarvvaṃ bhāvanā bhavati sphuṭaṃ ।
yadā na jñāyate tvacchadmādgṛhnanti kaulikaṃ ।।

mahāpātakinaścānye narakaṃ gantumicchatā ।
haṭhāt praveśaṃ kurvanti mahānte kulaśāsane ।।

vākyāmṛtāni yuṃjanti viṣamā yāni gopayet ।
yāvadākarṇayetsarvvaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ grāhayet priye ।।

paścāddoṣaṃ gamiṣyanti nirmūlaṃ yena gacchati ।
chadmāt praviśyate devi kauṭilyā mahatāścaye ।।

śaktipātavihīnāste patanti narake balāt ।
mahārthaṃ bhairavoktiḥ syād jñānavijñānabhāṇḍajāḥ ।।

svayaṃ gṛhītamācāraṃ dīkṣānugrahakṛtsadā ।
vikrīṇatyātmano bhogāṃrtha paśūnā vṛśātmanāṃ ।।

na vindanti durācārā hyātmacaurā narādhamāḥ ।
anyāyena gṛhītārthaṃ mahāntaṃ pārameśvaraṃ ।।

tādṛśena prayuṃjanti maryādābhedakādhamāḥ ।
te patanti mahāghore narake tāmasānvite ।।

saputrabhṛtyamitraiśca yāvatsaptakulaiḥ saha ।
na śaktuvunti vai tasya prāyaścittaviśuddhaye ।।

krame krame prapūryante samastanarakādiṣu ।
na coddhāraṃ kadācitsyātsvayaṃ gurupade tu yat ।।

saṃparkastaiśca vai sārddhaṃ yaḥ karoti pramādataḥ ।
sa vai tanmayatāṃ yāti patate tu mahārṇave ।।

āloka sthāne micchanti tamoṃdhākārasevitaṃ ।।
(121a)
bhavanti matsyakūrmāsu ātmanātmā na bhakṣayet ।।

evaṃ gatiṃ prāpnuvanti ye mūḍhāchadmacāriṇaḥ ।
tasmātsarvvaprayatnena vicāryeṇa suniścitaṃ ।।

kulamārgaprasaktā ye kālācārasya pāragāḥ ।
vīrācārāśca ye dhanyāstantrācāre ca sādhakāḥ ।।

kramapūjāratāsaktā jñānavijñānadhīmataḥ ।
bhaktiyuktā dṛḍhācārāḥ śūrāḥ kārpaṇyavarjitāḥ ।।

vīrabhojyā sadāsaktāḥ khānapānādilaṃpaṭāḥ ।
viṣayāsaktacittā ye gītavādyaratāśca ye ।।

ramanti vividhairbhāvairhāsalāsakathaiśca ye ।
na duḥkhaṃ baṃdhumaraṇe na tīrthe tilapātane ।।

saucācāraiśca rahitāḥ kaulācāraparāṅmukhāḥ ।
vedoktaṃ vaiṣṇavoktaṃ vā udakaṃ varjayetsadā ।।

piṃḍodakāśrupātaṃ ca paśubhāvaṃ vivarjayet ।
nityoditaṃ mahāvīryaṃ ātmabhāvena bhāvitaṃ ।।

sadoditamahātatvaṃ svasaṃvetti pade sthitaṃ ।
sasaṃkācārasarvve te bhakṣayenmṛtyuvadyathā ।।

vīrabhojyā prayatnena sarvvathā kāraṇaṃ prati ।
dayādākṣiṇyasaṃyuktaṃ vīrakarmaṇyavarjitaṃ ।।

yoginī yoganirato viraktaḥ paśuśāsane ।
dhanyoditā mahāvīrā yogimelāpake ratā ।।

evaṃ tu sādhakaṃ jñātvā prakāśyevaṃ kramaṃ tvidaṃ ।
ācāryo vīrasaṃyukto alobhī daṃbhavarjitaḥ ।।

laghupānalaghurbhojyaṃ jñānavijñānapāragaṃ ।
(121b)
asatpralāpamātsaryaṃ bhayadainyaparāṅmukhaṃ ।।

yoginīcārasaṃcāramarmalakṣaṇalakṣitaṃ ।
mṛtyustvakālamṛtyurvvā sarvvacihnāni lakṣayet ।।

kramāt kramāgamarataṃ caturtha kramaśobhitaṃ ।
mukhāgamarataṃ yogaṃ yoginī yogarakṣitaṃ ।।

dṛṣṭayogaprayogaṃ ca śarīraṃ lakṣayet kramāt ।
kaṃdakuṇḍalinī madhye * vyutthā jñāyate priye ।।

nābhi hṛtpadmamadhye tu amṛtākṛṣṇakarṣayet ।
hṛtpadmakaṃṭhakūpe tu gamāgamananetra ca ।।

ghaṃṭikābiṃdubhrūmadhye bhūtyāgātkhe gatirbhavet ।
brahmaraṃdhre sadānache yāvadbhṛśadaṃgulaṃ ।।

tāvatpathasamāyāte kurute gatirāgatiṃ ।
naṣṭachāyā prayogena brahmadṛṣṭvāvalokayet ।।

praviśya sarvvadeṣu jalārkkamiva suṃdari ।
evamabhyasanaṃ yasmātsaṃyogyo yogyatāṃ labhet ।।

sarvvalakṣaṇahīnopi sarvvaśāstravivarjitaḥ ।
sa bhavedbhairavaḥ sākṣātsaṃkarṇyākhyāsya vigrahaḥ ।।

saṃskarāḥ saṃnidhānāni sodhikārī kulāgame ।
evaṃ salakṣaṇaṃ samyaguptadhīnna prakāśayet ।।

kulācāryotha yogyo vā kṛśodaryāstadaṃśajaḥ ।
anyathā na prakāśyantadyadijanmaśatairapi ।।

evaṃ yogyānurūpeṇa kathitaṃ mama bhairavi ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrutaṃ me tvatprasādena lakṣaṇaṃ vīrasādhakaiḥ ।
yasyevaṃ kathanaṃ kāryaṃ tatkāryaṃ vada bhairava ।।

saptādaśākṣarā yā sā pīṭhavāsini vallabhā ।
(122a)
catuḥṣaṣṭhyaṣṭapīṭhāni madhye yā pīṭhanāyikā ।।

tasyā mantrapadaṃ brūhi caturṇāṃ kramanāyikāṃ ।
yasya nāsti samaṃ mantra mantrakoṭyayutāyutaiḥ ।।
taṃ vadasva mahādeva śrutaṃ me na kadācana ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

caturṇāṃ kramapūjānāṃ sarahasyamidaṃ priye ।
caturṇāṃ kālikākhyaṃ tu idaṃ mantreṇa nāmitaṃ ।।

tadviśeṣaṃ na cāstīti tava snehāt prakāśitaṃ ।
eṣā sā paramā devī bhedānaṃtavisarpiṇī ।।

ato bhedātsamuddhṛtya kṛtvā rūpaṃ trayodaśaṃ ।
pṛthatkṛtena vai nāmnā āvallyā vallayaḥ kramāt ।।

sakṛtpūjitamātreṇa tanmayatvaṃ prapadyate ।
sā śaktiḥ kathayiṣyāmi madhye yā bhogabhāginī ।।

bhūmibhāge same śuddhe susubhe guptadhīmatā ।
alinī mādhivīlipte kukumācchurite śubhe ।।

mṛgacaṃdanagaṃdhāḍhye karpūrāmodamodite ।
suramye puṣparacite sthūlapuṣpe(śva)saṃkule ।।

raktapuṣperavacchādye athavā ca svayaṃbhunā ।
bhūmerbahuvidhaiścitraisturuṣkā gurusihlakaiḥ ।।

athavā gugguluśreṣṭhairghṛtamadhyasamanvitaiḥ ।
taiśca dhūpaiḥ sudhūpitvā tato mantraṃ samuddharet ।।

mātṛkāṃ pūjayettatra mantramātāṃ gaṇāṃbike ।
vargāṣṭakena saṃyuktāṃ dīpamālāsudīpitāṃ ।।

taccakrapūjanaṃ kṛtvā tato vidyāṃ samuddharet ।
(122b)
niśisthitotha saṃrūḍhaḥ śaktistho vā dṛḍhavrataḥ ।।

bhakṣyabhojyā visargāṇi lehyapehyāni dāpayet ।
matsyāni dagdhamāṃsāni āmapakvaṃ nivedayet ।।

sthūlodbha(vā)ni sarvvāṇi antarikṣodbhavāni ca ।
susaṃskṛtāni pakvāni peyamadhyāni dāpayet ।।

kālalohitavarṇābhaṃ madhyaṃ paramaśobhanaṃ ।
parānaṃdarasāsvādaṃ gaṃḍo(dva)litabhairavaṃ ।।

padmarāganibhaṃ madyaṃ ānandamamṛtaṃ paraṃ ।
sugaṃdhaṃ bhāvayedyatra prayatnāttacca dāpayet ।।

tāni sarvvāṇi śubhage surāsīdhuyutāni ca ।
yogyairvvīraiśca yuktāni mantrayuktāni cetasā ।।

mantrahīnaiśca varjyāni siddhihāni bhavedyataḥ ।
bhūcaryācchidramicchanti dikcaryādyāstathaiva ca ।।

tasmāttāṃ varjayedyatnātpūrvalakṣaṇatāṃ smaret ।
parasparaṃ tu cakroktiṃ vīragoṣṭhīparasparaṃ ।।

yāstāḥ pīṭheṣu caktrasyo tathā vai sādhakeśvarāḥ ।
parasparaṃ tu yajanaṃ alipalaṃ suśobhanaṃ ।। ।।

iti jayadrathayāmale kālikule ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ādau tāvatsmareddūtiṃ savāhyābhyantaraṃ priye ।
vāsāṇi suvicitrāṇi mātṛbhaktiyutāni ca ।।

sarvvato dūti kartavyā dūtihīno na siddhibhāk ।
evaṃ cakroktavidhinā tato māhātmya me śṛṇu ।।

uddhṛtya kulakoṭī ca ekaviṃśaśatāṣṭakaṃ ।
rudraloke tu gacchanti yāvaccaṃdrārkatārakāḥ ।।
(123a)
seya cakroktavidhinā yadi syādbhāvitātmanāṃ ।
akulīnaiśca saṃparkkātttatkulātpatanaṃ bhavet ।।

ekapātre kulāmnāye kāryaṃ nānyatkadācana ।
yadicchedātmanaḥ śreyaḥ siddhirvvā vīravaṃdite ।।

jīvitaṃ suciraṃ vātha yoginyo melakaśca vā ।
atītānāgataṃ yogaṃ bhāvanā bhāvanāśanī ।।

taiḥ sārddhaṃ bhojanaṃ varjyaṃ pānagoṣṭhī ca yatnataḥ ।
teṣu saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ varjyaṃ saṃsparśaṃ kiṃ punaḥ priye ।।

adīkṣitajanasparśātsnānamāgneyamācaret ।
punarna darśanaiḥ sārddhaṃ samānairbhojanairyutaṃ ।।

yadi mohātkadācidvai lālāsatkārajāyate ।
prāyaścittamavāpnoti punaścādhvarapūjanaṃ ।।

dīkṣādikalanairyuktā mantrajāpī dṛḍhavrataḥ ।
kulopade(śī)mānī ca sarvvasaṃskārasaṃskṛtaḥ ।।

na siddhiḥ paśusaṃparkkāddarśanasyāpavādanāt ।
maṃdabuddhitvamāpnoti maṃtrasyādathavā navā ।।

etasmātkāraṇādbhadre varjanīyaṃ paśukramaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

paśubhāvaṃ tvayā proktaṃ prayatnādvarjayedyadi ।
patibhāvasvabhāvaṃ vai pūrvamevoditaṃ tvayā ।।
viśeṣaḥ kīdṛśaḥ prokto naiva jānāti mohitā ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yadi nānaiva siddhyati ।
gopanīyaṃ prayatnena sā vīrā vāsanā priye ।।

yayā vijñātamātrāyā lakṣaṇāt khecaro bhavet ।
(go)lakaṃ jāyate śīghraṃ paśutvaṃ tasya naśyati ।।
(123b)
aṭṭahāsaṃ śikhāsthāne caritraṃ ca karaṃdhrake ।
kullagirye priye karṇaṃ jayaṃtya cottarāyaṇe ।।

ujjayanyāṃ tu bhrūmadhye prayāgaṃ vaktramadhyagaṃ ।
vārāṇasīṃ tu hṛdaye śrīpīṭhaṃ skaṃdhayordvayoḥ ।।

kaṃṭhadeśe tu virajaṃ heruṃḍya udare priye ।
alaṃpuraṃ nābhimadhye saṃdorddhalāpuraṃ priye ।।

kaṃdādhāre tu gokarṇṇaṃ maruddeśaṃ bhagāntare ।
atha meḍhro paribhadre jñātavyaṃ sādhakena tu ।।

dakṣiṇe saktinagare vāmeśyāḥ pauḍravarddhane ।
vāmaskaṃdhe purastīraṃ pṛṣṭhāpuraṃ tu dakṣiṇe ।।

udyākeśī jānumadhye sopāraṃ cāntare smṛtaṃ ।
kṣīrikāṃ vāmahaste tu māyāpuryāntu dakṣiṇe ।।

āmrātakeśvaraṃ gulphe vāme rājagṛhaṃ śubhaṃ ।
pādādhāre tu brahmāṇī kālāgnya ca vidhārakī ।।

evaṃ nityoditā bhadre vāsanā hṛdi saṃsmaret ।
yadichedātmanaḥ śreyo nāryo pūjyāḥ svasiddhaye ।।

evaṃ ca codayaṃ jñātvā madhye (hyā)lakṛṃtanī ।
ūrṇṇātaṃtu nibhākārā śakti yā pārameśvarī ।।

tasyāṃtāṃte tu yā āste sā tu saṃkarṣaṇī smṛtā ।
ṛtuṣaṭkāntaraṃ yasya antarlīnaṃ pravartate ।।

taṃ kālaṃ grasate sā tu layodayavivarjitaḥ ।
prāṇāpānaparispaṃdaṃ yannodayalayaṃ priye ।।

viśrāmaṃ yatra kurute sahasrāṇyekaviṃśatiḥ ।
taṃ kālaṃ grasate sā tu trimārgapathayāyinaṃ ।।
(124a)
tatrāntaragatā sā tu bhāsārūpā manomayā ।
taddharmadharmiṇī śuddhā khamūrttiraparā kalā ।।

tvayā hataṃ priye tasya kharūpā khasarīriṇī ।
ūrmiṇyāṃtaravāsinyā prāṇāpānapravāhakī ।।

kālavahniṃ samutthāpya cakrādhāraprabhedinī ।
prabuddhā kaṃdaveśme tu bhujagākṛti sā sthitā ।।

somasūryasthitā vahni phaṇiratnaprakīrttitā ।
sthitā sā taṃturūpeṇa ūrddhamārgaprasarpiṇī ।।

vahantī śṛṃkhalāmadhye ekā trayabhedinī ।
mṛṇālataṃtusaṃkāśā hṛttāluparivāhinī ।।

dviṣaṭkāṃtāmanogṛhya amṛtaṃ payasannibhaṃ ।
viśantī svapuraṃ devi jīvasyāpyāyakāriṇī ।।

tāṃ namaskṛtya sarvveṣāṃ samastā cakranāyakī ।
tasya devātidevasya tato maṃtrāṇi me śṛṇu ।।

pūrvvoktabhūmibhāgaṃ tu pūrvvoktāḥ sādhakāḥ priye ।
pūrvvamācāryamevoktaṃ dūtiyuktaṃ jitendriyaṃ ।।

tataḥ samuddharenmaṃtrā nyadṛṣṭairmokṣamāpnuyāt ।
śṛṇuṣvekamanā bhadre uddhariṣyāmi tatvataḥ ।।

mohanī kālamālā ca vīranāthaṃ tu yojayet ।
maṃtrādyaṃ dāruṇākrāntaprāṇaṃ daṇḍasamanvitaṃ ।।

sarvvagaṃ navame yuktaṃ gaṇeśaṃ saptame yutaṃ ।
(ādyaṃ) turyaṃ rasārūḍhaṃ ṭāṭibhaṃ tritaye yutaṃ ।।

kūpavaktraṃ mahākālaṃ caturthasvarabhūṣitaṃ ।
tejopari samārūḍhaṃ gāṃḍīvaṃ tadanantaraṃ ।।

nīhārakālatejorha gāṇḍīvaṃ śarayā yutaṃ ।
vaiṣṇavaṃ kāmadevaṃ ca citrabhānvāsane sthitaṃ ।।
(124b)
madīyabhūṣaṇairyuktaṃ paṃcadhārāvamuddharet ।
saptādaśākṣarā vidyā ghoraghoratarā smṛtā ।।

asyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa brahmāṇḍādhipatirbhavet ।
sṛjate sarvvamakhilaṃ saṃharellīlayā naraḥ ।।

asyā devi viniṣkrāntā vidyānāṃ bhūrisaṃkhyayā ।
kramāṇi vividhānīha asaṃkhyāni kulāni ca ।।

pāraṃparyakramāvalyo gharayajñā hyanekaśaḥ ।
tannāsti yanna jānāti tannāsti yanna siddhyati ।।

tannāsti na tayā vyāptaṃ tannāsti yanna saṃharet ।
tannāsti yogamakhilaṃ yanna devyā vinirgataṃ ।।

kālasaṃkarṣaṇī nāma krīḍaiṣā parameśvarī ।
tā vidhānāni vastūni nānākāravikārajā ।।

nānāmaṇigaṇākīrṇā nānālokāntarāṇi ca ।
brahmāṇḍakoṭisaṃkhyāni sarvvābhyantaravāsinī ।।

tasyā devātideveśyā mahāmāyā vinirgatā ।
yathedaṃ vyāptamakhilaṃ lokotpattirmuhurmuhuḥ ।।

tena sā kathayiṣyanti padaṃ nyesti paraḥ śivaḥ ।
leśato deśamātreṇa kathitaṃ tava suvrate ।।

śṛṇu cānyadyathārthantu vadāmi tava śobhane ।
hanāhalaṃ lakṣmibījaṃ dhyāyate tejasā yutaṃ ।।

tejasā śirasākrāntaṃ kulapiṃḍadvayaṃ smṛtaṃ ।
sarvvacakreṣu maṃtroyaṃ sarvvasaubhāgyavarddhanaṃ ।।

kṣetrapālakulā vṛkṣāṃ āyudhā nāma saṃyutāṃ ।
mantrapīṭhanamo pāda īṣapūjāgnitatkramāt ।।

pūrvadakṣiṇavāruṇyā saumyāścaiva diśaḥ priye ।
(125a)
ṣaṭka ṣaṭka prakarttavyaḥ tatra pūjā krameṇa tu ।।

śmaśānakalpavṛkṣā ye yoginyaḥ kṣetrajāḥ priye ।
arcitā śvetapuṣpaiśca divyairdhūpairmanoramaiḥ ।।

caturvviṃśatidīpāśca dikṣu dikṣu pradāpayet ।
pṛṣṭhāpunādya cādhāre madhyakṣāmāsu vartulā ।।

aṣṭāṃgulapramāṇasthā śobhanā caturaṃgulā ।
adha ūrddhasamāyuktā tatpramāṇena kārayet ।।

kṛtaṃ dīpena saṃyuktā raktavartyopari sthitā ।
kalaśaṃ netrabaṃdhāśca maṇḍalāni visarjayet ।।

tairvinā na bhavetsiddhiragnirvaistu śruvādayaḥ ।
madyapūrṇeṣu bhāṇḍeṣu pūrvokteṣu gaṇāṃbike ।।

rasāyanamayoktena madhye pūjyā kṛśodarī ।
caturdikṣu varārohe pūjyā vai pīṭhanāyakī ।।

madyena surayā sādhu atha bhūtāṃgaśobhane ।
kapilā gomaye lipte punarmadhye na lepayet ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale kālikākule pūjānirṇayaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

arghapātradvayaṃ kṛtvā athavā ekameva tu ।
śaktyāvalokitaṃ kṛtvā paratatvasamanvitaṃ ।।

tadbhāvabhāvanāyuktaḥ saṃvitti bhāvayet sadā ।
grāhaye cārghapātraṃ tu raupyasauvarṇṇatāṃ vrajet ।।

athavā mṛnmayaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ trāsavraṇavivarjitaṃ ।
akālamūlajaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tadgrāhyaṃ nyāsamārabhet ।।

pūrvāhne cāparāhne vā ahorātraṃ hi yāvadhī ।
(125b)
pīṭhastotraṃ japedrātrau japaṃ kuryātsamāhitaḥ ।।

prabhāte vimale prāptaṃ vīrabhojyaṃ tu kārayet ।
mahābhāgyodayaṃ jāyedrājyaṃ vā khecaro bhavet ।।

bhakṣaṇātsiddhyate vīro yadi syānmaṃdabuddhayaḥ ।
vyādhidurbhikṣamaraṇaṃ cauramagniṣu taskaraṃ ।।

pararāṣṭrabhayaṃ ghoramakālaṃ mṛtyupātanaṃ ।
anyāni vividhāṃ dṛṣṭvā yeṣu saṃkhyā na vidyate ।।

nāśayetsatataṃ devi yadevāmoghaśaktayaḥ ।
kumārī bhojayeccātra vīrāntatulyayoginaḥ ।।

brahmahatyāsahasrāṇi prāyaścittaśatāni ca ।
sakṛtsaṃpūjanaṃ kṛtvā koṭipūjāruho bhavet ।।

śmaśāne kalpavṛkṣe ca yoginyaḥ kṣetrameva ca ।
śmaśāne cābhicārārthaṃ kalpavṛkṣe tu siddhidaṃ ।।
yoginyaḥ kṣetramelāpe sarvvasiddhikulāgame ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

kulāgamantu deveśa kimanyosti vada prabho ।
proktaṃ tava mahādeva hyasmin vai kulamādhave ।।

yadanyosti viśeṣeṇa prasādādvaktumarhasi ।
śmaśānaṃ kīdṛśaṃ proktaṃ kalpavṛkṣaṃ vadasva me ।।
yoginīkṣetra me brūhi yadanyonugrahaḥ kvacit ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śmaśānaṃ hṛtpradeśaṃ tu kalpavṛkṣaṃ tu kuṃḍalī ।
yoginyaḥ kṣetra bhrūmadhye jñātavyaṃ yoginīkule ।।

īdṛśaṃ tu mahādevi kulapūjātra vai priye ।
siddhyate tatkṣaṇāddevi yadi cāsti mukhāgamaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।
(126a)
mā gopayasvame deva kramahīnaṃ na siddhyati ।
khaṇḍajñānaṃ bhavetsarvvaṃ khaṇḍacakraṃ na muktibhāk ।।

māvamānaya me nātha sarvvasvaṃ na pragopayet ।
kathayasva svabhāvaṃ vai yadi snehaṃ mamopari ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

vikāsaṃ yatra saṃkocaṃ tatrocchā aparāmṛtā ।
mṛnālataṃtusadṛśā jñānavijñānagrāhakī ।।

calate sarvvacakreśī cakrasthā ca * madhyagā ।
nirguṇā nirvikalpā sā bhuvanādhāradhārakī ।।

paṃcadhā ca tridhā sā ca kulasatvapradāyakī ।
tāṃ praviśya prayogena akuleśāśca te pade ।।

cittacaitanyayogena maṃtrasaptādaśākṣare ।
rodhayitvā ca dvau spaṃdau layodayavivarjitau ।।

tyajyate prākṛtaṃ dehaṃ ahamityeva nānyathā ।
pūrvvapaścimakarmāṇi prakṛtākṣatākṛtā ।।

vighnarūpāṇi te sarvvaṃ mohayaṃti balāt priye ।
na teṣu ramate cittaṃ cittaṃ madhye niveśayet ।।

nāhamasti na cānyosti kevalaḥ śāntarūpake ।
kṣaṇamapyatra viśrāmaṃ sahajaṃ yadi bhāvayet ।।

tadā sa khecaro bhūtvā yoginīmelakaṃ bhavet ।
tanmadhye bhāvanāṃ kṛtvā praviśeddehajān bahūn ।।

tayāvalokitaṃ cakraṃ caturviṃśa * saṃkulaṃ ।
manmadhye bhāvanā yasyā sa vai coktā mukhāgame ।।

tataḥ pūjākramaṃ bhadre cānyathā paśubhāvanā ।
anenāsau prayogena kramaṃ pūjyānyathā paśuḥ ।।

tadbhāvaśakto yaḥ kaścitpūjakaḥ kramapūjakaḥ ।
(126b)
anyathā vākpralāpī syānmantrataṃtrabahiḥkṛtaḥ ।।

dvitīyaṃ mārgamevoktaṃ śṛṇuṣvekamanāḥ priye ।
śmaśānaṃ yatra vikhyātaṃ grāme vā nagarepi vā ।।

vayogatāśca ye lokā bhāvayanti janāśca ye ।
grāmānte grāmamadhye vā ekavṛkṣetha nirjare ।।

veśyāgṛhe samūhaṃ tu jñātavyaṃ yoginīgṛhaṃ ।
gopanīyaṃ sadā devi jñātavyaṃ sādhakena tu ।।

śmaśānaṃ veśmamadhye tu mukhāgamasusaṃsthitaṃ ।
brahmasaṃvittiko jñātvā gurvvāmnāyastu vai * * ।।

yoginī kṣetradeveśi saṃdhi vai sarvvasiddhiṣu ।
kalpavṛkṣaṃ tu śubhage madhye cādhāradhārakaṃ ।।

sarvvayoginisaṃghasya melāpaṃ cātra saṃsthitaṃ ।
evaṃ yo vetti tatvajñaḥ sa bhaveta kulāgamī ।।

evaṃ yo vai na jā(nā)ti trividhaṃ sthānapūjanaṃ ।
janmajanme paśuḥ so hi bhramate dehajān bahūn ।।

ādau cādhyātmikaṃ yogaṃ aliphalgu subhojanaṃ ।
svasaṃvittiprayogena sakṛdāpūjanaṃ yadi ।।

kulakoṭyarvudairyukto vasetkhecaramadhyagaḥ ।
sarvvakṛtsarvvabhoktā ca sraṣṭāraṃ bhuvaneśvaraṃ ।।
yena jānanti tatvajñāḥ kathaṃ bāhye tu pūjanaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrutaṃ sarvvamaśeṣeṇa kiṃtu kautūhalārthinī ।।
ye cānye darśane bhaktāḥ kathaṃ siddhyaṃti te bhuvi ।।

asmānmārgādvihīnā ye guptāmnāyaṃ na biṃdati ।
vīryahīnāśca ye mantrāḥ sādhakā bhrāntabuddhayaḥ ।।

melāpakavihīnā ye saṃnidhānārthavarjitāḥ ।
(127a)
kutaḥ siddhirbhavetteṣāṃ vadamanyaṃ viśeṣataḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ye yasminmantrayogena taṃtrācārapade sthitaḥ ।
tasya tatkathayiṣyāmi yathātatvena siddhyati ।।

anena kramayogena yajanaṃ cārabhet priye ।
svakramaṃ tu yajenmadhye dvaitācāraṃ tu varjayet ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

dvaitācāraṃ kathaṃ proktaṃ advaitaṃ kīdṛśaṃ prabho ।।
yena niḥsaṃśayo bhūtvā bhrānti vai na bhavet kvacit ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

vāmadakṣiṇataṃtrā ye akulāni kulāni ye ।
sauragārutraye proktā advaitadravyasambhavāḥ ।।

madyādipānā ye hārāḥ surāpānaiḥ suveśitāḥ ।
advaitācārasaṃyuktā dravyadvaitāśca ye smṛtāḥ ।।

te maṃtrāṇi vikalpāśca yeṣu mātrā samūhayaḥ ।
* śāktāśeṣasaṃsiddhaste pūjyāścakramadhyataḥ ।।

siddhāntā vaiṣṇavā bauddhā vedoktāḥ syātadarśanāḥ ।
tatprayatnena vai varjyā yasmātte paśavaḥ smṛtāḥ ।।

advaitadravyasaṃparkātsannidhānaṃ tyajanti vai ।
parāṅmukhatvamāpnoti nirjīvā jīvavarjitā ।।

teṣāṃ vai pūjanaṃ nāsti ābalyaḥ kramavarjitāḥ ।
na siddhiphalabhāgā ye pratyayaiśca vivarjitāḥ ।।

tapojapavihīnā ye pūjāhomavivarjitāḥ ।
mantrabuddhitvamāpannāste maṃtrāḥ paśutāṃ gatāḥ ।।

svaśaktirahitāste vai dīptiḥ sānnidhyavarjitāḥ ।
tatprasādācca siddhyanti śāktā cakroktipūjane ।।

tato bhavanti phaladā mokṣakāmārthasiddhidāḥ ।
(127b)
na deyaṃ yasya kasyāpi datvā siddhirna dṛśyate ।।

pūjanīyaṃ yathāmnāyaṃ kulāgamaratāśca ye ।
taṃtrācāravihīnā ye te tu varjyāḥ prayatnataḥ ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale kālikule taṃtrācāranirṇayo nāma ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

mūlamaṃtraṃ tvayā proktaṃ advaitayajanakramaṃ ।
vinā yena bhavetsiddhiryatkiṃcitparameśvara ।।

tanmamācakṣva deveśa dravyāṇāṃ nirṇṇayaṃ paraṃ ।
yadvinā na bhavedjñānaṃ saṃpradāyaṃ ca śāśvataṃ ।।

kīdṛkvakuladravyāṇi kulapānaṃ ca kīdṛśaṃ ।
kīdṛśaṃ bhojanaṃ teṣāṃ varttanaṃ kīdṛśaṃ bhavet ।।

pūjanaṃ kulamārgeṣu advaitācāravarttināṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

aprakāśyamidaṃ gopyaṃ rahasyaṃ bhairavoditaṃ ।
rakṣayeta prayatnādvai yathālokaṃ na garhati ।।

palāṇḍaṃ laśunādīni laṃvuṃśalaratarkaśā ।
anyāni teṣāmaṃgāni vāmā kusumasaṃyutaṃ ।।

dravyāṇyetāni subhage śaśyante kulamārgake ।
śivāṃbuṃ ca surāraktaṃ madyaṃ mahātailasīdhukaṃ ।।

kuṃḍagolodbhavaṃ devi hyete pānāḥ kulodbhavāḥ ।
matsyamāṃsaṃ gahāgotthaṃ sthalajākāśagāmināṃ ।।

nirvikalpastu sarvveṣāṃ bhojanaṃ kurute sadā ।
mātā vā bhaginī bhāryā duhitā baṃdhubāṃdhavī ।।

mātaṃgī kajjalī śauṇḍī kaṃdukī carmaraṃginī ।
dhvajinī cāsthivijalī dhīvarī cakriṇī priye ।।

ete caiva mahāpīṭhāḥ samudbhūtā kulāṃganāḥ ।
(128a)
taiḥ sadā varttanaṃ kuryādyadicchetsiddhimātmanaḥ ।।

taiḥ sarvvendravyajātīnāṃ parānāṃ yairniyojitāḥ ।
bhojane tatvayuktānāṃ dūtīnāṃ taiśvasaṃyutaṃ ।।

evaṃ saṃsārayogena pūjā vaiśeṣikī smṛtā ।
niḥsaṃkācāramārgena pūjanaṃ tu bhavettadā ।।

tadāsau siddhyate devi tairbhuktaṃ bhāvitaṃ yadi ।
tatpānāsparśadāhārā pāśachedakarā smṛtāḥ ।।

gopitaṃ tanmayā pūrvvaṃ advaitācāraśobhanaṃ ।
kaulopadeśayuktā ye pūrvācāraratāśca ye ।।

te ca paśu pavitrāśca pavitrāḥ paśavaḥ smṛtāḥ ।
anenāhārayogena ajñātvā narakaṃ vrajet ।।

jñānabhāvagatāḥ samyagvrajanti gagaṇāntaraṃ ।
hitāya sādhakendrāṇāṃ yāgārthe prakaṭīkṛtaṃ ।।

tayā śaktyāvihīnā ye na te siddhimavāpnuyāt ।
tasmātsarvvaprayatnena yāgaṃ kuryāttayā saha ।।

anyathā kurute yāgaṃ sarvvaṃ tasya vinaśyati ।
sugupte nirjane ramye vīrakṣetretha siddhiṣu ।।

digvāso hrivinirmuktāścādvaitakulapūjakaṃ ।
parasparaṃ yajeddehaḥ khānapānādisevitaiḥ ।।

parasparaṃ tu ratinau kuṃḍagolādisaṃyutāḥ ।
mānamaṃḍakasaṃyuktau bhoktārau ca parasparaṃ ।।

vaktrādvaktreṣu yojyantau bhojanaṃ pānacuṃbanaṃ ।
haritāni vicitrāṇi vividhāni śubhāni ca ।।

indrahaste yathā vajraṃ brahmaṇaśca kamaṇḍaluṃ ।
mama haste yathā śūlaṃ cakraṃ caiva hareryathā ।।

tathā yogīśvarī vīrāḥ laśunādipavitrakāḥ ।
(128b)
palāṇḍuṃ laśunādīni anye ye dravyacoditāḥ ।।

pavitrā maṃgalā vṛddhyā yāgānāṃ rakṣaṇe smṛtāḥ ।
nirvighnā vighnasamanā āśuddhipramadāḥ śubhāḥ ।।

na yatra laśunaṃ cāsti na siddhimokṣameva ca ।
vīrabhojyaṃ na cānyatra na ca yāgaṃ hi siddhidaṃ ।।

saṃnidhīkaraṇaṃ caiva melāpaṃ caiva jāyate ।
yathā yajñādayaḥ sarvve vinā cājyena niṣphalāḥ ।।

tathā kulākule devi somaratnairvinā priye ।
somaṃ palāṃḍumityuktaṃ laśunaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ।।

anye tadaṃśakāye ca tiṣṭhanti himavadgirau ।
utkaṭaṃ cāsya vai gaṃdhaṃ aśuddhāścaturaskṛtāḥ ।।

sarvve śuddhā hitā caiva pavitrāḥ sarvvathā smṛtāḥ ।
sarvvavyādhiharā bhadre sarvvasaubhāgyadāyakāḥ ।।

sānnidhyaṃ kulakaulasya bhuktimuktiphalapradaṃ ।
śāntipuṣṭivaśīkartumabhīṣṭaphaladāyakaṃ ।।

sarvvopadravahantāraṃ sarvvabhūtapriyaṃ priye ।
amṛtābhutavatve te na vanābhedi vai śrutaḥ ।।

siddhyarthe vīrasiṃhasya tadatyā sā kṣayo bhavet ।
guptaṃ tu bhūgṛhāntasthaṃ mṛgamodārasaṃyutaṃ ।।

prāśayedyopi nityasthaḥ śivavaccākṣayo bhavet ।
karttā hartā ca yo loke siddhiḥ sā rahasyada ।।

bhairavasya priyo nityaṃ bahumātṛgaṇasya ca ।
agramāpūrayedbhakṣorabhakṣaiśca laukikaḥ ।।

evaṃ vidhiṃ purā kṛtvā guhyakaulaṃ mayoditaṃ ।
ekamekena vīryeṇa prāsitenājarāmaraḥ ।।

sarvvasiddhivaro vīro divyasthā hyamṛtāśanaḥ ।
(129a)
melāpakaṃ tu devīnāṃ daheddhūpaṃ punaḥ punaḥ ।।

yatra sthāne bhaveddravyaṃ nirṇṇayaṃ tatra cābhavet ।
laśunasya palāṃḍo vāṃ surā madyasya bhairavi ।।

matsyamāṃsāhiyuktasya tatra krīḍanti devatāḥ ।
mātaraḥ khecarī sarvvā bhūcarī siddhimicchatā ।।

kulakaulodayaṃ tatra yatrasthā tu kulāṃganā ।
sarvvasiddhodayaṃ jāyedyatrasthe kulasādhake ।।

mahādravyaiśca vividhaiḥ krīḍantaśca parasparaṃ ।
sarvvaduḥkhavinirmuktaḥ sarvvaśokavivarjitaḥ ।।

sarvvasya mokṣamāpnoti varttate nirbhayaḥ sadā ।
kimanyatkathayiṣyāmi sādhakasya vibhūtayaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

kiṃ māhātmyaṃ tu vīrasya dravyāṇāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ ।
kulopadeśasaṃyuktā advaitācārasaṃyutāḥ ।।

prabhāvaṃ tvatprasādena na śrutaṃ me kadācana ।
adya me saphalaṃ janma tvatprasādāvadhāritaṃ ।।

vedāntairdūṣitāste tu vaiṣṇavādisamantataḥ ।
kimarthaṃ teṣu vai duḥkhaṃ bhāgyahīnā kathaṃ ca te ।।

asya dravyasya pānasya amṛtasya na bāhyataḥ ।
niḥphalaṃ kriyate sarvvaṃ mānuṣyaṃ bhuvi durlabhaṃ ।।

akasmāt prāpyate bhūyo yadi syānugrahaṃ kvacit ।
prāptedhikārajanmesmin sarvve gatvā nirarthakaḥ ।।

etanme saṃśayaṃ deva kathaṃ varjyaṃ ca teṣu vai ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

nāvidau kriyate dravyaṃ ayaṣṭvā ca hyanukramaṃ ।
(129b)
vidhihīnaṃ na siddhiḥ syāddatvā tu narakaṃ vrajet ।।

brāhmaṇe vidhiyuktyā tu yajñasautrāmaṇau smṛtaṃ ।
vaiṣṇave siṃhacakre ca grāhyaṃ niḥṣakaṃ rasaṃ ।।

teṣu bhāvavihīnā ye te mūḍhāḥ paśavaḥ smṛtāḥ ।
niṃdanti paramaṃ dravyaṃ cakṣuḥ nāśā bahiḥkṛtaṃ ।।

tadā tenaiva doṣeṇa ajñatvamupajāyate ।
yāvajjanmasahasrāṇi āyātāni punaḥ punaḥ ।।

punaṣkṛtya punarjanma bhramanti ca punaḥ punaḥ ।
yāvanna śaktipātena paramādvaitavarttinā ।।

tāvanmuktiḥ kutastasya viśeṣādvaitadravyasu ।
advaitabhāvanā divyaiḥ sa mukto bhavabaṃdhanāt ।।

koṭimadhyā tathaikasya bhāvanā yāti tatvataḥ ।
maṃtrairdravyākulasyāsya yogasya gatayaḥ smṛtāḥ ।।

niḥśaṃkācārabhāvena madhye krīḍanti sādhakāḥ ।
aho dravyasya māhātmyaṃ pānabhojanakādiṣu ।।

kulāṅganābhiyukteṣu kimetatkathayāmi te ।
janmakoṭisahasrāṇi tapojaparatāni ca ।।

vratāni niyamānīha kṛchracāṃdrāyaṇāni ca ।
sarvvayajñaśca dānāni pratiṣṭhāṃ naiva sarvvataḥ ।।

koṭi hyasaṃkhyasaṃkhyeṣu phalaṃ yatsamudāhṛtaṃ ।
sakṛt prāsitamātreṇa kulajño labhate phalaṃ ।।

kiṃ punaḥ śraddhayāyukto viśeṣakramapūjakaḥ ।
vidhiyuktasya deveśi sādhakasya hyudīritaṃ ।।

kulāmnāyeṣu ye śaktā ebhirdravyairbahiḥ kṛtāḥ ।
(130a)
paśavaste samuddiṣṭā na teṣu saha varttanaṃ ।।

etadvīreṣu māhātmyaṃ tvayi snehāt prakāśitaṃ ।
pūrvoktā vīrarājeśi vaktre syātpīṭhavāsinī ।।

tayā sārddhaṃ yajeddevi tadgṛhe pīṭhamuttamaṃ ।
mātaṃgī veśmasubhage prayāgaṃ parikīrttitaṃ ।।

kajjalī vāruṇākhyaṃ ca śauṇḍī kaulagṛhe priye ।
kaṃdukī cāṭṭahāsaṃ tu jayantī carmacaṃgiṇī ।।

caritraṃ dhvajinī proktaṃ ekāmrāstha vidāriṇī ।
navamaṃ cakriṇī pīṭhaṃ yatsurairapi durlabhaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

taṃ sthānaṃ kutra vai khyātaṃ yattaddeveṣu durlabhaṃ ।
hitāya vīravīreṣu pratyayaṃ paramaṃ vibho ।।

navamaṃ cakriṇī pīṭhaṃ tvayoktaṃ navadhāritaṃ ।
sarvvabījaistu kiṃ proktaṃ tato bhūtaṃ vaśaṃ ca yat ।।

kiṃ vīryaṃ kaḥ prabhāvoyaṃ kauḍilyākhyasya bhairava ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

navamā cakriṇī yā sā bhramantī viśvamadhyagā ।
sarvvabījāni pīḍyantī rasaṣaṭkairdvidhā kṛtau ।।

sā ca kuṃḍalinī nāma kaṃdaveśmādvinirgatā ।
aṣṭapīṭhodbhavāsāsu navamāsā ca siddhidā ।।

tayā hīnā ca yā kācitsiddhimelāpakena sa ।
tadā melāpakaṃ kuryāt sarvvayoginisaṃgame ।।

iḍāsuṣumne saṃghaṭṭe prayāgaṃ varavarṇṇini ।
varuṇākhyaṃ bhruvormadhye kaulaṃ laṃbikayā tathā ।।

nāsāgraṃ cāṭṭahāsaṃ tu jayantī tālaraṃdhrake ।
biṃdūparicaritrā ca ekāmrānābhigocare ।।
(130b)
devīkoṭaṃ tu kaṃdasthaṃ jñātvā paryaṭanaṃ bhavet ।
kaṃdordhvākuṃḍalī śaktiryathā vai mohitaṃ jagat ।।
etatte kathitaṃ sarvvaṃ kimanyaṃ vada bhairavi ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale kālikule ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

yā sā kuṃḍalinī śaktirguhyacakre vyavasthitā ।
gūhanīyaṃ sadā bhadre tatsthānaṃ sarvvakāmadaṃ ।।

tadā guhyeti vi(khyā)taṃ guhyādguhyataraṃ sadā ।
tadudbhavaṃ tu amṛtaṃ durlabhaṃ yatsurāsuraiḥ ।।

yasya janmasahasrāṇi kṣayaṃ yānti kulārṇave ।
saṃsāravāsanā yasya niḥśeṣā gati priye ।।

tadā saṃśuddhadehasya bhāvanā yāti tatpade ।
yasya devātidevena bhairavena mahātmanā ।।

anugrahaṃ yadā prāptaṃ tadā mati bhavetsadā ।
amāsomakalā yā tu brahmadvāre vyavasthitā ।।

tatra yātaṃ jalaṃ śuddhaṃ patitaṃ kauṇḍilī hṛdi ।
tasya dehādviniḥkrāntamamṛtaṃ kauḍilīrasaṃ ।।

tena prāśitamātreṇa bhavanti hyajarāmarāḥ ।
balīpalitanirmuktāḥ sarvvavyādhivivarjitāḥ ।।

karpūrāgurugaṃdhāni mṛgajāni viśeṣataḥ ।
pītamātreṇa deveśi kiṃ punarmantracetasā ।।

divyadehopi tṛptātmā dagdhabījaḥ punarbhavet ।
aṣṭādaśavidhaṃkuṣṭhaṃ jvararogagarādikaṃ ।।

vi(ṣāṃ)śca vividhān gho(rāṃ)ścakṣurogānanekaśaḥ ।
pītamātreṇa deveśi sarvvasiddhyāruho bhavet ।।

ādau yoginisaṃghasya yajanaṃ pūjanaṃ hitaṃ ।
(131a)
kulakaulasya sānidhyaṃ yogeśīṃ vīravallabhaṃ ।।

vīranāthaṃ svayaṃ devi bhavabaṃdhanisūdanaṃ ।
bhavaṃ bhavodbhavaṃ bhāvaṃ bhāvagrāhyaṃ bhavāntakaṃ ।।

bhasmasātkurute sarvvaṃ māyīyāṇavakarmajaṃ ।
sparśanāddarśanāt prāśyātpītvā sarvvārthasiddhidāṃ ।।

madhyotthā madhyamāsedhyā madhyabhāvapradā śubhā ।
kulodbhavā kulāyātā kulamātā kulākulā ।।

kulāvalyastu sāṃnidhyaṃ vīrabhāṇḍena saṃsthitaṃ ।
na viśvasanti ye vīrā ātmayogyopakāriṇaḥ ।।

vahante cātmasaṃsthaṃ tu vīravat kriyate carīṃ ।
yadā lokā hi jānanti tadā siddhiṃ vinaśyati ।।

āyurddhanaṃ ca vīryaṃ ca siddhibuddhiprabhāvataḥ ।
haranti yoginī sarvvā maṃtrāśca kulasambhavāḥ ।।

yogopadeśasadbhāvaṃ khecara(tvaṃ) saśuddhadhīḥ ।
kāryākāraṇasarvvāṇi siddhayo vividhāni ca ।।

etasmātkāraṇādgopyaṃ sadā yoginisambhavaṃ ।
niyātaṃ tiṣṭhate tasya karamadhyagatasya vai ।।

cintāmaṇiriva prakhyaṃ tadā melāpakaṃ bhavet ।
samūhaṃ kulavṛṃdasya kramasya ca viśeṣataḥ ।।

saṃnidhīkaraṇaṃ proktaṃ kule cocchuṣmasaṃbhave ।
etasmātkāraṇādbhadre guptyā vai siddhibhāgbhavet ।।

khecaratvamavāpnoti sarvvalokādhipo bhavet ।
sṛṣṭisaṃhārakarttā ca nānātve rūpabhedataḥ ।।

caṃdrakāpālikā nāma bhavate prāśanātsakṛt ।
eko deśo śivānte ca pāśavicchedake tathā ।।
(131b)
sarvvaduḥkhāni naśyanti aṣṭaridvīśvaro bhavet ।
kulakaulasamūhasya mātṛcakrasya bhairavi ।।

taṃtrācārasya sarvvasya bhairavākhyasya suṃdari ।
āhvāne dravya me yoktaṃ mantrayuktaṃ viśeṣataḥ ।।
mantrayuktaṃ dadedyatnānmantrahīnaṃ na siddhyati ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

ekavīrastvayā proktaṃ mantrayo na prakāśitaḥ ।
yena sā labhate siddhiṃ dravyaṃ devyā rasaṃ bhavet ।।

amṛtatvaṃ bhavedyena vīrāṇāṃ sannidhirbhavet ।
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi kulasiddhirbhavedyathā ।।

kulasiddhi paraṃ mukhyaṃ sarvvamadhye vyavasthitaṃ ।
tato vidhānaṃ paramaṃ mantrayuktaṃ śivapradaṃ ।।
prasādādvaktu me nātha mantreśaṃ mantranāyakaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣvekamanā bhadre akathyaṃ kathayāmi te ।
yā sā kuṃḍalinī nāma guptasthā yā ca nityaśaḥ ।।

śvetaraktanibhākārā śravantī yonimadhyage ।
meḍhravāhāparaṃdhreṇa sādhakasya sthitā tu yā ।।

tasyā devātideveśyā maṃtroyaṃ kathayāmi te ।
anākhyeyaṃ paraṃ kaulaṃ kulasārasya nirṇṇayaṃ ।।

yena vai smṛtamātreṇa yoginī vīrasaṃkhyayā ।
saṃnidhānaṃ prakurvvanti mātṛyuktāśca bhāvini ।।

saṃsāradamanī nāma pāśavicchedakārayā ।
dāridradamanī nāma bhūtāpasmāranāśanī ।।

yā khyātā picutantreṣu advaitācāravallabhā ।
vikalpotpātasamanī śāntavīryā parā kalā ।।

śāntā śāṃtodbhavā saumyā sukhasaubhāgyadāparā ।
(132a)
akālamṛtyuśamanī kālakalpāntabhakṣaṇī ।।

smṛtamātreṇa yāneha sa japtena ca bhairavī ।
parāvibhūtayastasya vādyaistiṣṭhanti śāṃkare ।।

eṣā vibhūtidā nāmā anāmā ca vadāmyahaṃ ।
karṇadeśe prayāge vā yadā jñātā na kecit ।।

anāhatāhataṃ devi prāṇeśī prāṇavallabhaṃ ।
spaṃdanādya samā * * tṛtīyaṃ varavarṇṇini ।।

caṃdrādhāre tṛtīyaṃ tu asyādhāraṃ tu paṃcamaṃ ।
ārūḍhaṃ tu tato devi vahniveśma na saṃyutaṃ ।।

kapālavarṇatritayaṃ aṃkuśākrāntapūrvake ।
aṃkuśā dvitaye yuktaṃ tṛtīyaṃ varṇṇanāyakaṃ ।।

tamevaṃ devadeveśi paṃcamāntasya yojayet ।
homāntapaṃcamairyuktaṃ maṃtreśaṃ mantranāyakaṃ ।।

navakoṭipravistīrṇaṃ matasāramatottamaṃ ।
tasyā madhyādviniḥkrāntā cintāmaṇimaṇiryathā ।।

tapo japavinirmuktā kleśāyā savivarjitā ।
nāstyatra sadṛśaḥ kaścinmantramasya śivāmbhasā ।।

yena vai smṛtamātreṇa pāśā bhasmībhavanti hi ।
bhavasāgaraduḥkhasya nirmūlakaraṇaṃ paraṃ ।।

kāmadhenuparādhāmnā hyakāmā kāmasambhavā ।
kāmacāracarā hyeṣā sarvvaprāṇāntamadhyagā ।।

madhyamā madhyaraṃdhrasya madhye madhyā śubhā smṛtā ।
asyāḥ sakāśātsarvvasya viśvasyaiva viśeṣataḥ ।।

sambhavapralayau jātau na caivānyasya kasyacit ।
nirvikalpā nirābhāsā śuddhāśuddhavatī śubhā ।।
(132b)
śubhāśubhasya karmasya vikalpadhvaṃsakasya ca ।
uditā tu parā hyekā yamanodyataniścitā ।।

amā nāma kalā hyeṣā bhīṣaṇā mantravigrahā ।
tasmāttāṃ gopayedyatnādagopyānmaraṇaṃ bhavet ।।

na deyaṃ yasya kasyaiva bhavet prāṇāntikaṃ phalaṃ ।
navadhā yasya cakraṃ tu pūrvavatkathitaṃ mayā ।।

bhakṣayanti ca tāstasya mardayaṃti ca dehakaṃ ।
vilasaṃti yatheccheha yathā śatruśca śatravaḥ ।।

medamajjāvasāśukraṃ raktaṃ māṃsaṃ tvacaṃ tathā ।
asthikarmendriyāṃ sarvvāṃ rūpayauvanasaṃśrayāṃ ।।

manobuddhirahaṃkāraṃ tanmātrendriyasaṃgrahaḥ ।
satyaṃ śaucaṃ tathāyuṣyaṃ bhāvanā mantrayogajaṃ ।।

haraṃti prasabhaṃ sarvvaṃ saroṣāmukharānanaḥ ।
tasyā gopyā hyakathyā ca kathanātkathitaṃ bhavet ।।

evamevaṃ hi subhage hyavicāreṇa jāyate ।
nirmūlakaraṇā hyeṣā dviprakāreṇa bhairavi ।।

guptācāro bhavedekā saṃsocchinnayedruvaṃ ।
prakāśayati mohātmā dvitīye patatādbhayaṃ ।।

evaṃ jñātvā prayatnena yattvamicchasi tatkuru ।
eṣā devātideveśī ekopāyena siddhyati ।।

kuṃḍalyāṣṭa na saṃyogāddhyātvā japtvā tu siddhyati ।
eṣotra paramottārasādhakasya gaṇāṃbike ।।

mahā sapuṇyakarmā ca ekajanmā punarbhavaḥ ।
nirbhayoyamiti khyātaḥ sarvveṣāṃ grasanogrataḥ ।।

niropasyā guṇāstasya yasyaiṣā vāsanā hṛdi ।
sa śivaḥ sādhakaḥ sākṣāt prāptamaṃtro gurormukhāt ।।
(133a)
yasya nāsti samaṃ devaṃ vidyā vā mantrameva vā ।
avahatā nirācārā avyayā paramānuyā ।।

kapālinī mahāvidyā sarvvamasyāḥ pratiṣṭhitaṃ ।
ye maṃtrā ye mahāvidyā yā devyo yāśca mātaraḥ ।।

samastādhvavisarpiṇyaḥ cātra vai saṃsthitāḥ priye ।
catuṣṣaṣṭhikulā ye ca śmaśānāścaiva bhairavi ।।

catuḥṣaṣṭhistu ovalyo ūrddhapīṭhādisaṃkulāḥ ।
tatra saṃnihitāḥ sarvve sakṛtsmaraṇamātrataḥ ।।

vidyayā nātra saṃdeha iti satyaṃ na cānyathā ।
yāvannā bhairavaṃ sākṣānmantrametanna labhyate ।।

labdhesminniti mantavyaṃ prasannā medya bhairavī ।
yasya devātideveśī kapālī hṛdi saṃsthitā ।।

kapālāntaravāsinyā maṃtramātā kulāraṇī ।
smṛtamātreṇa yeneha bhavanti sarvvasiddhayaḥ ।।

sādhakasya na saṃdeho matistvakaluṣā yadi ।
prākṛtaṃ dehamutsṛjya divyaṃ rūpaṃ tu yādṛśaṃ ।।

athavā bhairavākāraṃ sādhakaścintayetsadā ।
parāvṛtyāsane maṃtrī sarvvādhvoparisaṃsthitaḥ ।।

tatrastho niścalo bhūtvā śabdādiviṣayojjhitaṃ ।
saṃkocajñānajaṃ kṛtvā co anāvasthitaḥ sadā ।।

uccareccetasā tatra vidyāṃ ca paramāṃ priyāṃ ।
anāmiketi vikhyātā kāryopāṃgā kuleśvarī ।।

sahajoccārayogena yāvadvyomaparaṃ śivaṃ ।
dviṣaṭkāṃtāmṛtaṃ gṛhya siṃcedātmānato hṛdi ।।

adhūtatanu bhūtvā ca paratatvāvalaṃbakaḥ ।
(133b)
tadbhāvabhāvanāyukta saṃvidānaṃdarūpavān ।।

mahālakṣmīṃ parasthānā khasthitāmavadhārayet ।
ciccakrenārcyavidhinā śuddhabodhaikabhāvanā ।।

manasā suviśuddhena kharūpā khaśarīriṇī ।
ajākṣesita ārūḍhāṃ vyomabhairavasaptake ।।

icchārūpadharā devī sādhakecchānusārataḥ ।
paramārthena deveśī susaumyāmṛtasuṃdarī ।।

tanmayastadgato bhūtvā praviśaṃ śeśvake kule ।
yā sā pūrvaṃ samākhyātā nirlepā nirupaplavā ।।

vastumātrā svasaṃvedyā śaktirādyā manonmanī ।
etatkulaṃ vijānīyādakulaṃ śāśvataṃ padaṃ ।।

kulākulavibhedena vahnirūṣmeva saṃsthitā ।
anāhataparechāto niṣkalajñānarūpiṇī ।।

cakrametadvijānīyāttatra sarvvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ ।
sarvvadikkamanābhāsaṃ cidrūpaṃ cetanātmakaṃ ।।

akhaṇḍamaṇḍalākāraṃ dehaṃ devyāḥ sudhātmake ।
sa śivaḥ sā parāśaktirjñānaṃ jñeyaṃ ca tatparaṃ ।।

cittacaitanyasaṃyogānmantraṃ mantrī smareddhṛdi ।
kṣaṇamāpyatraviśrāmaṃ jāyate yasya kasyacit ।।

sa bhaivedbhairavaḥ sākṣātsahajoccārameva ca ।
tadā sā kurute pūjāṃ yogeśīnāṃ śivasya ca ।।

pūrvavadvidhimāsādya sādhayenmanasepsitaṃ ।
sakṛtya smṛtya tatsarvvaṃ kulavidyāṃ tu mantravit ।।

kulācāravidhānena aliguptatareṇa ca ।
nirācāreṇa bhāvena avadhūtena karmaṇā ।।

dehanyāsādikaṃ kuryāddūtyārṣādiṣu roditaṃ ।
(134a)
pūrvaddīpagaṃdhāḍhyaṃ pūrvākhye kulacodite ।।

śaktyuccāca pareṇaiva madyanādyā tu vartmanā ।
agnīṣomavibhāgena vilomāgrānulomataḥ ।।

śivaśaktimataṃ jñātvā advinābhāvayogataḥ ।
uccāreta nirācāraṃ kauleśī hṛdi saṃsthitā ।।

svasaṃcitparayā bhāsā cidrūpā citkalāvyayā ।
mudraiṣāparamā devyā hyanāmā baṃdhavarjitāṃ ।।

aparākārabaṃdhena kapālākhyāmṛtottamā ।
khaṭvāṅgañca tathaiveha yugmabhūtaṃ pradarśayet ।।

siddhakaulikavijñānaṃ tavādya prakaṭīkṛtaṃ ।
avinābhāvayogena kālikāpālikā mataṃ ।।

amṛtaṃ yatparaṃ śuddhaṃ vidhānaṃ ruhyanuttamaṃ ।
nyāsaḥ saṃkṣepataḥ sāraṃ mahāśrībhairavāgataṃ ।।

mantrapīṭhādisarvveṣu navanābhādiyojitaṃ ।
sarvveṣāṃ mūrddhnito devī bhairavyādyeṣu pūjayet ।।

pūvvaiva kathitā devī matasāre matottame ।
mahāśriyottamā devī mahālakṣmīti viśrutā ।।

pūrvavadyajanaṃ kṛtvā dīkṣādikramapūrvakaṃ ।
vidhinādarśayedyāgaṃ samayastasya nānyathā ।।

dṛṣṭamātreṇa yeneha mucyate bhavabaṃdhanāt ।
yasya * * samo yogaḥ satyametanmahātape ।।

phalaṃ na śakyate vaktuṃ jihvākoṭiśatairapi ।
tathāpi leśato devi kathitaṃ tava śobhane ।।

ya evaṃ jñāyate maṃtrī mahāsiddhyāruho bhavet ।
samagraparivārosau labhate śāśvataṃ padaṃ ।।

kimanyatkathayiṣyāmi yadanyaṃ nāsti tatvataḥ ।
(134b)
sādhakasya kulasyāsya vibhūtiḥ kathitā tava ।।

ityevaṃ sarvvamākhyātaṃ snehātkaulikamuttamaṃ ।
navayoginisaṃghasya saṃpradāyaṃ mukhodgataṃ ।।

udgīrṇṇante mayā devi sārātsmaratarottaraṃ ।
na kasyacinmayā bhadre saṃpradāyaṃ prakāśitaṃ ।।

mukhakaulodbhavaṃ nāma saṃcāraṃ yanmukhānmukhaṃ ।
siddhidaṃ sarvvatantrāṇāṃ jaḍānāṃ pratibodhakaṃ ।।

asiddha * yātyavīraḥ śaktimahābhavet ।
amaṃtro maṃtratāmeti madhye yukto jape yadi ।।

āvalyakramayukto vā siddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
(svakulā)mnāyabhraṣṭā ye ye bhraṣṭā lekhadoṣataḥ ।।

vaktramnāyavihīnā ye te siddhā mādhave kule ।
pūjanāddhavanājjāpāt svayaṃ devī prakāśitā ।।

utkīlanaṃ tu yatkiṃcitsarvvaṃ tatkathayiṣyati ।
na dadyādyasya kasyāpi mu(ktvā) śiṣyaṃ dṛḍhavrataṃ ।।

jātigrahavinirmuktaṃ sarvvasaṃkāvivarjitaṃ ।
alaulyaṃ paramādvaitaṃ suguptena nibhāvitaṃ ।।

tyāginaṃ dambhanirmuktaṃ krodhamātsaryavarjitaḥ ।
samaḥ sarvveṣu bhūteṣu satyavākkramapūjakaḥ ।।

devībhaktaḥ prasannātmā īdṛśaścātrabhojanaḥ ।
na calo tena ca prītyā nāparādhe kadācana ।।

saṃpūjya vidhivadbhaktyā prakāśye nānyathā kvacit ।
anyathā varttate yastu sa bhakṣaḥ śākinīkule ।।

tasmātsarvvaprayatnena kuryādājñā tu bhairavī ।
rakṣayet prāṇavajjantoścārebhyo draviṇaṃ yathā ।।

evaṃ samāsataḥ proktaṃ taṃtresmintava śobhane ।
(135a)
etadeva hi saṃkṣepānmaṃdarāgre ca mūrddhani ।।

bhūyo vakṣyāmi subhage mahāmādhavake kule ।
kālīkulamidaṃ guhyaṃ mukhapāraṃparāgataṃ ।।

kimanyatkathayiṣyāmi vada cakreśi vaṃdi * ।। ।।

।। iti śrījayadrathayāmale caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke kālikulapaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

evamākarṇya deveśi kālikākhyaṃ mahākulaṃ ।
punaḥ provāca deveśaṃ praṇipatya surārcitaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

tṛptāsmi bhagavan samyak jñānaughaṃ pariśṛṇvatī ।
adhunā śrotumicchāmi cakramelāpake vidhiṃ ।।

kathaṃ devī samārādhyāddhṛdayeśī sukālikā ।
śrutvaitadvacanaṃ devyā cakrendraspaṃditaṃ mahat ।।

sāṭṭahāsamuvācedaṃ khaṃḍendukṛtaśekharaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge sarvvajñānārthabhājane ।
vakṣyāmi niṣkale nādya tava parvvatanaṃdini ।।

tricakreśī vidhiṃ ghoraṃ sarvvamelāpakārakaṃ ।
vīrasthānaṃ samāsādya prastarenmātṛkāṃ śubhāṃ ।।

sarvvavīropacāreṇa vidyā saṃpūjya coddharet ।
ṣāntaṃ sāntaṃ mahākālaṃ dhāmamāpāsabiṃdukaṃ ।।

piṃḍametanmahādevi trailokyagrāsaghasmaraṃ ।
kuṃḍalāghaṃ kevalinaṃ dhāmatadveśmacihnitaṃ ।।

asyādyaṃ punaruddhāryaṃ dhāmākuśasamanvitaṃ ।
kuṃḍalādyaṃ tato deyaṃ punarbbāhuṃ saveśmakaṃ ।।

sarvvādyaṃ punarevānya asyādhyo dvau punaḥ priye ।
tribiṃdukaṃ paṃcamādyaṃ citrabhānugataṃ likhet ।।
(135b)
tṛtīyādyaṃ punardeyaṃ yonipūrvvaṃ savahnikaṃ ।
vahniveśmasamāyuktaṃ vadvametatsamuddharet ।।

varuṇāntaṃ ca tatkūṭaṃ bāhuṃ biṃdudvayāṃkitaṃ ।
nādavahni * taṃ kṛtvā sṛṣṭiyuktaṃ tataḥ kuru ।।

bhīmāṅkuśaṃ dvidhā kṛtvā vidyā sarvvottamottamā ।
aṣṭādaśākṣarā ghorā trailokyagrāsaghasmarā ।।

sarvvasiddhikarī raudrā cakramelāpasiddhidā ।
dhyāye hutaghanākāśa prakhyā paramabhairavī ।।

kṛśāṅgī ghoravadanā viśvarūpā mahaujasā ।
mahāpretasamārūḍhā sarvvāyudhakarodyatā ।।

parāparāthavā śuddhā hyaparātha parāthavā ।
evaṃ dhyāyeddevadevīṃ tāsāṃ pṛṣṭhasthitā mama ।।

tāśca śūlasthitā devyo mahāpretakṛtāsanā ।
tadā nārāsanā devī ghoraghorā karaṃkiṇī ।।

evaṃ dhyātvā śūlagatāṃ yajenmaṇḍalamadhyataḥ ।
sarvvavīropacāreṇa ekavīrāṃ durāsadāṃ ।।

evamabhyarcya vidhivatkaṃdodaravinirgate ।
mahāśūle viśvacaṃḍāṃ paścātsādhanamārabhet ।।

vīrasthānaṃ samāsādya saṃkhyā lakṣākṣaraṃ japet ।
mahāvīrapalaṃ hutvā paśyate parameśvarī ।।

tasyā eva tadā * rdhvaṃ plavate khecarī padaṃ ।
sādhakenāsti saṃdehaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ gaṇāmbike ।।

athavā melakārthīyo sa tu kuryādvidhiṃ idaṃ ।
gatvā pitṛvanaṃ rātrau sādhakaḥ kṛtaniściyaḥ ।।

bahuvīravaraiḥ sārddhaṃ sarvvavarṇasamudbhavaiḥ ।।
eko vā sādhakendrosau śavaṃ gṛhya mahāmatiḥ ।।
(136a)
tṛtpṛṣṭharūḍho subhage mudrāṃ badhvā karaṃkiṇīṃ ।
yāvajjapeḥ śataṃ devi tāvadāyānti devatāḥ ।।

tāsāmarghaṃ pradātavyaṃ bhitvā vāmāṃgamātmanaḥ ।
tata utpadyate vyoma tābhiḥ sārddhaṃ surārcitaḥ ।।

bhairaveva bhaveddevi sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣamāraṇoccāṭanādikaṃ ।।

līlayā kurute maṃtrī nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
anyānyapi hi karmāṇi kīrttitāni mayā purā ।।

yāvanti tāni deveśi sādhayetparameśvarīṃ ।
bhogamokṣapradā seyamākhyātā te yathārthataḥ ।।

tvayā guptatarā kāryā na deyā yasya kasyacit ।
evaṃ saubhāgyadā proktā kimanyatkathayāmi te ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
parontakālyāsādhanapaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

bhagavansarvvamākhyātamāditaḥ samanukramāt ।
adhunā śrotumicchāmi mahābhāgyodayaṃ paraṃ ।।

yena saubhāgyamatulaṃ sādhakādiṣu jāyate ।
vallabhatvaṃ jagatyasmin sarvvamantre svaratvatā ।।

subhagatvaṃ mahādeva pātralaṃ sarvvasādhane ।
yena jāyeta devendra tamupāyaṃ vadasva me ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu bhadre pravakṣyāmi yathāvaccodito hyahaṃ ।
mahābhāgyodayaṃ nāma sarvvasāraṃ sumadhyame ।।

prastārya mātṛkāṃ devīṃ pūjayitvā yathāvidhi ।
(136b)
paścātsamuddharedvidyāṃ sukhasaubhāgyabhūtidāṃ ।।

varuṇāntaṃ vahniyutaṃ mohanī biṃdulāṃcchitaṃ ।
mahānādaṃ tadvadeva dvitīyaṃ varṇṇanāyakaṃ ।।

praṇavena samopetaṃ varuṇāntaṃ samuddharet ।
kālādyamaṃkuśākrāntaṃ varuṇaṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ ।।

adhoṃkuśaṃ tribiṃdvaṅkaṃ kālādyāmapi sāṃkuśaṃ ।
gaṇendraṃ vāyusahitaṃ kālādyaṃ rudrabheditaṃ ।।

yathoṅkuśaṃ vahniveśmaṃ ekatraiva samuddharet ।
sadāśivaṃ bhīmaśaṃ * homāntaṃ ca tatoddharet ।।

caturddaśākṣarāseyaṃ vidyā sarvvottamottamā ।
mahābhāgyodayakarī bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।

asyānusmaraṇāddevi yopi duḥkṛtakṛnnaraḥ ।
api dāsakule jāto rājyaṃ dhatte na saṃśayaḥ ।।

asyā eva hi deveśyā na tithiṃ na vratādikaṃ ।
kevalaṃ hṛnmahāpadma madhyamaṃ smaraṇāt priye ।।

mahāsaubhāgyakaraṇā mokṣadā bhogavarddhanī ।
asyāḥ samarcanaṃ devi śṛṇuṣvārādhanaṃ tathā ।।

śubhaṃ sthānaṃ samāsādya tatra yāgaṃ yaje tu vai ।
padmagarbhāṃ samālikhya maṇḍalaṃ sarvvakāmadaṃ ।।

tanmadhye vinyaseddevīṃ bhāgyakālīṃ mahābalāṃ ।
raktapadmopamā ramyāṃ susaṃpūrṇāṅgayaṣṭikāṃ ।।

sarvvābharaṇasaṃyuktāṃ hārasragdāmamaṃḍitāṃ ।
mahāpadmasamārūḍhaṃ mahāpadmadharāṃ tathā ।।

pāśāṅkuśadharāṃ śubhrāṃ kapālakaraśobhitāṃ ।
trinetrāṃ lakṣaṇairyuktāṃ padmaṣaṇḍagatāṃ smaret ।।
(137a)
ekavīrāṃ mahodagrāṃ sphuratkiraṇabhāsurāṃ ।
mahāratnasahasraughavarṣayantīṃ vicintayet ।।

evaṃ vinyasya ca tadā tarpayedvibhavaṃ tathā ।
nānāvidhopahāraiśca surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

tato homaṃ prakurvīta śrīphalairghṛtasaṃyutaiḥ ।
kalaśaṃ pūjayitvā tu paṭṭasraggaṃdhaveṣṭitaṃ ।।

śatābhimaṃtritaṃ kṛtvā tenātmānaṃ niṣecayet ।
bhiṣiktamātrātsubhagaḥ sākṣātkāma ivāparaḥ ।।

sureśvarapuraṃdhrīṇāṃ spṛhaṇīyaśca jāyate ।
sarvvabhāgyasamāyuktaḥ sarvvasiddhisamanvitaḥ ।।

bhavatyeva hi vīrendro abhiṣekānna saṃśayaḥ ।
lakṣāṇāṃ paṃcakaṃ devi yadi giryagramāśritaḥ ।।

japatyeva hi vīrendrastaddhyānagatamānasaḥ ।
ekacchatrāṃ savasudhāṃ japāntādeva bhuṃjate ।।

homamantraprakarttavyaṃ bilvaguggululāpalaiḥ ।
padmaiśca parameśāni sādhakaḥ siddhipūraṇaṃ ।।

evaṃ sarvvottamā kālī sarvvakāmaphalapradā ।
śobhāvatīti vikhyātā mahābhāgyodaye priyā ।।
tavākhyā maheśāni kiṃ bhūyaḥ paripṛcchasi ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
mahābhāgyodayavidhiḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi viṣanirnāsanīṃ parāṃ ।
yathā kālī mahābhāgā tathā māṃ brūhi bhairava ।। ।।
(137b)
śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu devendranamite rahasyamidamuttamaṃ ।
viṣadarpapraśamanaṃ kaliduḥsvapnanāśanaṃ ।।

devyāvidhānamatulaṃ duṣṭoraganipātanaṃ ।
kulikāntakarī yā sā sā śṛṇu pravareśvarī ।।

saṃpūjya mātṛkāṃ devīṃ paścādvidyāṃ samuddharet ।
khaganāthavahniyutaṃ mohanībiṃdulāṃchitaṃ ।।

punaḥ khageśvaraṃ devaṃ gaṇendravahnidhāmayuk ।
varuṇāntaṃ tacchitaṃ ca bāhuṃ biṃdutrayāṃkitaṃ ।।
