saṃhāraṃ ṣaṣṭhasaṃyuktaṃ biṃdunādavibhūṣitaṃ ।
yonipūrvvaṃ pañcamasthaṃ śakraṃ bindutrayāṅkitaṃ ।।

yonipūrvvaṃ tribiṃdvaṅkamamṛtaṃ punaruddharet ।
bāhuṃ vāruṇasaṃrūḍhaṃ tribiṃdvaṅkaṃ ca vāruṇaṃ ।।

amṛtoghaṃ punardatvā gaṇendraṃ vahnisaṃsthitaṃ ।
amṛtaṃ kevalaṃ bhūyaḥ tribidvaṅkanniṣedhakaṃ ।।

somaṃ varuṇasaṃrūḍhaṃ sṛṣṭiyuktaṃ mahābalaṃ ।
ekonaviṃśatirvarṇṇā vidyeyaṃ kulikāntakī ।।

tārkṣaskandhasthitā pūjyā sarpamekhalamaṇḍitāṃ ।
sukṛṣā nīlajīmūtasadṛśā sā karaṃkiṇī ।।

trinetrā sūryakoṭīnāṃ tābhirhanti maheśvarī ।
grasantī kulanāgendrātpibantī madirāsavaṃ ।।

ghorahuṃkāranihrādabhāsurāṃ bhayanāśanīṃ ।
pūjayeta yathānyāyaṃ sarvvaviropacārataḥ ।।

surāsavādimāṃsaiśca tarpayeta karaṃkiṇī ।
juhuyāduttamaṃ mānsaṃ tataḥ siddhyati sā bhṛśaṃ ।।

tataḥ prabhṛtyasau devi bhakṣayedviṣaparvvatāṃ ।
(138a)
kulanāgakulaṃ sarvvaṃ grasatyeva kimadbhutaṃ ।।

yatra yatra smareddevīṃ yojanānāṃ śatāntare ।
phaṇīndrāḥ pravinaśyanti patanti viṣaparvvatāḥ ।।

garā vināśamāyāti bahunā kathitena kiṃ ।
sthāvaraṃ kṛtrimaṃ caiva jaṃgamākhyaṃ viṣaṃ tathā ।।

śaṃkākhyaṃ lohaliṃgākhyaṃ lūtākhyamatidāruṇaṃ ।
darśanādevasubhage nāśayetsādhakeśvaraḥ ।।

kṣobhayennāgabhuvanaṃ krodhātsaṃtāḍanena tu ।।
gatvā girivaraṃ bhadre japedakṣarasaṃkhyayā ।।

lakṣamekaṃ mahāvīro japānto plavave namaḥ ।
sarvvaiśvaryasamopeto dvitīya iva bhairavaḥ ।।

athavā lakṣajāpena sarvvakarmāṇi sādhayet ।
vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣamāraṇoccāṭanādikaṃ ।।

nāganigrahamevāgryaṃ viśanirnāśanaṃ ca yat ।
anyānyapi karmāṇi sādhakaḥ sādhayiṣyati ।।

evaṃ tava samākhyātā viṣanigrahakārikā ।
nāgāśanīti vikhyātā sarahasyā maheśvari ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
nāgāśanīvidhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi meghakālī bhayānakā ।
yasyāḥ prasādato deva meghanigrahamadbhutaṃ ।।

siddhyate parameśāna karmāṇyetāni yāni ca ।
tāṃ me vadasva bhagavan bhogamokṣaphalapradaṃ ।। ।।
(138b)
śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu vīrendranamite meghakālī bhayapradaṃ ।
yasyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa brahmāṇḍamiva kaṃpate ।।

calanti parvvatāḥ sarvve nāgā yānti diśodaśa ।
tāṃ śṛṇuṣva mahābhāge trailokyātaṃkaśātanīṃ ।।

guptadeśaṃ samāsādya pūjyatāṃ mantramātaraṃ ।
sarvvavīropacāreṇa tato vidyāṃ samuddharet ।।

caturddhā bhairavādyaṃ syādantimaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃyutaṃ ।
amṛtaṃ śakrasaṃrūḍhaṃ biṃdupraṇavalāṃcchitaṃ ।।

ṣaṣṭhādyaṃ tu yathoddhṛtya vahnyāsanasamanvitaṃ ।
prathamaṃ cāti veśmāṅkaṃ dvitīyaṃ cāṅkuśāhataṃ ।।

tṛtīyaṃ praṇavākrāntaṃ caturthaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃyutaṃ ।
sarvvabindusamāyuktā nādameva tu kevalaṃ ।।

muśalaṃ svāgasaṃrūḍhaṃ vahniveśmasamanvitaṃ ।
ṣaṣṭhādyamaṃkuśākrāntaṃ caṃdrakevalamuddharet ।।

samīraṇaṃ tathā deyaṃ śaṃkunirodhirodhitaṃ ।
daśapaṃcākṣarā kālī meghāśaninivāraṇī ।।

sarvvasaṃpatpradākhyātā kālīkālabhayapradā ।
kṛṣṇābhragaganaprakhyāṃ mahānīhārasaṃnibhāṃ ।।

nirmāṃsāṃ vikṛtāṃ raudrāṃ kālāgnyāyutaghaṭṭinīṃ ।
mahātārkṣasamārūḍhāṃ śavakarṇāvataṃsinīṃ ।।

sarpahārakṛtā yethā kulanāgakṣayaṃkarīṃ ।
sarvvāyudhadharāṃ devīṃ jagadgrāsaikaghasmarāṃ ।।

evaṃ dhyātvā nyaseddevi maṃḍale paṃkajopari ।
sarvvavīropacāreṇa pūjayeta karaṃkiṇī ।।

paṃcāmṛta samāviṣṭe vyomamadhyā susiddhidaṃ ।
evaṃ saṃpūjya sā devī paścāllakṣatrayaṃ japet ।।
(139a)
uttamaṃ juhuyāttatra japāntādvai daśāṃśataḥ ।
tatastvā gacchate tasya vimānaṃ sārvvakāmadaṃ ।।

kalpavṛkṣasamopetaṃ devadevyā prabhāvataḥ ।
tamākarṇya bhavetsākṣādyathā bhairavanāyakaḥ ।।

athavā yāgamātreṇa siddhayaḥ sādhayiṣyati ।
sāmānyāstaṃtramadhye yāḥ purā proktā mayā tava ।।

meghanigrahaṇe yojyā bhasmasarṣapamaṃtraṇaiḥ ।
tadā nivārayedrodraṃ meghaṃ kālakṣayopamaṃ ।।

yojanānāṃ śate devi sīmābaṃdhaṃ prajāyate ।
vidravanti bhayaḥ sarvve hyanantādyāḥ phaṇīśvarāḥ ।।

ekoccāreṇa subhage yadi megho na naśyati ।
tadā kruddhena manasā triruccārātsamuccaret ।।

śatadhā sphoṭate mūrddhnā kulasyā * pi bhairavi ।
evaṃ śamanamākhyātaṃ meghānāṃ tava bhāmini ।।

sa rahasyaṃ tvekavīraṃ nākhyeyaṃ yasya kasyacit ।
adhunā kiṃ pravakṣyāmi vada parvvatanaṃdini ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
meghakālīvidhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

idānīṃ vada devendra sarvvapātālamarddanī ।
sarvvayaṃtraprathamanī karṣaṇī sarvvayoṣitāṃ ।।

pātālakṣobhaṇī raudrā japādeva hi kevalaṃ ।
tamākhyā hi mahādeva sarahasyaṃ mahābalaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge praśnametadvadāmyahaṃ ।
(139b)
gopitaṃ tanmayā devi tadidānīṃ śṛṇuṣva me ।।

śubhakṣetraṃ samāsādya pūjayenmantramātaraṃ ।
sarvvavīravidhānena tato vidyāṃ samuddharet ।।

triguṇaṃ proddhareccādau tato haṃsaṃ samuddharet ।
kālaṃ praṇavasaṃyuktaṃ tadādya kevalaṃ punaḥ ।।

gaṇendraṃ kevalaṃ bhūyaḥ paṃcamādyaṃ trisūryakaṃ ।
ābhyāṃ madhye ca varuṇaṃ proddharetparameśvari ।।

nādaṃ tridhā samuddhṛtya vahnibījaṃ sabiṃdukaṃ ।
prathamaṃ cāṃkuśākrāntaṃ dvitīyaṃ mohanāvṛtaṃ ।।

tṛtīyaṃ ṣaṣṭhasaṃrūḍhaṃ vāruṇāṃśatribindukaṃ ।
cāpādyamuddharedbhūyo bāhuṃ kevalinaṃ punaḥ ।।

padametaddhirabhyastaṃ amṛtaṃ ca sabindukaṃ ।
saṃhārapraṇavākrāntaṃ kālādyaṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ ।।

samīraṇaṃ tatoddhṛtya padametaddvidhā kuru ।
ṣaṣṭhādyamaṃkuśākrāntaṃ paṃcamādyaṃ ca sāṃkuśaṃ ।।

cakraṃ tribiṃdumūrddhānaṃ niṣedhaṃ ca savāyukaṃ ।
sṛṣṭiyuktamataḥ kṛtvā bhīmāṃkuśanirodhitaṃ ।।

catustriśākṣarā dhyātā vidyeyaṃ ghoravikramā ।
eṣā pātayate sūryaṃ somamante sureśvari ।।

saptapātālasamanī sarvvayantrapramarddanī ।
brahmāṇḍamathanī ghorā kālarātrī karaṃkiṇī ।।

asyoccāreṇasubhage vepate jagadāturaṃ ।
calate kaṃpate pūrṇṇe brahmāṇḍaṃ sacarācaraṃ ।।

naināmavāpnuyātkaścidvīrabhāgyā surārcite ।
āsuryo yoṣitaḥ sarvvā karṣayatyeva līlayā ।।

etāṃ prapūjayeddevīṃ paramādvaitamāśritaḥ ।
(140a)
kādye padmagate devi surākuṃbhethavā sadā ।।

sarvvakāmapradevāya maṃḍale parameśvarī ।
mahāvibhavasaṃbhārairarcayedviśvaghaṭṭinīṃ ।।

śaktiyaṃtrapravāhena madhyamārgoditāṃ kalāṃ ।
karmodvatāṃ caṃḍatarāṃ sakalāṃ ghoradarśanāṃ ।।

dhyāyedbrahmāṇḍalakṣaughaghaṭṭanī bhṛkuṭīmukhāṃ ।
ghorakalpāntakālāgnikoṭyarvudasamaprabhāṃ ।।

kālāliśaradākāśaśrīkaṃṭhagalasaṃnibhāṃ ।
sarvvāyudhamahodagrapāśāṃkuśavirājitāṃ ।।

kṛṣṇakesarisuskaṃdhasaṃsthitāṃ kṛśarūpiṇīṃ ।
sarvvāvayavabhogīndravibhūṣitaśarīriṇīṃ ।।

ghoraghaṃṭāṭanāṃkāravadhirīkṛtaviṣṭapāṃ ।
śiroruhadhayajvālā kalāpaparabhairavāṃ ।।

tathāṅgāramavarṣa * * * * śakarāṃ sadā ।
pātālayaṃtraraṃdhrāṇāṃ tacchayopi hṛdaṃvare ।।

kṣobhayaṃta jagatsarvve * pātāṃśca viśeṣataḥ ।
evaṃ dhyātvā prapūjyaināṃ maṇḍale sarvvakāmade ।।

paścājjapellakṣamekaṃ giryagre sādhakeśvaraḥ ।
uttamadravyahavanātsiddhidā sā daśāṃśataḥ ।।

paścādvrajedviladvāraṃ rātrau guru dine priye ।
jape dhyāyaṃ devadevī kālikāṃ viśvaghaṭṭinīṃ ।।

tatastu kaṃpate sarvvaṃ pātālaṃ daityayaṃnitaṃ ।
śatārddhāvarttamātreṇa yaṃtrāḥ sarvve pataṃti hi ।।

śatajāpātsureśāyāstāhyantama * yoṣitaḥ ।
arghamādāyatāḥ sarvvā nirgacchanti varāṅganāḥ ।।

dagdhadagddheti jalpantyo vepamānā bhayārditāḥ ।
(140b)
patanti pādayostūrṇaṃ sādhakasyārcayanti ca ।।

vijñāpayanti karuṇāṃ trāhi trāhi nareśvara ।
evaṃ śrutvā sādhakendro tābhiḥ sārddhaṃ viśettataḥ ।।

nāmnā garbhapuraṃ devi sānugaḥ saparicchadaḥ ।
svargātkoṭiguṇaṃ sthānaṃ duṣprāpamakṛtātmanāṃ ।।

tatra tābhiḥ samāyukto mahākalpaṃ sajīvati ।
sarvvajñāna mayā yukto vedavedāṃgapāragaḥ ।।

sākṣādrudravapurdevi koṭiḥ kāmayate striyāṃ ।
mahākālyāvasāne tu śive saṃlīyate pare ।।

amṛtaṃ athavā japtvā sādhakendro dṛḍhavrataḥ ।
sugamaṃ śrīmukhaṃ gatvā vidhinā tatra veviśet ।।

dīpikā pādalepaiśca sarvvayaṃtrāṇi mardayet ।
nirbhaya svagṛhaṃ yadvadyāvadvāpatate vibho ।

hāṭakasya * subhage pātālontaramaṃtravit ।।
taṃ pūjyāvarttayedenāṃ yāvadvidyāṃ śatāvadhi ।।

tāvadāyāntitāḥ sarvvā yoṣito bhayavihvalāḥ ।
pūrvvavatsiddhirevasyādviśeṣo na vidhīyate ।।

ārttikasya amāvāsyāṃ śrīmukhaṃ mukhamuttamaṃ ।
(rātrau) cāvarttayedvidyāṃ śaktiraṃdhraprayogataḥ ।।

nirgacchanti tataḥ kanyāḥ pūrvvavattanumadhyame ।
pūrvvavatsiddhimeṣyanti sādhakasya mahātmanaḥ ।।

evaṃ devī samākhyātā pātālakṣobhaṇī śivā ।
sarahasya mahābhāge kimanyatparipṛcchasi ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
pātālakalpavidhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।
(141a)
śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi durbhagānāṃ maheśvari ।
subhagī karaṇaṃ yena jāyate japitena ca ।।

sarvvapriyatvaṃ deveśa vallabhatvaṃ ca yoṣitaḥ ।
kenopāyena jāyeta tamupāyaṃ vadasva me ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu vīrendranamite vidhānamidamuttamaṃ ।
karaṃkiṇyā dīptataraṃ sarvvasamyaṅguṇāvahaṃ ।।

subhagaṃ deśamāsādya tatra vidyāṃ samuddharet ।
kuṃḍalaṃ vahni veśmāṅkaṃ vahnisthaṃ biṃdulāṃcchitaṃ ।।

dvijihvaṃ vāyusaṃrūḍhaṃ trikoṇaparimaṃḍitaṃ ।
amṛtādyantasaṃbhinnaṃ somasthaṃ sarvvasiddhidaṃ ।।

ṣaṣṭhādyaṃ vahnisaṃrūḍhaṃ tribiṃduśirasānvitaṃ ।
vāyuṃ kevalamuddhṛtya daṃṣṭrimaṃ karttanasthitaṃ ।।

tribiṃdukhacitaṃ mūrddhnā vāhnamevaṃ samuddharet ।
tejendrabhāryayā yuktā navārṇā jagadīśvarī ।।

raktāraktāmṛtābhāptā suṣūmnākoṭarekṣaṇā ।
pāśāṃkuśakaravyagrā mohayantī carācaraṃ ।।

mahāpretasamārūḍhā sarvvābharaṇabhūṣitā ।
dhyātvā saṃpūjayedbhaktyā maṃḍale paṃkajopari ।।

sarvvavīropacāreṇa surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।
kalaśaṃ śucamaṃtritvā sahasraṃ parivartya ca ।।

abhiṣekaḥ pradātavyaḥ svadehe vā parasya vā ।
mahābhāgyodayaṃ jāye hṛllabhatvaṃ jagatpattiṃ ।।

paścādgirivare gatvā lakṣamekamataṃdritaḥ ।
japeta kālikāṃ devīṃ juhuyāduttamottamaḥ ।।
(141b)
daśāṅgato mahābhāge tataḥ saṃsiddhyate priye ।
siddhā yā lakṣyate hyeta anoccāraṃ nibodhate ।।

āgacchanti janāḥ sarvve yathā tīrthaṃ tathaiva hi ।
pūjayanti ca tāṃ nityaṃ yathā devi maheśvari ।।

ātmārthadārairvividhairmahādānaiḥ sadeva hi ।
kṣobhayeta jagatsarvvaṃ ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogataḥ ।।

dāsabhāvena varttante janāstrailokyavāsinaḥ ।
eṣā jagadvallabhatve bhavamokṣaphalapradā ।।
sarvvakarmakarī khyātā kiṃ bhūyaḥ paripṛcchasi ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe jayadrathayāmale caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke priyakālyāvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

punareva samākhyāhi mahāpātakaśodhane ।
yadvidhānaṃ mahāraudraṃ pāṭhānmuktiphalapradaṃ ।।

kāṣṭhasānavinirmuktaṃ vratacaryyā vivarjitaṃ ।
tāmākhyā hi mahākālyāḥ śodhanaṃ pāṭhamātrataḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu pṛcchasi suśroṇi dayayā sādhakātmanāṃ ।
pravakṣyāmi ca taṃ bhadre mahāpātakanāśanaṃ ।।
vidhānaṃ devadeveśyāḥ susiddhaṃ bhogamokṣadaṃ ।।

saṃpūjya mātṛkāṃ devīṃ paścādvidyāṃ samuddharet ।
praṇavaṃ prāksamuddhṛtya ḍaṃvaraṃ tadanaṃtaraṃ ।।
amṛtaṃ sṛṣṭisahitaṃ niṣedhaṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ ।।

kālaṃ sapraṇavaṃ deyaṃ kālādyaṃ ca gaṇeśvaraṃ ।
varuṇaṃ kevalaṃ deyaṃ paṃcamādyaṃ trisūryayuk ।।
gaṇendratripadopetaṃ bāhuṃ biṃdutrayānvitaṃ ।।
(142a)
varuṇaṃ cāṅkuśāyuktaṃ gaṇendravaruṇasthitaṃ ।
tribiṃdudīpitaṃ kāryaṃ varuṇāntaṃ sa pañcamaṃ ।।

cāpādyaṃtasthitaṃ kuryādvahniveśmavibhūṣitaṃ ।
varuṇāntaṃ sapraṇavaṃ cāpaṃ caiva samīraṇaṃ ।।

punastu kalaśaṃ deyaṃ praṇavena samanvitaṃ ।
ādyamakṣarayugmaṃ tu punareva samuddharet ।।

amṛtaṃ tu dvidhā kāryaṃ biṃdunā lāṃchitaṃ kuru ।
dvitīyamaṃkuśākrāntaṃ kāmadevaṃ tatoddharet ।।

tejendrabhāryayā yuktaṃ ṣaṭviṃśā varṇṇabhūṣitā ।
vidyā tava samākhyātā mahāpātakanāśanī ।।

hatvā brahmāṇḍamakhilaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ paripūritaṃ ।
na sa pāpena lipyeta padmapatramivāmbhasā ।।

samayollaṃghanaṃ sarvvaṃ viruddhamapi yādṛśaṃ ।
saptāvarṇṇā praṇaśyeta devi kāmākṛtaṃ yadi ।।

sakṛtpūjya mahākuṃbhe kādye vā parameśvari ।
gaurīrūpadharā kālī śataṃ ca parivartya ca ।।

abhiṣiṃcedayaṃ devi dīkṣitaṃ taṃ bhavenmahat ।
sarvvamaṇḍalamukhyeṣu sarvvatantreṣu bhairavi ।।

mahāmohakule bhogī siddhibhāgjāyate naraḥ ।
sakṛdāvartya deveśī mokṣabhāgī bhaviṣyati ।।

ṣaṇmāsātsaraṇenaiva svayaṃ jñānaṃ pravarttate ।
anayā codakaṃ japtvā saptavārā mṛge kṣaṇe ।।

mṛtyukāle prokṣayeta ye yaṃ sādhakasattamaḥ ।
taṃ nayedbrahmasāyojyaṃ vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।।

sarvvakarmakarī seyaṃ tavākhyātā suśobhane ।
bahunā vā kimuktena yāvattantravare priye ।।
(142b)
sādhanaṃ varṇṇitaṃ tubhyaṃ siddhyate hyanayāva tat ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ paripṛcchasi ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
pādyāntakīvidhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

pāpiṣṭhānāṃ mahādeva divyastambhaṃ vada prabho ।
kā sā vidyā tadvidhāyāstava deva hṛdi sthitā ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu vīrendranamite śubhakālīṃ mahottamāṃ ।
yayau cāritayā devi vaśayet sacarācaraṃ ।।

pūjya maṃtrāraṇīṃ devīṃ paścādvidyāṃ samuddharet ।
prathamaṃ vāyumuddhṛtya kālādyaṃ vahnisaṃsthitaṃ ।।

ṣaṣṭhaṃ bindusamāyuktaṃ piṃḍeśaṃ sarvvasiddhidaṃ ।
ḍaṃbaraṃ ca mahākālaṃ cāpādyaṃ puruṣaṃ tathā ।।

praṇavena samāyuktaṃ nādaṃ kevalamuddharet ।
tribiṃduvahnitaṃ kāryaṃ niṣedhaṃ parameśvari ।।

cāpaṃ kevalamuddhṛtya vāhnakālasthitaṃ kuru ।
vāruṇaṃ kevalaṃ deyaṃ tadantāṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ ।।

daṃṣṭrinaṃ karttanārūḍhaṃ bāhuṃ biṃdutrayāṃkitaṃ ।
punarāḍambaraṃ deyaṃ ādyavarṇadvayaṃ punaḥ ।।

saptādaśārṇṇā vidyeyaṃ muṇḍapādavivarjitā ।
sarvvottamā mahākālī mahāpātakabhakṣaṇī ।।

dvitīya ṣaṭkaparyante yathāpūrvvaṃ prakāśitā ।
mahālakṣmī tadvadeśā viśeṣonmasti kaścana ।।

tadvaddhyānaṃ ca cakraṃ ca pūjā cāsyāḥ prakīrttitā ।
atha vakṣyāmi vidyeśyā yantrayogaṃ mahābalaṃ ।।
(143a)
yena saṃstambhaye divyāṃ āyojanaśatādapi ।
nadītīrātsamāhṛtya mṛvaṃ liṅgaṃ tataḥ kuru ।।

tasyāntare nidhātavyaṃ sādhya nāma puṭīkṛtaṃ ।
taddivyapadasaṃyuktaṃ stambhayeti samanvitaṃ ।।

māyayā saptadhā veṣṭya rocanā kuṃkumena ca ।
taṃ pūjayeta vidyeśyāstāpaye kṣīravāriṇā ।।

avicchinnāṃ dahedvārāṃ liṃgasyopari sādhakaḥ ।
tāvatsaṃtarate samyakpūrvvadivyairna saṃśayaḥ ।।

ghaṭataṃdulakeśebhya vahnito viṣabhakṣaṇāt ।
karate saukṣaṇārddhena yasyā nāma puṭīkṛtaṃ ।।

āyojanāyutāṃ devi satyaṃ satyaṃ na cānyathā ।
eṣā vidyā mahābhāsā parataṃtropacārataḥ ।।

sarvvakarmakarī khyātā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi garbhastambhaṃ sudāruṇaṃ ।।

rūpānanaṃ sṛṣṭiyutaṃ * * vahnipuṭīkṛtaṃ ।
savisargaṃ tataḥ kāryaṃ tadvadeva dvijihvalaṃ ।।

trivarṇā paramākālī garbhastaṃbhe niyojayet ।
iṣṭikodaramālikhya pītadravyena kenacit ।।

nāma saṃpuṭya sādhyāyā cchādayeta dvitīyakaṃ ।
evaṃ saṃstabhayedgarbhaṃ satyaṃ devi na cānyathā ।।

eṣa mantravaro devi sarvvakarmakaraḥ smṛtaḥ ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ staṃbhanastatra madhyataḥ ।।

duṣṭarājñāmapi sadā taṃtradveṣṭiṣu kārayet ।
kiṃ bhūyaḥ kathayiṣyāmi vadasva kuliśodari ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
staṃbhakālīvidhiḥ ।। ।।
(143b)
śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi duṣṭarājñāṃ nipātanaṃ ।
tatra dviṣṭeṣvapi tathā vada devādyamāciraṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

vakṣyāmi subhage tubhyaṃ tantrarājasya madhyataḥ ।
ghorāṃ śatrukṣayakarīṃ kālaṃ kālabhayapradāṃ ।।

yā mārayeti sarvvasya trailokyasya na saṃśayaḥ ।
gatvā harālayaṃ maṃtrī tantravidyā samuddharet ।।

kaṃkālaghṛṣṭavasudhā kṛtvā pūjya yathākramaṃ ।
mahākālaṃ samuddhṛtya varttanaṃ jīva varjitaṃ ।।

kuṃḍalaṃ kevalaṃ deyaṃ śāktaṃ śāntaṃ sthitaṃ kuru ।
bhīmaśaṃkuṃ tato deyaṃ vidyā ghoraparākramā ।।

caturvvarṇṇā mahāraudrā arddhavarṇṇadvayānvitā ।
kālaṃ saṃhārakaraṇī śatrupakṣakṣayaṃkarī ।।

nairāṃ samuccaretsamya(gsā)yadvā vīravaṃdite ।
yāvattacchatrusantāpaṃ yāvadrājñā na kheditaḥ ।।

khinnastu tāḍitaścaiva prayuṃjeta japetavā ।
tadvidhānaṃ [pradhānaṃ ca] pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvekāgramānasaḥ ।।

(ekaliṃ)gaṃ harāvāsaṃ gatvā rātrau nareśvaraḥ ।
tatra santapta vasudhā kṛtvā kruddhena cetasā ।।

trikoṇabhuvanasyāntaḥ sarvvakṛṣṇopacārataḥ ।
yajeta ghoranīhārakālālikulasannibhāṃ ।।

digvāsāmūrddhakeśāṃ ca vikarālāṃ suśuktikāṃ ।
khaḍgahastāṃ jvalatkeśāṃ kharasthāṃ kekarekṣaṇāṃ ।।

sarvvavīropacāreṇa dhyātvā pūjyaṃ yathāvidhi ।
(144a)
śatamāvarttayettatra tadā siddhyatyasaṃśayaḥ ।।

evamārādhya sā tatra paścānmāraṇamārabhet ।
kuṃbhakena prayogena nāma saṃyujya sādhakaḥ ।।

sādhye syo(ccaurgha)rādisthā śatoccārā trivāsarāt ।
pṛthivīśaṃ samuddhṛtya māraye nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

rakṣā na vidyate tasya yasya cainaṃ vimācaret ।
sāmānyaṃ puruṣaṃ devi mārayedekavāsarān ।।

na sadā ghoranirghātabhedanāt * * * * * ।।
jalaṃ triśūlavivarādbhedānmārayate ripuṃ ।।

yena yena prakāreṇa kruddhastu japate priye ।
tena tena prakāreṇa mārayettriśānapi ।।

evaṃ devī kālarātrī na deyā yasya kasyacit ।
gopanīyā prayatnena prāṇāntepi na dāpayet ।।

yadi mohādarthalobhādbhayādvā dīyate priye ।
tadā kūṣmāṇḍa ca vane pacyate kālamakṣayaṃ ।।

evaṃ hitaṃ vicāryaivadyathecchasi tathā kuru ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ kimanyatkathayāmi te ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
kālarātryāvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

vada saṃgrāmasamaye yathā hyāyudhamaṃtraṇāt ।
japamāpnoti vīrendro rājñā saha kadācana ।।

kurvato yāgamatulaṃ dviṣaṃto vā yadā gatāḥ ।
yena yāthāstute duṣṭāstamupāyaṃ vadeśvara ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu pṛcchasi bhadraṃ te vākyaṃ bhaktānukaṃpayā ।
(144b)
vakṣyāmi tattava snehādvidhānaṃ sarvvamohanaṃ ।।

saṃpūjya mātṛkāṃ devīṃ paścātkālīṃ samuddharet ।
dvijihva * masaṃrūḍhaṃ sṛṣṭiyuktaṃ tataḥ kuru ।।

karttanāntaṃ kevalinaṃ cakraṃ kālāgnisaṃsthitaṃ ।
biṃduyuktaṃ mahābhāge caṃdrādyaṃ sa tribiṃdukaṃ ।।

tridhānaparyaṃtaṃ * * ca paraṃ sṛṣṭimūrcchitaṃ ।
saptārṇṇā ghoravidyeśi pāṭhātsiddhipradaṃ nṛṇāṃ ।।

yathā sā rāviṇī proktā tadvaddeśā prakīrttitā ।
tat prabhāvaṃ ca tadbīryaṃ tadrūpaṃ tadvijṛṃbhitaṃ ।।

tathā ca pūjanaṃ devi kāryamatra na bhedavat ।
tathā yāgena siddhiḥ syāddhṛdayesminma(hī)cale ।।

siddhasya hṛdayasyāsya trailokyenāstyasādhitaṃ ।
lokāntarāṇi yāgāni yadā kurvvan dviṣaṃti ye ।।

sahasaive ca ni[rvi]ghnaṃti teṣāṃ nāśe smaret sadā ।
khaḍgādyāyudhajātāni prokṣyāvatyāśataṃ sakṛt ।।

tāḍyāyudhāni saṃgṛhya kṣobhayedripuvāhinīṃ ।
ekastviṃdrabalaṃ sākṣānmārayenmohayet kṣaṇāt ।।

varṇāntaṃ dṛśyate sākṣātkālāntakayamopamāṃ ।
sādhakaṃ śatrusainyasthai yodhvai bhairavarūpiṇāṃ ।।

sarvvai te paśavo devi ghātayitvā khamutpatat ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātāṃ hyastralakṣmī prasiddhidā ।।

(kiṃ bhūyaḥ) kathayiṣyāmi vada bhairavi bhāmini ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturthaṣaṭke kālyāvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

athāpi devadevendra na jñātaṃ tatparammayā ।
(145a)
rahasyamelakaṃ nāma kathaṃ kāryaṃ susiddhidaṃ ।।

kairvā sādhakasaṃsādhyaiḥ kairupāyaiśca kathyatāṃ ।
kaṣṭasādhananirmuktaṃ vadavakasureśvara ।।

evamākarṇya bhagavān surāsuranamaskṛta ।
provāca devadeveśyā rahasyaṃ paramādbhutaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu samyakpravakṣyāmi yannākhyātaṃ kadācana ।
rahasyamamalaṃ guhya devyā hṛdayamuttamaṃ ।।

rahasyaṃ sthānamāsādya dūtīyukto gurūttamaḥ ।
madirānaṃdacaitanyaḥ kṛto balividhikramaḥ ।।

prāśyapaṃcāmṛtaṃ tatsthaṃ tato vidyāṃ samuddharet ।
dvijihvamamṛtārūḍhaṃ punarvāruṇasaṃsthitaṃ ।।

kālāgnibiṃdusaṃdīptaṃ punarnādaṃ saśakrakaṃ ।
biṃdupraṇavamūrddhāntaṃ kuṃḍalaṃ bāhusaṃsthitaṃ ।।

patitendusamāyuktaṃ vidyā sarvvottamottamā ।
sārddhatrivarṇā deveśī viśvarūpā mahaujasā ।।

ādyādhāre pūjanīyā madbhāvibhavayogataḥ ।
tarpyamantraṃ mahāramyaṃ kṛtvā puṣpādidhūpitaṃ ।।

pracchādādipaṭairyuktaṃ vitānopariśobhitaṃ ।
tatra dū * saṃmānīya kṛtottānāṃ sulālasāṃ ।।

supuṣpitāṃ sutāṃbūlāṃ malayādyairvicitritāṃ ।
tasyādhāraṃ tato śodhya cakrāṃvu pravikūrjanaiḥ ।।

tatrasthāṃ pūjayeddevīṃ kāliṃ viśvāraṇīṃ parāṃ ।
suraktāṃ śubhagā devī sarvvābharaṇabhūṣitāṃ ।।

viśvarūpadharāṃ samyak paṃcāmṛtasulālasāṃ ।
sarvvavīropacāreṇa paścādvidyāṃ japennṛpai ।।
(145b)
tallayastanmano bhūtvā mudrāṃ kāmāturāṃ japet ।
evaṃ yāvadatikrāntā muhūrttāstraya eva hi ।।

vācadrasmikulāṃ caiva vikase sādhakeśvare ।
sa bāhyābhyantaraṃ sarvvaṃ vyaktimeti na saṃśayaḥ ।।

melāpamatra jāyeta rahasyākhyāṃ mahādbhutaṃ ।
evaṃ sarvvāsvavasthāsu melāpaṃ na vinaśyati ।।

militasyāsya melāpaṃ melāpā militastu saḥ ।
śivavadraśmimelāpānmelāpa iha kīrttitaṃ ।।

rahasyākhyaṃ rahasyena sarahasyaṃ rahasyavat ।
tadrahasya samāpatyā rahasyāmalabhāgbhavet ।।

siddhāṃgane siddhavīro siddhistho siddhimelakaḥ ।
siddhicakrakulāpīḍaḥ siddhimelāpabhāgataḥ ।।

jyeṣṭhā kuletha sajyeṣṭho jyeṣṭhāṃdhujyeṣṭhayogataḥ ।
jyeṣṭhamelāpabhāgjyeṣṭho bhavatyeva na cādbhutaṃ ।।

yadā navakulārūḍhaḥ kramametatsamācaret ।
navapīṭhavahe mārge navayāgra * laṃpaṭaḥ ।।

evaṃ nave nave naiva rahasyaṃ melakaṃ bhavet ।
aṭaṃtyomyaṃ aṭaṃtyasya aṭato hyaṭataḥ svayaṃ ।।

naivāṭaṃtyo hyaṭaṃtyasya kālayaṃtyo hyaṭaṃti vā ।
evaṃ rahasya melāpī sādhakendro bhavatyalaṃ ।।

yāvatpuroditaṃ sarvvaṃ melāpe vidhivistare ।
vidhāneṣu samasteṣu tatsarvvamanayā priye ।।
sādhanānāṃ naravalairviśeṣo nātra vidyate ।।

evaṃ rahasyamelāpaṃ tantrarāje jayadrathe ।
tavākhyātaṃ mayā subhru kimanyaṃ kathayāmi te ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṅśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
melāpakālīvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।
(146a)
śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi caṃdracūḍāmaṇehara ।
sarvvākarṣakarī yā sā kālī kālāntakāriṇī ।।

yasyā smaraṇamātreṇa jagadākarṣa * drutaṃ ।
tāmākhyā hi prasādena mahyaṃ sarvvāmarārcite ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi sarvvākarṣaṃ sumadhyame ।
gatvā harasya saṃsthānaṃ tatra vidyāṃ samuddharet ।।

yonipūrvvaṃ vahniyutaṃ biṃdupraṇavalāṃchitaṃ ।
punaryonyādyamuddhṛtya susulaṃ caramasthitaṃ ।।

dvidhāpadamidaṃ kāryaṃ tṛtīyādyaṃ tribiṃduyuk ।
tadvadvāhnaṃ samuddhṛtya kālādyaṃ kevalaṃ tathā ।।

kālāgniyuktaṃ bāhuṃ ca musalaṃ ca tribiṃduyuk ।
mahāmohamathoddhṛtya jagadākarṣaṇaṃ kṣamā ।।

ekādaśārṇā vidyeyaṃ mahāpātakanāśanī ।
bharūḍī nāma vikhyātā kālī kālanikṛntanī ।।

pāśāṃkuśadharīraudrā jagadgrāsaikaṭaṃpaṭā ।
kapālakhaṭvāṃgadharā mahāhi kṛtamekhalā ।।

mahāpretasamārūḍhā sukṛśāraktavarṣaṇī ।
siṃdūrapuṃjasadṛśā sarvvāvayavabhūṣitā ।।

pūjanīyā trikoṇāntaḥ kādyaikaṃdodgatā śivā ।
sarvvavīropacāreṇa surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

paścāddhomaṃ prakurvvīta narakṣyājāvimīnakaiḥ ।
avyaktairvvā deveśi tataḥ sādhanamārabhet ।।

lakṣatraya guhāntastho japedvidyāṃ ataṃdritaḥ ।
(146b)
pūrvoktaṃ homamātiṣṭhettadā siddhyati līlayā ।।

tataḥ prabhṛti vīrendro vicarasthāśca yoṣitaḥ ।
karṣayeta balādeva devadevyāḥ prabhāvataḥ ।।

saptāhājjapahomena dīpaparvvaṇisādhakaḥ ।
nāgālayānnāgakanyāḥ karṣayellīlayā sadā ।।

rājikālavaṇaṃ hutvā karṣayeddevayoṣitaḥ ।
rājā vā rājapatnyaśca karṣayejjapayogataḥ ।।

prāṇaśaktivaśāsthityārādāṃkuśamasesataḥ ।
kṣobhabhāti samāhārāt karṣayejjagadāturaṃ ।।

anena yogavīryeṇa mahāvidyā prabhāvataḥ ।
tannāsti yanna śubhage karṣayettatkṣaṇādasau ।।

evaṃ saṃkarṣaṇīkālī sarvvākarṣakarī parā ।
sarahasyā mahābhāge tavādya prakaṭīkṛtā ।।

ākarṣaṇī samastasya jagatosya mahābalā ।
sarvvākarṣakarī seyaṃ tavādya prakaṭīkṛtā ।।
kiṃ vācyaṃ vada vakṣyāmi sarvvavīrendravaṃdite ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale caturthaṣaṭke sarvvākarṣakālīvidhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

jīvākarṣamato deva śrotumicchāmi sāṃprataṃ ।
atiraudraṃ ca śoṣaṃ ca yena śu * ṣu jāyate ।।
evaṃ niḥśeṣato deva brūhi me tripurāntaka ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yadya saṃcoditastvayā ।
gopanīyaṃ prayatnena taskarebhyo dhanaṃ yathā ।।

hālāhalavidhānoyaṃ jaganmāraṇatatparaṃ ।
(147a)
gatvā pitṛvanaṃ ghoraṃ tatra vidyāṃ samuddharet ।।

karttanāntaṃ sṛṣṭiyutaṃ jihvalaṃvahṛdeva hi ।
nādamaṃkuśasaṃyuktaṃ śakraṃ tadvatsamuddharet ।।

punarnādaṃ kevalinaṃ śakramagnigṛhānvitaṃ ।
kālamaṃkuśasaṃyuktaṃ bāhuṃ kevalamuddharet ।।

samīraṇaṃ karttanaṃ ca nādaṃ karttanameva ca ।
punarnnādaṃ kevalinaṃ bhīmaśaṃkuṃ nirodhitaṃ ।।

caturddaśākṣarā vidyā jīvākarṣaṇatatparā ।
asyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa mārayetsasurāsurān ।।

krūrāṃ ghora mahāmṛtyurvāhanāṃ bhrukuṭīmukhāṃ ।
ūrddhakeśīṃ karālākṣāṃ sūrpahastāṃ maholvaṇāṃ ।।

kapālakhaṭvāṅgadharāṃ ghora nistriṃśadhāriṇīṃ ।
mahāśani karavyagrāṃ kṛṣṇakākālisaṃnibhāṃ ।।

ni(rmāṃ)sā bhīmaracanāṃ trailokyakṣayakārikāṃ ।
śavāvayavadigdhāṅgīṃ vyālahāravirājitāṃ ।।

prakurvvantīti śakulāṃ mahājvālā kadaṃbakāṃ ।
āpibantī jīvakulaṃ carvvayantīha kāraṇaṃ ।।

evaṃ hālāhalaṃ raudraṃ dhyātvā rātrau prapūjayet ।
sarvvakṛṣṇopacāreṇa sutapte vasudhātale ।।

surāmāṃsāsavairdivyairmahādhūpopahārakaiḥ ।
evaṃ sā pūjya ghoreśīṃ paścātsādhyaṃ nivedayet ।।

kṛtvā māṣāṭakamayaṃ raktāstātāṃsukīlitaṃ ।
vaktavyaṃ devi dadatte paśuḥ puruṣasaṃjñitaṃ ।।

evamuktāśirastasya chiṃdyāttīkṣṇāsilā tadā ।
(147b)
tatkṣaṇājjīvaharaṇī api sā * chatakratoḥ ।।

evaṃ vidhena vidhinā mārayetsa surāsurāṃ ।
anivarttakamevaitanmāraṇaṃ samudāhṛtaṃ ।।

śoṣaṇaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi ta * devi yathārthataḥ ।
pūrvvavat proddharedvidyāṃ trilokyasyāpi śoṣaṇīṃ ।।

varuṇāntaṃ ca śakrasthaṃ mohanī biṃdulāṃchitaṃ ।
kālaṃ bindusamāyuktaṃ amṛtaṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ ।।

varuṇaṃ punaruddhṛtya amṛtaṃ sāṃkuśaṃ tathā ।
kālādyaṃ caiva saṃhāraṃ harṣaṃ biṃdu trayāṃkitaṃ ।।

punaḥ kālādyamuddhṛtya sarvvāntaṃ punaruddharet ।
samīraṇaṃ bhīmaśaṅkuṃ vidyeyaṃ dvādaśākṣarī ।।

śoṣaṇī sarvvajagatāṃ ghoravādyaṃtagāṃ smaret ।
virūkṣādhūmasaṃkāśāṃ ghorāṃdhakare vā kulāṃ ।।

vātacakrasthitāṃ dhyāyedviśvarūpāṃ karaṃkiṇīṃ ।
anena dhyānayogena śoṣayejjagadadbhutaṃ ।।

jaṃpuṭena prayogena japātkoṣayate ripuṃ ।
saṃśuṣkamacchiśoṣantaṃ mārayennātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

sarvvadā tu parikṣīṇaṃ divasā saptamāt priye ।
evaṃ guptatarākhyātā ghorarūpā karaṃkiṇī ।।

nidrā chedamato vakṣye śatrormāraṇa tatparaṃ ।
prāgṛḍaṃvaramuddhṛtya kuṃḍalaṃ vahnisaṃsthitaṃ ।।

tadveśmabiṃdusaṃyuktaṃ caṃdraṃ sṛṣṭisamanvitaṃ ।
karttanaṃ kevalaṃ datvā nādabiṃdusamāyutaṃ ।।

karttanaṃ paṃcamāśīnaṃ nādaṃ vahnikṣayānvitaṃ ।
bhīmaśaṃ * niruddhaṃ tadvidyā gho(ra)tarā priye ।।
(148a)
tannāsti yanna kurute veśyādyaṃ māraṇāntikaṃ ।
yena yena prakāreṇa saṃsmaredviśvavigrahā ।।

tataḥ karma prakurute satyaṃ nāstyatra saṃśayaṃ ।
kṛtvāmā - takenaiva śatroḥ prakṛti meva hi ।।

vidyayā puṭitaṃ nāma rudhirāṃgāralekhitaṃ ।
padayuktaṃ vinikṣipya yadabhīṣṭa hi sādhake ।।

udare sukṛte retuṃ culyādhau(ni)khanena vai ।
arddharātreṇa maraṇaṃ śatrorjīvati bhīṣaṇaṃ ।।

anivarttakrameṇaivanna deyaṃ yasya kasyacit ।
dātā narakamāpnoti sādākhyaṃ vatsaroṣṭakaṃ ।।

evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ ghoraṃ śaktitrayaṃ mahat ।
mahārahasyamatulaṃ bhogamokṣaphalapradaṃ ।।
adhunā vada suśroṇi kiṃ pravakṣyāmi bhairavi ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
jīvākarṣikālyāvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

punaḥ śrotuṃ manīṣā me viṣādigaranāśanī ।
kāsau devī hyekavīrā vada kāmāṅganāśanaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu * saṃpravakṣyāmi viśanāśanamuttamaṃ ।
yanna kasya cidākhyātaṃ tavādya kathayāmi te ।।

proddharethāmṛtaṃ bījaṃ varuṇasthaṃ ca śakragaṃ ।
vāyuṣaṣṭhaṃ samāsānaṃ biṃdunādādilāṃchitaṃ ।।

piṃḍametanmaheśāni sākṣādamṛtamuddhṛtaṃ ।
(148b)
kālaṃ kevalamujvālya jihvilāsvātmagaṃ kuru ।।

sāṃkuśaṃ ca gaṇeśaṃ ca paṃcamādyaṃ sabiṃdukaṃ ।
nādaṃ ca bāhumuddhṛtya vāruṇaṃ ca tribiṃduyuk ।।

sūryācaṃdramasau deyau nābhyāṃ sūryaṃ sabiṃdukaṃ ।
caṃdrasṛṣṭiyutaṃ kāryaṃ vidyeyaṃ * daśākṣarā ।।

viṣabhārasahasrāṇi garabhāraśatāni ca ।
asyāḥ prasādātsubhage bhakṣayetsādhako bhṛśaṃ ।।

etāmārādhya deveśi purā maṃdiramūrdhani ।
garuḍena tataḥ prāptaṃ vīryaṃ yadviṣabhakṣaṇaṃ ।।

etasyā eva subhage prasādā nāgasaṃtati ।
bhakṣitāṃ garuḍendreṇa karaṃkiṇyā prasādataḥ ।।

dhyāyedenāṃ tuṣārādriśuddhasphaṭikanirmalāṃ ।
nirmāṃsāṃ garuḍārūḍhāṃ śivāvayavaśobhitāṃ ।।

vyālahāravicitrāṃgī sudhākalaśadhāriṇīṃ ।
caṃdrārkakarahastāntāṃ pāśāṃkuśadharāṃ śivāṃ ।।

viśvarūpāṃ viśvagatāṃ kālāntakaraṇīṃ śivāṃ ।।
citrāṃpaśobhitāṃ citrāṃ citrakarmaprasādhanīṃ ।।

saṃpūjya maṃḍale devī sarvvavīropacārataḥ ।
surāmāṃsāsavaiścitrairjuhuyāttilataṃḍulāṃ ।।

evaṃ sā siddhidā proktā sadā vipanivāraṇe ।
vinatānaṃdanaṃ yadvannāgāḥ paśyanti bhīṣaṇaṃ ।।

tasmātkoṭiguṇaṃ nityaṃ nāgāḥ paśyanti sādhakaṃ ।
viṣayaṃ trividhaṃ devi darśanādeṣa nāśayet ।।

garapānīyadānena śaṃkākhyamayamājanān ।
ḍākinyā karṇapāśaśca darśanā bhūtarakṣasā ।।
(149a)
evaṃ ghoratarā devī tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।
kimanyadadhunā vacmi vada vīrendravaṃdite ।।

evamākarṇya sā devī devadevāttrilocanāt ।
punaḥ prāha prasannāsyā jagatāṃ patimādarāt ।।

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi villāsaṃ yogamelake ।
vidhānamatulaṃ yacca vada vīrendramāciraṃ ।।

śrutvaivaṃ punare - tya jagadārttaharo * * ।
mahāmelāpamatulaṃ cillāsaṃ yogasaṃjñitaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva saṃpravakṣyāmi mahāmelāpamuttamaṃ ।
yena dhītena subhage bhavedvīreśvareśvaraṃ ।।

gatvā harāspadaṃ rātrau tatra vidyā samuddharet ।
kakālagharṣitāṃ kṛtvā vadhu{dhāṃ}citimadhyagaḥ ।।

tatoddharenmahāvidyāṃ sarvvayoginikarṣaṇīṃ ।
sarvvādyantatridhāśakraṃ punaḥ sarvvādimaṃ dadet ।।

punaḥ śakramohanāṅkaṃ punaḥ sarvvādimaṃ kuru ।
punaḥ śakraṃ tribiṃdvaṅkaṃ tadva * ṣṭādimaṃ kuru ।।

varuṇāntaṃ tadvadeva paṃcamādyaṃ ca sāṃkuśaṃ ।
punaśca varuṇāntaṃ vai niṣedhaṃ ca tribiṃduyuk ।।

tṛtīyādyaṃ punardatvā hayaṃ muśalasaṃsthitaṃ ।
magniveśmāṅkitaṃ kṛtvā kuṇḍalaṃ paṃcamasthitaṃ ।।

mahāsṛṣṭiyutaṃ kāryaṃ vidyeyaṃ ṣoḍaśākṣarī ।
atidīptatarā ghorā jagadākarṣaṇeratā ।।

nāsyādhikā dīptatarā vidyate bhuvanodare ।
sahasrabāhubharaṇā sahasramukhabhīṣaṇā ।।

sukṛśā viśvarūpā sā ghorā paṃcānanā sthitā ।
(149b)
savipukarasaṃsthāpya pakṣiṇaścillarūpakā ।
evaṃ dhyātvā mahāraudrāṃ pūjayenna * * dale ।।

pratyagre parameśānīṃ sarvvavīropacārataḥ ।
cillā māṃsaṃ juhettatra * snehāktaṃ nareśvaraṃ ।
paścācca guhikā kāryā cillā saṃkūcyalūkhale ।।

bālakesarasamopetāṃ dhūpārthaṃ vīranāyakaḥ ।
sahasrā maṃ(tri)tāṃ kṛtvā paścāṅgachetpitṛgṛhaṃ ।।

madirānaṃdacetanyo darpitaḥ parameśvari ।
tatra vedi samāśritya mudrāṃ badhvā karaṃkiṇīṃ ।।

dhūpaṃ dāhatataṃ rātrau yāvatsādhakapuṃgavaḥ ।
tāvadāyānti yoginyaḥ cillā rūpā mahābalāḥ ।।

koṭiśo devadeveśi kṛtvā kālakalāravaṃ ।
tāsāmarghaṃ pradātavyaṃ bhitvā vāmāṅgamātmanaḥ ।।

tatastu varayaṃ(tyetā) naivanaṃ bhīṣayaṃti hi ।
abhiṣṭantu varaṃ prārthya tāsāṃ gacchet sadaiva ca ।।

yogeśvara vapurbhūtvā krīḍate yatra rocate ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ cillāsaṃ yogamelakaṃ ।।
kimanyatkathayiṣyāmi vada parvvatanaṃdini ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
melāpakālyāvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi jagadvaśyaṃ surārcita ।
yena dravantitānāryaḥ trailokye yā sthitā prabho ।।
tadvadasva mahādeva prasādānmama sāṃprataṃ ।। ।।
(150a)
śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇutvaṃ parameśāni jagadvaśyaṃ mahābalaṃ ।
yena vai kṣobhamāyānti trijagadyoṣitaḥ sadā ।।

ghorāpaṃcaśarākālī tāṃ śṛṇuṣva samāsataḥ ।
śubhaṃ bhūbhāgamāśritya mātṛkāṃ tatra vai * * ।।

proddharetparamāṃ kālīṃ jagadvidrāvaṇaṃ śubhāṃ ।
cāpādyaṃ ca dvidhoddhṛtya biṃduyuktaṃ mahābalaṃ ।।

evamaṃkuśasaṃyuktamaparaṃ mohanāvṛtaṃ ।
kaṃdalaṃ śakrasaṃrūḍhaṃ tribiṃduṃ biṃdubhūṣitaṃ ।।

kuṇḍalāntaṃ śakrasaṃsthaṃ mahāpaṃcakasaṃyutaṃ ।
paṃcapaṃcakasaṃyuktāmamṛtaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃyutaṃ ।।

paṃcā(kṣara)samākhyātā jagadrāvaṇatatparā ।
eṣā sā tava deveśi nitya klinnā tu kālikā ।।

ākhyātā paramārthena mahāvidrāvaṇī parā ।
mahāvaśyakarī devī proddhṛtā ca yathārthataḥ ।।

nāsyāstulyā prapaśyāmi vidyāpīṭhe mahābale ।
drāvaṇī suranārīṇāṃ mohanī tridaśeṣvapi ।।

trikoṇapadmamadhyasthāṃ yajedenāṃ kṛśodarī ।
mahāvibhavasaṃbhāraiḥ surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

sarvvaraktopacāreṇa vīrācāraiśca suvrate ।
dhyāyettrikoṇamadhyasthāṃ lākṣāruṇasamaprabhāṃ ।।

sukṛśāmekavadanāṃ netratretāgnisaṃnibhāṃ ।
pāśāṃkuśadharāṃ raudrāṃ śaracāpakarodyatāṃ ।।

mohaṇaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ caiva drāvaṇaṃ kledanantathā ।
kṣobhaṇaṃ paṃca ityete śarāḥ saṃdhārayatkare ।।

cintanīyā jagallakṣmī nityākālī maholvaṇā ।
(150b)
evaṃ dhyātvā prapūjyaivaṃ kāmadeva samo bhavet ।
kṣobhayedakhilaṃ viśvaṃ darśanādeva sādhakaḥ ।।

naināṃ smaredgurujano emamekadācit,
saṃkṣobhinī tribhuvane kalalāṃsabhūtā ।
yasyā kṣubhaṃtya surāsurapannagendra,
kanyā smarānalamadajvaravihvalāṃgyaḥ ।।

sravanti retoghajalapravāhaṃ,
raktādikaṃ darśanano sadaiva ।
dhāvanti vepanti galanti sarvvāḥ,
striyosya samyaksmaraṇena bhadre ।।

tasmātsadākṣobhakarī mahogrā,
smareta sā parvatakaṃdarasthā ।
kāmāraṇāmapyagataḥ sukāmī,
kāmeśvarāṃ kāmakulāntarasthaḥ ।।

kāmānanāṃ kāmakaletvanantāṃ,
kāmeśvaraḥ kṣobhakṛdeva nityaṃ ।
vicitra citrāṃvaramadhyavarttī,
svānaṃdadhāmni pravijṛṃbhamānaḥ ।।

prakāśakoṭī kulakālikasthaḥ,
saṃplāvayetsarvvajagannarendraḥ ।
ṣaṭkona cakrāntarasūryabimba,
bhāsāsphuradraśmisahasracitte ।।

kāmaraṇī maṃtrakulāraṇī,
saṃkledanī kālirūpā mahogrā ।
gītānubhāvādapi mattagaṃdha,
saṃkocanāllavate bhīmaveśā ।।

śāktaṃ samāveśamatośca maṃtraṃ prabhāvādapi sarvvakārī ।।

evaṃ vidrāvaṇī nāma tavoktā parameśvari ।
mahārahasya saṃyuktā tantrarāje jayadrathe ।।
vada kiṃ vādhunā vakṣye sarvvavīrendravaṃdite ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
nityākālīvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।
(151a)
śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi kaliduḥsvapnanāśanī ।
kālī kṣobhakarī raudrāṃ vadatāṃ parameśvara ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi yadarthaṃ coditastvayā ।
mahākālī hanakālī mahākṣobhaharī tathā ।।

mahānādaṃ vahnirūḍhaṃ kālānalagataṃ punaḥ ।
tripade taṃ samāyuktaṃ biṃdunādasamanvitaṃ ।।

piṃḍametanmahābhāge trailokyatrāṇatatparaṃ ।
vāhnaṃ paṃcamasaṃrūḍhaṃ cāpādyaṃ vahnisaṃsthitaṃ ।।

aṃkuśenāhataṃ mūrddhnā hayaṃ bindutrayāṃkitaṃ ।
bhīmaśaṃkunirudvaiṣā paṃcārṇāṃ parameśvarī ।।

śubhrābhraharahā indu haraśaṃkhaṃ tuṣāravat ।
cintayet parameśānīṃ śuklapadmāsanasthitāṃ ।।

caturbhujāmekavaktrāṃ netratritayabhāsurāṃ ।
pāśāṃkuśadharī raudrāṃ caṃdrārkakaramaṃḍitāṃ ।।

sarvvasaṃpatsudhāsāravarṣaṇīṃ paramāṃ kalāṃ ।
pūjayet kalaśāntasthā jagadārpyayanodyatāṃ ।।

sarvvābharaṇasaṃyuktāṃ sarvvādhārāṃ mahābalāṃ ।
saṃpūjyātha śataṃ maṃtryaṃ kalaśaṃ kṣīrasaṃyutaṃ ।।

etena cābhiṣiṃceta ātmānaṃ parameśvari ।
tenābhiṣiktamātrasya jāyante sarvvasaṃpadaḥ ।।

mahāvyādhipraśamanaṃ mahāmohavināśanaṃ ।
kaliduḥsvapnamathanaṃ rājopadravanāśanaṃ ।।

garalūlādivyādhīnāṃ dhvaṃsanaṃ cābhiṣecanaṃ ।
sarvvāpamṛtyumathanaṃ grahonmādavināśanaṃ ।।

mudraṇonmudraṇaṃ dhyeyamabhiṣekaṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।
(151b)
anyasyāpi mahābhāge sarvvasaṃpatkaraṃ smṛtaṃ ।।

abhiṣekaṃ kṣobhaharaṃ sarvvasaubhāgyavarddhanaṃ ।
eṣā devī sarvvasiddhiḥ puroktā sādhayiṣyati ।।

lakṣajāpena deveśi tannāsti yanna sādhayet ।
mokṣapradā samākhyātā tavaiṣā parameśvarī ।।
kimanyatkathayiṣyāmi kathayasva sulocane ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
śāntikālyāvidhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

idānīṃ brūhi devendra paramākṣaranirṇṇayaṃ ।
kathaṃ vā svātasya devī vācakosyāḥ kathaṃ hi saḥ ।। ।।

bhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi devyā hṛdayamuttamaṃ ।
paramārka kule kālī jagadgrasanagha(smarā) ।।

tā muddharedurāvāse saṃpūjya parameśvarī ।
sarvvāntamathakālāgnibiṃdunādi * śobhitāṃ ।।

hṛdayaṃ paramaṃ proktaṃ devadevyāḥ susiddhidaṃ ।
āṣacho nakulādīptaṃ dvādaśārkaprabhakṣakaṃ ।।

raśmināthaṃ samākhyātaṃ devyā hṛdayamuttamaṃ ।
eṣā sā paramā kālī sarvvabhakṣāparā kalā ।।

karaṃkiṇī ghoracaṃḍā kālī kālakṣayaṃkarī ।
suśuṣkā koṭarākṣī sā netratretāgnibhāsurā ।।

kapālakhaṭvāṃgadharā naramuṃḍopaśobhitā ।
mahāpadmakaravyagrā trailokyagrāsaghasmarā ।।

mahāpretasamārūḍhā ekavīrā maholvaṇā ।
jagadārtti praśamanī madhyamaprāṇavāhinī ।।
(152a)
nirācārā nirvibhāgā cidrūpā paramā kalā ।
sarvvasiddhikarī devī bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।

yajedenāṃ smaredenāṃ japedenāṃ sadā priye ।
na tadvastvasti deveśi tatvasaṃtānavartmani ।।

anayā yanna siddhyeta japamātrān maheśvari ।
vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣamāraṇoccāṭanādikaṃ ।।

sambhaṃstobhaṃ tathā kṣobhaṃ mohanaṃ kīlanaṃ tathā ।
viṣanirnāśanaṃ yacca nāga vidrāvaṇaṃ mahat ।।

nidhibaṃdhaprasenā ca vetālotthāpanaṃ paraṃ ।
mahākiṃkarajā siddhiścāravī siddhiruttamā ।।

pātālākāśagā siddhiraṇimādiprasādhanaṃ ।
naṣṭacchāyā yakṣasiddhiśceṭakānāṃ ca sādhanaṃ ।।

preṣakā bhūtasiddhiśca haṭhamelāpakādikaṃ ।
raktakṛṣṇopacāraiśca surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

kāmāraṇikulāntasthā tāṃ tatraiva yā jaṃtava ।
atha bhakṣaṇayuktyā vā mahāmārttaṇḍabhakṣaṇī ।।

trayodaśātmasaṃhārī śuṣkā paramabhairavī ।
cidacitparatau yā sā sā kalāsūryabhakṣaṇī ।।

tayā saṃbhakṣya sakalaṃ jagadetaccarācaraṃ ।
bhakṣaṇātsā tato bhūtvā bhakṣābhakṣe * bhakṣavat ।।

sarvvabhakṣakrameṇaiva sarvvagrā ca bhaviṣyati ।
atha yācitabhikṣāśī devyā hṛdayamuttamaṃ ।।

saṃsādhayedvīravaro yathā devi tathā śṛṇu ।
proddhareḍḍaṃbaraṃ pūrvvaṃ sarvvāntaṃ vahnisaṃsthitaṃ ।।

mohanī biṃdusahitaṃ tadva * dviprakīrttitaṃ ।
sarvvapūrvvaṃ punardatvā cāṃdraṃ kevalamuddharet ।।
(152b)
ṣaṣṭhādyaṃ ca tathā caṃdraṃ kālādyaṃ ca tribiṃdukaṃ ।
sarvvāntamaṃ kuśākrāntaṃ biṃdunā samalaṃkṛtaṃ ।।

cāpādyamagniveśmāṅkaṃ nādaṃ trisvaraśobhitaṃ ।
yonipūrvvaṃ sṛṣṭiyutaṃ vidyeyaṃ dvādaśākṣarā ।।

anayā mantrayetpātraṃ grāmāntavasatiḥ sadā ।
śatadhā mantritaṃ kṛtvā tannāstiryanna karṣayet ।।

pūrvvasevārthamasyā vai japedayutameva hi ।
tataḥ prabhṛti tasyecchāściñcitaṃ bhojanambhavet ।।

deva devyāḥ prasādena matistvakaluṣā yadi ।
evamādau piṃḍayātrāṃ sādhyakarmaṇi sādhayet ।।

taṃtroktāni sureśāni anya taṃtrotthitānyapi ।
anena vidhinā yoge sādhayetparameśvarīṃ ।।

yā sādhayati brahmāṇḍaṃ susiddhā sarvvagā śivā ।
madhyādhamottamākhyāśca līlayā sādhayiṣyati ।।

mahāpāpaughadalanī bhairavī vigatā mayā ।
citparā parame dhāmni vijṛṃbhati sadā śivā ।।

ghorāmaṃtharavīryā sā sarvvakāmārthasiddhidā ।
netratraye ca deveśī jagadvaśyakarī smṛtā ।।
etattava samākhyātaṃ kimanyatparipṛcchati ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
paramākṣara devyākalpaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

netratrayāṃkitā kālī jagadvaśyaikasaṃsthitā ।
tāmahaṃ śrotumicchāmi prasādānme vada prabhoḥ ।। ।।
(153a)
śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi yathāhaṃ coditastvayā ।
mama netra trayasthā sā netrakālī jagadvaśet ।।

caṃdrārkavahnivakrāntaḥ krīḍanāvaśavasthitā ।
amṛtaṃ vahnisaṃrūḍhaṃ sṛṣṭiyuktaṃ dvidhā kuru ।।

ābhyāṃ madhye ḍaṃbarākhyaṃ jaganmohakaraṃ paraṃ ।
tryakṣarā te samākhyātā netrakālī jagadvaśe ।।

ciccidviśrāmadhāmānta yojanātparameśvarī ।
jagadvaśyakarī proktā nyāsamātrā na saṃśayaḥ ।।

dhāmatraya yathārūḍhā sphuradraśmiśatākulā ।
parāraśmisamākṣepādvaśayet sacarācaraṃ ।।

tridhā tristhānayogena tritridhāgni tridhāditā ।
tridhā karṣakarī tristhā cidvasyaṃdetha kaṃdataḥ ।।

haṃsa haṃsarahārūḍhāṃ haṃsamākramya haṃsavat ।।
netrahaṃsāntare haṃsaṃ tadvatsaṃyojya haṃsabhāk ।।

tadbhogī yo jagadvaśyaḥ tasyādyāntanumadhyame ।
evaṃ yogivarārūḍho vaśayet sacarācaraṃ ।।

atha yogavihīnāya tasyāpi vidhirucyate ।
dhyāyedātmānamatulaṃ devīrūpamataṃdritaṃ ।।

sā ca devī dravallākṣā siṃdūrāruṇavarcasā ।
susaṃpūrṇṇā caturvvaktrā nayanadvādaśāvṛtā ।।

bhujāṣṭaka susaṃnaddhā sarvvābharaṇabhūṣitā ।
kapālakhaṭvāṅgadharā pāśāṃkuśadhanuryutā ।।

śara(kha)ḍgasphoṭakāṃkā viśvarūpātha sā smaret ।
mahāpretasamārūḍhāṃ devadevīṃ vicintayet ।।
(153b)
evaṃ dhyātvā nyaseddehe prāṇāpāna pravāhavat ।
paścānmaṃḍalagā yājyā surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

juhuyātkaravīrāṇi devyāḥ saṃtarpaṇāya vai ।
paścājjapeta ayutaṃ hutvā siddhimavāpyate ।।

evaṃ siddhā netragatā vaśayetsacarācaraṃ ।
ālokanena śubhage nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

dhyāyennirīkṣate yaṃ yaṃ taṃ dāsaṃ ca kariṣyati ।
hantuṃ samāgataṃ śatruṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃmohayet kṣaṇāt ।।

bhogamokṣa pradāseyaṃ jagat kṣobhakarī parā ।
trailokyatrāṇakaraṇī mahāsaubhāgyadāyikā ।।

vidhānavilasiddhyādikṣipraṃ saṃsādhayiṣyati ।
yathā nidhīśvarī devī tadvadeṣā prakīrttitā ।।

evaṃ tava samākhyātā netrakālī susiddhidā ।
kimanyaṃ tava vakṣyāmi vada vīrendravaṃdite ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
netrakālyāvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

kāsau nidhīśvarī nāma kālī ghoraparākramā ।
sarvvā siddhipradā devi kathaṃ nidhiprasādhanī ।।

sarahasyāṃ vada vibho niḥśeṣaṃ kiṃdhi saṃśayaḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu vadiṣyāmi yattvayā codito hyahaṃ ।
nāmnā kuberajananī śṛṇu kālī mahābalā ।।

subhagaṃ deśamāsādya pūjayenmantramātaraṃ ।
tataḥ samuddharedvidyāṃ caturviṃśārṇṇaśobhitāṃ ।।
(154a)
triguṇaṃ pū(rṇṇa)muddhṛtya saśaraṃ cāpamuddharet ।।

kūpavaktraṃ tataścāpaṃ trayobiṃduvibhūṣitaṃ ।
mohanī biṃdusaṃdīptā ghorā kālāgnisannibhā ।।

tribiṃdukhacitaṃ mūrddhnā niṣedhaṃ dāpayetpunaḥ ।
cāpaṃ māyā samāyuktaṃ varuṇasthaṃ tadārimaṃ ।।

tribiṃdubhūṣitaṃ bāhuṃ karttanaṃ paṃcasaṃsthitaṃ ।
varuṇaṃ vahniveśmāṅgaṃ kevalaṃ bāhumuddharet ।।

dvijihvamuddharetpaścānniṣedhaṃ ca dvidhā kuru ।
agnimaṃtrisvarākrāntaṃ cāpādyaṃ cāṃkuśāhataṃ ।।

punarbāhuṃ kevalinaṃ vāyuṃ kevalamuddharet ।
ṣaṣṭhādyaṃ vahnisaṃrūḍhaṃ dāruṇākrānta eva hi ।।

taṃ punarvahninā hīnaṃ samīre * valaṃ punaḥ ।
bhīmaśaṃkuṃ tridhā paścāddātavyaṃ * * suṃdari ।।

evaṃ nidhīśvarī devī vyākhātāṃ paramārthataḥ ।
kuberajananī kālī nidhānākarṣaṇī parā ।।

caturviṃśativarṇṇeyaṃ ghorarūpāṃ durāsadāṃ ।
kaṃdacakroditāṃ bhīmāṃ trailokyagrāsaghasmarāṃ ।।

sphuratkalpāntakālāgnikoṭikoṭyāyutaprabhāṃ ।
ghoradaṃṣṭrāvini(ṣṭhyū)ta bhīmakalpāntapāvakāṃ ।।

dravatkanakaphenormi budbudāvarttalocanāṃ ।
vajraśūcī niśātogra śiroruha bhayānakāṃ ।।

kālāliśaradākāśa śrīkaṃṭhagalasaṃnibhāṃ ।
śukrāntasnāyubhībhatsa rūpāṃ gho * dbhaṭānanāṃ ।।

brahmāṇḍaghaṭṭanoddānā phūtkāraravabhairavāṃ ।
pāśāṃkuśamahāpātrakhaṭvāṃkakarabhāsurāṃ ।।

gadāvajramahāmuṇḍanṛpa(dyā)tapāṇinīṃ ।
(154b)
kuberaskaṃdhavasitāṃ nidhānavṛṃdamadhyagāṃ ।।

mahāratnakadaṃvātavirājitaśarīriṇāṃ ।
karṣayaṃtī nidhānāni nāgalokagatānyapi ।।

dhyātvaivaṃ pūjyavibhavairvīrācāreṇa suṃdari ।
juhuyānmudrayā yuktaṃ pāśāṃkuśavibhūṣitaḥ ।।

mahākṣmā japalādīni tryakṣāni parameśvari ।
paścādgatvā girivaraṃ paṃcalakṣāṃ japedyadi ।।

tadā khecaratāṃ yānti devadevyā prasādataḥ ।
lakṣatrayeṇa subhage madhyamāḥ siddhayokhilāḥ ।।

siddhyante nātra saṃdeho vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
lakṣajāpena subhage nidhānādhipatirbhavet ।।

gatvā nidhāna saṃsthānaṃ sarṣapāṃ tatra cāpayet ।
kṣīraśiktāmaṇiśataṃ japtvā pātrasthitāṃ vapet ।।

yathā pādairasaṃspṛṣṭā bhavatyeva sumadhyame ।
evaṃ su(ptvā)valiṃ tatra kṣeptavyaṃ mīnamiśritaṃ ।।

tataḥ prabhātasamaye saptāhādvā samāgamaṃ ।
bhajaṃte sarṣapā yatra tatra cittaṃ vinirdiśet ।।

ratnasaṃcayato liṃgaṃ pūrṇṇacaṃdraṃ mahānidhau ।
kanakahyarddhacaṃdraṃ syātpadmaṃ rajabhasaṃcayaṃ ।।

cakrākāraṃ bhavennāmnāṃ madhye chidrāvile smṛtā ।
evaṃ lakṣaṇato jñātvā paścātsādhanamārabhet ।।

digbaṃdhamatrakurvvīta bhasmasarṣapamaṃtraṇaiḥ ।
kāṃḍaiśca rajubhirvvātha khādiraiḥ kīlakaiḥ śubhaiḥ ।।

tatastu pūjya deveśīṃ sarvvavīropacārataḥ ।
juhuyātsarṣapāstatra viṣasaiṃdhavasaṃyutāḥ ।।

dhyānāviṣṭastato bhūyo japedvidyāṃ mahābalāṃ ।
(155a)
sahasrajapayogena karṣayennidhisaṃcayaṃ ।
āpi vāsukitaḥ sākṣādrakṣitaṃ yadi śaṃkari ।।

satvā bhūmiṃ mantrabalādagrataḥ sādhakasya tu ।
samāgacchatyasaṃdehādvidyārājyaḥ prabhāvataḥ ।
nirviśaṃkena manasā gṛhītavyaṃ sadā budhaiḥ ।।

vibhīṣikā vinaśyanti kṣetrapāla bhayādikaṃ ।।
saṃcayotra na karttavyo nidhipūrṇāvasuṃdharā ।।

utpādya devadeveśi vibhajedbhogatatparaḥ ।
gurūṇāṃ sādhakendrāṇāṃ putrakeṣvapi bhairavi ।।

samayajñeṣu dātavyaṃ arthānāṃ nicayaṃ mahat ।
svayaṃ bhairavayāgāni karttavyāni dine dine ।।

devāgārāḥ prakarttavyā vāpyo kūpāḥ prapākaṭāḥ ।
dīnānātheṣu dātavyaṃ yācakeṣu ca sarvvadā ।।

sarvvakālaṃ prakarttavyaṃ nidhivādaṃ tu maṃtriṇā ।
kṣaṇamekaṃ nirudyogo naiva tiṣṭheta jātucit ।।

tasmādanityaḥ saṃsāro ghorākāraḥ sudāruṇaḥ ।
anityaṃ jīvitamidaṃ yauvanaṃ saṃpa(daśva)lā ।।

tasmādbhaktiḥ prakarttavyā śivasyāśivahāriṇaḥ ।
maṃtramārgeṣu bhairavyā * * * śca viśeṣataḥ ।।

adhruvaṃ sarvvamevedaṃ saṃsāraṃ trikalātmakaṃ ।
jarārogobhayagraste śokāyāsakadarthinaṃ ।।

duḥkhatrayāgnisaṃtaptaṃ vicārya tanumadhyame ।
sthiraṃ samāśrayenmārgaṃ mahāmāheśvaraṃ śubhaṃ ।।

sarvvamanyatyājyamadhruvajīvisati ।
dhruvaṃ samāśrayetmārgaṃ mātraṃ yogaṃ ca bhairavi ।।
evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ kimanyatparipṛcchasi ।। ।।
(155b)
।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
kuberajananyāvidhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

devyuvāca ।।

adhunā sārahṛdayaṃ śrotumicchāmi kāmadaṃ ।
sarvvasiddhipradaṃ sāraṃ bhogamokṣapradaṃ vada ।।

bhairava uvāca ।।

vakṣyāmyavasaraprāptaṃ hṛdayaṃ marddani smṛtaṃ ।
atighorāgramatulaṃ sarvvavīrairanuṣṭhitaṃ ।।

gatvā pitṛvanaṃ devi vīrācārarataḥ sudhīḥ ।
kaṃkālaghṛṣṭāṃ vasudhāṃ kṛtvā * * samuddharet ।।

dvijihvaṃ vahnipuṭitaṃ mohanī biṃdulāṃchitaṃ ।
evaṃ vīraṃ varṇṇavaraṃ devyā hṛdayamuttamaṃ ।।

hṛcchiraśca śikhā carma netrādyavayavojjhitaḥ ।
dhyāyet kaṃdotthitāṃ bhīmāṃ trailokyāntakaśātanīṃ ।।

trinetrāmeva *danāṃ bhujāṣṭakavibhūṣitāṃ ।
narāntrakṛtasragdāmāṃ halamālā vibhūṣitāṃ ।।

mahāpretāsanāṃ raudrāṃ kādyakhaṭvāṃgadhāriṇīṃ ।
vajrāṃkuśakaravyagrāṃ gajacarmadharīṃ śivāṃ ।।

narāntrapūritamukhīṃ kākakokilasannibhāṃ ।
kalpātamārutakṣudhvāṃ dvādaśārkayutaujasāṃ ।।

viśvarūpāmathadhyāyedyathābhimatarūpiṇīṃ ।
kulanāgāṣṭakalitāṃ sarvvasiddhikarīṃ parāṃ ।।

evaṃ dhyātvā yajedetāṃ ṣaṭkoṇabhuvanodare ।
sarvvavīropacāreṇa sūrāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

evaṃ saṃpūjya juhuyāduttamādhamamadhyamāṃ ।
(156a)
tato girivaraṃ gatvā japellakṣāyutaṃ priye ।।

pūrvvāktaṃ juhuyādravyaṃ tryaktaṃ sidhyarthameva hi ।
homāntādagnimadhyātsā samudeti svayaṃ śivā ।।

varadā sādhakendrāya yatheṣṭaphaladā bhavet ।
madhyasāmānyabhedena yadi sādhanamīhate ।।

tadānurūpajayataḥ sādhayenmanasīpsitaṃ ।
athavā dāhajanane śatrūṇāṃ yojayedyadi ।।

tadā pretarathārūḍhāṃ vidyāmaṃtrāpyanusmaret ।
ṣoḍhābāhuṃ samuddhṛtya paryantau dvau sabiṃdukau ।।

sāṃkuśau devadeveśi ṣaṣṭhādyaṃ bahusaṃyutaṃ ।
tadveśma śirasākrāntaṃ tadvatpaṃcādyamuddharet ।।

kiṃ tu vahnivihīnaṃ syādbhīmaśaṃkunirodhitaṃ ।
navākṣarā mahāvidyā jagaddahanatatparā ।।

yasya kruddhastu deveśi nāma saṃpūṭya vai japet ।
dāhena mārayettaṃ vai saptāhānnātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

nāma saṃlikhya sādhyasya viṣāṃgārā sṛjā priye ।
saṃtāpyabhūrjapatrasthaṃ * * * dāhayedbhṛśaṃ ।।

vidarbhyanāma sādhyasya cūlyodho vai śiveśayet ।
dāhajvarakaraṃ ghoraṃ śatrūṇāṃ kuṭilodarāṃ ।।

eṣaiva māraṇe yotsā mahāvidyā mahābalā ।
evaṃmantimakarmādau yojayet parameśvarī ।।

evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ tantrarājasya madhyataḥ ।
sārottamaṃ tu hṛdayaṃ mahopahṛdayānvitaṃ ।।
kimanyat kathayiṣyāmi vadacakracareśvari ।। ।।

(156b)
।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
hṛdayakālyāvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

devyuvāca ।। ।।

idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi yāgād manamanī parā ।
kālī kālānalaprakhyā kathayādya mama prabho ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

atha devi pravakṣyāmi kālī nāgāntakī parā ।
baṃdhanātsarvvanāgānāṃ mahāpātakanāśanī ।।

suśuddhabhūpradeśe tu proddharetparameśvari ।
bhairavaṃ vahnisaṃyuktaṃ mohanī biṃdulāṃcchitaṃ ।।

trikoṇamuddharetpaścātpaṃcamādyaṃ tu kevalaṃ ।
vahnitadveśmasaṃyuktaṃ niṣedhaṃ cāṃkuśāhataṃ ।।

gaṇeśaṃ kevalaṃ bhūyo cāpādyaṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ ।
puruṣaṃ kobalaṃ paścāddātavyaṃ surasuṃdari ।।

niṣedhaṃ trisvarākrāntaṃ kuṇḍalaṃ vahnimadhyagaṃ ।
sṛṣṭiyuktā mahāvidyā rudrārṇṇā parikīrttitā ।।

nāmnā ekatarā kālī kalpāntārkāyutaprabhā ।
trailokyatrāsakaraṇī bhūtabetālamarddanī ।।

sphuratsūryāyutābhāsā kalpāntānalavarccasāṃ ।
caturvvaktrāṃ dīptakeśāṃ ghoranetrāṃ bhayānakāṃ ।।

viśolvaṇamahānāgaphaṇaphūtkāraśobhitāṃ ।
kulanāgāṣṭakā pīḍāṃ gonāsaśatamaṇḍitāṃ ।।

putrahastāṃ tathā vajramahāṃkuśavirājitāṃ ।
pāśakhaḍgakaravyagrāṃ nāgabhakṣaṇatatparāṃ ।।

mahāpretāsanārūḍhāṃ viśvarūpāmathāpi vā ।
yathābhimatarūpāṃ vā dhyātvā samyak prapūjayet ।।
(157a)
maṃḍale sarvvakāmākhye puroktā vā sulakṣaṇā ।
nānādhūpopahāraiśca surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

saṃpūjya juhuyāttatra tryaktaṃ vākṣyājasaṃbhavet ।
evaṃ saṃtarpya vidhivattataḥ sādhanamārabhet ।।

yathā kāmānusāreṇa uttamādhamamadhyamaṃ ।
uttamenottamāṃ siddhiṃ madhyame madhyamāṃ bhavet ।।

kanīyasajapādeva siddhirnyūnā prasiddhyati ।
atha nāgālayaṃ gatvā viṣātamidamācaret ।।

vainavaṃ caṃturādāyaekaviṃśaḥ karottamaṃ ।
khādiraṃ vajramatulaṃ bhasmasarṣapameva ca ।।

evaṃ saṃgṛhya sambhāraṃ tataḥ sādhanamārabhet ।
sahasramaṃtritaṃ kṛtvā tīkṣṇaṃ khadirakīlakaṃ ।।

dikṣuṣṭhāsu khanettāni bhasmasarṣapameva hi ।
tatastu juhuyāttatra viṣamāsurikaṃ tathā ।।

kaṭutailaplutāṃ kṛtvā sahasaṃ cāṣṭasaṃmitaṃ ।
tatastu śuṣyate toyaṃ sāgarapratimaṃ hradaṃ ।।

yadi no śuṣyate kasmāccharānāśīviṣopamāṃ ।
śatamāvartya caikaukāṃ prakṣipetkuddhameva hi ।।

tadā jvalatya saṃdehādanantādiphaṇīśvarāḥ ।
tataḥ kṣepyaṃ mahāvajraṃ sahasrā mantritaṃ krudhā ।।

taṃ jvalantaṃ śiro gatvā nāgasyāste mahābalaṃ ।
tata āyātyasaubhītaḥ sabhṛtya valavāhanaḥ ।।

trāhitrāhiti jalpanta dagdhadagdheti cābruvaṃ ।
rasaṃ rasāyanaṃ khaḍgaṃ śilārocanapāduke ।।

upavītāṃjanā devi yadanyadrā samīhitaṃ ।
(157b)
taṃ grāhayettastasmānnāgakanyāthavā priye ।।

nīce hṛsvā tu taṃ nāma sarparūpaṃ ghaṭāntare ।
ajaleṣvapi deśeṣu sthāpayedaviśaṃkitaḥ ।।

ācaṃdrārka bhuvaṃ yāvattāvattiṣṭhatya yaṃtritaḥ ।
devadevyāḥ prabhāvena vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।।

nāgavidrāvaṇī hyeṣā lakṣajāpā na saṃśayaḥ ।
vicarāṃ kṣobhayetsarvvāṃ vilayaṃtrāṇi pātayet ।।

cūrṇṇayedadrisaṃghātāṃ mahāpātakasaṃcayāt ।
smaraṇāddevadeveśī sarvvakarmakarī smṛtā ।।
trilakṣācakravarttitvaṃ sādhayejjapayogataḥ ।।

evaṃ tāvagirije vyākhyātaṃ nāgabaṃdhanaṃ paramaṃ ।
anyadbrūhi kimadhunā vakṣyā tava sarvvage mahātaṃtre ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
ekatarāvidhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā brūhi bhagavan vāgvidhānaṃ mahābalaṃ ।
vāksiddhe nāparāsiddhiryasmādiha maheśvaraṃ ।।
tasmāttāṃ vada viśveśa prasādānmama sāṃprataṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

atha vakṣyāmi sarvvajña vāgeśī kāliravyayā ।
yayoccāritamātrāyā sākṣādvāgeśvaro bhavet ।।

śucau deśe prapūjyādau mātrikāṃ mantravigrahāṃ ।
tataḥ samuddharedvidyāṃ vāgvidhānārthakārikāṃ ।।

kāmeśvarīṃ vahnirūḍhāṃ mohanī viṃdulācchitaṃ ।
(158a)
vāgbhavaṃ prathamaṃ hyetatsarvvavācaḥ pravarttanaṃ ।।

varuṇaṃ kevalaṃ bhūyaścāpādyaṃ kevalaṃ tathā ।
dvidhā padavaraṃ kāryaṃ varuṇaṃ cāṃkuśāhataṃ ।।

gaṇendraṃ varuṇārūḍhaṃ vāruṇākrāntamastakaṃ ।
cāpādyaṃ trisvarākrāntaṃ niṣedhaṃ tena bhūṣitaṃ ।।

tejendrabhāryayā yuktā daśaikārṇā mahābalā ।
vidyā sarvvottamā jñeyā * * mahātamonudā ।।

dhyāyecchubhābhrahārendu sphurat sphāṭikanirmalāṃ ।
kaṃdamaṃdirasattejo vivarttāvarttavigrahāṃ ।।

vākcatuṣṭayajātsyaṃdasphūtajñātaughasantatiṃ ।
madhyamārgoditajyotsnā prakāśitabhavā vaṭāt ।।

viśālavaktratābhyantaścitrāṃ citrārthadāyikāṃ ।
kālayaṃtī mahājñānarāśimatyantanirmalā ।।

sphuratsaṃpuṭasaṃghaṭṭamadhyabhramarikāṃ kalāṃ ।
raśmicakrakṛtāhārā vāntasadbhaśmimālitāṃ ।।

helādolāvihārāntakārmukāṣṭakavigrahāṃ ।
cidviśrāmalayāṃtasthāṃ cittasaṃbodhalakṣaṇāt ।।

hṛtpadmakarṇikārūḍhapravāhāhāravāsinīṃ ।
tatkeśarasarodvāmamattāmāmiṣalaṃpaṭāṃ ।।

catuṣkalā kalākoṭi biṃduvigraharūpiṇīṃ ।
trikoṇalaṃbikā madhya gajanāsā samasthitiṃ ।।

kalāṣoḍaśacakrāmbhakrāntasusvaramaṃḍalāt ।
śivacaṃdrasudhāsāradhārādurmi na maṃtharīṃ ।।

apakṣāpakṣaviśrāmavicitravarasārasīṃ ।
caturddaśamahādvārasphurat sphuritavigrahāṃ ।।
(158b)
ghorāntasanasatpadmasamā satrasvarūpiṇīṃ ।
bhavārṇavamahāvīci cakrākārai * vāḍavāṃ ।।

svasvarūpasvarūpotthā vṛttirājitacitkalāṃ ।
dvikubjadahanāsīnamāyāvibhramavaindavāt ।।

trinetraikāmukhe hṛtkāṃ sūdhā saṃplāvitasvarāṃ ।
caturbhujasamākrāntaṃ padmapuṣkakādikāṃ ।।

khaṭvāṃgacāmarāpīḍavirājitaśarīriṇīṃ ।
mahāmukuṭasadratnakuṃḍalodbhāsitānanāṃ ।।

sphuranmaṃjīrakeyūramekhalādhāmamaṃḍitāṃ ।
mahāpretasamārūḍhā śarīrāṃ vyaktavigrahāṃ ।।

evamādeśato dhyātumāviśet svasvarūpagāṃ ।
vāgeśī kāliratulā vāguktyā sakarī nṛṇāṃ ।।

mahatāpi prayatnena pūjya sanmaṃḍalāntare ।
saṃtarpyottamahomena tataḥ siddhyati nānyathā ।।

prāksvarūpasamāveśavaśādānena māpnuyāt ।
yenāveśo na vāgrūpamullāsamupalabhyate ।।

tallābhādanuṣaṃginyaḥ siddhayastatra kīrttitāḥ ।
bhavantyeva hi vīrendraistabhyastasvasvarūpiṇi ।।

sarvvajñatvaṃ ca kartṛtvaṃ asyākṛtrimamiṣyate ।
vāṇīnuṣaṃgaphalapravarttanyasya suṃdarā ।।

vākyālaṃkārasatkarma pramāṇapadamaṃtharā ।
avicchinnapravāhena gaṃgā śrotasi bhairavi ।।

saubhāgyamatulaṃ cāsya jāyate sarvvadurlabhaṃ ।
darśanādvidravastasya vākpateryaśasā priye ।।

vādate kṣitimārgasthā devāḥ siddhāśca pannagāḥ ।
(159a)
dravanti nāryaḥ sahasāstasya saṃdarśanena vai ।।

kāmadeva vapuḥ sākṣādṛśyate golakāntare ।
tannāsti yatra deveśyāḥ svarūpālaṃbane sati ।।
siddhimeti tadā devi satyametadbravīmi te ।

etattavādya kathitaṃ surasuṃdarīṇāṃ,
saṃmohane pravarataṃtravarasya madhye ।
kiṃ cādhunā tava dadāmi surendravaṃdye,
samyagvadāsu mama saddhṛdayāntara(stu) ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
vāgvidhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

devadeva śaśāṅkāṅkatriśūla(ka)rapāṇaye ।
kathayasva mameśatvaṃ duṣṭagrahavimardanaṃ ।।

sarvvabhūtapraśamanaṃ vidhānamiti kautukaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu vīrendranamite vidyāṃ ghoraparākramāṃ ।
(ne)traroṣaṇikā nāmnī kālī kālānalaprabhā ।।

ādau haṃsaṃ samuddhṛtya vahnāṃkuśasabiṃdukā ।
caṃdreti ca padaṃ deyaṃ caṃḍaroṣaśivai padaṃ ।।

bhīmaśabdamathoddhṛtya padaṃ bhīṣaṇi(saṃ)jñakaṃ ।
grahānāveśayapadaṃ madīśabdaṃ dvidhā kuru ।।

hananāśaya vai vītsā cūrṇayeti padaṃ dvidhā ।
bhīmaśaṃkuniruddheyaṃ vidyā ghoraparākramā ।।

catvāriṃśati varṇeyaṃ brahmāṇḍa(grā)saghasmarā ।
samagraduṣṭavṛṃdānāṃ dahanodyatarūpiṇī ।।

kṣayamārttaṇḍakālāgnivṛddhasanti bharūpikā ।
ghorakoṭarasannetra vāntakalpaṃtapāvakā ।।

ghorabhrūkuṭihuṃ(kāra)trāsita tridaśāśayā ।
(159b)
ghaṃṭāḍamarunirghoṣa * * jaṭīkṛta trayā ।।

khaṭvāṃgaśūlamusalapāśakhaḍgakarodyatāṃ ।
mahāmuṃḍakṛtārīḍapakṣasauvarṇaśekharā ।।

kṛśagātrāṃ mahodagrāṃ saṭāpaṭalabhairavāṃ ।
śaradgaganakā kālikajjalādrinibhopamāṃ ।।

ghoranṛsiṃhasaṃrūḍhaśarīrādbhutavikramāṃ ।
nāgendraghoragonāsaphaṇimālā virājitāṃ ।।

mahāmātaṃgasaṃkṛtviśoṇitāḍyaṃ varottamāṃ ।
bhūtabetālayakṣaughaduṣṭagrahavināśanīṃ ।।

dhyātvā yajeta deveśīṃ maṃḍale paṃkajopari ।
sarvvavīropacāreṇa juhuyāduttamādhikāṃ ।।

tataḥ prabhṛti sā devī sarvvakarmakarī smṛtā ।
darśanādbhūtayakṣaughamāveśayati bhairavī ।।

apasmārādayaḥ sarvve nāśayedapamārjanāt ।
trividhaṃ yadviṣaṃ ghoraṃ nāśayettamamātrataḥ ।।

vilayaṃtrapramathanī sāgarodakaśoṣaṇī ।
sarvvasiddhipradā jñeyā deva devī karaṃkiṇī ।।

mahāpāpapraśamanī mahābhāgyā(ca)rohiṇī ।
mahodagrā mahāviccā mahāsaṃhārabhairavī ।।

mahābhogapradā jñeyā mokṣadā parameśvarī ।
pāṭhāsiddhipradā jñeyā saṃsāre yā pratiṣṭhitā ।।
evaṃ tava samākhyātā kimanyatparipṛcchati ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
caṃḍaroṣaṇyāṃ vidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।
(160a)
śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrotumicchāmi bhagavan rahasyamidamadbhutaṃ ।
yathā jagat kṣobhaṇī sā kālīkāla(praya)pradā ।।
kṣobhayedvā yathā samyaktathā me brūhi bhairava ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

pravakṣyāmi samāsena śṛṇu vīrendravaṃdite ।
asmāttaṃtrottamādūrddhvaṃ na kvacit pratipāditaṃ ।।

vidhānamidamatyugraṃ jagatsaṃkṣobhakārakaṃ ।
praṇavaṃ proddharetpūrvvaṃ namaśabdaṃ tataḥ paraṃ ।।

bhairaveśvarisaṃyojya kālaśabdamataḥ paraṃ ।
saṃkarṣaṇipadaṃ yojya pare ḍāmari vai padaṃ ।।

vikaṭe padamuddhṛtya kṣobhayeti padadvidhā ।
cālayeti dvidhā yojya utpādaya padaṃ dvidhā ।।

gaṇendraṃ ca tridhā deyaṃ paryantaṃ ca visarginaṃ ।
tripaṃcāśākṣarāseyaṃ vidyā ghoraparākramā ।।

sarvvasaṃpatpradāśremā jagatkṣobhakarī parā ।
mohanī śatrusainyānāṃ kṣobhaṇī bhuvanodare ।।

bhaṃjanī vilayantrāṇāṃ nāśanī suravidviṣāṃ ।
dhyāyenmahāśaktipadā vidyudutpādaniḥsvanā ।।

sauṣumnā mārgavāheṇa sphuliṃgānekasaṃkulā ।
siṃdūrapuṃjasaṃkāśāṃ dravallākṣārasopamā ।।

madirāpūrasadṛśāṃ pūrayaṃtī jagatsmaret ।
sarvvaṃ jagadvā japitvā punaḥ (svā)hṛdayāya ca ।।

praviṣṭāṃ cintayitvā tāṃ ekībhāvagatāṃ smaret ।
evamekāntamāsīno deva pūjanatatparaḥ ।।

saptāhaṃ dhyānayogena kṣobhayetpurapattanaṃ ।
trisaptāhānmahādevi maṃḍalaṃ kṣobhayiṣyati ।।
(160b)
māsamātreṇa subhage janāstatra nivāsinaḥ ।
ātmanā ca dhanairdāraiḥ pūjayanti narottamaṃ ।।

necchantamapi tasyaiva balādāḍhaukayaṃti te ।
ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena pṛthivī kṣobhayiṣyati ।।

maṃḍalīkanarendrāṇāṃ kiṃ kurvvāṇo vidheyatāṃ ।
bhavatyavāsya niyatasādhakendrasya suvrate ।।

yathā saṃnihitā yoge rāhugraste divākare ।
janāstīrthaṃ samāyānti tacca deva hi sādhake ।।

ātmadā dhanadāḥ sarvve dāradāḥ preṣyakāriṇaḥ ।
bhavatyeva na saṃdeho mahāvidyā prabhāvataḥ ।।

yathābhimatarūpaiḥ sā dhyātavyā parameśvarī ।
trailokyatrāsaharaṇī trailokyavaśakāriṇī ।।

trailokyātaṃ kaharaṇī tannāsti yanna sādhayet ।
tannāsti yanna kurute mahādevī karaṃkiṇī ।।

bhogamokṣapradā jñeyā mahāmoha pranāśinī ।
pāṭhātsiddhipradā khyātā tantresmintantranāyakī ।।
evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ kimanyatkathayāmi te ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
jagatkṣobhakālyāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā ghaṭṭanaṃ brūhi pyevai ghaṭṭayate jagat ।
tamākhyā hi kramaṃ kālyā mama tripurasūdana ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

adhunā kathayāmyādya ghaṭṭanaṃ viśvaghaṭṭanaṃ ।
yena mārayate kṛtsnaṃ jagadetaccarācaraṃ ।।
(161a)
gatvā harālayaṃ ghore pūjyā vidyāṃ samuddharet ।
bhairavaṃ pūrvvamuddhṛtya vahnyārūḍhaṃ * sṛṣṭikaṃ ।।

tadvadrāvaṃ mahābījaṃ trailokyaṃ grāsaghasmaraṃ ।
triśūlaṃ ṣaṣṭhasaṃrūḍhaṃ bāhumaśvasthitaṃ tataḥ ।।

samīraṇaṃ tato deyaṃ padametattrirakṣaraṃ ।
ghoraśūlaṃ punardeyaṃ caṃdraścāgasthitaṃ punaḥ ।।

punaḥ samīraṇaṃ deyaṃ kālamaṃkuśabheditaṃ ।
kevalaṃ vāhnamuddhṛtya vāyavyaṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ ।।

mardanaṃ vahnisaṃrūḍhaṃ akuśākrāntamastakaṃ ।
biṃduyuktaṃ mahāvarṇṇaṃ viśvaghaṭṭanatatparaṃ ।।

gaṇeśādyaṃ vahnirūḍhaṃ mohanī sṛṣṭibhūṣitaṃ ।
haṃsaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ samārūḍhaṃ tripadena samāyutaṃ ।।

biṃdunādasamāyuktaṃ bhīmaśaṃku nirodhitaṃ ।
eṣā vidyā mahāghorā jaganmāraṇatatparā ।।

kṛṣṇajīmūtasaṃkāśāṃ śaradgagaṇasaṃnibhāṃ ।
jvalatkalpāntakālāgnikoṭikoṭyāyutaprabhāṃ ।।

jvālāhastāṃ kṛśāṃ rodrāṃ trinetrāṃ bhīmavikramāṃ ।
śūrpavajrakarāṃ ghorāṃ vidyutkoṭisamaprabhāṃ ।।

mahāmahiṣamārūḍhāṃ jagadgrasanalaṃpaṭāṃ ।
yajettrikoṇabhuvane raktakṛṣṇopacārataḥ ।।

juhuyātkaṭutailoktā sarpiṣā tanumadhyame ।
paścājjapeta deveśīṃ daśapaṃcākṣarāṃ śivāṃ ।।

lakṣamekaṃ mahābhāge pūrvvoktaṃ juhuyāttataḥ ।
tataḥ siddhyati sā devī raudrī bhīmaparākramāṃ ।।

smaraṇādvyadṛyetsarvvaṃ jagadetaccarācaraṃ ।
sādhyanāmayutā devī yadi samyakkaraṃkiṇī ।।
(161b)
japeta ghaṭṭayetsādhyaṃ puraṃdarasamaṃ yadi ।
grāmaṃ vā saṃpuṭīkṛtya janavṛṃdasamākulāṃ ।।

japayogānmārayate pattanaṃ purameva vā ।
yena yena prayogena japedvidyāṃ mahābalāṃ ।।

tena tena prayogena ghaṭṭayejjagadadbhutaṃ ।
likhitā yantrayogena vidarbhya parameśvarīṃ ।।

ghaṭṭayetsādhyamevaiṣā mārayettridaśānapi ।
pūjya vā pūrvvavidhinā haraveśmāntarasthitāṃ ।।

tena nirmālya yogena mārayet sacarācaraṃ ।
evaṃ siddhipradā devī tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।

pūrvvakarmakarī jñeyā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।
anayā siddhayā devi nāsādhyaṃ vidyate bhuvi ।।

bhogamokṣapradā seyaṃ tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।
kimanyat kathayiṣyāmi vada vīrendravaṃdite ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
ghaṭṭanakālyāvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā brūhi bhagavansaṃsārārṇavatāraṇaṃ ।
dīkṣā vidhānamatulaṃ mahāmoha vināśanaṃ ।।

yena mokṣamavāśyanti mahāpātakinaḥ priye ।
kaṣṭakriyāvinirmuktaṃ mahājñānasamanvitaṃ ।।
taṃ vidhānaṃ samākhyāhi prasādātparameśvara ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge yattvayā paricoditaṃ ।
tantrasārataraṃ samyagvakṣyāmi tava sāṃprataṃ ।।
(162a)
nāmnā bhuvanamāleyaṃ kālikā parameśvarī ।
yasya śrotrapathe yāti sa mukto bhavabaṃdhanāt ।।

kimu vai jāpako hyasyā sadāśivasamo hyasau ।
tadā paścimaṃ janma * jñātavyaṃ vīrasuṃdari ।।

yadā tu likhitā sṛṣṭā vidyeyaṃ bhuvaneśvarī ।
smaraṇātsarvvataṃtreṣu dīkṣitosau bhaviṣyati ।।

tasyoddhāraṃ śṛṇuṣvedaṃ mahāpātakanāśanaṃ ।
ādāveva mahānādaṃ ṣaṣṭhabiṃdusamanvitaṃ ।।

kalpādyaṃ paṃcamāsīnaṃ varuṇaṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ ।
niṣedhaṃ kevalaṃ bhūyaḥ kālamaṃkuśasaṃyutaṃ ।।

śakraṃ trisvaramūrdhānaṃ tadvadeva niṣedhakaṃ ।
śānta śānta punarvāhnaṃ mohanī biṃdulāṃcchitaṃ ।।

aṣṭākṣarā samākhyātā vidyā bhuvanamālinī ।
liṃgotpāṭādikāṃ pāpāṃ smaraṇādeva nāśayet ।।

pāśarāśipradahate pudgalasya maheśvarī ।
yātā śrotapathenaiva pūjitā vā sumadhyame ।।
dhyānāthavā viśvarūpā mokṣadā vadhanāśanī ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

kasya pāśāḥ samākhyātā pudgalā kīdṛśo mataḥ ।
kathaṃ te baṃdhakāstasya chiṃdi me saṃśayaṃ vibho ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ya eṣa pudgalo devi sa ātmā paripacyate ।
tasya pāśāḥ samākhyātāstāmrakālikavat priye ।।

āvṛtaṃ cābhilāśena malena sahajena vai ।
āṇavaḥ sa samākhyātaḥ karmāsevamanādikaṃ ।।
(162b)
ekānonmohanī khyāto māyā sadasadātmikā ।
tat kṣobhātsaṃsmaratyeṣa paśutvenaiva pudgalaḥ ।।

evaṃ pāśatrayaṃ khyātaṃ saṃsārāṃkurakāraṇaṃ ।
anenaiva dvai baṃdhaśca muktomukta udāhṛtaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

mūrtto hyamūrttau vā deva yoyamātmā prabhāṣyate ।
mūrttau caturvirucyeta nityatvaṃ cāpyanādinā ।।

mūrtto na nityaḥ sarvvaḥ syādvṛkṣyāmyākāra eva saḥ ।
kṛtakatvādanāditvaṃ vināśitvādviruddhyate ।।

tasmānmūrttatvamevāsya pudgalasya na siddhyati ।
amūrttañca mahādevaṃ yadyātmāṇastvayocyate ।।

tadamūrttasya tasyaivaṃ kathaṃ pāśā tirohitā ।
etanme saṃśayaṃ deva yathā tadvaktumarhasi ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śaktipāśavaśādeṣā māyā kṣobhāccharīritā ।
aṇoreva samākhyātā te * devyupacaryate ।।

mūrttatvamātmanaḥ samyaṅmūrttatvādanumīyate ।
pāśitvaṃ tasya nāmāṇo dhūmādagniya(thaiva) hi ।।

pāśāstu kāraṇaṃ devi kṣobhakṛtparameśvaraḥ ।
kāryaṃ śarīramuddiṣṭaṃ ca yame tadvicāryatāṃ ।।

kāryakāraṇabhedena suprasiddhaṃ sadā sthitaṃ ।
śarīrasthaḥ sadā grāhyaḥ pramāṇairanureva hi ।।

śabdasparśastathārūparasogaṃdhamano gataiḥ ।
gaṃdhādīsaṃjñakairdevi cidrūpo mūrtta eva saḥ ।।

yasyātmā grāhako nasyācchabdādīn grāhayekataḥ ।
viṣayā vyavasāyaṃ vā kaḥ karoti tadā priye ।।
(163a)
saṃkalpamātramevātra nātmā yadi karoti kaḥ ।
athavā manyase subhruśarīrāvyatiriktatā ।।

ātmāno naiva tatsiddhaṃ śavadehādidarśanāt ।
vidyamāne śarīresmiṃ śavonāghrāti kiṃcana ।।

na śabdādikamevāsau grāhayetkāṣṭhatāṃ gataḥ ।
tasmādamūrttaḥ cidrūpaḥ siddho dehādivarjitaḥ ।।

nityatvamasya deveśi saṃsiddhaṃ mūrttivarjanāt ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ mūrttāmūrttavinirṇṇayaṃ ।। ।।

devyuvāca ।।

amūrttasya mahādeva kathaṃ pāśāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ ।
yaiḥ sadbhirvaddha ityevaṃ śṛśyate paśunāsvaca ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

anādisaṃsmṛtaścātmā * ścidrūpaḥ śvetātmakaḥ ।
saṃvidrūpaṃ tvamevāsya vyāpakatvamakhaṃḍitaḥ ।।

taṃ nirūddhaṃ sadā pāśaiḥ sahajāstasya kīrttitaḥ ।
cetanatvaṃ yathā cāsya susiddhaṃ bhrāntivarjitaṃ ।।

tatsaṃcintyāśasaṃyuktā samalā nirmalāni hi ।
boddhavyā śuddhaviddharmarūpeva parameśvari ।।

saṃvidgate malastasmāstasyāmūrttasya śītavat ।
amūrttāste mahāpāśāḥ paśorevaṃ vyavasthitāḥ ।।

taiḥ sadbhirbhava evāsya samasto bhogasaṃjñakaḥ ।
tena saṃsāritāvāsya mūrttatvamupacaryate ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

evaṃ ca dātmanaḥ siddhaṃ pāśitvaṃ parameśvara ।
śivasyāpi kathaṃ naiva yuktinabhrabravīhi me ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

anānirmalaḥ śuddhaḥ sarvvavyāpī niraṃjanaḥ ।
(163b)
śivasvarūpī cidrūpī citparaḥ parameśvaraḥ ।।

svarūpaḥ śuddhakarttā ca jñātā ca parameśvaraḥ ।
amūrttaḥ kālavatsiddhaḥ paśūnāṃ palasādhanaṃ ।।

citparā tasya yā śakti tatsāmarthyaṃ tadīyakaṃ ।
tenāsau śaktimān devaḥ śaktiśca paribhīṣyate ।।

tasyāḥ pātastriyā jñeyā hyaghoro ghorarūpakaḥ ।
tathā ghoratara(śvī)nyaḥ kāryamedāddhi bhidyate ।।

punaḥ prayāti devaśi kāryabhedādanantatāṃ ।
yasmādbhūṣṭhamānaistadanuvaktraṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।।

teṣāṃ pāśatirohatvaṃ nānākāraṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।
bahutvaṃ devadevasya śaktitaḥ parikīrttitaṃ ।।

āṇūnāṃ svasamānatvaṃ pradadāti maheśvaraḥ ।
kartṛtvaṃ nāma tattasya jñātvā pāśa valāvalaṃ ।।

tena karttā tathā jñātā siddhosau kāryadarśanāt ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

evametan mahādeva yathāvaduditaṃ tvayā ।
dvitvenaiva hi tatsiddhaṃ kathamadvayasiddhidā ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

caitanyamātmano rūpaṃ prasiddhaṃ paramārthataḥ ।
baṃdhatvaṃ nāma tasyoktaṃ śarīrāhaṃ tatā hi yā ।।

tadvimuktā tadaivāsya śivatvaṃ citparatvatā ।
citparatvaṃ svarūpaṃ yattavadvayamiheṣyate ।।

muktāṇavāḥ citparāste taiḥ sahaikaṃ śivādhvaraṃ ।
yasmā saṃvicitparayā śivatvaṃ nāma tadviduḥ ।।

nirvyāpāraṃ layātītaṃ hetu dṛṣṭāntavarjitaṃ ।
tadrūpā hyāṇavāḥ sarvve cidaikyādaikyamāsthitāḥ ।।
(164a)
tasmātkathaṃ devadevi dvayameva prarohati ।
svasaṃvidā vicāryaivaṃ bhava niḥsaṃśayā priye ।।

biṃduṃ nameva balavān sarvvakarttā maheśvaraḥ ।
jñātvā ca parameśāni śivaścoktaḥ sanātanaḥ ।।

paramātmā tadā haṃsaḥ śaktimāṃ sarvvakāraṇaḥ ।
sarvvasya prakaṭasyeyaṃ svasvarūpaḥ svarūpataḥ ।।

kāraṇeśo vibhurdātā nihnatenaiva kenacit ।
nirmalatvaṃ śivasyaivaṃ sphāṭikasya yathāsthitaṃ ।।

jahajaṃ parameśāni tāmrakālikavatpaśoḥ ।
paśutvaṃ sarvvadā siddhaṃ kasmādevaṃ vimuhyase ।।

taducchidyaiḥ kriyāśaktirjñānākhyā ca vijṛmbhate ।
mantrayoga kriyādīkṣā śivarūpā sadaiva hi ।।

etadarthe mahātaṃtre dīkṣā samyak prakīrttitā ।
yadā kartukamaśaktiḥ syādgurorajñānatopi vā ।।

tadarthaṃ viddhi vidyeyaṃ nāmnā bhuvanamālinī ।
asyāḥ prasādataḥ samyakpāśāḥ pūrvva pracoditāḥ ।।

nāśamāyāti bhūyopi na prarohaṃti jātucit ।
tena dīkṣākramārthaṃ vai vidyeyamupavarṇṇitā ।।

sarvvatobhadragā pūjyā yathābhimanavaibhavaiḥ ।
dhyāyetsarvvagatāṃ devī yathābhimatarūpiṇīṃ ।।

viśvarūpāṃ mahābhāgāṃ sadasatpāśakarttanīṃ ।
ṣaḍādhvānadharīṃ devīṃ sarvvāmantrāraṇīṃ śubhāṃ ।।

tatvādhvā ca padādhvā ca varṇādhvā bhuvanātidhaḥ ।
maṃtrādhvā ca kalpadhvā ca ṣoḍhā yaḥ parikīrttitaḥ ।।
(164b)
sarvvasyāsyaiva saṃśuddhirvidyayā homayogataḥ ।
tilādyairdevadeveśī nāmasaṃpuṭya daiśikaḥ ।।

sahasramāyutaṃ vātha juhuyātparameśvari ।
pūrṇāhutiprayogena sthāvarāṇyapi dīkṣayet ।।

dīkṣitaḥ śivatāṃ yānti bahunā kathitena kiṃ ।
asyāḥ ṣaṇmāsajāpena jñānaṃ yatpārameśvaraṃ ।।

svayamevopajāyeta sādhakāya mahātmane ।
sakṛdeva japedasyāḥ sarvvamaṃḍaladīkṣitaṃ ।।

bhavatyeva mahābhāge vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
antaḥkālena yasyaiṣā samya(kkvā)tra gatā japet ।।

tasya pāśā vinaśyante na prarohaṃti vai punaḥ ।
mokṣameti na saṃdehaṃ devadevyāḥ prabhāvataḥ ।।

sarvvakarmakarā jñeyā bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।
na tadasti iyatteṣāṃ sādhayetparameśvarī ।।

evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ pāśadāhaṃ maheśvari ।
kimanyat kathayiṣyāmi vada parvvatanaṃdini ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
bhuvanamālinīvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

evaṃ śrutvā haramukhāt prabuddhakumudānanā ।
punarāha mahādevī saṃtoṣāmṛtapūritā ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

tṛptāhaṃ bhairaveśāna mātṛsadbhāva pūjitaḥ ।
adhunā śrotumicchāmi rahasyamatulaṃ vibho ।।

dravyāvidhānamevānyadyattadveśahṛdi sthitaṃ ।
yena vijñātamātreṇa sarvvasiddhārhato bhavet ।।
(165a)
brahmāṇḍaṃ cūrṇayatpāśu loṣṭhavatparameśvara ।
tamākhyā hi vidhānaṃ me devyā yaddhṛdayaṃ paraṃ ।।

yasyā ghoratarā nānyā vidyate bhuvanodare ।
evamākarṇya vīrendraḥ sphuraccaṃdrārddhaśekharaḥ ।।
sāṭṭahāsamuvācedaṃ rahasyaṃ paramādbhutaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge jayadrathahitaiṣiṇi ।
vakṣyāmi yattvayā pṛṣṭhaṃ akathyaṃ yadapi priye ।।

gatvā harāspadaṃ subhru vidyāmenāṃ samuddharet ।
taṃ pūjya vidhisaṃbhārairmahākālī suvīravat ।।

saśaraṃ cāpamuddhṛtya vahnyadhordhva prapīḍitaṃ ।
mohanī sṛṣṭisahitaṃ kuṇḍalaṃ tadvaduddharet ।।

tadvadvijihvaṃ paramaṃ devyā hṛdayamuttamaṃ ।
tryakṣaraṃ paramaṃ dhāma ekaṃ vīraṃ mahābalaṃ ।।

karṇṇātkarṇṇagataṃ devi na lekhyaṃ pustakādiṣu ।
karmamaṃdiramadhyasthaṃ yattacchāktaṃ ca bhairavaṃ ।।

sphuradāghātasaṃkocavikāśādvaktrajāpataḥ ।
samāviśetsādhakendro (ma)hāvidyā prabhāvataḥ ।।

āveśa vaśataḥ sarvvamatītānāgatādikaṃ ।
kathayennātra saṃdeho mudrāḥ sarvvāḥ pravarttate ।।

śivarāvaṃ tathā geyaṃ karoti bhṛṣamaṃḍasā ।
(kaṃ)pate dehapiṇḍastu bhuvaścotpatate mahat ।।

sakṛtsmaraṇayogena mahāpātakanāśanaṃ ।
sarvvāvaraṇabhaṃgañca sādhakasyopajāyate ।।

trikoṇa gahane pūjyā sarvva(vī)ropacārataḥ ।
(165b)
surāmāṃsāsavairdivyairāmiṣairvividhaistathā ।।

mahātailapradīpaiśca mahāmāṃsādihomataḥ ।
siddhyate sā mahākālī trinetrā ghoradarśanā ।।

ghorakalpāntakālāgnikoṭiduḥsaha vigrahā ।
nirmāṃsā kṛṣṇanīhārakarakaṃṭhanibhopamā ।।

vajrasūcī kharasparśarāvorddhabhrukuṭīmukhā ।
kṣayasāgarasaṃghaṭṭakṣudhvajīmūtanisvanā ।।

anaṃtā hi guṇaprotasuradehasamujvalā ।
brahmaviṣṇuśarīraughamekhalādhāmamaṃḍitāṃ ।।

rudreśvaramahāmuṇḍakeyūrapravirājitāṃ ।
vajrasūcīkharasparśaromārddhabhrukuṭīmukhāṃ ।।

kulanāgaguṇaprotamuṇḍantasragvibhūṣitā ।
nānāghoramahāmātriratnatālaśatākulāṃ ।।

mahānṛsiṃhasaṃrūḍhāṃ mahāpretagatāṃ priye ।
triśūlacakrakhaṭvāṃgavajrāṃkuśa virājitāṃ ।।

kapālapāśasanmuṇḍavirājita karadhvajāṃ ।
saṃcintya parameśānī cidacitpadamadhyagāṃ ।।

sādhayedvividhāṃ kāmāṃ devi yanmanaśīśitaṃ ।
japedgirivare paścāddevī lakṣāṣṭakaṃ sadā ।।

mahākṣmā japalādyaṃ vai juhuyāttu daśāṅśataḥ ।
homāntātkuṃḍamadhyātsā samudeti sureśvarī ।।

abhīṣṭena vareṇaiva varayitvā tu sādhakaṃ ।
vrajatyasau devadevyā sāyojyaṃ sādhakeśvaraḥ ।।

athavāha melāpavidau yojyā mahā mahābalā ।
gatvā pitṛvanaṃ bhīmaṃ ghorāṃ vṛttiṃ samāśrayet ।।

mudrāṃ karaṃkiṇīṃ baddhā devyā hṛdayamuddharet ।
(166a)
yāvadeṣṭaṃ śataṃ mantrī tāvadāyānti devatā ।।

nānārūpairmahāmātryaḥ sādhakāḥ pūjayanti hi ।
tāsāṃ cakreśvaro bhūtvā plavate gaganāntaraṃ ।।

sarvvasiddhisamāyuktaḥ sākṣādbhairavadbhavet ।
vaśyādikāḥ sādhayedvā lakṣajāpena bhairavī ।।

sarvvasiddhipradā jñeyā devadevī kṛśodarī ।
nāsti devadeveśī yanna sādhayate balāt ।।

evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ hṛdayaṃ kāmadaṃ * * ।
kimanyat kathayiṣyāmi vada bhairavi māciraṃ ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
hṛdayakālyāvidhiḥ ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

paritṛptāsmi bhagavan śāstraughaṃ pariśṛṇvatī ।
tvat prasādānmahādeva rahasyāmṛtanirbharāṃ ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi hṛdayaṃ cāparaṃ mahat ।
yairnadhītena bhagavan bhogamokṣādisiddhibhāk ।।
bhavatyeva hi vīrendrastanme kathaya bhairava ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu vīrendra namite hṛdayaṃ ca paraṃ śubhaṃ ।
yena saṃprāptamātreṇa bhairaveva bhave naraḥ ।।

anāśrite pare dhāmni saptadhā kāliravyayā ।
proditā pararūpeṇa saptārṇā bhairaveśvarī ।।

svadaṃdāsvāmaraṃ kandaṃ yāvadvagalitā parā ।
sarvvadhāmnāṃ nidhāmnāni sā tasyoccā mataḥ śṛṇu ।।

sucirbhūbhāgamālipya vīradravyairmaheśvarī ।
tatrārcayitvā maṃtreśī tato vidyāṃ samuddharet ।।
(166b)
parāmṛtaṃ ca varuṇaṃ śaktibiṃdusamanvitaṃ ।
tribiṃduyuktaṃ varuṇaṃ dvitīyaṃ varṇṇamuttamaṃ ।।

vāruṇaṃ ca tadanāṃkaṃ trikoṇāhatamastakaṃ ।
tameva punaruddhṛtya kiṃ tu lāṃcchana varjitaṃ ।।

bāhuṃ tridhā sukhacitaṃ samuccāryaṃ mahābalaṃ ।
bāhuṃ ca vāruṇaṃ māyā biṃduyuktaṃ samuddharet ।।

dvijihvaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃyuktaṃ vidyeyaṃ sarvvanāyakī ।
na cāsyā dīpako deyo na cāpi pratibodhakaḥ ।।

svayaṃ dīptatarā khyātā ghorā kālakaraṃkiṇī ।
parāmṛtarasāsāravarṣiṇī parameśvarī ।।

viśveśvarī viśvarūpā cidacitpadamadhyagā ।
vyaktā ghoratarā raudrā jagadgrāsaikaghasmarā ।।

ghorakalpāntakālāgnikoṭikoṭyārvudaprabhāṃ ।
śrīkaṃṭhakaṃṭhakākāliśaradgaganasannibhāṃ ।।

ghorārkāgrāsasaṃkṣobhakṣubhitodaravahnikāṃ ।
lalajjihvāsahasraughaiḥ svādayaṃtī vasāmiṣaṃ ।।

niḥśeṣarāvacakreśa carvvaṇoccārarāviṇī ।
malakarmamahāmāyā pāśaplāvaikapāvakīṃ ।।

satkuśeśaya satpretarūḍhāṃ bhairavamālinīṃ ।
lalāmabhūtāṃ sarvvatra cintayetkālakarṣaṇīṃ ।।

evaṃ saṃcintya ṣaṭkoṇā bhuvane pūjayetparāṃ ।
sarvvavīropacāreṇa surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

mahāpalaṃ ca juhuyādvidyā saṃtṛptaye sadā ।
tatastu sādhayenmantrī lakṣākṣarajapādinā ।।

japāntādeva śubhage svayaṃ bhairavamālinī ।
śmaśānagasya sā seti sādhakasya mahātmanaḥ ।।
(167a)
sahaiva nayate tūrṇṇaṃ sādhakendraṃ dṛḍhavrataṃ ।
tattulyatā bhavettatra bahunā kathitena kiṃ ।।

eṣaiva devadeveśī pūrvvatantrapracoditā ।
sādhayetsarvvamakhilaṃ tadvatsādhanayogataḥ ।।

smaraṇādeva subhage sādhakaḥ saṃprajāyate ।
yataḥ prabhṛti hṛccheṣā sādhakasya mahātmanaḥ ।।

tataḥ prabhṛti śakrasya pratimaḥ sādhako bhavet ।
pustakena likhedenāṃ yadicheddīrghajīvitaṃ ।।

chidrayanti na deveśyo na kadācinnareśvaraṃ ।
asyoccāraṇamātreṇa trirātraṃtaparāvarāt ।।

śaktivyaktibhavedvyaktaṃ sadyaḥ pratyayakārikāṃ ।
evaṃ pratyayamāsādya saṃtuṣṭaḥ sādhako bhavet ।।
evaṃ te sarvvamākhyātaṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ paripṛchasi ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavasrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
bhairavamālinyāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā sārahṛdayaṃ pravrūhi tripurāntaka ।
yena vijñātamātreṇa vicarāmi yatheṣṭavat ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu vakṣyāmi yatsāraṃ gopitaṃ brahmayāmale ।
asmin jayadrathe tantre tavādya prakaṭīkṛtaṃ ।।

kālyā hṛdayasāraṃ tu sarvvasaṃdohalakṣaṇaṃ ।
yenaikena sphuṭaṃ jāyenmantrakośaṃ mahābalaṃ ।।

yattatsarvvaṃ jagatsarvvaṃ vilāpya pravijṛmbhate ।
tadvāsaghorasaṃhārakramaceśvaraṃ mahat ।।
(167b)
uddhṛtyaivā smṛtaṃ yasmāttaddhṛtyai vinivarttitaṃ ।
saṃhārakāraṇaṃ tena hṛdayaṃ parigīyate ।।

tasyoddhāramahaṃvakṣye sārātsāratarasya tu ।
gatvā harāspadaṃ ghoraṃ vedimadhyaṃ samāśritaḥ ।।

tatra kaṃkālasaṃghṛṣṭāṃ vasudhāṃ raktacarcitāṃ ।
paṃcāmṛtaviliptāṃ tu kṛtvā maṃtreśvarīṃ yajet ।।

tataḥ samuddharedvidyāmanekādbhutasiddhidāṃ ।
mahāsaṃhāranātheśaṃ haṃsaṣaṣṭhaṃ sabindukaṃ ।।

mahāśaktiṃ samuddhṛtya punaḥ saṃhārabhairavaṃ ।
caturvarṇṇā bhavedevaṃ kālī kālaprayapradā ।।

mahāsamayadoṣaghnī mahāpātakanāśanī ।
mahāmohapraśamanī mahāsaṃkaranāśanī ।।

mahābhāgyodayakarī mahājñānavivarddhanī ।
mahāsaṃhārakālaghnī bhayadāridraghātanī ।।

mahāvinodābhiratā mahāsaṃhārabhairavī ।
muṇḍapādavinirmuktā sarvvamaṃtreśvareśvarī ।।

ekavīrāhuṃdumukhī yoṃgaḍāyutavarcasā ।
dhyāyedbhinnāṃjanābhāsāṃ sphuratmecakasaṃnibhāṃ ।।

śrīkaṃṭhakaṃṭhaśaradākāśasaṃkāśavigrahāṃ ।
bhavanītrāsanodagrasadṛśograśarīriṇī ।।

saṃhārabhuvi narttanti vasāmiṣamahoddhatāṃ ।
kramaśukrāmaniryātasarvvakārabhakṣaṇī ।।

sadasadghorasaṃkalpavilāpanakarāṃ parāṃ ।
nirargalamahācittapralayasvasvarūpagāṃ ।।

bhedadviradasatsiṃharūḍhāmadvayaśālinīṃ ।
sarvvabhāvāntarasphārasphurasphuraṇavigrahāṃ ।।

asaṃkucitaciddhāmavyaktisuṃdarasaṃpadā ।
(168a)
vyaṃgavyaṃjakayoraṃ tu virājitajitāṃtarāṃ ।।

manmānasamahāṃbhoja krīḍālolāṃ susārasāṃ ।
vicitraśabdabhramarajhaṃkṛtiścarvalonmukhāṃ ।।

cidacit sparśaviśrāmapūrṇṇabādhendurūpiṇīṃ ।
tridhāmavyaktisadrūpaviśrāntyaṃjitacitkalāṃ ।।

śrutalaṃbhakaṣadrūparasāsvādanatatparāṃ ।
maṃtarāṃdhamahāsvādalaṃpaṭāṃ viśvarāviṇīṃ ।।

catuṣkalamahābiṃducakramagrasanodbhagaṃ ।
ūhakādiśivāntoṃgracakraghaṭṭanapāṭavāṃ ।।

prollasatsasvarūpormi prasaraṃ jayadiṃḍimāṃ ।
hāhāhasitasaṃnādavyaktisādākhya kālikāṃ ।।

aṣṭadhā prasphuradvāsya mudrāvīryasvarūpiṇīṃ ।
spaṃdāspaṃdātarodāravīryavyaktikarālayāṃ ।।

kālīṃ kālānalāpīḍavirājitajitaujasāṃ ।
viśvarūpāṃ sadā dhyāyedyathābhimatarūpiṇīṃ ।।

yajedvīropacāraiśca ṣaṭkoṇabhuvanodare ।
juhuyānnaramāṃsasya sahasramathavā yutaṃ ।।

paścātsaṃsādhayeddevīṃ trailokyadalanīṃ śivā ।
gatvā harāspadāṃ rātrau mudrā badhvā karaṃkiṇīṃ ।।

samuccarecchataṃ yāvacchavāsīno dṛḍhavrataḥ ।
tāvadāyānti deveśyo nānārūpā mahābalā ।।

sādhakendravyadeveśi lobhayanti muhurmuhuḥ ।
brahmatvamatha viṣṇutvaṃ rudratvamatha ceśvaraṃ ।।

sadāśivaṃ ca yacchāro tava sādhakasattama ।
tāsāṃvaro nispṛheta nāpi saṃbhāṣayedvacaḥ ।।

mudrābaṃdhanamuceta vidyāmenāmanusmaret ।
(168b)
praharārddhātsamabhyeti devadevī karaṃkiṇīṃ ।।

parivārayutāṃ ghorāṃ sādhakasya varapradā ।
sābhīṣṭena vareṇaiva varayetsakeśvaraṃ ।।

tasmāt sāyojyatāṃ vātha yātyevaṃ sādhakāḥ priye ।
atyuttamā samākhyātā siddhireṣā * hyuttamā ।।

athavā japayogena tannāsti yanna sādhayet ।
lakṣajāpena subhage sāmānyāḥ sarvvasiddhayaḥ ।।

siddhyante sādhakendrāṇāṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
dviguṇe madhyamāṃ siddhiṃ triguṇena tu cottamāṃ ।।

sādhayennātra saṃdeho vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātā nāmnā naṭṭeśvarī śivā ।।

saṃharacakrasamaye nṛtyate parameśvarī ।
taṃtroditā sā paramā devadevī karaṃkiṇīṃ ।।

kimanyatkathayiṣyāmi vada yaccetaso gataṃ ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
naṭṭeśvarīsaṃhāracakrodayaḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

ākarṇyeyaṃ devadevī devadevānmaheśvarān ।
romāṃcitavapurbhūtvā punarāha maheśvaraṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi mahābhairava vallabhā ।
yā sā phekkāriṇī raudrā mudrā maṃdrāstu nāyakī ।।

tasyā vyāpti kathaṃ deva kathaṃ vā sādhanāni ca ।
etatsamāsato brūhi mahātripurasūdana ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi rahasyatamamadbhutaṃ ।
na mayā kasyacit khyātaṃ rahasyaṃ rāviṇī kramaṃ ।।
(169a)
iyaṃ phetkāriṇī raudrā ghorasaṃrāvarāviṇīṃ ।
samayantu vinā naināṃ smaredbhūtabhayaṃkarīṃ ।।

gatvā harāspadaṃ vīro madirānaṃdanaṃditaḥ ।
prāsya paṃcāmṛtacaruṃ vedimadhyaṃ samāśritaḥ ।।

kaṃkālaghṛṣṭāṃ vasudhāṃ kṛtvā paṃcoditāṃ tataḥ ।
tatra vīroditairmadyaiḥ pūjayenmantramātrikāṃ ।।

mudrāṃ tu rāviṇīṃ baddhvā tato vidyāṃ samuddharet ।
aṣṭadhārāvacakreṇa muddharetparameśvaraṃ ।।

prathamaṃ vahnicakrasthaṃ biṃdutadveśmabhūṣitaṃ ।
dvitīyaṃ yonikhacitaṃ catvāraḥ kevalā smṛtāḥ ।।

sānicitraṃ saptamaṃsyādaṣṭamaṃ pūrvvavat smṛtaṃ ।
evamaṣṭākṣarī ghorā rāviṇī rāvaṇārcitā ।।

samagrapāpamathanī sakṛdāveśakārikā ।
stobharomāṃcakaraṇī mudrābhedakarī mahat ।।

pūrvvokta rāviṇī bhede idaṃ hṛdayamuttamaṃ ।
nānena sadṛśāṃ devi vidyate bhuvanodare ।।

asyāṃ vyāptiṃ * māhātmyaṃ vibhūtiṃ vīryasaṃpadaḥ ।
vaktuṃ na śakyate devī sādākhyai tātsarāyanaiḥ ।।

nistaraṃgā niraupamyāṃ nirnāmā paramā kalā ।
śivaśaktyontaraudāryaprasṛtāmṛtarūpiṇī ।।

vyaktākrameṇa suśroṇi āśivā * hukāvadhi ।
tayā cāntaṃ kāraṇānāṃ vrātaṃ yat prākpracoditaṃ ।।

svavīryavyaktisāmarthyaṃ vibhavāmodamaṃtharaṃ ।
* krumānvayayogena mahārāva prayogataḥ ।।

grastametatpurāvāṃtaṃ rāvinyā yat pracoditaṃ ।
(169b)
evaṃ prasarasaṃkocau vidhāya bhagamālinīṃ ।।

spandāspandātararatā nityā paramabhairavī ।
kramavyāptyā smaredenāṃ śubhrābhraharahāsavat ।।

tuṣāraśaṃkhakuṃdābhāṃ sphurat sphaṭikavigrahāṃ ।
śatapūrṇaniśīnātha koṭikoṭyarbudaprabhāṃ ।।

yathābhimatasadrūpā pūrṇacaṃdrakṛtāsanāṃ ।
tadā pīḍā mahāmohadhvāntaghaṭṭanacaṃdrikāṃ ।।

caṃdrasrajāṃ caṃdracitrāṃ smare * ṣṭivatīṃ sadā ।
tāmeva kālanavyagrāṃ smarennīhārasannibhāṃ ।।

śaradgananakālāliharakaṇṭhanibhopamāṃ ।
mahāpretaskandasaṃsthāṃ bhairavoḍḍāmarāṃ smṛtāṃ ।।

rāvayantī rāviṇīṃ yā bhakṣayantī mahāśayāṃ ।
kramavyutkramaviśrāntāṃ smare citpārarūpiṇīṃ ।।

evaṃ dhyātvā trirūpāntāṃ sadyaḥ pratyayakārikāṃ ।
kampate dehamakhilaṃ vidhūnvatpāśajālakāṃ ।।

mudrābaṃdhaṃ tathādeya prakaroti nareśvaraḥ ।
saṃpūjayettrikoṇāntasaṃkārakaraṇīṃ yadi ।।

sṛṣṭirūpāṃ mahādevīṃ ṣoḍaśachadapadmagā ।
yajedbhagavatī saumyāmanekādbhutasiddhidāṃ ।।

nistaraṃgāṃ cidratā tāṃ maṇḍalādau ca pūjayet ।
evaṃ vīravaradravyairyajeta parameśvarīṃ ।।

mahākṣmā japalādyaiśca surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।
juhuyātpūrvvadravyāṇi mahātailocitāni ca ।।

evaṃ saṃpūjya vidhivattataḥ śiṣyaṃ praveśayet ।
baddhanetrasāṃjalīkaṃ dhṛtapuṣpamaniṃditaṃ ।।
(170a)
kṣipāpayettataḥ puṣpāṃ tāḍitābhyukṣitaṃ yadā ।
paścādyutkrāmayogena phaṇapaṃcakarāviṇīṃ ।।

phekkārāṣṭakayogena vedhayecchiṣyameva tat ।
evaṃ saṃsiddhamātrastha dhunate kaṃpate bhṛśaṃ ।।

chinnamūladrumaṃ yadvat patate kāśyapītale ।
tadvatpatatyasaṃdehā śiṣyosau vedhito yathā ।।

paścācchivasvabhāvajño jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
evaṃ dīkṣī samākhyātā saṃsārachedakāriṇī ।।

atha kriyāvatī kāryā yathā pūrvvaṃ pracoditāṃ ।
evamasyādhikāraṃ vai devaṃ tu guruṇā sadā ।।

tataḥ prabhṛti sarvvāsu sādhayerho bhavatyasau ।
sādhanī * tato devī gatvā haragṛhaṃ mahat ।।

japedakṣaralakṣaṃ tu sarvvamantreśvarīṃ śivāṃ ।
juhuyānnaramāṃsasya tryaktasya daśamāṃśataḥ ।।

karṣamātrāstu guhikā sādhakendro dṛḍhavrataḥ ।
tatastu kaṃpate bhūmiṃ cakravadbhramate muhuḥ ।।

jvalanti ca diśaḥ sarvvā hyulkā nirghātasaṃkaṭāḥ ।
śivārāvāṇi bahudhā cātivātāḥ subhairavāḥ ।।

yoginaḥ sarvvacakrasthāḥ samāgacchati tatkṣaṇāt ।
gṛdhravaktrāścillavaktrāśvānavaktrāstathāparā ।।

hayamārjāravadanā gokharadvijavaktragāḥ ।
siṃhasūkaravaktrāśca nānāvaktrā madotkaṭāḥ ।।

tata āyāti sā devī ghorarūpā karaṃkiṇī ।
svayaṃ sādhakamukhyena nayate khecaraṃ padaṃ ।।

parivārayutasyāsya nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
(170b)
athavā lakṣajāpena sāmānyāḥ siddhayokhilāḥ ।।

sa sādhayati deveśī līlayā sādhakātmanāṃ ।
trilakṣajāpāddeveśī madhyamaḥ sādhayiṣyati ।।

athavā sādhanāhīnā tasya sādhanamucyate ।
satvavān vīryasaṃpannastyāgavānapyalolupaḥ ।।

akṣatāṅgaṃ śavaṃ gṛhya sādhakendro harālayaṃ ।
yāyāddigaṃbaro bhūtvā yāvarttavedimadhyagaṃ ।।

tatrārcayeta vīrendro devadevīṃ kṛśodarīṃ ।
nānābalyopahāraiśca surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

śavaṃ vibhūṣayetsamyakkuṃkumasragvilepanaiḥ ।
vastrayugmena ca tataḥ paścānmudrāṃ prabaṃdhayet ।।

rāviṇīṃ ghorasaṃsārarāviṇīṃ tāṃ samuddharet ।
tāvaduttiṣṭhate vegācchavosau vikṛtānanaḥ ।।

kṛtvārāvaṃ mahābhīmaṃ nṛttate valgate bhṛśaṃ ।
brūvate sādhakendrasya kiṃkarāmīti vā naghe ।।

yatheṣṭaṃ tasya vaktavyaṃ sādhakendreṇa dhīmatā ।
rasaṃ rasāyanaṃ khaḍgaṃ pādukāṃjanarocanāṃ ।।

nidhānādyakhilaṃ sarvvaṃ prayacchati na saṃśayaḥ ।
eṣā kṛśodarī devī sarvvasampadguṇāvahā ।।

nāmādhyaṃ vidyate kiṃcidanayā siddhayā priye ।
vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣamāraṇoccāṭanādiṣu ।।

līlayā sādhayenmaṃtrī prasenāvarttipātanaṃ ।
sarṣipā khārikāveśaṃ svasthāveśanamuttamaṃ ।।

tannāsti yanna kurute paramābiṃdubhairavī ।
(171a)
mahāmelāpakādyāstu siddhayaḥ sādhayiṣyati ।।

evaṃ tava samākhyātaṃ hadayaṃ sāramuttamaṃ ।
yamavāpya na śocanti sādhako varavarṇṇini ।।
kimanyat kathayiṣyāmi vada vīrendravaṃdite ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke rāviṇyupadahṛdayavidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

devyuvāca ।।

kālatrayāṃ śrayaṃ * * sādhakānāṃ viceṣṭitaṃ ।
tadjñānena binā śaṃbho sādhakāścāntyamāgatāḥ ।।

kathaṃ jñāyate * * * sādhakairmantratatparaiḥ ।
etadicchāmi bhagavan jñātuṃ tanme vadāśubhoḥ ।।

yena vijñātamātreṇa sarvvasiddhyarthabhāgbhavet ।। ।।

bhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge yadahaṃ coditastvayā ।
tata pravakṣyāmi nikhilaṃ śṛṇu parvvatanaṃdini ।
