trikālavedanī kālī bhogamokṣaphalapradā ।।
proddharedbhuvaneśānaṃ krodhārgalapuṭīkṛtaṃ ।।

vātāṃkuśapuṭaṃ kṛtvā biṃdunā samalaṃkṛtaṃ ।
paṃcādyeva hi saṃrūḍhaṃ tribiṃdu śirasāhataṃ ।।

yonyādyamaṃkuśākrāntaṃ śakrabījaṃ punardadet ।
biṃduyuktaṃ mahācaṃdraṃ yonyāṅkaṃ vāruṇaṃ punaḥ ।।

cāpādyaṃ kevalaṃ deyaṃ graṃthikaṃ trisvarāhataṃ ।
punaḥ pūrvvaṃ mahāpiṇḍaṃ samuccāryaṃ surārcitaṃ ।।

navākṣarā mahākālī kālatrayanivedanī ।
sakṛduccāritā samyak sarvvapāpavanodanīṃ ।।
(171b)
sarvvaviśvapraśamanī duṣṭadāridryamardanī ।
sarvvasiddhipradā khyātā mahālakṣmī pravarttikā ।।

yathābhimatarūpaiṣā dhyātavyā kālikā śivā ।
lakṣaṃ japedgirivare sādhakendro dṛḍhavrataḥ ।।

juhuyādgugguluṃ jātyaṃ śatāṅgena maheśvari ।
daśāṅśena tilāstyaktvā śatāṃśe nājyamuttamaṃ ।।

evaṃ sā siddhidā proktā kālatrayanivedikā ।
dīpe jaletha nabhasi khaḍgādau darpaṇodare ।।

aṃguṣṭhe cakṣuṣorvvāpi kumārīṇāṃ mahātape ।
āvāhayeduseneyaṃ devadevī kṛśodarī ।।

svayaṃ dṛśyā kathayate kālatrayamalaṃ śivā ।
ātmano vā parasyāpi dṛśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

atītānāgataṃ sarvvaṃ varttamānaṃ ca yadbhavet ।
dviguṇena tu jāpena nikhilaṃ paśyate svayaṃ ।।

triguṇena tu jāpena śṛṇute sarvvameva hi ।
svayaṃ sa sādhakavaraḥ saṃśayo nātra kaścana ।।

susiddhā mānave devi svapnākhyaṃ sādhayiṣyati ।
śirodeśe pūjanena sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

evaṃ kālatrayaṃ jñātvā kuru karma yathecchayā ।
etāṃ jñātvā bhagavatīṃ kālīṃ kālabhayapradāṃ ।।

nigrahānugrahaṃ karma kurvvanna pratihanyate ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātā kiṃ bhūyaḥ paripṛcchasi ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe jayadrathayāmale caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
kālasaṃvedanyāsādhanapaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi nigrahaṃ kathamācaret ।
(172a)
kruddhacittaḥ sādhakendro ripūṇāṃ duṣṭacetasāṃ ।।

ūhakānāṃ sādhanaṃ vā kathaṃ vaśyādisādhanaṃ ।
dravyairmantrasamāyuktairyathā kāryaṃ tathā vada ।।

evamākarṇya bhagavān prasphuraccaṃdraśekharaṃ ।
prayogamaṃjarī caiva * * * * yathārthavat ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi yogaṃ paramadurlabhaṃ ।
yairvijñātairmahādevi sarvvakarmāṇi kārayet ।।

mohanīṃ pūrvvamuddhṛtya nādamagnikṣayāṃkitaṃ ।
sabiṃduṃ proddharitvā tu kūpacakraṃ ca kevalaṃ ।।

cāpaṃ ca yonipūrvvaṃ ca dvidhā varṇṇadvayaṃ kuru ।
jihvilaṃ varuṇārūḍhaṃ śakraṃ kevalamuddharet ।।

dvidhāpadamidaṃ kāryaṃ dvivarṇaṃ śatrutāpanaṃ ।
śakradhobhāvasaṃbhinnaṃ marmaṃ kevalamuddharet ।।

dvidhā padamidaṃ kāryaṃ punaḥ kuṃḍalamuddharet ।
vahnimāyāvicitrāṅgaṃ biṃdunā samalaṃkṛtaṃ ।।

bhīmakuviruddheyaṃ vidyā ghoraparākramā ।
saptādaśākṣarā caṃḍā ripūnāṃ kṣayakārikāṃ ।।

pāṭhamātrātsiddhideyaṃ vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
ṛkṣakarṇī vidhau pūrvvaṃ yadvastu samudīritaṃ ।।

tatsarvvamanayā sādhyaṃ tajjapadhyānayogataḥ ।
athavā prakṛtiḥ kāryāmāṣāḍhakamayī priye ।।

viṣaraktāṃgāramiśrāṃ sādhyapāṃśulikācitāṃ ।
prādeśamātrāṃ kṛtvaivaṃ tasyā hṛdayagaṃ kuru ।।

sādhyanāma samāyuktaṃ vyādhinā sa samanvitaṃ ।
vidyāṃ viṣavimiśreṇa kṛtaje nālikhettadā ।।
(172b)
kṛtvaivaṃ vedimadhyasthāṃ tāpaye jvarakarmaṇi ।
yeṣu ye svāṃgadeśeṣu vyādhyutpatti samicchati ।।

teṣu kīlakasanyāsaṃ karttavyaṃ kuliśodari ।
tatra ghorā mahāvyādhiṃ saptahā janmayedbhṛśaṃ ।।

na tadvārayituṃ śakyaṃ sendrairapi surāsuraiḥ ।
yāvattenaiva deveśi karuṇā kṛṣṭacetasā ।।

guḍakṣīrasnānapūrvvaṃ yacca bhraṃśaṃ tavākṛtaṃ ।
tāvatkutaḥ svacchatāsyā sā(vya)sya surasuṃdari ।।

yadā mārayituṃ vāṃcchetsādhyaṃ śakra samantataḥ ।
cidagnau dāhayedenā krodhasaṃraktalocanaḥ ।।

sadā mārayate samyak praharārddhā na saṃśayaḥ ।
evaṃ nigrahakarmādi praproktaṃ tanumadhyame ।।

atha maṃtravihīnāni prayogāni niśāmaya ।
tulyaṃ madhughṛtaṃ kṛtvā bījamāhṛtya sāntalaṃ ।।

kārayedajane rātrau kādyaṃ bhūtāhni sādhakaṃ ।
kṣīrasyāṃjanadānena yāvajjīvaṃ prajāyate ।।

utsādamakulaṃ ghoraṃ yāvajjīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ।
atha pānapadānādvā tatkarma kurute bhṛśaṃ ।।

tulyaṃ madhughṛtaṃ nātha bhojanaṃ yasya dīyate ।
taṃ māraya na saṃdeho nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

tantu nārddhaṃ yathā dattaṃ tatkarma kurute bhṛśaṃ ।
kṛkalābhaṃ samāhṛtya tadbhasmānāharetsakṛt ।।

tatrāntare vinikṣipya carcitaṃ sādya cakrataḥ ।
tāṃbūlaṃ parameśāni adhovaktraṃ tu lambayet ।।

tasya syātsukharogograṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
(173a)
uddhṛte mokṣamāyāti mukharogāstu dāruṇāṃ ।।

evaṃ tāṃbūlamādāya dantakāṣṭhamathāpi vā ।
kṛṣṇasarpamukhe kṣiptaṃ mukharo * karaṃ bhṛśaṃ ।।

svakeśo dakṣamāhṛtya svānāntavaravarṇini ।
tenāyaṃ tāḍayettasya vyalīkamupajāyate ।।

pūrṇṇādghaṭādvipraṣovā tathā karmakarā smṛtā ।
tato bhojanasammiśrā vasādardurusaṃbhavā ।।

yasyasyākṣatamatyugraṃ yasya dehāntakārakaṃ ।
mṛtayāścūpanī tasya yonau khaditakīlakaṃ ।।

nikṣipya dāhakāle tu grāhyamaṃgāravadgataṃ ।
taṃ sūryaguptaṃ gṛhnīta ṣṭhīcaneṃnamigharṣayet ।।

yatkapāle mahābhāge tadrūpastilakā havet ।
śvānaṃ kharamavicchāgaṃ hayamaśvataraṃ tu vā ।।

naragosarpamahiṣaṃ siṃhavyāghrādikārakṛt ।
yasyā yasya samīheta (ta)sya rūpāṃtate naraḥ ।।

tattadrūpaṃ bhavatyāśusādhakena samīhitaṃ ।
evaṃ rūpaparāvṛttirnarāṇāṃ duṣṭacetasāṃ ।।

jāyate yā vaśājīvaṃ yāvantenaiva noddhṛtaḥ ।
athādvākhuramādāya viśvaliptaṃ tataḥ kuru ।।

mṛtakeśaiḥ samāveṣṭya gṛhe yasya nikhādayet ।
tamuccāṭayate śīghraṃ sabhṛtyavalavāhanaṃ ।।

evaṃ vidhena vidhinā mānuṣāsthimayaṃ mahat ।
kīlakoccārinihitaṃ proccāṭayati tadgṛhaṃ ।।

cikārāṣṭasya deveśi vidhirevaṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।
kākasyāpi tathaivaṃ syādvacastvavitathaṃ mama ।।

citaloṣṭhaṃ vāyubhāgādvā vāhnādapi sa saṃhṛtaṃ ।
(173b)
viśvākeśasamāyuktāṃ gṛhe yasya nikhādayet ।।

evamuccāṭane śastaṃ kṛtyākalahakārakaṃ ।
anyāvyādhikaraṃ ghoraṃ dāhajvarakaraṃ bhṛśaṃ ।।

taduccārādbhavecchānti sādhyasya varavarṇini ।
kukkuṭāṇḍakapālāni citrakaṃ padmakaṃ tathā ।।

mātulaṃ vivare yuktaṃ dhūpaṃ trailokyakaṃpanaṃ ।
etena dhūparājena mohayet sacarācaraṃ ।

bhramaraṃ mātulānī ca mātulaṃ viṣameva ca ।
kukkuṭāṇḍakapālāni dhūpoyaṃ cittamohakaḥ ।।

nṝntāpayati sarvvasya yenāghrātaṃ tu dhūpa(rā)t ।
ghṛtabhyaktau svakau raṃdhrau karttavyau mṛgalocane ।
praphullapuṣpamādāya viṣayatūracūrṇṇitaṃ ।।

tadāghrātaṃ vedhayate mohayetpātayedbhuvi ।
rasendramaṣṭasaṃgṛhya yattu devi samudbhavāṃ ।।

taṃ tāmrabhāṇḍagaṃ kṛtvā vajralepanaghaṭṭitaṃ ।
taṃ gṛhoparisaṃnyastaṃ tasyorddhavahnidīpanaṃ ।।

kāryaṃ sādhakamukhyena yāvantaṃ tāpameti hi ।
yāvat prakampate sarvvaṃ mandiraṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

gaṃdhakaṃ śubhamāvṛtya śuṣkaṃ trikoṇagopitaṃ ।
taṃ taptaghṛtasaṃyuktamanagnijvalanālakaṃ ।।

atha śrotojanaṃ (gṛ)hya strīyonau puṣpakālagaṃ ।
śuddhiṃ yāvat sthitaṃ tatra evaṃ saptasu kārayet ।।

gṛhītaṃ raktavastrāntaḥ suguptaṃ na parīkṣayet ।
taṃ vahnigaṃ samāhṛtya darśayetpuruṣottamamaṃ ।।

vahni * * samudbhūtaṃ dīpyamānaṃ svatejasaṃ ।
(174a)
mahāścaryakaraṃ caitat prayogaṃ parikīrttitaṃ ।।

atha vidyudvatādvṛkṣātkālaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ samāhṛtaṃ ।
tena kāraṃ prakurvvīta yadyanmanasi rocate ।।

tattadvyomni nirādhāratiṣṭhatyeva na saṃśayaḥ ।
bhaṅgābhidhānamāsādya oṣadhī kṛṣṇavāsare ।।

tithyayogena tanmūla vyaktakaupinarātritaḥ ।
nṛttaṃ hāsaṃ haredvālāṃ suguptaṃ mohamānayet ।।

tadhūpanāṃ tathā tasyā tādṛśaṃ kurute bhṛśaṃ ।
cillasyaiva tu cilleyā vṛkṣo yatraiva maithunaṃ ।।

tatkālamāharedvṛkṣādantavalkalamūrddhagāṃ ।
adhogataṃ stambavṛtyā cūrṇṇayeta pṛthakkuru ।।

tenāvacūrṇṇitaṃ mūrdhnā yaṃ yaṃ sādhyaṃ maheśvari ।
tamadho vātasaṃyukto yāvajjīvaṃ bhaviṣyati ।।

ūrddhacūrṇṇaprayogena mokṣamasyā vi * * * ।
athavā sāra(mā)yāni grāhyamante nibaṃdhanaṃ ।।

yahyajātaṃ tathā samyakkulligāmāharedgiri ।
chāyā śuṣkaṃ tu taṃ kṛtvā ślakṣṇāṃ dṛśadi cūrṇṇayet ।।

taṃ cūrṇṇaṃ maithune kāle yeṣāmuparidīyate ।
bhagaliṃgasya baṃdhaṃ vai teṣāmevopajāyate ।।

saptāhamātraṃ śubhage satyānayanavarjitaṃ ।
etena cūrṇṇarājena ghṛtamadhvāyutena ca ।।

liṃgamālepayatsamyagyāstrī kāmayate sadā ।
sā vaśyatāṃ prayātyeva yadi sākṣāttrilottamā ।।

nānyaṃ bharttāramīheta vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
ūrddhādhogavalo yasyāṃ spaṃdataughavidhṛṣyataḥ ।।

tābhyāmagraṃ samāsāya cūrṇṇapatraṃ pṛthakkuru ।
(174b)
adhogatasya cūrṇena bhagaṃ yasyāḥ samācaret ।।

kanyābhāvaṃ prayātyāśu sa nārī nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
ūrddhacūrṇṇe samāveśāt prakṛtiryāti sā punaḥ ।।

evaṃ khaṇḍadvayaṃ kāryaṃ gopālaṃ kīṭanāyakaṃ ।
taṃ dṛgbhāgasamāyuktaṃ budhnā kkanyāṃ karaṃ smṛtaṃ ।।

ūrddhaprakṛtimāyāti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
atha markaṭaliṃgotthaṃ cūrṇṇammadhughṛtānvitaṃ ।।

tenāliptadhvajaṃ vīraṃ kāmeśvarasamo bhavet ।
nāmnā kāmāṃkuśaṃ nāma leparājamudāhṛtaṃ ।।

atha kākodare kṣiptaṃ kṛṣṇehni kṛṣṇasaidhavaṃ ।
kṛṣṇamātulakāṣṭhaiśca dahe devi caturtha * ।।

lavaṇaṃ tatsamādāya * tvendriyakṣajānvitaṃ ।
yasyaivaṃ pānakaṃ dattaṃ savasodāsavadbhavet ।।

athavā mallakā vāsau samāhṛtya tacchidragaṃ ।
ekasmin pannagaṃ lekhya śmaśānāṃgārakena ca ।।

aparasminnaitateyaṃ ūrddhamallakasaṃsthitaṃ ।
adhyāmudagaraṃ nāma sādhyarthaṃ mallike priye ।।

susaṃvilepitaṃ kṛtvā pramāṇe tanmaheśvari ।
paścātkuṃbhodare cāgniḥ kṛtvā tatra vinikṣipet ।।

mārjārākhubhavāṃ vṛṣṭiṃ tallolā sahasaiṃdhavā ।
taddhūpaṃ dāpayettasmin puṭe kuṃbhordhvasaṃsthite ।।

dattamātraṃ dveśayate kāmadhūtirapi priye ।
dharmajaṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ viśveśaṃ yantramuttamaṃ ।।

atha paiśāca vyakṣāṃdhva sphaṭitau kīlakāvubhau ।
tābhyāṃ samālikhennāma napuṃsakavidarbhitaṃ ।।
(175a)

viṣāsṛgdhamaraiḥ pūrṇṇavimukhaṃ nadikhātitaṃ ।
vidveṣaṇakaraṃ viddhi jagatyasmin carācare ।।

athāparaṃ mahāyantraṃ nāmnā śatrapurābhidhaṃ ।
pravakṣyāmi mahādevi sarvvastambhakaraṃ mahat ।।

sādhyaṃ nāma likhetpūrvvaṃ paścāttatpṛthagāṃ likhet ।
śakrasthaṃ gaṇanāmādyaṃ tripadena samanvitaṃ ।।

binduyogaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā hyadhorddhaṃ tena veṣṭayet ।
paścācca madhyagaṃ kāryaṃ punarvvajrapurāntakaṃ ।।

kalārthacitritāṃ kṛtvā māyāṃtatripraveṣṭitaṃ ।
kuḍyacāphalake likhyaṃ bhūrje vā tanumadhyame ।।

pītadravyaistambavṛtyā likhitaṃ chādayedaru ।
iṣṭikā chāditaṃ kāryaṃ stambhaye hantumudyataṃ ।।

vākstambhaṃ sainyasaṃstambhaṃ garbhastambhaṃ sakṛdbhavet ।
stambhayenmantramukhyeyaṃ tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।

pragṛhya kṛkalāsaṃ vai tasyāṃ cakre niveśayet ।
sūcūkṛtaṃ raktasūtraṃ sā ca tāmramayī kuru ।।

adhorddhāreṇa niṣkrānta tatsūtraṃ laṃghaye bhayā ।
sā raktaṃ sravate bhūrisaśukraṃ pilvilaṃ bahu ।।

pratīpollaṃghanātsvasthāṃ vrajate sā na saṃśayaḥ ।
tameva raktakaṃṭhaṃ vai tasya kuryādamuṃvidhiṃ ।।

prakṣipetkuṃbhamadhye tu tridinaṃ tamupeśayet ।
chinnanāsāmugdhakāsmai bhakṣārthaṃ dāyayedgiriḥ ।।

vadviṣṭāṃ tu samādāya yasya nāsā vilepayet ।
chinnā pradṛśyate tasya guḍakṣīreṇa mokṣaṇaṃ ।।

tasyaiva tālakaṃ deyaṃ bhojane surasuṃdari ।
(175b)
tadviṣṭayā liptahasto nayanāṃ tāḍanaṃ haret ।।

citraṃ vā citrabhittisthaṃ muṣṭibaṃdhātsamāharet ।
atha strī saṃbhavaṃ gṛhyā jarāyuṃ tālamiśritāṃ ।।

taddhūpanā citrabhittau citraṃ rodāpayedbhṛśaṃ ।
puṃsā svakīlakaṃ gṛhya pramāṇā caturaṃgulaṃ ।।

mahāraktena vā liptaṃ sarvvasya vadane kṣipet ।
arijvare nikhātavyaṃ nakṣatre vahnidaivate ।।

saptarātreṇa sā kṛtyā tatra devi prajāyate ।
vinaśyate gṛhaṃ sarvvaṃ śmaśānasadṛśaṃ bhavet ।।

brahmacaṃḍiviḍālākhuromāsarpasya kaṃcukaṃ ।
gavāsthi mānuṣāsthi ca tāsāṃ viṣṭvā prayeśayet ।।

catuṣyadantakāṣṭhe svadantaṃ dhūpakaṃ dadet ।
animantraśire dantaṃ vidveṣamubhayorapi ।।

atra mantraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu siddhipradaṃ mahat ।
praṇavaṃ prāksamuddhṛtya prītichedana vai paraṃ ।।

mohanīti padaṃ paścādamukasyāmukena vai ।
prītichedaṃ kariṣyāmi svāhāntapadabhūṣitā ।।

vidyeyaṃ mantrane proktā tavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi ghoramāraṇamadbhutaṃ ।।

praṇavaṃ proddhareccādau ḍambaraṃ tu dvidhā punaḥ ।
marmāntaṃ vahnisaṃrūḍhaṃ biṃdutaddhaṣmalāṃcchitaṃ ।।

dvidhā datvā bījavaraṃ sadāśivamato dadet ।
bhīmaṃ śaṃkuṃ tataḥ paścāddevadattaṃ tataḥ punaḥ ।।

grasa grasa padaṃ datvā vidyā paṃcadaśākṣarā ।
asthikhaṇḍaṃ samādāya kiṣkamātraṃ pramāṇataḥ ।।

tasya madhye likhennāma maṃtreṇānena buddhimān ।
(176a)
pādapāṃśu viṣāṃgāraiḥ āsurī miśritaistathā ।।

dakṣiṇāśā mukho bhūtvā mṛtākeśena veṣṭayet ।
pūrvvoktadravyasaṃliptaṃ nikhane citimadhyagaṃ ।।

saptāhaṃ pūjayettatra tāvacchatru vinaśyati ।
sākṣādiṃdrasamāyo vai mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

pratyānayanamevātra nāsti bhairavabhāmini ।
atha ghorataraṃ vakṣye māraṇaṃ pāpakārakaṃ ।।

tripadaṃ pūrvvamuddhṛtya ḍaṃbareṇa samanvitaṃ ।
cāpādyaṃ yonikhacitaṃ vāruṇaṃ vāyusaṃsthitaṃ ।।

aṃkuśena samākrāntaṃ cāpāntaṃ yonibhūṣitaṃ ।
yonipūrvvaṃ sapraṇavaṃ saṃhārapadamuddharet ।।

tribiṃdumuddharatyeva bālaṃ bindusamanvitaṃ ।
naraṃ paṃthamathoddhṛtya naivedyaṃ syātpadaṃ punaḥ ।।

grasamāvayavāśānta sadāśivamathoddharet ।
mālasaṃjño niruddhaṃ tatkālarātriprakīrttitā ।।

ekonaviṃśatirvarṇṇā vidyākālabhayaṃkarī ।
citibhasmasamādāya māṣāṭakavimiśritaṃ ।।

pādapāṃśusamāyuktaṃ kuryāt prakṛtimīdṛśaṃ ।
vahnidaivatyarikṣeṇa vaktragrīvāṃ vicetanāṃ ।।

prādeśamātrā karttavyā viṣāsṛggramarairlikhet ।
vidyānāmavimiśre tu hadaye tasya nikṣipet ।।

saṃhāramudrā dātavyā sorustāḍanamūrdhikā ।
punaḥ punaśca sadghoraṃ hikkāsaṃhāravarṇṇayuk ।।

vīrapuṣpaiḥ samarcyeta tadrūpaṃ tadbaliṃ dadet ।
te naivā lepayeccaināṃ pratimāṃ niśigocare ।।

saptarātrāttu nayate ṛddhimantakamaṃdiraṃ ।
(176b)
tulyādho tāpitāṃ vāpta tadguptaṃ sādhakeśvaraḥ ।।

dāhajvaraṃ tato jāyeta sphoṭāṃ vai vahnivarṇṇakāṃ ।
toyamadhyetha cainyastāṃ pītaṃ śleṣmaṃ ca jāyate ।।

gṛhorddhagā śoṣakarī pratimā nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
śuktamadhyagatā dāhaṃ gopayet kṛmikārikā ।।

kṣāramadhyagatā caiṣā śāśirorjirjanayedbhṛśaṃ ।
evaṃ karma prakarttavyaṃ saṃhārodayakārakaṃ ।।

śanibhaumadine saure triṣu pūrvvāsu kārayet ।
bharaṇyāmathavā śleṣā māghāmāsādya vā sadā ।।

deśabhaṃge mahāduḥkhe bhaye śokethavā priye ।
karttavyameva niyataṃ māraṇaṃ ghorameva hi ।।

uddhṛtya bhavate śānti kṣīrasnānānna saṃśayaḥ ।
uddha * sya manuṣyasya dakṣiṇāntarjanī priye ।।

grāhaye dakṣiṇāṃguṣṭha tarjanībhyāṃ nareśvaraḥ ।
yadgṛhe bhrāmayettāṃ vai tadgṛhe sarvvamāraṇaṃ ।।

bhavatyeva na saṃdeho yogarājaprabhāvataḥ ।
pāśodbaṃdhaṃ patasyaivaṃ citau kakṣaṃ pradāhaye ।।

tadbhasmaliptapuṣpāṇi kṣiptāni vyomni sāṃprataṃ ।
antarddhānaṃ prayāntyete mahadāścaryakāriṇaḥ ।।

kūpavaktreṇa subhage kṛtvā nāmikamaṃtraṇaṃ ।
savisargeṇa tenaiva viṣaṃ karagataṃ haret ।।

bhakṣayedbhaktavattaṃ vai devināstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।
oṣadhī bala devasya yā sā praharaṇāhvayā ।।

payoguḍasamāyuktāṃ nibena sahasaṃyutāṃ ।
(177a)
pāyayeta ca yā nārī garbhaṃ tasya na saṃśayaḥ ।।

patatyeva mahābhāge vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
pārāvatajarāyurvai gṛhītvā śaṃkhapuṣpikīṃ ।।

evaṃ vai guṭikāṃ kṛtvā bhasmapūrṇāṃ tu khorikāṃ ।
tasya coparitāṃ kṛtvā muktā vrajati khorikāṃ ।।

pucchadevābhidhaṃ mantraṃ gāruḍaṃ kīrttitaṃ ca yat ।
taṃ likhyā chādayettena nidhāni pradarśayet ।।

anenaiva hi maṃtreṇa oṣadhena ca suvrate ।
liptāśca sarṣapāḥ samyak kurvvantyevārthasevanāt ।।

mātuliṃgasya bījaistu tailamevāharedgiriḥ ।
tattailaṃ tāmrakhaṃḍasthaṃ madhyāhnasthe divākare ।।

sarathaṃ dṛśyate tatra sūryaṃ saparivārakaṃ ।
sarūpaṃ divyadehaṃ ca nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

ghṛtamādau tu pānārthaṃ yasya dattaṃ punardadet ।
suśītaṃ cānupānīyaṃ saviśvaṃ tena paśyati ।।

kapotamāṃsaṃ airandrakāṣṭhapakvaṃ niveśayet ।
gulmakṛtparamaṃ proktaṃ satyametadbravīmi te ।।

mayūramāṃsaṃ śubhage tai(la) sārṣipabharjitaṃ ।
jvareṇa mārayatyāśu nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

kṛṣṇatailena deveśi dāhajvarakaraṃ mahat ।
krakacaṃ mārayatyāśubharjitaṃ parameśvari ।।

kausuṃbha(bho)naivenaina bhuktamātrānna saṃśayaḥ ।
kāpiṃjalaṃ tathā māṃsaṃ taitiraṃ vāpi suvrate ।।

māhiṣeṇa ghṛtenaiva kaṭurājitatailataḥ ।
bharjanādbhakṣaṇādeva ākālapalitākaraṃ ।।
(177b)
asalītailasaṃpakvaṃthamāṃsaṃ tu bhakṣitaṃ ।
anekarogajanakaṃ mahāvyādhikaraṃ smṛtaṃ ।।

haṃsamāṃsaṃ tathā kāgaṃ ghṛtasaṃbharjitaṃ yadā ।
tadā saṃbhakṣaṇāddevi vahnimādyaṃ kariṣyati ।।

śvivīsaṃjananaṃ vāpi jñeyametanmahābalaṃ ।
godhāmāṃsaṃ tathā pakvaṃ kaṭubhuṃvīrasena tu ।।

anena romakṛt proktaṃ devināstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।
kukkuṭasya tathā māṃsaṃ mātulyaṃ tailavarjitaṃ ।।

prameha vyādhikṛtproktaṃ śatrūṇāṃ duṣṭacetasāṃ ।
kṛkalāsaṃbhojanena dattaṃ rogakaraṃ tathā ।।

nāpitaṃ nāma yatkīṭabhojanena sahaiva tu ।
anāhena bhṛśaṃ devi mārayennātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

rogeṇa bhramaraṃ samyak kṛṣkasarpaṃ ca yakṣmanā ।
gulmodari na maṃḍūkaṃ mārayennātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

nīlamakṣikasaṃyuktā kāṣṭhabhārikasaṃyutāṃ ।
bhojanādagdhakṛtproktaṃ pṛthagānāha śitritāṃ ।।

kṣārakīṭaṃ pānadantaṃ citrīkaraṇameva ca ।
unmādanāntakṛtproktaṃ kauṃḍinyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

pratīkīṭamapasmāraṃ karaṇaṃ bhakṣmaṇātsmṛtaṃ ।
chuchudarī ca māṃsaṃ vai anāhenāntakārakaṃ ।।

paśupālābhidhaṃ kīṭaṃ mārayedrājayakṣmaṇā ।
uccaṭāṅgaṃ tathā kīṭaṃ grahajvaravipoṣakaṃ ।।

gomārikā kṛcchrakarī druṃdrutaṃ raktapittakṛt ।
udaravyādhinā samyaṅmārayetsā jalaukasāṃ ।।

abhīṣṭaṃ gāntakṛjñeyo gṛhāṃgolla na saṃśayaḥ ।
(178a)
indragopaṃ kīṭavaraṃ mūtrakṛcchreṇa mārayet ।।

pipīlikā bhojanārthaṃ yasya devi pradīyate ।
piṭakairmārayettaṃ vai satyametanna cānyathā ।।

nāgikāṃ pārśvaśūlena mārayennātra saṃśayaḥ ।
ete cānyāśca bahavaḥ kuhakāḥ saṃprakīrttitāḥ ।।

eṣāmupasamorthāya bravīmīdamuttamaṃ ।
abhayā citrakaṃ caiva tṛvṛtā ca tathāparā ।।

śatāvarā guḍūcī ca tathā dagdharuhāparā ।
somarājā indrahastā mahākarkaṭikāparā ।।

baṃdhyā cāṃḍālikā cānyā tathānyā gaṃdhanākulī ।
mahāgaṃdhā vīrabhayā tathā karkaṭakāparā ।।

triphalātyūṣaṇaṃ caiva pātāśva sa manāgikāḥ ।
navanīta tathā kṣaudrabhāvinā maṃtritā sakṛt ।।

bhakṣanānnaśyati viṣaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।
kūhako kṛttimaṃ vāpi sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamantathā ।।

nāśayedagadendreyaṃ mahāmṛtavapurddharaḥ ।
atha pratyaṃgirāṃ devīṃ kuhakādivighātinīṃ ।।

sarvvālakṣmīpraśamanī japetsādhakasattamaḥ ।
tasyoddhāramahaṃ vakṣye śṛṇu padmadale kṣaṇe ।।

sṛṣṭiyuktaṃ yonipūrvvaṃ tadvaccāmṛtamuddharet ।
tripūrvvaṃ kevalaṃ deyaṃ hayamarmasthitaṃ punaḥ ।।

tribiṃdulāṃcchitaṃ kṛtvā niṣedhaṃ tava muddharet ।
punaḥ somaṃ sṛṣṭiyutaṃ tadvadyonyādyamuddharet ।।

saptākṣarā samākhyātā caṇḍakālī mahābalā ।
sarvvapāpapraśamanī sarvvāriṣṭapramardanī ।।
(178b)
sarvvasaṃkaradoṣaghnī duṣṭadāridryamardanī ।
sarvvavyādhipraśamanī sarvvayaṃtrārgalāpahā ।।

sarvvaghātapradalanī sarvvasampadguṇāvahā ।
sarvvābhāgyakṣayakarī mahābhāgyāvarohaṇī ।।

liṃgātpāṭhādipāpāni līlayā nāśayiṣyati ।
yataḥ prabhṛti karṇṇasthā vidyeyaṃ ghoradarśanā ।।

tataḥ prabhṛti sarvvatra bhairaveva prapūjyate ।
nāsya saṃvādane kaścidduṣṭamaṃtrārgalādikaḥ ।।

tasyāriṣṭo devadevi yaḥ samācarate naraḥ ।
lakṣakoṭiguṇaṃ samyaktasyaiva pratibādhate ।।

mantrasiddhirbhavettasya yasyeyaṃ hṛdi saṃsthitā ।
nānayā sadṛśī vidyā bhuviveha ca dṛśyate ।।

eṣā dīptatarā ghorā ghoracaṃḍā maholvaṇā ।
tannāsti yanna dahate tannāsti yanna sādhayet ।।

pratyaṃgirā mahākālī smaraṇātsādhakātmanāṃ ।
kālavelananiyamaṃ na ca dhyānajapādikaṃ ।।

na vrataṃ na tapaścāsya vidyate bhuvaneśvari ।
kevalaṃ smaraṇādeva sarvvakāryārthasādhanī ।।

chidyate naiva kenāpi kadācitkuliśodari ।
smaraṇādeva suśroṇi dravante sarvvayoṣitaḥ ।।

vihvalānmadanāntāstāḥ sādhakā liṃgaṇotsukā ।
bhavatyeva mahābhāge vidyā darpahatāḥ sadā ।।

iyaṃ kāmaraṇī khyātā sarvvamaṃtrāraṇī śivā ।
hṛccakramadhyakuhare satataṃ pravirājate ।।

sadāspaṃdakṛtagrāsā tathā caṃḍeśvarī smṛtā ।
(179a)
cicceta dhāmnidhūryā sā dhīrāryapravikāsinī ।।

smaraṇādeva niyataṃ pāśajālātimohikāṃ ।
saptakoṭyastu mantrāṇāṃ yasyāddehātsamutthitaḥ ।।

prativarṇṇāntarā devi saptakoṭeśvarī tathā ।
asyāḥ prabhāvaṃ vīryaṃ ca vyāptirvyaktiparākramaṃ ।।

vaktuṃ na śakyate devi sādākhyairvatsarāyutaiḥ ।
catuḥpīṭheṣu deveśi smaraṇāddīkṣito bhavet ।।

abhiṣi * śca subhage bhairavevaṃ bhaviṣyati ।
pūjyo mānyaśca jāyeta sarvvasampadguṇāvahaṃ ।।

yadā japennaravarastadā siddhikare sthitā ।
evaṃ tava samākhyātā vidyā ghoraparākramā ।।

yasyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa nirviśaṃkaścanma(hīṃ) ।
yatra yatra vrajetpāśu tatra bhairavavadbhavet ।।
kimanyat kathayiṣyāmi vada sarvveśvareśvarī ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke prayogamaṃjaryākuhakādividhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

evaṃ samākarṇya girīndra kanyā,
sarvveśvarādyogavarānanekāt ।
yantrārṇavaṃ śrotumalaṃ tadā sā,
uvāca devī jagatāṃ hitāya ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrutaṃ mayā mahādeva sarvvaśāstrārthasaṃgrahaṃ ।
asyāṃ prayoga maṃjaryāṃ madhye vai rāviṇī kule ।।

guhyataṃtrodbhavaṃ sarvvaṃ mantrasadbhāvameva ca ।
nanu tṛptāsmi deveśa mantrayogaṃ binā hara ।।

yantrārṇṇavaṃ vada vibho sadyaḥ pratyayakārakaṃ ।
(179b)
yena saṃlikhyamātreṇa narāḥ siddhyati bhūtale ।।

vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣamāraṇoccāṭanādi ca ।
stambhakāryāṇi sarvvāṇi nigrahāścāpyanekadhā ।।

dravyayogayogādivadāṃdhakanisūdana ।
saṃsāre duḥkhabahule ādhivyādhi prapīḍite ।।

alpāyuṣolpavittaśca hīna satvāśca mānavāḥ ।
śatrubhiḥ pīḍyamānānāṃ prāṇadravyāpahāribhiḥ ।।

yathā teṣāṃ bhave tuṣṭistathā me kathaya prabho ।
evaṃ niśamya deveśyā vacanaṃ parameśvaraḥ ।।
procaret punarevāsyā yantrayogaṃ yathārthataḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

kathitantu mayā tubhyaṃ sarvvaśāstreṣu suṃdari ।
yantrayogaṃ binā bhadre sukhaṃ na prāpyate naraḥ ।।

tathā śṛṇuṣva deveśi kathayāmi tavākhilaṃ ।
mahāsaṃmohane tantre śatakoṭipravistare ।।

tantrakhyātaṃ yatra yogaṃ saṃkṣepādiha kathyate ।
tīvrabhāvena siddhyanti yantradravyabalena tu ।।

yā sā pūrvvaṃ mayā tubhyaṃ mātṛkā pratipāditā ।
tāṃ prapūjya mahābhāge sarvvadravyopacārataḥ ।।
tataḥ samuddharedyantrāṃ yathāvadanupūrvvaśaḥ ।।

ananta pūrvvaṃ punarasya pūrvvaṃ,
pūrvvādyapūrvvīmamṛtasya pīṭhaṃ ।
likheta rūdaṃ sa nabho sa caṃdraṃ,
piṃḍottamaṃ yaṃtravaraṃ ca bhadre ।।

bījaṃ likhedbhūrjapaṭetha vastre,
gorocanā caṃdana raktamiśraṃ ।
sādhyasya saṃjñā tadanantaraṃ hi,
vaśī kururvarṇṇa matastu deyaṃ ।।

pakṣeṇa lekhyaṃ śikhinaśca jāte,
sūtreṇa baddhā dyutiraktakena ।
(180a)
pūjāṃ ca kṛtvā divasaikameka,
nikṣipya garbhakramapaṇyacāraṃ ।।

vāsaṃ ca raktaṃ dṛḍhabhāvanā ca,
kṛtvā supūjāṃ punarevata * ।
* * * * * * * * * * * *,
* * * * * * * * * * * * ।।

* * * * * * * * * * * * ।
* * * * * * * * * * * * ,
* * * * * * * * * * * * ।।

* * * * * * * * * * * * ,
* * * * * * * * * * * * ।
* * * * * * * * * * * * ,
* * * * * * * * * * * * ।।

* * * * * * * * * * * * * * * raṃ ।
kṣakārodanagaṃ nāma sādhyasya tu samālikhet ।।

japādīnāṃ tu ye mantrāḥ kuṭādyāṃ dikṣu saṃsthitāḥ ।
catuṣkalaṃ paraṃ bījaṃ vidiśāstraṃ samālikhet ।।

svaraṣoḍaśakaṃ bāhye ātmanatveti saṃsthitaṃ ।
tadbāhyekādimāntena vidyātatvena veṣṭayet ।।

tatorddhe śivatatvaṃ ca yā dikṣāntasamantataḥ ।
caturdikṣvaṅkuśaṃ bāhye ā(tmanā) veṣṭayettridhā ।।
(180b)
evaṃ likhitvā vidhivadvocanāyā varānane ।
samyaṅniśāyāṃ saṃpūjya pratimāṃ madanodbhavāṃ ।।

kāradaivaṃ vidhānena ta * sthaṃ pūjayetpunaḥ ।
pūjya saptadinaṃ bhadre tāpayet khadirāgninā ।।

tāpyamānā maheśāni prajāpatisamo yadi ।
vaśamānayate kṣipraṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।। 4 ।।

vaśye ।।

ākarṣaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sādhakaiḥ kriyate yathā ।
raktaṃ paṭaṃ samādāya cāpaṃ tatra samālikhet ।।

kusuṃbhena varārohe rocanā sahite na ca ।
kroṅkārādyaṃ saruddhaṃ ca māyobhayasamanvitaṃ ।।

pūjayitvā vidhānena khanitvā khādanikṣipet ।
taddiśābhimukho bhūtvā secayettaṃḍulāṃbunā ।।

siddhyamāne maheśāni āśayecchatayojanāt ।
etadyantraṃ maheśāni raktasūtreṇa veṣṭayet ।। 5 ।।

bhūrjapatre tu saṃlikhya nāma sādhyasya codare ।
dvitīyena svareṇaiva ukāraṃ veṣṭayet priye ।।

māyayā yatnasaṃruddhaṃ raktasūtreṇa veṣṭayet ।
kāṃjilyāntaḥ kṣipedyantraṃ pūjayettadiśā sthitaṃ ।।
baṃdhanasthopi deveśi kṣaṇādāgacchate dhruvaṃ ।। 6 ।।

hakārodaragaṃ nāma sādhyasya tu samālikhet ।
kṣīravṛkṣātva māyāṃ tu ikāro bhayasaṃyutaṃ ।।

pūjayitvā vidhānena nāṃgapraśravaṇe kṣipet ।
yantraṃ saṃsevyamānosau kṣaṇādāgacchate balāt ।। 7 ।।

phāntamadhyagataṃ nāma sādhyasya varavarṇini ।
ṣaṣṭhasvarā dantamuktā praṣṭu * śravaṇā kṣipet ।।
(181a)
siddhyamānoṣṭu sūtreṇa ānayedbhūdharāṇyapi ।। 8 ।। ।।

ākarṣaṇavidhiḥ ।। ।।

stambhanaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi vāyorapi sureśvari ।
hrīṃkāramadhyagaṃ nāma kalāṣoḍaśasaṃyutaṃ ।।

kṣīkāreṇa tridhā veṣṭya rajanyā lekhanena tu ।
pītasūtreṇa saṃveṣṭya śilāyādho vinikṣipet ।।
gatistambhaṃ paraṃ hyetatkhageśasyāpi suṃdari ।। 1 ।।

lakāramadhyagaṃ nāma saṃvṛtasvaraveṣṭitaṃ ।
caturasraṃ puraṃ bāhye vṛkṣādho nikṣipet priye ।।
pītapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya staṃbhayettridaśānapi ।। 2 ।।

nāmākṣarāṇi sarvvāṇi lakāroparilekhayet ।
ikārādyantaruddhāni māyayā veṣṭayettridhā ।।

pītapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya śilādho varavarṇini ।
gatistaṃbhaṃ paraṃ hyetanmārutasyāpi suṃdari ।। 11 ।। 3 ।।

hodaraṃ sādhya nāmaṃtu lakārādyaṃtasaṃyutaṃ ।
indrantu maṇḍalaṃ bāhye vajrākrāntaṃ maheśvari ।।

kuṃbhakāramṛdāyāntu pratimāṃ kārayecchubhāṃ ।
tasyāsye nikṣipedyantraṃ śaṃkunāsthimayena tu ।।

jihvāvedhyā varārohe māṣacūrṇe vilepayet ।
śarāvasaṃpuṭe kṛtvā nikhanetpitṛmaṃdire ।।
harate vā kṛtervācaṃ manujeṣvapi kā kathā ।। 12 ।। 4 ।।

stambhanavidhiḥ ।। ।।

vidveṣaṇaṃ pravakṣyāmi śatrūṇāṃ kriyate yathā ।
kṣodare sā nāmaṃ tu harādyaṃtarodhitaṃ ।।

āgneya maṇḍalāntasthaṃ tadbāhye vāruṇaṃ puraṃ ।
(181b)
māhiṣeṇā sṛjālikhya uṣṭracarmaṇi lekhayet ।।
vidveṣaṇena miṃdreṇa ratiḥ kāmena suṃdari ।। 13 ।।

mārjāravayasā likhya nāmaṃ vai śvānacarmaṇi ।
yāntavarṇādyāntayuktaṃ vāṃtenādhorddhasaṃyutaṃ ।।

nīlasūtreṇa saṃveṣṭya nikhanedvaśamānayet ।
vidviṣṭo bhavate jaṃtu tāṃ diśaṃ pratipadyate ।। 18 ।। 3 ।।

ulūkakākasaṃmiśraṃ raktasaṃmiśrya bhāvayet ।
sādhya gotraṃ likhettena rephe nādyaṃtarodhitaṃ ।।

caturasraṃ puraṃ bāhye tadbāhye vahnimaṇḍalaṃ ।
pītasūtreṇa saṃveṣṭya rājarathyāṃ nidhārayet ।।
vidveṣaṇamavāpnoti ripuḥ sarvvajanasya tu । 18 । 2 ।।

mārjārasya tu raktena nāma sādhyasya cālikhet ।
mūṣakasya tvacāyāṃ tu hetvādāvarttasaṃyutaṃ ।।

rathyākarparakau gṛhya madhyatobhyāṃ prapūjayet ।
ekavṛkṣe tvadhaḥ kṣiptaṃ vidveṣaṃ sarvvajaṃtuṣu ।। 1 ।।

vidveṣaṇavidhiḥ ।। ।।

uccāṭanaṃ pravakṣyāmi śatrūṇāṃ duṣṭacetasāṃ ।
kākapakṣeṇa saṃkhasya nāmaṃ gairika dhātunā ।।

yakārādyaṃtasaṃyuktaṃ paphārobhayasaṃpuṭaṃ ।
nīlasūtreṇa saṃveṣṭya kākakaṃṭhe tu baṃdhayet ।।
uccāṭena samākhyātaṃ giriṇāmapi suṃdari ।। 71 । 700 ।।

uṣṭracarmalikhennāma kākaraktena suṃdari ।
nāmākṣarāṇi samāṇi vāyubījorddhago likhet ।।

sa eva savisargantu ādyante tasya dāpayet ।
uṣṭrorddhabaṃdhayeccarmaṃ uccāṭe sthāvarāṇyapi ।। 78 । 7 ।।
(182a)
uṣṭraraktena saṃlikhya saṃjñāsu tapasā sthitaḥ ।
yakārodyantasaṃruddhaṃ phaṭkārājyena saṃyutaṃ ।।
kṛṣṇapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya uccāṭabadhabaṃdhanāt ।। 78 । 3 ।।

yakārodaragaṃ nāma kākapakṣeṇa lekhayet ।
utmattasya rasenaiva phaṭkārobhayasaṃsthitaṃ ।।

vāyavyamaṃḍalāntastha uṣṭrakaṃṭhe nibaṃdhayet ।।
uccāṭanaṃ samākhyātaṃ śatrūṇāṃ garvitātmanāṃ ।। 20 । 8 ।।

uccāṭanaṃ ।। ।।

nigrahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śatrūṇāṃ kriyate yathā ।
viṣeṇa raktamiśreṇa nāmamasthiśalākayā ।।
bhūrjapatre likhitvā tu rephādyantanirodhitaṃ ।। 17 । 1 ।।

anenaiva prayogeṇa ṣakārodaramadhyagaṃ ।
uragasya śiro gṛhya sūkṣmācūrṇaṃ tu kārayet ।।

cūrṇamiśraṃ mahadyantraṃ prakṣipeccāyudhāśraye ।
raktātīsārakaṃ ghoraṃ śatrūṇāṃ kriyate dhruvaṃ ।। 77 । 7 ।।

viṣonmattarasenaiva bhūrjanāmaṃ tu lekhayet ।
phakārādyena saṃruddhaṃ rephādorddha samanvitaṃ ।।

āgneya maṇḍalāntasthaṃ sthāpya gomūtramadhyagaṃ ।
śatrūṇāntu śiraḥ śūlaṃ bhavate na vicāraṇā ।। 23 । 3 ।।

anenaiva prayogena nāmamādyantasaṃyutaṃ ।
śaśisūryātmakairbījairveṣṭayedvaravarṇini ।।

trikoṇamaṇḍalāntasthaṃ culyādho saṃpratāpayet ।
cakṣubhraṃśaṃ ṛpūṇāntu bhavate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।। 2 । 4 ।।

nigrahaṃ ।। ।।

māraṇaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi ghoraśatrubhayaṃkaraṃ ।
(182b)
apakārakarāṇāntu sādhakaiḥ kriyate yathā ।।

kapālakhaṇḍamādāya tābhyāṃ nāma samālikhet ।
viṣeṇa raktamiśrena citāṃgārayutena ca ।।

rāphādorddhagataṃ varṇṇaṃ pratyekaṃ phadyadāntagaṃ ।
trikoṇamaṇḍale bāhye prakṣipeccitimadhyataḥ ।।

saptarātre vyatikrānte rātrau pretavane sthitaḥ ।
kapālayugmaṃ saṃgṛhya gharṣayeta parasparaṃ ।।

nagnonmattaśikho bhūtvā krodhāviṣṭastu sādhakaḥ ।
tāvannigharṣayettautu yāvacca śakalīgatau ।।

sakalau tu samādāya homayeccitimadhyataḥ ।
homānte samanuprāpte raktapūrṇāhutiṃ juhet ।।

pūrvvoktairdravyasaṃbhāraiḥ kṛṣṇasūtreṇa veṣṭayet ।
śuktamāṣāḍhasaṃyuktātkuryātputtalikāṃ śubhāṃ ।।

tasyāṃ hṛdi kṣipedyantraṃ śaṃkunāsthimayena tu ।
hṛdayaṃ kīlayitvā tu sthāpayeccitimadhyataḥ ।।
saptarātre vyatikrānte amarānapi mārayet ।। 17 । 3 ।।

ebhirdrarvyairlikhennāma sādhako vīrake ripuṃ ।
phaṭkārādyantasaṃruddhaṃ rephādhorddhasamanvitaṃ ।।

āgneyamaṇḍalāntasthaṃ taccāsthi sakale kṣipet ।
nikhane citimadhye tu vahniṃ prajvālya copari ।।
saptarātre vyatikrānte mārayettriśānapi ।।

māraṇavidhiḥ ।। ।।

āpyāyanaṃ pravakṣyāmi mahāmṛtyuṃjayaṃ paraṃ ।
jīvamadhye likhennāma rocanā kuṃkumena tu ।।

kalaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirveṣṭya * pakārodamadhyataḥ ।
ṭhādivarṇaṃ punarbbāhye ṭakārānte punarvaset ।।
(183a)
kṣīramadhye likhedyantraṃ mahāmṛtyuvināśanaṃ ।। 7 ।।

pūrvvavatsaṃlikhennāma sakārodaragaṃ priye ।
sarvvavarṇṇamayaṃ bījaṃ caturdikṣu niveśayet ।।
māyābījaṃ mahābījaṃ vidiśasthaṃ samālikhet ।।

ṭhakāraṃ madhyagaṃ kṛtvā kalaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirvṛtaṃ ।
kūṭamantraṃ tu yat proktaṃ anantasya maheśvari ।।

tanmadhye nikṣipitvā tu māyayā veṣṭitaṃ tridhā ।
trilohaveṣṭitaṃ kṛtvā kaṃṭhe vaṭkaukhabaṃdhayet ।।

athavā kṣīramadhye tu sthāpayedvaravarṇṇini ।
mahāmṛtyuṃjayā hyeṣā mayākhyātā sugopitā ।। 30 ।। 7 ।।

jīvamadhye likhennāma pūrvvavatsurasuṃdari ।
śaśisūryātmakairbījaiḥ sakalau pariveṣṭayet ।।

kṣakārodaragaṃ kṛtvā māyayā veṣṭayettridhā ।
jaṃtunā veṣṭayitvā tu śuklapuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet ।।
kaṃṭhe bāhau śikhāyāṃ ca dhārayenmṛtyunāśanaṃ ।। 21 । 3 ।।

codare saṃlikhennāma sodare punareva tu ।
khodare tu punarlikhya lodare punareva tu ।।

trikoṇamaṇḍalāntasthaṃ samastaṃ codare kṣipet ।
kṣodare tu punaḥ sarvvā caturdikṣu kuśena tu ।।

māyayā veṣṭayitvā tu caturasraṃ punaḥ punaḥ ।
evaṃ likhitvā vidhiva - tamadhye vinikṣipet ।।

ghṛtakṣīrasya madhye tu śuklapuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet ।
mahāpratyaṃgirā hyeṣā mahāmṛtyuvināśanī ।।

etadyantravaraṃ devi mayākhyātaṃ sugopitaṃ ।
ma(hā)saṃmohane tantre śatakoṭipravistare ।
(183b)
purākhyātaṃ punaścādya tantrarāje jayadrathe ।।

uddhṛtaṃ paramaṃ hyetannāmnā vai yantrasaṃgrahaṃ ।
nāśiṣyāya pradātavyaṃ nāgurudveṣiṇe tathā ।।

gurudevāgnibhaktāya śaktitantraratāya ca ।
satvasāhasayuktāya śiṣyāya vitatātmane ।। 28 ।।

ārppayavidhiḥ ।। ।।

dātavyaṃ paramaṃ hyeta cintāmaṇi * saṃ priye ।। ।।

samāptaṃ yantrasaṃgrahaṃ ।। ।।

pravakṣye divyamatulaṃ sarvvayaṃtrārthasaṃgrahaṃ ।
yāgahomādisaṃyuktaṃ saubhāgyakaraṇaṃ śubhaṃ ।।

bahudhā caiva matulaṃ divyakarmakaraṃ śubhaṃ ।
vaśyādimāraṇāntāni śāntikādīni yāni ca ।।

amoghānyapyavadhyāni japasādhyāni yāni ca ।
mantrayuktānikarmāṇi ṛkṣayogayutāni ca ।।

kathayāmi samāsena sādhakānāṃ hitāya vai ।
samyakprayogānyetāni siddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

vedamādyaṃ ca tasyāntamaṃkuśe (rā)hataṃ punaḥ ।
dvijihvaṃ somasodaṃsaṃ bhrūlatā bheditaṃ tataḥ ।।

yamāntāntaṃ yamaṃ caiva tasyāntaṃ ca tataḥ punaḥ ।
tattribhedaṃ ca tasyādyaṃ tasyāntena śirānvitaṃ ।।

sodaṃśaṃ bhrūlatorddhaṃ ca yamāntāntaṃ tataḥ punaḥ ।
aṃkuśāntamadhorddhvaṃ ca tattribhedaṃ tataḥ punaḥ ।।

bhrūlatorddhena saṃbhinnaṃ saudaṃsaṃ bedhakaṃ tataḥ ।
bedhakaṃ ca tato deva bedhakaṃ bhrūlatānvitaṃ ।।

niruddhaṃ śaktipūrvvaṃ ca ṭeṭitaṃ tasya cāntimaṃ ।
aśvarāntaṃ samuddhṛtya tasya cāpyaṣṭamānvitaṃ ।।
(184a)
tasya cādyaṃ samuddhṛtya yojayet kramaśaḥ punaḥ ।
devaḍaṃvaramuddhṛtya kvedakaṃ somapūrvvakaṃ ।।

devacakrena bhetavyaṃ tasyāntaṃ na ca tadbhavaṃ ।
ṭeṭibhaṃ cāṃkuśe bhitvā janāntaṃ tasya cāntimaṃ ।।

evaṃ cakrāntare maṃtrā nyāsapūrvvantataḥ punaḥ ।
adhunā cakrapṛṣṭhe tu kuṃḍalākāravidyayā ।।

vyāpinī ca samuddhṛtya tasya mātā visa * ṇī ।
purastā somamuddhṛtya hyudare ṭīṭibhānvitaṃ ।।

somamasvarasaṃyuktaṃ uddharettadanantaraṃ ।
sthirāntaṃ śaktipūrvvaṃ ca chedakaṃ ca tataḥ punaḥ ।।

ṭeṭibhaṃ śaktipūrvve ca trayamekatra yojayet ।
aśvāntamaṃkuśākrāntaṃ rāvadyāṃ ca vidhā punaḥ ।।

yamāntāṃ tena bhetavyaṃ yamāntaṃ ca yamantataḥ ।
aṃkuśena śirābhinnaṃ yamāntāntaṃ tribiṃdukaṃ ।।

nirguṇaṃ rāvamādyādyaṃ nirguṇaṃ ḍambarānvitaṃ ।
yamāntāñca tataḥ kṛtvā kaṃdalākuṭilānvitaṃ ।।

krodhameva samuddhṛtya satvāntaguṇayojitaṃ ।
pūrvvamāntaṃ tṛtīyena dvirabhyāsapadena tu ।।

niṣaṭke tasya coddhṛtya tadantena vibheditaṃ ।
hṛdisthaṃ śaktipūrvvaṃ ca hṛdisthaṃ tadanantaraṃ ।।

krodhāmanāntamākāśaṃ kadalaṃ tadanantaraṃ ।
ravipūrvvaṃ samuddhṛtya maṃtreśaṃ mantranāyakaṃ ।।

anena maṃtrarājena bhūrbhuvaṃ vaśamānayet ।
mārayedasurānsarvvān mohayedacarācaraṃ ।।

śoṣayetsthāvarān kruddhaścūrṇṇayeddhimavānapi ।
(184b)
stambhayenmārutaṃ cāpi dhārayetsacarācaraṃ ।।

asyaiva mantrarājasya na dīkṣā nāgnitarpaṇaṃ ।
nakṣatrayogasaṃyuktaṃ cakrasthā sādhayedbhṛśaṃ ।।

citrākāraṃ ca kurute lakṣajāpānna saṃśayaḥ ।
dvādaśāraṃ likheccakraṃ pretocchiṣṭapadaṃ tathā ।।

śmaśānāṃgārasaṃghṛṣṭamunmattakarasānvitaṃ ।
kākapakṣeṇa saṃlikhya nakṣatre rudradaivate ।।

śmaśāne nikhanetsamyagvāyavyābhimukhasthitaḥ ।
kṣipramuccāṭayasthānāt kṣipraṃ nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।।

māraṇādīni karmāṇi śāntikādīni yāni tu ।
praserādīni sarvvāṇi sādhaketyanisaṃbalāt ।।

nakṣatrāṇi ca sarvvāṇi divasā nimiṣānyathā ।
pravakṣyāmi samāsena yantrasādhanasiddhidā ।।

māraṇe yama dehasthaṃ nakṣatre yamadaivate ।
vidveṣo mahiṣāśvatthāmaśleṣā * likhedbudhaḥ ।।

preśaṇe karabhasthaṃ ca hastena tu samālikhet ।
rohiṇye śāntike likhya caṃdrasaṃsthātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

nārīṇāṃ hṛdi vaśye syāt pu(ṣparkṣa)ṇa ca taṃ likhet ।
pauṣṭike hayapṛṣṭhasthaṃ mūlena tu samālikhet ।।

mūkatve pūrvvaṣāḍhāyāṃ meṣasthā parimālikhet ।
staṃbhane śravaṇe likhya merumadhye na saṃśayaḥ ।।

saunake vahnidehasthaṃ kṛttikāsu likhedbudhaḥ ।
jvale tathaiva saṃlikhyaṃ tadehasthaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ।।

ākarṣe śarabhasthaṃ tu jyeṣṭhāccottaraphālguṇī ।
divyānāṃ stambhane likhya vācanā(nnātra saṃ)śayaḥ ।।
(185a)
sarvvakarmāṇiliṃgasthā na sādhyaṃ sādhayedbalāt ।
lūtajvarādināśaya liṃgasthāṃ ca samālikhet ।।

evamādīni karmāṇi vāmānāḍyānukārayet ।
atha yaṃtrādirājasya mantrakośaṃ suvistaraṃ ।।

pravakṣyāmi samāsena prasenādiṣu sarvvataḥ ।
yena pratyakṣato yāti bhūrbhuvaṃ ca karārpitaṃ ।।

mṛtatretāgnibhasmaṃ ca tadvastreṇa vimiśritaṃ ।
pūjayedvidhivanmaṃtrī mahāsnehena sevayet ।।

śatāvarttaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā haṭhapākena taṃ dahet ।
grāhitaṃ mantrarājena śatamāvartya mantravit ।।

bhasmanānena vidhinā ādarśañcaiva (mā)rjayet ।
arghapuṣpaiśca dhūpaiśca dīpanaṃ caruṇā tathā ।।

tasyāgrato japaṃ kuryācchatamaṣṭādhikaṃ budhaḥ ।
tatastu mantrarājena devadevena maṃtritaṃ ।।

dṛśyate bhūrbhuvaṃ sarvvaṃ mantrate balena tu ।
dṛśyate sarvvalokasthaṃ vāṅmayaṃ yata pravarttitaṃ ।।

sarvvabhaumāvatāroyaṃ prasenā nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
māraṇaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi moghaṃ śatruvināśanaṃ ।।

mṛtavastre tu saṃlikhya cakraṃ cakracaraṃ śubhaṃ ।
śmaśānāṃgārasaṃyuktamasthicūrṇavimiśritaṃ ।।

viṣāsṛglekhanīyāstā likhenmaṃtramanuttamaṃ ।
pūrvvoktasya hṛdisthaṃ ca nāgnamuktaśikhistathā ।।

kṛṣṇapakṣe caturddaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet ।
(mā)saistu vividhairmantraiḥ cakrarājaṃ prapūjayet ।।

śmaśāne nikhanenmantrī pretacakrethavā punaḥ ।
(ni)śisthaḥ krodhasaṃtapto mārayettridaśānapi ।।
(185b)
puruṣānāṃ kathā nāsti yadi syādbhāvitātmanaḥ ।
raktanimbarasābhyāṃ tu arakhūraphalāhakī ।।

mahiṣāścāpi mukhagamadhyasaṃstho likhedbudhaḥ ।
vidveṣayapadairyuktaṃ sādhyanāma niyojitaṃ ।।

śmaśāne nikhanenmaṃtrī madhyāhnasamupasthitaṃ ।
vidveṣayati indrasya manujeṣvatra kā kathā ।।

jātī rasena saṃlikhya karabhasthaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ।
cakraṃ cakravaraṃ divyaṃ dvādaśāraṃ suśobhanaṃ ।।

paṭe rakte tu vidhivatsaṃpuṭāntaṃ nidhāpayet ।
pūjayenmantrarājānaṃ sthāpayenmallakena tu ।।

tridinaṃ saptakaṃ cāpi mantrarājaprabhāvataḥ ।
ripuśca mitratāṃ yāti yadi prāṇāntiko bhavet ।।

kṣīrapṛṣṭhena saṃlikhya caṃdanena tu mantravit ।
cakraṃ cakravaraṃ divyaṃ dehalakṣeṇa lekhayet ।।

kṣīramadhye tu saṃsthāpya śāntirbhavati tasya vai ।
rasena caṃpakasyaiva striyaṃ bhūrje samālikhet ।।

paṭe samālikhettasyāṃ dvādaśāraṃ suśobhanaṃ ।
raktapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya madhumadhye nivedayet ।।

vaśya mānayate kṣipraṃ urvvaśī vā tilottamā ।
bhūrje tu kuṃkumenātha likhedasvasya pṛṣṭhataḥ ।।

ghṛtamadhye likheccakraṃ tacca puṣṭikaraṃ śubhaṃ ।
bhūrjapatre likheccakraṃ unmattaka rasena tu ।।

vākstambhaṃ na padairyuktaṃ huḍasyorddhasthitaṃ purā ।
svasthaṃ gomayaveṣṭhyaṃ ca chāyāśuṣkaṃ ca kārayet ।।

mūṣikā vivare kṣipyaṃ pūjayitvā nirantaraṃ ।
carācarasya jagato mūkatvaṃ jāyate kṣaṇāt ।।
(186a)
merumadhyagataṃ cakraṃ haridrā dvitayena ca ।
digvidikṣu samantāttu vajrapaṃjaralāṃcchitaṃ ।।

nāmaṃ ca gataṃ likhya pāṣāṇe caiva mantravit ।
dvitīyenopariṣṭāṃ tu saṃcchādya vijane gṛhe ।।

gatistaṃbhaṃ ca kurute pakṣīśasya na saṃśayaḥ ।
karotyaviṣaraktena pādapāṃśu samanvitaṃ ।।

ulūkasya tu pakṣeṇa cakrarājaṃ samālikhet ।
śoṣayeti padairyuktaṃ pāvakasyopari nyaset ।।

trisaṃdhyaṃ tāpayenmantrī divasānekaviṃśatiḥ ।
tatastulyāmadhonyastaṃ śoṣayetsāgarānyapi ।।

kiṃ punarmānavo loke nityaṃ duḥkhasamanvitaḥ ।
krūrāṇāṃ śatrugamane eṣa eva vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ।।

jñāye tadāruṇaṃ ghoraṃ jñātaṃ nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।
evaṃ ekartu mantrajño mṛtakeśena veṣṭayet ।।

rājasaṃ dvayamiśreṇa viṣeṇa bhuvi lepayet ।
rocanā kuṃkumābhyāṃ ca ātmaraktena miśritaṃ ।।

kevalaṃ cakramālekhya sākhyanāma samanvitaṃ ।
pārāvārāmṛdāṃ gṛhya (paṃ)cagavyena marditāṃ ।।

devadeve ca vai kṛtvā tasya madhye niveśayet ।
dvitīyena tu saṃcchādya arccayedvidhivannaraḥ ।।

gaṃdhapuṣpaiḥ samabhyarcya kṛśarā pāyasaistathā ।
pūrṇṇakuṃbhe tu nikṣipya upariṣṭāttu kādayet ।।

tasyābhayaṃvaraṃ divyaṃ śarāvaṃ pūrayetsamaṃ ।
tatorddhe sthāpayeddīpaṃ ghṛtasnehena saṃśayaḥ ।।

punararcetavidhivaddhūpaṃ dadyāttu gugguluṃ ।
yāvattiṣṭhati tatrastho dīpaḥ paramaśobhanaḥ ।।
(186b)
tāvatsarvvāṇi divyāni staṃbhayennātra saṃśayaḥ ।
pūrvvoktena vidhānena cakramenaṃ samālikhet ।।

rocanā kuṃkumairyuktaṃ cakraṃ caiva tu saṃlikhet ।
pānīye kṣīramadhye tu cakrameva nidhāpayet ।।

jvarāṇāṃ caiva sarvveṣāṃ nāśanaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
gorocanaṃ samālikhya cakraṃ paramaśobhanaṃ ।।

ūrddhe tu śarabhasyedaṃ sādhyanāmaṃ gataṃ śubhaṃ ।
madhye surāyāṃ saṃkṣipya pūjayedvidhivannaraḥ ।।

dakṣiṇāṃ mūrttimādāya ekaliṃge nidāpayet ।
meghākṛṣṭistu kurute mantradravyaprabhāvataḥ ।।

anena mantrarājena uktaughaśamanena tu ।
sādhayettridaśānsarvvān bhuvi martyeṣu kā kathā ।।

chedanaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi mantrāṇāṃ paramaṃ yathā ।
śāktāśca śāmbhavā mantrā āṇavāḥ śāstracoditāḥ ।।

nikṛtyaṃti ca sarvvāṇi likhyamātrā na saṃśayaḥ ।
likheccakravaraṃ divyaṃ vaibhītakaphalahakaiḥ ।।

cityāgāreṇa vidhivadviṣaraktena lekhayet ।
akṣarāntaritaṃ mantraṃ yamarājena kārayet ।।

dikṣvaṣṭāsu ca sarvvatra vajreṇoparilāṃcchitaṃ ।
mahārajvānusaṃveṣṭya sthāpayet ghaṭakarpare ।।

nyasenmātṛgṛhe maṃtrī vidādau nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
chedane sarvvamantrāṇāṃ niśistho jāyate balāt ।।

asya maṃtrādhirājasya yamarājasya cāparaṃ ।
ajīva kaṭakādyena krodhādyena tu maṃtriṇā ।।
(187a)
sādhyābhidhānaṃ yoktavyaṃ karmabhedena vai punaḥ ।
veṣṭayenmantrarājānaṃ mantraviṃśatrayena tu ।।

yantrarāṭsarvvasāmānyaṃ asādhyaṃ sādhayedbalāt ।
sādhayetsarvvathā vainaṃ gaṇeśaṃ pūjayetsadā ।।

iti prayogamaṃjarī mayā tavādya varṇṇitā ।
ataḥ paraṃ bravīmi kiṃ vadasva vīravaṃdite ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavasrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
siddhaprayogamaṃjarīvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

evamākarṇya vīrendra vaṃditā pāpasūdanī ।
punarapyāha deveśaṃ praṇipatya surārcitaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrutaṃ sarvvamaśeṣeṇa tantramatyuttamottamaṃ ।
sarvvasiddhipradaṃ sāraṃ pādārabhya samastakaṃ ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi yatsārottaramuttamaṃ ।
kathaṃ sā paramākālī sarvvakāmaphalapradā ।।

cakreśvarī ghoratarā jayadā jayavṛddhidā ।
kathaṃ bhairavavaktrā sā kathamadbhutasiddhidā ।।

tāmākhyā hi samāsena sarahasyā mahāphalā ।
iti śrutvā vaco devyā vaktrāṃbujasamudbhavaṃ ।।
prāha gaṃbhīrayā vācā cakreśvaryā vidhiḥ paraṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu te kathayiṣyāmi devyāvidhiranuttamīṃ ।
yamavāpya na śocanti sādhakāḥ siddhikāṃkṣiṇaḥ ।।

nistaraṃgo dadhiprakhyaṃ yanna citparamucyate ।
vyāpyakaṃ cāpyanaṃ taṃ ca śivapūrṇṇamanāmayaṃ ।।

taddharmadharmiṇī śaktiḥ kanakasya dyutiryathā ।
(187b)
galitā prasarairmukhyā sargatrayavidhāyikā ।।

śaktisargamanantātaśuddhamantramudāhṛtaṃ ।
pauruṣaṃ prākṛtaṃ sarvvamaśuddhaṃ dviprakārajaṃ ।।

prākṛte kāraṇaṃ māyā kṣobhyā kṣobhā prasūyate ।
sarvvaṃ tadrūpamevātra sādhaḥ syācchaktirūpakaṃ ।।

viśeṣavyaktimāpannapāśasaṃkṣobhaṇādaṇoḥ ।
tasmāttadvastu nātīha yacchakterna vijṛṃbhitaṃ ।।

paṃcadhā paṃcadhā devī galitājñasvarūpiṇī ।
kriyecchātha balāsūkṣmā nityamutsarpiṇī kalā ।।

paṃcaviṃśati(rva)sā tena jñeyā tu bhairavī ।
sarvvāraṇikalāpādyā ṣaḍruśrutidohinī ।।

paṃcaratnasudhāsāradurmine ca pramuṃcatī ।
suśuddhaṃ cāndramamṛtaṃ kalājālapariṣkṛtaṃ ।।

mahānandakaraṃ citrya pūrakaṃ caṃdanābhidhaṃ ।
tamojamojasāṃ sākṣācchukraṃ cāmṛtarūpiṇaṃ ।।

rasamatyadbhutaṃ bhūyaḥ kuṃḍalyāṃtarasaṃsthitaṃ ।
sarvvogā pūrakaṃ śubhraṃ śivasadasadātmakaṃ ।।

tataḥ parataraṃ biṃdo gālitaṃ cājyamuttamaṃ ।
ghaṃṭācakrāsrutaṃ tacca śāktaṃ syāccakramuttamaṃ ।

saṃhārotthaṃ caturthaḥ syāt sthūlaṃ sarvvaśrutaṃ mahat ।
taṃ śaktipiṇḍasaṃbhūtaṃ ajāmarapadātmakaṃ ।।

paramaṃ lavaṇaṃ nāma raktaṃ śivapadoditaṃ ।
sarvvātmakaṃ sarvvagataṃ mahāmāyā vipāṭanaṃ ।।

evaṃ sā paṃcadhā devī galitā dravyarūpiṇī ।
punaḥ paṃcaprakārā sā paṃcadhā paṃcavigrahā ।।

paṃcaratnaprabhedena paṃcaratneśvarīti sā ।
(188a)
brahmakāraṇatānātra mahābhūtasvarūpiṇī ।।

kālātmikā paṃcadhaivaṃ jñeyā varṇaistathaiva sā ।
paṃcaviṃśatibhirdevi tasyoddhāraṃ vadāmyahaṃ ।।

śucideśaṃ samāsādya paṃcaratnopalepitaṃ ।
puṣpaprakarasaṃcchannaṃ sudhūpāmodamaṃtharaṃ ।।

tatra dūtīṃ samāhūya sarvvalakṣaṇalakṣitāṃ ।
surūpāṃ cārusarvvāṅgīṃ śaraccaṃdranibhānanāṃ ।।

nītyalākṣīṃ subhagāṃ cāpavaddhū vicitritāṃ ।
aṣṭamī caṃdrakhaṇḍānaṃ sulalāṭāṃ manoharāṃ ।।

nīlālikulasaṃkāśakeśabhāsurasaṃskṛtāṃ ।
kaṃvugrīvāṃ sudarśanāṃ tuganāsāṃ sugaṃdhikāṃ ।।

haṃsagadgadavācāntāṃ gajakumbhanibhastanāṃ ।
nimnamadhyāṃ mahābhāgāṃ trivalī valitāṃtarāṃ ।।

prottuṃgajaghanāṃ ramyāṃ śroṇibhārālasīkṛtāṃ ।
rambhāruṃkataruṃ (sta)mbhā majjātoṃgūḍhagulphinīṃ ।।

raktapādā sucārvvaṅgī dīrghāṅgulivirājitāṃ ।
vṛttopacitasadbāhuṃ bhūṣitāṃ cāruhāsinīṃ ।।

satyaṃ ca phaṇi saṃkāśahastāṃ lāvaṇyavāhinīṃ ।
sarvvābharaṇasaṃyuktāṃ sādhakasyānurāgiṇīṃ ।।

śāstrabhaktāṃ gurorbhaktāṃ devī rudraparāyaṇāṃ ।
nirvvikalpāṃ siddhiratāṃ paṃcaratnāmimāṃ priyāṃ ।।

susuptāṃ satvasaṃyuktāṃ dūtiṃ kuryātsadaiva hi ।
īdṛśī yadi nakhyādvā nirvikalpā praśasyate ।।

sarvvalakṣaṇahīnāpi satviṣṭhā devi sasyate ।
sarvvajātyudbhavākāryā sādhakairiti uttamā ।।
(188b)
athāparā vīrasiddhaiḥ kāryā dūricarā priye ।
sukṛśārūkṣavadanā vikaṭā vikaṭekṣaṇā ।।

rūkṣakeśā rūkṣacārā raktakṛṣṇā subhīṣaṇā ।
nirvikalpā mahānāsā kāryā vā dūtiruttamā ।।

tāmutsaṃgagatāṃ kṛtvā cuṃbayedgūhayedbhṛśaṃ ।
vaktreṇa vaktratāṃ tasyāḥ kāryaṃ vai madhyabhairavaṃ ।।

yāvadvāvapi sānaṃdau tato bhūrjedbhutoditaṃ ।
paścādvidyā samuccāryā yathā devi tathā śṛṇu ।।

somaṃ varuṇasaṃrūḍhaṃ vidvaṃkuśasamanvitaṃ ।
mahānilapathaṃ māyā vahnicūrṇaṃ tu yojitaṃ ।।

ādyapiṇḍaṃ munerdeyaṃ kiṃtu biṃduvivarjitaṃ ।
tribiṃdubhūṣitaṃ kālaṃ tena lāṃcchya niṣedhitaṃ ।।
jihvilaṃ caiva vāyuṃ ca trisūryāṅkañca vāruṇaṃ ।।

cāpādyaṃ vāyusaṃrūḍhaṃ agnivāsaśirāṃhritaṃ ।
varuṇasthaṃ tadādyaṃ ca trisūryāṅkañca vahnikaṃ ।।

tṛtīyādyaṃ kevalinaṃ piṃḍitaṃ yonipūrvvakaṃ ।
vahnyārūḍhaṃ trikoṇāṅkaṃ varuṇāntaṃ ca tat sthitaṃ ।।

tribiṃducitritaṃ bāhuṃ kālādyaṃ praṇavādiyuk ।
kevalaṃ bāhumuddhṛtya vāruṇaṃ cāṃkuśāhataṃ ।।

niṣedhaṃ deyamatulaṃ punastaṃ cāgni veṣmayuk ।
ḍaṃvaraṃ ca tataḥ paścādvidyā pūrvvaṃ punarddadet ।।

bhīmaśaṃkustridhādheyaṃ vidyeyaṃ ghoravikramā ।
paṃcaviṃśativarṇe yā mahāpātakanāśanī ।।

tannāsti parameśāni yanna sādhayate haṭhāt ।
jagadetatsamagraṃ vā vedavidbhiḥ prapūritaṃ ।।
(189a)
hatvā smaraṇamātreṇa na sapāpena lipyate ।
ekavīrā niraṃgeyaṃ sarvvakāmaphalapradā ।।

kevalaṃ smaraṇātsiddhā bahubhogatarāvaha ।
mahāvīryasamopetā jananī mātrināyakī ।।

anekāryasaṃyuktā jagadgrasanaghasmarā ।
mahāvinodābhiratā niratā śivamaṃdire ।।

kavalīkṛtya sarvvaṃ sā nadatyutsavalālasā ।
triśīrṣā pāpasamanī triśīrṣakasamutthitā ।।

triśironyā na niryātā parāditritayeśvarī ।
tridhāmasaṃghaṭṭamayī vīrā ghorā karaṃkiṇī ।।

tritridhāmni tridhā bhinnā tritridhāmni tridhā tridhā ।
trirūpā tripathā tristhā triśīrṣakavilāśinī ।।

bhūmikā jñānacakreśī deveśī bhairavānanā ।
ādhāramuktā tahyūna sphuradughā na valganī ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

bhairavodyānamākhyā hi yasmāddevī vijṛṃbhitā ।
triśīrṣātāvadeśāna yathāśakti trayeśvarī ।।

yathā sā bhūmicakreśī taddhyānaṃ yadakīdṛśaṃ ।
yathā samyagahaṃ vedmi tathā me brūhi bhairava ।।

evamākarṇya sahasā romāṃcitavapurharaḥ ।
uvāca devadeveśyā vacaḥ saṃdehaśāntayoḥ ।। ।।

bhairava uvāca ।।

mahārahasyamatulaṃ kaṣṭaṃ pṛcchasi bhairavi ।
gopyaṃ yadapi viśveśi tathāpi prabravīmi te ।।

yosau paraḥ śivaḥ śuddhaḥ sattā māstu kevalaḥ ।
(189b)
sarvvajñaḥ sarvvakarttā ca tryavasthaḥ parameśvaraḥ ।
kanakadyutivaṃtasya śaktistaddharmadharmiṇī ।।

sādhvi skaṃdhā prollasantī icchā ciccakramadhyagā ।
parā caivāparā caiva santā caiva parāparā ।।

triskaṃdhā sā tridhāmasthā icchājñānakriyātmikā ।
vāmā jyeṣṭhā tathā raudrā triśīrṣā triśirotthitā ।।

aghorā ghorarūpā ca ghorā ghoratarātmikā ।
śāntā ghorā tathā raudrā bhavā caivābhavā tathā ।।

tathā hyatibhavā devī naraśaktiśivātmikā ।
prasarpitā sā viśveśī dravyamantrakriyātmikā ।।

tathā ratnasvarūpeṇa vyākhyātā te yathārthataḥ ।
mahā(sa)rāviṇi devī kulakaulavikāśinī ।।
namasyasyeṣatāṃ devīṃ bhūmikāṃ gahvaroditāṃ ।

prāṇāpānamṛṇālaiḥ śivakamalarajo mūlabhūtvā tu pītvā,
mattāṣaṭvaktrapānairbhrajati śivakarī bhūmikānaṃdarūpā ।
āhlādādullasaṃtī vrajati pitṛgṛhaṃ janmabhūmyorddhadṛṣṭiṃ,
tadgarbhe śūnyabhūtāṃ viśati bhujaginīṃ kaṃdamūlāhatā ca ।।

helādaṃḍādhirūḍhāṃ prasarati kamalaṃ spaṃdacakrāspadaṃ ca,
tatrasthā brahmapatnī guhasarasi viṣat bhakṣayitvā kaṇāṃ vai ।
bhoge bho * ni cāṣṭau anubhavati tataḥ kośakuṃbhaṃ samījya,
tat kuṃbhaṃ bhānudīptā galita manubhavatyacchate sparśadhārāṃ ।।

pīyūṣaṃ tatra pītvā bhramati śivaśivā saṃgamādūrddhabhūmiṃ,
sparśākhyaṃ tatra buddhvā paṭu viśati satīkaṃ pathāreṇubhūmiṃ ।
jñātvā tatraiṣarūpā hṛjarāmarasamāyāti sā brahmalakṣmī,
(190a)
muktvā tatrāṭṭahāsaṃ viśati śaśikalāṃ sṛṣṭipadmasya garbhāṃ ।।

sparśe muktvā tu bhāvaṃ śatamatha kuṭilāṃ yāti śaktivisargaṃ,
kṛtvā saṃgaṃ satīnāṃ ramati paravaśāṃ nādabiṃdudvayaṃ ca ।
krīḍāṃ kṛtvā sutṛptāṃ viramati sakalāṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakarttṛ,
nnityaṃ sānaṃdatā vā praṇamitamuku ye naumi saṃ cāśaktiḥ ।। ।।

devyuvāca ।।

bhagavan kulakauleśa ṣoḍaśādhāranirbhara ।
bhairava śrotaniryāte triśīrṣe nayakālike ।।

bhūmikācakravinyāsaṃ viprakīrṇaṃ tvayoditaṃ ।
adhunā śrotumicchāmi kūrmādiśivagocaraṃ ।।

krameṇa jñāyate yadvasopāyaṃ mārgasaṃyutaṃ ।
tathā me brūhi bhagavan yathā tat prāt pracoditaṃ ।।
yena vijñātamātreṇa sarvvabhūmīśvaro bhavet ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

yattat paramanirvvāṇaṃ bhairavaṃ bhavagocaraṃ ।
tasyecchā bharitā śakti nirgatā kulakaulinī ।।

tayā vyāptamidaṃ sarvvaṃ kulasthaṃ bhūmigarbhagaṃ ।
ekadhā dvividhā tristhā catuḥpaṃcanavāśritāḥ ।।

tritatvakramayogena jñātvā tasyāgamāgamaṃ ।
mārgadvayanirodhaṃ tu kṛtvā tu yugapatsudhīḥ ।।

mattagaṃdhādaviralasvabhāvena tu yojayet ।
ṣaṭcakrarasasaṃpū(rṇṇa)manubhāvataṭānvite ।।

nimajya vinimajyeta śūnyāmbhasi pariplutaṃ ।
stokamāyāti sakalaṃ niṣkalāhuṣakarbhitaṃ ।।

divyagaṃdhasamāyuktaṃ sarvvaṃ paśyati śūnyavat ।
ku - tu karaṇaṃ kṛtvā grāhayenmūlabhūmikāṃ ।
(190b)
paṃcānāṃ vṛttiharaṇī divyo yoga prakarṣikān ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

kūrmaṃ ca kīdṛśaṃ deva karaṇaṃ yena labhyate ।
yadābhyasati yogeṃdrastathā brūhi sureśvara ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

paṃcamānte padaṃ baddhvā vanīyaṃ kiraṇāsavaṃ ।
kaṃdasthā kuṃbhayedvṛttīṃ yojayet prasthagocare ।।

punarjanmanikuṃbheta tadadho(tsṛ)jyalakṣayet ।
kacchapaṃ nāmakaraṇaṃ kīrttitaṃ tu suśobhane ।।

apānasya gatiṃ baddhvā recayecchaktikuṃjaraṃ ।
ānaṃtacatakadrāraṃ ramlāmaṃjurikarṣaṇaṃ ।।

cittaṃ kṛtvā tu cittasthaṃ cetasā viddhilokayet ।
svabhāvamūlasudṛḍhaṃ calatkaṃdapṛthodyataṃ ।।

dīptānubhāvasacchākhāṃ sthirapallavaśobhitāṃ ।
dravanna bhāvasarasaṃ nabho phalamanopamaṃ ।।

eṣa saṃkalpaviṭapaṃ parāhlādamadotthitaṃ ।
samanasthaṃ tadāruhya cārukī karaṇena tu ।।

tatrasthaṃ kampamāyāti śaktimārutakaṃpitaṃ ।
śivaśaktisamāviṣṭaḥ sarvvaṃ paśyati śūnyavat ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

karuṇā cāhakīnāma sānaṃdapadavedakaṃ ।
kathaṃ sā jñāpale kartuṃ tatvena vada bhairava ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

ubhayaspaṃdasaṃrodhāt kuṃbhayedbhānupaṃkajaṃ ।
śakti padmalaghugatiṃ kuṃbhakīṃ vṛttinotsṛjet ।
sparśādho kuṃcayedvaktraṃ bījakī karaṇaṃ priye ।।
recaye spṛṣṭigatirnā mārutaṃ nāḍisaṃśrayaṃ ।।

uccārya mantraṃ suśrūṣaṃ yaduktaṃ virataṃ tataḥ ।
(191a)
kṛtvā vittaṃ cidantasthaṃ kārayedvṛttivarjitaṃ ।।

spandasarvvasvalapanā matsyasyagaṃtibaṃdhanāt ।
mātrāśatānirodhācca svabhāvamavalaṃbayet ।।

yadā vicalate dṛṣṭiṃ tadā dīptaṃ bhaviṣyati ।
sthirīkṛtvā tu tattejaṃ dravate graṃthipaṃjaraṃ ।।

viśleṣasuciraṃ bhūtvā prasthedutpatate vaśaṃ ।
kṣobhamāyāti tadanu svānubhūtyekalakṣaṇaṃ ।।
nirghātavidyuccikṣepa paśyate prasthabhūmikā ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

nirghāta vidyuvijñeyaṃ karaṇaṃ yat prakīrttitaṃ ।
jñāyate vidhivaddeva tamācakṣvaśūladhṛk ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

tritatvakramabhedena kṛtvā sṛṣṭilayo hyadā ।
mūlakarmayathānīyakaraṃdhrātmūlagocaraṃ ।।

atha saṃkocanaṃ kṛtvā toyānalapadaṃ vrajet ।
vidyudvikṣepakaraṇaṃ priyeṣa prakaṭīkṛtāṃ ।।

dvilakṣamekataḥ kṛtvā trilakṣe viniveśayet ।
triśūlaṃ tu tataḥ kṛtvā triśūnyamavalokayet ।।

tāvadālokayedbhūmiṃ yācanālokanaṃ raveḥ ।
śaśinā bodhayetsūryaṃ śaktau bhāvaṃ nibodhayet ।।

bhāvo svabhāvataḥ kṛtvā niṣkalatvaṃ prajāyate ।
pūrvvamātrā dvisaṃkhyānaṃ ekacittaṃ yadā bhavet ।।

suptājanmaguhāyāntu mahāraṇyabhujaṃginī ।
khinnādūrddhvaṃ prasarati yadā lakṣaṃ na hīyate ।।

śaktikṣobhāttadā yogī viddho patati bhūtale ।
vāḍavena prayogena janmabhūmyāmupāruhā ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

janmabhūmyavabodhantu vāḍa(vāṃ) karaṇaṃ prabho ।
(191b)
na vidmi paramārthena sopāyaṃ vada kaulika ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

yathā nadyo viśūnyāstu dahyate ca ja * mukha ।
tayā vṛttiṃ gṛhītvā tu nirddahe kūrmavahni(nā) ।।

na nirodhaṃ na coccāraṃ na ca rūpaṃ vitarkaṇaṃ ।
kevalaṃ bhāvaniyamaṃ vāḍavaṃ saṃprakīrttitaṃ ।।

mahāvarttakabaṃdhena badhvāvittaṃ turaṃgamaṃ ।
somasūryamahāraṇye śaktiparyāṇa yo(ji)tā ।।

kūrmaika eṣamī * yaṃ karmānte kūrmagocaraṃ ।
dakṣiṇe nātha vāmena helādaṇḍena sādhakaḥ ।।

kaṃdagokalamādāya ṣaṭcakrabhramanirbharaṃ ।
āhavaṃ vipuladhyeyaṃ gra(ha)ṇaṃ sastramaṃ viduḥ ।।

bhavate sādhakendrastu kaṃdagolakasevanāt ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

mahāvarttasya karaṇaṃ ṣaṭcakra(mu)bhavaṃ tathā ।
brūhi tatvena bhagavan karmayogaphalodayaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

uccārya mantravipulaṃ trigatistripathaikabhṛt ।
yadā bhavati svasvasthaṃ mahāvarttantu ucyate ।।

tatra mantraṃ svarūpaṃ ca ātmanaḥ sthiravittamā ।
tadā mahāvarttakaraṇaṃ kavitvamavabodhadaṃ ।।

vadvalakṣasvabhāvena bhāvanādhiṣṭhitaṃ yadā ।
āgataṃ tatsamākhyātaṃ sānaṃdaṃ vipulocyate ।।

dhyeyaṃ svarūpasaṃvetti gahanaṃ bhūmikaṃ padaṃ ।
tatra sthitasya yā labdhiḥ saṃbhramantaṃ vijānataḥ ।।

trikāladarśanādeva vibhutvaṃ bhavati priye ।
padaṃ paṃcakaviśrāmādūrddhvadho kṣepavarjanāt ।।

bhavatebjasamāpatti vyāpticakrasya melakaṃ ।
(192a)
mantrasthaṃ ca svarūpasthaṃ namaruṃtagatisthitaṃ ।।

viccenā saṃprayogaṃ ca kṛtvā sadbhāvavarjitaṃ ।
saptāviṃśatirodhena jāyate romaviplavaṃ ।।

dviguṇaṃstadvadṛṣṭitvaṃ triguṇe dehagauravaṃ ।
caturguṇena cādeśaṃ mātṛvaṃ * prakāśakaṃ ।।

dṛṣṭvā mātṛsamūhaṃ tu prathame rodanaṃ bhavet ।
dvitīyeha menaṃ devi tṛtīyedgavicāraṇā ।।

caturthe plavanaṃ kuryāt paṃcameva * niśitā ।
ṣaṣṭhe jñeyasya kathanaṃ saptame svapadaṃ labhet ।।

svapadastho mahāpadmo mātṛsāmānyatāṃ vrajet ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

* * * * * * * * urddhavikṣepavarjanāt ।
kriyate yena yogena karaṇaṃ tadvabravīhi me ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

karaṇā śodhinaṃ kṛtvā marunnāḍisamīkṛtaṃ ।
suśāntapadamāsthāya sūryendukiraṇaplutaṃ ।।

padmasaṃpuṭamadhye tu vṛtta * * * sṛṣṭibhuk ।
śaśinaṃ sūryacakraṃ tu sūryaṃ śaśimukhe hutaṃ ।।

sasaṃhāragrāsayogaṃ ubhaya * ttiyojanaṃ ।
sahasradalasaṃvedhī priyamelāpakārakaṃ ।।

tālvoṣṭhapuṭayogena mantramuccārya saplutaṃ ।
ātmatatvāvadhiryāvat kṛtvā tatra nirodhanaṃ ।।

śikhāmūrddhvagatāṃ badhvā nibaddhaṃgasāgaraṃ ।
tvejasāṃtajalaṃ kṛtvā na kiṃcidapi cintayet ।।

mātrāśatanirodhena paśyate kṣīrasāgaraṃ ।
tadūrmi madhyagaṃ padmaṃ dṛṣṭvā ūrmikalaplutaṃ ।।

tatrasthaṃ vaṃdayet saṃdhyāṃ yāvat kṣobhaḥ prajāyate ।
(192b)
kṣudhvasya bhavate dṛṣṭiṃ divyāṃ jñānavibhākarīṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

antarjalākhyakaraṇaṃ na vidmastatvato vibho ।
karoti yena yogīndro gaṃgāsāgaramaṃjanaṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

pūrvvoktāmāsanaṃ ca * bhinnaṃ nābhau niyojayet ।।

daṇḍakāreṇa kūrme tu khatrayaṃ sāvalokayet ।
gṛhītvā tvarito bhāvo yadā kṣīṇāha hoyate ।।

antarjjalaṃ sakathitaṃ gaṃgāmbhasi mahāplavaṃ ।
pūrvvavaddhvaniruccārya vidyātatvāvadhiṃ kramāt ।।

saṃpūrya brahmavivaraṃ kalāgrāsaṃ tu kārayet ।
virodhaṃ kriyate tatra yāvadvādaśamī kalā ।।

caṃdrasya sambhavaṃ dṛṣṭvā kṣobhayedamṛtārṇavaṃ ।
tadāmṛtarasesiktāṃ ṣaṇmāsānmṛtyujidbhavet ।।

na cātrakāraṇaṃ kaścinna ca mārutabaṃdhanaṃ ।
kevalaṃ cāraviśrāmaṃ bhujaṃgagṛhaveśmagaṃ ।।

uccārya pūrvvavaṃ nāda vṛttitrayanirodhakaṃ ।
trijanmani padaṃ badhvā mārgadvaya vilopakaṃ ।।

kuryārkā kaṃṭhagaṃ nāvaṃ dṛṣṭibiṃdu guhāgataṃ ।
nādena pūryahṛdayaṃ trigarbhaviniveśayet ।।

manaḥśakti sūryaṃ biṃdunādodare gatau ।
ekataścityamekaikaṃ bhogapahṛtisu sthitaṃ ।।

padadvādaśarodhena śṛṇu netramaradhvani ।
dviguṇena niruddhena sparśaṃ vindati śītalaṃ ।।

tato stutirvaṃdyamāno divyastrī sahamādavaṃ ।
biṃdate vadvalakṣastu triguṇaṃ sarvvadarśinaṃ ।।

caturguṇaṃ paraṃ vyoma śaśāṅkakiraṇaplutaṃ ।
divyadehastu bhavati vicarenaṃgatadbhuvi ।।
(193a)
evaṃ saṃkhyā tirodhādhva divyagaṃdhiḥ prajāyate ।
yā sā ghrāyajagatsarvvaṃ vaśe tiṣṭhati dāsavat ।।

ṣaḍguṇe sarvvadravyāṇāṃ bhoktā bhavasādhakaṃ ।
saptabhiḥ sarvvakarmāṇi kurute nirviśaṃkayā ।।

aṣṭame śivavatsarvvaṃ paśyate tasya jaṃgamaṃ ।
evaṃ vṛttibhujo yogī labhate bhogabhūmikāṃ ।।

tatrasthā recayettrīṇi aṣṭagrīveva sarpiṇīṃ ।
aṃśakāti svavṛttisthaḥ kokilākhyaṃ padaṃ viśet ।।

tatrasthaṃ kośakalaśaṃ mudritaṃ biṃdunādayoḥ ।
janmajātā samutpādya grāhayetkūrmakaṃdale ।।

ekabhāvaśatāvedau kṛtvā bhāvairvivarjitau ।
vadvalakṣau sunidraiva śūlaṃ śūnye vicintayet ।।

brahmaṇā padavī jñātvā dhārāsaritasaṃgame ।
nirīkṣayedviṣṇupadaṃ sarvvākhyaṃ sarvvasaṃbhavaṃ ।।

hrasvadīrghakrameṇaiva dīrghahrasvakrameṇa ca ।
plutamuccārayennādaṃ yaḥ sadātmasamaśrutaṃ ।।

pibedvāramṛtaṃ tacca yadugdhaṃ gostanairiva ।
tenāmṛtena tṛptastu balīpali * varjitaḥ ।।

kānyaṃ ca kurute śreṣṭaṃ padalakṣaṇasaṃyutaṃ ।
pṛṣṭhe kathayate sarvvaṃ gaṃdharvvaiḥ sahagīyate ।।

ūrddhakāryordhvakṛtsnadhvamīlitāśca sastuvaṃ ।
plutodāntaprayogena ninade janmavāgbhavet ।।

viṣṇumastakasaṃprāptaraśanā śūnyasaṃgame ।
īṣasparśavivarjā tu tāluraṃdhragataṃ tathā ।।

dvikacuṃcupuṭaprakhyaṃ vaktraṃ kṛtvā tathā dvija ।
uddhṛtya tadanu sparśaṃ yāvadbhāvaṃ sthirīgataṃ ।।
(193b)
dṛḍhabhāvagato yogī svasthaṃ pravamavāpnuyāt ।
tatrasthasya ca viśrāmādgaṃdhadvaya vicāraṇāt ।।

* pāmṛtaṃ prasravati śūnyendurasanāhataṃ ।
tadā svāditacidrūpaṃ ūrddhaṃ gacchatyaśaṃkitaṃ ।।

krauṃcikotpāṭanaṃ hyeṣa śivaśaktisamāgama ।
śivavyāptikṛtodyānaṃ plutoccārorddhvadṛkkriyaḥ ।।

lalanā tāluke yojya spaṃdaśaktiyutaṃ dadet ।
kaṃṭhotthatālu vivaraṃ (yā)vaddvādaśabhūmikaḥ ।।

āhataṃ hutate matsyā anāhatapadaṃ labhet ।
tadbhāvabhāvanāprāptanāmamaṣṭavidhaṃ dadet ।।

vedhastambhavaśākarṣadṛṣṭvā sarvvaṃ kariṣyati ।
anāhatapadā(rū)ḍhamūrddhaṃśaktinayedbalāt ।।

nādaṃ sparśagataṃ kṛtvā sparśaṃ kṛtvā tu nādagaṃ ।
tāvubhau biṃducakre tu niveśyakarikuṃbhavat ।।

kuṃbhasthaṃ paśyate sarvvaṃ kavāṭāṭavidurghaṭaṃ ।
sahasrara(śmi)khacitaṃ saliloścārasan kramāt ।।

tejomālāyutāṃ muktvā ūrddhagopanagocarān ।
nimeṣadvayasaṃyogā krūrmakaṃ eṣa gocarān ।।

bhāvāṃ paśyati bhāvena svabhāvaparibṛṃhitaṃ ।
tricakradhyānayogena trilakṣaparivarttanāt ।।
triśūnya samatā śūnyāṃ utpate gaganāntare ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

yathāśaktinayedūrddhvaṃ kampate padabo * nī ।
tathā (brū)hi kapālīśa karaṇaṃ yena buddhyati ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

āhṛtya samanābhāvamunmanastho * laṃkṛśaḥ ।
ādhyāyakarmamarutānmūlakālānalādbhṛśaṃ ।।

tatra cittagajaṃ baddhā śaktyaṃkuśa vaśīkṛtaṃ ।
(194a)
ūrddhavirecayet prāṇaṃ * śaṃbhādhvaninādavat ।।

argalāṃ dhṛṣyamānopi yāva * padāvadhiṃ ।
tatrasthaṃ kuṃbhayet prāṇaṃ yāvadūrddhapadaṃ labhet ।।

helāgolaplutaṃ nāma karaṇaṃ śakticidgataṃ ।
sanmukhaṃ karaṇaṃ kṛtvā kaliṃdī tripathāntagaṃ ।।

kapathātmagataṃ kṛtvā dṛṣṭihyaṃgulamāriṇī ।
gaganāhatanirghātaṃ dvikubjakiraṇāhataṃ ।।

bhāvayedbhāvabodhena svabhāvaṃ īśvarāntagaṃ ।
tatrastho lakṣayeddhīmāniṣṭhāniṣṭhavicāraṇāt ।।

aśeṣamātṛvṛṃdasya melakaṃ kurute haṭhāt ।
navātmānaṃ samuccārya navaśaktiradhiṣṭhitaṃ ।।

navaraṃdhratirodhena navatatvāvadhikramāt ।
akāraśca ukāraśca makāro biṃdureva ca ।।

arddhacaṃdranirodhaśca nādo nādāntagastathā ।
svabhāvanāma kṛṣyādhucaṃdramaṇḍalamāruhet ।।

lalāṭanikaṭe baddhā śabdaṃ cinicinināyate ।
tacchabdamadhyage bhāvaṃ bhāvadvayavivarjitaṃ ।।

caṃdrārkasaṃgamadhyānte ajaṃ bindati sādhakaḥ ।
tuhināṃśutaṭāvasthāṃ bhānuraśmivibodhitaṃ ।।

piṃḍasthairyaṃ prakurute paritatva vivekitaḥ ।
ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena yogīndraḥ sarvvavidbhavet ।।

yathā japedasaṃvitti bhavate sādhakasya tu ।
tadā manacchatārūpaṃ biṃdate bhūmibhāvagaḥ ।।

padadvādaśamānte tu tritatvāhlādakārakaṃ ।
vyāpinyā vyāpya madhyasthaṃ samanā garbhavṛṃhitaṃ ।।

śivādhiṣṭhitacaitanyaṃ śivaśaktikṛtāspadaṃ ।
(194b)
ṣoḍaśārasya cakrasya mātṛmadhye samodati ।।

pītvā tu monmanābhāsāraṃ samaṃ bhāvaṃ sa paśyati ।
yatra krīḍaṃ triśūlastho viśvādyaḥ ṣaṭkaśakta(yaḥ) ।।

mahāmadirapānoktamadyabhairavasanmukhaḥ ।
sitā raktā tathā pītā kṛṣṇā vā nīlalohitā ।।

prathamā kurute stobhaṃ dvitīyā kaṃpate bhṛśaṃ ।
tṛtīyā bhramate (ca)kre caturthā plāvayettu taṃ ।।

paṃcamā plāvayedbhūmau ṣaṣṭhāsārthaṃ samodati ।
śaktyā saiva mahāhlādaṃ pūrṇṇe phetkāratānyata ।।

ūrddhanādaṃ prasarati yatra śabdāstuṣaṃ śubhaṃ ।
uccāreṇa saṃsthānaṃ yatra nādaṃ pralīyate ।।

pralīnaśabdayogīndra maṃtrātmaśivaśaktigaṃ ।
bhāvayadrāvagaṃ buddhyā nimiṣādhorddhavṛttigaḥ ।।

catuṣkaṃ mātarastatra tiṣṭhanti padabṛṃhitāḥ ।
ta(tra) pītvā mahocchuṣme ucchūṣme saha modati ।।

niśivāntaramātreṇa saptalokāṃ gamiṣyati ।
nirālaṃbamṛṇālasya kalikā pṛṣṭhavasthitāḥ ।।

yā sadāśivaḍaṃsasya bhogabhūmiḥ parā bhavet ।
tāṃ gṛhītvā mahāpāna jālinaṃ dyumaghūrmitā ।।

tatonnatāni kurute nardate hasate muhuḥ ।
spaṃdamānaṃ tu taṃ haṃsaṃ grāhayedbhāvapaṃjare ।।

triśūnyarajunā baddho haṃso raṭati cakravat ।
yāvatsaṃprāpya vimalaṃ karoti gatirāgatiṃ ।।

parāparamahāmārgaṃ tricakramatibhiḥ saha ।
pārādyāḥ śaktayastatra tiṣṭhanti śivaśūlagā ।।
(195a)
yāṃ dṛṣṭvā tisṛbhistṛskāstritayaṃ bindate śiśuḥ ।
tadūrddhe sṛṣṭibhuvanaṃ bhairavasya paraṃ padaṃ ।।

tatra sā bhramate śaktiricchākhyā śivadharmiṇī ।
yayā sarvvaṃ prabhavati yasmāt sarvvaṃ nivarttate ।।

aṣṭāraṃ biṃdate tatra cakravidyeṣavaṃditaṃ ।
tatra baddhā padaṃ yogī paṃcacakrakṛto bhavet ।।

tatra sarvvasamutpannā rudrā bhairavamātaraḥ ।
biṃdunādau sthitau tatra sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārikā ।।

tatrasthau biṃdate cakrā cakrāradaśaṣaṭkagāṃ ।
yātitā kathitā loke aṣṭau bhairavamātaraḥ ।।

tāni tasya narendrasya vaśe tiṣṭhanti kolikaḥ ।
tatra sthairyamataḥ prāpta biṃdate ca padatrayaṃ ।।

praśāntaṃ praviśantañca paraṃ ca bhuvanādhipaṃ ।
praśāntakurute vedhaṃ nādabiṃdusamāgamaṃ ।।

visargaṃ nādamārgasthaṃ parakāyapraveśakaṃ ।
parāpare samāpattirjantu cakrasya yoginiṣu ।।

kurute nirvikālasthā cuṃbitaḥ paśupāśakaḥ ।
ṣaḍadhvapadabhedena yojayet kulanaṃdanaṃ ।।

jñānaśaktiṃ sthitā sparśe kriyākhyā tu sarmagā ।
icchākhyā tu pare līnā nigrahānugrahe ratā ।।

tābhirgrathitacittastu sādhakaḥ sarvvakarmakṛt ।
marudgajaṃ na dordaṇḍasthitā cakra vibhedayet ।।

pluto dāntaprayogena nāśikābjaṃ praviśyate ।
dharmādharmasvarūpasthaḥ prāntamuṣṭitrayāntagau ।।

svarūparahitaṃ viddhi jahikā hṛdi gocarau ।
romakūpaguhāvasthau śūnyākhya karaṇena tu ।।

evaṃ te bhūmikaṃ jñānaṃ śatadhārāmṛtaplutaṃ ।
(195b)
kathitaṃ sarahasyaṃ tu ṣaṭcakrapadabṛṃhitaṃ ।।

karaṇopāyasaṃyuktamavasthātrayamaṃḍitaṃ ।
bhairavaśrotasiryātaṃ rahasyanayakaulikaṃ ।।

na deyaṃ duṣṭasatvānāṃ (mā)tritaṃtravivekitāṃ ।
samayajñeṣu dātavyaṃ avabodhapadotsakaṃ ।।

guruparvvakramāyātaṃ sakṛdbhrāntamanekadhā ।
navāvyadvidyate jñānaṃ karaṇānalagocare ।।
evaṃ te varṇṇitaṃ sāraṃ anāmoghā na nirgataṃ ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṅśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke bhairavānanāvidhau bhūmikāvidhiḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

etatsarvvaṃ mayā jñātaṃ rahasyāmṛtamuttamaṃ ।
idānīṃ jñātumicchāmi bhairavodyānanirṇayaṃ ।।

sūcitaṃ yanmayā deva na ca tat prakaṭīkṛtaṃ ।
kathaṃ tadudbhavādevā cakreśī bhairavānanā ।।
triśīrṣā kathamuddiṣṭā etadicchāmi vedituṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu vīrendranamite pravakṣyāmi yathātathaṃ ।
paripūrṇaḥ praśāntātmā vyāpakaḥ karaṇojjhitaḥ ।।

bhūtirdhṛtiḥ prakāśātmā sānaṃdollāsamaṃkuraṃ ।
paṃcātmakamanāmeṣaṃ devamudyānabhairavaṃ ।।

navātmakaṃ na cāntasthaṃ navadhā navavigrahaṃ ।
navacakreśvaraṃ devamanantaṃ kiraṇākulaṃ ।।

prabodhānaṃdagahanaṃ bhairavoddāmaraṃ ca tat ।
tadudgatā mahāśaktistriśīrṣā vīryamālinī ।।

prakāśānaṃdamadhyasthāṃ prollasanmantravigrahā ।
nistaraṃgā nirādhāni nirnāmā nirahetukā ।।

nirūpāni hatojaskā kamalāntaramālinī ।
(196a)
prakāśānandamadhyasthā kādyākaṃkālabhairavī ।।

kakālātkuṭilā kālī kalāntaraprasarpiṇī ।
khasvarūpā khamadhyasthā khagagā kharparā khagā ।।

khaṭṭakī gajagāgāṃtā gaṃdheśī khaḍgatoditā ।
ghorā ghorāntarālīnā ghoṣavigrahadhāriṇī ।।

ghargharā carcitā caṃḍā caṃḍā cidvāmacarcinī ।
carcarī kātarāntasthā caṃḍakālī ca kārikā ।।

chamanī chadmagamanī jayā jambhanakārikā ।
jihvilā jalajā jālyā jhāṃkāreśītha jharjharā ।।

ṭaṃkāṃkitā viṭaṃkāṃkī ṭādyavigrahaṭhānakī ।
ṭhamarī kūpanetrā ca ḍamarī ḍāmarānanā ।।

ḍaṃbarā ḍahalā ḍāvyā ḍaṭṭakarṇā mahaujasā ।
taraṃginī taraṃgā ca tanuśīlā tapaśvinī ।।

svacirāya manīṣāśī damanī dāmanī damā ।
dhenudhīrā dhīramātā dhūryādhīrārthadāyinī ।।

padmodbhavā padmagarbhā padmāpadmānanodbhavā ।
padmānānāntakīṃ devīṃ phakkarī sphuritādharā ।।

phaphaiḥ phāvai ca phaṃkārī phekkārā rāvapūraṇī ।
bodharūpā bodhagamyā balavaktrā balāpahā ।।

bhairavī bhītiśamanī bhavakṣayakarī smṛtā ।
ḍātsaṇā na svarūpeṇa māntā paṃcārthavādikā ।।

paṃcavāmāṃtapaṃceśī paṃcapaṃcasvarūpiṇī ।
paṃcoditā paṃcavahā paṃcakālanatatparā ।।

yamaghaṃṭā yamā yāmyā yamasyāntakarīyasā ।
raminī ratijā rambhā rathanī retasāṃkayā ।।
(196b)
laṃbikā lalanā lālyā lālāntālalanīparā ।
varṣaṇī vamanī vāntā varhi vāhā vaṭeśvarī ।।

śaktirūpā śamātasthā śamanī śaśilaṃpaṭā ।
śavarī ṣaṭkalādhārā ṣaṭkuśeṣayamadhyagā ।।

ṣaṭcakranāthā ṣaḍrūpā ṣoḍhāśadgalitānanā ।
ṣaḍbiṃdupadamadhyasthā ṣaṭkāreṇa kulāntakī ।।

ṣaṭcakragrasanavyagrā ṣadgrathapravipāṭinī ।
sarvvarūpā sarvvabhakṣā sarvvānaṃdā sanātanī ।।

saroṣā śaparā śārā hṛtkuśeṣayaśāyinī ।
sarasī sarasā sāṃbhā sāntābhānantarūpiṇī ।।

haṃsāhārāspadāṃ hasthā hasinī hasitānanā ।
hāhāṭṭahāsaniratā hāhārāvavirāviṇī ।।

kṣayakṛt kṣapaṇī kṣāṇā kṣayamaṃdiranāyakī ।
adbhutā ajarā aṃbā amarānantayoginī ।।

āśā āśamayī āśyā ivikā īśvararūpiṇī ।
īśvarī īrujā nāśī uddhṛtodhāryamaṃtharā ।।

utpalā vāpi socchūṣmā ūrddhakeśī bhayānakā ।
ṛktadehāruṇānāthī liṃgarūpāligūttarā ।।

jvālājvālāmayī jñānā ekarākhyā trikoṇinī ।
aikārā aiśvarī aiśā mahāmohavighaṭṭanī ।।

oja ojasvinī jñeyā ṛdārya padatāsurā ।
antakāntakarī antā aṃbā amarasuṃdarī ।।

visargavivṛtākārā visargāmṛtasuṃdarī ।
saṃkocaprasarādhārā ṣoḍaśādhāravigrahā ।।

mahāmātreśvarī devī saivodyānasvarūpiṇī ।
tayā vinā na deveśo sā vai tena vinā na hi ।।
(197a)
aparā ca parā caiva tathā cānyā parāparā ।
trividhā tritridhā tristhā trimārgaśritacitkalā ।।

na madhyasthā na tiryasthā norddhasthāna tu kuṃcitā ।
sarvvādhārā sarvvamayī paripūrṇā mahaujasā ।।

evaṃ vidhā bhagavatī svasvarūpā prakāśinī ।
sādhanīyā mahāmātrī yathādevi nigadyate ।।

gatvā harāspadaṃ rātrau pretaskaṃdavyavasthitaḥ ।
gṛhītvā ḍamaruṃ mantrī vādayedvigatavyathaḥ ।।

mudrāṃ tu bhairavīṃ baddhvā smarettāṃ bhairavānanāṃ ।
śatārdhoccārayogena kaṃpate vasudhātalaṃ ।।

jvalanti ca diśaḥ sarvvā hyulkāsaṃpātamadbhutaṃ ।
tato halahalāśabdaṃ samudbhavati dāruṇaṃ ।।

tata āyānti yoginyo nānārūpā maholvaṇā ।
āveṣṭyā sādhakāḥ sarvvāstiṣṭhanti tanumadhyame ।।

yadābhīṣṭaṃ pradāsyaṃti sādhakāya mahātmane ।
nocedanyacchatāntaṃve devadevī samuccaret ।।

ya āste vīrarājendraḥ tasyā devī svayaṃ śivā ।
cakreśvarī bhairavādyā svayameti na saṃśayaḥ ।।

sahaiva sādhakendrasya nayantyeva na saṃśayaḥ ।
athavā japayogena lakṣāyutajapena hi  ।।

sarvvottamāḥ siddhayaśca yāḥ pūrvvaṃ tatra madhyataḥ ।
prakīrttinā mayā devi tāḥ siddhyanti na saṃśayaḥ ।।

trilakṣajāpayogena madhyamā sādhayiṣyati ।
siddhayaḥ sādhakendrosau devi nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।।

athavā viśvarūpāntāṃ dhyāyedghoraparākramāṃ ।
(197b)
kṛṣṇākākālinīhārakālametha samadyutiṃ ।।

trivaktrāṃ sā smaredraudrāṃ aṣṭādaśabhujānvitāṃ ।
sukṛṣāṃ bhīmanihrādahrādinīṃ viśvaghaṭṭanīṃ ।।

mahāpretakṛtāvasthāṃ nānāpraharaṇodyatāṃ ।
halamālāvanaddhāṅgīṃ ūrddhakeśāṃ bhayānakāṃ ।।

evaṃ dhyātvā bhagavatīṃ juheta naraphuphūsaṃ ।
tryatryaṃ daśamabhāvena sādhakendro dṛḍhavrataḥ ।।

homāvasānato devi sādhayenmanasīpsitaṃ ।
svayamabhyeti sā devī vahnimadhyānna saṃśayaḥ ।।

cakreśvaratvaṃ vinare sādhakāya mahātmane ।
tannāsti yanna siddhyeta devadevyāḥ prabhāvataḥ ।।

yāvaṃti pūrvvamuktāni tantrarājasya madhyataḥ ।
sādhanāni mayā tubhyaṃ tāvatsaṃsādhayiṣyati ।।

sāmānyānyapi karmāṇi sādhayetsmaraṇādapi ।
vaśyākarṣaṇavidveṣamāraṇoccāṭanādiṣu ।।

staṃbhastobhaṃ tathā dveṣaṃ mohanaṃ vābhicārukaṃ ।
kṛtyā betālasaṃsiddhi meghanigrahamadbhutaṃ ।।

purapraveśamāveśaṃ prasanā svapna sādhakaṃ ।
kāṃḍāveśaṃ paṭāveśaṃ muśalāveśanaṃ ca yat ।।

kaṭorāveśanādyaṃ ca khanyadhāturasakriyā ।
sādhayellīlayā maṃtrī pāṭhamātrānna saṃśayaḥ ।।

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi carusādhanamuttamaṃ ।
yamī caretsādhakendro bhaginī vā manoramā ।।

yenāśukṛtamātreṇa piṃḍasiddhiḥ prajāyate ।
mahāhava * taṃ gṛhya tacchīrṣa māṃ sameva ca ।।
(198a)
grāhayedvaraveśmānta * sthastatsādhayet priye ।
vedimadhye samāśritya tatkāṣṭhe tatra pācayet ।।

tatrānya bhāṃḍo deveśi cālanādikamācaret ।
japettāṃ yāvatatsiddhaṃ tata utpādya suvrate ।।

pragṛhyo chuṣmapātrasthaṃ mantraṃ yena matandritaḥ ।
baddhā karaṃkiṇīṃ mudrāṃ devadevīṃ samuccaret ।।

yāvadāste muhurttante tāvadāyānti devatāḥ ।
mṛgayaṃti caruṃ dehi sādhakendrānya mā ciraṃ ।।

tāsāṃ vīreṇa vaktavyaṃ yuṣmāsu vitarāmi te ।
tayā tatparameśānī mayā dṛṣṭā karaṃkiṇī ।।

tasyāḥ kṛte caruvaraṃ mayā sādhimeva hi ।
tameva tasmāntāṃ draṣṭuṃ paścādyuṣmāsu dīyate ।।

evamākarṇyatāṃ devyo devīmāvāhayiṣyati ।
tatra āyānti sā ghorā carukāhāralaṃpaṭā ।।

saumyaghoreṇa rūpeṇa sādhakāya varapradā ।
praṇipatyavatāṃ devīṃ arghaṃ vāmāṃgato dadet ।।

paścāccaruṃ pradātavyaṃ mudrāṃ baddhā karaṃkiṇīṃ ।
tadā tuṣṭā bhaveddevī carukaṃ grāhayiṣyati ।।

pragṛhya koṭiguṇitaṃ jāyate kṣaṇamātrataḥ ।
tadāṃṣṇāti svayaṃ cārddhaṃ devībhyaḥ śiṣṭameva tat ।।

prayacchati karaṃkeśī tataḥ pānaṃ samāharet ।
yaṃ tatsaḍrasasaṃbhūtaṃ kāraṇānalamadhyagaṃ ।।

sānaṃdāpānamātreṇa nṛtyate bhuvaneśvarī ।
tadā devyastuṣṭamanāḥ paśubhirvividhaistathā ।।

santarpayanti subhage brahmaviṣṇukulodgatāḥ ।
tāstu sādhakamukhyasya divyaṃ pānaṃ dadāti hi ।।
(198b)
sopi devi vapurbhūtvā piṇḍasiddhimavāpnuyāt ।
tasyāḥ samīgo bhūtvā yatheṣṭaṃ krīḍate naraḥ ।।

evaṃ tava samākhyātā carukāsiddhiruttamā ।
kimanyat kathayiṣyāmi vada bhairavabhāminī ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
bhairava - ptividhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrotumicchāmi bhagavan varṇānāṃ nāma niścayaṃ ।
yena vijñātamātreṇa sarvvamaṃtrātigo bhavet ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇuṣva kathayiṣyāmi yatsāraṃ pṛcchasi priye ।

oṃkāraṃ vyāpakaṃ caiva pratodaṃ,
viśveśānaṃ praṇavaṃ mantradīpakaṃ ।
sarvvātmānaṃ tripadaṃ trisvarūpaṃ,
dhāmātmānaṃ nāmabhiḥ svasvarūpaṃ ।।

akāravyāpakaṃ cādyaṃ sarvvātmā vyāpakaṃ śivaṃ ।
nārāyaṇamanākāramaṃkuśaṃ mukhadāruṇaṃ ।।

dīrghādyaṃ nāmabhijñeyaṃ ikāraṃ ca tribindukaṃ ।
trisūryabrahmadhāmā ca māyādya samudāhṛtaṃ ।।

paṃcabiṃdumahāmāyā śaktimohanaśīlinī ।
caturthaṃ cānumākhyātaṃ īkāraṃ surasuṃdari ।।

rudraṃ paṃcamabhānuṃ ca pañcamaṃ śrītithiṃ tathā ।
krodhāsanaṃ samākhyātaṃmukārākhyaṃ mahāsvaraṃ ।।

ṣaṣṭhakālānalaṃ prāṇaṃ tathā tejo jvalaṃ śubhaṃ ।
ūrddhvādhokarṣaṇaṃ nāma ūkāraṃ tanumadhyame ।।

ṛktadevakalādyaṃ ca saptamaṃ bhānuruttamaṃ ।
napuṃsakādyaṃ vikhyātaṃ ṛkāraṃ nābhi * ḥ sadā ।।
(199a)
kalāddīrghaṃ ca urddhasthaṃ kalāvalitavigrahaṃ ।
dānaṃ caiva samākhyātaṃ ṛkāraṃ kāntamujvalaṃ ।।

praguṃ guhāśayaṃ bhṛṃgaṃ navamaṃ bhānuruttamaṃ ।
trikataṃ caivamākhyātaṃ sakāraṃ nāmabhiḥ sadā ।।

kālāntaṃ daśamaṃ sūryaṃ antaṃ cāpi napuṃsakaṃ ।
ākhyātamevaṃ * kāraṃ ūrddhabāhumatismṛtaṃ ।।

trikoṇamekādaśamaṃ vahnigehaṃ ca yonigaṃ ।
śṛṃgāṭaṃ caiva ekāraṃ nāmabhiḥ kīrtitaṃ * * ।।

kaṃdaṃ ṣaḍasraṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ kāmaṃ kāmojasaṃ mahat ।
vāgbhavaṃ binduvaktreśaṃ ekārākhyamudāhṛtaṃ ।।

praṇavaṃ caiva mārttaṇḍaṃ obhāvaṃ cākuśaṃ tathā ।
tripadādyaṃ ca okāraṃ nāmabhiḥ parigīyate ।।

tripadaṃ ūrddhahastaṃ ca taṃtrīśaṃ ca caturddaśaṃ ।
okāraṃ nāmabhirdevi tathaiva parikīrttitaṃ ।।

tithīśaṃ cāpyamāvāsyā grahaṇe samudāhṛtaṃ ।
lāṃcchanaṃ cānusvāraṃ ca bhūṣaṇaṃ mantrapaddhatau ।।

biṃdveśa pa * biṃduṃ ca aṃkārākhyaṃ nigadyate ।
svarāntaṃ ca visargaṃ ca ka(lā)ntaṃ ca kṣayantathā ।।

ṣoḍaśaṃ ca mahāsūryaṃ akāraṃ sṛṣṭirucyate ।
ya ete kīrttitā rudrāḥ sarvve sūryāśca ṣoḍaśa ।।

adhunā yonisaṃbhūtā catustriṃśati ye śṛṇu ।
yonyā(dyaṃ ka)rttanaṃ caiva kalaśaṃ parikīrttitaṃ ।।
brahmāṇaṃ śīrṣakaṃ caiva kakāraṃ paripaṭhyate ।।

chedakāntaṃ karparaṃ ca khageśaṃ ca nagaṃ tathā ।
aikārāmiti vikhyātaṃ paryāyaiśca kharottama ।।
(199b)
ekadaṃṣṭraṃ ca mātaṃgaṃ gaṇeśaṃ gajamastakaṃ ।
kharparāntaṃ samākhyātaṃ gakāraṃ varavarṇini ।।

ghargharaṃ ghoraninadaṃ triśūlaṃ parighargharaṃ ।
triśīrṣaṃ vyāvṛtāsyaṃ ca gha(kā)raṃ maṇisa * taṃ ।।

rāmanāsā tathā daṃṣṭrā ṛktaṃ ṛktodaraṃ tathā ।
dhyātanāspadakāraṃ ca nāmabhiḥ paribhāṣyate ।।

tṛtīyādyaṃ tathā caṇḍaṃ graṃthivaktraṃ karālinaṃ ।
caṃḍabhairavanāmānaṃ cakāraṃ paribhāṣyate ।।

padmāsanaṃ * dvakūpaṃ chamanaṃ chomakaṃ tathā ।
ekanālaṃ chakāraṃ ca nāmabhistaṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।।

jamadagniṃ jayādyaṃ ca jihvilaṃ ca dvijihvikaṃ ।
rasā nāsāntaraṃ caiva jakāramiha kīrttitaṃ ।।

bhairavaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ śūlaṃ ḍāmaraṃ cordhvabāhukaṃ ।
jhakāraṃ nāmabhirdevi mayā te samudāhṛtaṃ ।। ।।

adhobāhuṃ saṃkaṭaṃ ca mahābhīmaravaṃ tathā ।
sakāraṃ te samākhyātaṃ ṭakāramadhunocyate ।।

somaṃ caṃdraṃ cāmṛtaṃ ca arddhacaṃdramudāhṛtaṃ ।
kūpavaktraṃ ṭhakāraṃ ca pūrṇacaṃdrañca varttulaṃ ।।

akhaṇḍamaṇḍalākāraṃ mayā te parikīrttitaṃ ।
musalaṃ caiva mardañca yoginīnāṃ ca vallabhaṃ ।।

ḍakāraṃ ḍaṃvarādhāraṃ nāmabhi jñeyameva hi ।
bhairavaṃ dṛḍhakarṇaṃ ca caṃḍograṃ haripiṃgalaṃ ।।

graṃthīśaṃ ca ḍhakāraṃ ca vijñeyaṃ nāmabhiḥ sadā ।
aśvaṃ meghamadhāvaktraṃ ṇakāraṃ parikīrttitaṃ ।।

adhoṅkuśāṃṣaṃ ca meṣaṃ taraṃḍaṃ taraṇantathā ।
takāraṃ te samākhyātaṃ ṣakāramadhunocyate ।।
(200a)
cāṣaṃ madhyasaraṃ śarṣa ṛtadhāmanamucyate ।
graṃthikaṃ dāruṇaṃ dāntaṃ dakāraṃ nāmabhiḥ smṛtaṃ ।।

cāpaṃ dhanurddharādhānaṃ madhyaśūnyaṃ ca kārakaṃ ।
niṣedhaṃ cārddhvaśaṃkuṃ ca sa graṃthimalinaṃ tathā ।।

nakāraṃ nāmabhiḥ khyātaṃ mayā suravarārcite ।
vyāvṛtāsyaṃ tathā padmaṃ dvibhujaṃ ca pakārakaṃ ।।

kuṃḍalaṃ bhairavaṃ rāvaṃ rāviṇī yoginīpriyaṃ ।
kuṃḍalīnāthamatulaṃ kruraṃ vai bhīmaśaṃkukaṃ ।।

phakāradhāmadhāmānaṃ nāmabhiḥ samudāhṛtaṃ ।
kumbhaṃ kuṃbheśvaraṃ * * * kāraṃ paripacyate ।।

bhakāraṃ bhairavaṃ bhīmaṃ adhojaṃghamudāhṛtaṃ ।
paṃcamāntaṃ lāṃcchanaṃ ca kālaṃ puruṣameva ca ।।

paśuṃ marmāntaraṃgaṃ ca makāraṃ samudāhṛtaṃ ।
vāyusādhāranāthaṃ ca prāṇaṃ caiva yakārakaṃ ।।

bāhuṃ ca ṭeṭibhaṃ caiva devāsyaṃ bhīmavigrahaṃ ।
phephaṃ krodhasamākhyātaṃ tathā tejojvalaṃ ca tat ।।

pṛthivīśaṃ kramavalaṃ sthiraṃ ca jamudāhṛtaṃ ।
lakāraṃ lakṣmijananaṃ paryāyaiḥ parikīrttitaṃ ।।

vakāraṃ vāruṇaṃ candraṃ dadhāsaudhaṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।
śiśireśaṃ varaṃ śaṃbhuṃ śakāraṃ samudāhṛtaṃ ।।

dvigahvaraṃ tathā ḍāvyaṃ śaṃkaraṃ ca ḍāḍārakaṃ ।
somaṃ cāmṛtanāthaṃ ca sudhāsāraṃ sudhānidhiṃ ।।

sakāraṃ ṣadvasādhāraṃ nāmabhiḥ samudāhṛtaṃ ।
haṃsaṃ śūnyaṃ tathā prāṇaṃ mahānāvaṃ mahākalā ।।

mahācchāyā dvikubjaṃ ca hakāraṃ nāmabhiḥ smṛtaṃ ।
kṣa(kā)raṃ bhuvaneśānaṃ sūryaṃ kṣayamudāhṛtaṃ ।।
(200b)
sarvvāntaṃ bhāskaraṃ dhāmamātṛkāsāramuttamaṃ ।
evaṃ te varṇavṛṃdasya nāma nirṇayamuttamaṃ ।।

samākhyātaṃ mayā devi sarvvasāramanuttamaṃ ।
anena rahitāḥ sarvve jaḍā saṃparikīrttitāḥ ।।

mantramārge mahālakṣmi sārametadudāhṛtaṃ ।
asmintantravare khyātaṃ etatsāraṃ mayā tava ।।

na deyaṃ duṣṭabuddhīnāṃ nānya devaratātmanāṃ ।
śraddhālave gurau bhakte naiśreyasarate śubhe ।।

deyametanmahājñānaṃ śivavaktrāṃbujodgataṃ ।
ye bhyasanti mahātmānaḥ pūjayanti paṭhantyapi ।।
sarvve te bhairavā jñeyāḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ ।।

iti varṇavidhānamuttamaṃ te,
sukaraṃ samyagihoditāṃ ca nānyat ।
vada parvvata vallabhe sphuṭaṃ,
tattava hṛdaya sarojagaṃ subhru ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraścchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
varṇanāmapaṭalaḥ ।।

ityākarṇya surendravaṃdyacaraṇā devānmahābhaivāt,
bhūyopyāha girīndrajā priyatamaṃ yadvānyamastāhataṃ ।
saṃsārāśca viśoṣapāvakamalaṃ yadvāḍavaṃ bhāsuraṃ,
mokṣaśrīkramadeśikaṃ krama * raṃ sarvvātigaṃ śāśvataṃ ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

tṛptāsmi parameśāna jñānaughaṃ pariśṛṇvatī ।
kṛtakṛtyādyajātāsmi rājā cāyaṃ jayadrathaḥ ।।

adyāpi na mayā jñātaṃ yatpurā sūcitaṃ tvayā ।
yathā tantrasya paryante kramajñānaprapūjanaṃ ।।
(201a)
vaktavyamakhilaṃ samyakkasmāttadgopayāmyahaṃ ।
yadi tuṣṭosi bhagavan tadākhyā hi prasādataḥ ।।

evamākarṇya bhagavān prasphuradraśmijālavat ।
prāśyādyayāgasaṃbhūtamuvāca ca vacomṛtaṃ ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu pravakṣyāmi yattvayā codito hyahaṃ ।
mahārahasyamatulaṃ gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ ।।

tribhedabhinnaṃ vakṣyāmi jñānaṃ paramadurlabhaṃ ।
sāvalaṃbanirālaṃbaṃ dvayameva samasthitaṃ ।।

ābhyāmantaragaṃ cānyadbhāvasaṃhāramadbhutaṃ ।
iti tridhāmamākhyātamadhunā prabravīmi te ।।

ādyaṃ śāktaṃ sphuradvīryaṃ prollasadraśmimālinaṃ ।
cidullāsākaraṃ dīptaṃ samastapadamūrcchanaṃ ।।

ekamālaṃvya suvyaktaṃ dvayaṃ trikamathāparaṃ ।
catuṣkaṃ paṃcakaṃ cānyaṃ ṣoḍhā vai saptadhāparaṃ ।।

aṣṭadhā navadhā cānyaddaśaṃ caikādaśātmakaṃ ।
dvādaśātmantathā gauri trayodaśavidhau(lava)t ।।

caturddaśātmā śubhage sālaṃbaṃ vyaktimāgataṃ ।
śaktivīryamavādyaṃ syāccicchaktyāryaṃ dvidhā mataṃ ।।

citparaṃ śakticaitanya trikabhedamudāhṛtaṃ ।
etattrikamanaścaiva catuṣkamiti kīrttitaṃ ।।

bhūtirdhṛtirmahānaṃdā bhāsāvyaktiśca paṃcadhā ।
ṣoḍhā ṣaṭcakrabhedena saptadhā cāntabhedataḥ ।।

bhraṣṭadhātutribhedena navadhā ca tvabhedataḥ ।
bhūtendriyasamāpatyā daśadhā tava kīrttitaṃ ।।

manohaṃkārasaṃbuddhi madhyavṛtti samāśrayāt ।
(201b)
caturddaśāntaboddhavyaṃ kramātsālaṃbameva tat ।।

tatrākhaṃḍitacidvīryavalanā vyaktibhāsurām ।
raśmivṛṃdasphurat sphārasphītavṛtyavalokanā(t) ।।

sālambamevamāyāti vyaktiḥ suravarārcite ।
atra raśmisamūhasya vibhāgaṃ śṛṇu sāṃprataṃ ।।

siddhāraktā suśuṣkānyā utpalā parikīrttitā ।
kālī ca kālarātrī (ca) kāladhāre kaleśvarī ।।

siṃhavaktrā ca mārjārī uṣṭrā kāpālinī tathā ।
khararūpā ca virūpā ca meṣarūpā mahoragā ।।

raktākṣī raktavāsā ca laṃbakarṇī tathaiva ca ।
pṛthodarī tvekanetrā lokanāthā bhayaṃkarī ।।

ulūkavadanā cānyā kālavaktrā ca khiṃkhinī ।
karaṃkā bhadrakālī ca tathaivānyā mahābalā ।।

bharuḍā hyaṭṭahāsā ca rākṣasī hyāsurī tathā ।
etā eva smṛtā raśmyā dṛṣṭyājñāṣṭakabhedataḥ ।।

kulavidyāpūrvvayuktā svanāmakṛtamadhyagā ।
padārvvā pūjanīyāstā samyakprālaṃbasiddhidāḥ ।।

sphuraccakrakramāntasthā svasthānapravijṛṃbhitaḥ ।
prakṣīṇabhāvavṛṃdaughā sarvvāhārāḥ sulaṃpaṭāḥ ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale sālaṃbakramavidhibhedaḥ ।।

ataḥ paraṃ nirālaṃ * śṛṇu vīrendravaṃdite ।
nirīhaṃ nistaraṅgañca niradhvānaṃ nirīśvaraṃ ।।

śivaṃ sarvvagataṃ vyāptiṃ sadasatpadavarjitaṃ ।
cittātītaṃ praśāntāntaṃ nistaraṃgārṇavopamaṃ ।।

sarvvābhāsamasaṃsaktaṃ bharaṇādbhairaveśvaraṃ ।
(202a)
nirālambaṃ samākhyātaṃ nirupādhiranantakaṃ ।।

ta * ktivīryasaṃdoha labhyaṃ pūjyaṃ svarūpagaṃ ।
ṣaṭtriṃśatatvakathitaṃ piṃḍādipadabhūmigaṃ ।।

jāgradādisvarūpasthaṃ nirālaṃbamudāhṛtaṃ ।
atra bhairava santyā * sphuṇaṃ svāṃśagaṃ ca yat ।।

sālaṃbhepi purā tadvatparitaḥ parikīrttitaṃ ।
yatsvasvabhāvaviśrīmasuṃdaraṃ pūrṇamadvayaṃ ।।

taṃ nirālambamākhyātaṃ na śāntaṃ gaganopamaṃ ।
atra raśmyaugha madhyāsye yathāvacchṛṇu tāvubhā ।।

dhūmrā jvālā ca gāṃdhārī gahaneśī tathāparā ।
māyā cānyā mahāmāyā nityā cānyā hyanityatā ।।

śāmbhavegā pratiṣṭhā ca hyapratiṣṭhā tathāparā ।
dvādaśaiva samākhyātā raśmayo vīramaṃtharā ।।

āsāmaṣṭakamanyacca madhye (bhai)ravasaṃjñakaṃ ।
śrīvatsaṃ śaṃvaraṃ śāntaṃ jagadādhārasaṃjñakaṃ ।।

jagaddyutiṃ ca viśveśaṃ īśānamajasaṃjñitaṃ ।
aṣṭāvete samākhyātāḥ sphuradvīryapadoditāḥ ।।

āsāṃ madhye ta(thai)śāṃ ca nirālambaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ ।
hrī̃ śrī̃kārasamāyogā nāma pādāntayojitā ।।

pūjākramavidhānotra sarvveṣāṃ mantramuddhṛtaṃ ।
rahasyamevamākhyātaṃ nirālaṃbaṃ maheśvari ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale nirālaṃbakramapūjāvidhipaṭalaḥ ।।

athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi rahasyatamamuttamaṃ ।
ābhyāṃ madhye dvibhīti pūrvvāparavivarjitaṃ ।।

bhāvasaṃhāramatulaṃ sarvvasaṃhārabhāsuraṃ ।
(202b)
yatkramadvayamākhyātaṃ tatra yat sphuraṇaṃ mahat ।।

vyaktaṃ raśmiprabhedena ghoramunmārgamāgataṃ ।
jvalatsphuradghoraraśmikālanaikā(sti) bhāsuraṃ ।।

tatra saṃhārakaraṇī kālikā ghoravigrahā ।
sarvvabhakṣā bhairavāsyā kṛtāntakaraṇīparā ।।

atra raśmoghamākhyāsye yathāvattanumadhyame ।
saṃhāriṇī kālama(tha)nī prathamā parikīrttitā ।।

rakteśvareśvarī cānyā kṛtāntāṃ taṃ * rī tathā ।
kālarātrī samākhyātā caṃḍā caṃḍeśvarī tathā ।।

vīrabhadrā tathā cānyā tava devi prakīrttitā ।
ru(dra)saṃhārasaṃhārī jagatkālanatatparā ।।

mahābhairavasaṃhārasaṃhāriṇyāmudāhṛtāḥ ।
evamekādaśaibhāstā raśmayo ghoradarśanāḥ ।।

kulavidyā pūrvvayuktā pādāṃtāstāḥ prakīrttitāḥ ।
viparītakrameṇaiva pūjanīyāḥ sumadhyame ।।

paṃktiyogetha cakre vā raktavastra trikoṇake ।
pūjanīyaṃ kramavaraṃ tridhā yatparikīrttitaṃ ।।

sarvvavīropacāreṇa surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।
mahāmāṃsāsavādibhyo vīrapānaiśca nirbharaiḥ ।।

mahāpaśusamūhaiśca hāṭhaje vīrasādhanaiḥ ।
saṃpūjyayet kramavaraṃ mahābhairavavallabhaṃ ।।

pūjanāt kramabhedasya saṃcare jñānamuttamaṃ ।
yacca ta{t} kramasarvvasvaṃ tridhā caiva caturyadhā ।।

paṃcadhā vā mahābhāge vacastvavitathaṃ mama ।
atyantagopayenmuṣṭiṃ rahasyatamamadbhutaṃ ।।
(203a)
prāṇānte nāpi subhage na vaktavyaṃ kramottamaṃ ।
dvayasyāpīha kathanādbhairavopyutkrame svayaṃ ।।

ādyayāgavidhāvatra caturthaṃ bhedamuttamaṃ ।
taṃ jñātvā ca trayānāṃ vā kathanaṃ kuru bhairavi ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale kramabhedabhāvasaṃhārabhedavidhiḥ ।।

evamākarṇya girijā romāṃcitaśarīriṇī ।
prāha prasannagaṃbhīraṃ stotrametadanuttamaṃ ।।

namastejasvarūpāya raśmicakracarāya ca ।
sṛṣṭirūpāya devāya tathā sthitividhāya ca ।।

avabhārakramasthāya kālicakrasthitāya ca ।
mahāsaṃhārarūpāya namo mūrttasvarūpiṇe ।।

namaścaṃdrārkasadgrāsaparitṛptāya śāsine ।
cidrāhagrāsasaṃdīptabhairavāya namo namaḥ ।।

vahnitrayakṛtāhāramadamattāya śaṃbhave ।
namaḥ kalā maṇḍa(li)ne raśmyāhārāya mṛtyave ।।

vicitrapadadhāmnisthā namaścaṇḍogramūrttaye ।
namo dvādaśasaṃhāra namaḥ ṣoḍaśalaṃpa * ।।

trisvabhāvadhurādhīśa tathaikādaśanirbhara ।
paṃcā(hā)raprapaṃcetha paṃcaratnanamostute ।।

trikoṇānaṃdasaṃtṛptatrikoṇādhāralaṃpaṭa ।
trisvabhāvadhurādhīśa tripathoditavigraha ।।

triskaṃdhatriradhiṣṭhānacaṃdanāhāragṛhnate ।
namaḥ sṛṣṭitrayagrāsaghasmarāya smarāraye ।।

namo namo namastubhyaṃ bhairavāya bhavacchide ।
prapūrṇāsmi sutṛptāsmi rahasyāmṛtasevanāt ।।

jñātumicchāmi bhagavan (ra)hasyatamanuttamaṃ ।
(203b)
yadādya yāgamatulaṃ yathāvadvaktumarhasi ।।

evaṃ stuto yadā devyā bhairavobhūribhogadaḥ ।
uvāca paramarthāto yāgamādyaṃ yathārthavat ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge viśuddhajñānabhājane ।
varṇayiṣyāmi saṃkṣepādādyayāgaṃ surārcitaṃ ।।

brahmāviṣṇustathā rudra īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ ।
bhairavo nāśritaścaiva tathānye muktapūruṣāḥ ।।

śakrogniryamarakṣoṃbha vāyuḥ somārtha īśvaraḥ ।
vaśavośvinaādityāgrahānāgāsurādayaḥ ।।

mahākālotha vighneśo naṃdirudro vṛṣastathā ।
bhṛṃgī ca gaṇayāḥ sarvve tathā dakṣādayopi ca ।।

yoginyaḥ sarvva evātra devyo vai śaktayastathā ।
yaccānya surasaṃghātaṃ siddhisaṃghātameva ca ।।

ādyayāgaṃ bhavatteṣāṃ maithunākhyaṃ mahottamaṃ ।
teṣāṃ maithunamevātra japatvenopakalpitaṃ ।।

pūjanaṃ bhagamadhyasthaṃ cubanaṃ bhagamadhyagaṃ ।
sānnidhyaṃ bhagamadhyasthaṃ yogamanyadbhagasthitaṃ ।।

sādhanaṃ bhagapadmasthaṃ maṃḍalaṃ bhagapadmajaṃ ।
devatā bhagapadmasthā sugaṃ ca bhagamadhyagaṃ ।।

caruṃ ca bhagagaṃ teṣāṃ bhagoddhāraṃ bhagāntaraṃ ।
tasmādbhaginyo deveśi prapūjyaḥ sarvvasiddhaye ।।

lāṃcchanaṃ bhaga me bāhuḥ śaktimudrā bhagaṃ smṛtaṃ ।
bhedakaṃ bhaga me bāhurdevadevaṃ bhagaṃ paraṃ ।।

bhagodbhavaṃ jagatsarvvaṃ bhagamadhye layaṃ mataṃ ।
bhagaliṃgāṅkitaṃ sarvvaṃ jagadetaccarācaraṃ ।।

tasmādādyaṃ (mahādyaṃ) mahāyāgaṃ śṛṇu vīrendravaṃdite ।
(204a)
yamavāpya na śocaṃti sādhakāḥ siddhikāṃkṣiṇaḥ ।।

sadānuṣṭhānaniratāḥ sadā mohabahiḥkṛtāḥ ।
suptaṃ veśma samāsādyamupali(ptaṃ) suśobhanaṃ ।।

nānācitrasamāyuktaṃ sudhūpāmodamaṃtharaṃ ।
caṃdanāgurusaṃsiktaṃ puṣpāmodabharālasaṃ ।।

labhasragdāmaracitā citānodyupaśobhitaṃ ।
netraṃ vahakṛtāṭopāṃ kiṃkiṇī jālamaṃḍitaṃ ।।

cakravākacakorāviśukapārāvabhāvitaṃ ।
tatra khaṭvā śubhāṃ kṛtvā daṃtahetādicitritāṃ ।।

nānāśobhā susaṃpūrṇṇāṃ kiṃkiṇī jālamaṃḍitāṃ ।
pratipādaiḥ suracitāṃ sālaṃ bhaṃjikasaṃyutāṃ ।।

ihopanā ca nisre tu tūlikā prastare giriḥ ।
citravastraṃ pracchadārthaṃ āstīryaṃ tatra bhāmini ।।

karpūrāccharitaṃ kāryaṃ yadvā śṛṃgāradīpakaṃ ।
tattattatrā carenmaṃtrī yena saṃdīpanaṃ bhavet ।।

tatastatrā nayeddūtīṃ madaghūrṇitalocanāṃ ।
biṃboṣṭhī cārudaśanāṃ sabhūbhaṃgānanāṃ śubhāṃ ।।

ta(ntra)vālamṛgābhāsa nayanāṃ cāruhāsinīṃ ।
