sphuradbhramarasaṃghātanibhasatkeśapāśikāṃ ।।

kāmakārmukasaṃkāśaṃ bhabhaṃgetaranekṣaṇāṃ ।
dravaccāgīkarāvarṇṇāṃ savarṇṇāṃ nistaraṃgiṇīṃ ।।

karṇṇābharaṇasaccitra(śo)bhāśataśuśobhanāṃ ।
satkaṃṭhanabhasatkaṃṭha * * * varabhūṣiṇaṃ ।।

gajakuṃbhanibhoddāmastanabhārāvanāmitāṃ ।
suvṛttopacilākārabāhukadalimaṃḍitāṃ ।।

satyaṃ ca phaṇasaṃkāśakaraśākhāvirājitāṃ ।
(204b)
sphuradratnaśikhā citra ūrmikaṃgulibhūṣitāṃ ।।

pūrṇṇenduvaralāvaṇyaṃ vadanāṃ cittahāriṇīṃ ।
hariheti mahāsiṃha pipīlavaramadhyagāṃ ।।

trivali(śro)ṇi sadvimvajaghanālasagāmināṃ ।
raṃbhākarikarākāravaroruvarujaṃghikāṃ ।।

satkāmaravacakrābhagulphapādasuśobhanāṃ ।
pralaṃva hemāraraṇahārāvalivirājitāṃ ।।

sphuranmaṃjīrasaṃkārarasanā mukharasvarāṃ ।
pārihārya jhanatkāravalayadhvānamaṃtharān ।।

mattanāgendrasaṃkāśagamāṃ gambhīranābhikāṃ ।
haṃsagadgadavāgvaṃśasadṛśāṃ śubhabhā(ṣi)ṇīṃ ।।

keyūraśūtrikāsārapuṣpasragdāmabhūṣitāṃ ।
mahāmohaparikṣīṇacetasāmindubhāvitāṃ ।।

bhairavaikacamatkāracarvarṇaikasvarūpiṇīṃ ।
parabodhāmṛtāsvādaparamānaṃdapūrṇṇitāṃ ।।

madirāsavasanmāsaparitṛptasvarūpiṇīṃ ।
sāhūtirmohanīmudrā jagatyasmiṃścarācare ।।

rūpeṇāharate cānyāṃ sparśe nānyāṃ prabhāṣaṇāt ।
anyāṃ salāsaṃcāreṇa līlāceṣṭādike pare ।।

vilolālokanetrānye cuṃbanenāpare priye ।
gāḍhāliṃganake nānyā nāharetsa mahābalā ।।

sā vai cāṣṭakulodbhūtā karttavyā mṛgalocane ।
tāmāropamahā * lpe nyeseddevīṃ kṛśodarīṃ ।।

vastrayugmena saṃcchādya tatra yāgaṃ samārabhet ।
bhagapadme suvistīrṇe parvvapīṭhopapīṭhini ।।

kurunālaṃ ca pādāṃkaṃ vṛttadvārasuṃdare ।
(205a)
klinnāṃ lāṃcchanasahyaṃṭe jaṣadhvajapadākule ।।

sadrajaḥ paṃkasājjāte retodyāvarttalālini ।
tena maṃtrāstrakalaśe trikoṇapariveṣṭinī ।।

mukhopapaṃkajākīrṇṇe bāhukeśopalāṃcchane ।
manodbhavabharoddāma satsaṃnidhisumaṃthare ।।

sītkāraravasanmaṃtra proccaretkilikiṃcite ।
tatrādau devadeveśyau pūjayetparameśvari ।।

(ni)tyaklinnā tathā raktā mohakoṭā tu yāśritā ।
smaramaṃdiramadhyasthā śuṣkāṃ bhairavavallabhāṃ ।।

bhāmadhye pūjayeddevīṃ vāmāveśātpranāśanī ।
sālaṃvetha nirālaṃve bhāvasaṃhārake ca yā ।।

devyastā eva pūjyātra nityādiprakrameṇa ca ।
mahopahārasaṃbhārairarcayedvibhavaṃ yathā ।।

nānāmāṃsāsavaiścitraiḥ sahajaiḥ kṛtrimaistathā ।
caṃdanāgurukarpūraiḥ puṣpairnānāvidhaistathā ।।

gītamaṃgalanirghoṣairvīṇāpaṇavabhiḥ svanaiḥ ।
evaṃ prapūjya tatrasthaṃ kramametanmahādhipe ।।

paścāttu juhuyāttatra yathā yacca tathā śṛṇu ।
tāmunnāna (ta)taḥ kṛtvā sa saprabhena cetasā ।।

kāmayeta śaśītkāraṃ harṣavyākulitekṣaṇaḥ ।
trikoṇabhagakuṃḍātalliṃgaśrucyājuhedalaṃ ।।

ādyaṃ tacchukramatulaṃ tarparṇārthaṃ tato ju(he)t ।
evaṃ homaṃ samātiṣṭhedyāvadvaikṣaṇamātrakaṃ ।।

tāvadā viśate dūtiḥ kṛtvā kaṃṭharavaṃ mahat ।
snehātkaulikamevaiṣa kathayennātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

tatkāla eva saṃcārakramapūrṇṇaiḥ prajāyate ।
(205b)
priya melāpamāyānti devyā devyastu sādhake ।।

sa * * * * * * * prārthayeccāmṛtaṃ śubhaṃ ।
āpivedghaṃṭikāvaktraṃ picuvaktraṃ prayogataḥ ।।

ākarṣātkarṣa(ye)t karṣāṃ karṣyakendupakarṣaṇāt ।
cuṃbetpivellihedyāvattāva * sarvvasiddhibhāk ।।

nistaraṃgini ciṃkalyo hyadvaitācārabhāvitaṃ ।
siddhiṃ ca prāpnuyāddevi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

praṇayanti ca taṃ nityaṃ yoginyo bhairavaiḥ saha ।
yāṃtamatrānuyāsyaṃti raśmayo nātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

yāvadevaṃ mahāyāgaṃ na kṛtaṃ sādhakaiḥ tathā ।
* vaktramārthamatulaṃ na * sārati bhairavi ।।

etadyāgavaraṃ siddhaṃ mahābhairavapālitaṃ ।
sakṛtsiddhipradaṃ khyātaṃ mahābhoga * * vahaṃ ।।

tadasti priyavastu * yatra sādhayate balāt ।
yadi lakṣaṇahīnaḥ syāddūtīnāṃ sādhakātmanāṃ ।।

tadvīracittāniṣkaṃpā sarvvakarmasu sasyate ।
āhārantādṛśī * * * thavā vīramelake ।।

etattava samākhyātaṃ ādyayāgaṃ surārcitaṃ ।
kiṃ bhūyaḥ kathayiṣyāmi vada yatte hṛdi sthitaṃ ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
ādyayāgavidhiḥ kramārthapūjāpaṭalaḥ ।।

evamākarṇya girijā praharṣotphullalocanā ।
uvāca parameśānaṃ praṇipatya maheśvarī ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

bhagavan śaśikhaṃḍāṅkaparamāmṛtanirbhara ।
(206a)
prabuddhivīramelāpavidhimatyantamottamaṃ ।।

yenāsukṛtamātreṇa kramajñānārthabhāgbhavet ।
itthamākarṇya girijā vaktrapadmotthijaṃ vacaḥ ।।
prāha gaṃbhīrayā vācā bhagavān vīramelakaṃ ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge yanmātvaṃ paripṛcchasi ।
rahasyametadākhyātaṃ tathāpi prabravīmi te ।।

āśritya suptaṃ śaraṇaṃ sarvvabādhāvivarjitaṃ ।
suśobhaṃ kumbhamāmodasudhūpavaramaṃtharaṃ ।।

kulaparvvaṃ samāsādya vīramelāpamācaret ।
nimantrayettataḥ sarvve bhairavācārapālakāḥ ।।

samayajñā devi bhaktāḥ santuṣṭāḥ kramatatparāḥ ।
samayinaḥ putrakāśca sādhakā daiśikāḥ pare ।।

yoginyo yāḥ prabuddhāśca bhaktā pātādalabhataḥ ।
ānīyatāpyasahatāṃ vrūtādi(yu)tāṃ kuru ।।

āsanānyatra deyāni yathā tat kramayogataḥ ।
pūjyāni praṇavenaiva gaṃdhadhūpasragādibhiḥ ।।

tatopaveśayet samyagyathācakraniyogataḥ ।
gurūṇāṃ prathamaṃ cakraṃ sādhakānāṃ dvitīyakaṃ ।।

tṛtīyaṃ putrakānāṃ syāccaturthaṃ samayeṣvatha ।
yoginīnāṃ paṃcamaṃ syādevaṃ kuryāt krameṇa ca ।।

sarvvavīrasamāyoge tatra āvarttayedgiriḥ ।
paṃcāśāṇi mayā śaktiḥ kālī sā sakalāvyayā ।।

na ca vīrasamāyogaṃ tasyāntaḥ pravijṛmbhate ।
tena tadvīramelāpaṃ pūjayet parameśvarī ।।

arghaiḥ puṣpaistathā dhūpaiścaṃdanāguru(carci)taiḥ ।
(206b)
pratyaikamarcayettatra yathā vibhavayogataḥ ।।

praticakrasya madhyasthaṃ pūjayenmadhyabhairavaṃ ।
kṛṣṇalohitaraktākṣaṃ paramāmodavāsitaṃ ।।

mahānaṃdakaraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ jagadunmādakārakaṃ ।
evaṃ saṃpūjya madhyasthaṃ pātrānyeṣā prapūrayet ।।

mahākusumapūrṇṇāni paṃcaratnacitāni ca ।
paścādvividhamāhārateṣāṃ deyamatandritaṃ ।।

yadyasyābhimataṃ devi ta(tta)sya vicaretsakṛta ।
nānāmāṃsāni citrāṇi bhakṣānyuccāvacāni ca ।।

lehyapehyāni cuṣyāṇi pānāni vividhāni ca ।
kaṭutiktakaṣāyāni madhurāmlāni yāni ca ।।

evamādi(ra)nekaiśca tarpayeccakrapaṃcakaṃ ।
madhyāhne bhūya santarpya yāvadānaṃdamanthanaṃ ।।।

tato hyarghaistathā geyairvvīṇāvaṃśasvanaistathā ।
śrotrodakakaraiścitraistarpayeccakrapaṃcakaṃ ।।

tatastaccakragā raśmyaḥ prollasanti sutejitāḥ ।
sphuranti mahadānaṃdā vilāsaśatasaṃkulā ।।

nṛtyanti ca hasaṃtyuccairvvalānti krīḍatonmukāṃ ।

patanti dhāvanti vasanti sarvve,
vepanti khidyanti layaṃti tatra ।
jalpanti mantrāṇi rahasyamanye,
sanmarmayuktāni cacāṃsi cānye ।।

kekoktapaścāpi vicitra pūpāḥ,
parasparaṃ te pravadanti devi ।
kecicca kekāracanāvireti,
kecicca mudrāvalimārabhaṃte ।।

kecicca kaulāgamajairvilāsaiḥ ।
saṃsthānakai narttanamārabhante ।।

kecittāṇḍavamārambhaṃti subhaṭāḥ kecicca sarvvoddhatāḥ,
kecidbhairavabhāvanāhitamanājalpaṃti kālaṃ pare ।
(207a)
kecidvādaśanekadhā pracalitaṃ kurvvanti tatroccakaiḥ,
kecidrodanatatparāḥ kimaparaṃ kecidvrajaṃtyāvilaṃ ।।

kecitpaṃcavilāsajārasavaraṃ bhuṃjanti tatrotsukāḥ,
kecitprāvaraṇāni cakravaraṇaiḥ saṃdarśayatyudbhaṭaṃ ।
keciccakravinirgatena sahasā svaṃdehamālaṃvati,
kecidvāntamathaṃ vighoravapuṣaṃ kācittruṭaṃ totkaṭaṃ ।।

kecitmaithunamārabhanti ca pivaṃtyatrodbhavaṃ nirbharāḥ,
evaṃ vīravarendravaṃdyacaraṇe melāpamatyuttamaṃ ।
vīrākhyaṃ pravitatparaśminikarānuccaiḥ samutpadyate,
dṛṣṭvā yat prabhavanti tat kramavarā kiṃ vā bahurvarṇyate ।।

saṃcārakramanirbharāḥ priyatame paṃcai tathaikāśayā,
yadyasya pravalopadeśakara * maudrakramoddīritaṃ ।
tattasya pravi(jṛṃ)bhate paramahosatyaṃ na caivāmṛtaṃ,
vīraṃ tāṇḍavametatte * * * mayā samupavarṇitaṃ ।।

yenāśvakṛtamātreṇa sarvvasiddhyarhatā bhavet ।
kramajñānaikanipuṇo bhavatyeva hi sā(dha)kaṃ ।।

nātaḥ parataraṃ yogaṃ vidyate bhuvanodare ।
sarvvacārthapraśamanaṃ sarvvopadravanāśanaṃ ।।

anena kālamātreṇa jñāyate bhairave kule ।
māsamātrātpiṃḍasiddhirbhava(tye)va na saṃśayaḥ ।।

plavanti gaganaṃ bhogaṃ sarvve te vigatajvarāḥ ।
aṇimādiguṇāvāptisaṃyuktā bhogalālasāḥ ।।

na tadvastvasti subhage yatra sādhayate balāt ।
atha kiṃ ba(hu)noktena saṃkṣepādiha ucyate ।।
(207b)
grahāṇāṃ bhāskaro yadvaccaṃdrastārākule yathā ।
dhāmnāṃ vahniryathā dīptaḥ sarvvāstre ca triśūlarāṭ ।।

vidyāmantrakadaṃveṣu yadvatkālo mahābalāḥ ।
gaṃgānadīkule yadvaddaṃbhasāṃpatyatirvvaraḥ ।।

tadvadyāgeṣu sarvveṣu vīrayāgaṃ praśasyate ।
ādyayāgaṃ tathā jñeyaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ ।।

anayoryāgayordevi māhātmyaṃ vatsarāyutaiḥ ।
sādākhyaṃ śakyatenaiva vaktuṃ jihvāyutairapi ।।

yathā yāgavarānetau yathā devī kṛśodarī ।
tacca taṃtreṣu sarvveṣu rājarājo hyayaṃ priye ।।
vidhinā likhitaṃ pūjyaṃ sarvvakāmaphalapradaṃ ।।

evaṃ yatkramataṃtrasādhanamalaṃ tatsāṃprataṃ varṇṇitaṃ,
tredhānyaktimupaiti yena subhage sadyogaratneśvaraṃ ।
kiṃ cānyaṃ nanu madhyame vadatarāṃ yatte sthitaṃ cetasi ।
yāvadvacmi tavādya sarvvamakhilaṃ taṃtresvarasyāttare ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraścchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
vīratāṃḍavavidhikramajñānapaṭalaḥ ।।

evamākarṇya vīrendrādvaravaṃditaśāsanāt ।
punarapyāha deveśī praṇipatya surārcitaṃ ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

bhagavan śrotumicchāmi tantrarājavidhiṃ yathā ।
kathametaṃ pratiṣṭhāpyaṃ kathaṃ vā likhyamuttamaṃ ।।

pūjyaṃ vā kathamevaitaṃ pūjyatvādvada kiṃ bhavet ।
prabhāvaḥ kiṃ viśiṣṭhasya kathamutkarṣakārakaṃ ।।
(208a)
etadicchāmi bhagavan khyātiṃ matimatāṃvara ।
evamākarṇya devendro devyāvacanamuttamaṃ ।।
prāha prasannagaṃbhīro girimatāmudāradhīḥ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu pravakṣyāmi yadāhaṃ coditastvayā ।
sārametanmahābhāge yattatra varapūjanaṃ ।।

tatrādau sthānamāsādya nirdvandvaṃ sumanoharaṃ ।
sarvvabādhāvinirmuktaṃ sarvvapuṣpaphalottaraṃ ।।

tatra veśmaṃ śubhaṃ kṛtvā caṃdanāgurucarcitaṃ ।
sadhūpāmodabahulaṃ vitānādyupaśobhitaṃ ।।

tatra saṃpūjavidhinā vakṣyamāṇena su(vra)te ।
paścānmaṃtrī mantrayitvā lekhanīyāpi suṃdari ।।

bhūrjapatrāṇi cāmaṃtrya kṛtakautukamaṃgalaḥ ।
likhedvarṇṇāni citrāṇi samāni suśirāṇi ca ।।

samaśīrṣāṇi ramyā(ṇi) citraṃ virddhakināni ca ।
māṃsalāni vicitrāṇi lolāmātrāṇi suvrate ।।

aghanāni suśuddhāni samāni paramāni ca ।
ślokānāṃ paṃcakaṃ lekhyaṃ daśakaṃ triṃśameva ca ।।

śa(ta)rddhānādhikaṃ devi lekhanīyaṃ kadācana ।
padavākyapramāṇajño lekhakotra sudīkṣitaḥ ।।

hemāṃgulīyakaṃ cāsmai dātavyaṃ ratnabhūṣitaṃ ।
vastrayugmaṃ śubhaṃ cānyatkaṭakā(bha)raṇāni ca ।।

avicchinnaṃ daheddhūpaṃ yāvallekhyaṃ samāpyate ।
tāvanmadyādivikṣepamavicchinnaṃ pradāpayet ।।

paścādyallikhitaṃ subhru pustakercyaṃ samarpayet ।
lekhakaṃ tarpayetpa(śu)nmadhuraiḥ svādubhojanaiḥ ।।
(208b)
evaṃ vidhena vidhinā yāvattantraṃ samāpyate ।
pratiṣaṭkasamāptau tu vīrayāgaṃ prakalpayet ।।

evaṃ niṣpādya taṃtreśaṃ śraddhābhaktiyuto giriḥ ।
paścātpratiṣṭhayeddevaṃ yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu ।।

āhutya yāgasaṃbhāraṃ surāmāṃsāsavādikaṃ ।
kuṃkumāgurukarpūraṃ mṛgajā caṃdanārcitaṃ ।।

tilādyādi ca yatsarvvaṃ dhātudravyānyanekaśaḥ ।
evamādidasaṃbhāraṃ rājopakaraṇādikaṃ ।।

śaktyādhivāsaye devaṃ pustakena sahaiva tu ।
bhadrapīṭhoparisthāpyaṃ puṣpadhūpādiryutaṃ ।।

saṃpūjayetpustakendraṃ nānārghasragvilepanaiḥ ।
mātṛkā sarvvamaṃtreśī tayā pūjādikaṃ kuru ।।

adhivāsārthamāhṛtya naivedyādikamācaret ।
anantaraṃ gaṇeśāna kṣetrapālādikaṃ yajet ।।

pa(ścā)tpustakagarbhantu devadevīṃ kṛśodarīṃ ।
yathābhimatarūpeṇa mātṛkāyā * * bhivet ।।

vāgeśī skaṃdamārūḍhāṃ parāṃdvyomaḥ samānayet ।
biṃdudhāmāpasadrūpatārāvalibhibhānyaset ।।

svasvarūpasvarūpeṇa svarūpaṇasvarūpaṇāt ।
nyasyāvatārayetsamyaṅmaṇḍalaṃ paṅkajopari ।।

tatra saṃpūjya vidhinā daleṣvapi yajettataḥ ।
vargāṣṭakena deveśi vāgeśvaryāṣṭakaṃ pṛthak ।।

haṃsā citrā bhairavānyā kadaṃbākhyā tathāparā ।
kamalā caiva nityā ca subhagā gauri cāṣṭamā ।।
(209a)
sarvvāścaṃdropamā devyā sarvvāḥ pratikṛtāsanāḥ ।
sarvvāścaturbhujā jñeyāḥ pāśāṃkuśakarāśvatāḥ ।।

devyaiḥ stutiparāḥ sarvvābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ ।
yathābhimatarūpāśca madhye dhyeyātra bhairavi ।।

akulāduditāvyaktarāviṇī vyaktimāgatā ।
mātṛkāskaṃdhavasitāmanekādbhutadarśanāt ।।

evaṃ pustakasaṃsthā vai pūjayet paramā kalā ।
nānādhūpopahāraiśca surāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

puṣpairnānāvidhaiścāpi paritaḥ paripūjayet ।
bahusaṃbhṛtasaṃbhāravittasāṭhyavivarjitaḥ ।।

juhuyādājyasanmiśraṃ gugulaṃ varavarṇṇini ।
tata ānīya śubhagaṃ vimānaṃ sarvvaśobhanaṃ ।।

nānālaṃkārasaṃcitraṃ nānāsragdāmamaṃḍitaṃ ।
nānākiṃkiṇisaṃyuktaṃ ghaṃṭācāmaracihnitaṃ ।।

patākādhvajacitrāṅgaṃ bahuvaśvopagūhitaṃ ।
bahuśobhāsamāyuktaṃ bahubhaṃgī śatācitaṃ ।।

tatrārccayetpustakendraṃ netrapaṭṭopagūhitaṃ ।
voḍhāropyavimānasya sarvvālaṃkāraśobhitāḥ ।।

karttavyāḥ subhagāḥ sarvve surāmāṃsāditarpitāḥ ।
agre turyaravāṃścitrān śaṃkhakāhalani(sva)nān ।।

dānaṃ tataścāvanaddhaṃ suśiraṃ yaścaturvidhaṃ ।
āṭopaṃ kārayeccāgre vimānasya maheśvari ।।

veśyāścaiva vilāśinyo tapasvinyaśca cāgragāḥ ।
nṛtyamānāḥ prakarttavyā madaghūrṇṇitalocanāḥ ।।
(209b)
śivaliṃga na evāgre triśūlavarapāṇayaḥ ।
nṛtyamānā valgayanti vimānasyāgragāminaḥ ।।

vṛṣabhāḥ paṭṭasaṃcchannāścitritāḥ puṣpaśobhitāḥ ।
dhāvantogre ca karttavyāstathānye naṭamallakāḥ ।।

cāraṇā vividhāḥ kāryāḥ sarvvābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ ।
vikire tatrato hemamaṇimuktapravālakāḥ ।।

tāratāṃmrādisarvvañca dvittasāṭhyavivarjitaḥ ।
evaṃ krameṇa nagaraṃ samastaṃ bhrāmayet priye ।।

vada devaṃ prasannā me raśmayo bhairavoditāḥ ।
apaścimamidaṃ janma yatpustakavaraṃ śubhaṃ ।।

avāptaṃ puṇyanicayairdevadeva prasādataḥ ।
evamuccārayaṃścāgre nṛttaṃ yā yānusādhakaḥ ।।

bhairavoditasadgītiṃ madhyamagrāmamāśritaṃ ।
gāyamānāḥ prahṛṣṭātmā kṛtakṛtyograto bhavet ।।
bhāṃbhagītiṃ viśālākṣi śṛṇuparvvatanaṃdini ।।

sakala asurendra tanutantravareśi vibho ।
mama vinidhihitāyathamalaṃ jagaduddharaṇe ।।

sakalarahasya tantravidyā mayamālayasāṃ ।
amalamalaṃ kuruṣva mama cintamidaṃ tu sadā ।।

iti yaṃ pravrajadagre prahṛṣṭanāntarātmanā ।
tato bhramitvā nagaraṃ pāyādgehamataṃdritaḥ ।।

tatrāvatārya subhage pustakaṃ kṛtamaṃgalaṃ ।
maṇḍalopari vinisya punaḥ saṃpūjayet priye ।।

nānāpuṣpopahāraiśca sarvvavīropacārataḥ ।
yadā ca vittahīnaḥ syādgṛha eva paribhramat ।।
(210a)
utsavaṃ tatra karttavyaṃ yathāvibhavarūpataḥ ।
tatovatārayetpustaṃ bhadrapīṭhe same śubhe ।।

śrīparṇīdāraracite devadārumayethavā ।
dakṣayellekhakaṃ paścādgobhūratnahiraṇyakaṃ ।।

anantaraṃ tatra sarvve dakṣayedye vyavasthitāḥ ।
tatastu bhojayetsarvve nānāmāṃsāsavādibhiḥ ।।

paścāttu tāṇḍavaṃ kāryaṃ yathātadvīramelake ।
evamekadinaṃ kāryaṃ tridinaṃ paṃca sapta vā ।।

daśa ṣoḍaśa vā māsaṃ yathā vibhavistarāt ।
evaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ yena pustakaṃ tanumadhyame ।।

ābrahmabhuvanaṃ tena jagatsarvvaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ ।
kiṃ tasya bahubhiryajñaiḥ kiṃ vā maṃḍalatarpaṇaiḥ ।।

kiṃ vā japavratairdevi yogasādhanasādhanaiḥ ।
yasyaivaṃ pustakaṃ gehe samukto bha(va)baṃdhanāt ।।

tasmin gṛhavare bhadre nityaṃ sannihito haraḥ ।
kālakaraṇī devī tathānye bhairaveśvarāḥ ।।

saptāsaptati koṭyastu mantrāṇāṃ ghoradarśanāṃ ।
dakṣiṇasrotasodbhūtaṃ tatra tiṣṭhanti nityaśaḥ ।।

yāśca devyaḥ śaktayaśca yoginyo ghoramātaraḥ ।
ḍāvyo ḍāmarikā lāmā svedyā kiṃkarikāstathā ।।

nityaṃ sannihitāḥ sarvvāstuṣṭāḥ sarvvāhitaiṣiṇaḥ ।
japaśabdaṃ prakurvvanti tasmin gṛhavare sadā ।।

sṛjaṃti dhanadhānyādi āyurārogyasaṃpadaḥ ।
tasmāt prapūjayennityaṃ yathāvibhavarūpataḥ ।।

yasmānmadhukarāyadvanmadhugolakamāśritāḥ ।
tadvanmaṃtrārṇave devyaḥ pustakendraṃ samāśritāḥ ।।
(210b)
ṣaṇmāsapūjanāttantraṃ piṇḍaṃ siddhipradāyakaṃ ।
svayamudbhavate jñānaṃ ṣaṇmāsāt sādhakasya tu ।।

yojanāntaramātraṃ vai yāvat kṣetraṃ vidhīyate ।
tantrāntare mṛtā ye tu rudralokaṃ vrajanti te ।।

pustakaṃ ye prapaśyanti pūjitaṃ surasuṃdari ।
rudralokaṃ prayāntyete devi nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।।

śrīpūrvvaṃ nāma vai grāhyaṃ (śi)raścchedeti noccaret ।
jayadratheṃdro vaktavyaṃ tantrarājānamaṃbike ।।

yogakāle śiraścchedamiti vaktavyameva hi ।
yāvantyakṣarajātāni pustakendrasthitāni tu  ।।

tāvanti nāma grahaṇādvarṣapūgāyutāni ca ।
rudraloke * * nmatryo jñānavijñānavarjitaḥ ।।

prabuddho yo smarennityaṃ tasya jñānaṃ pravarttate ।
yattatkālimukhe rūḍhaṃ ṣāṇmāsānnātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

tasminkāle prajānīyāt prasannāśaktayo mama ।
tasminkāle tantravaraṃ prāptaṃ puṇyena karmaṇā ।।

apaścimaṃ tadā janma jñātavyaṃ sādhakottamaiḥ ।
athavā bahunoktena śṛṇu saṃkṣepatodhunā ।।

asya tantravarasyaiva pūjanātpaṭhanātsadā ।
dhāraṇācchravaṇāddhyānātsmaraṇādyatphalaṃ mahat ।।

tadvarṇṇayitu me vāhaṃ notsahakalpalakṣakaiḥ ।
sādākhyaiḥ parameśāni jihvānāma yutāyutaiḥ ।।

pūjyametat tantravaraṃ sarvvakāmaphalapradaṃ ।
sṛṣṭibhedavaraṃ devaṃ praṇavaṃ grāhayedyadā ।।

grāhayetpustakakaraṃ tadā vittasamaṃ bhavet ।
(211a)
viṣuvaṃ jāyate yogaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ।।

evaṃ pratyayamālocya pūjayet pustakaṃ sadā ।
labdhaṃ tantravaraṃ samyaktyajetsarvvaṃ palālavat ।।

na kasyacit pradātavyaṃ tantrametadanuttamaṃ ।
dātānarakamāpnoti siddhihāniśca jāyate ।।
tasmānna dāpayenmaṃtrī yāvacchāsanakṛnna hi ।।

evaṃ pustakapūjanaṃ priyatame prātiṣṭhitaṃ varṇṇitaṃ,
sarvvaśreṣṭhatamaṃ jagadvitakaraṃ satsiddhimokṣāvahaṃ ।
saṃsārārṇavatāraṇaṃ kimadhunā vakṣyāmi te sāṃprataṃ,
tattatpūrṇṇaniśādhināthavadane yantraṃ vyadāsottamaṃ ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
pustakārikārapaṭalaḥ ।।

evaṃ devavarānnisamyagirijā saṃsāravicchinnaye,
bhūyopyāha surārcitāthanamitaṃ caṃdrārddhacūḍaṃ haraṃ ।
vyākhyānakramalolupā priyatamā prahvānatāṣṭāgavat,
stotre stutya punaḥ punaḥ priyatama sarvvāntimaṃ bhairavaṃ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

devadevaśaśāṅkāṅkatṛptāsmi parameśvara ।
jñānāmṛtasamaṃ digdhaṃ tvattaḥ saṃśṛṇvatī bhṛśaṃ ।।

adhunā śrotumicchāmi vyākhyānakramamaṃtrayat ।
kathaṃ vyākhyā prakarttavyā guruṇā tatvavedinā ।।
kathaṃ vā devadeveśa tathā me brūhi bhairava ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu vakṣyāmi subhage yadahaṃ coditastvayā ।
śāsane(smi)n yathā siddhirbhavatyeva maheśvari ।।
(211b)
suguptaṃ gṛhamāsādya sudhūpāmodavāsitaṃ ।
puṣpaprakarasaṃkīrṇaṃ caṃdanāgurucarcitaṃ ।।

śiṣyaiḥ parivṛtastatra pūjayet pustakaṃ varaṃ ।
nānāvalyupahāraiśca puṣpadhūpasugaṃdhibhiḥ ।।

gurukramaṃ samastaṃ ca mahāvibhavasaṃyutaṃ ।
ātmānamapiśiṣyāśca puṣpadhūpaiḥ samarcayet ।।

acchidraṃ vādayitvā tu pustakaṃ varavarṇini ।
prapūjayitvā tadvācyaṃ vyākhyeyaṃ padalaṃkṛtaṃ ।।

svareṇa puṣpamādena rasabhāvayutena ca ।
padavākyapramāṇajño vācakaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ ।।

anantaraṃ priye vyākhyā karttavyā guruṇā sakṛt ।
ādau padavibhākoktyā samāsaprakriyāṃ tayā ।।

pūrvvāparānusaṃdhānādvākyārthapraticāraṇāt ।
viśeṣoktyā mahāvākya vyaktyā vyākhyānamārabhet ।।

dhūlibhedamupanyanyā hṛdbhedamanuvācayet ।
vādaṃ jalpavitaṃḍā ca pakṣāpakṣavipakṣavat ।।

vākyāni pravicāryāṇi vidhīni prakriyāni ca ।
pravarttakaniṣedhāni pramāṇārthayutāni ca ।।

nyāyānyatra vicāryāṇi vibhāgoktyā maheśvari ।
siṃhāvalokitādīni ekaviṃśati yāni ca ।
nānyatra pravicāryāṇi samāsoktyā na vistarāt ।।
saṃbaṃdhamabhidheyaṃ ca prayojanayathāparaṃ ।।

tat prayojanamevānyadabhidhānādikaṃ ca yat ।
pravicārya pravaktavyaṃ guruṇā tatvavedinā ।।

sūtraṃ sūtrottaraṃ cānyattadāntikamathāparaṃ ।
(212a)
pravibhaktapravaktavyaṃ paṭalaṃ pratibhāmini ।।

yānyatra praviruddhāni darśanāni pramāṇavat ।
valanīyāni nānyaiva pramāṇaiḥ prāgvicārayet ।।

pūrvvaṣaṭke yathā samyaktathā kāryaṃ vipaścitā ।
pratyakṣamanumānaṃ ca tathaivāgamameva ca  ।।

indriyavyavasoyaṃ ca kalpanāpoḍha eva ca ।
abhrāntamiti pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ pravicāritaṃ ।।

anumānaṃ ca tatpūrvvaṃ trividhaiścāpyanekadhā ।
pūrvvavaccheṣa vaccānyatsāmānyadṛṣṭimeva ca ।।

liṃgapūrvvaṃ kvacidjñeyaṃ kvacitpūrvvaṃ kvacitsmṛtaṃ ।
evamatrānumāne syāttantrarājāntaro - taṃ ।।

āgamaṃ pareśotthaṃ vākyārka kiraṇāni ca ।
ajñānadvāntavidhvaṃsapaṭṭāni sumahānti ca ।।

sarvvajñaḥ sarvvakartṛtvāttaduktirnā virudhyate ।
pramāṇāntarakaiścānyairhyasarvvajñaḥ prakalpitaḥ ।।

etānyeva pramāṇāni citsvabhāvānugāni ca ।
antarvaktrānubhāvena prabhavanti na cānyathā ।।

svasaṃvedana sāmarthyādanubhāvānubhūṣitaḥ ।
sannāla(bhaṃ)tiṣenaite tatpramāṇāmakhaṃḍitaṃ ।।

evaṃ pramāṇikoktyā ca vicāryākhyeya maṃjasā ।
ṣoḍhāsambandhamākhyeyaṃ pratibhajya vicāryate ।।

parā mahānaṃtarālo divyodivya(sta)thāparaḥ ।
itaretaranāmānyo ṣaṣṭhamaṃ parakīrttitaḥ ।।

tatvaṣaḍviṃśatāyeyaṃ śuddhāśuddhasvarūpataḥ ।
dvayādvayavibhāgoktyā hyābhidheyamudāhṛtaṃ ।।

saṃsāradalanaṃ nāma malakarmādipāṭanaṃ ।
(212b)
prayojanamidaṃ jñeyaṃ tatprayojanamucyate ।।

siddhimuktiphalaprāptistathaivaṃ pradipāditaṃ ।
tṛtīyaṣaṭke deveśi abhidhānaṃ vicāritaṃ ।।

evaṃ krameṇa vyākhyeyaṃ tantrarājānamuttamaṃ ।
devyāsvarūpamākhyeyaṃ viśeṣapūjya yatnataḥ ।।

evaṃ vyākhyāmataṃ kṛtvā niyamyaṃ pustakaṃ tataḥ ।
saṃpūjya pūrvvaṃ vidhināmahaṃ saṃsārasaṃbhṛtaiḥ ।।

prapūjyedguruṃ paścātparamādvaitamāśritaṃ ।
anantaraṃ vīrayāgaṃ karttavyaṃ pratijānatā ।।

avicchinnaṃ daheddhūpaṃ vyākhyāsamaya eva hi ।
nādhītavyaṃ pratipade caturthyāmapi suṃdari ।।

aṣṭamyāṃ ca navamyāṃ vā caturddaśyāṃ ca suṃdari ।
amāpūrṇṇimayoścaiva na vyākhyeyaṃ kadācana ।।

bhūmikaṃpeva digdāhe deśabhaṃgeṣu dāruṇe ।
rājahānau tathā daustha cittahānau na jātucit ।।

ācāryāṇāṃ vipattau ca na vyākhyeyaṃ kadācana ।
saṃkrāntiṣu vyatīpāte yaṃtrāsvevaṃ samācaret ।।

nimittapūjā karttavyā suviśeṣeṇa bhāmini ।
śiṣyān parīkṣayet samyakpravicāreṇa bhairavi ।।

saṃdigdhaṃ ca viparyastaṃ vyutpattirahitaṃ tathā ।
tridhā vicārya nipunaṃ paścādvyākhyānamārabhet ।।

pālakaṃ grāhakaṃ cānyadvātaraṃṭakasaṃjñitaṃ ।
nāma * caṃkapotaṃ ca śiṣyadoṣāḥ prakīrttitāḥ ।।

na kutracit sthito yastu kimidaṃ syāmidaṃ tu kiṃ ।
svatarkapravibhāsena saṃdigdhaṃ samudāhṛtaṃ ।।
(213a)
guruvākyavicāroktau viruddhātī ca niṣṭhuraḥ ।
parapramāṇanipuna īṣoktāva vicārakaḥ ।।

necchaṃte kiṃcidapyeṣa viparyasta iheṣyate ।
mūḍhovyutpattirahitaḥ praviṣṭo nanu kutracit ।।

avyutpannaṣṭavokta * śilādehataraścayaḥ ।
pālakopātipāṃḍityamātmano paṃḍito bhavet ।।

niṣṭhāktaṃ naiva vākyārthaṃ pracchanne gurusannidhau ।
gālakasya yadā khyāta galate tasya tacca vai ।।

na sthitiṃ yāti taddehe śrutametadyathātathaṃ ।
vātareṭakanāmāya ścalaḥ sautra prakīrttitaḥ ।।

yatra yatra bhavedvyākhyā tatra tatra sadaiva hi ।
vyākhyāraṃbhodyamī nityaṃ vivāhaṃ naiva kutracit ।।

prayāti devadeveśi vānareṭakasaṃjñitaḥ ।
nāmamasya bhavennidrā guroragre sadaiva hi ।।

vyākhyārambhe matā devi tāmaso sā vudīritāḥ ।
kapota saṃjñako yo vai sa ca vakti sadā priye ।।

etasmānna māyā kiṃcicchratameva kadācana ।
na kiṃci devi jāna(nti) ahaṃ jānāmi tatvataḥ ।।

evaṃ kāpotakaṃ saṃjña śiṣya utpaya vā sthitaḥ ।
evaṃ bahuvidhā chiṣyān sadon pravicārayet ।।

vicāryākhyānamārabhyaṃ saguṇātatvavedi(tā) ।
tantranīti sadā pālyā sādhakaiḥ putrakairapi ।।

samayajñairmahābhāgairgurubhiśca viśeṣataḥ ।
pratiṣaṭkādimadhyānte viśeṣotsavamārabhet ।।

samāptau tantrarājasya (vī)ramelāpamācaret ।
(213b)
bhaktiḥ śuddhā samopetaḥ śrotavyaṃ tantramuttamaṃ ।।
śrutvopadeśa bahulaṃ guptaṃ kāryaṃ sadā budhaiḥ ।।

iti tantravarānusārato yadvyākhyākramamasti ta(tka)thitaṃ ।
vaktavyamato gurucaritaṃ śiṣyasya ca varttanaṃ tathā devi ।। ।।

iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātaṃtre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
vyākhyākramapaṭalaḥ ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

guruṇā devadeveśa kathaṃ bhāgyāṃ sadaiva hi ।
kimācārastasya deva lakṣaṇāstasya ke matāḥ ।।

śiṣyeṇa kathamāstavyaṃ guroragre maheśvara ।
anayoḥ phalamākhyā hi yathāvat parameśvara ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

gurulakṣaṇamevoktaṃ pūrvvaṣaṭke mavānavaḥ ।
śiṣyasyāpi samāsena tathāpyuddeśatodhunā ।।

kathayāmi mahābhāge kramaprāptaṃ yathā tava ।
bhairavācāranirato nistaraṃgo hyalolupaḥ ।।

śivabhaktaḥ praśāntātmā satvavān vītamatsaraḥ ।
sarvvaśāstrārthakuśalo guruparvvakramānugaḥ ।।

sarvvaśilpakalābhijñaḥ śūro dhīro hyudāradhīḥ ।
sarvvāvayavasaṃpannaḥ satyavādī dṛḍhavrataḥ ।।

arvvāgnihotranirataḥ śraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ ।
na pānaya parijñānī lobhamoha vivarjitaḥ ।।

īdṛśastu guruḥ proktaḥ taṃtrarāje jayadrathe ।
śiṣyaḥ sarvvaguṇairyuktaḥ satvavān nirvikalpakaḥ ।।

śraddhābhaktiyuto vāgmī vīrācāro dṛḍhavrataḥ ।
(214a)
sukhaduḥkha sahotyāmī siddhisādhanatatparaḥ ।।

varttitavyaṃ yathā tena gruroragre tathā śṛṇu ।
maunī tiṣṭhedvinītātmā satyavāksaṃyatendriyaḥ ।।

śuci - kṣo tathā prahva kṛtāṃjali puraḥsaraḥ ।
tacchāyāṃ vā tadājñāṃ vā prāṇāntepi na laṃghayat ।।

ṣṭhīvanaṃ cāśrupātaṃ ca kṣuṃbhanaṃ kṣunameva ca ।
asadvādaṃ tathā goṣṭhī paranindāṃ vivarjayet ।।

noccairhasettasya cāgre na kruddho na ca śokavān ।
na dīya sahitaṃ kiṃcittadagreṇa pradarśayet ।।

kūṣmāṇḍabhuvane ghore svataptaḥ paripaṭhyate ।
brāhmaṃ varṣaśataṃ sā(gra) tacchāyā laṃghanāntaraḥ ।।

tadājñā laṃghanāt tatra triguṇaṃ kālamāvasan ।
ṣṭhīvanatyajanādagre raurave kalpa mā vaset ।।

śokāśrupatanādagre avīcau paśyate ciraṃ ।
kṣatādvai jṛṃbhanāddevi kumbhe pāke ciraṃ vaset ।।

asadvādhāṃ tasya sārddhaṃ kūṭaśalmaligo bhavet ।
yāvanmanvantarāstriṃśatsiddhopi parameśvari ।।

tena sārddhaṃ tadagre vā yadā goṣṭhīṃ samācaret ।
tadā brāhmaṃ vatsaraikaṃ kūṣmāṇḍabhuvane vaset ।।

paraniṃdā(di)karaṇāt siddhopi narakaṃ vrajet ।
kruddhaḥ śokāturaḥ cāsyāt tadagre yadi mānavaḥ ।।

tadā varṣāyutaṃ sāgryaṃ brāhmaṃ kūṣmāṇḍago bhavet ।
akāraṇādavidyādvā hasanāt tadvadeva hi ।।

tadagre viśanaṃ devi vyavahāraṃ sudurghaṭaṃ ।
tasmādvinīta vattiṣṭheddevyābhāvaika (ta)tparaḥ ।।
(214b)
tadīya sahitaṃ kiṃcidravasmādya vibhāgataṃ ।
tadā nivāraṇīyaṃ tatprāṇadravyāghapekṣaṇaiḥ ।।

na vade tasya cāgre tadyāvattenaiva noditaṃ ।
gururdevaṃ gururdevīṃ gurumantraṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।।

guruḥ śāstraṃ gururmātā gurureva pitāmahaḥ ।
nādhanī jāyate deva gurureva kadācana ।।

rasāyanena ratnaiśca brahmāṇḍaṃ paripūritaṃ ।
pradīyate yadā tasya nāṛṇī jāyate tadā ।।

yadradvattā mahāmoha viṣamādbhavasāgarāt ।
tasya kenā ṛṇī bhūyo naro devi vadasva me ।।

tasmāt sadāgurorbhaktiḥ karmaṇā manasā kuru ।
vataṃdasti tadvastu yadbhaktyā na sādhayet ।।

yatnena sahasaṃparka tanmokṣoyābhikṣīyate ।
evamiṃgitatatvajñastadagre suciraṃ vaset ।।

tasya vainānviṣe jñāni na śīlaṃ caritaṃ tathā ।
na citraṃ na ca kāryāṇi tadīyāni vicārayet ।।

gadāpramupadeveśi ṣvanāśā yojyato guruḥ ।
tadā nivāraṇī yo sau praṇatena vipaścitā ।।

evamācārasargotra pūrvvaṣaṭke mayoditaḥ ।
saṃkṣepādiha vā khyātaṃ śāstragauravabhītitaḥ ।।

etatte sarvvamākhyātaṃ tantrasārataraṃ laghu ।
adhunā kiṃ pravakṣyāmi vada cakreśvarārcitau ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe jayadrathayāmale caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke guruśiṣyācāravidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।।

evamākarṇya sā devī vepamānā bhayārditā ।
vātāhatālayā yacca akaṃpata bhṛśaṃ paraṃ ।।
(215a)
devadevasya sā bhūyo vacasā gadgadena ca ।
uvācābhrasumanasā stutvā ca parameśvaraṃ ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

aho ghorataraṃ khyātaṃ hṛtkaṃpajananaṃ mama ।
tvayā kāru * kekādya kiṃ karttavyamato mayā ।।

devanaṃdoktayaścitrāḥ kṛtāstvavyatrato mayā ।
niyantritāstathā ceṣṭāḥ kṛtāstāḥ sarvvadā prabho ।।

tasmātkarttavyamevātra kiṃ mayā parameśvara ।
prāyaścittaṃ śarīrāntaṃ ācarāmyadya sāṃprataṃ ।।

yena me pātamityuccairna bhave narakārṇave ।
ājñāpayasva kiṃ kāryaṃ mayā tripurasūdana ।।

trāhi trāhi mahābāho gahane patitāsmyahaṃ ।
ityuktvā parameśānaṃ gaurī syātkulitāntarā ।।

apatanmūḍhamanasā bhūmāvuparivihvalā ।
tāvadutthāpayāmāsa bhagavān śaśibhūṣaṇaḥ ।।

sāṃtvapittā samāstvāsyā pramṛjñā nayate priyāṃ ।
uvāca devadeveśo meghagambhīrayā girā ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca  ।।

priyamākhedamātiṣṭha priyāsi samanityaśaḥ ।
nirbhayā bhava tanvaṃgi pūrvvamuktvā svayaṃ mayā ।।

yathā tavāstu niyataṃ sarvvalokaviśuddhatā ।
na deva svaracārvaṃgismṛtvāmāśu ca bhairavī ।।

yasmāṃvarorājñayā ca kṛtaṃ tat kṛtameva tat ।
prītena tena yaddattaṃ caraṃ tadvaramuttamaṃ ।।

kaṣṭena vā mahāśāpaṃ taddīkṣā bhraṃśakārakaṃ ।
sarvvasiddhiharaṃ khyātaṃ tasyāttadvatha ācaret ।।
(215b)
tadanuṣṭhānato gauri sa ca siddhiphalena ca ।
tathāpi bhītavattiṣṭhet samaryāvāpyanusmaret ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

ādiśasva mahābāho yena doṣa nivarhaṇaṃ ।
jāyate sādhakendrāṇāṃ prasādādvṛṣalāṃcchanāṃ ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu priye pravakṣyāmi sarvvadoṣanivarhaṇīṃ ।
mahāśaṃkarapāpaghnīṃ duritaughavināśanīṃ ।।

yadi hyuktāpurā devi vidyā sarvvārthasiddhidā ।
tathāpi tava vakṣyāmi vidyā ghoraparākramā ।।

yasyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa brahmāṇḍaṃ calate bhṛśaṃ ।
tāṃ śṛṇu tvaṃ mahābhāge sarvvapāpāpanodanīṃ ।।

śubhadeśaṃ samāsādya sudhūpāmodamaṃdiraṃ ।
puṣpaprakarasaṃcchannaṃ madirāmodavāsitaṃ ।।

tatra prapūjayeddevīṃ mātṛkāṃ mantramātaraṃ ।
sarvvavīropacāreṇa tato vidyāṃ samuddharet ।।

māyā sarvvagataṃ haṃsaṃ vahnibiṃdusamanvitaṃ ।
prathamaṃ bījamākhyātaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca śṛṇuṣva me ।।

dvijihvamanujaṃ tasya bhīmārgalapuṭīkṛtaṃ ।
biṃdumāyā prameyonaṃ dvitīyaṃ bīja cāsmṛtaṃ ।।

samīraṃ praṇavākrāntaṃ vahniveśmāṅkitaṃ gaṇaṃ ।
varuṇañca tadantañca piṃḍībhūtaḥ samuddharet ।।

praṇavādyaṃ tathā bāhuṃ samīraṇasamasthitaṃ ।
niśevaṃ kevalaṃ sānau kālaṃ sṛṣṭiyutaṃ punaḥ ।।

praṇavārṇeya * * * mahābhāgyāvarohaṇī ।
(216a)
mahāpātakasaṃbhāraṃ nāśaye smaraṇādapi ।।

na tatpāpamihāstīti yanna nāśayate balāt ।
asyāstulyā na paśyāmi vidyā pāpaviṣāṭanī ।।

ekataḥ sarvvamantrāṇāṃ vidyāścāsurasuṃdari ।
ekataścaiva vidyeyaṃ samānāni samā priye ।।

yaḥ smṛtā dahate pāpaṃ tūlarāśiṃ yathānalaḥ ।
seyaṃ tava samākhyātā mayā samayadoṣanut ।।

ādyabījaṃ kevalinaṃ ṣaḍdīrghapravirājitaṃ ।
biṃdunā bheditaṃ kṛtvā ṣaḍaṅgaṃ sa prakalpayet ।।

yonipūrvvaṃ samuddhṛtya bidvaṃkuśavibheditaṃ ।
mahākā * samākhyātaṃ dvāradakṣiṇamāśritaṃ ।।

ḍaṃbareṇa śivādūtī dvāravāme prapūjayet ।
mahāpretaskaṃdagatāmasurāsyāthavā smaran ।।

ṣaṭkoṇabhuvanasyāntaḥ pūjayetkuliśodari ।
madhye niveśayeddevīṃ śivāyātāṃ śivāraṇīṃ ।।

proditāṃ madhyasadvāmadhurādhīrāṃ karaṃkiṇīṃ ।
ṣoḍaśadvādaśakalākālanaikākṛtāhnikā ।।

mahākālaka(lā)koṭi vipāṭanapaṭīyasīṃ ।
sphuradyagnisaścakrakalākalanaśīlilīṃ ।।

ṣaṭcakraṣoḍaśādhāratricakragrāsaghasmarāṃ ।
aśeṣacakracakreśa vamanagrāsaniṣṭhurāṃ ।।

(sva)rūpā vaṭamadhyottha prasphuraccaṃdrikāṃ * * ।
nirargalamahādhāmaprakaṭānaṃdamadhyagāṃ ।।

nirvvāṇamārgasopānaśreṇirūpāṃ kulāraṇīṃ ।
sphuracinnalinīkhaṃḍavikāsanaśivākarāṃ ।।
(216b)
svasvarūpotthayeyūṣavarṣaṇaikaniśākarāṃ ।
sphuratsūryasudhādhāmatejopyaṃjanavāhnikāṃ ।।

dhāmatrayakṛtagrāsacitparaṃ bodharūpiṇīṃ ।
śivaśaktyoḥ sphuranmadhya vivarṇṇaspaṃdavigrahāṃ ।।

ṣaḍūrvikṛtasaṃgrāsaprollasadvāntakāraṇāṃ ।
svarūpaviśvarūpāntavicitra tribhavānukāṃ ।।

parāparaparodhārya mudrāmantreṇa vigrahāṃ ।
svarūpe dhyānaviśrāni svānaṃdānaṃdanirbharāṃ ।।

svasvarūpasudhāsāravarṣaṇābhṛtavigrahāṃ ।
sthūlākārograsa * ktyā trāsita tridaśāvaliṃ ।।

kālāli harakaṃṭābhakṛṣṇadehyaṃ bhayānakāṃ ।
kāraṇāvatimuṃḍoghavirājitasuśekharāṃ ।।

lokapālāṃtra hastādilaṃvasragdāmabhūṣitāṃ ।
pragṛhyakarayugmena narāntrasvādanodyatā ।।

jagadvālavagocakraṃ pragṛhītaṃ tayā mahat ।
brahmapātramatonyena dhārayantīṃ yadā smaret ।।

ṣaḍbhujāmekavadanāṃ pāśāṃkuśavirājitāṃ ।
sphuradghoramahānetrā dvayanirvvāntapāvakāṃ ।।

bhīma(bhū)bhaṅgavikṣepakṣubhitoccaṃḍamaṇḍalāṃ ।
akāṇḍakṣiptabrahmāṇḍapādapārṇṇinipātanīṃ ।।

kulanāganibaddhograkeyūravaramekhalāṃ ।
kṣapārkaghoralakṣaughaghaṭṭanodyatatejasāṃ ।।

yoginīgaṇavala * dantatālaśatākulāṃ ।
vīravīrendravṛkṣaughaśivārāvavirāviṇīṃ ।।

sarvvākṣayaśmaśānāgre niraṇaṃ bhīmadarśanāṃ ।
sphuracchiroruhajvālāṃ jvālinākhiladitkulāṃ ।।
(217a)
ghorahuṃkāranirbhagnabrahmāṇḍaśakarparāṃ ।
viśvakṣayanimittottha bhṛkuṭyāyepabhīṣaṇāṃ ।।

śramācchroṇī taṭodagraniryaṃghorāgnimatkaṇaṃ ।
mahāvratoparigatāmathadaityopari smaret ।।

dvārasthā ghorakalpānta pāvakāyutavigrahāṃ ।
mahākālaṃ mahākālaśataghasmaranāditaṃ ।।

khaṭvāṅga daṇḍa pāśāsi hatamālāvicitritaṃ ।
koṭarākṣaṃ bhīmamukhaṃ tuṃbuvīdharāṃ smarā smaret ।।

śivādūtīṃ ghoratarāṃ mārttaṇḍāyutavigrahā ।
śivāmukhāṃ vyātanāsyāṃ pāśāṃkuśadharodyatāṃ ।।

khaḍgamuṇḍamahāpātrāṃ khaṭvāṃgakarabhūṣaṇāṃ ।
trinetrāmugradaśanāṃ trailokyagrāsaghasmarā ।।

ghaṇṭhāḍamaruhastāṃ ca smareddaṇḍaṃ śṛṇu jvalāṃ ।
devyā nirīkṣaṇaparaṃ smṛtvānyasyātha maṇḍale ।।

evaṃ dhyātvābhimatadā paścāt pūjāṃ prakārayet ।
surāmāṃsāsavaiścitrai gaṃdhadhūpasragādibhiḥ ।।

mahāpaśuśatairdevi jalajaiḥ sthalajaistathā ।
antarikṣabhavaiścānyairarcayedvividhaṃ yathā ।।

śuṣkulyo modakāṃścaiva nopikākhaṇḍalaḍukāḥ ।
mahāthakuṭamāheśī dadhyodanaguḍodanaṃ ।।

nivedayedyathāśaktyā bhudhihyāghaṃ sadā punaḥ ।
vittaśāṭhyamakurvīta yadi ccheddīrghajīvitaṃ ।।

paścāddhomaṃ prakarttavyaṃ mahāmāṃsasurādibhiḥ ।
kṣājavikulasaṃbhūtaṃ tryakṣyaṃ vidyā pratarpaṇaṃ ।।

tataḥ praveśayecchiṣyaṃ vīrāro dṛḍhavrataṃ ।
(217b)
paramadvaitamāpannamanekaguṇamaṇḍitaṃ ।।

baddhanetraḥ prokṣitaṃ ca bhūmau kṛtvā tu jānunī ।
puṣpāṃjalidharaṃ devi tāmeva prakṣipāpayet ।।

tadghāṭite kṣaṇasyātha devadevīṃ pradarśayet ।
daṇḍavat praṇametaṃ vai bhūyo bhūyaḥ prahṛṣṭavat ।।

pūjāpayettataḥ śiṣyaṃ devīṃ maṇḍalamāsthitāṃ ।
pūrvvādārabhya koṇeṣu hṛdayādīni pūjayet ।।

vāmadakṣiṇatodvāradvārapālau prapūjayet ।
evaṃ pūjāpi deveśaṃ tato dīkṣāṃ samārabhet ।।

pūrvvoktena vidhānena yathā tantre pracoditāṃ ।
evaṃ saṃdīkṣayitvā tu śiṣyaṃ caivābhiṣecayet ।।

mantramasmai dadeddevi mudrāyuktaṃ mahābalaṃ ।
mudrāṇāṃ baṃdhamadhunā vacmi vīrendravaṃdite ।।

yayaiva * dvā plavate gaganaṃ sādhakeśvaraḥ ।
daṣṭurā ca karaṃkā ca krodhānyā bhairaveti ca ।।

lelihānā khecarī ca mudrā ṣaṭkamatodivaṃ ।
vaktradeśe parāvṛttau karau kṛtvā ca bhairavi ।।

madhyanāme samākuṃcya ubhābhyāṃ puruṣāhatāḥ ।
tarjanī tarjanī caiva kaniṣṭhā ca kaniṣṭhike ।।

graṃthaye sudṛḍhaṃ devi madhye jihvaṃ vinikṣipet ।
koṭarākṣau bhīmamukhau mudrāvīrya samāśritaḥ ।।

baṃdhayedyogakāle tu mudreśī daṃturā priye ।
etāṃ pitṛvane baddhā saptāhaṃ rāvamuccaret ।।

tadāsau plavate vyomni khecaraiḥ saha modate ।
piṇḍasiddhimavāpnoti bahunā kathitena kiṃ ।।

kuṃcayedvāmapādaṃ ca śira unnāsya vīradhīḥ ।
(218a)
akṣaṇī koṭare * ddaijihvāsaṃlālayedbhṛśaṃ ।।

tarjanyāṃgulyasaṃyuktā parivartyakarau priye ।
adhomukhāṃ kuṃcitabhrūḥ koṭare cākṣiṇī priye ।।

udaraṃ pṛṣṭhasaṃlagnaṃ darśayeta vicakṣaṇaḥ ।
phetkāramuccarenmaṃtrī ṣaḍbhirmāsaistu siddhyati ।।

iyaṃ karaṃkiṇīmudrā sarvvāścarya prasādhikā ।
vidyā yuktā yadā devi bhūtāhne pitṛveśmataḥ ।।

śavopari prabaṃdhena tadā cakreśvaro bhavet ।
bhruvoḥ saṃkocakaṃ kṛtvā kruddhādṛṣṭiṃ nipātayet ।।

agne vāmapaṭaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇaṃ dāsya paścime ।
vāmapāṇiṃ tu saṃkucya kaṭipārśve tu yojayet ।।

dakṣiṇaṃ karṇṇamūle tu praśyatāṃgulidarśayet ।
savisargaṃ prasādaṃ tu aṣṭadhā coddharedbudhaḥ ।।

krodhamudreti vikhyātā bhairavyā bhairavapriyā ।
anayāhṛtamelāpaṃ saptāhādevasiddhyati ।।

ṣaṇmāsātkevalāṃ baddhā bhairaveva bhavennaraḥ ।
sarvvasampadguṇāmodamudito nityameva hi ।।

sthānaṃ tu maṃḍalaṃ kṛtvā bāhuyugmaṃ prasādhayet ।
vyāvṛttaṃ vadanaṃ kuryāt phukkāraṃ coccaredbudhaḥ ।।

kuṃcitau tu karau kṛtvā cādhorddhasamavasthitau ।
dakṣiṇo corddhasaṃsthantu vāmabāhumadhasthitaṃ ।।

ī(śa)dvihasya mantrajño mudraiṣā bhairavī smṛtā ।
baddhamātrānnaravaraḥ svarūpānaṃdanirbharaḥ ।।

bhavatyeva mahābhāge saptāhātpūrvvavadbhavet ।
ṣaṇmāsādbhairavaḥ sākṣādbhavatyeva narottamaḥ ।।
(218b)
namitā vāmahastasya īśaśvatkṛtvā pradeśinī ।
vaktrasyābhimukhāvāde vaktradṛṣṭyāvalokayet ।।

visphāritākṣako mantrī haṭhādgātraṃ viśoṣayet ।
arddhamākuṃcayejjātu jihvāmevātra lālaye ।।

tarjanyāṃguṣṭhayoryogadakṣiṇasya karasya ca ।
mudrasaṃvalanaṃ tena dakṣiṇaśravaṇopari ।।

karttavyaṃ bhairaveśāni pūrvvavīryavalaṃbanāt ।
lelihānā smṛtā mudrā sarvvavīraprakāśitā ।।

anayā baddhamātrāyā haṭhamelāpatā bhavet ।
plavate gaganābhogaṃ bhairavākāravigrahaḥ ।।

tantrarājavare cāsmin tavādya prakaṭīkṛtā ।
atastu khecarīmudrā bhedenānyena vai śṛṇu ।।

aṅgamaṅge tu saṃyojya mukhapadmaṃ samāśrayet ।
śaktigarbhasthito yogī haṃsacchāyā samākramet ।।

khecarīti samākhyātā mudrā yogeśi pūjitā ।
guruvaktrairvinā devi bhairavānāṃ sudurlabhā ।।

vadvaitāṃ tatkṣaṇādeva vyomago jāyate naraḥ ।
evaṃ mudrā samākhyātā sadbhiryoginī pūjitā ।।

mudrāvīryasamāyuktaṃ japedyogeśvareśvarī ।
kaṃdamaṃdiramadhyasthāṃ madhyamārgavisarpiṇīṃ ।।

kalaṃkārgalaniryātāṃ tatra vidyāmanusmaret ।
yogeśvarī yogamāyāṃ lakṣamakṣarasaṃkhyayā ।।

japtvā daśāṅgato maṃtrī juhuyātryaktameva tu ।
mahākṣmā japalādyaṃ yaddhomāntātsiddhimiṣyati ।।
(219a)
yogeśvarī vahnimadhyāt saumyarūpeṇa bhairavi ।
samudeti na saṃdehaḥ sarvvasiddhiphalapradā ।।

tathaiva sārddhaṃ deveśi plavate gagaṇāṅgaṇaṃ ।
evamatyuttamāsiddhistavākhyātā yathārthataḥ ।।

lakṣatrayajapenaiva madhyamā sādhayiṣyati ।
siddhireva mahābhāge sādhayennātra saṃśayaḥ ।।

athavā lakṣajāpena sādhayennātra saṃśayaḥ ।
prākṛtānyapi karmāṇi pāṭhāmātrātprasādhayet ।।

mahāsamayadoṣāṇi smaraṇānnāśayiṣyati ।
mahāsaubhāgyadā devī satataṃ smaraṇārcanāt ।।

na tadastīha tadvastu yanna sādhayate balāt ।
bhogamokṣapradā jñeyā siddhayogeśvarī śivā ।।
athāsvacchaṃdajananī tatheyaṃ parameśvarī ।

evaṃ vidhānamatulaṃ tava devadevyāḥ,
proktaṃ mayā sakalasiddhiphalapradaṃ ca ।
kiṃ cādhunā vada sureśvari vacmi tubhyaṃ,
anyaṃtaguptataramasti hṛdī hayante ।। ।।

।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturvviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
siddhayogeśvarīvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

evamākarṇya deveśī surendravaranaṃditā ।
punaḥ provāca caṃdrārkaṃ praṇipatya yathārthavat ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

śrotumicchāmi deveśa rahasyamidamadbhutaṃ ।
yā sā svacchaṃdajananī śaktirādyā maheśvarī ।।
tāmākhyāhi ma(he)śāna prasādātparameśvara ।। ।।
(219b)
śrībhairava uvāca ।।

sādhu sādhu mahābhāge rahasyajñānabhājanī ।।
yade te pṛcchase bhadre mahārāviṇinirṇṇayaṃ ।

nākhyeyametaddeveśi tathāpīha nirūpyate ।
yasmāt tatravaraṃ sāraṃ sarvvameva prakīrttitaṃ ।।

śivasya paripūrṇasya vyāpakasyā dvayasya ca ।
prollāsātpūrvvakoṭinyā sā kalā kālakarṣaṇī ।।

svacchandabhairavaṃ devaṃ prollasadrūparūpiṇaṃ ।
tataḥ kuṃḍalirūpaṃ yadā dhārākhyaṃ visarpiṇaṃ ।।

kuṃbhīrāśana evā sā vastutadvibhavāṇḍakaṃ ।
eva māpiṃḍya mābhāti yatsvarūpaṃ suduḥsahaṃ ।।

vamanagrāsarūpeṇa vivṛtadvārameva tat ।
jayā vārāhikā khedveraśmatatra vijṛmbhate ।।

śāktayavaṃ samullāsaṃ tava devi prakāśitaṃ ।
pareṇa paradhāmnā yatparātparaphalodayaṃ ।।

mahāvinodametāṃ vā sthūlasūkṣmavyavasthayā ।
kramārthantu yadākhyātaṃ etaccaṇḍataraṃ tridhā ।।

prāptā catra [t] sphuradbindu vidūrvvapravikāśinī ।
īṣadāmīpya kalaśaṃ ghorabhairavamudrayā ।।

caturddaśamahāmārgaparipūraṇalālasaḥ ।
viśvarūpasthirasthivyā tadāstirjayate mahat ।।

punaḥ sthūlataredānīṃ khecarīvaṃ baṃdhanāt ।
mahāmelāpamabhyeti jayāvārāhyupāsanāt ।।

tasyāvādhavidhānogramākarṇaya sumadhyame ।
yadyupyuktā tantravare mudreyaṃ bahudhā priye ।।

tathātyuttamamāseyaṃ pūrvvavīryāvalaṃ balāt ।
(220a)
mahārāviti nāmeti khecarībhiśca paṭhyate ।।

yasyā baṃdhanamātreṇa sphuracchaktikadaṃbakaṃ ।
darśanādyāti sahasā praharārddhānna saṃśayaḥ ।।

niḥśeṣa devatācakre tricakre yat pratiṣṭhitaṃ ।
tamagrataḥ kiṃkarale baṃdhamātrāt pratiṣṭhitaṃ ।।

ḍāvyā ḍāmarikā sarvvā lāmocchūṣmāśca mātaraḥ ।
muhūrttāddarśanaṃ yāti rāvamudrā prabhāvataḥ ।।

strīṇāmatrādhikārā vai viśeṣāt parikīrttitaḥ ।
nistaraṃge nirvikalpe vīrabhūmau tu sādhake ।।

adhiko baddhanetra * nānyasya tanumadhyame ।
āśṛtyāddhṛtya saṃpūrya vilokyā citkalāṃ * * ।।

koṭarākṣaścorddhavaktro mṛṣṭavakṣodhvakaṃdaraḥ ।
vāmapādopagā devī mūlamānaṃdajaṃtu vā ।।

protthitasyāmupārṣṇistho dakṣiṇasvaṣṭakuṃcitaṃ ।
upaveśaprayogena vīrāvali vikāsanāt ।।

saṃkocāccāpi subhage bāhubhyāṃ kuṃcanādapi ।
madhyamānāmike dve tu karayorubhayorapi ।।

kuṃcitāṅguṣṭhayogena tarjanī toraṇākṛtau ।
kaniṣṭhe grathite cobhe parivartyaṃ prabaṃdhayet ।।

yāvadvaktrapradeśāntaṃ citukāgragate śubhe ।
anāmā madhyamā baṃdhamadhye jihve vinikṣipet ।।

ghoramuccārayedrāvaṃ mudrāmaramataṃdritaḥ ।
evaṃ sā rāviṇīmudrā khecarīti prakīrttitā ।।

anayā * * * devi tatkṣaṇāt khecaro bhavet ।
melāpabhāgvīravaro devi nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ।।
(220b)
mudrāpīṭhasthitā mudrā yāvantyaḥ surasuṃdari ।
tāvatsūnāyikā seyaṃ sadyaḥ pratyayakārikā ।।

atha devyā mantravaraṃ pravakṣyāmi tanūdari ।
dvijihvaṃ bhairavaṃ māyā krodhārgalapuṭīkṛtaṃ ।।

biṃduprameya khacitāṃ mantramekākṣarasmṛtaṃ ।
ekavīraṃ mahābhīmaṃ adbhutāścaryakārakaṃ ।।

anena smṛtamātreṇa jagaddamadanāyate ।
kampate dehamakhilaṃ sphurate kaṃdamaṃdiraṃ ।।

vapate hṛdayagraṃthiṃ calaṃtyādhāracakrakāṃ ।
devyāḥ sāṃnidhyatāṃ yānti śivā rāvāḥ samuccate ।।

bhasmasādyāti pāpaughaṃ samayollaṃghanāśca yat ।
atha kiṃ bahunoktena devi saṃkṣiptamucyate ।।

yāvattantravare vīryaṃ vidyākhyamupavarṇṇitaṃ ।
tāvatsaṃpiṇḍadeveśi sarvvamantraiva saṃsthitaṃ ।।

evaṃ vidyā ghoratarā tavādya prakaṭīkṛtā ।
bhairavaṃ dīrghasaṃbhinnaṃ krodhārgalavibheditaṃ ।।

biṃdubhinnaṃ ca vai ṣoḍhā hyaṃgaṣaṭkamudīritaṃ ।
haṃsaṃ kālāgnirudrasthaṃ biṃdunādaśirāhataṃ ।।

svacchandasya samākhyātaṃ janmamṛtyuvināśanaṃ ।
kuṃbhīrasya mahādevi praṇavaṃ nāma vai namaḥ ।।

dvijihvaṃ biṃdusaṃyuktaṃ jayāmantramudāhṛtaṃ ।
ḍaṃbaraṃ kevalaṃ devirāvāhyā mantramuttamaṃ ।।

evaṃ mantramidaṃ cāgraṃ tavaiva prakaṭīkṛtaṃ ।
dhyāyet kalpāntakālāgnikoṭikoṭyāyutaprabhāṃ ।।

kṛṣṇakākālinīhāraharakaṃṭhanibhopamāt ।
sphuradulkāsahasraughavīryaṃ na * * bhāsurāṃ ।।

pāśāṃkuśāsi khaṭvāṃga kapālakaraśobhitāṃ ।
(221a)
mahāmuṃḍaṃ mahāpadma narāntrā pūritānanāṃ ।।

siṃhasūkarasadvaktrabhāsurastanamaṃḍitāṃ ।
tadutsaṃgagataṃ devaṃ svacchandaṃ viśvanāyakaṃ ।।

paṃcavaktraṃ daśabhujaṃ sarvvāyudhadharaṃ mahat ।
pibantaṃ snāyumevāsau vāmaṃ devyā bhayānakaṃ ।।

kuṃbhīrāsuramūrdhnisthāṃ jagadgrasanaghasmarāṃ ।
dhyāyejjayāṃ caturvaktrāṃ bhujāṣṭakavibhūṣitāṃ ।।

sarvvāyudhadharāṃ devīṃ caṃdrakoṭyāyutaprabhāṃ ।
vārāhī kaulameghābhāṃ yugāntamarutopamān ।।

varāhavadanāṃ bhīmāṃ trailokyakavalodyatāṃ ।
kapālakhaṭvāṅgadharāṃ pāśāṃkuśavirājitāṃ ।।

vāmadakṣiṇayordevyo dvārasyaiva sadā smaret ।
padmamadhye yajeddevīṃ patraṣaṭkeṅgaṣaṭkakaṃ ।।

pūjyā pūrvvopacāraiśca vīradravyairaśeṣataḥ ।
mahāpiśitasaṃbhāraiḥ surāmāṃsāsavāditaiḥ ।।

saṃpūjayedyathāśaktyā vittaśāṭhya vivarjitaḥ ।
evaṃ prapūjya deveśīṃ juhuyānnaraphuphusaṃ ।।

paścāt praveśaye chiṣyaṃ vīrācāraṃ dṛḍhavrataṃ ।
kṣapāpayetpuṣpavarāṃ dīkṣayetpūrvva - śvataṃ ।।

dīkṣitaścābhiṣikaśca sādhayet paramāṃ kalāṃ ।
girikaṃdarako gatvā japellakṣāyutaṃ mahat ।।

homaṃ pūrvvokta mā tiṣṭheddaśāṃśā (ta)numadhyame ।
homāvasānato devi devyāḥ sāṃnidhyatāṃ vrajet ।।

atha vīrasya cittasyā tadā melāpasādhanaṃ ।
karttavyaṃ vīrabhūmisthairakṣatāṃgasamopameḥ ।।

alaṃkṛtaṃ tamāhṛtya tatpṛṣṭhastho japecchivāṃ ।
mudrāṃ baṃdhaprayogena praharārddhākhamutpatet ।।
(221b)
athavā pāśuyogena tarpayet parameśvarīṃ ।
vidhānamucyate sūkṣmaṃ kiṃcitsaṃkṣepatodhunā ।।

prapūjya kālasaṃhāraṃ pūrvvavaktramamāsthitaḥ ।
ucchūkṣmamallakayugamūrddhacchidraṃ prapūjya ca ।।

chagayecchidramevāsya citākāṭhyena bhairavi ।
paścānnirgatya svagṛhādakākolūkakālagaḥ ।।

yamagre varttate tasya tasyā pāṃśuṃ samāharet ।
striyāpuruṣabhedena vāmadakṣiṇabhedataḥ ।।

mṛgī baddhāsa * grāhyaṃ nirvikalpena cetasā ।
gṛhītvā mallakavapuḥ prakṣipya tanumadhyame ।।

vidhāya pūjayet paścāt kṣatāvaṃ prasano naraḥ ।
tadvadvarttitagātrastu mudrābaṃdhaṃ samācaret ।।

anyathā naiva mudreśī baṃdhayeta kadācana ।
ca tāṃ pṛṣṭhagṛhe pārśve varddhamāne śmaśānataḥ ।।

catvare parvvate nadyāṃ vīre vā baṃdhamācaret ।
aṣṭadhārāvamuccārya haṃsaṃ sṛṣṭiyutaṃ punaḥ ।।

evamābaṃdhamātreṇa tatra loṣṭhādayaśca ye ।
veṣyaviṃśati deveśi sphuṭaṃti viśarā ravaḥ ।।

jvalanti ca dasaḥ sarvvāṃ jvālā vaktro bhavet svayaṃ ।
evaṃ mudrā prabhāvena karṣayejjagasantatiṃ ।।

saptāhānnātra saṃdeho yadi sākṣād bṛhaspatiḥ ।
sūryataṃ mallakapuṭaṃ lohitenāmṛtena ca ।।

arddhaṃ nivedya deveśyā prāśya paścācca paryaṭet ।
mahāpitṛvanaṃ bhīmaṃ mudrābaṃdhasamācaret ।।

tata āyāntitāḥ sarvvāḥ ṣaḍrasāhāralaṃpaṭāḥ ।
(222a)
yoginyā divyavapuṣo bhogamokṣaphalapradāḥ ।।

tāsāmarghaṃ pradeyaṃ tadbhogaṃ vāpi carūttamaṃ ।
evaṃ melāpamākhyātaṃ pāśuyogānmayā tava ।।

melāpārthaṃ bhaveddevi paśusaptakabhakṣaṇāt ।
sarvvajño jāyate martyaḥ sarvvakarttā maheśvaraḥ ।।

tannāsti yanna kurute tannāsti yanna sādhayet ।
mudrāṃ vā kevalāṃ badhvā puroktavidhinā tataḥ ।।

plavatyevaṃ mahāvīraḥ khecarīvaktranāyakaḥ ।
strī vā karoti deveśi mudrābhyāsamanākulā ।।

tadā saptāhamātreṇa yoginī sā bhaviṣyati ।
evaṃ kramavaraṃ khyātaṃ duṣyā evamakṛtātmanāṃ ।।

na deyaṃ duṣṭabuddhīnāṃ cintāmaṇi śatopamaṃ ।
rahasyakramasarvvasvaṃ bhogamokṣacarāvahaṃ ।।
kimanyadadhunā vacmi vada vīrendravatsale ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke svacchaṃdajananyāvidhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

evamākarṇya deveśī santoṣāmṛtanirbharā ।
punaḥ provāca deveśaṃ praṇipatya yathārthavat ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

namaḥ paramadhāmāya svabodhapravikāśine ।
sarvvagrāmaikarūpāya mahālakṣmyaikamūrttaye ।।

anupāsya svarūpāya sarvvabhāvaikarūpiṇe ।
tṛptāsmi paripūrṇṇāsmi valgāmi puratastava ।।

śrutaṃ tayā yāmalendraṃ tadārabhya samastakaṃ ।
mantravīryasamopetaṃ mudrāvīryupavṛṃhitaṃ ।।

mahāsādhanasaṃyuktaṃ rahasyānekasaṃkulaṃ ।
bahuvijñānakhacitaṃ caryāmelāpamaṃḍitaṃ ।।
(222b)
adhunā śrotumicchāmi mahālakṣmīmataṃ śubhaṃ ।
utpattivratavijñānacaryāmantrasamāyutaṃ ।।

sādhakānāṃ yathākarma mudrāmaṃḍalapūjanaṃ ।
vahnikāryaṃ yathāvasthaṃ japaṃ sarvvārthasiddhidaṃ ।।

tadbrūhi deva deveśa yathā rājyādisādhanaṃ ।
evaṃ niśamya deveśaḥ priyā vaktroditaṃ vacaḥ ।।

prāhitaṃ tu varasyānte mahālakṣmī mataṃ śubhaṃ ।। ।।

bhairava uvāca ।।

atiguptataraṃ bhadre kaṣṭaṃ pṛcchasi bhairavi ।
tava prītyā vadiṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvekāgramānasā ।।

adṛśya vigrahā chāntā chivāchaktiḥ samutthitā ।
sā ca dvādaśabhedena saṃsthitā cakrabhedataḥ ।।

madhye sthitaṃ mahādevaṃ bhairavaṃ vikṛtānanaṃ ।
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ tuhinādrisamaprabhaṃ ।।

sitakuṃdenduvarṇābhaṃ śaṃkhagokṣīrapāṇḍuraṃ ।
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanaṃ mahograṃ bhṛkuṭīmukhaṃ ।।

ūrddhakeśaṃ virūpākṣaṃ nāgayajñopavītinaṃ ।
sarvvābharaṇasaṃyuktaṃ sa(rpa)goṃṇāśamaṃḍitaṃ ।।

suvāsaṃkṛttivasanaṃ gajacarmmāṃvarāvṛtaṃ ।
paṃcavaktraṃ daśabhujaṃ tripaṃcanayanairyutaṃ ।।

kapālakhaṭvāṅgadharaṃ khaḍgakheṭakadhāriṇaṃ ।
śaracāpakaravyagraṃ śūlapaṭṭiśadhāriṇaṃ ।।

varadābhayahastaṃ ca naṃdaṃtaṃ śaktimadhyataḥ ।
kiṃtu raktamayaṃ devaṃ maṃthānatvena saṃsthitaṃ ।।

kusuṃbharajasaṃkāśaṃ dāḍimīkusumārcitaṃ ।
jātyāhiṃgulakaprakhyaṃ nitambatamuttamaṃ ।।

nibaddhamekhalodāmaghargharāvalimaṃḍitaṃ ।
(223a)
maṇibhūṣaṇaśobhāḍhyaṃ śiropādādibhistathā ।।

suveśaṃ veśasaṃpannaṃ divyagaṃdhānulepanaṃ ।
mahālāvaṇyasaṃyuktaṃ kiṃcidvihasitānanaṃ ।।

icchārūpadharaṃ devaṃ pare kṣīrārṇave sthitaṃ ।
devyāḥ svarūpasaṃyuktā vāhanāsvādisaṃyutāḥ ।।

dvādaśāre mahādevī śṛṇu nāmānyathārthataḥ ।
siddhiṛddhistathā lakṣmī dīptimālā śivā tathā ।।

sumukhī cāmarī naṃdā harikeśī hayānanā ।
viśveśī ca samākhyātā yogeśvaryā mahābalā ।।

dvādaśasvarabhedena saṃsthitā cakramadhyataḥ ।
grathitā kramasaṃsthena yatte rājñānuvarttinī ।।

madhyamānaṃ mahābhāge mahālakṣmī samutthitāḥ ।
caṃdrakoṭipratīkāśāṃ pūrvvāyutasamaprabhāṃ ।।

aśvavaktradharī bhīmāṃ dvādaśairbhujabhūṣitāṃ ।
netraiḥ paṃcadaśairyuktāṃ pañcavaktrāṃ maholvaṇāṃ ।।

sarvvagrāsakarīṃ raudrāṃ mahālāvaṇyasaṃyutāṃ ।
uttare kaularūpā sā dakṣiṇe nārasiṃhikā ।।

ūrddhe kharānanā devī paścime kapicā(na)tā ।
trimuṇḍasaṃsthitāṃ devīṃ kaṭṭārakavibhūṣitāṃ ।।

kapālaśūlakhaḍgaṃ ca śaraścābhayapāśinīṃ ।
dhanurghaṃṭā tathā muṇḍaṃ ḍamaruṃ vāmake kare ।।

akṣamālāsavāliṃgaṃ dhārayeddakṣiṇe kare ।
jaṭājūṭadharāṃ devīṃ śaśāṃkakṛtaśekharāṃ ।।

sarvvābharaṇasaṃyuktāṃ halamālā vibhūṣitāṃ ।
vyālayajñopavītāñca vyāghracarmāmbarānvitaṃ ।।

mahāsaubhāgyalāvaṇyāṃ mahāduḥkhaniketanīṃ ।
(223b)
candramaṇḍalamadhyasthāṃ tripadmapariveṣṭitāṃ ।।

etadrūpadharāṃ devīṃ praṇatārtti vināśanīṃ ।
yaḥ smaretsādhakendro hi sa rājyaphalabhāgbhavet ।।

parasthānāttu saṃcitya praviśantī svakāṃtanuṃ ।
brahmaraṃdhrapravāheṇa yāvattanmūlamaṇḍalaṃ ।।

tasthaṃ saṃpūrayedyāvacchivādyavanīgocaraṃ ।
mahāmṛtapravāheṇa oghasāparameṇa ca ।।

evaṃ pratidinadhyānādamaratvaṃ prajāyate ।
lakṣmīḥ pravarddhate tasya alakṣmīrnaśyate kṣaṇāt ।।

saubhāgyamatulaṃ devi jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
tata mṛtyujarārogaṣaḍadhvāślidvyate yamī ।।

dāridradamanī ghorā saubhāgyajananī śubhā ।
asyāḥ prasādāddeveśi mahīpālāśca garvitāḥ ।।

anya tantre mayā proktā eṣā cāntaḥ pravarttinī ।
mahādūtisthitāścānyaistathā saṃkarṣaṇī parā ।।

manujāḥ pārthivendratvaṃ prasādāccāsya varttate ।
asyā mantraṃ yathāvasthaṃ paramekāgramānasaḥ ।।

bhūmibhāge same śuddhe gomayenopalepile ।
mahodyāna vane ramye * * pṛṣṭhaistu saṃsthite ।।

sumahāhlādajanake samantāccitrite śubhe ।
subhittikṛtavinyaste suśilyena susaṃskṛte ।।

dhūpāmodasugaṃdhāḍhye puṣpaprakarabhūṣite ।
madirānaṃdasaṃyuktamācāryaprāṅmukhoruhet ।।

raktavāsaṃ ca saṃyuktaṃ mukhatāṃbūlabhūṣitaṃ ।
kuṃkumena samālasya āpādatalamastakaṃ ।।

mṛganābhiyutaṃ mūrdhnā caṃdracaṃdanacarcitaṃ ।
(224a)
khaṭikāhastasaṃyuktaṃ likhetpadmaṃ suśobhanaṃ ।।

karṇṇikāṃ lekhyasaṃhāraṃ mṛtyustasyoparisthitaṃ ।
ṣa(dbhra)rṇṇaṃ kramabhedena paṭṭisthā mātṛkāṃ likhet ।।

pūjayedādyasiddhāstu palālibṛṃhitena tu ।
bhakaro adhasaṃgṛhya cakārārddhena yojayet ।।

ai ūrddhā yojayeddevi ūrūrddhā vinyaset kramāt ।
etadbījaṃ varārohe mahāsaubhāgyadāyakaṃ ।।

kālaṃ tu kevalaṃ gṛhya takārā * tataḥ punaḥ ।
* ādyā yojayeddevi krame lekhyaṃ sumadhyame ।।

* davantā likhedvarṇṇaṃ saṃhāraṃbha samālikhet ।
adhastānmṛtyuyojyeta tadadhastāttriyojanāt ।।

ī ādhastānniyojyeta ṛ ūrddhātkevalaṃ likhet ।
ga ūrddhātkevalaṃ devi ḍa dvitīyāya kevalaṃ ।।

ekārādho niyojyeta varṇṇasuravarārcite ।
nādhasthā yojayeddevi aṃ ūrddhā kevalaṃ likhet ।।

dvitīyāvadhasaṃgṛhya sadvitīyāvabhūṣitaṃ ।
gha dviyorddhasaṃyuktaṃ ṇa dvitīyorddhabhūṣitaṃ ।।

etadbījaṃ varārohe rāsvasya hṛdayaṃ paraṃ ।
ḍa dvitīyādhasaṃgṛhya kha tṛtīyādadhoyutaṃ ।।

ka tṛtīyā pade yojya te tṛtīyorddhalāṃcchitaṃ ।
taddvitīyādhasaṃgṛhya da dvitīyorddhvadhasthitaṃ ।।

ṇa dvitīyorddhasaṃbhinnaṃ bījamuktipradaṃ śubhaṃ ।
ṣakārādūrdhvasaṃlikhya ghakārādho samanvitaṃ  ।।

ukārā ūrdhvapatitaṃ pañcadhā tu samuddharet ।
saptādādaśākṣarā devī mahāduḥkhanikṛntanī ।।

surāpasmeyahārī ca brahmahā gurutalpagaḥ ।
(224b)
mucyate smaraṇāccāsya vāhyanekāyato priye ।।

aśvamedhasahasrāṇi tathā brahmavināśanaṃ ।
sakṛduccāritā devī nāśattatra saṃśayaḥ ।।

viṣayairviṣayośakto mahābaṃdhaiścaravṛtā ।
ye smaranti mahāvidyāṃ te muktāḥ sarvvabaṃdhanāt ।।

akṣarākṣarasaṃcintya praviśanti svakāntanuṃ ।
tasya janmajarā(roga) mṛtyuśca pralayaṃ gatāḥ ।।

ṣaṇmāsādamaro devi yoginī samatāṃ vrajet ।
prāpya sarvvajñā * māśu śivo vā śivayogavit ।।

vīrasaṃsyasāmānyaṃ siddhasaṃghasya suṃdari ।
sarvvayogi namasyaiva prabhāvāścākṣarākṣaraṃ ।।

dūtyāścāpi mahāsatvāḥ subhaktāśca sulakṣaṇāḥ ।
vinyasettu mahāvidyāṃ trikasthāne sureśvari ।।

māsadvayena sā devi (si)ddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ।
puruṣeṇādhikārotra teṣu tava prasiddhidā ।।

yatra nirāmayaṃ vyomaṃ tatpadācārasaṃsthitā ।
vibhuśaktiranantā sā tadvanmantreṇa saṃyutā ।।

durlakṣā sarvvadā devi medhānāṃ tena lakṣite ।
traiṣkālyaṃ smaravedyajñā tasya siddhikare sthitāḥ ।।

udayaṃ hṛtpradeśe tu madhyāhnasaṃsthitā bhuvau ।
trikālaṃ mūrddhnicakre tu yāvadastamanaṃgataḥ ।।

eṣā vidyā mahādevi sarvvadeveṣu saṃsthitā ।
mahāpāpapraśamanī apasmāravināśanī ।।

dadāti pṛthivīśvaryaṃ acirānnātra saṃśayaḥ ।
gopitā sarvvatantreṣu tava snehāt prakāśitā ।।
(225a)
yoginī garbhasaṃbhūtā rudrāṃśo malavarjitaḥ ।
śaktipātāt prabuddhasya matprasādācca bhairavi ।।

yogeśi nāvalokena prāpyavidyāmanuttamāṃ ।
tasmātsarvvaprayatnena gopanīya mṛgekṣaṇe ।।

tadā deśāttu dātavyaṃ atiyuktasya bhairavi ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke mahālakṣmīmatacakre devyutpattimaṃtroddhārapaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

athātaḥ śṛṇu tatvaṅgi pūjā cāsya varānane ।
hṛdayādīni cāṃgāni yathā dūtī ca saṃ * tā ।।

maṇḍalaṃ ca tripadmotthaṃ hotavyaṃ sarvvakāmikaṃ ।
tat pravakṣyāmi deveśi yathā jñāsyati sādhakaḥ ।।

ja tṛtīyādhasaṃgṛhya sa tṛtīyādhasaṃsthitaṃ ।
saṃhāraṃ kālasahitaṃ ga tṛtīyā ca dhānvi * ।।

gha tṛtīyādhasaṃyuktaṃ ka tṛtīyādhabhūṣitaṃ ।
ṇa tṛtīyordhvadho yojya ca tṛtīyorddhabhūṣitaṃ ।।

praṇavena samāyuktaṃ ka tṛtīyādhabhūṣitaṃ ।
eṣātmā devadevaśi sarvvabhūteṣvavasthitaṃ ।।

yantravāhasmṛto hyeṣa mantreṣu trividheṣvapi ।
eṣa bhairavarūpastu sarvvānugrahakārakaḥ ।।

sa eṣa prāṇināṃ prāṇaḥ sa eṣa paramākṣaraḥ ।
(sa) eṣa sarvvadhātā ca sa eṣa paramaḥ śivaḥ ।।

sa evāṣṭarasairdivyaiḥ siddhasaṃghaiḥ sa kinnaraiḥ ।
siddhyate satataṃ devistantrīvādyādimaṇḍale ।।

sa eva pararūpī tu parāpara(vibhā)gaśaḥ ।
(225b)
brahmaghnopi mahādevi gurudroharatopi ca ।।

siddhyate sarvvasiddhīni hṛdayasya prabhāvataḥ ।
vicaretsarvvago bhūtvā khecarībhiḥ samantataḥ ।।

sāṃnidhyaṃ satataṃ devi turyātītapadānugā ।
caturbhistu guṇairdevi nirmuktaḥ kālavarjitaḥ ।।

tadaiva paramaṃ brahma paramānandalakṣaṇaṃ ।
tasmātsarvvaprayatnena pūjayeddhṛdayaṃ sadā ।।

ha tṛtīyorddhasaṃgṛhya ya dvitīyorddhasaṃsthitaṃ ।
kha caturthaṃ rathārūḍhaṃ dva tṛtīyorddhabhūṣitaḥ ।।

ṇa dvitīyorddhasaṃbhinnaṃ śiramaikākṣaraṃ paraṃ ।
svāhā praṇavasaṃyuktaṃ mahāpāpakṣayaṃkaraṃ ।।

ekārā tṛtayaṃ gṛhya ṛ tṛtīyamadhoyutaṃ ।
ca caturthaśirobhinnaṃ ṇa tṛtīya vibhūṣitaṃ ।।

praṇavādiśikhāyeti vauṣaḍante niyojayet ।
tha tṛtīyaṃ ca saṃgṛhya ṭa tṛtīyamadhoyutaṃ ।।

ḍa tṛtīyorddhvadho yojya ṇa tṛtīyordhvabhūṣitaṃ ।
durbhedyaṃ kavacaṃ nāma chabjaṃ ādyata bhūṣitaṃ ।।

netraṃ tu saṃpravakṣyāmi yena śrīkāmadā bhavet ।
praṇavaṃ uddharetpūrvvaṃ tatsameti tatoddharet ।।

vaṣaṭkārastato yojya śriyā bhuktipradaṃ śubhaṃ ।
astraṃ tu saṃpravakṣyāmi yena muktirbhavennṛṇāṃ ।।

dakāratritayaṃ gṛhya ṭa tṛtīyamadhoyutaṃ ।
gha tṛtīyena saṃbhinnaṃ cha dvitīyena saṃyutaṃ ।।

mahāraudrapadaṃ likhya hu phaṭkārasamanvitaṃ ।
praṇavādyena saṃyuktaṃ mahācāstraṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।।

sahasrārci pratīkāśaṃ sūryāyutasamaprabhaṃ ।
ekapādastrinetraṃ ca sahasravadanaṃ śubhaṃ ।।
(226a)
sahasrabhujasannaddhāṃ ahikaṃkaṇabhūṣitāṃ ।
nāmnā brahmāstrakaṃ devi sarvvapāpakṣayaṃkaraṃ ।।

na deyaṃ duṣṭasatvānāṃ yadiche chreyamātmanaḥ ।
vaktrāṇāṃ tu pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu mantraṃ yathāsthitaṃ ।।

saṃhāraṃ kālamārūḍhaṃ tatṛyamadhoyutaṃ ।
punaḥ ṣaṣṭhāṣṭadeveśi o dvitīya vibhūṣitaṃ ।।

taccaturthaśiropetaṃ pūrvvavaktramudāhṛtaṃ ।
kha caturthaṃ tu saṃgṛhya saṃhāreṇa madhoyutaṃ ।।

adhastānmṛtyusaṃyuktaṃ ra caturthamadhoyutaṃ ।
ṛ ūrddhā tu samārūḍhaṃ biṃdumastakabhūṣitaṃ ।।

etaddakṣiṇavaktraṃ tu mahāsiṃhaṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।
ca dvitīyaṃ tu saṃgṛhya ṭha tṛtīyamadhoyutaṃ ।।

i tṛtīyorddhasaṃyuktaṃ ṇa dvitīya bhūṣitaṃ ।
uttaraṃ ca mahāvaktraṃ kaularūpaṃ vinirdiśet ।।

bhakārādau likhetpūrvvaṃ da ādyāścāṃdhayojitaṃ ।
mṛtyumūrttisadā rakṣya * * * paścicānanaṃ ।।

ā ūrddhā uddhareddevi ṛ dadhādadhayojitaṃ ।
ḍa caturthayutaṃ kuryātsaṃhārodho śire yutaṃ ।।

mahāmokṣapradaṃ vaktraṃ paṃcamaṃ rāsabhānanaṃ ।
kiṃ tasya bahubhirmantraistaṃtrai vā dhyānadhāraṇaiḥ ।।

yaḥ smarenmanasā devi mahāpāpakṣayo bhavet ।
bahubhede mayā devi kathitā kīlitā priye ।।

kāvidagdhāstathā chinnā hutāmaṃtā ca suptavat ।
avalā bālarūpī ca vṛddhā ca taruṇā priye ।।

samastā ca tathā vyastā grathitā ca sureśvari ।
asyā vidyā na caivāsti anyeṣāṃ vidyate sadā ।।
(226b)
yadi cchedrājyasaṃpattistathārādhyāḥ prasannadhīḥ ।
pūjayecca yathāvasthā śṛṇu caivāgramānasaḥ ।।

pūrvvoktaṃ tu yathā karma karttavyaṃ deśikena tu ।
tato lekhya mahāpadmaṃ aṣṭapatraṃ suśobhanaṃ ।।

tadvāhya ṣoḍaśaṃ likhya punardvātriṃśasuṃdari ।
pramathe pauṇḍarīkābhā dvitīye aruṇaprabhā ।।

haritālasamaprakhyā tṛtīye suranāyikā ।
tadbāhye vyomarekhā tu nīlāṃjanasamaprabhā ।।

keśarāśca caturviṃśatpadmamadhye tu kārayet ।
astragrāmaṃ likhedbāhye vajrādi parameśvari ।।

aṣṭau ca kalaśasthāpya sarvvauṣadhisamanvitaṃ ।
śvetacaṃdanasaṃjuṣṭāṃ tathā vastrairalaṃkṛtāṃ ।।

yathā prakṣipya kalaśe tripatraṃ yena jāyate ।
śrotāṃjanaṃ paṃcaratnaṃ pucchakaṃ gairikaṃ tathā ।।

hiṃgulīkṛtabiṃdu ca kaṃjuṣṭaṃ ca tathāparaṃ ।
gorocanaṃ ca sauvīraṃ tagaraṃ nāgakaṃ śubhaṃ ।।

agaruṃ sahakarpūraṃ māṃsikā pūrikā tathā ।
lavaṅgañcandanaṃ śvetaṃ kuṃkumena vimiśritaṃ ।।

jātīphalaṃ sadā śreṣṭhaṃ kalaśe ca surādhipa ।
prakṣipya daiśikendreṇa sādhakāya hitāya vai ।।

padmānusārairdīpāśca ājyapūrṇṇāṃ sulocane ।
tathā karttavya hṛdyāṅgaṃ mānasaṃ ca sureśvari ।।

karanyāsaṃ tataḥ paścātkarttavyaṃ guruṇā priye ।
mahālakṣmatatoṃguṣṭhe tarjanī hṛdaye smaran ।।

madhyamā śirasi proktā anāmā tu śikhā bhavet ।
kaniṣṭhikā tu kavacaṃ nyastavyaṃ tu varānane ।।

parvvamūle tathā netraṃ diśāsvastraṃ vinikṣipet ।
(227a)
dehavaktrastathāṅgāni tathā dhyānaṃ mahādhipe ।।

sarvvagrāsī tathā madhye nyastavyā daiśikena tu ।
aṣṭakaṃ ca tatonyasya aruṇādyā suśobhane ।।

aruṇā ghoṣavatyā ca caṃḍī tu sumukhī tathā ।
viṣvaksenā tathā lakṣmī ānandā bhadramaṃgalā ।।

etairvyāptamidaṃ sarvvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaraṃ ।
dvitīye śṛṇu tanvaṅgi sunaṃdādiprakalpayet ।।

sunaṃdā viśvasaṃhārī subhagā kṣaudrabhojanī ।
mahāraṇyā mahānādī mahānityā suśobhanā ।।

mahādhaneśvarī devī khaḍgajihvā mahābalā ।
maṃtreśvarī cānu * khī gokarṇṇī vijayā tathā ।।

jayantī ca tathā kālī mahotkṛṣṭā tu ṣoḍaśī ।
jātabhuktipradā devi madhye cakre tu saṃsthitā ।।

bhadrakarṇṇā vegavatī vikaṭā kaṭapūtanā ।
tataḥ kālavaśā vidyā cipiṭā saptamī smṛtā ।।

kapotī vinatā bhadrā vidrāvaṇi mahojvalā ।
karṇṇāmoṭī ca ḍamarī jāninī ca mahābalā ।।

kekarā rudrakālī ca viḍālavadanāparā ।
cillā ca duṣṭa chāyā ca vārāhī vikarālikā ।।

mohanī saiñjikā ghorā kumārī vāyasā tathā ।
vikaṭā vānarī śreṣṭhā hariṇī ca tathā * * ।।

mudbaṃdhinī sukhakarī sādhakānāṃ varapradā ।
raktākṣī ghorarūpā ca khacārī rājyadāparā ।।

vinyaseddevadeveśī dvātriṃśaṅguhyamātarāṃ ।
khapadme saṃsthitā nityaṃ sādhakānāṃ varapradā ।।
(227b)
apūjyā hānimicchanti daurbhāgyaṃ janayanti ca ।
tasmāddevi sadā pūjyā bhuktimuktijigīṣuṇāṃ ।।

susnigdhe snehasubhraiśca sutārairativibhramaiḥ ।
vīkṣyanti yogi(nī) tasya pūjākāle surādhipe ।।

yadi jñātvā tu tāṃ devīṃ kenacicchubhakarmaṇā ।
pūrvvajanmakṛtaiḥ puṇyairyoginyodaranirgamā ।।

kṣaṇātmādavalenaiva jhāṃkāradhvanijāyate ।
paraspa(ra)samopetā yojanaṃ cātikalpate ।।

teṣāṃ dhyānaṃ yathāvasthaṃ śṛṇu mekāgramānasaḥ ।
sarvvāścotphullanayanā sarvvā prahasitānanā ।।

dvibāhumekavadanā śvetavastrairalaṃkṛtā ।
ma(hā)lāvaṇyasaṃyuktā mauktikā bharaṇānvitā ।।

tāṃbūlamukhasaṃcchannā caṃdrāmṛtaghaṭānvitā ।
pratyekasmindale saṃsthā antaḥpātyā tu varttinī ।।

teṣāṃ mantraṃ yathāvasthaṃ śṛṇu ekāgrasuṃdari ।
takārātritayaṃ gṛhya ṛ tṛtīya adhasthitaṃ ।।

gha tṛtīyorddhasaṃbhinnaṃ ṇa tṛtīyorddhabhūṣitaṃ ।
etadbījaṃ varārohe yoginī hṛdayaṃ paraṃ ।।

ḍa caturthantu saṃlekhya kha caturtha adhoyutaṃ ।
ṭha caturthaśiropetaṃ caturthena vibhūṣitaṃ ।।

etadbījaṃ varārohe sarvvaṃ yoginī * ti ca ।
svanāmalaṃkṛte devi svāhākārāntasaṃsthitaṃ ।।

etatsādhāraṇaṃ devi khaḍgasiddhipradāyikā ।
acirātpārthivendratvaṃ sādhasya dadantitāḥ ।।

suśobhāḍhyaṃ svahametu kārayeta mahādhipe ।
suśilpacitritaṃ devi sudhūpaiśca suvāsitaṃ ।।

yādṛgyāgaṃ likhedbhūmyāṃ vitānaṃ tādṛśaṃ bhavet ।
(228a)
kuryācchabdaninādaiśca vaṃdī maṃgalasaṃyutaḥ ।।

āvāhayetparāṃ mūrttiṃ kālasaṃkarṣaṇī priye ।
mahāmūrttisamāsthāya daiśikena samasthitaṃ ।।

mahāśaṃkhārghapātrantu mahāāsavapūritaṃ ।
mahāmudrāparāṃ baddhā nāmnā kālāntakīṃ śubhāṃ ।।

saṃmukhau tu karau kṛtvā viparītakrameṇa tu ।
tarjanīnāmikāṃ kṣiptvā tārakau bhramayetsakṛt ।।

lalanā cālayecchīghraṃ pheṭkārāstrī samuccaret ।
aṣṭadhā grasate samyakpadmamudreti kīrttitā ।।

apare śūlakhaṭvāṃgavajradaṇḍa ca mudgaraṃ ।
kapālaṃ padmasahitaṃ mudrāgaṇamiho(cya)te ।।

pūjākāle tu draṣṭavyā anyathā doṣabhāgbhavet ।
prathamaṃ gaṇanāthaṃ tu pūjayecca vidhānataḥ ।।

paṃcavaktraṃ śūladharaṃ trinetraṃ mūṣakasthitaṃ ।
ehyehi gaṇanāthantu pūjā māṃ prayacchata ।।

anena mantre deveśi vighnā tasya na bādhyate ।
kṣetrapālaṃ prapūjyeta mahārādhyaṃ surādhipe ।।

gurave pūjanaṃ kāryaṃ yāvattṛptaṃ mṛgekṣaṇe ।
indrādyāḥ pūjayeddevi (dha)rmādyāstu viśeṣataḥ ।।

kālāgnyādiprapūjyeta yāvanmuṇḍaṃ surādhipe ।
trivarṇāccārabhedena āsadaṃ pūjayetsadā ।।

tatopari mahākṣmīṃ pūjayeta vidhānataḥ ।
hṛdayādīni cāṃgāni niyojyā karṇṇikāntagā ।।

bāhye tu devatā pūjyā krameṇa suranāyike ।
yathā pūjā tathā nyāsaṃ karttavyaṃ deśikena tu ।।

sanmiśraṃ tu tathā dhūpaṃ dadyādghaṃṭā tu vādayet ।
(nai)vedyā vividhākārān sthāpayeddevatāgrataḥ ।।
(228b)
gaṃdhadhūpālināpuṣpai raktaiścātha sugaṃdhibhiḥ ।
matsyamāṃsādimāṃsebhya agramāpūrayecchubhaiḥ ।।

naivedyaṃ trividhaṃ dadyātsakhaṃḍaṃ śarkkarānvitaṃ ।
dadhikṣīraghṛtāktāśca pāyasaiḥ kṛsarādayaḥ ।।

miśrāṇyatyuttamādeyā laḍukā vaṭakāstathā ।
pūrikā ghārikopetā naivedyādbhaktisaṃyutā ।।

tato vāmāmṛtaṃ devi drākṣārasavimiśritaṃ ।
āsavaṃ sīdhumadhvāsu tarpayedavikalpataḥ ।।

pānaṃ kavirasaṃ devyā mahāsaubhāgyadāyakaṃ ।
dāpayeccarukaṃ divyaṃ mahālakṣmivanotthitaṃ ।।

tataḥ kṣamāpya deveśīṃ aṣṭāṅgena sureśvarīṃ ।
mahāśaṃkhākṣasūtreṇa devyagre japamārabhet ।।

nābhyadhastāttu saṃcintyaṃ jvalantaṃ bhairavānanaṃ ।
sahasrārcipratīkāśaṃ dvādaśārkkasamaprabhaṃ ।।

paṃcavaktradharaḥ śrīmān dakṣiṇāvarttasaṃyutaṃ ।
madhyavaktrā tu saṃcintya mahājvālā subhīṣaṇā ।।

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśā sūryāyutasamaprabhā ।
nirgacchantīntatodhyāyetproktā cākṣaramālikā ।।

kramaśo nīyate urddhvaṃ yāvanmuṣṭitrayaṃ śubhaṃ ।
prāṇasthaṃ tu japaṃ kuryātkiṃcitsāṃśuṃ sulocane ।।

etajjapaṃ mahādevi bhuktimuktiphalapradaṃ ।
śeṣā vā cākṣarā proktā na siddhirna ca sādhanā ।।

tasmātsarvvaprayatnena gopanīyaṃ mṛgekṣaṇe ।
kathanāddevadeveśī mahāhāniḥ pravarttate ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke mahālakṣmyāyāgāpaṭalaḥ ।। ।।
(229a)
ataḥ paraṃ mahāraudraṃ rahasyaṃ mātṛkālayaṃ ।
śṛṇu ekāgra deveśi pūjāṃte caiva yatsmṛtaṃ ।।

vartulaṃ hastamātraṃ tu padmapatrāṣṭakānvitaṃ ।
vivikte cintite sthāne kārayetkuṃḍamuttamaṃ ।।

uttarābhimukho bhūtvā sarvvabhūṣaṇabhūṣitaḥ ।
nityasetu mahālakṣmīṃ svāhā praṇavasaṃyutāṃ ।।

devatā pūrvvavaddhyātvā ekaikaṃ kusumaṃ kṣipet ।
kramaśo darśayenmudrāṃ svāgataṃ cātibhāṣayet ।।

yathā maṇḍalapūjā ca tathā hyagnau prakalpayet ।
yonisthānaṃ hṛdaye pūjayeta prasannadhīḥ ।।

jihvāsthāne śikhābhāvyā ekaikaṃ kusumaṃ kṣipet ।
varmaṇāṃ rakṣayedagnidiśāsvastraṃ vinikṣipet ।।

madhye tu vinyasennetraṃ śiro mūrdhni niyojayet ।
ākāśamātarāyāstu pūrvvoktāḥ ṣoḍaśa priye ।।

bhramantyastāstato dhyāyetsūryāvarttagaṇāmbike ।
aparā vyomarūpāśca dvātriṃśadguhyamātaraḥ ।।

caṃdrāvarttāstathā dhyeyādbhuktimuktijigīṣuṇā ।
tagatastāmānayeta mahālakṣmī vanaṃ paśuṃ ।।

caṃdanena samāliptaṃ kāśmīrakavilepitaṃ ।
puṣpasragadāmasaṃyuktaṃ mahālakṣmyābhimantritaṃ ।।

uccare trīṇi phetkārā mahālakṣmīmanusmaran ।
karttarī gṛhya nistaṃśāṃ hṛdayenābhimaṃtritāṃ ।।

ūrddhavaktraṃ śavaṃ sthāpya japalakṣmīmahāmatiḥ ।
yadā prasyaṃdate mūrttiṃ mahārāvāṃśca muṃcati ।।

tadā gṛhītvā muṇḍantatsādhakasthāsane ruhet ।
tatrāsanopaviṣṭastu punarmātramanu - - - ।।
(229b)
khaṇḍaśastamano bhūtvā kārayedgugulānvitaḥ ।
tryaktāśca gulikāḥ sarvvā vīracittastu sādhakaḥ ।।

asthiśeṣajuhenmantrī baliṃ ca suranāyakau ।
(grā)hayeccāsthimajjānnaṃ - - kaṃ taṃḍulānvitaṃ ।।

sādhaye tu manodivyaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradaṃ ।
pānujaṃ proccaret paścānmahārāveṇa bhairavi ।।

rāvasyānte tu ye cihnāḥ śṛṇu vakṣyāmi suvrate ।
mahāvarṣamahā - - mahāpakṣisamāgamaṃ ।।

ulkāpāto diśādāho bhūmikaṃ paśvadāruṇaḥ ।
mantravidbhirna bhettavyaṃ madhye tacchukrajālavat ।।

mahāmṛtena saṃpūrṇṇā dadyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ śubhaṃ ।
tata āyānti deveśyā rātryā vaiśvarināyikā ।।

kāściccotphullanayanāḥ kāścit prahasitānanāḥ ।
kāścidvarāhavadanāḥ kāścitsvānamukhāḥ śubhāḥ ।।

pātālādāgatāḥ kāścinma - rāvaṇyasaṃyutāḥ ।
ṛkṣakarṇṇīnāthakāścitkāścitsiṃhānanāḥ śubhāḥ ।।

rudantī dṛśyate kācitkācit khaṭvāṃgadhāriṇī ।
nagnavāsāsthitā kācinmadanānalapīḍitāḥ ।।

kāścicchūlograhastāśca trinetrā vṛṣabhāṃkitāḥ ।
maṃdārapuṣpahastākhyā svargamārgagatāḥ parāḥ ।।

ghaṃṭāvādanaśīlānyā rūpalāvaṇyagarvitāḥ ।
kāścicchaktyudyatakarāḥ (ka)ścinmuṇḍaśca saṃsthitāḥ ।।

anekaśatasāhasrādvyāgacchanti parecchayā ।
mahārāvaṃ sumuṃcantyo trāsayaṃtyo diśodaśa ।।

na bhetavyaṃ varārohe * manyopamanugrahaṃ ।
(230a)
bhrūlagnajānuyugalastāsāmarghaṃ pradāpayet ।।

carukaṃ juhuyāddevi bhāgaṃ tāsāṃ pradāpayet ।
dadanti pṛthivīśva(ryaṃ pi)ṇḍasiddhiratontataḥ ।।

aṇimādiguṇāvāptirjāyate smaraṇādapi ।
asyāḥ saṃsmaraṇā devi śrībhavetsaptapauruṣī ।।

lakṣajāpānmahādevi homāduttamatastathā ।
padmaśrīphalahomādvā mahālakṣmīḥ pravarddhate ।।

viṣṇorvvakṣasthale yā sā brahmaṇaścāpi bhairavi ।
śakrasya ca mahābhāge lakṣmī nityaṃ vyavasthitā ।।

tathā vidhā bhavatyeṣā sādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ ।
dīkṣābhiṣekau karttavyau pūrvvoktamapi niścitau ।।

viśeṣadīkṣākarttavyā samayesminsadā priye ।
yadyapyasminmahātantre jātibhedo na vidyate ।।

tathāpi dīkṣā prāraṃbhe bhavatyeva sulocane ।
dīkṣitānāṃ na jātiḥ syādekājātistu caiśvarī ।।

tasmādviśeṣadīkṣāyāṃ vakṣyamāṇāṃ samācaret ।
brāhmaṇāścālipātena kṣatriyaśca śivāṃbunā ।।

vaiśyāścaṃdanapānena śudrā vai viśvabhasmanā ।
striyo vīrāṃgasaṃsparśā dīkṣayeta sumadhyame ।।

ataścaivābhiṣekaṃ tu śṛṇu vīrendravaṃdite ।
kumāryaṣṭau samānīya vastrālaṃkāraśobhitāḥ ।।

caṃdanāguruli(ptāṃ)gāḥ puṣpasragdāmaśobhitāḥ ।
viliptā mānitāścaiva tarpitāḥ susamāhitāḥ ।।

tāsāṃ parigataṃ nyāsaṃ hṛdayādiṣu kīrttitaṃ ।
svakarairveṇibaddhaistu kumārīṇāṃ parasparaṃ ।।
(230b)
pūjānte pūjayetkuṃbhāṃ navasaṃkhyāhṛtā priye ।
nyasya maṃtrāhṛtāḥ samyageṣacaite yathāvidhi ।।

aruṇādigaṇaṃ tatra kumārīṇāṃ prakalpayet ।
paścimādidiśaścai(va) yāvannaiṛtagocaraṃ ।।

tannāmānyatra saṃcintya madhyato yāgamadhyamaṃ ।
mahālakṣmīstato mūrddhni cintayedavanītalaṃ ।।

tāsu mantrāmvarārohe devīnāṃ yojayedguruḥ ।
lakṣmyā (na) pūjayedbhaktyā kalasaṃ hastaye dadet ।।

navamaṃ śiṣyahaste tu pūrvvavat prerayet kramāt ।
kṣepena vartulīkṛtya vidyate yena bṛṃhitaḥ ।।

navaite saṃdhayaḥ prājña ratena vidhinā sati ।
yāvantakampamāyāti kramaśaḥ saṃbhavanti ca ।।

svahastasthitamevantu navatiḥ sādhako bhavet ।
śāpānugrahe sāmarthyaṃ bhogamokṣapradaṃ bhavet ।।

guruṇā samanujñātaḥ siddhiḥ śiṣye prasiddhyati ।
mahāla(kṣmyā)bhiṣekaṃ tu paraṃsaubhāgyadāyakaṃ ।।

gopayedduṣṭasatvānāṃ nāstikānāṃ ca bhairavi ।
jijñāsūnāṃ ca dhūrttānāṃ dambhamāyopajīvināṃ ।।

anyatantraratānā(ñca) kṛte sarvvatra gopitaṃ ।
sasphuraṃ lakṣmivijñānaṃ tasmātteṣu gopitaṃ ।।

gūhitavyaṃ guhasyāpi kimutānyatra bhairavi ।
abhiṣiktamanujñāto vīro lakṣmyārthapāragaḥ ।।

pṛthivīśvaryamanvicchetsiddhiḥ kṣetrānyataḥ śṛṇu ।
yeṣu saṃsiddhyate maṃtrī yena rājyaṃ labhaveddhruvaṃ ।।

uttamottamamadhye ca tathā caivottamaṃ priye ।
adhamottamacādha(rmaṃ) yeṣu daśeṣu siddhyati ।।
(231a)
paṃcamantraprakāraṃ tu paṃcadhā siddhayonaghe ।
śṛṇvedānīṃ maheśānīṃ sarvvavāgjālavarjitaṃ ।।

vidhānavakṣyate samyagrājyaniṣpattikāraṇaṃ ।
mahendre malaye sahye (śrī)śaile viṃdhyaparvvate ।।

mahodadhitaṭāṃtasthe tugāre himavadgirau ।
etaccāṣṭau mahādevi sthānā rājyaprasādhane ।।

uttamottamasaṃjñāsya śriyāyāya nanāmi ca ।
teṣu sthāne japaṃ kuryātsiddhyate hyaviśaṃkitaḥ ।।

pratyakṣaṃ paśyate lakṣmī abhiṣekaṃ dhruvaṃ labhet ।।
tattatpaśyāmi deveśi tatsarvvaṃ pṛthivī naraṃ ।।

mahāsaubhāgyasaṃpanno mahābalaparākramaḥ ।
atha kiṃ bahunoktena dvitīyaṃ tu śatakratuṃ ।।

uttamākhya ca māśritya śṛṇu cottamarājyadā ।।
sthānānyeva sureśāni śṛṇuṣvānyaṃ vadāmyahaṃ ।।

kaliṅgaṃ kāmarūpaṃ ca kuṃkaṇaṃ vīravaṃdite ।
kacchadeśe pūrṇagirau magahā kāliṃjare tathā ।।

devikoṭṭe mahādevi siddhyate vottamāstathā ।
japedakṣaralakṣaṃ tu teṣu sthāneṣu suṃdari ।।

daśāṅgena hunenmantrī mahāphalguyutena ca ।
siddhyate nātra saṃdeho acirānmanujeśvaraṃ ।।

na tasya vighnakarttāro bhavati pṛthivī balāt ।
maṃḍalīkanarendrāya sadā dvāreṣu tiṣṭhati ।।

ante khagati deveśi kṣayaṃ tasya na vidyate ।
uttamoyaṃ mahādevi khecarī bhuktidāyakaṃ ।।

prayāgaṃ varuṇaṃ caiva vārāṇasyāṃ viśeṣataḥ ।
arvudaṃ caiva nepālaṃ ujjayanyāṃ mahādhipe ।।

(a)ṭṭahāse tu deveśi siddhyate siddhimadhyamā ।
(231b)
dviguṇaṃ tu jaṣaṃ kuryānmudrālaṃkārabhūṣitaḥ ।।

nṛmuṃḍasāsane sakto rātrau piśitabhojanaḥ ।
ṣaṇmāsātsiddhyate vīro maṃtrajo labhate - vaṃ ।।

tato jayajayāśabdaṃ śrūyate sādhako na bhāk ।
ante padaṃ labheddevi śāṅkarantu anāmayaṃ ।।

jālaṃdhare kulūtāyāṃ urūśā cottare sthite ।
pīṭhe sarvvāśrite caiva adhikārājñadāyikā ।।

triguṇaṃ tu japaṃ kuryānmahāvratasamanvitaṃ ।
piśite homakarmantu guggulena vimiśritāṃ ।।

samvatsare narendratvaṃ ante śivamanāmayaṃ ।
kāsmīre kanyakubje tu rā(ja)puryāṃ mahātape ।।

jayantyāṃ caiva deveśi tathā laṃpākadeśataḥ ।
araṇye ca viśeṣeṇa daśabhirvāravaṃdite ।।

siddhyate caiva saṃrājyaṃ mahāsiddhipradāyakaṃ ।
gocarīgaṇasaṃ(se)vyaṃ viṣṇunā samadhiṣṭhitaṃ ।।

caturguṇaṃ japaṃ kuryācchrībhūṣaṇavibhūṣitaḥ ।
halamālādharo devi brahmacaryarataḥ sadā ।।

śmaśānamaṭate nityaṃ mahābhūtivilepitaḥ ।
śūla(pro)taṃ yatra paśuṃ pūjayedavisaṃkitaḥ ।।

unmattaveśadhārī ca he he nirmālya pūjanaṃ ।
mahālakṣmī vane ghore caturddaṃṣṭra bhayānake ।।

japettatra mahālakṣmīmicchayā sādhakeśvaraḥ ।
daśāṃśena juhenmantrī māṃsaṃ guggulusaṃyutaṃ ।।

prāpyate piṇḍasaṃsiddhiṃ mantrī mokṣapadaṃ dhruvaṃ ।
varjyantu nagaradvāre mahālakṣmyā * jaṃ śubhaṃ ।।

yasmādāyānti deveśi tatra vighnāśca sarvvadā ।
(232a)
puṃ kṣubhyānyakhilāstatra śaktisaṃghā varānane ।।

sadyaḥ prakāśitāḥ sarvvā madanoddāntacetasā ।
divyādivyeryathāvasthā gṛhaśilpakramasthitaḥ ।।

nirīkṣya sādhakendrasya valavālitalocanā ।
kaṭākṣākṣepavikṣepataratāyatalocanāḥ ।।

tāśca vibhrāntamanaso madavibhrāntamaṃtharāḥ ।
varajaghanakallolalālitoruyugacchalāḥ ।।

tyaktā lajjāsmasītkāramadanānalapīḍitāḥ ।
snehālāpaiḥ sumadhurāḥ kāṃkṣantyaḥ sādhakasya tu ।।

tasyopari suraktāsyātsnehātkaulaṃ dadantilāḥ ।
gopitaṃ sarvvaśāstreṣu madṛtai pauruṣe tatau ।।

tasmānna dāpayedvidyā mahālakṣmīti sādhane ।
kathanāddevadeveśi mahāvyādhiḥ prajāyate ।। ।।

।। iti jayadrathayāmale caturviṃśati(sā)hasre caturthaṣaṭke mahālakṣmyādisiddhipaṭalaḥ ।। ।।

atha devi pravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvvaśaḥ ।
lakṣmī vijñānamatulaṃ yat sureṣvapi durlabhaṃ ।।

anekākārabhede(na) sadyaḥ pratyayakārakaṃ ।
dvidhābhedena tanvaṅgi vijñānaṃ tadvicāritaṃ ।।

vijñānayogasaṃsiddhi viśrāmasahitaṃ priye ।
svabhāvastha tathā cātmamamovṛtyāniyāmihaṃ ।।

vijñā(naṃ) śaktijaṃ devi tasya bhedā niyāmite ।
sā sthitā bahubhedena ekānekatva tāṃ gatā ।।

kaivalyā vaindavīnāṃ tvāmātvī caiva kalātmikā ।
sautikī cinmayī bhāvī jyotiḥ yā ca apātmikī ।।
(232b)
nānābhedagatā śaktiviśvādhārā parāparā ।
cillakṣmī lakṣmicakrasthā raśmilakṣmīparā hi sā ।।

śabdalakṣmī sparśalakṣmī rūpalakṣmī rasātmikā ।
gaṃdhala(kṣmī) manolakṣmī brahmahaṃkāralakṣmikā ।।

tattadbhāvagataṃ vastu tallakṣmī grasatodbhaṭā ।
taccidbhairavaraśmisthā prollasatkiraṇātmikā ।।

kaivalyā kevalā śuddhā etau taddharmadharmiṇī ।
baindavī biṃdurūpā ca kalāsaṃyogadāyikā ।।

tāṃtvī adhvagatā devī dārakī ṣaḍvidhasya tu ।
adhvānaṃ tu varārohe matvāmatvena saṃsthitā ।।

tātvī tasmātsa vijñeyā yadā (sā) tatvarūpiṇī ।
ādhārādheyabhāvena grāhyagrāhakabhedataḥ ।।

grāhyaśaktisvarūpāsyādgrāhakaḥ paramaḥ śivaḥ ।
grāhyagrāhakavaccetā cinmātraṃ cetanāyutaṃ ।।

sa eva lakṣmīmamalāṃvṛhya sarvvaṃ vyavasthitāṃ ।
māṃ trīpaṃcāśabhedena varṇṇarūpā maheśvari ।।

varṇṇādvālaginī tasmāttadā sāṃ trīviduḥ priye ।
kalāvalaya caitanyaṃ yadā sarvveṣu va * * ।
* * * * mahābhāge śṛṇu vakṣyāmi te yathā ।।

rajorāśīntamo mohātsatyaṃ buddhyātmavāsanāt ।।

samatvāmucyate jaṃtu jīvanmukto maheśvari ।
ghoraghoratarā ghorā * * * * * * * * ।।

kalānāṃ tu samākhyātaṃ svaguṇāśrayatāṃ śatā ।
bhautikībhūtabhāvatvācchāntā sā bhedasaṃyutā ।।

bhūtānāṃ sama * * * * * * * * * * * ।
* * * * * * * * * bodhatya na saṃśayaḥ  ।।
(233a)
cinmātrāccaiva boddhavyo bhāvī ca kathayāmyahaṃ ।
bhāvanā sā tanma * * sarvvabhāva * * tu sā ।।

* * * * * * * * rāya sarvvamidaṃ tataḥ ।
svabhāvasthaṃ nirābhāsaṃ vyāpyaviśvaṃ vyavasthitaṃ ।।

cobho bhāratā sūkṣmā sthūlā cākārarūpiṇī ।
* * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * ।।

sānantāṃ sarvvago jñeyā tatvadravyagu * * sā ।
jyotiḥ syācchṛṇutanvaṅgivakṣyamāṇaṃ samāsataḥ ।।

sphuranti karaṇākīrṇṇā mantrakoṭyairanāvṛtā ।
jyotiḥ parā * * * * prakāśayati sarvvataḥ ।।

indriyāṇīndriyārthebhyaḥ tathā jyotiṣmatī viduḥ ।
amābhāvaṃ samāsthāya caṃdramaṇḍalamadhyagā ।।

āpyāyanakarī ādyā jagatsthāvarajaṅgamaṃ ।
pareṇaivāmṛtaughena pūraye * likhaṃ jagat ।।

tato ādhyātmikā jñeyā saijñānairmittikīdṛśī ।
paryāyabhedamākhyātaṃ sarvvavyāpi * * * * ।

* * vahnistile tailaṃ paya(sā)vaghṛtaṃ yathā ।।
tadvat sthitā mahālakṣmī tatvasaṃdhānagocare ।।

ātmalakṣmī paraṃ tatvaṃ na muṃcati parasparaṃ ।
bhinnābhinnatvabhāteṣu jñeya mūlamidaṃ smṛtaṃ ।।

bodhavyaṃ layabhe(de)na svarūpaṃ pratyayācitaṃ ।।
puṣpāṃjalirdantakāsaṃ ghaṭāveśaṃ karoti ca ।

parakāyapraveśaṃ ca vijñānaharaṇaṃ tathā ।
rūpaṃ stambhaṃ tathākṛṣṭiṃ palitān visarpaṇaṃ ।।

pratimā(cā)lanaṃ devi svasthāveśanameva ca ।
(233b)
āpyāyavaśyavidveṣaṃ stambhanaṃ māraṇaṃ tathā ।।

mantracchedaṃ tathā mohaṃ puṣṭirāpyāya śāntikaṃ ।
dāhaḥ śoṣaṇasantāpastobhādijvarasantatiḥ ।।

niyuktadhyānayogena jitalakṣeṇamaṃtriṇā ।
sphaṭikadhyānavat svasthāṃ sudhau tāṃ varavastajaṃ ।।

tyasnaṃ viśeti deveśi padmasūtreva sarvvagā ।
yatra niyojyeta tatra tatra prasā(dha)yet ।।

sitaraktapītakṛṣṇāṃ dhāmnā kṛṣṇā maheśvari ।
sphaṭiko ca tapaccaiṣā tuhinajvalanaprabhā ।।

etatte sarvvamākhyātaṃ vijñānaṃ sārvvakāmikaṃ ।
lakṣmyodayavicārajño muktaeva na saṃśayaḥ ।।

tasmānna deyaṃ deveśi yasya kasyacideva hi ।
mahālakṣmī matamidaṃ bhāṣitaṃ divyarūpiṇā ।।

tatsakāśāt tu deveśi śrīkaṃṭhena mahātmanā ।
śukreṇa tu tato labdhaṃ tasyādrāvaṇa ātmavān ।।

vibhīṣaṇena tasmāt tu tena rāmasya cārpitaṃ ।
tato lokāntarī bhūtaṃ punaste kathitaṃ mayā ।।

śṛṇvatorvā paṭhantorvvā kurvvāṇaścānubhāvitaḥ ।
sarvvamuktimupayāṃ śriyā tejopabṛṃhitāḥ ।।

bhavanti bhavināṃ vāpi siddhimuttipradāyinaḥ ।
evantatra varasyānte mahālakṣmī prakīrttitā ।।

yasyā tulyā na paśyāmi vidyāpīṭhasya madhyataḥ ।
atra tantravare yāvadvidyāmaṃtrā udāhṛtāḥ ।।
tāvatsu samamevoktaṃ paramānaṃdabhairavaṃ । iti

lakṣmīmatamaṇḍatantrasyānte tavoditaṃ subhru ।
kimaparamadhunakṣye vadayatte hṛtsthitaṃ gauri ।।
(234a)
।। iti bhairavaśrotasi vidyāpīṭhe śiraśchede jayadrathayāmale mahātantre caturviṃśatisāhasre caturthaṣaṭke
mahālakṣmīvidhiḥ paṭalaḥ ।। ।।

śrīdevyuvāca ।।

bhagavan saṃśayosmākaṃ tadbrūhi tripurāntaka ।
paramānaṃdanāmeti bhairavaṃ yadudāhṛtaṃ ।।

samasarvvatra devīnāṃ yasmādevaṃ prakīrttitaṃ ।
kantamākhyā hi bhagavan yathāvadvaktumarhasi ।। ।।

śrībhairava uvāca ।।

śṛṇu pūrṇṇenduvadane yattvayā praśnamīritaṃ ।
idaṃ rahasyaṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ pāpanāśanaṃ ।।

yanna kasya cidākhyātaṃ tadadya kathayāmyahaṃ ।
tantrarājavarasyāṃte bhairavānaṃdanirṇṇayaṃ ।।

purā mahāyāgavaraḍaṃbarākhyeṣu bhairave ।
sutarpitohaṃ hṛṣṭātmā mahānaṃdamahākulaḥ ।।

śaktidehamathonyādya vikāsyānaṃdajaṃ kulaṃ ।
prakāśānaṃdamadhyastho narīnartti tadā bhṛśaṃ ।।

tāvat sphuranmahārāva rāviṇīkulacakrataḥ ।
udatiṣṭhanmahānaṃdamadarūpamabhairavaṃ ।।

lotākṣaṃ raktakṛṣṇaṃ bahupādvadanoddhṛtaṃ ।
anekavaktranayanaṃ anekāścaryamaṃtharaṃ ।।

bahuśaktisamāyuktaṃ bahuvādyaśatākulaṃ ।
bahubhaṃgīśatālīnaṃ bahubhāṇḍavamaṇḍitaṃ ।।

sphuradvīrapadodyūmaṃ paramāna(nda)naṃditaṃ ।
mahāpātakahantāraṃ brahmahatyākṣayaṃ karaṃ ।।

mahāsaubhāgyajananaṃ mahāsiddhipradāyakaṃ ।
mahālakṣmyudayakaraṃ bhadrasiddhipradāyikaṃ ।।

sravataṃ paramānandaṃ ra(sā)tsāramanantakaṃ ।
(234b)
evamālokya deveśi kṣuddhāhaṃ abhavattadā ।।

sarvvāvara * ṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ śaktibhiśca maheśvari ।
tatastu ttoṣayeddevi paramānaṃdabhairavaṃ ।।
yathā tacca pravakṣyāmi stotra rājānamadbhutaṃ ।।

namostute bhairavarūpa dhā - -,
namostu me sarvvariseṣamūrtte ।
namostute śaktiśatāśritāya,
namostute nityamadoddhatāya ।।

yenākhilabhairavacakramugraṃ ।
yogīśvarāṇāmapi sattamāsti,
yenākhilaṃ mantravalaṃ sutṛptaṃ ।
sadyoginī nāmapi mūrttamāste ।।

tathā vidhiṃ devi mayā yadehaṃ,
samastaṣaḍūparasottamottamaṃ ।। ।।

evamākarṇya tuṣṭe me deveśo madya bhairavaḥ ।
svarūpaṃ drāvayasararasarūpamarūttamaṃ ।।

taṃ mayā devadeveśi galadbiṃdusāmayaṃ ।
trikhaṇḍakhādyakhaṃḍasthaṃ gṛhītaṃ paramāmṛtaṃ ।।
(235a)
ekāśīti sphuranmarmā cakreśānyāstu tarpitāḥ ।
tatastu sphuṭitāḥ sarvvāḥ śaktayaḥ pūrṇavigrahāḥ ।।

tadā para(ma)rū(pe)ṇa mahākālī pratarpitā ।
bhairavaṃ rūpamāsthāya viśvarūpasamāgatā ।।

tadā tadbhairavaṃ rūpaṃ cakramatyuttamottamaṃ ।
saṃvṛtaṃ viśvasaṃhāraṃ ānaṃdādipadākulaṃ ।।

naṃdanto yugakoṭīnāṃ vṛṃda me rūpadoddhataṃ ।
tatopaśāntaṃ tumulaṃ kulākulaniyogataḥ ।।

tatastu saccaṃ devībhiḥ śaktibhiḥścābhibhairavi ।
varddhitaṃ vapasāhasraiḥ paramānaṃdabhairavaṃ ।।

tataḥ prabhṛtitaṃ devaṃ sarvvamaṇḍalatarpaṇaṃ ।
sarvvamantrakulānāṃ ca dīpanaṃ paramāmṛtaṃ ।।

yena ghrātaṃ śrutaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ pītaṃ spṛṣṭaṃ maheśvari ।
bhogamokṣapradaṃ tasyāt sarvvasiddhipravarttakaṃ ।।

mahāmantra(kuṃ)kulaṃ sarvvaṃ agre tiṣṭhati kiṃkaraṃ ।
varṇṇanti yoginī vṛṃdāḥ sarvvabhairavacakragāḥ ।।

naṃdati mattā valgaṃ saḥ krīḍāmo bhairavārṇṇave ।
gṛhīta mātreṇa sadā madyanāthena (suṃ)dari ।।

mokṣabhāgvaravīrasyānmukhyāsau bhogabhāgbhavet ।
tannāsti yanna siddhyeta sādhakendrasya bhāmini ।।

mahāmadyaprabhāvena sākṣādbhairavavadbhavet ।

atha kiṃ bahunoktena saṃkṣepādi * mucyate ।।

yathā bhairavacakreṣu nāyakaḥ śivabhaira(vāḥ) ।
(235b)
devatācakrasaṃdohe yathā kālāṃtakīparā ।।

* * tathā rasendrāṇāṃ rasendro pāranāyakaḥ ।
sadvibhūtipradā jñeyaḥ sarvvakāmaphalapradaḥ ।।

piṇḍasiddhiprado jñeyo ma(ho)ṣadhiparigrahaṃ ।
mahāyāgoditaṃ sarvvaṃ tena siddhyati līlayā ।।

nāstyarthaśuddhirdeveśi taṃ vinā rasanāyakaṃ ।

himāhayādi(de)śeṣu oṣadhyāyāḥ samutthitāḥ ।
tāsāṃ * * disiddhirno mārito jāritastuvā ।।

mahābījayutaḥ sūto baṃdhamāyāti susvaraṃ ।
hemābhrakaṃ ca tārābhraṃ śulvābhraṃ nāgavaṃka(jaṃ) ।।

mahābījamiti proktaṃ khahaṃcoparasātmakaṃ ।
jāritaṃ sāritaṃ devi koṭivedho bhaviṣyati ।।

valāmoḍhā viṣṇukrāntā kāṃcanī vividhā hi yā ।
pītaraktāsitā cānyā sthalajā padminī * yā ।।

mahāmedā somavallī nirguṃḍī lāṃgalī tathā ।
sarpākṣī kākajaṃghā ca rudatī mīnalocanā ।।

ekavīrā hanumatī kṣīrakā kolikā tathā ।
evamādyā mahābhāge divyauṣadhya udāhṛtāḥ ।।

āsāṃ saṃsparśamātreṇa bhūtakau baṃdhameṣyati ।
yena dehaṃ tathā bhūti ra(ktasya) sādhako bhavet ।।

ghanāṃvararave khaṃḍā baliśṛṃgī viṣāditi ।
yoṣinmeyūṣasaṃsikte bījayuktaṃ hi sūtakaṃ ।।

tamaṣṭāṃśena hemādicāryaṃ khinnāni yāsite ।
āśuddhaṃ ca suśuddhaiśca dīpayantragataṃ kuru ।।

tacchuddhe guḍapakveṣvavarttisodimanmanaṃ ।
(236a)
svedanaṃ doṣavigaṇairyathālābhasamutthitaiḥ ।।

paścādbījādikaṃ kāryaṃ yathoddiṣṭaṃ ca bhairava ।
gaṃdharvvādibhavettailo niṣākācchuddhyate baliḥ ।।

munisaṃkhyā krameṇaiva śuddhaṃ saṃyojayettataḥ ।

yāvanmuni dinaṃ paścājjvālayedagninā priye ।

sahasrāṇi mahārakṣā * * * * sa rakṣitaḥ ।

evaṃ jīrṇṇapatralepānvāre sau * * trayā ।

(236b)
ratnabhūmakhilādvanisthitaḥ ।। sarvvabhāvavararatnasiddhidaṃmityau ।।

(237a)
